

-- ====================================================================
-- GOLF HUB — Hunting Together RPG
-- Auto Farm Tab (GOLFGUI Engine) — WALK ONLY, NO TELEPORT
-- Anti-Stuck & Wall Avoidance System (Raycast Whiskers + Auto-Jump)
-- ====================================================================

-- "Close" means stop. Once this copy of the hub is closed (red dot, Unload GUI, reload) every thread of it parks for
-- good at its next task.wait and delayed callbacks are dropped. A flag check in each loop was not enough: a loop that
-- slept through the close still did its work once more (dismantle, Quick Match), and routines carried on to the end.
-- task.closer = the thread doing the shutdown: only that one may still wait.
local task = (function()
    local real = task
    local this = setmetatable({}, { __index = real })
    local function parkIfClosed()
        if this.closer and coroutine.running() ~= this.closer then coroutine.yield() end
    end
    this.wait = function(seconds)
        parkIfClosed()
        local waited = real.wait(seconds)
        parkIfClosed()
        return waited
    end
    this.delay = function(seconds, fn, ...)
        return real.delay(seconds, function(...) if not this.closer then return fn(...) end end, ...)
    end
    this.defer = function(fn, ...)
        return real.defer(function(...) if not this.closer then return fn(...) end end, ...)
    end
    return this
end)()

-- AutoExec & Environment Ready Guard
if not game:IsLoaded() then
    game.Loaded:Wait()
end
-- Crash guard: the client crashed ~8 s into fresh launches, right as autoexec ran this 1 MB script on top of the
-- game's own load. Let the character spawn and the game settle before compiling the rest of the hub.
do
    local plr = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer
    local t0 = os.clock()
    while not (plr and plr.Character and plr.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")) and os.clock() - t0 < 30 do
        task.wait(0.5)
        plr = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer
    end
    task.wait(4)
end

-- Target Game Check: Hunting Together RPG (UniverseId: 10769401924, PlaceId: 117483759937632, Dungeon: 100618538481971)
local TARGET_UNIVERSE_ID = 10769401924
local TARGET_PLACE_ID = 117483759937632
local DUNGEON_PLACE_ID = 100618538481971

if game.GameId ~= TARGET_UNIVERSE_ID and game.PlaceId ~= TARGET_PLACE_ID and game.PlaceId ~= DUNGEON_PLACE_ID then
    local isTargetGame = false
    pcall(function()
        local info = game:GetService("MarketplaceService"):GetProductInfo(game.PlaceId)
        if info and info.Name and (info.Name:lower():find("hunting together") or info.Name:lower():find("dungeon")) then
            isTargetGame = true
        end
    end)
    if not isTargetGame then
        return
    end
end

-- Unload previous Golf Hub instance if already running
if getgenv and getgenv().GolfHub_Unload then
    pcall(getgenv().GolfHub_Unload)
end

-- Reload hygiene: connections made through _G.GolfHub_Conn and polling loops that compare _G.GolfHub_Gen are dropped on the next reload / Unload,
-- so a manual re-execute never leaves two copies of the same handler or loop running.
_G.GolfHub_Gen = (tonumber(_G.GolfHub_Gen) or 0) + 1
_G.GolfHub_Conns = {}
_G.GolfHub_Conn = function(signal, fn)
    local c = signal:Connect(fn)
    table.insert(_G.GolfHub_Conns, c)
    return c
end

-- Player:RequestStreamAroundAsync can hang far past its timeout (seen live: calls with a 1s timeout still pending after 8s), and the dungeon
-- and boss loops called it inline, so one hang froze the whole loop and the character stood still until the dungeon timer ran out.
-- It now runs in its own thread, the caller waits at most timeout + 0.35s, and no new request is sent while an earlier one is still hung.
_G.GolfHub_StreamPendingSince = nil
_G.GolfHub_StreamAround = function(pos, timeout)
    if _G.GolfHub_StreamPendingSince and (tick() - _G.GolfHub_StreamPendingSince) < 15 then return false end
    local finished = false
    _G.GolfHub_StreamPendingSince = tick()
    task.spawn(function()
        pcall(function() game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer:RequestStreamAroundAsync(pos, timeout) end)
        finished = true
        _G.GolfHub_StreamPendingSince = nil
    end)
    local limit = tick() + (tonumber(timeout) or 1) + 0.35
    while not finished and tick() < limit do task.wait(0.03) end
    return finished
end

-- ====================================================================
-- FAST BOSS SCOUT — lives in its own file now (golf-scout.lua, deployed next to this one).
-- The autoexec loader runs it on the loading screen, before the character spawns and before this 1 MB file is
-- compiled, so a dead boss means a hop without waiting for any of that. Loading it here again only shares its
-- server list (_G.GolfHub_Scout.pickServer) with performServerHop; it checks the boss once per server.
-- ====================================================================
pcall(function()
    -- Skip redundant compilation if already executed by the autoexec loader
    if _G.GolfHub_Scout and type(_G.GolfHub_Scout.pickServer) == "function" then return end
    if isfile and isfile("GolfHub_HuntingTogether/golf-scout.lua") then
        local fn = loadstring(readfile("GolfHub_HuntingTogether/golf-scout.lua"))
        if fn then fn() end
    end
end)
task.wait() -- yield point: spread the start over frames (see the note before TAB: AUTO FARM)


-- ปิดระบบบอทเกมและเควสเกมทันทีตั้งแต่เสี้ยววินาทีแรกที่เริ่มรัน เพื่อไม่ให้เกมลากตัวละครเดินลงบันไดหนีห้องบอส
pcall(function()
    local lp = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer
    if lp then
        lp:SetAttribute("AutoOn", false)
        lp:SetAttribute("QuestNavId", "")
        local Remotes = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("Remotes")
        if Remotes then
            if Remotes:FindFirstChild("BotAutoState") then Remotes.BotAutoState:FireServer(false) end
            if Remotes:FindFirstChild("QuestAction") then Remotes.QuestAction:FireServer("nav", "") end
        end
    end
end)

-- Fail-Safe Input Unstick: ปล่อยปุ่ม KeyCode.E ทันที ป้องกันปุ่ม E ค้างในระบบ Roblox
pcall(function()
    local vim = game:GetService("VirtualInputManager")
    if vim then
        vim:SendKeyEvent(false, Enum.KeyCode.E, false, game)
    end
end)

-- Wait for LocalPlayer and Character
local PlayersService = game:GetService("Players")
local targetLp = PlayersService.LocalPlayer
if not targetLp then
    local pWaitStart = tick()
    while not PlayersService.LocalPlayer and (tick() - pWaitStart < 15) do
        task.wait(0.1)
    end
    targetLp = PlayersService.LocalPlayer
end
if targetLp then
    local char = targetLp.Character
    if not char then
        pcall(function() char = targetLp.CharacterAdded:Wait() end)
    end
    if char and not char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
        pcall(function() char:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 10) end)
    end
end

-- Wait for Game Remotes & Data
local RepStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
pcall(function() RepStorage:WaitForChild("Remotes", 15) end)

-- ====================================================================
-- AUTO DISCONNECT DETECTOR & CLEAN SHUTDOWN WATCHDOG
-- ====================================================================
-- When Roblox shows a Disconnected prompt (e.g. error 273, 277, 279, 267, 268, 529),
-- log code and message to GolfHub_HuntingTogether/<user>/disconnect.txt and call game:Shutdown().
-- This ensures the client terminates cleanly so GOLFManager can rejoin it alone after 15s.
do
    local handledDisconnect = false
    local lastTeleportStartedAt = 0

    local function markTeleportStarted()
        lastTeleportStartedAt = os.clock()
        _G.GolfHub_LastTeleportStartedAt = os.clock()
    end

    -- ดักจับ OnTeleport จาก TeleportService ของเครื่องผู้เล่น เพื่อรับรู้เมื่อเริ่มกระบวนการวาร์ป/ย้ายเซิร์ฟ
    pcall(function()
        local lp = (targetLp and targetLp.Name and targetLp) or PlayersService.LocalPlayer
        if lp then
            _G.GolfHub_Conn(lp.OnTeleport, function(state)
                local sName = tostring(state)
                if state == Enum.TeleportState.Started
                    or state == Enum.TeleportState.InProgress
                    or state == Enum.TeleportState.Requested
                    or sName:find("Started")
                    or sName:find("InProgress")
                    or sName:find("Requested") then
                    markTeleportStarted()
                end
            end)
        end
    end)

    local function isTeleportInProgress()
        local now = os.clock()
        if (now - lastTeleportStartedAt) < 30 then return true end
        local gStarted = tonumber(_G.GolfHub_LastTeleportStartedAt) or 0
        if (now - gStarted) < 30 then return true end
        local gHopUntil = tonumber(_G.GolfHub_HopInFlightUntil) or 0
        if tick() < gHopUntil then return true end
        return false
    end

    local function logDisconnectEntry(fileName, code, msg)
        pcall(function()
            local pName = (targetLp and targetLp.Name) or (PlayersService.LocalPlayer and PlayersService.LocalPlayer.Name) or "Unknown"
            local folder = "GolfHub_HuntingTogether/" .. pName
            if makefolder and not isfolder(folder) then pcall(makefolder, folder) end
            local fn = folder .. "/" .. fileName
            -- os.date โดยไม่ใส่ ! จะใช้เวลาท้องถิ่นของเครื่อง PC (Local Time) ตรงตามรูปแบบ DateTime.Now ของ GOLFManager
            local localTime = os.date("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")
            local cleanMsg = tostring(msg or ""):gsub("[\r\n]+", " ")
            local entry = string.format("%s | CODE: %s | %s\n", localTime, tostring(code), cleanMsg)
            if writefile then
                writefile(fn, entry)
            end
        end)
    end

    -- รายการรหัสข้อผิดพลาดตัดการเชื่อมต่อจริงจาก Roblox (TASK-018-r3)
    local REAL_DISCONNECT_CODES = {
        [273] = true, -- Same account launched experience from different device
        [277] = true, -- Lost connection to the game
        [279] = true, -- Failed to connect to ID (connection attempt failed)
        [267] = true, -- Kicked by server/script
        [268] = true, -- Kicked due to unexpected client behavior
        [529] = true, -- Roblox service unavailable / HTTP error
        [264] = true, -- Same account launched game from another device
        [266] = true, -- Connection timed out
        [278] = true, -- Kicked for being idle (AFK 20 minutes)
    }

    local function onDisconnectDetected(msg)
        msg = tostring(msg or "")
        if msg == "" then return end
        local code = msg:match("%(Error Code:%s*(%d+)%)") or msg:match("Error Code:%s*(%d+)") or "unknown"
        local codeNum = tonumber(code)
        local lowerMsg = string.lower(msg)

        -- 1. การออกจากเกมที่เกิดจากฝั่ง Client เอง (DisconnectClientInitiated, ย้ายเซิร์ฟ, หรือคำสั่งปิด)
        local isClientInitiated = lowerMsg:find("disconnectclientinitiated", 1, true) ~= nil
            or lowerMsg:find("clientinitiated", 1, true) ~= nil

        -- 2. ข้อความแจ้งเตือน Teleport ล้มเหลว (codes 768-775 หรือ Teleport failed)
        local isTeleportFailure = (codeNum and codeNum >= 768 and codeNum <= 775)
            or lowerMsg:find("teleport failed", 1, true) ~= nil
            or lowerMsg:find("teleporting failed", 1, true) ~= nil
            or lowerMsg:find("failed to teleport", 1, true) ~= nil
            or lowerMsg:find("cannot teleport", 1, true) ~= nil

        -- 3. ตรวจสอบว่ากำลังอยู่ระหว่างวาร์ป/ย้ายเซิร์ฟเวอร์ใน 30 วินาทีที่ผ่านมาหรือไม่
        local teleportActive = isTeleportInProgress()

        -- 4. ข้อความระบุการ Kick / หลุดการเชื่อมต่อที่ชัดเจน
        local hasExplicitText = lowerMsg:find("different device", 1, true) ~= nil
            or lowerMsg:find("another device", 1, true) ~= nil
            or lowerMsg:find("same account launched", 1, true) ~= nil
            or lowerMsg:find("lost connection", 1, true) ~= nil
            or lowerMsg:find("you have been kicked", 1, true) ~= nil
            or lowerMsg:find("kicked from this experience", 1, true) ~= nil
            or (lowerMsg:find("kicked", 1, true) ~= nil and lowerMsg:find("party", 1, true) == nil)

        -- เงื่อนไขตัดสินว่าเป็น Disconnect จริงที่ต้อง Shutdown:
        -- - ต้องไม่เป็นการออกที่ Client สั่งเอง (isClientInitiated)
        -- - ต้องไม่ใช่ Teleport ล้มเหลว (isTeleportFailure) ที่ระบบ Hop จะ retry เอง
        -- - ต้องไม่มี Teleport เกิดขึ้นใน 30 วินาทีที่ผ่านมา (teleportActive)
        -- - ถ้ารหัสระบุไม่ได้ (unknown) ต้องมีข้อความยืนยันชัดเจนว่าถูก kick / lost connection / different device
        -- - ถ้ารหัสระบุได้ ต้องอยู่ใน REAL_DISCONNECT_CODES (หรือมีข้อความ kick ชัดเจน)
        local hasValidCode = codeNum and REAL_DISCONNECT_CODES[codeNum] == true
        local isGenuineDisconnect = false

        if not isClientInitiated and not isTeleportFailure and not teleportActive then
            if hasValidCode or hasExplicitText then
                isGenuineDisconnect = true
            end
        end

        if not isGenuineDisconnect then
            -- กรณีออกเอง / กำลังวาร์ป / ย้ายเซิร์ฟ / Teleport fail: บันทึกเข้า ignored และห้าม Shutdown เด็ดขาด
            logDisconnectEntry("disconnect_ignored.txt", code, msg)
            return
        end

        if handledDisconnect then return end
        handledDisconnect = true

        -- หลุดการเชื่อมต่อจริง: บันทึก disconnect.txt เพื่อให้ GOLFManager จัดการ Rejoin เดียว
        logDisconnectEntry("disconnect.txt", code, msg)

        task.wait(0.5)
        pcall(function() game:Shutdown() end)
    end

    local gs = cloneref and cloneref(game:GetService("GuiService")) or game:GetService("GuiService")
    _G.GolfHub_Conn(gs.ErrorMessageChanged, function(msg)
        if msg and msg ~= "" then
            onDisconnectDetected(msg)
        end
    end)

    pcall(function()
        local curMsg = gs:GetErrorMessage()
        if curMsg and curMsg ~= "" then
            task.defer(onDisconnectDetected, curMsg)
        end
    end)

    pcall(function()
        local coreGui = cloneref and cloneref(game:GetService("CoreGui")) or game:GetService("CoreGui")
        local promptGui = coreGui:FindFirstChild("RobloxPromptGui")
        if promptGui then
            local overlay = promptGui:FindFirstChild("promptOverlay")
            if overlay then
                _G.GolfHub_Conn(overlay.ChildAdded, function(child)
                    if child.Name == "ErrorPrompt" then
                        task.wait(0.2)
                        local msg = ""
                        pcall(function()
                            local msgArea = child:FindFirstChild("MessageArea")
                            local errFrame = msgArea and msgArea:FindFirstChild("ErrorFrame")
                            local errLabel = errFrame and errFrame:FindFirstChild("ErrorMessage")
                            if errLabel then msg = errLabel.Text end
                        end)
                        if msg == "" then
                            pcall(function() msg = gs:GetErrorMessage() end)
                        end
                        if msg ~= "" then
                            onDisconnectDetected(msg)
                        end
                    end
                end)
                local existingPrompt = overlay:FindFirstChild("ErrorPrompt")
                if existingPrompt then
                    local msg = ""
                    pcall(function()
                        local msgArea = existingPrompt:FindFirstChild("MessageArea")
                        local errFrame = msgArea and msgArea:FindFirstChild("ErrorFrame")
                        local errLabel = errFrame and errFrame:FindFirstChild("ErrorMessage")
                        if errLabel then msg = errLabel.Text end
                    end)
                    if msg == "" then pcall(function() msg = gs:GetErrorMessage() end) end
                    if msg ~= "" then task.defer(onDisconnectDetected, msg) end
                end
            end
        end
    end)
end

local GOLFGUI = (function(...)
-- Ouwland Suite · src/golfgui.lua — GOLFGUI: the suite's own UI library
-- Loaded in order by main.lua (before gui). Exports S.GOLFGUI. Also runs standalone: loadstring(src)() returns the library.
--
-- The look follows the layout the user approved on 2026-09-28 (mockup https://claude.ai/artifact/SwQtuZqf1SwUGw64mfcF3k):
--   header bar (globe · title / version · window dots) · sidebar (search, grouped pages, profile) ·
--   status line on top of the content · page title · full-width cards whose rows are split by thin lines
-- Colors and sizes are the mockup's own values (T below). Fonts: IBM Plex Sans / Mono like the mockup (see FONT).
--
-- The public API (method names, Options[flag], Class names, config files) matches MacLib, so gui.lua and the saved
-- configs (OuwlandSuite/settings/*.json, recovery) keep working. The config save/load code is taken from MacLib:
--
--   MacLib by biggaboy212 / Proton Utilities — https://github.com/biggaboy212/Maclib — MIT License
--   Copyright (c) 2024 Proton Utilities
--   Permission is hereby granted, free of charge, to any person obtaining a copy of this software and associated
--   documentation files (the "Software"), to deal in the Software without restriction, including without limitation the
--   rights to use, copy, modify, merge, publish, distribute, sublicense, and/or sell copies of the Software, and to permit
--   persons to whom the Software is furnished to do so, subject to the following conditions:
--   The above copyright notice and this permission notice shall be included in all copies or substantial portions of
--   the Software.
--   THE SOFTWARE IS PROVIDED "AS IS", WITHOUT WARRANTY OF ANY KIND, EXPRESS OR IMPLIED, INCLUDING BUT NOT LIMITED TO THE
--   WARRANTIES OF MERCHANTABILITY, FITNESS FOR A PARTICULAR PURPOSE AND NONINFRINGEMENT. IN NO EVENT SHALL THE AUTHORS OR
--   COPYRIGHT HOLDERS BE LIABLE FOR ANY CLAIM, DAMAGES OR OTHER LIABILITY, WHETHER IN AN ACTION OF CONTRACT, TORT OR
--   OTHERWISE, ARISING FROM, OUT OF OR IN CONNECTION WITH THE SOFTWARE OR THE USE OR OTHER DEALINGS IN THE SOFTWARE.
--
-- Config fixes over MacLib: switches saved "off" are restored too · loaded slider values reach their callbacks.
--
-- API (what gui.lua uses)
--   GOLFGUI:Window{ Title, Subtitle, Size, GuiName, Keybind, HeaderHeight, StatusBar, SidebarWidth }
--     :TabGroup{ Name, Separator } → :Tab{ Name, Title, Description } → :Section() (a card) · Tab:Select() · Tab:SetBadge(on)
--     :SetStatus(title, text, right, tone) · :Notify{ Title, Description, Lifetime } · :Dialog{ Title, Description, Buttons }
--     :GlobalSetting{ Name, Default, Callback } · :SetState(open) / :GetState() · :Unload() · .onUnloaded(fn)
--   card: Header{ Name, Description, Tag } · Toggle{ Name, Description, Danger, Default, Callback } ·
--     Slider{ Name, Description, Default, Minimum, Maximum, Precision, Callback } ·
--     Dropdown{ Name, Description, Options, Default, Multi, Required, Search, Callback } ·
--     Status{ Name, Text, Tone } · Value{ Name, Text } · Label{ Text } · Paragraph{ Header, Body } · Warning{ Text } ·
--     Button{ Name, Callback, Primary } · Buttons{ {Name, Callback, Primary}, ... } · Disclosure{ Name, Callback } · Divider()
--   every row: :SetVisibility(on) · :SetNested(on) (an "advanced" row: darker, indented)
--   tones: "run" green · "wait" amber · "done" blue · "bad" red · "off" grey
local S = ...

local GOLFGUI = {
    Options = {},
    Folder = "GOLFGUI",
    GetService = function(service)
        return cloneref and cloneref(game:GetService(service)) or game:GetService(service)
    end,
}

local TweenService = GOLFGUI.GetService("TweenService")
local HttpService = GOLFGUI.GetService("HttpService")
local UserInputService = GOLFGUI.GetService("UserInputService")
local RunService = GOLFGUI.GetService("RunService")
local Players = GOLFGUI.GetService("Players")
local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
local isStudio = RunService:IsStudio()

local function rgb(r, g, b) return Color3.fromRGB(r, g, b) end

-- the mockup's colors
local T = {
    header = rgb(17, 18, 20), headerLine = rgb(36, 37, 41),
    sidebar = rgb(17, 19, 24), sidebarLine = rgb(35, 38, 46),
    content = rgb(24, 27, 33), windowStroke = rgb(42, 47, 56),
    card = rgb(31, 35, 43), cardStroke = rgb(42, 47, 56), line = rgb(42, 47, 56),
    nested = rgb(27, 30, 37),
    danger = rgb(42, 28, 31), dangerLine = rgb(74, 44, 48), dangerText = rgb(245, 138, 128), dangerOn = rgb(208, 88, 76),
    text = rgb(232, 234, 237), bright = rgb(241, 241, 242), white = rgb(255, 255, 255),
    sub = rgb(154, 161, 173), muted = rgb(142, 144, 151), value = rgb(201, 206, 214), log = rgb(184, 190, 200),
    nav = rgb(184, 190, 200), navActive = rgb(35, 50, 74), navHover = rgb(26, 29, 36), group = rgb(125, 132, 145),
    input = rgb(38, 42, 51), inputStroke = rgb(53, 59, 70),
    search = rgb(26, 29, 35), searchStroke = rgb(42, 46, 54), placeholder = rgb(139, 145, 156),
    accent = rgb(79, 143, 247), primary = rgb(59, 116, 214), off = rgb(58, 64, 75), knobOff = rgb(201, 206, 214),
    bar = rgb(27, 30, 36), barStroke = rgb(42, 46, 54), sep = rgb(74, 79, 89),
    warnBg = rgb(43, 38, 24), warnText = rgb(236, 208, 138),
    tagBg = rgb(35, 50, 74), tagText = rgb(169, 201, 255),
    icon = rgb(154, 156, 163), dotHollow = rgb(74, 76, 82),
    avatarBg = rgb(42, 45, 51), avatarStroke = rgb(58, 61, 68),
    scroll = rgb(58, 63, 72),
    rail = rgb(59, 59, 59), railLine = rgb(133, 133, 133),
}
local TONES = { run = rgb(76, 195, 138), wait = rgb(224, 168, 74), done = rgb(127, 176, 255), bad = rgb(240, 113, 103), off = rgb(139, 145, 156) }

-- Fonts: IBM Plex Sans / IBM Plex Mono, the mockup's own (ยฉ IBM Corp., SIL Open Font License 1.1). Roblox has no Plex
-- (only "IBM Plex Sans JP", whose metrics render smaller), so the TTFs are loaded from the executor workspace
-- (GOLFGUI/fonts/, downloaded once from IBM's GitHub when missing) through getcustomasset. Fallback: Inter / RobotoMono.
local FONT_DIR = "GOLFGUI/fonts"
local FONT_URL = "https://raw.githubusercontent.com/IBM/plex/master/packages/"
local function fontFamily(name, package, faces)
    if not (getcustomasset and writefile and isfile) then return nil end
    local ok, id = pcall(function()
        if makefolder and isfolder then
            if not isfolder("GOLFGUI") then makefolder("GOLFGUI") end
            if not isfolder(FONT_DIR) then makefolder(FONT_DIR) end
        end
        local list = {}
        for _, face in ipairs(faces) do
            local file = name .. "-" .. face[1] .. ".ttf"
            local path = FONT_DIR .. "/" .. file
            if not isfile(path) then
                local req = request or http_request
                local res = req and req({ Url = FONT_URL .. package .. "/fonts/complete/ttf/" .. file, Method = "GET" })
                local body = res and res.Body
                if type(body) ~= "string" or #body < 50000 or body:sub(1, 4) ~= "\0\1\0\0" then error("font download failed: " .. file) end
                writefile(path, body)
            end
            list[#list + 1] = { name = face[1], weight = face[2], style = "normal", assetId = getcustomasset(path) }
        end
        local json = FONT_DIR .. "/" .. name .. ".json"
        writefile(json, HttpService:JSONEncode({ name = name, faces = list }))
        return getcustomasset(json)
    end)
    if not ok then warn("[GOLFGUI] " .. tostring(id) .. " (using the fallback font)") end
    return ok and id or nil
end
local SANS = fontFamily("IBMPlexSans", "plex-sans", { { "Regular", 400 }, { "Medium", 500 }, { "SemiBold", 600 }, { "Bold", 700 } })
-- yield point: loading the font files and the icons as custom assets was the biggest single slice of the start (~80 ms)
task.wait()
local MONO = fontFamily("IBMPlexMono", "plex-mono", { { "Regular", 400 }, { "Medium", 500 } })
local SANS_ID = SANS or "rbxassetid://12187365364"
-- FONT.regular / medium / semibold are the mockup's CSS weights (400 / 500 / 600). Roblox rasterizes text lighter than
-- the browser: at the same weight "Enabled" gets ~25% less ink (176 vs Chrome 235), SemiBold too (312 vs 403).
-- One weight step up matches (Medium 254 ≈ 235), so every weight is drawn one step heavier.
local FONT = {
    regular = Font.new(SANS_ID, Enum.FontWeight.Medium),
    medium = Font.new(SANS_ID, Enum.FontWeight.SemiBold),
    semibold = Font.new(SANS_ID, Enum.FontWeight.Bold),
    mono = MONO and Font.new(MONO, Enum.FontWeight.Medium) or Font.new("rbxasset://fonts/families/RobotoMono.json"),
}
GOLFGUI.Fonts = FONT
GOLFGUI.FontSource = SANS and "IBM Plex (custom)" or "Inter (fallback)"
-- Every TextSize in this file is the mockup's CSS font-size. Roblox's TextSize is the font's LINE height, a browser's
-- font-size is the letter (em) size: Plex line = 1.3 em (hhea 1025/-275), measured Chrome/Roblox width ratio 1.29
-- (Inter 1.21). TextSize is whole numbers only, so 12.5px → 16, 13px → 17, 14px → 18.
local FONT_K = SANS and 1.29 or 1.21
local function fontPx(css) return math.floor(css * FONT_K + 0.5) end
GOLFGUI.FontPx = fontPx

local function b64decode(data)
    local dec = crypt and (crypt.base64decode or crypt.base64_decode)
    if dec then return dec(data) end
    local chars = "ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz0123456789+/"
    local map = {}
    for i = 1, 64 do map[chars:sub(i, i)] = i - 1 end
    local out, bits, n = {}, 0, 0
    for c in data:gmatch("[%w%+/]") do
        bits, n = bits * 64 + map[c], n + 6
        if n >= 8 then
            n = n - 8
            out[#out + 1] = string.char(math.floor(bits / 2 ^ n) % 256)
            bits = bits % 2 ^ n
        end
    end
    return table.concat(out)
end
local ICON = {}
task.wait() -- yield point (see the fonts above)
if getcustomasset and writefile then
    local ok, err = pcall(function()
        if makefolder and isfolder then
            if not isfolder("GOLFGUI") then makefolder("GOLFGUI") end
            if not isfolder("GOLFGUI/icons") then makefolder("GOLFGUI/icons") end
        end
        for name, data in pairs(ICON_PNG) do
            local path = "GOLFGUI/icons/" .. name .. ".png"
            -- written once; rewriting all 8 on every launch was disk work during the load stutter
            if not (isfile and isfile(path)) then writefile(path, b64decode(data)) end
            ICON[name] = getcustomasset(path)
        end
    end)
    if not ok then warn("[GOLFGUI] icons: " .. tostring(err)) end
end

-- ====================================================================
-- helpers
-- ====================================================================
local function mk(className, props, parent)
    local obj = Instance.new(className)
    if obj:IsA("GuiObject") then obj.BorderSizePixel = 0 end
    for k, v in pairs(props or {}) do obj[k] = v end
    if props and props.TextSize then obj.TextSize = fontPx(props.TextSize) end
    if parent then obj.Parent = parent end
    return obj
end
local function corner(parent, radius) return mk("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, radius) }, parent) end
-- borders sit INSIDE the frame like the mockup's CSS (box-sizing: border-box); outer = drawn outside (content-box)
local function stroke(parent, color, thickness, outer)
    local st = mk("UIStroke", { ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border, Color = color, Thickness = thickness or 1 }, parent)
    pcall(function() st.BorderStrokePosition = outer and Enum.BorderStrokePosition.Outer or Enum.BorderStrokePosition.Inner end)
    return st
end
local function padding(parent, top, right, bottom, left)
    return mk("UIPadding", {
        PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, top or 0), PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, right or 0),
        PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, bottom or 0), PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, left or 0),
    }, parent)
end
local function vlist(parent, gap)
    return mk("UIListLayout", { SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder, Padding = UDim.new(0, gap or 0) }, parent)
end
local function hlist(parent, gap, valign, halign)
    return mk("UIListLayout", {
        SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder, FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal, Padding = UDim.new(0, gap or 0),
        VerticalAlignment = valign or Enum.VerticalAlignment.Center, HorizontalAlignment = halign or Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Left,
    }, parent)
end
local function text(props, parent)
    local base = {
        BackgroundTransparency = 1, FontFace = FONT.regular, TextColor3 = T.text, TextSize = 13, RichText = true,
        TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center, Text = "",
    }
    for k, v in pairs(props or {}) do base[k] = v end
    return mk("TextLabel", base, parent)
end
local function tween(obj, time, props)
    local t = TweenService:Create(obj, TweenInfo.new(time, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), props)
    t:Play()
    return t
end

-- chevrons drawn from two thin bars (no icon assets): "right" (disclosure, rotates to point down) · "updown" (select box)
local function chevron(parent, kind, color, size)
    size = size or 12
    local holder = mk("Frame", { Name = "Chevron", BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.fromOffset(size, size) }, parent)
    local image = kind == "right" and ICON.chevronRight or ICON.chevronUpDown
    if image then
        local img = mk("ImageLabel", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, Image = image, ImageColor3 = color, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1) }, holder)
        if kind == "right" then
            holder:SetAttribute("Open", false)
            holder:GetAttributeChangedSignal("Open"):Connect(function()
                img.Rotation = holder:GetAttribute("Open") == true and 90 or 0
            end)
        end
        return holder
    end
    local k = size / 12
    local function bar(cx, cy, len, rot)
        local b = mk("Frame", {
            AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(cx * k, cy * k),
            Size = UDim2.fromOffset(len * k, 1.6), Rotation = rot, BackgroundColor3 = color,
        }, holder)
        corner(b, 1)
        return b
    end
    if kind == "right" then
        -- ">" closed, "v" open (a parent's Rotation does not turn its children → move the bars instead)
        local b1, b2 = bar(6, 4.5, 4.6, 45), bar(6, 7.5, 4.6, -45)
        holder:SetAttribute("Open", false)
        holder:GetAttributeChangedSignal("Open"):Connect(function()
            local open = holder:GetAttribute("Open") == true
            b1.Position = UDim2.fromOffset((open and 4.5 or 6) * k, (open and 6 or 4.5) * k)
            b2.Position = UDim2.fromOffset((open and 7.5 or 6) * k, (open and 6 or 7.5) * k)
        end)
    else
        bar(4.75, 3.25, 3.9, -45)
        bar(7.25, 3.25, 3.9, 45)
        bar(4.75, 8.75, 3.9, 45)
        bar(7.25, 8.75, 3.9, -45)
    end
    return holder
end

local function guiHost()
    if isStudio then return LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui") end
    local ok, h = pcall(function() return gethui and gethui() end)
    if ok and h then return h end
    return GOLFGUI.GetService("CoreGui")
end

-- connections the library makes itself (cut in Window:Unload)
local libConnections = {}
local function track(c)
    libConnections[#libConnections + 1] = c
    return c
end

local function fmtNumber(value, precision)
    precision = precision or 0
    if precision <= 0 then return tostring(math.floor(value + 0.5)) end
    return string.format("%." .. precision .. "f", value)
end

-- ====================================================================
-- rows (every card element is a row: thin line on top, label + help line on the left, control on the right)
-- ====================================================================
-- AbsoluteSize / TextBounds are screen pixels (after UIScale), UDim offsets and constraints are pixels before it.
-- Anything measured on screen goes through this before it is used as an offset, or it overflows at UI Scale 70%.
function GOLFGUI.Unscaled(obj, px)
    local node = obj
    while node and not node:IsA("LayerCollector") do
        local s = node:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIScale")
        if s and s.Scale > 0 then px = px / s.Scale end
        node = node.Parent
    end
    return px
end

local function newRow(card, opts)
    opts = opts or {}
    local row = mk("Frame", {
        Name = opts.name or "Row", BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0),
        AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, LayoutOrder = card.nextOrder(),
    }, card.frame)
    -- background (nested / danger rows): the last row of a card gets round bottom corners, "cap" keeps its top square
    local bg = mk("Frame", { Name = "Bg", BackgroundColor3 = T.nested, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), ZIndex = 1 }, row)
    local bgCorner = corner(bg, 0)
    -- the cap is the row's child, not bg's: a child inside bg made the auto-sized row grow on every layout pass (521px rows)
    local bgCap = mk("Frame", { Name = "Cap", BackgroundColor3 = T.nested, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, -10), Visible = false, ZIndex = 1 }, row)
    local line = mk("Frame", { Name = "Line", BackgroundColor3 = T.line, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 1), ZIndex = 3 }, row)
    local body = mk("Frame", { Name = "Body", BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, ZIndex = 2 }, row)
    local bodyPad = padding(body, 1 + (opts.padTop or 9), opts.padRight or 12, opts.padBottom or 9, opts.padLeft or 16)
    vlist(body, 0)
    local main = mk("Frame", {
        Name = "Main", BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, opts.minHeight or 30),
        AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, LayoutOrder = 1, ZIndex = 2,
    }, body)
    hlist(main, 16)

    local r = { frame = row, body = body, main = main, line = line, nested = false, danger = false, hasBg = false }
    local baseLeft = opts.padLeft or 16

    function r.paint()
        local color = r.danger and T.danger or T.nested
        r.hasBg = r.danger or r.nested
        bg.BackgroundColor3, bgCap.BackgroundColor3 = color, color
        bg.BackgroundTransparency = r.hasBg and 0 or 1
        line.BackgroundColor3 = r.danger and T.dangerLine or T.line
        bodyPad.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, baseLeft + (r.nested and 14 or 0))
    end
    function r.round(on)
        on = on and r.hasBg
        bgCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, on and 10 or 0)
        bgCap.Visible = on
    end
    -- r.want = what the row's owner asked for; a collapsed card hides every row on top of that
    r.want = true
    function r.setVisible(on)
        r.want = on ~= false
        row.Visible = r.want and not card.collapsed
        card.refresh()
    end
    function r.setNested(on)
        r.nested = on == true
        r.paint()
        card.refresh()
    end

    -- left column: name + grey help line
    function r.addLabel(name, description, danger)
        local col = mk("Frame", { Name = "Left", BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, LayoutOrder = 1, ZIndex = 2 }, main)
        vlist(col, 2)
        local nameLabel = text({
            Name = "Name", Text = name or "", TextSize = 14, TextWrapped = true, AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
            Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0), LayoutOrder = 1, ZIndex = 2,
            TextColor3 = danger and T.dangerText or T.text, FontFace = danger and FONT.medium or FONT.regular,
        }, col)
        local descLabel = text({
            Name = "Description", Text = description or "", TextSize = 12, LineHeight = 1.4 / 1.3, TextWrapped = true, TextColor3 = T.sub,
            -- min height 17 = the mockup's 12px × line-height 1.4
            AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 17), LayoutOrder = 2, Visible = (description or "") ~= "", ZIndex = 2,
        }, col)
        r.left, r.nameLabel, r.descLabel = col, nameLabel, descLabel
        if danger then
            r.danger = true
            r.paint()
        end
        return nameLabel, descLabel
    end
    -- right control: the left column gives up exactly the control's width (+ 16 gap)
    function r.setRight(ctrl)
        ctrl.LayoutOrder = 2
        ctrl.Parent = main
        local function fit()
            if r.left then r.left.Size = UDim2.new(1, -(math.ceil(GOLFGUI.Unscaled(ctrl, ctrl.AbsoluteSize.X)) + 16), 0, 0) end
        end
        -- again once layout has settled: wrapped text reports an intermediate width first
        local queued = false
        local function refit()
            fit()
            if queued then return end
            queued = true
            task.delay(0.05, function()
                queued = false
                fit()
            end)
        end
        ctrl:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteSize"):Connect(refit)
        main:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteSize"):Connect(refit)
        refit()
    end

    r.paint()
    row.Visible = not card.collapsed
    card.rows[#card.rows + 1] = r
    card.refresh()
    return r
end

local function rowApi(r, obj)
    function obj:SetVisibility(on) r.setVisible(on) end
    function obj:SetNested(on) r.setNested(on) end
    obj.Row = r
    return obj
end

-- ====================================================================
-- card (Section)
-- ====================================================================
local function newCard(W, tab)
    local frame = mk("Frame", {
        Name = "Section", BackgroundColor3 = T.card, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
        LayoutOrder = tab.nextOrder(),
    }, tab.page)
    corner(frame, 10)
    stroke(frame, T.cardStroke)
    padding(frame, 1, 1, 1, 1)
    vlist(frame, 0)
    local card = { frame = frame, rows = {}, order = 0, collapsed = false }
    function card.nextOrder()
        card.order = card.order + 1
        return card.order
    end
    local pending = false
    function card.refresh()
        if pending then return end
        pending = true
        task.defer(function()
            pending = false
            local last
            for _, r in ipairs(card.rows) do
                if r.frame.Visible and r.frame.Parent then last = r end
            end
            for _, r in ipairs(card.rows) do r.round(r == last) end
        end)
    end
    function card.setCollapsed(on)
        card.collapsed = on == true
        for _, r in ipairs(card.rows) do r.frame.Visible = r.want and not card.collapsed end
        card.refresh()
    end
    local function index(s) if s and s ~= "" then tab.index(s) end end

    local SF = {}

    -- Collapsible = true: clicking the header folds the whole card up to its title (chevron on the right,
    -- Summary text shown in the title line while folded) · OnCollapse(collapsed) is told on every click
    function SF:Header(Settings)
        local collapsible = Settings.Collapsible == true
        local head = mk(collapsible and "TextButton" or "Frame", {
            Name = "Header", BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, LayoutOrder = 0,
        }, frame)
        if collapsible then head.Text = "" head.AutoButtonColor = false end
        padding(head, 14, 16, 12, 16)
        vlist(head, 4)
        local titleRow = mk("Frame", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 20), LayoutOrder = 1 }, head)
        local title = text({ Name = "HeaderText", Text = Settings.Text or Settings.Name or "", FontFace = FONT.semibold, TextSize = 15, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1) }, titleRow)
        if Settings.Tag then
            local tag = text({
                Name = "Tag", Text = Settings.Tag, TextSize = 11, FontFace = FONT.medium, TextColor3 = T.tagText, BackgroundTransparency = 0,
                BackgroundColor3 = T.tagBg, AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.X, Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 18),
                AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5), TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center,
            }, titleRow)
            corner(tag, 9)
            padding(tag, 0, 7, 0, 7)
            local function place() tag.Position = UDim2.new(0, GOLFGUI.Unscaled(title, title.TextBounds.X) + 28, 0.5, 0) end
            title:GetPropertyChangedSignal("TextBounds"):Connect(place)
            place()
        end
        local desc = text({
            Name = "HeaderDescription", Text = Settings.Description or "", TextSize = 12.5, LineHeight = 1.45 / 1.3, TextColor3 = T.sub, TextWrapped = true,
            AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 18), LayoutOrder = 2, Visible = (Settings.Description or "") ~= "",
        }, head)
        index(Settings.Name) index(Settings.Description)
        local HF = { Settings = Settings }
        local descText = Settings.Description or ""
        function HF:UpdateName(v) title.Text = v end
        function HF:UpdateDescription(v)
            descText = v or ""
            desc.Text = descText
            desc.Visible = descText ~= "" and not card.collapsed
        end
        function HF:SetVisibility(on) head.Visible = on end
        if collapsible then
            local chev = chevron(titleRow, "right", T.sub, 12)
            chev.AnchorPoint, chev.Position = Vector2.new(1, 0.5), UDim2.new(1, 0, 0.5, 0)
            local summary = text({
                Name = "Summary", Text = "", TextSize = 12.5, TextColor3 = T.sub, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5),
                Position = UDim2.new(1, -22, 0.5, 0), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.X, Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 20),
                TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right, Visible = false,
            }, titleRow)
            local function apply()
                chev:SetAttribute("Open", not card.collapsed)
                summary.Visible = card.collapsed and summary.Text ~= ""
                desc.Visible = descText ~= "" and not card.collapsed
            end
            function HF:SetCollapsed(on)
                card.setCollapsed(on)
                apply()
            end
            function HF:SetSummary(t)
                summary.Text = t or ""
                apply()
            end
            HF.UpdateSummary = HF.SetSummary
            head.MouseEnter:Connect(function() title.TextColor3 = T.bright end)
            head.MouseLeave:Connect(function() title.TextColor3 = T.text end)
            head.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                HF:SetCollapsed(not card.collapsed)
                if Settings.OnCollapse then task.spawn(Settings.OnCollapse, card.collapsed) end
            end)
            HF:SetCollapsed(Settings.Collapsed == true)
        end
        return HF
    end

    function SF:Toggle(Settings, Flag)
        Flag = Flag or (Settings and Settings.Flag)
        local r = newRow(card)
        local nameLabel = r.addLabel(Settings.Name, Settings.Description, Settings.Danger)
        index(Settings.Name) index(Settings.Description)
        local button = mk("TextButton", { Name = "Switch", Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.fromOffset(52, 36) })
        local track = mk("Frame", { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(40, 24), BackgroundColor3 = T.off }, button)
        corner(track, 12)
        local knob = mk("Frame", { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(0, 3, 0.5, 0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(18, 18), BackgroundColor3 = T.knobOff }, track)
        corner(knob, 9)
        r.setRight(button)
        local TF = rowApi(r, { Settings = Settings, Class = "Toggle", IgnoreConfig = false, State = Settings.Default == true })
        local function render(animate)
            local on = TF.State == true
            local trackColor = on and (Settings.Danger and T.dangerOn or T.accent) or T.off
            local knobPos = UDim2.new(0, on and 19 or 3, 0.5, 0)
            local knobColor = on and T.white or T.knobOff
            if animate then
                tween(track, 0.15, { BackgroundColor3 = trackColor })
                tween(knob, 0.15, { Position = knobPos, BackgroundColor3 = knobColor })
            else
                track.BackgroundColor3, knob.Position, knob.BackgroundColor3 = trackColor, knobPos, knobColor
            end
        end
        function TF:UpdateState(state, silent)
            TF.State = state == true
            render(true)
            if not silent and Settings.Callback then Settings.Callback(TF.State) end
            if not silent and Flag and not TF.IgnoreConfig and GOLFGUI.TriggerAutoSave then GOLFGUI:TriggerAutoSave() end
        end
        function TF:Set(state) TF:UpdateState(state) end
        function TF:SetValue(state) TF:UpdateState(state) end
        function TF:Toggle() TF:UpdateState(not TF.State) end
        function TF:GetState() return TF.State end
        function TF:UpdateName(v) Settings.Name = v nameLabel.Text = v end
        button.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() TF.SaveAs = nil TF:Toggle() end) -- the player's click is the real choice
        render(false)
        if Flag then GOLFGUI.Options[Flag] = TF end
        return TF
    end

    function SF:Slider(Settings, Flag)
        Flag = Flag or (Settings and Settings.Flag)
        local r = newRow(card)
        local nameLabel = r.addLabel(Settings.Name, Settings.Description)
        index(Settings.Name) index(Settings.Description)
        local holder = mk("Frame", { Name = "Slider", BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.fromOffset(230, 24) })
        -- the browser's range input: 8px pill rail with a 1px grey edge, blue fill, 14px knob kept inside the rail
        local rail = mk("Frame", { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(0, 1, 0.5, 0), Size = UDim2.new(1, -54, 0, 8), BackgroundColor3 = T.rail }, holder)
        corner(rail, 4)
        stroke(rail, T.railLine, 1)
        local fill = mk("Frame", { Size = UDim2.fromScale(0, 1), BackgroundColor3 = T.accent }, rail)
        corner(fill, 4)
        local knob = mk("Frame", { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(0, 7, 0.5, 0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(14, 14), BackgroundColor3 = T.accent, ZIndex = 3 }, rail)
        corner(knob, 7)
        local valueLabel = text({
            AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0.5, 0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(44, 20),
            TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right, FontFace = FONT.mono, TextSize = 12.5, TextColor3 = T.text,
        }, holder)
        local hit = mk("TextButton", { Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, -44, 1, 0), ZIndex = 4 }, holder)
        r.setRight(holder)
        local minimum = Settings.Minimum or Settings.Min or 0
        local maximum = Settings.Maximum or Settings.Max or 100
        local precision = Settings.Precision or Settings.Decimals or 0
        local SL = rowApi(r, { Settings = Settings, Class = "Slider", IgnoreConfig = false })
        local function setValue(v, fire)
            v = tonumber(v) or minimum
            v = math.clamp(v, minimum, maximum)
            local mult = 10 ^ precision
            v = math.floor(v * mult + 0.5) / mult
            local frac = maximum > minimum and (v - minimum) / (maximum - minimum) or 0
            fill.Size = UDim2.new(frac, 7 - 14 * frac, 1, 0)
            knob.Position = UDim2.new(frac, 7 - 14 * frac, 0.5, 0)
            valueLabel.Text = (Settings.Prefix or "") .. fmtNumber(v, precision) .. (Settings.Suffix or "")
            local changed = SL.Value ~= v
            SL.Value = v
            if fire and changed and Settings.Callback then task.spawn(Settings.Callback, v) end
            if fire and changed and Flag and not SL.IgnoreConfig and GOLFGUI.TriggerAutoSave then GOLFGUI:TriggerAutoSave() end
        end
        local dragging = false
        -- the rail as it was when pressed: UI Scale moves and resizes it under the pointer, so the value jumped around
        local railX, railW = 0, 1
        local function fromX(x)
            local frac = math.clamp((x - railX) / math.max(1, railW), 0, 1)
            setValue(minimum + frac * (maximum - minimum), true)
        end
        hit.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
            if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                dragging = true
                railX, railW = rail.AbsolutePosition.X, rail.AbsoluteSize.X
                fromX(input.Position.X)
            end
        end)
        track(UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
            if dragging and (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
                fromX(input.Position.X)
            end
        end))
        track(UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
            if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then dragging = false end
        end))
        function SL:UpdateValue(v) setValue(v, false) end -- MacLib: moves the knob only (no callback)
        function SL:Set(v) setValue(v, true) end
        function SL:SetValue(v) setValue(v, true) end
        function SL:UpdateName(v) Settings.Name = v nameLabel.Text = v end
        setValue(Settings.Default or minimum, false)
        if Flag then GOLFGUI.Options[Flag] = SL end
        return SL
    end

    function SF:Dropdown(Settings, Flag)
        Flag = Flag or (Settings and Settings.Flag)
        local r = newRow(card)
        local nameLabel = r.addLabel(Settings.Name, Settings.Description)
        index(Settings.Name) index(Settings.Description)
        local multi = Settings.Multi == true
        local box = mk("TextButton", {
            Name = "Select", Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false, BackgroundColor3 = T.input,
            Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 32), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.X, ClipsDescendants = true,
        })
        corner(box, 7)
        local boxStroke = stroke(box, T.inputStroke)
        padding(box, 0, 8, 0, 11)
        hlist(box, 8)
        local valueText = text({
            Name = "Value", Text = "", TextSize = 13, AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.X, Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 32),
            TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd, LayoutOrder = 1,
        }, box)
        mk("UISizeConstraint", { MaxSize = Vector2.new(230, 32) }, valueText)
        chevron(box, "updown", T.sub, 12).LayoutOrder = 2
        r.setRight(box)

        -- options open under the row (inside the card)
        local panel = mk("Frame", { Name = "Options", BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, LayoutOrder = 2, Visible = false }, r.body)
        padding(panel, 8, 0, 2, 0)
        vlist(panel, 6)
        local searchBox
        if Settings.Search then
            searchBox = mk("TextBox", {
                Name = "Search", Text = "", PlaceholderText = "Search...", PlaceholderColor3 = T.placeholder, ClearTextOnFocus = false,
                BackgroundColor3 = T.search, TextColor3 = T.text, FontFace = FONT.regular, TextSize = 13,
                TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 30), LayoutOrder = 1,
            }, panel)
            corner(searchBox, 7)
            stroke(searchBox, T.searchStroke)
            padding(searchBox, 0, 10, 0, 10)
        end
        local list = mk("ScrollingFrame", {
            Name = "List", BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0), CanvasSize = UDim2.new(), AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
            ScrollBarThickness = 12, ScrollBarImageColor3 = T.scroll, ScrollingDirection = Enum.ScrollingDirection.Y, LayoutOrder = 2,
        }, panel)
        vlist(list, 2)

        local DF = rowApi(r, { Settings = Settings, Class = "Dropdown", IgnoreConfig = false })
        local options, objs, Selected = {}, {}, {}
        local function resize()
            local shown = 0
            for _, name in ipairs(options) do if objs[name] and objs[name].button.Visible then shown = shown + 1 end end
            list.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, math.min(shown * 32, 224))
        end
        local function paintOptions()
            for name, o in pairs(objs) do
                local on = table.find(Selected, name) ~= nil
                o.button.BackgroundTransparency = on and 0 or 1
                o.label.TextColor3 = on and T.white or T.text
                o.label.FontFace = on and FONT.medium or FONT.regular
            end
        end
        local function refreshText()
            if #Selected > 0 then
                valueText.Text = table.concat(Selected, ", ")
            else
                valueText.Text = multi and "None" or "..."
            end
            paintOptions()
        end
        local function fire()
            if not Settings.Callback then return end
            if multi then
                local out = {}
                for _, n in ipairs(Selected) do out[n] = true end
                Settings.Callback(out)
            else
                Settings.Callback(Selected[1])
            end
        end
        local function setOpen(open)
            if open and W.openDropdown and W.openDropdown ~= DF then pcall(W.openDropdown.Close, W.openDropdown) end
            panel.Visible = open
            boxStroke.Color = open and T.accent or T.inputStroke
            W.openDropdown = open and DF or (W.openDropdown ~= DF and W.openDropdown or nil)
            if open and searchBox then searchBox.Text = "" end
            resize()
        end
        function DF:Close() setOpen(false) end
        local function addOption(name)
            if objs[name] then return end
            local button = mk("TextButton", {
                Name = "Option", Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false, BackgroundColor3 = T.navActive, BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                Size = UDim2.new(1, -6, 0, 30), LayoutOrder = #options + 1,
            }, list)
            corner(button, 6)
            local label = text({ Text = tostring(name), TextSize = 13, Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 1, 0), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 0), TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd }, button)
            button.MouseEnter:Connect(function() if not table.find(Selected, name) then button.BackgroundTransparency = 0.6 end end)
            button.MouseLeave:Connect(function() if not table.find(Selected, name) then button.BackgroundTransparency = 1 end end)
            button.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                if multi then
                    local i = table.find(Selected, name)
                    if i then table.remove(Selected, i) else table.insert(Selected, name) end
                    DF.Value = Selected
                    refreshText()
                    fire()
                    if Flag and not DF.IgnoreConfig and GOLFGUI.TriggerAutoSave then GOLFGUI:TriggerAutoSave() end
                else
                    Selected = { name }
                    DF.Value = name
                    refreshText()
                    setOpen(false)
                    fire()
                    if Flag and not DF.IgnoreConfig and GOLFGUI.TriggerAutoSave then GOLFGUI:TriggerAutoSave() end
                end
            end)
            options[#options + 1] = name
            objs[name] = { button = button, label = label }
        end
        box.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() setOpen(not panel.Visible) end)
        if searchBox then
            searchBox:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
                local q = string.lower(searchBox.Text)
                for name, o in pairs(objs) do o.button.Visible = q == "" or string.find(string.lower(tostring(name)), q, 1, true) ~= nil end
                resize()
            end)
        end

        -- MacLib semantics: number = index, string = name, table = list of names · fires the callback
        function DF:UpdateSelection(sel)
            if sel == nil then return end
            local picked = {}
            if type(sel) == "number" then
                if options[sel] then picked = { options[sel] } end
            elseif type(sel) == "string" then
                if objs[sel] then picked = { sel } end
            elseif type(sel) == "table" then
                for _, n in ipairs(options) do if table.find(sel, n) then picked[#picked + 1] = n end end
            end
            Selected = picked
            DF.Value = multi and Selected or Selected[1]
            refreshText()
            fire()
            if Flag and not DF.IgnoreConfig and GOLFGUI.TriggerAutoSave then GOLFGUI:TriggerAutoSave() end
        end
        function DF:Set(sel) DF:UpdateSelection(sel) end
        function DF:InsertOptions(newOptions)
            for _, n in ipairs(newOptions or {}) do addOption(n) end
            Settings.Options = options
            resize()
        end
        function DF:ClearOptions()
            for _, o in pairs(objs) do o.button:Destroy() end
            options, objs, Selected = {}, {}, {}
            DF.Value = multi and Selected or nil
            refreshText()
            resize()
        end
        function DF:UpdateName(v) Settings.Name = v nameLabel.Text = v end

        DF:InsertOptions(Settings.Options or {})
        local default = Settings.Default
        if multi then
            if type(default) == "table" then
                for _, n in ipairs(options) do if table.find(default, n) then Selected[#Selected + 1] = n end end
            end
            DF.Value = Selected
        else
            if type(default) == "number" and options[default] then Selected = { options[default] }
            elseif type(default) == "string" and objs[default] then Selected = { default } end
            DF.Value = Selected[1]
        end
        refreshText()
        if Flag then GOLFGUI.Options[Flag] = DF end
        return DF
    end

    -- SF:EggPicker removed in TASK-028 (unused PlamorHub egg picker copy; Hunting Together RPG has no pet eggs)

    -- picture grid drawn right inside the card (no pop-up): Items = { {Name, Label?, Image | Model, Rarity = its tab, Sub} } ·
    -- Rarities = tab names ("All" first unless HideAll; no bar for one tab) · Single = one choice · Default = { names } ·
    -- Callback(list of names) · Rows = rows shown before the grid scrolls · KeepMissing = names not in Items stay selected ·
    -- :SetItems(items, rarities) · :SetInfoAll{ [name] = {text, tone} } · :UpdateSelection(names, silent) · :GetSelection()
    function SF:ImageGrid(Settings, Flag)
        local r = newRow(card, { padTop = 8, padBottom = 10 })
        local hasLabel = (Settings.Name or "") ~= "" or (Settings.Description or "") ~= ""
        local nameLabel
        if hasLabel then
            nameLabel = r.addLabel(Settings.Name, Settings.Description)
            index(Settings.Name) index(Settings.Description)
        else
            r.main.Visible = false
        end
        local countLabel
        if not Settings.Single and hasLabel then
            countLabel = text({ Text = "", TextSize = 12, TextColor3 = T.sub, AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.X, Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 30), TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right, ZIndex = 2 })
            r.setRight(countLabel)
        end
        local CW, CH, GAP = Settings.TileWidth or 96, Settings.TileHeight or 116, 8
        local tabBar = mk("ScrollingFrame", {
            BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 38), CanvasSize = UDim2.new(), AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.X,
            ScrollingDirection = Enum.ScrollingDirection.X, ScrollBarThickness = 8, ScrollBarImageColor3 = T.scroll, LayoutOrder = 2, ZIndex = 2,
        }, r.body)
        padding(tabBar, hasLabel and 6 or 0, 0, 4, 0)
        hlist(tabBar, 6)
        local grid = mk("ScrollingFrame", {
            BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, CH + 8), CanvasSize = UDim2.new(), AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
            ScrollingDirection = Enum.ScrollingDirection.Y, ScrollBarThickness = 12, ScrollBarImageColor3 = T.scroll, LayoutOrder = 3, ZIndex = 2,
        }, r.body)
        padding(grid, 4, 8, 4, 0)
        local layout = mk("UIGridLayout", { CellSize = UDim2.fromOffset(CW, CH), CellPadding = UDim2.fromOffset(GAP, GAP), SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder }, grid)
        local emptyLabel = text({ Text = Settings.EmptyText or "", TextSize = 12.5, TextColor3 = T.sub, TextWrapped = true, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 30), LayoutOrder = 4, Visible = false, ZIndex = 2 }, r.body)
        local IG = rowApi(r, { Settings = Settings, Class = "ImageGrid", IgnoreConfig = false })
        local items, cards, tabButtons, pending = {}, {}, {}, {}
        local selected = {}
        for _, n in ipairs(Settings.Default or {}) do selected[n] = true end
        local currentTab
        local function list()
            local out, seen = {}, {}
            for _, it in ipairs(items) do
                if selected[it.Name] then out[#out + 1] = it.Name seen[it.Name] = true end
            end
            if Settings.KeepMissing ~= false then
                for name in pairs(selected) do if not seen[name] then out[#out + 1] = name end end
            end
            return out
        end
        local function paint()
            local names = list()
            for name, c in pairs(cards) do
                local on = selected[name] == true
                c.frame.BackgroundColor3 = on and T.navActive or T.input
                c.stroke.Color = on and T.accent or (c.toneColor or T.inputStroke)
                c.check.Visible = on
            end
            if countLabel then countLabel.Text = #names > 0 and (Settings.CountText or "%d selected"):format(#names) or "" end
            IG.Value = names
        end
        local function fitHeight()
            local n = 0
            for _, c in pairs(cards) do if c.frame.Visible then n += 1 end end
            local cols = math.max(1, layout.AbsoluteCellCount.X)
            local rows = math.max(1, math.ceil(n / cols))
            local shown = math.min(rows, Settings.Rows or 2)
            local extra = rows > shown and 0.45 or 0 -- a cut-off row shows that the grid scrolls
            grid.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, math.floor((shown + extra) * (CH + GAP) + 8))
            grid.Visible = #items > 0
            emptyLabel.Visible = #items == 0 and (Settings.EmptyText or "") ~= ""
        end
        layout:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteCellCount"):Connect(fitHeight)
        local function showTab(tab)
            currentTab = tab
            for name, b in pairs(tabButtons) do
                local on = name == tab
                b.BackgroundColor3 = on and T.primary or T.input
                b.TextColor3 = on and T.white or T.nav
                b.FontFace = on and FONT.medium or FONT.regular
            end
            for _, c in pairs(cards) do c.frame.Visible = tab == "All" or c.rarity == tab end
            for name, build in pairs(pending) do
                local c = cards[name]
                if c and c.frame.Visible then
                    pcall(build)
                    pending[name] = nil
                end
            end
            grid.CanvasPosition = Vector2.zero
            fitHeight()
        end
        local function fire()
            if Settings.Callback then task.spawn(Settings.Callback, list()) end
        end
        local function buildTabs(rarities)
            for _, b in pairs(tabButtons) do b:Destroy() end
            tabButtons = {}
            local tabs = Settings.HideAll and {} or { "All" }
            for _, rr in ipairs(rarities or {}) do tabs[#tabs + 1] = rr end
            for i, tab in ipairs(tabs) do
                local b = mk("TextButton", {
                    Text = tab == "All" and (Settings.AllText or "All") or tab, AutoButtonColor = false, FontFace = FONT.regular, TextSize = 12.5,
                    TextColor3 = T.nav, BackgroundColor3 = T.input, AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.X, Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 28), LayoutOrder = i, ZIndex = 3,
                }, tabBar)
                corner(b, 7)
                padding(b, 0, 12, 0, 12)
                b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() showTab(tab) end)
                tabButtons[tab] = b
            end
            tabBar.Visible = #tabs > 1
            local keep = currentTab and tabButtons[currentTab] and currentTab
            return keep or Settings.StartTab and tabButtons[Settings.StartTab] and Settings.StartTab or tabs[1] or "All"
        end
        local function makeTile(i, it)
            local f = mk("TextButton", { Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false, BackgroundColor3 = T.input, LayoutOrder = i, ZIndex = 3 }, grid)
            corner(f, 9)
            local st = stroke(f, T.inputStroke)
            local img
            local picH = CH - 46
            if it.Model then
                local vp = mk("ViewportFrame", {
                    BackgroundTransparency = 1, Ambient = rgb(170, 170, 175), LightColor = T.white, LightDirection = Vector3.new(-1, -1, -1),
                    AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0), Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, 4), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(CW - 12, picH + 2), ZIndex = 4,
                }, f)
                pending[it.Name] = function()
                    local source = type(it.Model) == "function" and it.Model() or it.Model
                    local m = source and source:Clone()
                    if not m then return end
                    for _, d in ipairs(m:GetDescendants()) do
                        if d:IsA("LuaSourceContainer") or d:IsA("Sound") or d:IsA("ParticleEmitter") then d:Destroy() end
                    end
                    m.Parent = vp
                    local cf, size = m:GetBoundingBox()
                    local root = m:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    local look = root and root.CFrame.LookVector or cf.LookVector
                    local cam = mk("Camera", { FieldOfView = 40 }, vp)
                    local dist = math.max(size.Y, size.X * 0.7, 4) * 1.5
                    cam.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(cf.Position + look * dist + Vector3.new(0, size.Y * 0.1, 0), cf.Position)
                    vp.CurrentCamera = cam
                end
            else
                img = mk("ImageLabel", {
                    BackgroundTransparency = 1, Image = it.Image or "", ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Fit,
                    AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0), Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, 8), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(picH - 6, picH - 6), ZIndex = 4,
                }, f)
            end
            local nameText = text({ Text = it.Label or it.Name, FontFace = FONT.medium, TextSize = 12.5, TextScaled = true, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center, Position = UDim2.new(0, 5, 0, CH - 40), Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 18), ZIndex = 4 }, f)
            mk("UITextSizeConstraint", { MaxTextSize = fontPx(12.5), MinTextSize = 7 }, nameText)
            local sub = text({ Text = it.Sub or "", TextSize = 11, TextColor3 = T.sub, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center, TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd, Position = UDim2.new(0, 5, 0, CH - 21), Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 15), ZIndex = 4 }, f)
            local dot = mk("Frame", { BackgroundColor3 = TONES.off, Position = UDim2.fromOffset(7, 7), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(8, 8), Visible = false, ZIndex = 5 }, f)
            corner(dot, 4)
            local check = mk("Frame", { BackgroundColor3 = T.accent, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0), Position = UDim2.new(1, -6, 0, 6), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(18, 18), Visible = false, ZIndex = 5 }, f)
            corner(check, 9)
            if ICON.check then
                mk("ImageLabel", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, Image = ICON.check, ImageColor3 = T.white, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(11, 11), ZIndex = 6 }, check)
            else
                text({ Text = "✓", FontFace = FONT.semibold, TextSize = 11, TextColor3 = T.white, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), ZIndex = 6 }, check)
            end
            f.MouseEnter:Connect(function() if not selected[it.Name] then f.BackgroundColor3 = T.navHover end end)
            f.MouseLeave:Connect(function() if not selected[it.Name] then f.BackgroundColor3 = T.input end end)
            f.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                if Settings.Single then
                    selected = { [it.Name] = true }
                else
                    selected[it.Name] = not selected[it.Name] or nil
                end
                paint()
                fire()
            end)
            cards[it.Name] = { frame = f, stroke = st, check = check, rarity = it.Rarity, sub = sub, base = it.Sub or "", dot = dot, img = img }
        end
        local function buildTiles(newItems)
            for _, c in pairs(cards) do c.frame:Destroy() end
            cards, pending = {}, {}
            items = newItems or {}
            for i, it in ipairs(items) do makeTile(i, it) end
        end
        function IG:SetItems(newItems, rarities)
            buildTiles(newItems)
            local tab = buildTabs(rarities or Settings.Rarities)
            paint()
            showTab(tab)
        end
        function IG:GetSelection() return list() end
        function IG:UpdateSelection(names, silent)
            if type(names) == "string" then names = { names } end
            selected = {}
            for _, n in ipairs(type(names) == "table" and names or {}) do selected[n] = true end
            paint()
            if not silent then fire() end
        end
        function IG:UpdateName(v) Settings.Name = v if nameLabel then nameLabel.Text = v end end
        function IG:SetInfoAll(map)
            for name, c in pairs(cards) do
                local info = map and map[name]
                c.sub.Text = info and info[1] or c.base
                c.toneColor = info and info[2] and TONES[info[2]] or nil
                c.dot.Visible = c.toneColor ~= nil
                if c.toneColor then c.dot.BackgroundColor3 = c.toneColor end
            end
            paint()
        end
        IG:SetItems(Settings.Items or {}, Settings.Rarities)
        if Flag then GOLFGUI.Options[Flag] = IG end
        return IG
    end

    function SF:Status(Settings, Flag)
        local r = newRow(card)
        r.addLabel(Settings.Name or "Status")
        local holder = mk("Frame", { Name = "Status", BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 20), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.X })
        hlist(holder, 8)
        local dot = mk("Frame", { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(8, 8), BackgroundColor3 = TONES.off, LayoutOrder = 1 }, holder)
        corner(dot, 4)
        local value = text({ Name = "StatusValue", Text = "", TextSize = 13, AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.X, Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 20), LayoutOrder = 2, TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd }, holder)
        local limit = mk("UISizeConstraint", { MaxSize = Vector2.new(400, 20) }, value)
        r.setRight(holder)
        -- the value may use most of the row, the "Status" word keeps ~90 px
        r.main:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteSize"):Connect(function()
            limit.MaxSize = Vector2.new(math.max(80, GOLFGUI.Unscaled(r.main, r.main.AbsoluteSize.X) - 120), 20)
        end)
        local ST = rowApi(r, { Settings = Settings, Class = "Status" })
        local tone = Settings.Tone or "off"
        function ST:Set(t, newTone)
            local s = tostring(t or "")
            local nextTone = newTone or tone
            if value.Text == s and nextTone == tone and newTone ~= nil then return end
            if value.Text ~= s then value.Text = s end
            if newTone then
                tone = newTone
                dot.BackgroundColor3 = TONES[tone] or TONES.off
            end
        end
        function ST:UpdateName(t) ST:Set(t) end
        ST:Set(Settings.Text or "Off", tone)
        if Flag then GOLFGUI.Options[Flag] = ST end
        return ST
    end

    -- name on the left, monospace value on the right (Now / Next / Queue / counters ...)
    function SF:Value(Settings, Flag)
        local r = newRow(card)
        local nameLabel = r.addLabel(Settings.Name)
        index(Settings.Name)
        local value = text({
            Name = "Value", Text = Settings.Text or "-", FontFace = FONT.mono, TextSize = 12.5, TextColor3 = T.value,
            TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right, TextWrapped = true, AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.XY, Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 18),
        })
        local limit = mk("UISizeConstraint", { MaxSize = Vector2.new(380, 200) }, value)
        r.setRight(value)
        r.main:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteSize"):Connect(function()
            limit.MaxSize = Vector2.new(math.max(120, GOLFGUI.Unscaled(r.main, r.main.AbsoluteSize.X) - 150), 200)
        end)
        local V = rowApi(r, { Settings = Settings, Class = "Value" })
        function V:UpdateName(t)
            local s = tostring(t or "")
            if value.Text ~= s then value.Text = s end
        end
        if Flag then GOLFGUI.Options[Flag] = V end
        return V
    end

    function SF:Label(Settings, Flag)
        local r = newRow(card, { minHeight = 22 })
        local label = text({
            Name = "LabelText", Text = Settings.Text or Settings.Name or "", TextSize = 13, TextColor3 = T.value, TextWrapped = true,
            AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0), LayoutOrder = 1,
        }, r.main)
        local L = rowApi(r, { Settings = Settings, Class = "Label" })
        function L:UpdateName(t)
            local s = tostring(t or "")
            if label.Text ~= s then label.Text = s end
        end
        if Flag then GOLFGUI.Options[Flag] = L end
        return L
    end

    function SF:Paragraph(Settings, Flag)
        local r = newRow(card, { padTop = 10, padBottom = 14, minHeight = 0 })
        local col = mk("Frame", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, LayoutOrder = 1 }, r.main)
        vlist(col, 5)
        local header = text({ Text = Settings.Header or "", FontFace = FONT.semibold, TextSize = 13, AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0), TextWrapped = true, LayoutOrder = 1 }, col)
        local body = text({
            Text = Settings.Body or "", FontFace = FONT.mono, TextSize = 12, TextColor3 = T.log, TextWrapped = true,
            TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top, AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0), LayoutOrder = 2,
            LineHeight = (1.3 * 12 + 5) / (1.3 * 12), -- the mockup's log: one 12px line each, 5px apart
        }, col)
        local P = rowApi(r, { Settings = Settings, Class = "Paragraph" })
        header.Visible = (Settings.Header or "") ~= ""
        if Flag then GOLFGUI.Options[Flag] = P end
        return P
    end

    function SF:Warning(Settings)
        local r = newRow(card, { padTop = 10, padBottom = 12, minHeight = 0 })
        local box = mk("Frame", { BackgroundColor3 = T.warnBg, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, LayoutOrder = 1 }, r.main)
        corner(box, 8)
        padding(box, 10, 12, 10, 12)
        hlist(box, 10, Enum.VerticalAlignment.Top)
        if ICON.warning then
            mk("ImageLabel", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, Image = ICON.warning, ImageColor3 = T.warnText, Size = UDim2.fromOffset(16, 16), LayoutOrder = 1 }, box)
        else
            local icon = text({ Text = "!", FontFace = FONT.semibold, TextSize = 12, TextColor3 = T.warnBg, BackgroundTransparency = 0, BackgroundColor3 = T.warnText, Size = UDim2.fromOffset(16, 16), TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center, LayoutOrder = 1 }, box)
            corner(icon, 8)
        end
        local label = text({ Text = Settings.Text or "", TextSize = 12.5, LineHeight = 1.45 / 1.3, TextColor3 = T.warnText, TextWrapped = true, AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, Size = UDim2.new(1, -26, 0, 0), LayoutOrder = 2 }, box)
        local Wn = rowApi(r, { Settings = Settings, Class = "Warning" })
        function Wn:UpdateName(t) label.Text = t end
        return Wn
    end

    -- boxed buttons in one row: { {Name, Callback, Primary}, ... } → one object per button
    function SF:Buttons(list)
        local r = newRow(card, { padTop = 10, padBottom = 12, minHeight = 34 })
        local holder = mk("Frame", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 34), LayoutOrder = 1 }, r.main)
        hlist(holder, 8)
        local out = {}
        local function rowVisible()
            local any = false
            for _, b in ipairs(out) do if b.Button.Visible then any = true end end
            r.setVisible(any)
        end
        for i, s in ipairs(list) do
            local b = mk("TextButton", {
                Name = "Button", Text = s.Name or "", AutoButtonColor = false, FontFace = s.Primary and FONT.medium or FONT.regular, TextSize = 13,
                TextColor3 = s.Primary and T.white or T.text, BackgroundColor3 = s.Primary and T.primary or T.input,
                AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.X, Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 34), LayoutOrder = i,
            }, holder)
            corner(b, 7)
            stroke(b, s.Primary and T.accent or T.inputStroke)
            padding(b, 0, 14, 0, 14)
            index(s.Name)
            local base = b.BackgroundColor3
            b.MouseEnter:Connect(function() b.BackgroundColor3 = base:Lerp(T.white, 0.06) end)
            b.MouseLeave:Connect(function() b.BackgroundColor3 = base end)
            b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() if s.Callback then s.Callback() end end)
            local B = { Settings = s, Button = b, Row = r }
            function B:UpdateName(t) b.Text = t end
            function B:SetVisibility(on) b.Visible = on ~= false rowVisible() end
            function B:SetNested(on) r.setNested(on) end
            out[i] = B
        end
        return out
    end
    function SF:Button(Settings, Flag)
        local b = SF:Buttons({ Settings })[1]
        if Flag then GOLFGUI.Options[Flag] = b end
        return b
    end

    -- "Show advanced settings (n)": a row with a chevron that turns down when open
    function SF:Disclosure(Settings)
        local r = newRow(card, { padTop = 0, padBottom = 0, minHeight = 40 })
        local b = mk("TextButton", { Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 40), LayoutOrder = 1 }, r.main)
        hlist(b, 8)
        local chev = chevron(b, "right", T.sub, 12)
        chev.LayoutOrder = 1
        local label = text({ Text = Settings.Name or "", TextSize = 12.5, TextColor3 = T.sub, AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.X, Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 40), LayoutOrder = 2 }, b)
        b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() if Settings.Callback then Settings.Callback() end end)
        local D = rowApi(r, { Settings = Settings, Class = "Disclosure" })
        function D:UpdateName(t) label.Text = t end
        return D
    end

    function SF:Divider()
        local r = newRow(card, { padTop = 0, padBottom = 0, minHeight = 0 })
        return rowApi(r, { Class = "Divider", Remove = function() r.frame:Destroy() end })
    end
    function SF:Spacer()
        local r = newRow(card, { padTop = 4, padBottom = 4, minHeight = 0 })
        r.line.Visible = false
        return rowApi(r, { Class = "Spacer", Remove = function() r.frame:Destroy() end })
    end

    -- text box on the right (numbers / names)
    function SF:Input(Settings, Flag)
        local r = newRow(card)
