        local nameLabel = r.addLabel(Settings.Name, Settings.Description)
        local box = mk("TextBox", {
            Text = Settings.Default or "", PlaceholderText = Settings.Placeholder or "", PlaceholderColor3 = T.placeholder, ClearTextOnFocus = false,
            BackgroundColor3 = T.input, TextColor3 = T.text, FontFace = FONT.mono, TextSize = 12.5, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right,
            Size = UDim2.fromOffset(Settings.Width or 110, 32),
        })
        corner(box, 7)
        stroke(box, T.inputStroke)
        padding(box, 0, 10, 0, 10)
        r.setRight(box)
        local I = rowApi(r, { Settings = Settings, Class = "Input", IgnoreConfig = Settings.IgnoreConfig == true, Text = box.Text })
        function I:SetText(t) box.Text, I.Text = t, t end -- show only: no callback, no save
        box.FocusLost:Connect(function()
            local t = box.Text
            if Settings.AcceptedCharacters == "Numeric" then t = t:gsub("[^%d%.%-]", "") box.Text = t end
            I.Text = t
            if Settings.Callback then Settings.Callback(t) end
            if Flag and not I.IgnoreConfig and GOLFGUI.TriggerAutoSave then GOLFGUI:TriggerAutoSave() end
        end)
        function I:UpdateText(t)
            box.Text = t
            I.Text = t
            if Settings.Callback then Settings.Callback(t) end
            if Flag and not I.IgnoreConfig and GOLFGUI.TriggerAutoSave then GOLFGUI:TriggerAutoSave() end
        end
        function I:UpdateName(t) nameLabel.Text = t end
        if Flag then GOLFGUI.Options[Flag] = I end
        return I
    end

    return SF
end

-- ====================================================================
-- window
-- ====================================================================
function GOLFGUI:Window(Settings)
    local W = { Settings = Settings }
    local gui = mk("ScreenGui", {
        Name = Settings.GuiName or "GOLFGUI", ResetOnSpawn = false, ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling,
        DisplayOrder = 2147483647, IgnoreGuiInset = true,
    })
    pcall(function() gui.ScreenInsets = Enum.ScreenInsets.None end)
    gui.Parent = guiHost()
    W.Gui = gui

    local HH = Settings.HeaderHeight or 64
    local SW = Settings.SidebarWidth or 216
    local base = mk("Frame", {
        Name = "Base", AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5),
        Size = Settings.Size or UDim2.fromOffset(868, 700), BackgroundColor3 = T.content, Active = true,
    }, gui)
    corner(base, 12)
    stroke(base, T.windowStroke)
    padding(base, 1, 1, 1, 1)
    local baseScale = mk("UIScale", {}, base)

    -- ---------- header: globe · title / subtitle · window dots ----------
    local header = mk("Frame", { Name = "Header", BackgroundColor3 = T.header, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, HH), Active = true, ZIndex = 2 }, base)
    corner(header, 11) -- inside the window's 1px border (12 - 1)
    mk("Frame", { Name = "Square", BackgroundColor3 = T.header, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 1), Position = UDim2.fromScale(0, 1), Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 12), ZIndex = 2 }, header)
    mk("Frame", { Name = "Divider", BackgroundColor3 = T.headerLine, Position = UDim2.fromScale(0, 1), Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 1), ZIndex = 3 }, header)
    -- Settings.Logo = PNG (base64) แทนไอคอนโลกมุมซ้ายบน (กดแล้วเปิดเมนูตั้งค่าเหมือนเดิม) · ไม่มี getcustomasset = ใช้โลกเดิม
    local logo
    if Settings.Logo and getcustomasset and writefile then
        local ok, err = pcall(function()
            local path = "GOLFGUI/icons/logo.png"
            writefile(path, b64decode(Settings.Logo))
            logo = getcustomasset(path)
        end)
        if not ok then warn("[GOLFGUI] logo: " .. tostring(err)) end
    end
    local globe = mk("ImageButton", {
        Name = "GlobalSettingsButton", Image = logo or ICON.globe or ASSET.globe, ImageColor3 = logo and Color3.new(1, 1, 1) or T.icon, BackgroundTransparency = 1, AutoButtonColor = false,
        AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(0, logo and 17 or 20, 0.5, 0),
        Size = logo and UDim2.fromOffset(32, 32) or ICON.globe and UDim2.fromOffset(22, 22) or UDim2.fromOffset(20, 20), ZIndex = 3,
    }, header)
    local titleFrame = mk("Frame", { Name = "TitleFrame", BackgroundTransparency = 1, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5), Size = UDim2.new(0.6, 0, 1, 0), ZIndex = 3 }, header)
    mk("UIListLayout", { SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder, Padding = UDim.new(0, 2), HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Center, VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Center }, titleFrame)
    local title = text({ Name = "Title", Text = Settings.Title or "", FontFace = FONT.semibold, TextSize = 17, TextColor3 = T.bright, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 22), LayoutOrder = 1, ZIndex = 3 }, titleFrame)
    local subtitle = text({ Name = "Subtitle", Text = Settings.Subtitle or "", TextSize = 12.5, TextColor3 = T.muted, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 16), LayoutOrder = 2, ZIndex = 3 }, titleFrame)
    -- window dots in Windows order (user 2026-09-28): yellow = minimize, red = close at the far right (the hollow third
    -- dot of the mockup did nothing → removed) · hovering the pair shows what each does (– / ×) inside the dots
    local dots = mk("Frame", { Name = "Controls", BackgroundTransparency = 1, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0.5, 0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(27, 12), ZIndex = 3 }, header)
    hlist(dots, 7, Enum.VerticalAlignment.Center, Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Right)
    local function dot(name, color, order, icon, glyphColor)
        local d = mk("TextButton", { Name = name, Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false, BackgroundColor3 = color, Size = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 10), LayoutOrder = order, ZIndex = 3 }, dots)
        corner(d, 5)
        local glyph = ICON[icon] and mk("ImageLabel", {
            BackgroundTransparency = 1, Image = ICON[icon], ImageColor3 = glyphColor, ImageTransparency = 1,
            AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(7, 7), ZIndex = 4,
        }, d)
        return d, glyph
    end
    local minDot, minGlyph = dot("Minimize", rgb(254, 188, 46), 1, "winMinimize", rgb(110, 70, 0))
    local exitDot, exitGlyph = dot("Exit", rgb(255, 95, 87), 2, "winClose", rgb(110, 16, 10))
    local function showGlyphs(on)
        for _, g in ipairs({ minGlyph, exitGlyph }) do if g then g.ImageTransparency = on and 0 or 1 end end
    end
    for _, d in ipairs({ minDot, exitDot }) do
        d.MouseEnter:Connect(function() showGlyphs(true) end)
        d.MouseLeave:Connect(function() showGlyphs(false) end)
    end

    -- ---------- minimized: a small floating status bar (user 2026-09-28: "ช่วยคิดตอนกดย่อใหม่") ----------
    -- replaces the old thin edge bar: shows the status line (dot + title / text), so a hidden window still says what the
    -- script is doing · click = open the window · its spot is locked
    local menuKey = Settings.Keybind or Enum.KeyCode.RightControl
    local MINI_DEFAULT = Settings.MiniPosition or UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 405) -- top right, under the game's open menu (the user's spot)
    local MINI_LOCKED = (Settings.MiniLockPos ~= false) -- locked: never moves (MiniLockPos = false makes it draggable)
    local mini = mk("TextButton", {
        Name = "Mini", Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false, BackgroundColor3 = T.header, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0),
        Position = MINI_DEFAULT, Size = UDim2.fromOffset(300, 46), Visible = false, ZIndex = 60,
    }, gui)
    corner(mini, 10)
    stroke(mini, T.windowStroke)
    local miniDot = mk("Frame", { BackgroundColor3 = TONES.off, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0.5, 0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(8, 8), ZIndex = 61 }, mini)
    corner(miniDot, 4)
    local miniTitle = text({ Text = Settings.Title or "", FontFace = FONT.medium, TextSize = 13, Position = UDim2.fromOffset(32, 6), Size = UDim2.new(1, -70, 0, 17), TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd, ZIndex = 61 }, mini)
    local miniSub = text({ Text = "", TextSize = 12, TextColor3 = T.sub, Position = UDim2.fromOffset(32, 23), Size = UDim2.new(1, -70, 0, 16), TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd, ZIndex = 61 }, mini)
    local miniOpen = mk("ImageLabel", {
        BackgroundTransparency = 1, Image = ICON.expand or "", ImageColor3 = T.icon, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5),
        Position = UDim2.new(1, -16, 0.5, 0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(14, 14), ZIndex = 61,
    }, mini)
    local miniStatus = { title = "", text = "", tone = "off" }
    local miniHover, pulse = false, nil
    local function keyName()
        return (tostring(menuKey.Name):gsub("Control", " Ctrl"):gsub("Shift", " Shift"):gsub("Alt", " Alt"):gsub("^%s+", ""))
    end
    local function paintMini()
        miniTitle.Text = miniStatus.title ~= "" and miniStatus.title or (Settings.Title or "")
        miniSub.Text = miniHover and ("Click to open  ·  " .. keyName()) or miniStatus.text
        miniDot.BackgroundColor3 = TONES[miniStatus.tone] or TONES.off
        -- running = the dot breathes, so a glance tells "alive"
        local run = miniStatus.tone == "run" and mini.Visible
        if run and not pulse then
            pulse = TweenService:Create(miniDot, TweenInfo.new(0.9, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut, -1, true), { BackgroundTransparency = 0.65 })
            pulse:Play()
        elseif not run and pulse then
            pulse:Cancel()
            pulse = nil
            miniDot.BackgroundTransparency = 0
        end
    end
    local function setMiniStatus(t, t2, right, tone)
        t2, right = tostring(t2 or ""), tostring(right or "")
        miniStatus.title = tostring(t or "")
        miniStatus.text = t2 .. ((t2 ~= "" and right ~= "") and "  ·  " or "") .. right
        miniStatus.tone = tone or "off"
        paintMini()
    end
    mini.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
        miniHover = true
        mini.BackgroundColor3, miniOpen.ImageColor3 = T.navHover, T.bright
        paintMini()
    end)
    mini.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
        miniHover = false
        mini.BackgroundColor3, miniOpen.ImageColor3 = T.header, T.icon
        paintMini()
    end)
    local miniDrag, miniStart, miniMoved
    if MINI_LOCKED then
        -- Locked under the game's open menu (below Top Player), as the user asked: no drag, and it does not follow the
        -- menu or the Sleep screen. The spot depends on the window size only, so it is never off screen:
        -- 1920x1129 -> y 405 · 800x600 -> y 258 · very short windows (484x273) -> y 8 and a narrower bar.
        local function placeMini()
            local area, width = gui.AbsoluteSize, 300
            if not Settings.MiniPosition and area.X >= 200 and area.Y >= 100 then
                width = math.clamp(math.floor(area.X * 0.38), 180, 300)
                local y = area.Y < 360 and 8 or math.min(405, math.floor(area.Y * 0.43))
                MINI_DEFAULT = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, y) -- set first: the guard below snaps back to this
            end
            mini.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(width, 46)
            mini.Position = MINI_DEFAULT
        end
        mini.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0)
        placeMini()
        track(gui:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteSize"):Connect(placeMini))
        track(mini:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Position"):Connect(function()
            if mini.Position ~= MINI_DEFAULT then
                mini.Position = MINI_DEFAULT
            end
        end))
        track(mini:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AnchorPoint"):Connect(function()
            if mini.AnchorPoint ~= Vector2.new(1, 0) then
                mini.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0)
            end
        end))
        mini.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            W:SetState(true)
        end)
    else
        mini.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0)
        -- the whole bar stays on screen (offsets are from the top right corner)
        local function miniSpot(x, y)
            local area = gui.AbsoluteSize
            local w = mini.Size.X.Offset
            if area.X > w and area.Y > 46 then
                x, y = math.clamp(x, w - area.X, 0), math.clamp(y, 0, area.Y - 46)
            end
            return UDim2.new(1, math.round(x), 0, math.round(y))
        end
        mini.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
            if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                miniDrag, miniStart, miniMoved = input.Position, mini.Position, false
            end
        end)
        track(UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
            if miniDrag and (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
                local d = input.Position - miniDrag
                if miniMoved or d.Magnitude > 4 then
                    miniMoved = true
                    mini.Position = miniSpot(miniStart.X.Offset + d.X, miniStart.Y.Offset + d.Y)
                end
            end
        end))
    end

    -- ---------- body ----------
    local body = mk("Frame", { Name = "Body", BackgroundTransparency = 1, Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, HH + 1), Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, -(HH + 1)) }, base)
    local sidebar = mk("Frame", { Name = "Sidebar", BackgroundColor3 = T.sidebar, Size = UDim2.new(0, SW + 1, 1, 0) }, body)
    corner(sidebar, 11)
    mk("Frame", { Name = "SquareTop", BackgroundColor3 = T.sidebar, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 12) }, sidebar)
    mk("Frame", { Name = "SquareRight", BackgroundColor3 = T.sidebar, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0), Position = UDim2.fromScale(1, 0), Size = UDim2.new(0, 12, 1, 0) }, sidebar)
    mk("Frame", { Name = "Divider", BackgroundColor3 = T.sidebarLine, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0), Position = UDim2.fromScale(1, 0), Size = UDim2.new(0, 1, 1, 0), ZIndex = 2 }, sidebar)

    -- search (filters the pages by their names, card titles and settings)
    local searchHolder = mk("Frame", { Name = "SearchHolder", BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, -1, 0, 52), ZIndex = 2 }, sidebar)
    padding(searchHolder, 12, 10, 4, 10)
    local searchFrame = mk("Frame", { Name = "Search", BackgroundColor3 = T.search, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), ZIndex = 2 }, searchHolder)
    corner(searchFrame, 8)
    stroke(searchFrame, T.searchStroke)
    mk("ImageLabel", { Image = ICON.search or ASSET.search, ImageColor3 = T.placeholder, BackgroundTransparency = 1, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0.5, 0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(14, 14), ZIndex = 2 }, searchFrame)
    local searchBox = mk("TextBox", {
        Name = "SearchBox", Text = "", PlaceholderText = "Search settings", PlaceholderColor3 = T.placeholder, ClearTextOnFocus = false,
        BackgroundTransparency = 1, TextColor3 = T.text, FontFace = FONT.regular, TextSize = 13, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
        Position = UDim2.fromOffset(32, 0), Size = UDim2.new(1, -40, 1, 0), ZIndex = 2,
    }, searchFrame)

    local PROFILE_H = 72
    local nav = mk("ScrollingFrame", {
        Name = "Tabs", BackgroundTransparency = 1, Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 52), Size = UDim2.new(1, -1, 1, -(52 + PROFILE_H)),
        CanvasSize = UDim2.new(), AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, ScrollBarThickness = 12, ScrollBarImageColor3 = T.scroll,
        ScrollingDirection = Enum.ScrollingDirection.Y, ZIndex = 2,
    }, sidebar)
    padding(nav, 4, 10, 4, 10)
    vlist(nav, 2)

    -- profile (bottom-left; Discord link later)
    local profile = mk("Frame", { Name = "UserInfo", BackgroundTransparency = 1, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 1), Position = UDim2.fromScale(0, 1), Size = UDim2.new(1, -1, 0, PROFILE_H), ZIndex = 2 }, sidebar)
    mk("Frame", { Name = "Divider", BackgroundColor3 = T.sidebarLine, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 1), ZIndex = 2 }, profile)
    local avatar = mk("ImageLabel", { Name = "Headshot", BackgroundColor3 = T.avatarBg, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(0, 16, 0.5, 0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(40, 40), ZIndex = 2 }, profile)
    corner(avatar, 20)
    stroke(avatar, T.avatarStroke, 1, true)
    task.spawn(function()
        local ok, img = pcall(Players.GetUserThumbnailAsync, Players, LocalPlayer.UserId, Enum.ThumbnailType.AvatarBust, Enum.ThumbnailSize.Size100x100)
        if ok and img and avatar.Parent then avatar.Image = img end
    end)
    local names = mk("Frame", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(0, 68, 0.5, 0), Size = UDim2.new(1, -80, 0, 36), ZIndex = 2 }, profile)
    vlist(names, 1)
    text({ Name = "DisplayName", Text = LocalPlayer.DisplayName, FontFace = FONT.semibold, TextSize = 14, TextColor3 = T.bright, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 18), TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd, LayoutOrder = 1, ZIndex = 2 }, names)
    text({ Name = "Username", Text = "@" .. LocalPlayer.Name, TextSize = 12.5, TextColor3 = T.muted, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 16), TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd, LayoutOrder = 2, ZIndex = 2 }, names)

    -- ---------- content: status line + pages ----------
    local content = mk("Frame", { Name = "Content", BackgroundTransparency = 1, Position = UDim2.fromOffset(SW + 1, 0), Size = UDim2.new(1, -(SW + 1), 1, 0) }, body)
    local pageTop = 0
    if Settings.StatusBar ~= false then
        local bar = mk("Frame", { Name = "StatusBar", BackgroundColor3 = T.bar, Position = UDim2.fromOffset(24, 14), Size = UDim2.new(1, -48, 0, 38) }, content)
        corner(bar, 8)
        stroke(bar, T.barStroke)
        local left = mk("Frame", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, Position = UDim2.fromOffset(14, 0), Size = UDim2.new(1, -28, 1, 0), ClipsDescendants = true }, bar)
        hlist(left, 10)
        local barDot = mk("Frame", { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(8, 8), BackgroundColor3 = TONES.off, LayoutOrder = 1 }, left)
        corner(barDot, 4)
        local barTitle = text({ Text = "", FontFace = FONT.medium, TextSize = 13, AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.X, Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 38), LayoutOrder = 2 }, left)
        local barSep = text({ Text = "|", TextSize = 13, TextColor3 = T.sep, AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.X, Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 38), LayoutOrder = 3, Visible = false }, left)
        local barText = text({ Text = "", TextSize = 13, Size = UDim2.fromOffset(100, 38), TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd, LayoutOrder = 4 }, left)
        local barRight = text({ Text = "", TextSize = 13, TextColor3 = T.sub, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(1, -14, 0.5, 0), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.X, Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 38), TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right, TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd }, bar)
        -- ข้อความขวา (Next) กว้างไม่เกิน 40% → "ตอนนี้" ไม่ถูกตัดเหลือ "P..." (เดิม Next ยาวแย่งที่หมด, 2026-09-28)
        local rightMax = mk("UISizeConstraint", { MaxSize = Vector2.new(300, 38) }, barRight)
        local function fitBar()
            local function width(o) return GOLFGUI.Unscaled(o, o.AbsoluteSize.X) end
            local used = 8 + 10 + width(barTitle) + 10 + width(barSep) + 10
            local avail = width(left) - used
            rightMax.MaxSize = Vector2.new(math.max(60, math.floor(avail * 0.4)), 38)
            local rightW = barRight.Text ~= "" and (width(barRight) + 16) or 0
            barText.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(math.max(20, avail - rightW), 38)
        end
        for _, o in ipairs({ barTitle, barSep, barRight, left }) do o:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteSize"):Connect(fitBar) end
        -- title (bold) | text ··· right · tone = dot color
        function W:SetStatus(t, t2, right, tone)
            barTitle.Text = tostring(t or "")
            barText.Text = tostring(t2 or "")
            barSep.Visible = (t2 or "") ~= ""
            barRight.Text = tostring(right or "")
            barDot.BackgroundColor3 = TONES[tone or "off"] or TONES.off
            fitBar()
            setMiniStatus(t, t2, right, tone)
        end
        pageTop = 52
    else
        function W:SetStatus(t, t2, right, tone) setMiniStatus(t, t2, right, tone) end
    end
    local pages = mk("Frame", { Name = "Pages", BackgroundTransparency = 1, Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, pageTop), Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, -pageTop) }, content)

    -- ---------- dragging (header) ----------
    local dragStart, startPos
    header.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            dragStart, startPos = input.Position, base.Position
        end
    end)
    track(UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
        if dragStart and (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
            local delta = input.Position - dragStart
            base.Position = UDim2.new(startPos.X.Scale, startPos.X.Offset + delta.X, startPos.Y.Scale, startPos.Y.Offset + delta.Y)
        end
    end))
    track(UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then dragStart = nil end
    end))

    -- ---------- tabs ----------
    local tabs, groups = {}, {}
    local groupOrder = 0
    local current
    local function paintTab(t, hover)
        local on = t == current
        t.button.BackgroundColor3 = on and T.navActive or T.navHover
        t.button.BackgroundTransparency = (on or hover) and 0 or 1
        t.label.TextColor3 = on and T.white or T.nav
        t.label.FontFace = on and FONT.medium or FONT.regular
    end
    -- ค้นไม่เจอเลย → บอกผู้เล่น (เดิม sidebar ว่างเปล่า ดูเหมือนพัง)
    local noMatch = text({
        Name = "NoMatch", Text = "No matches", RichText = false, FontFace = FONT.regular, TextSize = 13, TextColor3 = T.placeholder,
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 30), LayoutOrder = 100000, Visible = false, ZIndex = 2,
    }, nav)
    local function applySearch()
        local q = string.lower(searchBox.Text or "")
        local found = false
        for _, g in ipairs(groups) do
            local any = false
            for _, t in ipairs(g.tabs) do
                local show = q == "" or string.find(t.search, q, 1, true) ~= nil
                t.button.Visible = show
                any = any or show
            end
            if g.label then g.label.Visible = any end
            if g.separator then g.separator.Visible = q == "" end
            g.frame.Visible = any or q == ""
            found = found or any
        end
        noMatch.Text = "No matches for \"" .. (searchBox.Text or "") .. "\""
        noMatch.Visible = q ~= "" and not found
    end
    searchBox:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(applySearch)

    function W:TabGroup(GroupSettings)
        GroupSettings = GroupSettings or {}
        groupOrder = groupOrder + 1
        local frame = mk("Frame", { Name = "Group", BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, LayoutOrder = groupOrder, ZIndex = 2 }, nav)
        vlist(frame, 2)
        local g = { frame = frame, tabs = {}, order = 0 }
        groups[#groups + 1] = g
        if GroupSettings.Separator then
            g.separator = mk("Frame", { Name = "Separator", BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 17), LayoutOrder = 0, ZIndex = 2 }, frame)
            mk("Frame", { BackgroundColor3 = T.sidebarLine, Position = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 9), Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 1), ZIndex = 2 }, g.separator)
        end
        if GroupSettings.Name then
            g.label = text({
                Name = "GroupLabel", Text = string.upper(GroupSettings.Name), FontFace = FONT.medium, TextSize = 11, TextColor3 = T.group,
                -- 31/17 instead of the mockup's 32/21: the mockup's own sidebar is ~12px taller than its window (it scrolls)
                Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 31), TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Bottom, LayoutOrder = 1, ZIndex = 2,
            }, frame)
            padding(g.label, 13, 10, 4, 10)
        end
        local GF = {}
        function GF:Tab(TabSettings)
            g.order = g.order + 1
            local button = mk("TextButton", {
                Name = "TabSwitcher", Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false, BackgroundColor3 = T.navHover, BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 36), LayoutOrder = 1 + g.order, ZIndex = 2,
            }, frame)
            corner(button, 7)
            local label = text({ Name = "TabSwitcherName", Text = TabSettings.Name or "", TextSize = 13.5, TextColor3 = T.nav, Position = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 0), Size = UDim2.new(1, -36, 1, 0), ZIndex = 2 }, button)
            local badge = mk("Frame", { Name = "Badge", BackgroundColor3 = TONES.run, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0.5, 0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(7, 7), Visible = false, ZIndex = 2 }, button)
            corner(badge, 4)

            local page = mk("ScrollingFrame", {
                Name = "Page", BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), CanvasSize = UDim2.new(), AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
                ScrollBarThickness = 12, ScrollBarImageColor3 = T.scroll, ScrollingDirection = Enum.ScrollingDirection.Y, Visible = false,
            }, pages)
            padding(page, 20, 24, 28, 24)
            vlist(page, 16)
            local t = { button = button, label = label, page = page, order = 0, search = string.lower(TabSettings.Name or "") }
            function t.nextOrder()
                t.order = t.order + 1
                return t.order
            end
            function t.index(s) t.search = t.search .. " " .. string.lower(tostring(s)) end
            if TabSettings.Title then
                local head = mk("Frame", { Name = "PageHeader", BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, LayoutOrder = 0 }, page)
                padding(head, 0, 0, 4, 0)
                vlist(head, 4)
                text({ Name = "PageTitle", Text = TabSettings.Title, FontFace = FONT.semibold, TextSize = 21, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 27), LayoutOrder = 1 }, head)
                if TabSettings.Description then
                    text({ Name = "PageDescription", Text = TabSettings.Description, TextSize = 13, TextColor3 = T.sub, TextWrapped = true, AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0), LayoutOrder = 2 }, head)
                end
                t.index(TabSettings.Title)
                t.index(TabSettings.Description or "")
            end
            tabs[#tabs + 1] = t
            g.tabs[#g.tabs + 1] = t

            local TF = { Settings = TabSettings }
            function TF:Select()
                if W.openDropdown then pcall(W.openDropdown.Close, W.openDropdown) end
                local previous = current
                current = t
                for _, other in ipairs(tabs) do other.page.Visible = other == t end
                if previous and previous ~= t then paintTab(previous) end
                paintTab(t)
            end
            function TF:SetBadge(on, color)
                badge.Visible = on == true
                if color then badge.BackgroundColor3 = color end
            end
            function TF:Section() return newCard(W, t) end
            button.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() TF:Select() end)
            button.MouseEnter:Connect(function() paintTab(t, true) end)
            button.MouseLeave:Connect(function() paintTab(t, false) end)
            paintTab(t)
            return TF
        end
        return GF
    end

    -- ---------- globe menu (global settings) ----------
    local globalMenu = mk("Frame", { Name = "GlobalSettings", BackgroundColor3 = T.card, Position = UDim2.fromOffset(14, HH - 6), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(220, 0), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, Visible = false, ZIndex = 20 }, base)
    corner(globalMenu, 10)
    stroke(globalMenu, T.cardStroke)
    padding(globalMenu, 6, 6, 6, 6)
    vlist(globalMenu, 2)
    globe.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() globalMenu.Visible = not globalMenu.Visible end)
    globe.MouseEnter:Connect(function() if logo then globe.ImageTransparency = 0.2 else globe.ImageColor3 = T.text end end)
    globe.MouseLeave:Connect(function() if logo then globe.ImageTransparency = 0 else globe.ImageColor3 = T.icon end end)
    function W:GlobalSetting(GS)
        local state = GS.Default == true
        local b = mk("TextButton", { Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false, BackgroundColor3 = T.navHover, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 32), ZIndex = 20 }, globalMenu)
        corner(b, 6)
        local lbl = text({ Text = GS.Name or "", TextSize = 13, Position = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 0), Size = UDim2.new(1, -60, 1, 0), ZIndex = 20 }, b)
        local trackF = mk("Frame", { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(1, -8, 0.5, 0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(30, 18), BackgroundColor3 = T.off, ZIndex = 20 }, b)
        corner(trackF, 9)
        local knob = mk("Frame", { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(0, 2, 0.5, 0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(14, 14), BackgroundColor3 = T.knobOff, ZIndex = 20 }, trackF)
        corner(knob, 7)
        local function render()
            trackF.BackgroundColor3 = state and T.accent or T.off
            knob.Position = UDim2.new(0, state and 14 or 2, 0.5, 0)
            knob.BackgroundColor3 = state and T.white or T.knobOff
        end
        render()
        b.MouseEnter:Connect(function() b.BackgroundTransparency = 0 end)
        b.MouseLeave:Connect(function() b.BackgroundTransparency = 1 end)
        -- With GS.Flag the switch is registered like a Toggle, so it is saved in / loaded from the player's config
        local G2 = { Class = "Toggle", IgnoreConfig = false, State = state }
        function G2:UpdateState(v)
            state = v == true
            G2.State = state
            render()
            if GS.Callback then GS.Callback(state) end
            if GS.Flag and GOLFGUI.TriggerAutoSave then GOLFGUI:TriggerAutoSave() end
        end
        function G2:UpdateName(v) lbl.Text = v end
        b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() G2:UpdateState(not state) end)
        if GS.Flag then GOLFGUI.Options[GS.Flag] = G2 end
        return G2
    end

    -- ---------- notifications (bottom-right) ----------
    local notifyHolder = mk("Frame", { Name = "Notifications", BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), ZIndex = 50 }, gui)
    padding(notifyHolder, 12, 12, 12, 12)
    mk("UIListLayout", { SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder, Padding = UDim.new(0, 8), HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Right, VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Bottom }, notifyHolder)
    local notificationsOn = true
    local notifyCount = 0
    function W:Notify(NS)
        local NF = {}
        if not notificationsOn then
            function NF:Cancel() end
            function NF:UpdateTitle() end
            function NF:UpdateDescription() end
            function NF:Resize() end
            return NF
        end
        notifyCount = notifyCount + 1
        local box = mk("Frame", { Name = "Notification", BackgroundColor3 = T.card, Size = UDim2.fromOffset(300, 0), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, LayoutOrder = notifyCount, ZIndex = 50 }, notifyHolder)
        corner(box, 10)
        stroke(box, T.cardStroke)
        padding(box, 12, 14, 12, 14)
        vlist(box, 3)
        local t1 = text({ Text = NS.Title or "", FontFace = FONT.semibold, TextSize = 13, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, TextWrapped = true, LayoutOrder = 1, ZIndex = 50 }, box)
        local t2 = text({ Text = NS.Description or "", TextSize = 13, TextColor3 = T.sub, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, TextWrapped = true, LayoutOrder = 2, ZIndex = 50 }, box)
        local closed = false
        function NF:Cancel()
            if closed then return end
            closed = true
            box:Destroy()
        end
        function NF:UpdateTitle(v) t1.Text = v end
        function NF:UpdateDescription(v) t2.Text = v end
        function NF:Resize(x) box.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(x, 0) end
        task.delay(NS.Lifetime or 5, function() NF:Cancel() end)
        return NF
    end

    -- ---------- dialog ----------
    function W:Dialog(DS)
        local shade = mk("TextButton", { Name = "Dialog", Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false, BackgroundColor3 = rgb(0, 0, 0), BackgroundTransparency = 0.45, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), ZIndex = 40 }, base)
        corner(shade, 12)
        local box = mk("Frame", { BackgroundColor3 = T.card, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(340, 0), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, ZIndex = 41 }, shade)
        corner(box, 10)
        stroke(box, T.cardStroke)
        padding(box, 16, 16, 16, 16)
        vlist(box, 8)
        text({ Text = DS.Title or "", FontFace = FONT.semibold, TextSize = 15, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 20), LayoutOrder = 1, ZIndex = 41 }, box)
        text({ Text = DS.Description or "", TextSize = 13, TextColor3 = T.sub, TextWrapped = true, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, LayoutOrder = 2, ZIndex = 41 }, box)
        local row = mk("Frame", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 34), LayoutOrder = 3, ZIndex = 41 }, box)
        hlist(row, 8, Enum.VerticalAlignment.Center, Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Right)
        local DF = {}
        function DF:Cancel() shade:Destroy() end
        for i, bs in ipairs(DS.Buttons or {}) do
            local primary = i == 1
            local b = mk("TextButton", {
                Text = bs.Name or "", AutoButtonColor = false, FontFace = primary and FONT.medium or FONT.regular, TextSize = 13,
                TextColor3 = primary and T.white or T.text, BackgroundColor3 = primary and T.primary or T.input,
                AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.X, Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 34), LayoutOrder = i, ZIndex = 41,
            }, row)
            corner(b, 7)
            stroke(b, primary and T.accent or T.inputStroke)
            padding(b, 0, 14, 0, 14)
            b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                DF:Cancel()
                if bs.Callback then bs.Callback() end
            end)
        end
        function DF:UpdateTitle() end
        function DF:UpdateDescription() end
        return DF
    end

    -- ---------- state / lifecycle ----------
    local windowOpen = true
    function W:SetState(open)
        windowOpen = open == true
        base.Visible = windowOpen
        if MINI_LOCKED then
            mini.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0)
            mini.Position = MINI_DEFAULT
        end
        mini.Visible = not windowOpen and Settings.MiniBar ~= false
        paintMini()
    end
    function W:GetState() return windowOpen end
    function W:SetKeybind(k) menuKey = k paintMini() end
    -- hiding shows the mini bar (it carries the "click to open / key" hint), so no toast any more
    local function toggleMenu()
        W:SetState(not windowOpen)
    end
    -- mini bar: a click (not a drag) opens the window · a drag saves where it was put
    track(UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
        if miniDrag and (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
            local moved = miniMoved
            miniDrag, miniMoved = nil, false
            if not moved then W:SetState(true) end
        end
    end))
    track(UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input, gpe)
        if gpe then return end
        if input.KeyCode == menuKey then toggleMenu() end
    end))
    minDot.MouseButton1Click:Connect(toggleMenu)
    local onUnload
    function W.onUnloaded(fn) onUnload = fn end
    function W:Unload()
        for _, c in ipairs(libConnections) do pcall(c.Disconnect, c) end
        table.clear(libConnections)
        if gui.Parent then gui:Destroy() end
        if onUnload then pcall(onUnload) end
    end
    exitDot.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        W:Dialog({
            Title = Settings.Title,
            Description = "Close the menu? Everything it runs stops until you start it again.",
            Buttons = { { Name = "Close", Callback = function() W:Unload() end }, { Name = "Cancel" } },
        })
    end)
    function W:SetSize(v) base.Size = v end
    function W:GetSize() return base.Size end
    function W:SetScale(v) baseScale.Scale = v end
    function W:UpdateTitle(v) title.Text = v end

    W.Base = base
    return W
end

-- ====================================================================
-- configs (MacLib format: <Folder>/settings/<name>.json = { objects = { {type, flag, ...} } })
-- ====================================================================
local ClassParser = {
    Toggle = {
        -- SaveAs: a switch paused by the hub (World Boss) keeps the player's own choice in the file, so a reload
        -- or server hop in the middle of the pause does not come back with Auto Farm off
        Save = function(flag, data)
            local st = data.State or false
            if data.SaveAs ~= nil then st = data.SaveAs end
            return { type = "Toggle", flag = flag, state = st }
        end,
        Load = function(flag, data)
            -- MacLib only loaded "on" → a switch the user turned off came back on (default on) after every server change
            local option = GOLFGUI.Options[flag]
            if option and data.state ~= nil and option.State ~= (data.state == true) then option:UpdateState(data.state == true) end
        end,
    },
    Slider = {
        Save = function(flag, data) return { type = "Slider", flag = flag, value = (data.Value and tostring(data.Value)) or false } end,
        Load = function(flag, data)
            local option = GOLFGUI.Options[flag]
            if option and data.value then
                option:UpdateValue(data.value)
                -- UpdateValue moves the knob only → hand the loaded value to the callback too (MacLib never did)
                if option.Settings and option.Settings.Callback and option.Value ~= nil then task.spawn(option.Settings.Callback, option.Value) end
            end
        end,
    },
    Input = {
        Save = function(flag, data) return { type = "Input", flag = flag, text = data.Text } end,
        Load = function(flag, data)
            if GOLFGUI.Options[flag] and type(data.text) == "string" then GOLFGUI.Options[flag]:UpdateText(data.text) end
        end,
    },
    Dropdown = {
        Save = function(flag, data) return { type = "Dropdown", flag = flag, value = data.Value } end,
        Load = function(flag, data)
            if GOLFGUI.Options[flag] and data.value then GOLFGUI.Options[flag]:UpdateSelection(data.value) end
        end,
    },
}

GOLFGUI.AutoSaveEnabled = true
GOLFGUI.AutoSaveName    = nil
GOLFGUI.UserName        = nil
GOLFGUI.IsLoadingConfig = false
GOLFGUI.OnAutoSaved     = nil
GOLFGUI._autoSaveToken  = 0
GOLFGUI._loadToken      = 0

local function buildFolderTree()
    if isStudio or not (isfolder and makefolder) then return end
    if not GOLFGUI.Folder or GOLFGUI.Folder == "" then return end
    -- Create every nested path segment step-by-step (supports multi-instance per-user paths like GolfHub_HuntingTogether/<PlayerName>/settings)
    local fullPath = GOLFGUI.Folder .. "/settings"
    local accum = nil
    for seg in string.gmatch(fullPath, "[^/\\]+") do
        accum = accum and (accum .. "/" .. seg) or seg
        if not isfolder(accum) then
            pcall(makefolder, accum)
        end
    end
end

function GOLFGUI:SetFolder(folder)
    if isStudio then return "Config system unavailable." end
    GOLFGUI.Folder = folder
    buildFolderTree()
end

function GOLFGUI:GetConfigFilePath(path)
    local name = (path and path ~= "") and path or GOLFGUI.AutoSaveName or "default"
    return (GOLFGUI.Folder or "GolfHub_HuntingTogether") .. "/settings/" .. name .. ".json"
end

function GOLFGUI:SaveConfig(path)
    if isStudio or not writefile then return false, "Config system unavailable." end
    if not path or path == "" then return false, "Please select a config file." end
    buildFolderTree()
    local localPlr = Players and Players.LocalPlayer
    local data = {
        player  = localPlr and localPlr.Name or GOLFGUI.UserName or "Unknown",
        userId  = localPlr and localPlr.UserId or 0,
        savedAt = os.time(),
        objects = {},
    }
    for flag, option in next, GOLFGUI.Options do
        local parser = ClassParser[option.Class]
        if parser and not option.IgnoreConfig then table.insert(data.objects, parser.Save(flag, option)) end
    end
    local ok, encoded = pcall(HttpService.JSONEncode, HttpService, data)
    if not ok then return false, "Unable to encode into JSON data" end
    local filePath = GOLFGUI.Folder .. "/settings/" .. path .. ".json"
    local wOk, wErr = pcall(writefile, filePath, encoded)
    if not wOk then return false, tostring(wErr) end
    return true, filePath
end

function GOLFGUI:TriggerAutoSave()
    if isStudio or not writefile then return end
    if not GOLFGUI.AutoSaveEnabled or GOLFGUI.IsLoadingConfig then return end
    local saveName = GOLFGUI.AutoSaveName or GOLFGUI.UserName
    if not saveName or saveName == "" then return end

    GOLFGUI._autoSaveToken = (GOLFGUI._autoSaveToken or 0) + 1
    local myToken = GOLFGUI._autoSaveToken
    task.delay(0.45, function()
        if myToken ~= GOLFGUI._autoSaveToken then return end
        if not GOLFGUI.AutoSaveEnabled or GOLFGUI.IsLoadingConfig then return end
        local ok, filePath = GOLFGUI:SaveConfig(saveName)
        if ok then
            -- Ensure this user's autoload points to their auto-saved profile if not set yet
            local curAuto = GOLFGUI:GetAutoload()
            if not curAuto or curAuto == "" then
                GOLFGUI:SetAutoload(saveName)
            end
            if type(GOLFGUI.OnAutoSaved) == "function" then
                pcall(GOLFGUI.OnAutoSaved, saveName, filePath)
            end
        end
    end)
end

function GOLFGUI:LoadConfig(path)
    if isStudio or not (isfile and readfile) then return false, "Config system unavailable." end
    if not path or path == "" then return false, "Please select a config file." end
    local file = GOLFGUI.Folder .. "/settings/" .. path .. ".json"
    if not isfile(file) then return false, "Invalid file" end
    local ok, decoded = pcall(HttpService.JSONDecode, HttpService, readfile(file))
    if not ok or type(decoded) ~= "table" or type(decoded.objects) ~= "table" then return false, "Unable to decode JSON data." end
    GOLFGUI.IsLoadingConfig = true
    GOLFGUI._loadToken = (GOLFGUI._loadToken or 0) + 1
    local myLoadToken = GOLFGUI._loadToken
    -- Spread config flag application across frames (batches of 6 per frame) to prevent a 60+ ms hitch on join
    task.spawn(function()
        local count = 0
        for _, option in next, decoded.objects do
            if myLoadToken ~= GOLFGUI._loadToken then break end
            local parser = ClassParser[option.type]
            local live = GOLFGUI.Options[option.flag]
            if parser and not (live and live.IgnoreConfig) then
                pcall(parser.Load, option.flag, option)
                count = count + 1
                if count % 6 == 0 then
                    task.wait()
                end
            end
        end
        -- Clear loading state only when the full batch loop finishes and token remains valid
        if myLoadToken == GOLFGUI._loadToken then
            GOLFGUI.IsLoadingConfig = false
        end
    end)
    return true
end

function GOLFGUI:RefreshConfigList()
    if isStudio or not (isfolder and listfiles) then return {} end
    local dir = GOLFGUI.Folder .. "/settings"
    local out = {}
    if not (isfolder(GOLFGUI.Folder) and isfolder(dir)) then return out end
    for _, file in ipairs(listfiles(dir)) do
        local name = file:match("([^/\\]+)%.json$")
        if name and name ~= "options" then out[#out + 1] = name end
    end
    return out
end

function GOLFGUI:LoadAutoLoadConfig()
    if isStudio or not (isfile and readfile) then return nil end
    local f = GOLFGUI.Folder .. "/settings/autoload.txt"
    if isfile(f) then
        local target = (readfile(f) or ""):gsub("^%s+", ""):gsub("%s+$", "")
        if target ~= "" and GOLFGUI:LoadConfig(target) then
            return target
        end
    end
    -- Fallback: auto-load this player's dedicated per-user auto-save file if it exists
    local userFileName = GOLFGUI.AutoSaveName or GOLFGUI.UserName
    if userFileName and userFileName ~= "" then
        local userPath = GOLFGUI.Folder .. "/settings/" .. userFileName .. ".json"
        if isfile(userPath) and GOLFGUI:LoadConfig(userFileName) then
            return userFileName
        end
    end
    return nil
end

function GOLFGUI:SetAutoload(path)
    if isStudio or not writefile then return false, "Config system unavailable." end
    if not path or path == "" then return false, "Please specify config name." end
    buildFolderTree()
    writefile(GOLFGUI.Folder .. "/settings/autoload.txt", path)
    return true
end

function GOLFGUI:GetAutoload()
    if isStudio or not (isfile and readfile) then return nil end
    local f = GOLFGUI.Folder .. "/settings/autoload.txt"
    if isfile(f) then return readfile(f) end
    return nil
end

function GOLFGUI:DeleteConfig(path)
    if isStudio or not (delfile or (isfile and writefile)) then return false, "Config system unavailable." end
    if not path or path == "" then return false, "Please specify config name." end
    local file = GOLFGUI.Folder .. "/settings/" .. path .. ".json"
    if isfile and isfile(file) then
        if delfile then
            delfile(file)
            return true
        else
            writefile(file, "")
            return true
        end
    end
    return false, "File not found"
end

if type(S) == "table" then S.GOLFGUI = GOLFGUI end
return GOLFGUI
end)()

-- Services
local Players            = cloneref and cloneref(game:GetService("Players"))            or game:GetService("Players")
local RunService         = cloneref and cloneref(game:GetService("RunService"))         or game:GetService("RunService")
local HttpService        = cloneref and cloneref(game:GetService("HttpService"))        or game:GetService("HttpService")
local PathfindingService = cloneref and cloneref(game:GetService("PathfindingService")) or game:GetService("PathfindingService")
local TeleportService    = cloneref and cloneref(game:GetService("TeleportService"))    or game:GetService("TeleportService")
local GuiService        = cloneref and cloneref(game:GetService("GuiService"))        or game:GetService("GuiService")
local CoreGui            = cloneref and cloneref(game:GetService("CoreGui"))            or game:GetService("CoreGui")

local lp = Players.LocalPlayer

-- Destroy previous instance
for _, host in ipairs({ (gethui and gethui()), CoreGui, lp:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui") }) do
    if host then
        local old = host:FindFirstChild("GolfHubHuntingTogether")
        if old then pcall(function() old:Destroy() end) end
    end
end

-- ====================================================================
-- ZONE TABLE & SEQUENTIAL HIGHWAY
-- ====================================================================
local ZONES = {
    { zone = "Field01", name = "Rabbit Field",         minLv = 1,  nextLv = 9   },
    { zone = "Field02", name = "Boar Forest",          minLv = 9,  nextLv = 17  },
    { zone = "Field03", name = "Bear Forest",          minLv = 17, nextLv = 26  },
    { zone = "Field04", name = "Hyena Den",            minLv = 26, nextLv = 34, prevBoss = "Boss01" },
    { zone = "Field05", name = "Rhino Thicket",        minLv = 34, nextLv = 42  },
    { zone = "Field06", name = "Tiger Thicket",        minLv = 42, nextLv = 51  },
    { zone = "Field07", name = "Scorpion Dunes",       minLv = 51, nextLv = 59, prevBoss = "Boss02" },
    { zone = "Field08", name = "Ram Cliffs",           minLv = 59, nextLv = 67  },

-- ====================================================================
-- HELPERS & FORWARD DECLARATIONS
-- ====================================================================
local function enforceAntiSeat(hum)
    if not hum then return end
    -- getCharRoot calls this ~45 times a second (measured on 770X880): read the state and write it only when the game
    -- has turned Seated back on, instead of writing it on every call. No closure per call either.
    local okGet, seatOn = pcall(hum.GetStateEnabled, hum, Enum.HumanoidStateType.Seated)
    if not okGet or seatOn then
        pcall(hum.SetStateEnabled, hum, Enum.HumanoidStateType.Seated, false)
    end
    if hum.Sit then
        hum.Sit = false
        hum.Jump = true
    end
end

-- ป้องกันตัวละครนั่งติดค้าง (Anti-Seat) ผ่าน Humanoid.Seated ลุกขึ้นทันที 100% ปลอดภัย ไม่กิน CPU
local function setupAntiSeat(char)
    if not char then return end
    local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if hum then
        -- through GolfHub_Conn so a reload drops this copy's handler instead of stacking one per reload
        _G.GolfHub_Conn(hum.Seated, function(active)
            if active then
                hum.Sit = false
                hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp)
            end
        end)
    end
end
pcall(function()
    if lp.Character then setupAntiSeat(lp.Character) end
    _G.GolfHub_Conn(lp.CharacterAdded, setupAntiSeat)
end)

local function getCharRoot()
    local char = lp.Character
    if not char then return nil, nil end
    local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if hum then
        enforceAntiSeat(hum)
    end
    return char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"), hum
end

local function getPrivateDataAttr(attrName)
    local pd = lp and lp:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui") and lp.PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("LumiPrivateData")
    if pd then
        local v = pd:GetAttribute(attrName)
        if v ~= nil then return v end
    end
    return lp and lp:GetAttribute(attrName)
end

local function getLevel()
    local lvl = lp and (lp:GetAttribute("Level") or lp:GetAttribute("JobLevel"))
    if lvl and lvl > 0 then return lvl end
    local jobs = getPrivateDataAttr("JobsJson")
    if type(jobs) == "string" and #jobs > 2 then
        local maxL = 0
        for lStr in jobs:gmatch('"Level":(%d+)') do
            local num = tonumber(lStr)
            if num and num > maxL then maxL = num end
        end
        if maxL > 0 then return maxL end
    end
    return 1
end

-- ====================================================================
-- SAFE PROXIMITY PROMPT TRIGGER HELPER
-- ====================================================================
local function triggerPromptSafe(prompt)
    if not prompt or not prompt:IsA("ProximityPrompt") or not prompt.Enabled then return false end
    local triggered = false
    pcall(function()
        if fireproximityprompt then
            pcall(function() fireproximityprompt(prompt) end)
            triggered = true
        end
    end)
    pcall(function()
        if not triggered and prompt.InputHoldBegin and prompt.InputHoldEnd then
            prompt:InputHoldBegin()
            task.wait(0.05)
            prompt:InputHoldEnd()
            triggered = true
        end
    end)
    pcall(function()
        local vim = game:GetService("VirtualInputManager")
        if vim and not triggered then
            local key = prompt.KeyboardKeyCode or Enum.KeyCode.E
            local hold = (prompt.HoldDuration and prompt.HoldDuration > 0) and (prompt.HoldDuration + 0.15) or 0.08
            vim:SendKeyEvent(true, key, false, game)
            task.wait(hold)
            vim:SendKeyEvent(false, key, false, game)
            triggered = true
        end
    end)
    return triggered
end

-- Only WarpPillars says which pillars are unlocked. Gates, achievements and level do not: guessing "unlocked" sent the
-- character to pillars it could not use.
local function isPillarUnlocked(pillarKey, list)
    if pillarKey == "Town" then return true end
    if list == nil then list = lp and lp:GetAttribute("WarpPillars") end
    if type(list) ~= "string" then return false end
    for entry in list:gmatch("[^,%s]+") do
        if entry == pillarKey then return true end
    end
    return false
end

local function getBossGates()
    local gates = {}
    local bgJson = getPrivateDataAttr("BossGatesJson")
    if type(bgJson) == "string" and #bgJson > 2 then
        for k in bgJson:gmatch('"([^"]+)":true') do
            gates[k] = true
        end
    end
    local achieve = getPrivateDataAttr("AchieveJson")
    if type(achieve) == "string" then
        if achieve:find('"boss_all":true') then
            gates["Boss01"] = true gates["Boss02"] = true gates["Boss03"] = true gates["Boss04"] = true gates["Boss05"] = true
        end
        if achieve:find('"b1_kill":true') then gates["Boss01"] = true end
        if achieve:find('"b2_kill":true') then gates["Boss02"] = true end
        if achieve:find('"b3_kill":true') then gates["Boss03"] = true end
        if achieve:find('"b4_kill":true') then gates["Boss04"] = true end
        if achieve:find('"b5_kill":true') then gates["Boss05"] = true end
    end
    return gates
end

-- The game's own rule (GateLockClient): a door is open only when BossGatesJson["BossNN"] is true. The FieldNN keys in the
-- same table do not open doors (Field07 can be true while the Boss02 door is solid), and neither do pillars or achievements.
local function isBossCleared(bossKey)
    local gateJson = lp and lp:GetAttribute("BossGatesJson")
    -- gate data not replicated yet: treat as open rather than walking into a boss room by mistake
    if type(gateJson) ~= "string" then return true end
    return gateJson:find('"' .. bossKey .. '":true', 1, true) ~= nil
end

local isPlayerInDungeon = nil
local toggleDungeonFarm = nil
local TOWN_COORDS = nil
local BOSS_TARGET_DEFS = nil
local selectedBossKey = "Boss03"
local selectedFarmZoneKey = "Auto"

local function zoneNameForZ(z)
    if z > -30 then return "Town" end
    if z > -250 then return "Field01" end
    if z > -500 then return "Field02" end
    if z > -800 then return "Field03" end
    if z > -950 then return "Boss01" end
    if z > -1150 then return "Field04" end
    if z > -1380 then return "Field05" end
    if z > -1650 then return "Field06" end
    if z > -1800 then return "Boss02" end
    if z > -2050 then return "Field07" end
    if z > -2300 then return "Field08" end
    if z > -2550 then return "Field09" end
    if z > -2700 then return "Boss03" end
    if z > -2950 then return "Field10" end
    if z > -3200 then return "Field11" end
    if z > -3450 then return "Field12" end
    if z > -3650 then return "Boss04" end
    if z > -3900 then return "Field13" end
    if z > -4150 then return "Field14" end
    if z > -4360 then return "Field15" end
    return "Boss05"
end

local function getCurrentActualZone()
    if game.PlaceId == DUNGEON_PLACE_ID or (isPlayerInDungeon and isPlayerInDungeon()) then
        return "Dungeon"
    end
    local root = getCharRoot()
    if not root then return "Town" end
    local z = root.Position.Z
    return zoneNameForZ(z)
end


local function getTargetZoneForLevel(lv)
    for i = #ZONES, 1, -1 do
        if lv >= ZONES[i].minLv then return ZONES[i] end
    end
    return ZONES[1]
end

local function resolveFarmTargetZone(lv)
    -- 1. Manual user choice from UI dropdown
    if selectedFarmZoneKey and selectedFarmZoneKey ~= "Auto" then
        for _, z in ipairs(ZONES) do
            -- while levelling every class, a zone picked for a 99 cannot hold the next, lower class
            if z.zone == selectedFarmZoneKey and not (_G.GolfHub_AllJobs and _G.GolfHub_AllJobs.on and (lv or getLevel()) < z.minLv) then
                return z
            end
        end
    end
    -- 2. Auto follows the level only. Not the map highlighted in the game's party board: the game pre-selects that
    --    once by itself and never updates it, so it held a Lv.92 character in Field11.
    --    Default: Choose highest zone matching current real-time level threshold (1-8 -> Field01 ... 99 -> Field15)
    return getTargetZoneForLevel(lv or getLevel())
end

local autoEquipCards       = true

_G.GolfHub_StarEquip = _G.GolfHub_StarEquip or { on = false, sets = {} }

-- Auto Equip triggers (event driven, debounced): class change, level up (new gear tier) and new gear in the bag
do
    local pending, lastRun = false, 0
    local function requestEquip(why)
        if pending then return end
        pending = true
        local gen = _G.GolfHub_Gen
        task.delay(2.5, function()
            pending = false
            if gen ~= _G.GolfHub_Gen or not equipBestGearForCurrentJob then return end
            if tick() - lastRun < 8 then return end
            lastRun = tick()
            pcall(equipBestGearForCurrentJob, true)
        end)
    end
    _G.GolfHub_Conn(lp:GetAttributeChangedSignal("Job"), function()
        if _G.GolfHub_StarEquip and _G.GolfHub_StarEquip.on then
            -- New class: the old class's weapon and gear may not fit it, and its level may be too low for every star
            -- piece (770X880 on MuayThai Lv 30, all star pieces ReqLevel 80). Let the game put on its own best first,
            -- once, here only; the star request (2 s debounce) then puts the chosen star pieces back on top.
            pcall(function()
                local R = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("Remotes")
                if R and R:FindFirstChild("EquipItem") then R.EquipItem:FireServer("@best") end
            end)
            if _G.GolfHub_StarEquip.request then _G.GolfHub_StarEquip.request("job") end
            if autoEquipCards and equipBestGearForCurrentJob then
                task.spawn(function() equipBestGearForCurrentJob(true) end)
            end
        elseif autoEquipOnJobChange then
            requestEquip("job")
        end
    end)
    _G.GolfHub_Conn(lp:GetAttributeChangedSignal("Level"), function()
        if autoEquipAlways and (tonumber(lp:GetAttribute("Level")) or 0) % 5 == 0 then requestEquip("level") end
    end)
    local lastGear
    _G.GolfHub_Conn(lp:GetAttributeChangedSignal("GearJson"), function()
        local g = lp:GetAttribute("GearJson")
        if autoEquipAlways and lastGear ~= nil and g ~= lastGear then requestEquip("gear") end
        lastGear = g
    end)
    lastGear = lp:GetAttribute("GearJson")
end
