local function setStatus(msg, tone)
    tone = tone or "run"
    if lblStatus then pcall(function() lblStatus:Set(msg, tone) end) end
    if Window and Window.SetStatus then pcall(function() Window:SetStatus("GOLF HUB", msg, nil, tone) end) end
end
local function setZoneLabel(msg)
    if lblCurrentZone then pcall(function() lblCurrentZone:UpdateName(msg) end) end
end
local function setLvLabel(msg)
    if lblCurrentLevel then pcall(function() lblCurrentLevel:UpdateName(msg) end) end
end

-- ====================================================================
-- ANTI-STUCK & WALL AVOIDANCE (RAYCAST WHISKER ENGINE)
-- ====================================================================
local raycastParams = RaycastParams.new()
raycastParams.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
raycastParams.RespectCanCollide = true

local lastRayFilterUpdate = 0
local function updateRaycastFilter()
    if tick() - lastRayFilterUpdate < 1.0 then return end
    lastRayFilterUpdate = tick()
    local filter = {}
    if lp.Character then table.insert(filter, lp.Character) end
    local monsters = workspace:FindFirstChild("Monsters")
    if monsters then table.insert(filter, monsters) end
    for _, p in ipairs(game:GetService("Players"):GetPlayers()) do
        if p ~= lp and p.Character then table.insert(filter, p.Character) end
    end
    raycastParams.FilterDescendantsInstances = filter
end

-- ตรวจสอบว่าพื้นผิวเป็นบันไดหรือทางลาด (Normal.Y >= 0.5 คือเดินขึ้นได้ ไม่ใช่กำแพง)
local function isWalkableSlope(rayHit)
    if not rayHit then return true end
    return rayHit.Normal.Y >= 0.5
end

-- Smart dodge & auto-jump for obstacles ahead (Optimized — Never jumps if already airborne)
local function avoidObstaclesAhead(root, hum, moveDir)
    updateRaycastFilter()
    local pos = root.Position
    local up = Vector3.new(0, 1, 0)
    local flatDir = Vector3.new(moveDir.X, 0, moveDir.Z)
    if flatDir.Magnitude < 0.1 then return moveDir end
    local dirUnit = flatDir.Unit

    local right = Vector3.new(-dirUnit.Z, 0, dirUnit.X)

    -- Low ray (knee/fence level)
    local lowHit = workspace:Raycast(pos - up * 1.4, dirUnit * 3.5, raycastParams)
    local highHit = workspace:Raycast(pos + up * 1.8, dirUnit * 3.5, raycastParams)

    if lowHit and isWalkableSlope(lowHit) then
        return moveDir
    end

    -- Case 1: Low obstacle -> Jump only if currently on ground
    if lowHit and not isWalkableSlope(lowHit) and (not highHit or isWalkableSlope(highHit)) then
        local st = hum:GetState()
        if st ~= Enum.HumanoidStateType.Freefall and st ~= Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping then
            hum.Jump = true
        end
        return moveDir
    end

    -- Case 2: High wall -> Steer around
    if (highHit and not isWalkableSlope(highHit)) or (lowHit and not isWalkableSlope(lowHit)) then
        local leftDir = (dirUnit - right * 0.9).Unit * 4.0
        local leftHit = workspace:Raycast(pos, leftDir, raycastParams)

        local rightDir = (dirUnit + right * 0.9).Unit * 4.0
        local rightHit = workspace:Raycast(pos, rightDir, raycastParams)

        if not leftHit or isWalkableSlope(leftHit) then
            return (dirUnit - right * 1.2).Unit
        elseif not rightHit or isWalkableSlope(rightHit) then
            return (dirUnit + right * 1.2).Unit
        else
            local st = hum:GetState()
            if st ~= Enum.HumanoidStateType.Freefall and st ~= Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping then
                hum.Jump = true
            end
            return right
        end
    end

    return moveDir
end

-- Emergency unstuck procedure: Steers to safe road centerline, never hops randomly into walls/alleys
local function performUnstuck(root, hum)
    if not root or not hum then return end
    -- one anti-stuck routine: while the Settings watchdog is running it owns every escape move, this is only the
    -- fallback for walks it does not cover (Anti-Stuck switched off, walks with no automation active)
    if tick() - (_G.GolfHub_WatchdogAt or 0) < 1 then return end
    if hum.Sit then
        hum.Sit = false
        hum.Jump = true
        task.wait(0.08)
    end
    hum.Jump = true
    task.wait(0.05)
    -- just off a pillar warp, standing still is the landing, not an obstacle: no sidestep (it stepped into the pillar)
    if tick() - (_G.GolfHub_LandedAt or 0) < 4 then return end

    local pos = root.Position
    -- 1. เมือง (Town): ดันตัวละครเข้าสู่แนวถนนกึ่งกลาง R = 27.0 ห่างบูธกลางและไม่ชนบ้าน
    if pos.Z > -25 and pos.Z < 105 then
        local cx, cz = 0.0, 37.15
        local dx, dz = pos.X - cx, pos.Z - cz
        local d = math.sqrt(dx * dx + dz * dz)
        if d > 0.1 then
            local ux, uz = dx / d, dz / d
            hum:MoveTo(Vector3.new(cx + ux * 27.0, pos.Y, cz + uz * 27.0))
            task.wait(0.35)
            return
        end
    end

    -- 2. สะพาน/ทางเดินบอส (Corridor / Bridge): ก้าวเข้าสู่เส้นดำกึ่งกลางถนน X = 0.0 เสมอ
    -- only on the narrow stretches (boss gates, ramps, bridge), never out of a field the character is farming in
    if pos.Z < -500 and math.abs(pos.X) > 2.0 and math.abs(pos.X) <= 20.0 then
        for _, wp in ipairs(ROUTE_WAYPOINTS) do
            local narrow = wp.isBossGate or wp.id:find("_Gate") or wp.id:find("_Foot") or wp.id:find("_Bridge")
            if narrow and math.abs(pos.Z - wp.pos.Z) <= 25 then
                hum:MoveTo(Vector3.new(0.0, pos.Y, pos.Z))
                task.wait(0.35)
                return
            end
        end
    end

    -- 3. อื่นๆ: ก้าวตรงไปข้างหน้าเล็กน้อย 3.5 studs พร้อมกระโดด (ห้ามกระโดดออกข้าง 8 studs ชนกำแพง)
    local fwd = root.CFrame.LookVector
    local toGoal = Vector3.new(hum.WalkToPoint.X - pos.X, 0, hum.WalkToPoint.Z - pos.Z)
    if toGoal.Magnitude > 1 then fwd = toGoal.Unit end -- toward where it is walking, not where it happens to face
    hum:MoveTo(pos + fwd * 3.5)
    task.wait(0.25)
end

-- ====================================================================
-- WALK ENGINE — PathfindingService with AgentRadius=2.0 & Anti-Stuck
-- ====================================================================
local sharedNavPath = PathfindingService:CreatePath({
    AgentRadius     = 2.0,
    AgentHeight     = 5.0,
    AgentCanJump    = true,
    AgentCanClimb   = false,
    WaypointSpacing = 6,
})

local computeSubPath
do
    local _lastPathComputeTime = 0
    local _lastFromPos, _lastToPos, _lastWaypoints
    computeSubPath = function(fromPos, toPos)
        if not fromPos or not toPos then return nil end
        local now = tick()
        -- Return cached waypoints if queried for the same from/to (within 4 studs) inside 1.0s
        if _lastWaypoints and (now - _lastPathComputeTime < 1.0)
            and _lastFromPos and (fromPos - _lastFromPos).Magnitude < 4.0
            and _lastToPos and (toPos - _lastToPos).Magnitude < 4.0 then
            return table.clone(_lastWaypoints)
        end
        local ok = pcall(function() sharedNavPath:ComputeAsync(fromPos, toPos) end)
        if ok and sharedNavPath.Status == Enum.PathStatus.Success then
            local wps = sharedNavPath:GetWaypoints()
            _lastPathComputeTime = now
            _lastFromPos = fromPos
            _lastToPos = toPos
            _lastWaypoints = wps
            return table.clone(wps)
        end
        return nil
    end
end

local function walkTo(targetPos, timeout, stopDist, abortFn, _skipTownRing, _skipBoss02Guard)
    timeout  = timeout or 25
    stopDist = stopDist or 5
    local root, hum = getCharRoot()
    if not root or not hum then return false end

    -- ====================================================================
    -- TOWN BLACK-RING / RED-CIRCLE NO-GO GUARD (Never step inside center booth!)
    -- Red No-Go Circle: center (0, 37.15), R = 17.5 | Clear Middle-Road Ring: R = 27.0
    -- ====================================================================
    if not _skipTownRing and targetPos then
        local myPos = root.Position
        local inTownSpan = (myPos.Z > -25 and myPos.Z < 105) or (targetPos.Z > -25 and targetPos.Z < 105)
        if inTownSpan then
            local cx, cz = 0.0, 37.15
            local redR, ringR = 17.5, 27.0
            local dx0, dz0 = myPos.X - cx, myPos.Z - cz
            local d0 = math.sqrt(dx0 * dx0 + dz0 * dz0)

            -- 1. If spawned or pushed inside Red Circle -> immediately step radially out onto middle-road ring
            if d0 < redR then
                local ux, uz = (d0 > 0.1) and (dx0 / d0) or 0.0, (d0 > 0.1) and (dz0 / d0) or 1.0
                local pushOut = Vector3.new(cx + ux * ringR, myPos.Y, cz + uz * ringR)
                hum.Jump = true
                hum:MoveTo(pushOut)
                local pushStart = tick()
                while tick() - pushStart < 2.0 do
                    task.wait(0.06)
                    root, hum = getCharRoot()
                    if not root or not hum then return false end
                    local cd = math.sqrt((root.Position.X - cx)^2 + (root.Position.Z - cz)^2)
                    if cd >= (redR + 2.0) then break end
                    hum:MoveTo(pushOut)
                end
                myPos = root.Position
            end

            -- 2. Check if straight 2D line segment (myPos -> targetPos) intersects Red Circle
            -- ถ้าทั้งคู่เลยออกไปทางใต้ (Z > 55) หรือทางเหนือ (Z < 18) ไม่มีทางตัดผ่านซุ้มกลางเมือง ไม่ต้องเบี่ยงเส้นทาง
            local bothSouth = (myPos.Z > 55 and targetPos.Z > 55)
            local bothNorth = (myPos.Z < 18 and targetPos.Z < 18)
            if not bothSouth and not bothNorth then
                local sx, sz = targetPos.X - myPos.X, targetPos.Z - myPos.Z
                local segLenSq = sx * sx + sz * sz
                if segLenSq > 1.0 then
                    local t = math.clamp(((cx - myPos.X) * sx + (cz - myPos.Z) * sz) / segLenSq, 0, 1)
                    local nearX = myPos.X + sx * t
                    local nearZ = myPos.Z + sz * t
                    local minDist = math.sqrt((nearX - cx)^2 + (nearZ - cz)^2)
                    if minDist < redR then
                        local aStart = math.atan2(myPos.Z - cz, myPos.X - cx)
                        local aEnd   = math.atan2(targetPos.Z - cz, targetPos.X - cx)
                        local delta  = ((aEnd - aStart + math.pi) % (math.pi * 2)) - math.pi
                        local steps  = math.max(3, math.ceil(math.abs(delta) / math.rad(18)))
                        for s = 1, steps do
                            if abortFn and abortFn() then return false end
                            local ang = aStart + delta * (s / steps)
                            local ringPt = Vector3.new(cx + math.cos(ang) * ringR, 4.25, cz + math.sin(ang) * ringR)
                            walkTo(ringPt, 6, 3.5, abortFn, true, true)
                        end
                        root, hum = getCharRoot()
                        if not root or not hum then return false end

                        -- ปิดท้ายด้วยการเดินจากจุดบนวงกลมดำเข้าหาเป้าหมายตรงๆ (พ้นวงแดงแล้ว 100%)
                        return walkTo(targetPos, timeout, stopDist, abortFn, true, true)
                    end
                end
            end

            -- 3. Billboard & Pond Guard in Town:
            -- ป้ายประกาศไม้อยู่ที่ (X=12.5, Z=96.5..107) และสระน้ำอยู่ข้างๆ (X=8..22, Z=80..115)
            -- เส้นทางเฉียงระหว่างจุดเกิดเมืองทางใต้ (Z > 72, X < 8) ไปยังเสาเมืองทางตะวันออก (X > 14, Z < 75)
            -- จะตัดผ่านป้ายประกาศและสระน้ำพอดี ให้บังคับเลี้ยวผ่าน SouthAlleyCenter (0, 4.3, 65.0) เสมอ!
            local isCrossBillboardPond = (myPos.Z > 72 and targetPos.X > 14 and targetPos.Z < 75)
                                     or (targetPos.Z > 72 and myPos.X > 14 and myPos.Z < 75)
            if isCrossBillboardPond and TOWN_COORDS and TOWN_COORDS.SouthAlleyCenter then
                local dToJunc = (myPos - TOWN_COORDS.SouthAlleyCenter).Magnitude
                if dToJunc > 4.0 then
                    walkTo(TOWN_COORDS.SouthAlleyCenter, 10, 3.0, abortFn, true, true)
                    root, hum = getCharRoot()
                    if not root or not hum then return false end
                    return walkTo(targetPos, timeout, stopDist, abortFn, true, true)
                end
            end
        end
    end

    -- ====================================================================
    -- BOSS 2 WAYPOINT GUARD (Whitefang Sabertooth Entrance Corridor)
    -- Perpendicular entry/exit along black line to avoid Wooden Sign, Barrels & Campfire
    -- ====================================================================
    if not _skipBoss02Guard and targetPos then
        local myPos = root.Position
        local inBoss02Span = (myPos.Z < -1665 and myPos.Z > -1755) or (targetPos.Z < -1665 and targetPos.Z > -1755)
        if inBoss02Span then
            local b2Junction = Vector3.new(0.0, 114.0, -1686.0)
            local myAtPillarSide = (myPos.X > 3.0 and myPos.Z < -1675 and myPos.Z > -1715)
            local targetAtPillarSide = (targetPos.X > 3.0 and targetPos.Z < -1675 and targetPos.Z > -1715)
            local targetInOrPastBridge = (targetPos.Z <= -1694.0)
            local myInOrPastBridge = (myPos.Z <= -1694.0)

            -- 1. จากเสาวาร์ป (X > 3) มุ่งหน้าเข้าสะพาน/ห้องบอส -> ต้องก้าวตั้งฉากเข้าจุดเชื่อมต่อถนน (0, 114, -1686) ก่อน!
            if myAtPillarSide and targetInOrPastBridge then
                if (myPos - b2Junction).Magnitude > 2.5 then
                    walkTo(b2Junction, 8, 2.0, abortFn, true, true)
                    root, hum = getCharRoot()
                    if not root or not hum then return false end
                    myPos = root.Position
                end
            -- 2. จากในห้องบอส/สะพาน (Z <= -1694) มุ่งหน้าไปหาเสาวาร์ป (X > 3) -> ต้องเดินตรงตามเส้นดำ X = 0 มาถึงจุดเชื่อมต่อ (0, 114, -1686) ก่อน แล้วค่อยเลี้ยวขวา!
            elseif myInOrPastBridge and targetAtPillarSide then
                if (myPos - b2Junction).Magnitude > 2.5 then
                    walkTo(b2Junction, 10, 2.0, abortFn, true, true)
                    root, hum = getCharRoot()
                    if not root or not hum then return false end
                    myPos = root.Position
                end
            -- 3. จากฝั่งแคมป์ไฟ (X < -3) -> ก้าวตั้งฉากเข้าสู่กึ่งกลางถนน (X = 0) ณ ตำแหน่ง Z ปัจจุบัน (ห้ามถอยหลัง)
            elseif myPos.X < -3.0 and (targetPos.X >= -2.0 or targetInOrPastBridge) then
                local campExit = Vector3.new(0.0, 114.0, (myPos.Z <= -1692.0) and myPos.Z or -1686.0)
                walkTo(campExit, 8, 2.0, abortFn, true, true)
                root, hum = getCharRoot()
                if not root or not hum then return false end
            end
        end
    end

    local deadline   = tick() + timeout
    local stuckTimer = tick()
    local lastPos    = root.Position
    -- "there" is measured on the ground: route points carry the floor height and the root rides 2.4 studs above it, so a
    -- 2-stud 3D check could never pass and the character stepped back and forth on the spot next to the pillar.
    local function reached(goal, dist)
        local d = root.Position - goal
        return d.Magnitude < dist or (math.abs(d.Y) <= 4 and Vector3.new(d.X, 0, d.Z).Magnitude < dist)
    end

    while tick() < deadline do
        if abortFn and abortFn() then
            if hum and root then pcall(function() hum:MoveTo(root.Position) end) end
            return false
        end
        root, hum = getCharRoot()
        if not root or not hum then return false end

        local distToGoal = (root.Position - targetPos).Magnitude
        if reached(targetPos, stopDist) then
            return true
        end

        -- Short distance: Walk straight to target without expensive Pathfinding ComputeAsync
        if distToGoal < 25 then
            _G.GolfHub_WalkingUntil, _G.GolfHub_WalkGoal = tick() + 2.3, targetPos
            if tick() >= (_G.GolfHub_UnstuckUntil or 0) then hum:MoveTo(targetPos) end
            local directStart = tick()
            while tick() - directStart < 2.0 do
                task.wait(0.08)
                if abortFn and abortFn() then return false end
                root, hum = getCharRoot()
                if not root or not hum then break end
                if reached(targetPos, stopDist) then return true end

                local distMoved = (root.Position - lastPos).Magnitude
                if distMoved > 0.4 then
                    stuckTimer = tick()
                    lastPos = root.Position
                elseif tick() - stuckTimer > 1.2 then
                    performUnstuck(root, hum)
                    stuckTimer = tick()
                    break
                end
            end
        else
            local waypoints = computeSubPath(root.Position, targetPos)

            if not waypoints or #waypoints == 0 then
                local dir = (targetPos - root.Position).Unit
                local steered = avoidObstaclesAhead(root, hum, dir)
                _G.GolfHub_WalkingUntil, _G.GolfHub_WalkGoal = tick() + 2.3, targetPos
                -- no path: walk the steered direction, not straight at the goal. The straight line is usually what the
                -- pathfinder could not get through, and walking it pressed the character into the same wall until timeout.
                local flatSteer = Vector3.new(steered.X, 0, steered.Z)
                local stepGoal = targetPos
                if flatSteer.Magnitude > 0.1 then
                    stepGoal = root.Position + flatSteer.Unit * math.min((targetPos - root.Position).Magnitude, 20)
                end
                if tick() >= (_G.GolfHub_UnstuckUntil or 0) then hum:MoveTo(stepGoal) end

                local moveStart = tick()
                while tick() - moveStart < 2.0 do
                    task.wait(0.08)
                    if abortFn and abortFn() then return false end
                    root, hum = getCharRoot()
                    if not root or not hum then break end

                    if reached(targetPos, stopDist) then
                        return true
                    end

                    local distMoved = (root.Position - lastPos).Magnitude
                    if distMoved > 0.4 then
                        stuckTimer = tick()
                        lastPos = root.Position
                    elseif tick() - stuckTimer > 1.2 then
                        performUnstuck(root, hum)
                        stuckTimer = tick()
                        break
                    end
                end
            else
                -- Walk through waypoints smoothly
                for i = 1, #waypoints do
                    if tick() > deadline then break end
                    if abortFn and abortFn() then return false end
                    root, hum = getCharRoot()
                    if not root or not hum then return false end

                    local wp = waypoints[i]
                    local wpPos = wp.Position
                    -- Never allow Roblox Pathfinding waypoints to cut inside Town Red Circle (0, 36) R=27
                    if wpPos.Z > 0 and wpPos.Z < 75 and math.abs(wpPos.X) < 32 then
                        local wdx, wdz = wpPos.X, wpPos.Z - 36.0
                        local wd = math.sqrt(wdx * wdx + wdz * wdz)
                        if wd < 27.0 then
                            local ux = wd > 0.1 and (wdx / wd) or 1.0
                            local uz = wd > 0.1 and (wdz / wd) or 0.0
                            wpPos = Vector3.new(ux * 27.5, wpPos.Y, 36.0 + uz * 27.5) -- just onto the ring: 35 overshot (zigzag, and a step back at the north exit)
                        end
                    end
                    if wp.Action == Enum.PathWaypointAction.Jump then
                        hum.Jump = true
                    end

                    _G.GolfHub_WalkingUntil, _G.GolfHub_WalkGoal = tick() + 2.3, targetPos
                    if tick() >= (_G.GolfHub_UnstuckUntil or 0) then hum:MoveTo(wpPos) end
                    local wpStart = tick()
                    while tick() - wpStart < 2.0 do
                        task.wait(0.08)
                        if abortFn and abortFn() then return false end
                        root, hum = getCharRoot()
                        if not root or not hum then break end

                        if reached(wpPos, 4.0) then break end

                        local distMoved = (root.Position - lastPos).Magnitude
                        if distMoved > 0.3 then
                            stuckTimer = tick()
                            lastPos = root.Position
                        elseif tick() - stuckTimer > 1.0 then
                            performUnstuck(root, hum)
                            stuckTimer = tick()
                            break
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        root = lp.Character and lp.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if root and reached(targetPos, stopDist) then
            return true
        end
        task.wait(0.05)
    end
    return false
end

_G.GolfHub_WalkTo = walkTo

-- Walk sequentially through the 41-point highway to reach target zone (Continuous Sprint, Zero Pauses)
local function walkHighwayToZone(targetZoneId, farmActiveCheck, setStatusRef)
    local lastBotGuard = 0
    local root = lp.Character and lp.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local hum  = lp.Character and lp.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not root or not hum then return end

    -- ถ้าเริ่มเดินจากในเมือง (Town Z > 4) ให้เดินตามเส้นวงกลมดำ (R=33) ออกทางเหนือ X=0 (ไม่เหยียบวงแดง)
    local hwAbort = farmActiveCheck and function() return not farmActiveCheck() end or nil
    if root.Position.Z > 4 and targetZoneId ~= "Town" then
        walkTo(Vector3.new(0.0, 4.2, 3.0), 15, 4.5, hwAbort)
        walkTo(Vector3.new(0.0, 4.2, 0.0), 8, 4.5, hwAbort, true)
        root = lp.Character and lp.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        hum  = lp.Character and lp.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not root or not hum then return end
    elseif root.Position.Z <= 4 and math.abs(root.Position.X) > 3.5 then
        -- เดินขยับเข้าเส้นดำกึ่งกลางถนน (X = 0) ก่อนวิ่งตรงผ่านสะพาน/บันได/ประตู
        walkTo(Vector3.new(0.0, root.Position.Y, root.Position.Z), 8, 2.5, hwAbort, true)
        root = lp.Character and lp.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        hum  = lp.Character and lp.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not root or not hum then return end
    end

    -- Find exact target zone waypoint index (avoid matching Boss05 when targeting Field15)
    local targetIdx = nil
    for i = 1, #ROUTE_WAYPOINTS do
        if ROUTE_WAYPOINTS[i].id == targetZoneId then
            targetIdx = i
            break
        end
    end
    if not targetIdx then
        for i = #ROUTE_WAYPOINTS, 1, -1 do
            if ROUTE_WAYPOINTS[i].targetZone == targetZoneId and not ROUTE_WAYPOINTS[i].isBoss and not ROUTE_WAYPOINTS[i].isBossGate then
                targetIdx = i
                break
            end
        end
    end
    targetIdx = targetIdx or #ROUTE_WAYPOINTS

    local wpTarget = ROUTE_WAYPOINTS[targetIdx]
    local goingBackward = wpTarget and (root.Position.Z < wpTarget.pos.Z - 30)

    local lastPos = Vector3.zero
    local stuckTime = tick()

    if goingBackward then
        -- Find current closest waypoint index
        local startIdx = targetIdx
        local bestDist = 999999
        for i = 1, #ROUTE_WAYPOINTS do
            local d = (root.Position - ROUTE_WAYPOINTS[i].pos).Magnitude
            if d < bestDist then
                bestDist = d
                startIdx = i
            end
        end

        for idx = startIdx, targetIdx, -1 do
            if not farmActiveCheck() then break end
            local wp = ROUTE_WAYPOINTS[idx]
            local char = lp.Character
            root = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            hum  = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if not root or not hum then break end

            local wpStart = tick()
            while tick() - wpStart < 25 do
                if not farmActiveCheck() then break end
                _G.GolfHub_Pillars.unlockOnPass(farmActiveCheck)
                if lp:GetAttribute("AutoOn") == true and (tick() - lastBotGuard) > 1.5 then lastBotGuard = tick() forceStopNativeAutoBot() end
                char = lp.Character
                root = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                hum  = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                if not root or not hum then task.wait(0.1) continue end

                local dX = root.Position.X - wp.pos.X
                local dZ = root.Position.Z - wp.pos.Z
                local dist2D = math.sqrt(dX*dX + dZ*dZ)

                if dist2D < 8.0 or root.Position.Z > wp.pos.Z + 3 then
                    break
                end

                local moved = (root.Position - lastPos).Magnitude
                if moved > 0.4 then
                    stuckTime = tick()
                    lastPos = root.Position
                elseif tick() - stuckTime > 1.2 then
                    if tick() - (_G.GolfHub_WatchdogAt or 0) >= 1 then
                        hum.Jump = true
                        hum:MoveTo(root.Position + Vector3.new(0, 0, 6))
                        task.wait(0.2)
                    end
                    stuckTime = tick()
                end

                _G.GolfHub_WalkingUntil, _G.GolfHub_WalkGoal = tick() + 0.8, wp.pos
                if tick() >= (_G.GolfHub_UnstuckUntil or 0) then hum:MoveTo(wp.pos) end
                task.wait(0.03)
            end
        end
    else
        -- วิ่งทะลุทีละจุดแบบต่อเนื่อง 100% ไม่มีหยุดพักหรือชะลอความเร็ว
        for idx = 1, targetIdx do
            if not farmActiveCheck() then break end
            local wp = ROUTE_WAYPOINTS[idx]
            local char = lp.Character
            root = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            hum  = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if not root or not hum then break end

            -- ข้ามจุดที่อยู่ข้างหลังตัวละครแล้ว (Z เดินไปทางลบ)
            if root.Position.Z < wp.pos.Z - 5 then
                continue
            end

            -- ถ้าเป็นประตูด่านบอส / ห้องบอสที่ยังไม่ปลดล็อค ให้เดินเข้าห้องบอสแล้วหยุดสู้เพื่อปลดโซนถัดไป
            if (wp.isBoss or wp.isBossGate) and wp.bossKey and not isBossCleared(wp.bossKey) then
                if setStatusRef then setStatusRef("⚔ เดินเข้าตีบอสปลดประตู: " .. (wp.bossName or wp.name)) end
                _G.GolfHub_Pillars.unlockOnPass(farmActiveCheck, wp.pos.Z) -- the boss pillar stands just before its gate
                walkTo(wp.pos, 18, 12, function()
                    if not farmActiveCheck() then return true end
                    -- AUTO with a target overrides MoveTo, so it stays off until the boss routine takes over
                    if lp:GetAttribute("AutoOn") == true and (tick() - lastBotGuard) > 0.8 then lastBotGuard = tick() forceStopNativeAutoBot() end
                    return false
                end)
                return "FIGHT_BOSS", wp.bossKey, (wp.bossName or wp.name)
            end

            -- วิ่งพุ่งตรงไปยังจุดแบบ Smooth Sprint (เปลี่ยนเป้าหมา·ี่ 8 studs ก่อนตัวละครเบรก)
            local wpStart = tick()
            while tick() - wpStart < 25 do
                if not farmActiveCheck() then break end
                _G.GolfHub_Pillars.unlockOnPass(farmActiveCheck)
                if lp:GetAttribute("AutoOn") == true and (tick() - lastBotGuard) > 1.5 then lastBotGuard = tick() forceStopNativeAutoBot() end
                char = lp.Character
                root = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                hum  = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                if not root or not hum then task.wait(0.1) continue end

                local dX = root.Position.X - wp.pos.X
                local dZ = root.Position.Z - wp.pos.Z
                local dist2D = math.sqrt(dX*dX + dZ*dZ)

                -- ผ่านจุดหรือเข้าใกล้ 8 studs -> เปลี่ยนจุดถัดไปทันทีโดยไม่เสียความเร็ว
                if dist2D < 8.0 or root.Position.Z < wp.pos.Z - 3 then
                    break
                end

                -- Anti-stuck ตรวจจับการติดขัด
                local moved = (root.Position - lastPos).Magnitude
                if moved > 0.4 then
                    stuckTime = tick()
                    lastPos = root.Position
                elseif tick() - stuckTime > 1.2 then
                    if tick() - (_G.GolfHub_WatchdogAt or 0) >= 1 then
                        hum.Jump = true
                        hum:MoveTo(root.Position + Vector3.new(0, 0, -6))
                        task.wait(0.2)
                    end
                    stuckTime = tick()
                end

                _G.GolfHub_WalkingUntil, _G.GolfHub_WalkGoal = tick() + 0.8, wp.pos
                if tick() >= (_G.GolfHub_UnstuckUntil or 0) then hum:MoveTo(wp.pos) end
                task.wait(0.03)
            end
        end
    end
    return "ARRIVED"
end

-- Walk along highway to any target world position (used for Boss Arenas and custom spots)

task.wait() -- yield point: spread core engine setup over frames
-- ====================================================================
-- WARP PILLAR SYSTEM (FAST TRAVEL VIA OFFICIAL GAME PILLARS)
-- ====================================================================
_G.GolfHub_isWarpingTravel = false

local WARP_PILLAR_DATA = {
    Town = {
        name = "WarpPillar_Town",
        remoteKey = "Town",
        pos = Vector3.new(22.4, 6.05, 71.4),
        standPos = Vector3.new(22.4, 4.25, 68.0),

        altKey = "Field04",

    },
    Boss02 = {
        name = "WarpPillar_Boss02",
        remoteKey = "Boss02",
        altKey = "Field07",
        pos = Vector3.new(18.77, 118.2, -1692.76),
        standPos = Vector3.new(18.77, 114.0, -1689.5),
        unlockCheck = function() return isPillarUnlocked("Boss02") end,
    },
    Boss03 = {
        name = "WarpPillar_Boss03",
        remoteKey = "Boss03",
        altKey = "Field10",
        pos = Vector3.new(18.77, 174.2, -2586.76),
        standPos = Vector3.new(18.77, 170.0, -2583.5),
        unlockCheck = function() return isPillarUnlocked("Boss03") end,
    },
    Boss04 = {
        name = "WarpPillar_Boss04",
        remoteKey = "Boss04",
        altKey = "Field12",
        pos = Vector3.new(18.77, 230.2, -3480.76),

        unlockCheck = function() return isPillarUnlocked("Boss04") end,
    },
    Boss05 = {

        pos = Vector3.new(18.77, 285.8, -4372.76),

}

-- Target Farm Zone -> Closest Unlocked Warp Pillar (Dynamically updated based on unlock status)

-- ค้นหาเสาวาร์ปที่ปลดล็อคแล้วซึ่งอยู่ใกล้จุดหมาย (Zone ID หรือ Vector3) มากที่สุดแบบไดนามิก
-- Level-locked barriers (Map.Barriers.BossLock_FieldNN, "Reach Lv.X to pass") sit across the road at x≈0.
-- Returns true when walking between z1 and z2 would cross a lock the player has not reached yet.
-- The map streams in around the character, so from town the far locks are not loaded and the check saw a clear
-- road (farm at Lv.89 then planned via the Boss04 pillar behind the Lv.92 lock). Known locks (read live in game)
-- are used whenever the real barrier is not loaded.
local KNOWN_LEVEL_LOCKS = {
    { name = "BossLock_Field01", z = -312.8, lv = 9 },
    { name = "BossLock_Field02", z = -568.8, lv = 17 },
    { name = "BossLock_Field03", z = -833.6, lv = 25 },
    { name = "BossLock_Field04", z = -1206.8, lv = 34 },
    { name = "BossLock_Field05", z = -1462.8, lv = 42 },
    { name = "BossLock_Field06", z = -1727.6, lv = 50 },
    { name = "BossLock_Field07", z = -2100.8, lv = 59 },
    { name = "BossLock_Field08", z = -2356.8, lv = 67 },
    { name = "BossLock_Field09", z = -2621.6, lv = 75 },
    { name = "BossLock_Field10", z = -2994.8, lv = 84 },
    { name = "BossLock_Field11", z = -3250.8, lv = 92 },
    { name = "BossLock_Field12", z = -3515.6, lv = 99 },
}

-- ONE rule set for planning, choosing and paying. ctx = { lv, list, gold, cleared, premium } replaces the live values,
-- so every combination can be checked without moving or paying (_G.GolfHub_Pillars).
-- The whole table is always used: from town the far barriers are not streamed in.
local function crossesLevelLock(z1, z2, ctx)
    local myLv = ctx and ctx.lv or tonumber(lp and lp:GetAttribute("Level")) or 0
    for _, k in ipairs(KNOWN_LEVEL_LOCKS) do
        if k.lv > myLv and ((z1 > k.z) ~= (z2 > k.z)) then return true end
    end
    if ctx then return false end
    local map = workspace:FindFirstChild("Map")
    local barriers = map and map:FindFirstChild("Barriers")
    if not barriers then return false end
    for _, b in ipairs(barriers:GetChildren()) do
        if b.Name:sub(1, 9) == "BossLock_" then
            local req = tonumber(tostring(b:GetAttribute("LockText") or ""):match("Lv%.?%s*(%d+)"))
            if req and req > myLv then
                local part = b:IsA("BasePart") and b or b:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)
                if part then
                    local lz = part.Position.Z
                    if (z1 > lz) ~= (z2 > lz) then return true end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return false
end

_G.GolfHub_Pillars = { paidFail = {}, tried = {} }
do
    local P = _G.GolfHub_Pillars
    local ORDER = { "Town", "Boss01", "Boss02", "Boss03", "Boss04", "Boss05" }
    local DOOR_Z = { Boss01 = -961, Boss02 = -1855, Boss03 = -2754, Boss04 = -3648 }

    -- walking from z1 to z2 is stopped by a level lock or by a boss door that is still closed
    function P.blocked(z1, z2, ctx)
        if crossesLevelLock(z1, z2, ctx) then return true end
        for key, z in pairs(DOOR_Z) do
            if (z1 > z) ~= (z2 > z) then
                local open
                if ctx and ctx.cleared then open = ctx.cleared[key] == true else open = isBossCleared(key) end
                if not open then return true end
            end
        end
        return false
    end

    -- the same rule as the game's WarpPillarClient
    function P.cost(key, ctx)
        local free
        if ctx then free = ctx.premium == true
        else free = lp:GetAttribute("Premium") == true or lp:GetAttribute("NewbieHatOn") == true end
        return free and 0 or (tonumber(key:match("(%d+)$")) or 0) * 1000
    end

    -- may we pay for a warp to this pillar? false + reason when not
    function P.canWarp(key, ctx)
        local data = WARP_PILLAR_DATA[key]
        if not data then return false, "unknown" end
        if key == "Town" then return true end
        if not isPillarUnlocked(key, ctx and ctx.list) then return false, "locked" end
        -- the game takes the Gold, then puts the character back in front of the gate
        if crossesLevelLock(0, data.pos.Z, ctx) then return false, "level" end
        local gold
        if ctx then gold = ctx.gold else gold = tonumber(lp:GetAttribute("Silver")) end
        if gold and gold < P.cost(key, ctx) then return false, "gold" end
        if not ctx and tick() < (P.paidFail[key] or 0) then return false, "paid" end
        return true
    end

    -- the pillar to walk to
    function P.start(pos, destKey, ctx)
        if pos.Z > -25 then return "Town", (WARP_PILLAR_DATA.Town.pos - pos).Magnitude end
        local best, bestDist = nil, math.huge
        for _, key in ipairs(ORDER) do
            -- town counts as a start unless it is the destination (walking there would be the whole trip)
            if (key ~= "Town" or destKey ~= "Town") and isPillarUnlocked(key, ctx and ctx.list) then
                local p = WARP_PILLAR_DATA[key].pos
                local d = (p - pos).Magnitude
                if d < bestDist and not P.blocked(pos.Z, p.Z, ctx) then best, bestDist = key, d end
            end
        end
        return best, bestDist
    end

    -- the pillar to land on: one we may pay for, with no level lock left before the target
    function P.dest(targetPos, ctx)
        local best, bestDist = "Town", (WARP_PILLAR_DATA.Town.pos - targetPos).Magnitude
        for _, key in ipairs(ORDER) do
            local p = WARP_PILLAR_DATA[key].pos
            local d = (p - targetPos).Magnitude
            if d < bestDist and P.canWarp(key, ctx) and not crossesLevelLock(p.Z, targetPos.Z, ctx) then best, bestDist = key, d end
        end
        return best, bestDist
    end

    function P.plan(pos, targetPos, ctx)
        local destKey, destDist = P.dest(targetPos, ctx)
        local startKey, startDist = P.start(pos, destKey, ctx)
        local use = startKey ~= nil and startKey ~= destKey and (startDist + destDist + 60) < (pos - targetPos).Magnitude
        return use, startKey, destKey, startDist, destDist
    end

    -- a pillar unlocks for free just by standing next to it, but it is 19 studs off the road: step over when passing.
    -- toZ = also one that lies between here and toZ
    function P.unlockOnPass(activeCheck, toZ)
        if tick() - (P.lastPassCheck or 0) < 0.5 then return end
        P.lastPassCheck = tick()
        local root = getCharRoot()
        if not root or type(lp:GetAttribute("WarpPillars")) ~= "string" then return end
        local z = root.Position.Z
        for _, key in ipairs(ORDER) do
            local data = WARP_PILLAR_DATA[key]
            local pz = data.pos.Z
            local passing = math.abs(z - pz) <= 14 or (toZ ~= nil and pz < math.max(z, toZ) and pz > math.min(z, toZ))
            if passing and key ~= "Town" and tick() >= (P.tried[key] or 0) and not isPillarUnlocked(key) and not P.blocked(z, pz) then
                P.tried[key] = tick() + 90
                local function stop()
                    if activeCheck and not activeCheck() then return true end
                    if lp:GetAttribute("AutoOn") == true then forceStopNativeAutoBot() end
                    return isPillarUnlocked(key)
                end
                local y = data.standPos.Y
                P.walk = _G.GolfHub_WalkTo
                P.walk(Vector3.new(0, y, data.standPos.Z), math.max(6, math.abs(z - pz) / 14 + 4), 6, stop, true)
                P.walk(data.standPos, 7, 2.5, stop, true)
                local t0 = tick()
                while tick() - t0 < 1.5 and not stop() do task.wait(0.1) end
                P.walk(Vector3.new(0, y, data.standPos.Z), 6, 4, function() return activeCheck ~= nil and not activeCheck() end, true)
            end
        end
    end
end

local function getBestPillarForZone(targetZoneOrPos)
    local targetPos = nil
    if typeof(targetZoneOrPos) == "Vector3" then
        targetPos = targetZoneOrPos
    elseif type(targetZoneOrPos) == "string" then
        if WARP_PILLAR_DATA[targetZoneOrPos] then
            return targetZoneOrPos, 0
        end
        -- the field's own centre first (the planner uses that), only then any point leading to it
        for _, wp in ipairs(ROUTE_WAYPOINTS) do
            if wp.id == targetZoneOrPos then targetPos = wp.pos break end
        end
        if not targetPos then
            for _, wp in ipairs(ROUTE_WAYPOINTS) do
                if wp.targetZone == targetZoneOrPos then targetPos = wp.pos break end
            end
        end
    end

    if not targetPos then return "Town", 999999 end
    return _G.GolfHub_Pillars.dest(targetPos)
end

local function getWarpPillarObject(pillarName)
    local map = workspace:FindFirstChild("Map")
    local wpFolder = map and map:FindFirstChild("WarpPillars")
    if wpFolder then
        local p = wpFolder:FindFirstChild(pillarName)
        if p then return p end
    end
    return workspace:FindFirstChild(pillarName, true)
end

local function getPillarPosition(pillarKey)
    local pData = WARP_PILLAR_DATA[pillarKey]
    if not pData then return nil, nil end
    local pObj = getWarpPillarObject(pData.name)
    local standPos = pData.standPos or pData.pos
    return standPos, pObj, pData.pos
end

-- Map destination pillar keys to HuntWarpPillar button row index (1 to 6)
local PILLAR_KEY_TO_INDEX = {
    Town   = 1, -- Village (Free)
    Boss01 = 2, -- Forest · Gorilla King (1,000)
    Boss02 = 3, -- Dry Forest · Whitefang Sabertooth (2,000)
    Boss03 = 4, -- Desert · Obsidian Pharaoh (3,000)
    Boss04 = 5, -- Snow · Tempest Dragon King (4,000)
    Boss05 = 6, -- Volcano · Volcano Turtle King (5,000)
}

-- Destination buttons in the HuntWarpPillar window (game order: Town, Boss01..Boss05)
local function getWarpPillarButtons(warpGui)
    local buttons = {}
    for _, d in ipairs(warpGui:GetDescendants()) do
        if d.Name == "KitButton" and d:IsA("GuiButton") then
            buttons[#buttons + 1] = d
        end
    end
    return buttons
end

-- The game's MouseButton1Click handler (WarpPillarClient) keeps the destination key as its first upvalue ("Town", "Boss01"..)
local function readWarpButtonKey(btn)
    if not (getconnections and debug and debug.getupvalues) then return nil end
    local ok, conns = pcall(getconnections, btn.MouseButton1Click)
    if not ok or type(conns) ~= "table" then return nil end
    for _, conn in ipairs(conns) do
        local fn = conn.Function
        if fn then
            local okUp, ups = pcall(debug.getupvalues, fn)
            if okUp and type(ups) == "table" and type(ups[1]) == "string" then
                return ups[1]
            end
        end
    end
    return nil
end

local function pickWarpButton(buttons, destKey, fallbackIdx)
    for _, b in ipairs(buttons) do
        if readWarpButtonKey(b) == destKey then return b end
    end
    return buttons[fallbackIdx]
end

local function triggerWarpPillar(pillarObj, targetDestKey)
    local pData = WARP_PILLAR_DATA[targetDestKey]
    local remKey = (pData and pData.remoteKey) or targetDestKey
    local targetIdx = PILLAR_KEY_TO_INDEX[targetDestKey] or 1

    local root, hum = getCharRoot()
    if not root then return false end

    -- 1. ค้นหาชิ้นส่วนเสาและ ProximityPrompt
    local prompt = nil
    local pCol = nil
    if pillarObj then
        pCol = pillarObj:FindFirstChild("Column") or pillarObj:FindFirstChild("Crystal") or pillarObj:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart")
        prompt = pillarObj:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("ProximityPrompt", true)
    end
    if not prompt or not pCol then
        local map = workspace:FindFirstChild("Map")
        local wpFolder = map and map:FindFirstChild("WarpPillars")
        if wpFolder then
            local bestD = 999999
            for _, p in ipairs(wpFolder:GetChildren()) do
                local pp = p:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("ProximityPrompt", true)
                local col = p:FindFirstChild("Column") or (pp and pp.Parent)
                if pp and col and col:IsA("BasePart") then
                    local d = (root.Position - col.Position).Magnitude
                    if d < bestD then
                        bestD = d
                        prompt = pp
                        pCol = col
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end

    if not prompt or not pCol then return false end

    local distToCol = (root.Position - pCol.Position).Magnitude
    if distToCol > 60.0 then return false end

    -- ก้าวเท้าแนบชิดเสา (<= 4.5 studs) ก่อนยิง ProximityPrompt เพื่อการันตีอยู่ใน MaxActivationDistance (12 studs)
    if hum and distToCol > 4.5 then
        local walkDeadline = tick() + 3.0
        while tick() < walkDeadline do
            local cr, ch = getCharRoot()
            if not cr or not ch then break end
            local d = (cr.Position - pCol.Position).Magnitude
            if d <= 4.5 then break end
            ch:MoveTo(pCol.Position)
            task.wait(0.05)
        end
    end

    -- หยุดตัวละครให้นิ่งสนิท เพื่อป้องกันไม่ให้เซิร์ฟเวอร์ปฏิเสธ
    root, hum = getCharRoot()
    if hum and root then
        pcall(function() hum:MoveTo(root.Position) end)
    end

    -- 2. Use the game's own pillar flow: ProximityPrompt -> HuntWarpPillar window opens -> click the destination button (the game fires PillarWarp itself)
    local pg = lp:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
    local warpGui = pg and pg:FindFirstChild("HuntWarpPillar")
    local clicked = false

    for _ = 1, 2 do
        if not (prompt and prompt.Enabled) then break end
        pcall(function()
            if fireproximityprompt then
                fireproximityprompt(prompt)
            else
                triggerPromptSafe(prompt)
            end
        end)

        local openDeadline = tick() + 2.5
        local buttons = {}
        while tick() < openDeadline do
            warpGui = warpGui or (pg and pg:FindFirstChild("HuntWarpPillar"))
            if warpGui and warpGui.Enabled then
                buttons = getWarpPillarButtons(warpGui)
                if #buttons >= targetIdx then break end
            end
            task.wait(0.1)
        end

        local targetBtn = (#buttons > 0) and pickWarpButton(buttons, remKey, targetIdx) or nil
        if targetBtn and firesignal then
            task.wait(0.15)
            clicked = pcall(firesignal, targetBtn.MouseButton1Click)
            if clicked then break end
        end
    end

    -- 3. Fallback only: window never opened or the click failed -> fire PillarWarp with the same key the game's button uses
    if not clicked then
        local Remotes = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("Remotes")
        local pWarp = Remotes and Remotes:FindFirstChild("PillarWarp")
        if pWarp then
            pcall(function() pWarp:FireServer(remKey) end)
        end
    end

    task.wait(0.3)
    pcall(function()
        local gui = pg and pg:FindFirstChild("HuntWarpPillar")
        if gui then gui.Enabled = false end
    end)
    return true
end

-- ====================================================================
-- FAST HOME WARP HELPER (Game Remotes.HomeWarp)
-- ====================================================================
local function castHomeWarp(dest)
    if game.PlaceId == DUNGEON_PLACE_ID or (isPlayerInDungeon and isPlayerInDungeon()) then
        return false
    end
    local Remotes = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("Remotes")
    local hw = Remotes and Remotes:FindFirstChild("HomeWarp")
    if not hw then return false end

    local readyAt = lp:GetAttribute("HomeWarpReadyAt")
    local serverTime = workspace:GetServerTimeNow()
    if readyAt and type(readyAt) == "number" and (readyAt - serverTime) > 0 then
        return false
    end

    -- หยุดตัวละครให้นิ่งสนิท เพื่อป้องกันไม่ให้เซิร์ฟเวอร์ยกเลิกการร่าย HomeWarp (5 วิ)
    local rootStop, humStop = getCharRoot()
    if humStop and rootStop then
        pcall(function() humStop:MoveTo(rootStop.Position) end)
    end

    if forceStopNativeAutoBot then
        forceStopNativeAutoBot()
    else
        pcall(function()
            local curTgt = lp:FindFirstChild("CurrentTarget")
            if curTgt and curTgt:IsA("ObjectValue") then curTgt.Value = nil end
            if lp:GetAttribute("AutoOn") == true and Remotes and Remotes:FindFirstChild("BotAutoState") then
                Remotes.BotAutoState:FireServer(false)
            end
        end)
    end

    if dest then
        hw:FireServer("start", dest)
    else
        hw:FireServer("start")
    end

    local waitStart = tick()
    while tick() - waitStart < 6.5 do
        task.wait(0.3)
        -- a target picked up during the cast makes the game walk to it, and moving cancels the HomeWarp cast
        pcall(function()
            local ct = lp:FindFirstChild("CurrentTarget")
            if ct and ct.Value ~= nil then
                ct.Value = nil
                local R = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("Remotes")
                if R and R:FindFirstChild("SetTarget") then R.SetTarget:FireServer(nil, false) end
            end
        end)
        local curZ = getCurrentActualZone()
        if curZ == "Town" then
            task.wait(0.5)
            return true
        end
    end

    return getCurrentActualZone() == "Town"
end

-- ====================================================================
-- TOWN NAVIGATION & BLACK-RING ROUTING (Strictly avoids Red Circle R=17.5!)
-- Clear Middle-Road Ring: R = 27.0 (ช่องตรงกลางระหว่างซุ้มกิจกรรมและบ้านเรือน)
-- ====================================================================
TOWN_COORDS = {
    Center            = Vector3.new(0.0, 4.25, 37.15),
    BarnabyShop       = Vector3.new(-50.4, 4.7, 30.5),
    BarnabySouthJunc  = Vector3.new(-35.0, 4.3, 56.0),
    SouthAlleyWest    = Vector3.new(-18.0, 4.3, 64.0),
    SouthAlleyCenter  = Vector3.new(0.0, 4.3, 65.0),
    SouthAlleyEast    = Vector3.new(18.0, 4.3, 64.0),
    TownPillarStand   = Vector3.new(22.4, 4.25, 66.0),
    TownPillarPos     = Vector3.new(22.4, 6.05, 71.4),
    TownEastAvenue    = Vector3.new(27.0, 4.3, 37.15),
    TownWestAvenue    = Vector3.new(-27.0, 4.3, 37.15),
    TownNorthRing     = Vector3.new(0.0, 4.25, 10.0),
    TownExit          = Vector3.new(0.0, 4.25, 0.0),
}

-- ตรวจสอบและปลดล็อคตัวละครหากอยู่ภายในวงแดงกลางเมือง (0, 37.15) R=17.5 -> ดันออกสู่ช่องตรงกลาง R=27.0 ทันที
local function escapeFestivalBoothIfStuck()
    local root, hum = getCharRoot()
    if not root or not hum then return end
    local pos = root.Position
    local dx, dz = pos.X, pos.Z - 37.15
    local dist2D = math.sqrt(dx * dx + dz * dz)
    if dist2D < 17.5 then
        local ux = dist2D > 0.1 and (dx / dist2D) or 0.0
        local uz = dist2D > 0.1 and (dz / dist2D) or 1.0
        local ringExit = Vector3.new(ux * 27.0, 4.25, 37.15 + uz * 27.0)
        hum.Jump = true
        task.wait(0.08)
        hum:MoveTo(ringExit)
        local escStart = tick()
        while tick() - escStart < 2.5 do
            task.wait(0.05)
            root, hum = getCharRoot()
            if not root or not hum then break end
            local cdx, cdz = root.Position.X, root.Position.Z - 37.15
            if math.sqrt(cdx * cdx + cdz * cdz) >= 22.0 then break end
            hum.Jump = true
            hum:MoveTo(ringExit)
        end
    end
end

-- Walk a fixed list of town waypoints without backtracking: start at the waypoint that gives the shortest total trip
-- (distance to it + the rest of the list), and never start a leg that cuts through the festival booth's red no-go circle
-- (centre (0, 37.15), R 17.5, plus a 3 stud margin). From the pillar this is still the south-alley road; from the town
-- spawn in the north it goes straight to the shop instead of first walking away to the east end of the alley.
local function townSegmentClear(a, b)
    local abx, abz = b.X - a.X, b.Z - a.Z
    local lenSq = abx * abx + abz * abz
    local t = 0
    if lenSq > 1e-6 then
        t = math.clamp(((0 - a.X) * abx + (37.15 - a.Z) * abz) / lenSq, 0, 1)
    end
    local px, pz = a.X + abx * t, a.Z + abz * t
    return math.sqrt(px * px + (pz - 37.15) * (pz - 37.15)) >= 20.5
end

local function walkTownWaypoints(waypoints, finishDist)
    local root = getCharRoot()
    if not root then return false end
    local rest = {}
    rest[#waypoints] = 0
    for i = #waypoints - 1, 1, -1 do
        rest[i] = rest[i + 1] + (waypoints[i] - waypoints[i + 1]).Magnitude
    end
    local startAt, bestCost = 1, math.huge
    for i = 1, #waypoints do
        local cost = (root.Position - waypoints[i]).Magnitude + rest[i]
        if i > 1 and not townSegmentClear(root.Position, waypoints[i]) then cost = cost + 1e6 end
        if cost < bestCost then startAt, bestCost = i, cost end
    end
    for i = startAt, #waypoints do
        local r, h = getCharRoot()
        if not r or not h then break end
        if (r.Position - waypoints[i]).Magnitude > 5 then
            walkTo(waypoints[i], 15, 5, nil, true)
        end
    end
    local r = getCharRoot()
    return r and (r.Position - waypoints[#waypoints]).Magnitude <= (finishDist or 8)
end

_G.GolfHub_SellMark = function(stage)
    _G.GolfHub_SellStage = stage
    _G.GolfHub_SellStageAt = tick()
end

-- เดินจากเสาวาร์ปเมืองไปยังร้าน Barnaby (ไม่เข้าวงแดง, ไม่เดินย้อน)
walkFromTownPillarToBarnaby = function()
    escapeFestivalBoothIfStuck()
    return walkTownWaypoints({
        TOWN_COORDS.SouthAlleyEast,
        TOWN_COORDS.SouthAlleyCenter,
        TOWN_COORDS.SouthAlleyWest,
        TOWN_COORDS.BarnabySouthJunc,
        TOWN_COORDS.BarnabyShop,
    }, 8)
end

-- เดินจากร้าน Barnaby ไปยังเสาวาร์ปเมือง (ไม่เข้าวงแดง, ไม่เดินย้อน)
walkFromBarnabyToTownPillar = function()
    escapeFestivalBoothIfStuck()
    return walkTownWaypoints({
        TOWN_COORDS.BarnabySouthJunc,
        TOWN_COORDS.SouthAlleyWest,
        TOWN_COORDS.SouthAlleyCenter,
        TOWN_COORDS.SouthAlleyEast,
        TOWN_COORDS.TownPillarStand,
    }, 10)
end

-- เดินอ้อมช่องตรงกลาง (R=27) ในเมือง — ห้ามเข้าวงแดง (0, 37.15) R=17.5 เด็ดขาด!
walkAroundFestivalBooth = function(goalPos, timeout)
    local root, hum = getCharRoot()
    if not root or not hum then return false end

    escapeFestivalBoothIfStuck()
    root = getCharRoot()
    if not root then return false end

    -- กรณีเป้าหมายคือร้าน Barnaby
    if (goalPos - TOWN_COORDS.BarnabyShop).Magnitude < 15 then
        return walkFromTownPillarToBarnaby()
    end
    -- กรณีเป้าหมายคือเสาวาร์ปเมือง
    if (goalPos - TOWN_COORDS.TownPillarStand).Magnitude < 15 or (goalPos - TOWN_COORDS.TownPillarPos).Magnitude < 15 then
        return walkTo(TOWN_COORDS.TownPillarStand, timeout or 20, 6, nil, true)
    end
    -- กรณีเป้าหมายคือทางออกเมือง (TownExit X=0, Z=0) -> เดินอ้อมวงกลมดำไปจุดเหนือสุด (0, 10) แล้วออกตรงกลางสะพาน X=0
    if (goalPos - TOWN_COORDS.TownExit).Magnitude < 22 then
        walkTo(TOWN_COORDS.TownNorthRing, timeout or 18, 4.5, nil, true)
        return walkTo(TOWN_COORDS.TownExit, 10, 4.5, nil, true)
    end

    return walkTo(goalPos, timeout or 25, 6)
end

local function warpViaPillar(targetDestKey, setStatusRef, activeCheck)
    local targetData = WARP_PILLAR_DATA[targetDestKey]
    if not targetData then return false end

    local root, hum = getCharRoot()
    if not root or not hum then return false end

    local myPos = root.Position
    local destPos = targetData.pos
    local bDefTarget = BOSS_TARGET_DEFS and BOSS_TARGET_DEFS[targetDestKey]
    local distToArena = bDefTarget and (myPos - bDefTarget.arenaPos).Magnitude or 999999
    local distToDestPillar = (myPos - destPos).Magnitude

    -- ตรวจสอบว่าตัวละครอยู่ในโซนเป้าหมายอยู่แล้วหรือไม่:
    if targetDestKey == "Town" then
        if myPos.Z > -25 or distToDestPillar <= 50 then return true end
    else
        -- ถ้าอยู่ใกล้เสาเป้าหมาย (<= 180 studs) หรืออยู่ในโซนห้องบอส (<= 250 studs)
        if distToDestPillar <= 180 or distToArena <= 250 then
            return true
        end
    end

    -- every caller goes through the same check before anything is paid
    local allowed, why = _G.GolfHub_Pillars.canWarp(targetDestKey)
    if not allowed then
        local WHY = { locked = "ยังไม่ปลดเสา", level = "เลเวลยังไม่ถึงด่านหน้าเสา", gold = "Gold ไม่พอ", paid = "เพิ่งจ่ายแล้ววาร์ปไม่ถึง พัก 5 นาที" }
        if setStatusRef then setStatusRef("🔒 ไม่วาร์ปไป " .. targetDestKey .. ": " .. (WHY[why] or tostring(why))) end
        task.wait(0.3)
        return false
    end

    local nearestKey, nearestDist = _G.GolfHub_Pillars.start(myPos, targetDestKey)
    local nearestStandPos, nearestObj
    if nearestKey then
        nearestStandPos, nearestObj = getPillarPosition(nearestKey)
        nearestDist = (myPos - nearestStandPos).Magnitude
    end

    if not nearestKey or not nearestStandPos then return false end

    -- Right after a server join the character stands at the town spawn for a moment before the game moves it to
    -- where it was. A Town choice made then is stale: if we are no longer in town, start over from the real position.
    if nearestKey == "Town" and myPos.Z > -25 then -- only a choice made while standing in town can be stale
        task.wait(1.5)
        local r2 = getCharRoot()
        if r2 and r2.Position.Z <= -25 then
            return warpViaPillar(targetDestKey, setStatusRef, activeCheck)
        end
    end

    -- ถ้าเสาที่ใกล้ที่สุดคือเสาเป้าหมาย และตัวละครอยู่ใกล้แล้ว ถือว่าถึงแล้ว
    if nearestKey == targetDestKey and nearestDist <= 180.0 then
        return true
    end

    -- ปิดบอท combat ชั่วคราว และเปิดแฟล็ก isWarpingTravel เพื่อเดินเข้าเสาวาร์ปอย่างอิสระ
    _G.GolfHub_isWarpingTravel = true
    _G.GolfHub_warpStartedAt = tick() -- readers ignore the flag after 45s, so a cancelled thread cannot leave it stuck
    setBotRunning(false)
    -- Arrival means "inside the pillar prompt's activation range (minus a margin)", not "standing on the stand point":
    -- the stand point Y is a few studs off on the raised pillar bases, so a 3D stop distance there never triggers and
    -- the character kept stepping back and forth in front of the pillar until the walk timed out.
    local function isInPromptRange()
        local r = getCharRoot()
        if not r then return false end
        local prompt = nearestObj and nearestObj:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("ProximityPrompt", true)
        local promptPart = prompt and prompt.Parent
        local promptPos = (promptPart and promptPart:IsA("BasePart") and promptPart.Position)
            or (WARP_PILLAR_DATA[nearestKey] and WARP_PILLAR_DATA[nearestKey].pos)
        if not promptPos then return false end
        local limit = math.max(4.0, ((prompt and prompt.MaxActivationDistance) or 12) - 4.0)
        return (r.Position - promptPos).Magnitude <= limit
    end
    local lastAutoStop = 0
    local abortFn = function()
        if activeCheck and not activeCheck() then return true end
        _G.GolfHub_warpStartedAt = tick() -- readers drop the flag after 45 s and a walk to a far pillar takes longer
        -- AUTO with a target overrides MoveTo and dragged the character around the field on the way to the pillar
        if lp:GetAttribute("AutoOn") == true and tick() - lastAutoStop > 0.8 then
            lastAutoStop = tick()
            forceStopNativeAutoBot()
        end
        -- With AUTO already off, a monster that hits us still becomes CurrentTarget (the game sets it on its own) and
        -- the game's chase drags us to it: seen live on Pahtzx, Auto Farm Dungeon walking to the Boss01 pillar,
        -- Horned / Wild Rabbits in Field01 kept re-targeted, 14 studs moved in 10 s. Drop that target while travelling.
        -- Only the target is cleared here (no hum:Move), so the walk's MoveTo keeps going.
        local ctv = lp:FindFirstChild("CurrentTarget")
        if ctv and ctv:IsA("ObjectValue") and ctv.Value ~= nil and tick() - (_G.GolfHub_TravelTgtClearAt or 0) > 0.3 then
            _G.GolfHub_TravelTgtClearAt = tick()
            pcall(function()
                local R = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("Remotes")
                if R and R:FindFirstChild("SetTarget") then R.SetTarget:FireServer(nil, false) end
                ctv.Value = nil
            end)
        end
        local r = getCharRoot()
        if r and (r.Position - destPos).Magnitude <= 180 and targetDestKey ~= "Town" then return true end -- walked there already
        return isInPromptRange()
    end
    local bName = bDefTarget and bDefTarget.name or targetDestKey

    -- การเดินเข้าหาเสาวาร์ป:
    if nearestKey == "Town" and root.Position.Z > -25 then
        if setStatusRef then
            setStatusRef(string.format("⚡ อยู่ในเมือง -> เดินเข้าเสาวาร์ปเมือง เพื่อวาร์ปไป %s...", bName))
        end
        local curPos = root.Position
        local pillarStand = nearestStandPos or TOWN_COORDS.TownPillarStand or Vector3.new(22.4, 4.25, 68.0)
        local distToPillar = (curPos - pillarStand).Magnitude
        if distToPillar > 4.5 then
            walkTo(pillarStand, 15, 2.5, abortFn, true)
        end
    else
        -- อยู่นอกเมือง: เดินตรงเข้าหาเสาวาร์ปที่ใกล้ที่สุด เพื่อวาร์ปตรงสู่เป้าหมาย (ทุกเสาสามารถวาร์ปข้ามไปเสาอื่นได้ 100% ห้ามกลับเมือง)
        if setStatusRef then
            setStatusRef(string.format("⚡ เดินเข้าเสาวาร์ป %s (ระยะ %.0f studs) เพื่อวาร์ปไป %s...", nearestKey, nearestDist, bName))
        end
        if nearestDist > 4.5 then
            -- A far pillar is reached by the road, leg by leg: a straight line runs into the cliffs between fields, and
            -- pathfinding does not answer over 800 studs.
            if nearestDist > 120 or nearestKey == "Town" then
                -- town: the road ends at the town exit, from there round the festival booth
                local fromZ, toZ = root.Position.Z, (nearestKey == "Town") and 1 or nearestStandPos.Z
                local legs = {}
                for _, wp in ipairs(ROUTE_WAYPOINTS) do
                    if wp.pos.Z > math.min(fromZ, toZ) and wp.pos.Z < math.max(fromZ, toZ) then legs[#legs + 1] = wp.pos end
                end
                table.sort(legs, function(a, b) return math.abs(a.Z - fromZ) < math.abs(b.Z - fromZ) end)
                -- onto the middle of the road first: the ramps between fields are only open around X = 0
                if math.abs(root.Position.X) > 4 then table.insert(legs, 1, Vector3.new(0, root.Position.Y, fromZ)) end
                for _, leg in ipairs(legs) do
                    if abortFn() then break end
                    local r = getCharRoot()
                    if not r then break end
                    -- passing a road point counts on the map only: on the ramps its height is several studs off
                    local function atLeg()
                        local n = getCharRoot()
                        return n ~= nil and Vector3.new(n.Position.X - leg.X, 0, n.Position.Z - leg.Z).Magnitude < 8
                    end
                    local arrived = walkTo(leg, math.max(8, (r.Position - leg).Magnitude / 14 + 6), 7, function()
                        return abortFn() or atLeg()
                    end, true)
                    if not arrived and not atLeg() then break end
                end
            end
            if nearestKey == "Town" then
                walkTo(nearestStandPos, 30, 2.5, abortFn) -- with the town ring guard
            else
                walkTo(nearestStandPos, 20, 2.5, abortFn, true)
            end
        end
    end

    if activeCheck and not activeCheck() then
        _G.GolfHub_isWarpingTravel = false
        return false
    end

    root, hum = getCharRoot()
    if not root then
        _G.GolfHub_isWarpingTravel = false
        return false
    end

    if targetDestKey ~= "Town" and (root.Position - destPos).Magnitude <= 180 then
        _G.GolfHub_isWarpingTravel = false
        return true
    end
    -- never press (and never wait 4.5 s for a warp) away from the pillar
    local pillarData = WARP_PILLAR_DATA[nearestKey]
    if pillarData and Vector3.new(root.Position.X - pillarData.pos.X, 0, root.Position.Z - pillarData.pos.Z).Magnitude > 30 then
        _G.GolfHub_isWarpingTravel = false
        return false
    end

    -- Refresh pillar object
    local _, refreshedObj = getPillarPosition(nearestKey)
    if refreshedObj then nearestObj = refreshedObj end

    if setStatusRef then
        setStatusRef(string.format("🌀 กำลังวาร์ปผ่านเสา %s -> %s...", nearestKey, bName))
    end

    -- Trigger warp (ProximityPrompt + UI + Remotes)
    local goldBefore = tonumber(lp:GetAttribute("Silver"))
    triggerWarpPillar(nearestObj, targetDestKey)

    -- Wait until arrived at destination pillar (timeout 4.5s)
    local waitStart = tick()
    while tick() - waitStart < 4.5 do
        if activeCheck and not activeCheck() then
            _G.GolfHub_isWarpingTravel = false
            return false
        end
        task.wait(0.15)
        root = getCharRoot()
        if root then
            local distToDest = (root.Position - destPos).Magnitude
            if distToDest <= 180 or (bDefTarget and (root.Position - bDefTarget.arenaPos).Magnitude <= 250) then
                -- the last walk order still points at the pillar we left: drop it, or the character walks back toward it
                _G.GolfHub_LandedAt = tick()
                local _, landedHum = getCharRoot()
                if landedHum then landedHum:MoveTo(root.Position) end
                if setStatusRef then setStatusRef(string.format("✅ ถึงเสาวาร์ป %s เรียบร้อย!", bName)) end
                pcall(function()
                    local pg = lp:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
                    local warpGui = pg and pg:FindFirstChild("HuntWarpPillar")
                    if warpGui then warpGui.Enabled = false end
                end)
                _G.GolfHub_isWarpingTravel = false
                task.wait(0.2)
                return true
            end
        end
    end

    _G.GolfHub_isWarpingTravel = false
    root = getCharRoot()
    local finalDist = root and (root.Position - destPos).Magnitude or 999999
    local finalArenaDist = (root and bDefTarget) and (root.Position - bDefTarget.arenaPos).Magnitude or 999999
    if finalDist <= 180 or finalArenaDist <= 250 then return true end
    -- paid but not there: never pay for the same pillar again straight away (callers retry 3-5 times)
    local goldNow, price = tonumber(lp:GetAttribute("Silver")), _G.GolfHub_Pillars.cost(targetDestKey)
    if goldBefore and goldNow and price > 0 and goldBefore - goldNow >= price / 2 then
        _G.GolfHub_Pillars.paidFail[targetDestKey] = tick() + 300
    end
    return false
end

-- Debug handle so the pillar flow can be driven from outside the UI (executor console / MCP)
if getgenv then
    getgenv().GolfHub_Debug = { warpViaPillar = warpViaPillar, triggerWarpPillar = triggerWarpPillar, walkTo = walkTo }
end

local function travelToZoneSmart(targetZoneId, farmActiveCheck, setStatusRef, allowPillarWarp)
    local root, hum = getCharRoot()
    if not root then return "FAILED" end

    -- Check if character is dead, respawn in town first
    if hum and hum.Health <= 0 then
        local rem = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("Remotes")
        if rem and rem:FindFirstChild("RespawnChoice") then
            rem.RespawnChoice:FireServer("town")
            task.wait(2.0)
        end
        -- the old root is the corpse in the field: plan from the new character in town, or the pillar plan
        -- below sees "already near the zone" and the trip is walked
        local t0 = tick()
        repeat
            root, hum = getCharRoot()
            if root and hum and hum.Health > 0 then break end
            task.wait(0.3)
        until tick() - t0 > 8
        if not root then return "FAILED" end
    end

    -- ตรวจสอบว่าอยู่ในโซนเป้าหมายอยู่แล้วหรือไม่
    local curZone = getCurrentActualZone()
    if curZone == targetZoneId then
        return "ARRIVED"
    end

    -- ตรวจสอบจุดศูนย์กลางของโซนเป้าหมายใน ROUTE_WAYPOINTS
    local targetWpPos = nil
    for _, wp in ipairs(ROUTE_WAYPOINTS) do
        if wp.id == targetZoneId then
            targetWpPos = wp.pos
            break
        end
    end
    if not targetWpPos then
        for _, wp in ipairs(ROUTE_WAYPOINTS) do
            if wp.targetZone == targetZoneId and not wp.isBoss and not wp.isBossGate then
                targetWpPos = wp.pos
                break
            end
        end
    end

    -- ใช้เสาวาร์ปของเกม (Warp Pillar) อัตโนมัติเมื่อช่วยให้ถึงจุดฟาร์มเร็วกว่าการเดินเท้าตรง (ห้าม CFrame Teleport)
    if allowPillarWarp ~= false and targetWpPos then
        local myPos = root.Position
        local usePillar, startKey, destKey = _G.GolfHub_Pillars.plan(myPos, targetWpPos)
        if usePillar then
            if setStatusRef then
                setStatusRef(string.format("🌀 ใช้เสาวาร์ป (%s -> %s) ไปโซนฟาร์มให้เร็วที่สุด...", startKey, destKey))
            end
            local ok = false
            for _ = 1, 3 do
                if farmActiveCheck and not farmActiveCheck() then return "CANCELLED" end
                ok = warpViaPillar(destKey, setStatusRef, farmActiveCheck)
                if ok or not _G.GolfHub_Pillars.canWarp(destKey) then break end
                task.wait(0.6)
            end
            if ok then
                task.wait(0.25)
                root = getCharRoot()
                curZone = getCurrentActualZone()
                if curZone == targetZoneId and root and (root.Position - targetWpPos).Magnitude <= 85 then
                    return "ARRIVED"
                end
                if setStatusRef then setStatusRef("🚶 เดินจากเสาวาร์ปเข้าจุดฟาร์ม: " .. targetZoneId) end
            end
        end
    end

    -- เดินเท้าตามเส้นทางถนนหลักเข้าสู่จุดฟาร์มหรือตีบอสปลดประตู (ห้าม CFrame Teleport)
    -- หากเริ่มเดินจากในเมือง ให้เดินเลียบขอบทางเพื่อเลี่ยงซุ้มกิจกรรมกลางเมือง
    curZone = getCurrentActualZone()
    if curZone == "Town" then
        walkAroundFestivalBooth(TOWN_COORDS.TownExit, 18)
    end

    return walkHighwayToZone(targetZoneId, farmActiveCheck, setStatusRef)
end

task.wait() -- yield point: spread core engine setup over frames
-- ====================================================================
-- LOOT & CHEST FINDERS (AUTO LOOT & AUTO OPEN BOXES)
-- ====================================================================
local function findNearestDrop(maxDist)
    maxDist = maxDist or 80
    local root = getCharRoot()
    if not root then return nil end
    local myPos = root.Position
    local best, bestDist = nil, maxDist
    local now = workspace:GetServerTimeNow()
    local cs = cloneref and cloneref(game:GetService("CollectionService")) or game:GetService("CollectionService")

    for _, d in ipairs(cs:GetTagged("Drop")) do
        if d:IsA("BasePart") and d.Parent then
            local dist = (d.Position - myPos).Magnitude
            if dist < bestDist then
                local owner = d:GetAttribute("OwnerUserId") or 0
                local freeAt = d:GetAttribute("FreeAt") or 0
                if owner == 0 or owner == lp.UserId or freeAt <= now then
                    best, bestDist = d, dist
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return best
end

-- ====================================================================
-- ====================================================================
-- CLEAN NPC / WARP / PROMPT SAFETY (ZERO SERVER & UI INTERFERENCE)
-- ====================================================================
-- ยกเลิกการดักจับสัญญาณ NPC/Warp และไม่แตะต้อง ScreenGui หรือ Attribute ของเกม
-- ปล่อยให้ระบบ ProximityPrompt, หน้าต่าง NPC, ร้านค้า และเสาวาร์ปของแมพทำงานร่วมกับ Server 100%
if getgenv then
    if type(getgenv().GolfHub_NpcConnections) == "table" then
        for _, conn in ipairs(getgenv().GolfHub_NpcConnections) do
            pcall(function() conn:Disconnect() end)
        end
    end
    getgenv().GolfHub_NpcConnections = nil
end

local function openLootBoxDirect(boxInst)
    if not boxInst or not boxInst.Parent then return false end
    local Remotes = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("Remotes")
    local root = getCharRoot()

    -- 1. หา BasePart ของกล่อง (ตัวที่เซิร์ฟเวอร์ใช้ตรวจสอบระยะและยิง Remote)
    local targetPart = nil
    if boxInst:IsA("BasePart") then
        targetPart = boxInst
    elseif boxInst:IsA("Model") then
        targetPart = boxInst:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart") or boxInst.PrimaryPart
    elseif boxInst:IsA("ProximityPrompt") then
        local p = boxInst.Parent
        targetPart = p and (p:IsA("BasePart") and p or p:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart"))
    end

    -- 2. ยิง Remote OpenLootBox โดยตรง (แบบเดียวกับ combat_client: u3039.OpenLootBox:FireServer(u3082))
    if Remotes and Remotes:FindFirstChild("OpenLootBox") then
        if targetPart then
            pcall(function() Remotes.OpenLootBox:FireServer(targetPart) end)
        end
        if boxInst:IsA("Model") then
            pcall(function() Remotes.OpenLootBox:FireServer(boxInst) end)
        end
    end

    -- 3. ยิง Custom ProximityPrompt (OpenPrompt) ของเกม
    pcall(function()
        if boxInst:IsA("ProximityPrompt") then
            triggerPromptSafe(boxInst)
        end
        for _, desc in ipairs(boxInst:GetDescendants()) do
            if desc:IsA("ProximityPrompt") then
                triggerPromptSafe(desc)
            end
        end
    end)

    return true
end

findNearestLootBox = function(maxDist)
    maxDist = maxDist or 90
    local root = getCharRoot()
    if not root then return nil end
    local myPos = root.Position
    local best, bestDist = nil, maxDist
    local now = workspace:GetServerTimeNow()
    local cs = cloneref and cloneref(game:GetService("CollectionService")) or game:GetService("CollectionService")

    local pool = {}
    local seen = {}

    local function addBoxCandidate(inst)
        if not inst or not inst.Parent or seen[inst] then return end
        seen[inst] = true
        table.insert(pool, inst)
    end

    -- 1. Scan from CollectionService "LootBox" (Tag ทางการของเกมที่เซิร์ฟเวอร์ติดให้ BasePart)
    for _, b in ipairs(cs:GetTagged("LootBox")) do
        addBoxCandidate(b)
    end

    -- 2. Scan from Workspace models (เช่น LootBoxModel_relic_box)
    for _, ch in ipairs(workspace:GetChildren()) do
        local n = ch.Name:lower()
        if n:find("lootbox") or n:find("box") or n:find("chest") then
            addBoxCandidate(ch)
        end
    end

    -- 3. Scan ProximityPromptService (OpenPrompt ของกล่อง)
    pcall(function()
        local pps = cloneref and cloneref(game:GetService("ProximityPromptService")) or game:GetService("ProximityPromptService")
        for _, p in ipairs(pps:GetPrompts()) do
            if p.Parent then
                local act = (p.ActionText or ""):lower()
                local obj = (p.ObjectText or ""):lower()
                local parentName = p.Parent.Name:lower()
                if p.Name == "OpenPrompt" or act:find("worker") or obj:find("worker") or parentName:find("lootbox") or parentName:find("box") then
                    addBoxCandidate(p.Parent)
                end
            end
        end
    end)

    for _, b in ipairs(pool) do
        local p = nil
        if b:IsA("BasePart") then
            p = b.Position
        elseif b:IsA("Model") then
            p = b:GetPivot().Position
        elseif b:IsA("ProximityPrompt") and b.Parent then
            local pp = b.Parent
            p = pp:IsA("BasePart") and pp.Position or (pp:IsA("Model") and pp:GetPivot().Position)
        end

        if p and b.Parent then
            local dist = (p - myPos).Magnitude
            if dist < bestDist then
                local owner = b:GetAttribute("OwnerUserId") or 0
                local freeAt = b:GetAttribute("FreeAt") or 0
                if owner == 0 or owner == lp.UserId or freeAt <= now then
                    best, bestDist = b, dist
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return best
end

-- ====================================================================
-- MONSTER FINDER + WALL-DODGING COMBAT WALKER (LEGIT WALK ONLY — NO TP)
-- ====================================================================
findNearestMonster = function(maxDist, allowLeader, onlyZone)
    maxDist = maxDist or 200
    allowLeader = (allowLeader ~= false)
    local root = getCharRoot()
    if not root then return nil, nil, maxDist end
    local myPos = root.Position
    local best, bestModel, bestDist = nil, nil, maxDist
    local bestMetric = nil
    local filter = _G.GolfHub_FarmTargetFilter or "All Nearby Monsters"
    local monstersFolder = workspace:FindFirstChild("Monsters")
    if not monstersFolder then return nil, nil, maxDist end
    local zoneCenter
    if onlyZone then
        for _, wp in ipairs(ROUTE_WAYPOINTS) do
            if wp.id == onlyZone then zoneCenter = wp.pos break end
        end
    end

    for _, obj in ipairs(monstersFolder:GetChildren()) do
        if obj:IsA("Model") then
            if obj:GetAttribute("AnimDead") == true or obj:GetAttribute("Dead") == true or obj:GetAttribute("IsDead") == true then
                continue
            end
            if obj:GetAttribute("IsDummy") == true or obj.Name:find("Dummy") or obj.Name:find("Pet") then
                continue
            end
            local hp = obj:GetAttribute("HP")
            if hp == nil then hp = obj:GetAttribute("Health") end
            local hum = obj:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            local isAlive = (hp ~= nil and hp > 0) or (hp == nil and hum ~= nil and hum.Health > 0) or (hp == nil and hum == nil)
            if isAlive then
                local r = obj:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or obj.PrimaryPart or obj:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart")
                if r then
                    -- a monster in another zone (a Field01 rabbit seen from the foot of Field02) may sit behind a river or fence and cannot be reached
                    -- the Z bands are only rough (Field01 rabbits spawn inside the Field02 band), so measure from the zone's own centre waypoint
                    -- never chase a monster across a level gate (e.g. Field09 begins behind the "Reach Lv.67" barrier): it must lie in this zone's own Z band
                    if zoneCenter then
                        local dC = Vector3.new(r.Position.X - zoneCenter.X, 0, r.Position.Z - zoneCenter.Z).Magnitude
                        if dC > 150 or (zoneNameForZ(r.Position.Z) ~= onlyZone and dC > 60) then continue end
                    end
                    -- the game marks them with IsLeader / IsMiniBoss / IsBoss (seen live); the toggle promises Mini-Boss too
                    local isLeader = (obj:GetAttribute("IsLeader") == true) or (obj:GetAttribute("IsMiniBoss") == true)
                        or (obj:GetAttribute("IsBoss") == true) or obj.Name:find("Leader") or obj.Name:find("Alpha")
                    if allowLeader or not isLeader then
                        local d = (r.Position - myPos).Magnitude
                        if d <= maxDist then
                            local curHp = tonumber(hp) or (hum and hum.Health) or 100
                            if filter == "Weakest First" then
                                if not bestMetric or curHp < bestMetric or (curHp == bestMetric and d < bestDist) then
                                    best, bestModel, bestDist, bestMetric = r, obj, d, curHp
                                end
                            elseif filter == "Highest HP First" then
                                if not bestMetric or curHp > bestMetric or (curHp == bestMetric and d < bestDist) then
                                    best, bestModel, bestDist, bestMetric = r, obj, d, curHp
                                end
                            else
                                if d < bestDist then
                                    best, bestModel, bestDist = r, obj, d
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return best, bestModel, bestDist
end


-- Approach & Action: Native [F] AUTO + Legit Move-While-Attacking (STRICTLY NO TELEPORT)
approachAndAttack = function(stopDist, farmRange, canLoot, canOpenBoxes, canAtkLeader, targetZoneId)
    stopDist     = stopDist     or 18
    farmRange    = farmRange    or 70
    canLoot      = (canLoot ~= false)
    canOpenBoxes = (canOpenBoxes ~= false)
    canAtkLeader = (canAtkLeader ~= false)

    local root, hum = getCharRoot()
    if not root or not hum then return end
    local Remotes = game.ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Remotes")
