    -- 1. Ensure game's native [F] AUTO combat & skills stay ON (debounced single-shot toggle)
    if lp:GetAttribute("AutoOn") ~= true and (tick() - (_G.GolfHub_LastExtAutoToggle or 0)) >= 0.9 then
        if getCurrentActualZone() ~= "Town" then
            _G.GolfHub_LastExtAutoToggle = tick()
            setBotRunning(true, {
                range        = farmRange,
                loot         = canLoot,
                openBoxes    = canOpenBoxes,
                attackLeader = canAtkLeader,
                preset       = farmPreset,
                hpPct        = farmHpPct,
            })
        end
    end
    if lp:GetAttribute("AutoSkill") == false and Remotes and Remotes:FindFirstChild("SetAutoSkill") then
        pcall(function()
            Remotes.SetAutoSkill:FireServer(true)
            lp:SetAttribute("AutoSkill", true)
        end)
    end
    if lp:GetAttribute("MoveLockUntil") and lp:GetAttribute("MoveLockUntil") > 0 then
        lp:SetAttribute("MoveLockUntil", 0)
    end

    -- While AUTO has a target the game's CombatClient calls Humanoid:Move every render frame, over any MoveTo of ours
    -- (measured: 151 hub orders in 55 s). So while AUTO has a live target the hub only locks it and stays off the legs.
    local autoTarget
    if lp:GetAttribute("AutoOn") == true then
        local ct = lp:FindFirstChild("CurrentTarget")
        local m = ct and ct.Value
        if m and m.Parent and (tonumber(m:GetAttribute("HP")) or 1) > 0 then autoTarget = m end
    end
    local hubMayWalk = tick() >= (_G.GolfHub_UnstuckUntil or 0)

    -- A drop or box on a ledge or behind a fence can never be reached: the walk below then pushed into the wall
    -- every tick. Walk to one item for at most 4 s, then leave it alone for 60 s.
    local lootTries = _G.GolfHub_LootTries
    if not lootTries then lootTries = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" }) _G.GolfHub_LootTries = lootTries end
    -- No "fresh try after a pause" reset: this is only called while the character stands still (MoveDirection < 0.1),
    -- so a walk that is stuck pushing into a wall never calls it, and such a reset re-armed the walk every time.
    local function mayWalkToLoot(inst)
        local now, t = tick(), lootTries[inst]
        if not t or now - t.first > 60 then t = { first = now, last = now } lootTries[inst] = t end
        t.last = now
        return now - t.first < 4
    end

    -- 2. Fast Auto Loot: Pick up nearby drops (walk to drop if slightly out of reach)
    if canLoot and Remotes and Remotes:FindFirstChild("PickupDrop") then
        local drop = findNearestDrop(farmRange)
        if drop and drop.Parent then
            local dropDist = (drop.Position - root.Position).Magnitude
            if dropDist <= 25 then
                pcall(function() Remotes.PickupDrop:FireServer(true) end)
            elseif dropDist <= 45 and hubMayWalk and not autoTarget and (hum.MoveDirection.Magnitude < 0.1) and mayWalkToLoot(drop) then
                hum:MoveTo(drop.Position)
            end
        end
    end

    -- 3. Fast Auto Open Boxes: Open nearby chests (walk if slightly out of reach)
    if canOpenBoxes then
        local box = findNearestLootBox(farmRange)
        if box and box.Parent then
            local boxPos = box:IsA("BasePart") and box.Position or (box:IsA("Model") and box:GetPivot().Position)
            if not boxPos and box:IsA("ProximityPrompt") and box.Parent then
                local pp = box.Parent
                boxPos = pp:IsA("BasePart") and pp.Position or (pp:IsA("Model") and pp:GetPivot().Position)
            end

            if boxPos then
                local boxDist = (boxPos - root.Position).Magnitude
                if boxDist <= 18 then
                    openLootBoxDirect(box)
                elseif boxDist <= 35 and hubMayWalk and not autoTarget and (hum.MoveDirection.Magnitude < 0.1) and mayWalkToLoot(box) then
                    hum:MoveTo(boxPos)
                end
            end
        end
    end

    -- 4. Target Lock + Move-While-Attacking (100% Legit Humanoid:MoveTo, ZERO Teleport)
    local searchDist = math.max(farmRange, 180)
    local target, mobModel, dist = findNearestMonster(searchDist, canAtkLeader, targetZoneId)
    if target and mobModel and mobModel.Parent then
        local now = tick()
        -- Lock target into the game's native combat system so [F] AUTO & skills 1-5 attack immediately
        if _G.GolfHub_LastExtTarget ~= mobModel or (now - (_G.GolfHub_LastExtLockTick or 0)) >= 0.45 then
            _G.GolfHub_LastExtTarget = mobModel
            _G.GolfHub_LastExtLockTick = now
            pcall(function()
                local lockEv = lp:FindFirstChild("LumiLockTarget")
                if lockEv then lockEv:Fire(mobModel) end
                if Remotes and Remotes:FindFirstChild("SetTarget") then
                    Remotes.SetTarget:FireServer(mobModel, false, true)
                end
            end)
        end

        local flatToMob = Vector3.new(target.Position.X - root.Position.X, 0, target.Position.Z - root.Position.Z)
        local flatDist = flatToMob.Magnitude
        local engageDist = math.clamp(stopDist * 0.75, 8, 16)

        -- AUTO drives inside farm range. It counts as working while we move or the target loses HP; after 2 s of
        -- neither, the hub walks for 2 s (obstacle steering) and then hands back.
        local autoDrives = false
        if lp:GetAttribute("AutoOn") == true and flatDist <= farmRange then
            local st = _G.GolfHub_AutoDrive
            if not st then st = { since = now, hubUntil = 0 } _G.GolfHub_AutoDrive = st end
            local watched = autoTarget or mobModel
            local watchedHp = tonumber(watched:GetAttribute("HP"))
            local moved = st.pos and (root.Position - st.pos).Magnitude > 1.2
            -- When closing distance (flatDist > engageDist), HP changes alone do NOT count as locomotion progress!
            -- Only actual physical movement proves the character is not running into an obstacle.
            local progress = moved or (flatDist <= engageDist and st.hp ~= watchedHp)
            if now - (st.seen or 0) > 2 or st.model ~= watched or progress or not st.pos then
                st.since, st.model, st.hp, st.pos = now, watched, watchedHp, root.Position
            elseif now - st.since > 2.0 then
                st.since, st.hubUntil = now, now + 2.0
                -- If pinned without moving for > 2 s while trying to approach, execute an unstuck impulse
                -- only when the Settings watchdog is not active (single-owner rule)
                local watchdogActive = tick() - (_G.GolfHub_WatchdogAt or 0) < 1
                if not watchdogActive and flatDist > engageDist and tick() >= (_G.GolfHub_UnstuckUntil or 0) then
                    performUnstuck(root, hum)
                end
            end
            st.seen = now
            autoDrives = now >= st.hubUntil
        end
        _G.GolfHub_AutoDrivingUntil = autoDrives and (now + 1) or 0

        local moveBehavior = _G.GolfHub_FarmMovementBehavior or "Walk To Monster (Pathfind + Wall Dodge)"
        if autoDrives or not hubMayWalk or moveBehavior == "Stand Still (Range Only)" then
            -- no movement order from the hub
        elseif flatDist > engageDist then
            -- Walk smoothly toward monster with obstacle avoidance
            local steered = avoidObstaclesAhead(root, hum, flatToMob.Unit)
            local stepLen = math.min(flatDist, 22)
            hum:MoveTo(root.Position + Vector3.new(steered.X, 0, steered.Z) * stepLen)
        else
            -- Actively strafe / orbit around monster while attacking (never stand frozen, never teleport)
            if (now - (_G.GolfHub_ExtStrafeTick or 0)) >= 0.65 then
                _G.GolfHub_ExtStrafeTick = now
                _G.GolfHub_ExtStrafeDir = -(_G.GolfHub_ExtStrafeDir or 1)
                local dirToMob = flatDist > 0.2 and flatToMob.Unit or root.CFrame.LookVector
                local tangent = Vector3.new(-dirToMob.Z, 0, dirToMob.X) * _G.GolfHub_ExtStrafeDir
                local desiredRadius = math.clamp(stopDist * 0.55, 6, 12)
                local radialAdjust = (flatDist < desiredRadius - 2) and -dirToMob * 3 or ((flatDist > desiredRadius + 2) and dirToMob * 3 or Vector3.zero)
                local moveVec = (tangent * 8 + radialAdjust)
                if moveVec.Magnitude > 0.2 then
                    local steered = avoidObstaclesAhead(root, hum, moveVec.Unit)
                    hum:MoveTo(root.Position + Vector3.new(steered.X, 0, steered.Z) * 8)
                end
            end
        end
    elseif targetZoneId and hubMayWalk and not autoTarget then
        -- No monsters in immediate range: walk legitimately toward the zone's spawn center & patrol
        local zonePos = nil
        for _, wp in ipairs(ROUTE_WAYPOINTS) do
            if wp.id == targetZoneId then
                zonePos = wp.pos
                break
            end
        end
        if zonePos then
            local toCenter = Vector3.new(zonePos.X - root.Position.X, 0, zonePos.Z - root.Position.Z)
            -- Far from the zone centre with nothing in range (for example at the foot of the hill where the zone border is): a straight line cuts across
            -- cliffs, so follow the road waypoints (ramps and stairs) instead. Without this the character stood at the zone border forever.
            -- In town or a whole map away (died and respawned in town): never walk the highway from here. The road walk
            -- blocks until it arrives, so farmLoop's 3 s "in town -> plan with pillars" check never ran (088H099 walked
            -- Town -> Field01..06 on foot to Field07). Hand back to farmLoop, which plans the trip with warp pillars.
            if toCenter.Magnitude > 60 then
                local zNow = getCurrentActualZone()
                if zNow ~= targetZoneId and (zNow == "Town" or math.abs(zonePos.Z - root.Position.Z) > 250) then
                    _G.GolfHub_FarmNeedReplan = true
                    return
                end
            end
            if toCenter.Magnitude > 60 and (tick() - (_G.GolfHub_LastRoadWalk or 0)) > 3 then
                _G.GolfHub_LastRoadWalk = tick()
                setBotRunning(false)
                walkHighwayToZone(targetZoneId, function() return farmActive end, setStatus)
                return
            end
            if toCenter.Magnitude > 22 then
                local steered = avoidObstaclesAhead(root, hum, toCenter.Unit)
                hum:MoveTo(root.Position + Vector3.new(steered.X, 0, steered.Z) * math.min(toCenter.Magnitude, 24))
            else
                local now = tick()
                if (now - (_G.GolfHub_ExtPatrolTick or 0)) >= 1.4 then
                    _G.GolfHub_ExtPatrolTick = now
                    local angle = ((now * 0.9) % (math.pi * 2))
                    local patrolPt = zonePos + Vector3.new(math.cos(angle) * 18, 0, math.sin(angle) * 18)
                    hum:MoveTo(patrolPt)
                end
            end
        end
    end

    -- (no unstuck step here: standing still while attacking is normal. The Settings watchdog handles real blocks,
    -- and the 3 s hand-over above handles a target AUTO cannot reach.)
end

-- ====================================================================
-- SKILL SELECTION & ORDER ENGINE (PRESERVE USER SKILLS IN BOTJSON)
-- ====================================================================
local savedSkillSlots = {
    [1] = true,
    [2] = true,
    [3] = true,
    [4] = true,
    [5] = true,
    [6] = true,
    [7] = true,
    [8] = true,
}

local savedSkillSlotsByJob = {}
local updatingBotFromScript = false
local hasUserTouchedSkillSlots = false

-- แคชชื่อสกิลภาษาอังกฤษเต็มและค่า SP จาก SkillData ในเกมจริง
local skillNameCache = {}
local skillSpCache = {}
pcall(function()
    local RS = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
    local Shared = RS:FindFirstChild("Shared") or RS:WaitForChild("Shared", 3)
    local SkillData = Shared and require(Shared:WaitForChild("SkillData", 3))
    if SkillData and type(SkillData.Skills) == "table" then
        for sId, data in pairs(SkillData.Skills) do
            if type(data) == "table" then
                if data.Name then
                    skillNameCache[sId] = data.Name
                end
                if type(data.SP) == "table" then
                    skillSpCache[sId] = data.SP[#data.SP]
                elseif type(data.SP) == "number" then
                    skillSpCache[sId] = data.SP
                end
            end
        end
    end
end)

local function getCurrentJobName()
    return lp:GetAttribute("Job") or lp:GetAttribute("CurrentJob") or "Archer"
end

local getSavedBotSettings
local getHotbarTable
do
    local function deepCopyTable(orig)
        if type(orig) ~= "table" then return orig end
        local copy = {}
        for k, v in pairs(orig) do
            copy[k] = (type(v) == "table") and deepCopyTable(v) or v
        end
        return copy
    end

    local _cachedBotSettings, _lastBotJsonRaw
    getSavedBotSettings = function()
        local botJson = lp:GetAttribute("BotJson")
        if botJson == _lastBotJsonRaw and _cachedBotSettings then
            return deepCopyTable(_cachedBotSettings)
        end
        _lastBotJsonRaw = botJson
        if botJson and #botJson > 2 then
            local ok, parsed = pcall(function() return HttpService:JSONDecode(botJson) end)
            if ok and typeof(parsed) == "table" then
                _cachedBotSettings = parsed
                return deepCopyTable(parsed)
            end
        end
        _cachedBotSettings = {}
        return {}
    end

    local _cachedHotbarTable, _lastHotbarJsonRaw
    getHotbarTable = function()
        local hotbarJsonStr = lp:GetAttribute("HotbarJson")
        if hotbarJsonStr == _lastHotbarJsonRaw and _cachedHotbarTable then
            return table.clone(_cachedHotbarTable)
        end
        _lastHotbarJsonRaw = hotbarJsonStr
        if hotbarJsonStr and #hotbarJsonStr > 2 then
            local ok, tbl = pcall(function() return HttpService:JSONDecode(hotbarJsonStr) end)
            if ok and typeof(tbl) == "table" then
                _cachedHotbarTable = tbl
                return table.clone(tbl)
            end
        end
        _cachedHotbarTable = {}
        return {}
    end
end

-- อ่าน Skill ID ของช่อง 1-8 จาก HotbarJson เป็นหลัก (ตรงกับช่องสกิล 1-8 บนหน้าจอ 100%)
local function getHotbarSkillIdForSlot(slotIdx)
    local hb = getHotbarTable()
    local raw = hb[tostring(slotIdx)]
    if type(raw) == "string" then
        local sId = string.match(raw, "^skill:(.+)$")
        if sId and #sId > 0 then
            return sId
        end
    end
    -- Fallback เฉพาะกรณี HotbarJson ยังไม่โหลด: ใช้ BotJson.Skills[curJob][slotIdx] (ต้องเป็น string เท่านั้น)
    local curJob = getCurrentJobName()
    local parsed = getSavedBotSettings()
    local jobSkills = parsed.Skills and parsed.Skills[curJob]
    if type(jobSkills) == "table" and type(jobSkills[slotIdx]) == "string" and #jobSkills[slotIdx] > 0 then
        return jobSkills[slotIdx]
    end
    return nil
end

-- Per-job skill choices persist in this player's own folder (skill_slots.json), so they survive rejoin / server hop
-- and two accounts open at once never overwrite each other.
local SKILL_SLOTS_FILE = "GolfHub_HuntingTogether/" .. (lp.Name:gsub("[^%w_%-]", "_")) .. "/skill_slots.json"
pcall(function()
    if isfile and isfile(SKILL_SLOTS_FILE) then
        local t = HttpService:JSONDecode(readfile(SKILL_SLOTS_FILE))
        if type(t) == "table" then
            for job, slots in pairs(t) do
                if type(slots) == "table" then
                    local row = {}
                    for i = 1, 8 do row[i] = slots[i] == true end
                    savedSkillSlotsByJob[job] = row
                end
            end
        end
    end
end)

local function saveCurrentJobSkillSlots()
    local curJob = getCurrentJobName()
    local tbl = {}
    for i = 1, 8 do
        tbl[i] = (savedSkillSlots[i] == true)
    end
    savedSkillSlotsByJob[curJob] = tbl
    pcall(function() if writefile then writefile(SKILL_SLOTS_FILE, HttpService:JSONEncode(savedSkillSlotsByJob)) end end)
end

local function syncSkillSlotsFromGame(forceFromGame)
    local curJob = getCurrentJobName()
    local parsed = getSavedBotSettings()
    local jobOff = parsed.SkillOff and parsed.SkillOff[curJob]
    local foundAny = false

    local jobSaved = savedSkillSlotsByJob[curJob]
    if jobSaved and not forceFromGame then
        for i = 1, 8 do
            if jobSaved[i] ~= nil then
                savedSkillSlots[i] = jobSaved[i]
                foundAny = true
            end
        end
        return foundAny
    end

    for slotIdx = 1, 8 do
        local skillId = getHotbarSkillIdForSlot(slotIdx)
        if skillId then
            foundAny = true
            if forceFromGame or (not hasUserTouchedSkillSlots) then
                if type(jobOff) == "table" and jobOff[skillId] == true then
                    savedSkillSlots[slotIdx] = false
                else
                    savedSkillSlots[slotIdx] = true
                end
            end
        end
    end
    if foundAny then
        saveCurrentJobSkillSlots()
    end
    return foundAny
end

local function getSkillSlotLabel(slotIdx)
    local skillId = getHotbarSkillIdForSlot(slotIdx)
    if skillId then
        local displayName = skillNameCache[skillId]
        local spCost = skillSpCache[skillId]
        local spText = spCost and (" · SP " .. spCost) or ""
        pcall(function()
            local pad = lp and lp:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui") and lp.PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("LumiHotbar") and lp.PlayerGui.LumiHotbar:FindFirstChild("ActionPad")
            local slot = pad and pad:FindFirstChild("Slot" .. slotIdx)
            local count = slot and slot:FindFirstChild("ItemCount")
            if count and count.Text ~= "" then
                spText = " · " .. count.Text
            end
        end)
        local romanTitle = skillId:sub(1,1):upper() .. skillId:sub(2)
        if displayName and displayName:lower() ~= skillId:lower() then
            return string.format("⚡ สกิล %d: %s (%s)%s", slotIdx, displayName, romanTitle, spText)
        else
            local title = displayName or romanTitle
            return string.format("⚡ สกิล %d: %s%s", slotIdx, title, spText)
        end
    end
    return string.format("สกิลช่อง %d: (ว่าง)", slotIdx)
end

-- ผสานการเลือกสกิลช่อง 1-8 เข้ากับ BotJson.Skills & BotJson.SkillOff อย่างเข้มงวด
-- (เชื่อมกับระบบ Bot ของเกมจริง 100% — สกิลที่เปิดจะใช้ สกิลที่ปิดจะใส่ SkillOff ทันที)
local function applyManagedSkillSettings(settings)
    local curJob = getCurrentJobName()

    settings.Skills = settings.Skills or {}
    settings.SkillOff = settings.SkillOff or {}
    settings.SkillOff[curJob] = {}

    local enabledSkillSet = {}
    local orderedHotbarSkills = {}
    local allKnownSkills = {}

    -- 1. เก็บสกิลจากช่อง Hotbar 1-8 ตามสถานะเปิด/ปิดจริง
    local hb = getHotbarTable()
    for slotIdx = 1, 8 do
        local raw = hb[tostring(slotIdx)]
        local skillId = type(raw) == "string" and string.match(raw, "^skill:(.+)$") or nil
        if not skillId then
            local existing = settings.Skills[curJob]
            if type(existing) == "table" and type(existing[slotIdx]) == "string" then
                skillId = existing[slotIdx]
            end
        end
        if skillId and #skillId > 0 then
            allKnownSkills[skillId] = true
            table.insert(orderedHotbarSkills, skillId)
            if savedSkillSlots[slotIdx] == true then
                enabledSkillSet[skillId] = true
            else
                settings.SkillOff[curJob][skillId] = true
            end
        end
    end

    -- 2. สแกนสกิลใน BotJson.Skills เดิม, และ SkillsJson ทั้งหมด -> ถ้าไม่ได้เปิดในช่อง 1-8 ให้ปิดใน SkillOff ทันที
    if type(settings.Skills[curJob]) == "table" then
        for _, sId in ipairs(settings.Skills[curJob]) do
            if type(sId) == "string" and #sId > 0 then
                allKnownSkills[sId] = true
            end
        end
    end
    local skillsJsonStr = lp:GetAttribute("SkillsJson")
    if type(skillsJsonStr) == "string" and #skillsJsonStr > 2 then
        pcall(function()
            local unlocked = HttpService:JSONDecode(skillsJsonStr)
            if type(unlocked) == "table" then
                for sId, lv in pairs(unlocked) do
                    if type(sId) == "string" and (tonumber(lv) or 0) > 0 then
                        allKnownSkills[sId] = true
                    end
                end
            end
        end)
    end

    for skillId in pairs(allKnownSkills) do
        if enabledSkillSet[skillId] then
            settings.SkillOff[curJob][skillId] = nil
        else
            settings.SkillOff[curJob][skillId] = true
        end
    end

    if #orderedHotbarSkills > 0 then
        settings.Skills[curJob] = orderedHotbarSkills
    end
end

-- ซิงค์เฉพาะการเลือกสกิลช่อง 1-5 เข้า BotJson ของเกมโดยไม่ไปปิดปุ่ม Auto Attack หลักของเกม!
local function syncSkillSelectionToBotJson(useSkillsOverride)
    local Remotes = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("Remotes")
    local setBotRemote = Remotes and Remotes:FindFirstChild("SetBotSettings")
    if not setBotRemote then return end

    local settings = getSavedBotSettings()
    -- ranged mode turns normal attacks off, except during a stun free-hit window (_G.GolfHub_StunMelee)
    if isPlayerInDungeon and isPlayerInDungeon() and (dngSkillOnly or (_G.GolfHub_DngRanged == true and _G.GolfHub_StunMelee ~= true)) then
        settings.NormalAttack = false
    else
        settings.NormalAttack = true
    end
    if useSkillsOverride ~= nil then
        settings.UseSkills = (useSkillsOverride == true)
    elseif settings.UseSkills == nil then
        settings.UseSkills = true
    end
    applyManagedSkillSettings(settings)

    updatingBotFromScript = true
    pcall(function()
        setBotRemote:FireServer(settings)
    end)
    task.delay(0.45, function()
        updatingBotFromScript = false
    end)
end

-- ====================================================================
-- FORCE STOP NATIVE GAME AUTO-BOT (CombatClient u3147 + QuestNavId)
-- ====================================================================
-- หมายเหตุ: BotAutoState:FireServer(false) อย่างเดียวไม่ได้ปิดตัวแปร AutoOn ใน CombatClient!
-- ต้องปิดผ่าน BindableEvent LumiMenuToggle("autoToggle") / LumiAutoPause(true) เพื่อหยุดบอทเกมไม่ให้ลากตัวละครไปตีมอน (เช่น กระต่ายโซน 1)
forceStopNativeAutoBot = function()
    -- กฎเหล็ก: ขณะอยู่ในดันเจี้ยน ห้ามปิดปุ่ม [F] AUTO เด็ดขาด (กัน LoadAutoLoadConfig / แท็บอื่นสั่ง setBotRunning(false) มาปิดปุ่ม AUTO ตอนเริ่มรอบใหม่!)
    if isPlayerInDungeon and isPlayerInDungeon() and not _G.GolfHub_IsUnloading then
        return
    end
    pcall(function()
        -- 1. ล้าง QuestNavId ป้องกัน Auto Quest ลากตัวละครไปตีมอนเควส
        local qNav = lp:GetAttribute("QuestNavId")
        if qNav and qNav ~= "" then
            lp:SetAttribute("QuestNavId", "")
        end

        -- 2. ปิดบอท Auto ของเกมฝั่ง Client (CombatClient toggleAuto)
        if lp:GetAttribute("AutoOn") == true then
            local menuToggle = lp:FindFirstChild("LumiMenuToggle")
            if menuToggle and menuToggle:IsA("BindableEvent") then
                menuToggle:Fire("autoToggle")
            end
            task.wait(0.05)
            -- Fallback กรณี AutoOn ยังไม่ปิด
            if lp:GetAttribute("AutoOn") == true then
                local pauseEv = lp:FindFirstChild("LumiAutoPause")
                if pauseEv and pauseEv:IsA("BindableEvent") then
                    pauseEv:Fire(true)
                end
            end
        end

        -- 3. แจ้ง Server ปิด BotAutoState + ล้างเป้าหมาย + หยุดแรงเดินเดิมทันที
        local Remotes = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("Remotes")
        if Remotes then
            if Remotes:FindFirstChild("BotAutoState") then
                Remotes.BotAutoState:FireServer(false)
            end
            if Remotes:FindFirstChild("SetTarget") then
                Remotes.SetTarget:FireServer(nil, false)
            end
        end
        local curTgt = lp:FindFirstChild("CurrentTarget")
        if curTgt and curTgt:IsA("ObjectValue") then
            curTgt.Value = nil
        end
        local _, hum = getCharRoot()
        if hum then
            hum:Move(Vector3.zero, false)
        end
    end)
end

-- ====================================================================
-- BOT SETTINGS (MERGE WITH EXISTING BOTJSON TO PRESERVE SETTINGS)
-- ====================================================================
setBotRunning = function(enabled, cfg)
    cfg = cfg or {}
    local Remotes = game.ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Remotes")
    if not Remotes then return end

    -- ดึงการตั้งค่าเดิมจากเกมเพื่อคงค่า UseItems, Skills, Exclude, LootSkip ฯลฯ
    local settings = getSavedBotSettings()

    -- Hard rule: with "skill only" on, no normal attacks inside a dungeon. This used to force NormalAttack back on
    -- whenever any mode called setBotRunning, until the dungeon's 10 s resync turned it off again.
    settings.NormalAttack = not ((dngSkillOnly == true or (_G.GolfHub_DngRanged == true and _G.GolfHub_StunMelee ~= true)) and isPlayerInDungeon and isPlayerInDungeon())
    settings.Range        = cfg.range or farmRange or settings.Range or 70
    settings.Radius       = cfg.range or farmRange or settings.Radius or 70
    settings.Loot         = if cfg.loot ~= nil then cfg.loot else (farmLoot ~= false)
    settings.OpenBoxes    = if cfg.openBoxes ~= nil then cfg.openBoxes else (farmOpenBoxes ~= false)
    settings.Mode         = "fight"
    settings.AttackLeader = if cfg.attackLeader ~= nil then cfg.attackLeader else (farmAtkLeader ~= false)
    settings.SkipLeader   = not settings.AttackLeader
    settings.SkipBoss     = false
    settings.HpPercent    = cfg.hpPct or farmHpPct or settings.HpPercent or 40
    settings.HealPercent  = cfg.healPct or settings.HealPercent or 50
    settings.RestHp       = 0
    settings.RestUntil    = 90
    settings.UseSkills    = true
    settings.FixedArea    = false
    settings.Preset       = cfg.preset or farmPreset or settings.Preset or "normal"

    -- ล็อกสกิลที่ผู้ใช้เลือกไว้ ไม่ให้ถูกล้าง
    applyManagedSkillSettings(settings)

    local setBotRemote = Remotes:FindFirstChild("SetBotSettings")
    if setBotRemote then
        updatingBotFromScript = true
        setBotRemote:FireServer(settings)
        task.delay(0.5, function() updatingBotFromScript = false end)
    end

    if not enabled then
        forceStopNativeAutoBot()
    else
        -- ถ้ากำลังเปิด Auto Hunt Boss อยู่ แต่ยังไม่พบบอสเป้าหมาย (กำลังเดินเข้าห้อง) ห้ามเปิด AutoBot เกมเด็ดขาด!
        -- ป้องกันไม่ให้บอทเกมแย่งควบคุมแล้วลากตัวละครเดินลงบันไดหนีไปฟิลด์ 08/09
        if bossFarmActive and not cfg.isBossCombat and not (lp:FindFirstChild("CurrentTarget") and lp.CurrentTarget.Value) then
            forceStopNativeAutoBot()
            return
        end

        local nowBot = tick()
        local autoOn = lp:GetAttribute("AutoOn")
        if autoOn ~= true and (nowBot - (_G.GolfHub_LastNativeAutoOnTick or 0) >= 0.5) then
            _G.GolfHub_LastNativeAutoOnTick = nowBot
            pcall(function()
                local menuToggle = lp:FindFirstChild("LumiMenuToggle")
                if menuToggle and menuToggle:IsA("BindableEvent") then
                    menuToggle:Fire("autoToggle")
                else
                    local vim = game:GetService("VirtualInputManager")
                    if vim then
                        vim:SendKeyEvent(true, Enum.KeyCode.F, false, game)
                        task.delay(0.03, function()
                            pcall(function() vim:SendKeyEvent(false, Enum.KeyCode.F, false, game) end)
                        end)
                    end
                end
            end)
        end
        pcall(function()
            local Remotes2 = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("Remotes")
            if Remotes2 and Remotes2:FindFirstChild("BotAutoState") then
                Remotes2.BotAutoState:FireServer(true)
            end
        end)
    end
end

-- ====================================================================
-- INVENTORY & BAG CAPACITY CHECKERS (ANTI-LOOP & ACCURATE ATTRIBUTES)
-- ====================================================================
getBagItemCount = function()
    local player = lp or game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer
    if not player then return 0 end

    -- 1. Direct Attribute from Game Server (Instant & 100% Accurate)
    local used = player:GetAttribute("BagSlotsUsed")
    if typeof(used) == "number" then
        return used
    end
    local attrs = player:GetAttributes() or {}
    if typeof(attrs.BagSlotsUsed) == "number" then
        return attrs.BagSlotsUsed
    end

    -- 2. Check UI Label in PlayerGui.LumiInventory
    local pg = player:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
    if pg then
        local inv = pg:FindFirstChild("LumiInventory")
        local slotGauge = inv and inv:FindFirstChild("SlotGauge", true)
        local slotLabel = slotGauge and slotGauge:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("TextLabel")
        if slotLabel and slotLabel.Text then
            local cur = slotLabel.Text:match("(%d+)%s*/")
            if cur then return tonumber(cur) end
        end

        -- 3. Check BagDock in LumiMenu (e.g. "100%")
        local menu = pg:FindFirstChild("LumiMenu")
        local bagDock = menu and menu:FindFirstChild("BagDock", true)
        local pctLabel = bagDock and bagDock:FindFirstChild("BagPercent")
        if pctLabel and pctLabel.Text then
            local pct = tonumber(pctLabel.Text:match("(%d+)"))
            if pct then
                local maxSlots = player:GetAttribute("BagSlotsMax") or 240
                return math.floor((pct / 100) * maxSlots)
            end
        end

        -- 4. Check LumiBagFull ScreenGui
        local fullGui = pg:FindFirstChild("LumiBagFull")
        if fullGui and fullGui.Enabled then
            return player:GetAttribute("BagSlotsMax") or 240
        end
    end

    -- 5. Fallback: GearJson
    local gearJson = player:GetAttribute("GearJson")
    if gearJson and #gearJson > 2 then
        local ok, parsed = pcall(function() return game:GetService("HttpService"):JSONDecode(gearJson) end)
        if ok and typeof(parsed) == "table" then
            local count = 0
            for _ in pairs(parsed) do
                count = count + 1
            end
            return count
        end
    end

    return 0
end

local lastSellAttemptTime = 0

isBagFullOrOverThreshold = function()
    if not autoSellEnabled then return false end
    if isSellingNow then return false end
    -- the last trip could not bring the bag under the limit (the rest is protected): going again right away only loops
    if tick() < (_G.GolfHub_SellBlockedUntil or 0) then return false end

    local player = lp or game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer
    if not player then return false end

    local maxSlots = player:GetAttribute("BagSlotsMax") or 240
    local usedSlots = 0

    local attrUsed = player:GetAttribute("BagSlotsUsed")
    if typeof(attrUsed) == "number" then
        usedSlots = attrUsed
    end

    -- 1. ตรวจสอบ UI Label ใน PlayerGui ตรงๆ (ตรงตามที่โชว์บนหน้าจอ เช่น "240 / 240 slots")
    if usedSlots == 0 then
        local pg = player:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
        if pg then
            local inv = pg:FindFirstChild("LumiInventory")
            local slotGauge = inv and inv:FindFirstChild("SlotGauge", true)
            local slotLabel = slotGauge and slotGauge:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("TextLabel")
            if slotLabel and slotLabel.Text then
                local cur, maxT = slotLabel.Text:match("(%d+)%s*/%s*(%d+)")
                if cur then
                    usedSlots = math.max(usedSlots, tonumber(cur) or 0)
                    if maxT then maxSlots = tonumber(maxT) or maxSlots end
                end
            end

            local fullGui = pg:FindFirstChild("LumiBagFull")
            if fullGui and fullGui.Enabled then
                return true
            end
            local bagDock = pg:FindFirstChild("LumiMenu") and pg.LumiMenu:FindFirstChild("BagDock")
            local bagPct = bagDock and bagDock:FindFirstChild("BagPercent")
            if bagPct and bagPct:IsA("TextLabel") and (bagPct.Text:find("100") or bagPct.Text:find("Full") or bagPct.Text:find("เต็ม")) then
                return true
            end
        end
    end

    if usedSlots == 0 then
        usedSlots = getBagItemCount()
    end

    local freeSlots = math.max(0, maxSlots - usedSlots)

    -- 1. ตรวจจับกระเป๋าเต็มวิกฤต: ช่องเต็ม 100% (>= maxSlots) หรือเหลือน้อยมาก (<= 2 ช่อง) บังคับขายทันที 100%!
    if usedSlots >= maxSlots or freeSlots <= 2 then
        return true
    end

    -- 2. ตรวจสอบตามโควต้า Threshold ที่ผู้ใช้ตั้งไว้ใน UI (sellThreshold)
    local threshold = tonumber(sellThreshold) or 220
    if usedSlots >= threshold then
        -- Anti-Loop Cooldown: หากเพิ่งไปขายของที่ Barnaby ในช่วง 25 วินาทีที่ผ่านมา
        local now = tick()
        if (now - lastSellAttemptTime) < 25 then
            if usedSlots >= maxSlots then
                return true
            end
            return false
        end
        return true
    end

    return false
end

-- ====================================================================
-- REAL-TIME LEVEL-UP & BOSS GATE UNLOCK LISTENER (ZERO EXTRA LOCALS)
-- ====================================================================
pcall(function()
    if type(_G.GolfHub_FarmLevelConns) == "table" then
        for _, c in ipairs(_G.GolfHub_FarmLevelConns) do
            pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end)
        end
    end
    _G.GolfHub_FarmLevelConns = {}
    local function onProgressionChanged()
        local newLv = getLevel()
        setLvLabel(tostring(newLv))
        local gates = getBossGates()
        if lblBoss1 then pcall(function() lblBoss1:Set(gates.Boss01 and "✅ Cleared" or "🔒 Locked", gates.Boss01 and "run" or "bad") end) end
        if lblBoss2 then pcall(function() lblBoss2:Set(gates.Boss02 and "✅ Cleared" or "🔒 Locked", gates.Boss02 and "run" or "bad") end) end
        if lblBoss3 then pcall(function() lblBoss3:Set(gates.Boss03 and "✅ Cleared" or "🔒 Locked", gates.Boss03 and "run" or "bad") end) end
        if lblBoss4 then pcall(function() lblBoss4:Set(gates.Boss04 and "✅ Cleared" or "🔒 Locked", gates.Boss04 and "run" or "bad") end) end
        _G.GolfHub_FarmNeedZoneReeval = true
    end
    for _, attrName in ipairs({ "Level", "JobLevel", "WarpPillars", "AchieveJson" }) do
        table.insert(_G.GolfHub_FarmLevelConns, lp:GetAttributeChangedSignal(attrName):Connect(onProgressionChanged))
    end
end)

task.wait() -- yield point: spread core engine setup over frames
-- ====================================================================
-- FARM LOOP — HIGHWAY NAVIGATION + WALL DODGER + REAL-TIME LEVEL UNLOCK
-- ====================================================================
-- The boss whose closed door stands between the character and the farm zone, or nil.
-- The Z bands of zoneNameForZ are rough: the Boss01 door is at -961 but the band already says Field04 from -950, so a
-- new character pushing the door counted as "arrived" and never fought the boss. Uses the real barrier when it is
-- streamed in, otherwise a point 20 studs in front of the first road waypoint behind that door.
_G.GolfHub_BlockingBoss = function(targetZoneKey)
    local root = getCharRoot()
    if not root or not BOSS_INFO then return nil end
    local barriers = workspace:FindFirstChild("Map") and workspace.Map:FindFirstChild("Barriers")
    local targetZ
    for _, wp in ipairs(ROUTE_WAYPOINTS) do
        if wp.id == targetZoneKey then targetZ = wp.pos.Z break end
    end
    if not targetZ then return nil end
    for _, key in ipairs({ "Boss01", "Boss02", "Boss03", "Boss04" }) do
        local info = BOSS_INFO[key]
        if info and not isBossCleared(key) then
            local lock = barriers and barriers:FindFirstChild("BossLock_" .. key)
            local doorZ = lock and lock:IsA("BasePart") and lock.Position.Z
            if not doorZ then
                for _, wp in ipairs(ROUTE_WAYPOINTS) do
                    if wp.targetZone == info.unlocks and not wp.isBoss and not wp.isBossGate then doorZ = wp.pos.Z + 20 break end
                end
            end
            if doorZ and root.Position.Z > doorZ and targetZ < doorZ then return key, info.name end
        end
    end
    return nil
end

-- Open a closed boss door: walk into the room with AUTO off (monsters just outside the room used to hold AUTO, and
-- the hub stays off the legs while AUTO has a target), then let AUTO fight that boss and nothing else.
-- Returns when the door is open, after a sale trip, or when the character is no longer near that room.
_G.GolfHub_FightDoorBoss = function(bossKey, bossName, farmZoneKey)
    local def = BOSS_TARGET_DEFS and BOSS_TARGET_DEFS[bossKey]
    if not def then task.wait(1) return end
    local name = bossName or def.name
    local center, doorZ = def.arenaPos, def.doorZ
    local function flatDist(a, b) return Vector3.new(a.X - b.X, 0, a.Z - b.Z).Magnitude end
    local function inRoom(p) return p.Z <= doorZ - 12 and flatDist(p, center) <= def.arenaRadius + 25 end
    local function findBoss()
        local mf = workspace:FindFirstChild("Monsters")
        for _, m in ipairs(mf and mf:GetChildren() or {}) do
            if m:IsA("Model") and (m:GetAttribute("MonsterId") == def.monsterId or m.Name == def.name)
                and (tonumber(m:GetAttribute("HP")) or 0) > 0 and m:GetAttribute("Dead") ~= true then
                local p = m:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or m.PrimaryPart or m:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart")
                if p and flatDist(p.Position, center) <= def.arenaRadius + 25 then return m, p end
            end
        end
        return nil
    end
    local lastStop = 0
    local function autoOff()
        if lp:GetAttribute("AutoOn") == true and tick() - lastStop > 0.8 then
            lastStop = tick()
            forceStopNativeAutoBot()
        end
    end

    while farmActive and not isBossCleared(bossKey) do
        if farmZoneKey and resolveFarmTargetZone(getLevel()).zone ~= farmZoneKey then return end -- job / level / map changed: plan again
        if autoSellEnabled and not isSellingNow and isBagFullOrOverThreshold() then
            executeSellRoutine(farmZoneKey, function() return farmActive end, setStatus)
            return
        end
        local root, hum = getCharRoot()
        if root and hum and hum.Health > 0 then
            local pos = root.Position
            if flatDist(pos, center) > 350 then return end -- died / warped away: plan the trip again

            if not inRoom(pos) then
                autoOff()
                setStatus("🚶 เดินเข้าห้องบอส " .. name .. " (พัก AUTO ระหว่างทาง)")
                -- through the middle of the doorway: first just outside it, then well inside
                local goal
                if pos.Z > doorZ + 16 or (pos.Z > doorZ and math.abs(pos.X) > 6) then
                    goal = Vector3.new(0, pos.Y, doorZ + 10)
                else
                    goal = Vector3.new(0, center.Y, doorZ - 30) -- the arena floor can be higher than the approach
                end
                walkTo(goal, 8, 5, function()
                    if not farmActive then return true end
                    autoOff()
                    local r = getCharRoot()
                    return r ~= nil and inRoom(r.Position)
                end)
            else
                local boss, part = findBoss()
                if boss then
                    local range = math.max(farmRange, 40)
                    if flatDist(pos, part.Position) > range then
                        autoOff()
                        setStatus("🚶 เดินเข้าหาบอส " .. name)
                        walkTo(part.Position, 4, range * 0.5, function() return not farmActive end)
                    else
                        if lp:GetAttribute("AutoOn") ~= true then
                            setBotRunning(true, { range = range, loot = farmLoot, openBoxes = false, attackLeader = true,
                                preset = farmPreset, hpPct = farmHpPct, isBossCombat = true })
                        end
                        pcall(function()
                            local ct = lp:FindFirstChild("CurrentTarget")
                            if ct and ct.Value ~= boss then ct.Value = boss end
                            local lockEv = lp:FindFirstChild("LumiLockTarget")
                            if lockEv then lockEv:Fire(boss) end
                            local rem = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("Remotes")
                            if rem and rem:FindFirstChild("SetTarget") then rem.SetTarget:FireServer(boss, false, true) end
                        end)
                        _G.GolfHub_AutoDrivingUntil = tick() + 1 -- standing to attack is not being stuck
                        setStatus("⚔ ตีบอสปลดประตู: " .. name)
                    end
                else
                    autoOff()
                    local nextAt = tonumber(workspace:GetAttribute("BossNext_" .. def.monsterId)) or 0
                    local left = math.max(0, math.ceil(nextAt - workspace:GetServerTimeNow()))
                    setStatus(left > 0 and string.format("⏳ รอ %s เกิดในห้อง (%d วินาที)", name, left)
                        or ("⏳ รอ " .. name .. " ในห้องบอส"))
                    -- wait inside the game's zone part (Boss02: 70 x 50 studs), which is far smaller than arenaRadius:
                    -- waiting by the exit door left BossAlive true with no boss model on the client
                    if flatDist(pos, center) > 20 then
                        walkTo(Vector3.new(0, center.Y, center.Z), 8, 10, function() return not farmActive or findBoss() ~= nil end)
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        task.wait(0.45)
    end
end

-- Level every class to 99: at 99 walk into a pillar back to town (free), change class at the Master Class NPC, farm on.
-- The game's own rules (PageJobClient, JobData): a class already played changes for free; the first time a class is
-- played costs ClassUnlockCost (50,000 Gold); a special class is unlocked with UnlockJob for its own Gold price once its
-- requirements are met (Req: named classes at Lv.70, or AnyCount classes at AnyLevel). The player asked for every class
-- at 99, so this unlocks with Gold when the next class needs it (never Diamonds or Robux), keeping RESERVE in hand.
_G.GolfHub_AllJobs = { on = false, skip = {}, npc = Vector3.new(31.41, 4.68, -1.59), stand = Vector3.new(26.5, 4.25, 2.5), RESERVE = 50000 }
do
    local A = _G.GolfHub_AllJobs
    local NAMES = { MuayThai = "Muay Thai", SwordGod = "Swordcaster" }
    local SPECIAL = { Alchemist = true, Samurai = true, Necromancer = true, MuayThai = true, SwordGod = true }
    function A.name(job) return NAMES[job] or tostring(job) end
    local function readJson(attr)
        local ok, t = pcall(function() return HttpService:JSONDecode(lp:GetAttribute(attr)) end)
        return (ok and type(t) == "table") and t or nil
    end

    local jobData, firstCost
    local function rules()
        if not jobData then
            pcall(function() jobData = require(game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").Shared.JobData) end)
            pcall(function()
                firstCost = tonumber(require(game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").Shared.GameConfig).ClassUnlockCost)
            end)
        end
        return jobData or {}, firstCost or 50000
    end

    -- todo = classes to level for free (closest to 99 first) · buy = classes that need Gold first, cheapest first
    -- ({ job, cost, unlock = Gold of UnlockJob, special }) · locked = requirements not met yet. nil = data not here yet.
    -- ctx = { job, levels, special, jobs, jobData, firstCost } replaces the live values for checks.
    function A.list(ctx)
        local levels = ctx and ctx.levels or readJson("JobLevelsJson")
        if not levels then return nil end
        local special = ctx and ctx.special or readJson("SpecialJobsJson") or {}
        local played = ctx and ctx.jobs or readJson("JobsJson") or {}
        local current = ctx and ctx.job or lp:GetAttribute("Job")
        if not current then return nil end
        local data, fee = rules()
        if ctx and ctx.jobData then data = ctx.jobData end
        if ctx and ctx.firstCost then fee = ctx.firstCost end
        local specials = type(data.Special) == "table" and data.Special or {}
        local function lv(job)
            local v = tonumber(levels[job]) or 0
            if job == current then v = math.max(v, tonumber(ctx and ctx.lv or lp:GetAttribute("Level")) or 0) end
            return v
        end
        local function reqMet(def)
            if type(def.Req) == "table" then
                for job, need in pairs(def.Req) do if lv(job) < (tonumber(need) or 0) then return false end end
            end
            if tonumber(def.AnyCount) then
                local n = 0
                for job in pairs(levels) do if lv(job) >= (tonumber(def.AnyLevel) or 0) then n = n + 1 end end
                if n < tonumber(def.AnyCount) then return false end
            end
            return true
        end
        local todo, buy, locked = {}, {}, {}
        for job in pairs(levels) do
            local level = lv(job)
            local isSpecial = SPECIAL[job] or specials[job] ~= nil
            if job ~= current and level < 99 then
                -- the same test as the game's button: played before = free
                local changeFee = (played[job] ~= nil or level > 0) and 0 or fee
                if isSpecial and special[job] ~= true then
                    local def = specials[job]
                    if def and reqMet(def) then
                        local price = tonumber(def.Gold) or 0
                        buy[#buy + 1] = { job = job, cost = price + changeFee, unlock = price, special = true }
                    else
                        locked[#locked + 1] = job
                    end
                elseif changeFee > 0 then
                    buy[#buy + 1] = { job = job, cost = changeFee, unlock = 0 }
                else
                    todo[#todo + 1] = { job = job, lv = level }
                end
            end
        end
        table.sort(todo, function(a, b) if a.lv ~= b.lv then return a.lv > b.lv end return a.job < b.job end)
        table.sort(buy, function(a, b) if a.cost ~= b.cost then return a.cost < b.cost end return a.job < b.job end)
        table.sort(locked)
        return todo, locked, buy
    end

    -- what to do once this class is at 99: { job, lv, cost, unlock } or "gold" (wait for Gold) or "done"
    function A.next(ctx)
        local todo, locked, buy = A.list(ctx)
        if not todo then return nil end
        if todo[1] then return todo[1] end
        if buy[1] then
            local gold = tonumber(ctx and ctx.gold or lp:GetAttribute("Silver")) or 0
            if gold - buy[1].cost >= A.RESERVE then return buy[1] end
            return "gold", buy[1]
        end
        if #locked > 0 then return "gold" end -- requirements are met by levelling the others: they are all 99 here
        return "done"
    end

    function A.due()
        if not (A.on and (tonumber(lp:GetAttribute("Level")) or 0) >= 99) then return false end
        local pick = A.next()
        return pick ~= nil and pick ~= "gold" -- short of Gold: keep farming this 99 for it
    end

    -- While levelling every class the player wants no item kept locked (their choice: after this, sale and dismantle
    -- treat these items like any other; worn gear stays protected by the sale/dismantle rules themselves).
    -- Same call as the bag's lock button: LockItem(ref, false).
    function A.unlockAll()
        if A.unlocking then return 0 end
        local locks = readJson("LocksJson")
        local remote = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("Remotes")
        remote = remote and remote:FindFirstChild("LockItem")
        if type(locks) ~= "table" or not remote then return 0 end
        A.unlocking = true
        local n = 0
        for ref, on in pairs(locks) do
            if on == true and A.on then
                pcall(function() remote:FireServer(ref, false) end)
                n = n + 1
                task.wait(0.15)
            end
        end
        A.unlocking = false
        if n > 0 and A.log then A.log("ปลดล็อกไอเทม " .. n .. " ชิ้น") end
        return n
    end

    function A.show(text)
        if A.label then pcall(function() A.label:UpdateName(text) end) end
    end

    function A.describe()
        local todo, locked, buy = A.list()
        if not todo then return "รอข้อมูลอาชีพจากเกม" end
        local parts = {}
        local mine = tonumber(lp:GetAttribute("Level")) or 99
        if mine < 99 then parts[1] = A.name(lp:GetAttribute("Job")) .. " " .. mine end
        for _, entry in ipairs(todo) do parts[#parts + 1] = A.name(entry.job) .. " " .. entry.lv end
        local text = #parts > 0 and ("เหลือ: " .. table.concat(parts, ", ")) or "อาชีพที่ปลดแล้วครบ 99"
        if #buy > 0 then
            local total, names = 0, {}
            for _, b in ipairs(buy) do total = total + b.cost names[#names + 1] = A.name(b.job) end
            text = text .. string.format(" · รอปลด %s (%s Gold)", table.concat(names, ", "), tostring(total))
        end
        if #locked > 0 then text = text .. " · ยังไม่ถึงเงื่อนไข: " .. #locked end
        return text
    end

    local function active() return farmActive and A.on end
    -- every step also goes to <player folder>/alljobs_log.txt, so a rotation that happened hours ago can be checked
    function A.log(text)
        pcall(function()
            local line = string.format("%s | %s Lv.%s | Gold %s | %s\n", os.date("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S"),
                tostring(lp:GetAttribute("Job")), tostring(lp:GetAttribute("Level")), tostring(lp:GetAttribute("Silver")), text)
            local file = (GOLFGUI.Folder or "GolfHub_HuntingTogether") .. "/alljobs_log.txt"
            if appendfile and isfile and isfile(file) then appendfile(file, line) else writefile(file, line) end
        end)
    end
    local function report(text)
        setStatus(text)
        A.show(text)
        if text ~= A.lastLogged then A.lastLogged = text A.log(text) end
    end

    local function goTown()
        if getCurrentActualZone() == "Town" then return true end
        setBotRunning(false)
        local here = getCharRoot()
        if here then
            local key, dist = _G.GolfHub_Pillars.start(here.Position, "Town")
            if here.Position.Z > -700 and (not key or math.abs(here.Position.Z) < dist) then
                -- the first fields: town itself is nearer than any pillar
                report("🚶 ครบ Lv.99 — เมืองอยู่ใกล้กว่าเสา เดินกลับเมือง")
                walkHighwayToZone("TownExit", active, setStatus)
                if getCurrentActualZone() == "Town" then return true end
                if not active() then return false end
            end
        end
        report("🌀 ครบ Lv.99 — เดินเข้าเสาวาร์ปกลับเมือง (ฟรี)")
        for _ = 1, 2 do
            if not active() then return false end
            if warpViaPillar("Town", setStatus, active) and getCurrentActualZone() == "Town" then return true end
            if getCurrentActualZone() == "Town" then return true end
        end
        if not active() then return false end
        report("🌀 เข้าเสาไม่ได้ — ใช้ Home Warp ของเกมกลับเมือง")
        return castHomeWarp() == true
    end

    function A.step()
        if (isPlayerInDungeon and isPlayerInDungeon()) or lp:GetAttribute("InWorldBoss") == true then task.wait(3) return end
        local hum = lp.Character and lp.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not hum or hum.Health <= 0 then task.wait(2) return end
        local pick, short = A.next()
        if pick == nil then report("⏳ รอข้อมูลอาชีพจากเกม") task.wait(3) return end
        if pick == "gold" then
            report("💰 Gold ยังไม่พอปลด " .. (short and (A.name(short.job) .. " (" .. short.cost .. ")") or "อาชีพต่อไป") .. " — ฟาร์มต่อ")
            task.wait(5)
            return
        end
        if type(pick) == "table" and tick() < (A.skip[pick.job] or 0) then
            report("⏳ เปลี่ยนอาชีพยังไม่ได้ — รอลองใหม่")
            task.wait(5)
            return
        end
        if not goTown() then task.wait(3) return end

        if pick == "done" then
            report("🏆 ครบ Lv.99 ทุกอาชีพแล้ว — หยุดฟาร์มในเมือง")
            -- defer, not spawn: spawn runs at once inside the farm thread, which then cannot be cancelled
            task.defer(function()
                if A.toggle then A.toggle:Set(false) end
                if toggleAutoFarm then toggleAutoFarm:Set(false) end
                report("🏆 ครบ Lv.99 ทุกอาชีพแล้ว")
            end)
            task.wait(1)
            return
        end

        local cost = pick.cost or 0
        local title = A.name(pick.job) .. (pick.lv and (" (Lv." .. pick.lv .. ")") or (" (ปลด " .. cost .. " Gold)"))
        setBotRunning(false)
        report("🚶 เดินไปหา Master Class เพื่อเปลี่ยนเป็น " .. title)
        local function atNpc()
            local r = getCharRoot()
            return r ~= nil and Vector3.new(r.Position.X - A.npc.X, 0, r.Position.Z - A.npc.Z).Magnitude <= 9
        end
        -- the game wants free bag slots to change class (the worn gear goes back into the bag)
        local function bagFree()
            local used, cap = tonumber(lp:GetAttribute("BagSlotsUsed")), tonumber(lp:GetAttribute("BagSlotsMax"))
            return (used and cap) and (cap - used) or math.huge
        end
        if bagFree() < 15 then
            if autoSellEnabled and _G.GolfHub_SellAtShop and tick() - (A.soldAt or 0) > 120 then
                A.soldAt = tick()
                report("🎒 กระเป๋าต้องว่างก่อนเปลี่ยนอาชีพ — แวะขายที่ Barnaby")
                isSellingNow = true
                pcall(_G.GolfHub_SellAtShop)
                isSellingNow = false
            end
            if not active() then return end
            if bagFree() < 15 then
                report("🎒 กระเป๋าว่างไม่ถึง 15 ช่อง — ยังเปลี่ยนอาชีพไม่ได้ ช่วยจัดกระเป๋าก่อน")
                task.wait(5)
                return
            end
        end
        if not atNpc() then walkTo(A.stand, 30, 3, function() return not active() end) end
        if not active() then return end
        if not atNpc() then task.wait(1) return end
        if lp:GetAttribute("CanChangeJob") == false then report("⏳ เกมยังไม่ให้เปลี่ยนอาชีพ") task.wait(4) return end

        -- plan again right before paying or changing: still the same class at the same price?
        local fresh = A.next()
        if type(fresh) ~= "table" or fresh.job ~= pick.job or (fresh.cost or 0) ~= cost then return end
        local remotes = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("Remotes")
        local change = remotes and remotes:FindFirstChild("ChangeJob")
        if not change then task.wait(3) return end
        local goldStart = tonumber(lp:GetAttribute("Silver"))
        if cost > 0 and (not goldStart or goldStart - cost < A.RESERVE) then return end

        if (pick.unlock or 0) > 0 then
            -- special class: the game's "Unlock" button (UnlockJob), then a normal change
            local unlockJob = remotes:FindFirstChild("UnlockJob")
            if not unlockJob then task.wait(3) return end
            report("🔓 ปลด " .. A.name(pick.job) .. " — " .. pick.unlock .. " Gold")
            unlockJob:FireServer(pick.job)
            local t0 = tick()
            while tick() - t0 < 6 do
                local sj = readJson("SpecialJobsJson")
                if sj and sj[pick.job] == true then break end
                task.wait(0.2)
            end
            local sj = readJson("SpecialJobsJson")
            if not (sj and sj[pick.job] == true) then
                A.skip[pick.job] = tick() + 120
                report("⚠️ เกมไม่ให้ปลด " .. A.name(pick.job) .. " — ลองใหม่ใน 2 นาที")
                return
            end
        end
        report("🔄 เปลี่ยนเป็น " .. title)
        local goldBefore = tonumber(lp:GetAttribute("Silver"))
        -- a class without its own saved skill pattern reads it from the game instead of copying the last class's
        hasUserTouchedSkillSlots = false
        -- The game then asks "Save your gear to Loadout?" (LoadoutAsk). The answer is "No, to bag" (the player's choice):
        -- press that button of the game's own popup, which sends ChangeJob(job, "skip").
        local function pressNoToBag()
            local popup = lp.PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("HuntLoadout")
            local layer = popup and popup:FindFirstChild("PopupLayer", true)
            if not layer then return false end
            for _, b in ipairs(layer:GetDescendants()) do
                if b:IsA("GuiButton") and b.Name == "KitButton" then
                    local label = b:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("TextLabel", true)
                    local text = label and label.Text or ""
                    if text:find("กระเป๋า", 1, true) or text:lower():find("bag", 1, true) then
                        for _, c in ipairs(getconnections(b.MouseButton1Click)) do pcall(function() c:Fire() end) end
                        return true
                    end
                end
            end
            return false
        end
        change:FireServer(pick.job)
        local started, answered = tick(), false
        while tick() - started < 8 and lp:GetAttribute("Job") ~= pick.job do
            if not answered and tick() - started > 0.6 then
                answered = pressNoToBag()
                if not answered and tick() - started > 3 then
                    change:FireServer(pick.job, "skip") -- the popup never showed: same answer straight to the game
                    answered = true
                end
            end
            task.wait(0.2)
        end
        local goldNow = tonumber(lp:GetAttribute("Silver"))
        if goldStart and goldNow and goldStart - goldNow > cost + 5000 then
            -- paid more than the game's own price list says: never again without the player
            if A.toggle then A.toggle:Set(false) end
            A.on = false
            report(string.format("⚠️ เกมคิด %d Gold (คาดไว้ %d) — ปิดระบบวนอาชีพแล้ว", goldStart - goldNow, cost))
            return
        end
        if lp:GetAttribute("Job") ~= pick.job then
            A.skip[pick.job] = tick() + 60
            report("⚠️ เกมไม่รับการเปลี่ยนเป็น " .. title .. " — ลองใหม่ใน 1 นาที")
            task.wait(2)
            return
        end
        -- Level follows Job a moment later; planning before that would be the trip of the old class
        local changed = tick()
        while tick() - changed < 6 and (tonumber(lp:GetAttribute("Level")) or 99) >= 99 do task.wait(0.2) end
        task.wait(1)
        pcall(function()
            syncSkillSlotsFromGame()
            syncSkillSelectionToBotJson()
        end)
        report("✅ เปลี่ยนเป็น " .. title .. " แล้ว — ไปฟาร์ม")
        A.show(A.describe())
    end
end

local function farmLoop()
    while farmActive do
        _G.GolfHub_FarmNeedZoneReeval = false
        -- The class rotation touches the game's GUIs, remotes and files. farmLoop has no pcall of its own, so one error in
        -- it ended the thread: the Auto Farm switch still showed ON while the character stood still. An error now only
        -- costs 5 s, is written to the console and alljobs_log.txt, and the loop carries on.
        local okDue, isDue = pcall(_G.GolfHub_AllJobs.due)
        if okDue and isDue then
            local okStep, stepErr = pcall(_G.GolfHub_AllJobs.step)
            if not okStep then
                warn("[GOLF HUB] class rotation error: " .. tostring(stepErr))
                pcall(_G.GolfHub_AllJobs.log, "error: " .. tostring(stepErr))
                setStatus("⚠️ ระบบวนอาชีพ error — ลองใหม่ใน 5 วิ")
                task.wait(5)
            end
            task.wait(0.3)
            continue
        end
        local lv = getLevel()
        setLvLabel(tostring(lv))
        local targetZone = resolveFarmTargetZone(lv)
        local curZone    = getCurrentActualZone()
        -- A trip is only good while its zone is still the target. A job change moves the character to town a moment
        -- before Level changes, so a trip planned then is for the old job (a Lv.1 was walked at the Field02 level lock).
        local function planStillValid()
            return farmActive and resolveFarmTargetZone(getLevel()).zone == targetZone.zone
        end
        setZoneLabel(curZone)

        -- Auto Sell Trigger before traveling: If bag is already full, sell first!
        if farmActive and autoSellEnabled and isBagFullOrOverThreshold() and not isSellingNow then
            executeSellRoutine(targetZone.zone, planStillValid, setStatus)
            if farmActive and not planStillValid() then task.wait(0.3) continue end
            curZone = getCurrentActualZone()
        end

        -- Check if player needs to travel to the target zone (uses nearby official Warp Pillar if faster, then walks in)
        local doorBoss, doorBossName = _G.GolfHub_BlockingBoss(targetZone.zone)
        if farmActive and (curZone ~= targetZone.zone or doorBoss) then
            setStatus("🌀 เดินทางไปโซนฟาร์ม: " .. targetZone.name .. " (Lv." .. lv .. ")")
            setBotRunning(false) -- the game's [F] AUTO bot chases monsters beside the road and drags the character off the route
            local result, bossKey, bossName
            local doorDef = doorBoss and BOSS_TARGET_DEFS and BOSS_TARGET_DEFS[doorBoss]
            local here = doorDef and getCharRoot()
            -- "at the room" = from 60 studs before its entrance up to the closed door (BlockingBoss checked the door side).
            -- The highway skips waypoints behind the character, so from inside the room it would only push the door.
            if doorBoss and (curZone == targetZone.zone or (here and here.Position.Z <= doorDef.doorZ + 60)) then
                -- already at the boss room, in front of the closed door: nothing to travel, fight that boss
                result, bossKey, bossName = "FIGHT_BOSS", doorBoss, doorBossName
            else
                result, bossKey, bossName = travelToZoneSmart(targetZone.zone, planStillValid, setStatus, true)
                if farmActive and not planStillValid() then
                    setStatus("🔄 โซนเป้าหมายเปลี่ยน — คำนวณเส้นทางใหม่")
                    task.wait(0.3)
                    continue
                end
            end

            -- If encounter a locked boss along the highway, fight it to unlock the next zone in real time
            if result == "FIGHT_BOSS" and farmActive then
                setStatus("⚔ ตีบอสปลดประตูโซน: " .. (bossName or bossKey))
                _G.GolfHub_FightDoorBoss(bossKey, bossName, targetZone.zone)
                task.wait(1.5)
                -- refresh boss gates immediately & continue walking into the unlocked zone
                local gates = getBossGates()
                if lblBoss1 then pcall(function() lblBoss1:Set(gates.Boss01 and "✅ Cleared" or "🔒 Locked", gates.Boss01 and "run" or "bad") end) end
                if lblBoss2 then pcall(function() lblBoss2:Set(gates.Boss02 and "✅ Cleared" or "🔒 Locked", gates.Boss02 and "run" or "bad") end) end
                if lblBoss3 then pcall(function() lblBoss3:Set(gates.Boss03 and "✅ Cleared" or "🔒 Locked", gates.Boss03 and "run" or "bad") end) end
                if lblBoss4 then pcall(function() lblBoss4:Set(gates.Boss04 and "✅ Cleared" or "🔒 Locked", gates.Boss04 and "run" or "bad") end) end
                continue
            end
        end

        -- In Zone: Farm monsters with move-while-attacking
        setStatus("✅ ฟาร์มอยู่: " .. targetZone.name .. " (Lv." .. lv .. ")")
        setZoneLabel(targetZone.name)

        setBotRunning(true, {
            range        = farmRange,
            loot         = farmLoot,
            openBoxes    = farmOpenBoxes,
            attackLeader = farmAtkLeader,
            preset       = farmPreset,
            hpPct        = farmHpPct,
        })

        -- Update boss status
        local gates = getBossGates()
        if lblBoss1 then pcall(function() lblBoss1:Set(gates.Boss01 and "✅ Cleared" or "🔒 Locked", gates.Boss01 and "run" or "bad") end) end
        if lblBoss2 then pcall(function() lblBoss2:Set(gates.Boss02 and "✅ Cleared" or "🔒 Locked", gates.Boss02 and "run" or "bad") end) end
        if lblBoss3 then pcall(function() lblBoss3:Set(gates.Boss03 and "✅ Cleared" or "🔒 Locked", gates.Boss03 and "run" or "bad") end) end
        if lblBoss4 then pcall(function() lblBoss4:Set(gates.Boss04 and "✅ Cleared" or "🔒 Locked", gates.Boss04 and "run" or "bad") end) end

        -- Farm loop until real-time level-up / zone change
        local pollStart = tick()
        local lastAutoEquipTick = tick()
        local lastFarmAutoCheckTick = 0
        while farmActive do
            -- คงสถานะปุ่ม [F] AUTO เปิดไว้ตลอดขณะฟาร์มในโซน (เผื่อตายแล้วเกิดใหม่หรือหลุดปิดกลางทาง)
            if (not isSellingNow) and lp:GetAttribute("AutoOn") ~= true and (tick() - lastFarmAutoCheckTick >= 1.5) then
                local curZNow = getCurrentActualZone()
                if curZNow ~= "Town" then
                    lastFarmAutoCheckTick = tick()
                    setBotRunning(true, {
                        range        = farmRange,
                        loot         = farmLoot,
                        openBoxes    = farmOpenBoxes,
                        attackLeader = farmAtkLeader,
                        preset       = farmPreset,
                        hpPct        = farmHpPct,
                    })
                end
            end

            _G.GolfHub_FarmNeedReplan = false
            approachAndAttack(farmStopDist, farmRange, farmLoot, farmOpenBoxes, farmAtkLeader, targetZone.zone)
            if _G.GolfHub_FarmNeedReplan then
                -- respawned in town / far away: plan the trip again (warp pillar if faster) instead of walking it
                _G.GolfHub_FarmNeedReplan = false
                setBotRunning(false)
                break
            end
            task.wait(0.45)

            -- Real-time level-up / unlock / dropdown zone transition check (instant — zero delay!)
            if _G.GolfHub_FarmNeedZoneReeval then
                _G.GolfHub_FarmNeedZoneReeval = false
                local newLv = getLevel()
                setLvLabel(tostring(newLv))
                local desiredZone = resolveFarmTargetZone(newLv)
                if desiredZone and desiredZone.zone ~= targetZone.zone then
                    setStatus("🔓 ปลดล็อคโซนใหม่: " .. desiredZone.name .. " (Lv." .. newLv .. ") — กำลังเดินไป...")
                    break
                end
            end

            -- Auto Sell Trigger when bag item count >= sellThreshold
            if autoSellEnabled and isBagFullOrOverThreshold() and not isSellingNow then
                executeSellRoutine(targetZone.zone, planStillValid, setStatus)
                if autoEquipAlways and equipBestGearForCurrentJob then
                    task.spawn(function() equipBestGearForCurrentJob(true) end)
                end
            end

            -- Periodic Auto Equip: checks every 35s for newly dropped weapons / gear
            if autoEquipAlways and tick() - lastAutoEquipTick > 120 then
                lastAutoEquipTick = tick()
                task.spawn(function()
                    if equipBestGearForCurrentJob then
                        equipBestGearForCurrentJob(true)
                    end
                end)
            end

            if tick() - pollStart > 3 then
                pollStart = tick()
                if select(2, pcall(_G.GolfHub_AllJobs.due)) == true then break end -- this class is done: town, next class (an error here keeps farming)
                local newLv = getLevel()
                setLvLabel(tostring(newLv))
                setStatus("✅ ฟาร์มอยู่: " .. targetZone.name .. " (Lv." .. newLv .. ")")
                -- เช็คว่าผู้ใช้เปลี่ยนแมพเป้าหมาย หรือเลเวลอัปถึงโซนถัดไปแล้ว
                local desiredZone = resolveFarmTargetZone(newLv)
                if desiredZone and desiredZone.zone ~= targetZone.zone then
                    break
                end
                if not targetZone.endZone and newLv >= targetZone.nextLv then
                    break
                end
                local checkCurZone = getCurrentActualZone()
                if _G.GolfHub_BlockingBoss(targetZone.zone) then break end -- a closed boss door is in the way
                if checkCurZone ~= targetZone.zone then
                    if checkCurZone == "Town" then break end
                    -- far from the zone (respawned and already out of town when this 3 s check ran): plan the trip
                    -- with pillars. The farm patrol used to walk the whole map on foot, shown as "farming".
                    local farRoot, centre = getCharRoot(), nil
                    for _, wp in ipairs(ROUTE_WAYPOINTS) do
                        if wp.id == targetZone.zone then centre = wp.pos break end
                    end
                    if farRoot and centre and math.abs(farRoot.Position.Z - centre.Z) > 250 then break end
                end
            end
        end
    end

    setBotRunning(false)
    setStatus("⏹ หยุดฟาร์มแล้ว")
end
