local function getClassPriorityText(job)
    local arc = CLASS_ARCHETYPES[job] or CLASS_ARCHETYPES.Archer
    return arc.buildFocus or "Balanced Stats"
end

local function getEquippedWeaponText()
    local weaponKey = lp:GetAttribute("WeaponId")
    if not weaponKey or weaponKey == "" then return "None" end
    local rep = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
    local itemData = require(rep.Shared.ItemData)
    local raw = lp:GetAttribute("GearJson") or "{}"
    local ok, gj = pcall(function() return HttpService:JSONDecode(raw) end)
    if ok and type(gj) == "table" and gj[weaponKey] then
        local g = gj[weaponKey]
        local def = itemData[g.Id]
        if def and def.Name then
            local refine = lp:GetAttribute("RefineWeapon") or 0
            local refTag = refine > 0 and (" +" .. refine) or ""
            return def.Name .. refTag
        end
        return g.Id or weaponKey
    end
    local def = itemData[weaponKey]
    return def and (def.Name or weaponKey) or weaponKey
end

local function scoreGearForJob(gearKey, gearData, jobName, playerLevel)
    local rep = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
    local itemData = require(rep.Shared.ItemData)
    local def = itemData[gearData.Id]
    if not def then return -999, "unknown" end

    -- Check level requirement (against base Level)
    if def.ReqLevel and def.ReqLevel > playerLevel then
        return -999, "level-locked"
    end

    -- Weapon must strictly match the Class!
    if def.Kind == "weapon" then
        if not def.Jobs or not table.find(def.Jobs, jobName) then
            return -999, "wrong-job"
        end
    end

    local arc = CLASS_ARCHETYPES[jobName] or CLASS_ARCHETYPES.Archer
    local score = 0

    -- 1. Base Tier & Rarity Scaling
    score = score + (gearData.T or def.Tier or 1) * 150
    score = score + (gearData.R or 0) * 35

    -- 2. Base Main Stat (M) & Weapon Refine
    if def.Kind == "weapon" then
        score = score + (gearData.M or 0) * 3.5
        local weaponRefine = lp:GetAttribute("RefineWeapon") or 0
        score = score + (weaponRefine * 25)
    else
        score = score + (gearData.M or 0) * 0.8
    end

    -- 3. Armor Set Affinity & Opposing Set Penalty
    local setId = gearData.S or def.SetId
    if not setId then
        local nm = (def.Name or gearData.Id or ""):lower()
        if nm:find("mage") or nm:find("robe") or nm:find("wizard") then
            setId = "archmage"
        elseif nm:find("warrior") or nm:find("knight") or nm:find("plate") or nm:find("hunter") or nm:find("archer") then
            setId = "warrior"
        end
    end

    if setId then
        if arc.preferredSet and setId == arc.preferredSet then
            score = score + 600 -- Huge boost to lock in 5-piece class set!
        elseif arc.penaltySets and arc.penaltySets[setId] then
            score = score - 3000 -- Drastic penalty to completely eliminate wrong sets (e.g. Archmage on Archer)!
        end
    end

    -- 4. Ring / Accessory Affinity
    local slot = def.Slot or (def.Kind == "weapon" and "RightHand" or "Unknown")
    if slot == "Acc" or slot == "Acc1" or slot == "Acc2" then
        if arc.ringFilter then
            if (gearData.Id and gearData.Id:find(arc.ringFilter)) or (def.Name and def.Name:lower():find(arc.ringFilter:find("fire") and "atk" or "matk")) then
                score = score + 500
            else
                score = score - 1500 -- Ban wrong ring type
            end
        end
    end

    -- 5. Option stats matching Class priorities
    if gearData.O and type(gearData.O) == "table" then
        for statKey, statVal in pairs(gearData.O) do
            local w = arc.primaryStats[statKey] or arc.bannedStats[statKey] or 1
            score = score + statVal * w
        end
    end

    return score, slot, def, setId
end

local function getBestGearPicks(targetJob)
    targetJob = targetJob or lp:GetAttribute("Job") or "Archer"
    local arc = CLASS_ARCHETYPES[targetJob] or CLASS_ARCHETYPES.Archer
    local playerLevel = lp:GetAttribute("Level") or 99
    local raw = lp:GetAttribute("GearJson") or "{}"
    local gearJson = {}
    pcall(function() gearJson = HttpService:JSONDecode(raw) end)

    local bestBySlot = {
        RightHand = nil,
        HeadTop   = nil,
        HeadMid   = nil,
        Body      = nil,
        Garment   = nil,
        Shoes     = nil,
    }
    local allAccessories = {}

    -- Group armor candidates by slot and by SetId
    local armorCandidates = {
        HeadTop = {}, HeadMid = {}, Body = {}, Garment = {}, Shoes = {}
    }
    local bestWeapon = nil

    for gearKey, g in pairs(gearJson) do
        local score, slot, def, setId = scoreGearForJob(gearKey, g, targetJob, playerLevel)
        if score > 0 then
            local entry = {
                key     = gearKey,
                id      = g.Id,
                name    = def.Name or g.Id,
                tier    = g.T or def.Tier or 1,
                rarity  = g.R or 0,
                score   = score,
                options = g.O or {},
                def     = def,
                setId   = setId,
            }

            if slot == "RightHand" then
                if not bestWeapon or score > bestWeapon.score then
                    bestWeapon = entry
                end
            elseif slot == "Acc" or slot == "Acc1" or slot == "Acc2" then
                table.insert(allAccessories, entry)
            elseif armorCandidates[slot] then
                table.insert(armorCandidates[slot], entry)
            end
        end
    end

    bestBySlot.RightHand = bestWeapon

    -- 5-Piece Set Solver: Prioritize full matching set (e.g. SetWarrior 5/5)
    local armorSlots = { "HeadTop", "HeadMid", "Body", "Garment", "Shoes" }
    local hasFullPreferredSet = true

    if arc.preferredSet then
        for _, s in ipairs(armorSlots) do
            local foundMatching = false
            for _, item in ipairs(armorCandidates[s]) do
                if item.setId == arc.preferredSet then
                    foundMatching = true
                    break
                end
            end
            if not foundMatching then
                hasFullPreferredSet = false
                break
            end
        end
    else
        hasFullPreferredSet = false
    end

    for _, s in ipairs(armorSlots) do
        local candidates = armorCandidates[s]
        table.sort(candidates, function(a, b) return a.score > b.score end)
        if hasFullPreferredSet then
            -- Choose the best piece within the preferred set
            for _, item in ipairs(candidates) do
                if item.setId == arc.preferredSet then
                    bestBySlot[s] = item
                    break
                end
            end
        else
            -- Fallback: take the overall best candidate
            bestBySlot[s] = candidates[1]
        end
    end

    table.sort(allAccessories, function(a, b) return a.score > b.score end)
    local bestAcc1 = allAccessories[1]
    local bestAcc2 = allAccessories[2]

    return bestBySlot, bestAcc1, bestAcc2, targetJob
end

equipBestGearForCurrentJob = function(silent)
    local Remotes = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("Remotes")
    if not Remotes or not Remotes:FindFirstChild("EquipItem") then return false, "No remote" end

    local job = lp:GetAttribute("Job") or "Unknown"

    -- 0. เปิดกล่องการ์ดที่ตกค้างในกระเป๋าให้อัตโนมัติ (CardOpenAll)
    pcall(function()
        if Remotes:FindFirstChild("CardOpenAll") then
            Remotes.CardOpenAll:InvokeServer()
        end
    end)

    -- 1. สวมใส่อุปกรณ์และอาวุธที่ดีที่สุดตรงสายผ่านเซิร์ฟเวอร์เกม (ข้ามเมื่อเปิดใส่ชุดดาวอัตโนมัติ เพื่อไม่ให้สวมทับ)
    if not (_G.GolfHub_StarEquip and _G.GolfHub_StarEquip.on) then
        Remotes.EquipItem:FireServer("@best")
    end

    -- 2. คัดเลือกและสวมใส่การ์ดที่ดีที่สุดลงทุก Slot แบบไดนามิก (Smart Card Equip)
    if autoEquipCards and Remotes:FindFirstChild("CardAction") then
        pcall(function()
            local itemsRaw = lp:GetAttribute("ItemsJson")
            if itemsRaw and #itemsRaw > 2 then
                local items = HttpService:JSONDecode(itemsRaw)
                local candidateCards = {}
                for itemId, count in pairs(items) do
                    if itemId:find("^card_") and not itemId:find("^fragment_") and (tonumber(count) or 0) > 0 then
                        table.insert(candidateCards, itemId)
                    end
                end
                -- เรียงลำดับการ์ด: การ์ดบอสใหญ่ (Big Boss) -> การ์ดบอส (Boss) -> การ์ดลีดเดอร์ (Leader) -> การ์ดมอนทั่วไป
                table.sort(candidateCards, function(a, b)
                    local aScore = (a:find("bigboss") and 100) or (a:find("boss") and 80) or (a:find("leader") and 50) or 10
                    local bScore = (b:find("bigboss") and 100) or (b:find("boss") and 80) or (b:find("leader") and 50) or 10
                    return aScore > bScore
                end)
                for _, cardId in ipairs(candidateCards) do
                    Remotes.CardAction:FireServer("smart", cardId)
                end
            end
        end)
    end

    -- 3. รวมการ์ดขยะที่ซ้ำกัน (Fuse All) เพื่อรับกล่องสุ่มการ์ดระดับสูง แล้วเปิดกล่องใหม่ทันที
    pcall(function()
        if Remotes:FindFirstChild("CardFuseAll") then
            Remotes.CardFuseAll:InvokeServer()
            if Remotes:FindFirstChild("CardOpenAll") then
                Remotes.CardOpenAll:InvokeServer()
            end
        end
    end)

    task.wait(0.25)

    -- 4. Fallback: ตรวจสอบและสวมใส่อาวุธประจำอาชีพให้แน่นอน
    local curWeapon = lp:GetAttribute("WeaponId")
    if not curWeapon or curWeapon == "" then
        local bestPicks = getBestGearPicks()
        if bestPicks and bestPicks.RightHand then
            if Remotes:FindFirstChild("EquipWeapon") then
                Remotes.EquipWeapon:FireServer(bestPicks.RightHand.key)
            elseif Remotes:FindFirstChild("EquipItem") then
                Remotes.EquipItem:FireServer(bestPicks.RightHand.key)
            end
            task.wait(0.2)
        end
    end

    -- 5. จัดสรรแต้มสเตตัส (Auto Stat Allocation) ตาม Class อัตโนมัติเมื่อมีแต้มคงเหลือ
    pcall(function()
        local statPts = lp:GetAttribute("StatPoints") or 0
        if statPts > 0 and Remotes:FindFirstChild("AllocateStat") then
            local arc = CLASS_ARCHETYPES[job] or CLASS_ARCHETYPES.Archer
            local prioStats = {}
            for st, weight in pairs(arc.primaryStats or {}) do
                if weight > 10 and (st == "STR" or st == "AGI" or st == "VIT" or st == "INT" or st == "DEX" or st == "LUK") then
                    table.insert(prioStats, {stat = st, weight = weight})
                end
            end
            table.sort(prioStats, function(a, b) return a.weight > b.weight end)
            if #prioStats > 0 then
                for _, p in ipairs(prioStats) do
                    Remotes.AllocateStat:FireServer(p.stat, 10)
                end
            end
        end
    end)

    -- 6. กดรับของรางวัล Battle Pass & Daily Login อัตโนมัติเมื่อพร้อมรับ
    pcall(function()
        if Remotes:FindFirstChild("DailyClaim") then
            Remotes.DailyClaim:FireServer()
        end
        local bpReady = lp:GetAttribute("BattlePassReady") or 0
        if bpReady > 0 and Remotes:FindFirstChild("BattlePassAction") then
            for tier = 1, 30 do
                Remotes.BattlePassAction:FireServer("claim", tier, "free")
            end
        end
    end)

    if updateEquipmentUiLabels then
        updateEquipmentUiLabels()
    end

    if not silent and Window and Window.Notify then
        local curCP = tostring(lp:GetAttribute("CombatPower") or 0)
        local wepText = getEquippedWeaponText()
        Window:Notify({
            Title       = "Auto Equip สำเร็จ!",
            Description = string.format("✅ สวมใส่อาวุธ (%s) และอุปกรณ์ %s ครบเซ็ต (CP: %s)", wepText, job, curCP),
            Lifetime    = 4,
        })
    end

    return true, 8, job
end

local valCurrentClass, valCombatPower, valBuildFocus, valSetSynergy, valEquipWeapon
local recWeapon, recHeadTop, recHeadMid, recBody, recGarment, recShoes, recAcc1, recAcc2

updateEquipmentUiLabels = function()
    local job = lp:GetAttribute("Job") or "Unknown"
    local cp = tostring(lp:GetAttribute("CombatPower") or 0)
    local arc = CLASS_ARCHETYPES[job] or CLASS_ARCHETYPES.Archer
    local bestPicks, acc1, acc2 = getBestGearPicks(job)

    if valCurrentClass then pcall(function() valCurrentClass:UpdateName(job) end) end
    if valCombatPower then pcall(function() valCombatPower:UpdateName(cp .. " CP") end) end
    if valBuildFocus then pcall(function() valBuildFocus:UpdateName(arc.buildFocus or "Balanced") end) end
    if valSetSynergy then pcall(function() valSetSynergy:UpdateName(arc.setBonusText or "-") end) end
    if valEquipWeapon then pcall(function() valEquipWeapon:UpdateName(getEquippedWeaponText()) end) end

    local function formatRec(item)
        if not item then return "None" end
        local setTag = item.setId and (" [" .. tostring(item.setId):upper() .. "]") or ""
        return item.name .. setTag .. " (Score: " .. math.floor(item.score) .. ")"
    end

    if recWeapon then pcall(function() recWeapon:UpdateName(formatRec(bestPicks.RightHand)) end) end
    if recHeadTop then pcall(function() recHeadTop:UpdateName(formatRec(bestPicks.HeadTop)) end) end
    if recHeadMid then pcall(function() recHeadMid:UpdateName(formatRec(bestPicks.HeadMid)) end) end
    if recBody then pcall(function() recBody:UpdateName(formatRec(bestPicks.Body)) end) end
    if recGarment then pcall(function() recGarment:UpdateName(formatRec(bestPicks.Garment)) end) end
    if recShoes then pcall(function() recShoes:UpdateName(formatRec(bestPicks.Shoes)) end) end
    if recAcc1 then pcall(function() recAcc1:UpdateName(formatRec(acc1)) end) end
    if recAcc2 then pcall(function() recAcc2:UpdateName(formatRec(acc2)) end) end
end

task.wait() -- yield point: spread core engine setup over frames
-- ====================================================================
-- AUTO SELL & MERCHANT (BARNABY) ENGINE
-- ====================================================================

local function updateSellUiLabels()
    local count = getBagItemCount()
    local maxSlots = (lp and lp:GetAttribute("BagSlotsMax")) or 240
    if valBagCount then
        pcall(function()
            local pct = math.floor((count / math.max(1, maxSlots)) * 100)
            local thresh = tonumber(sellThreshold) or 220
            valBagCount:UpdateName(string.format("%d / %d slots (%d%%) [ขายเมื่อถึง %d ชิ้น]", count, maxSlots, pct, thresh))
        end)
    end
end

pcall(function()
    if lp then
        _G.GolfHub_Conn(lp:GetAttributeChangedSignal("BagSlotsUsed"), updateSellUiLabels)
        _G.GolfHub_Conn(lp:GetAttributeChangedSignal("BagSlotsMax"), updateSellUiLabels)
    end
end)

task.spawn(function()
    local myGen = _G.GolfHub_Gen
    while _G.GolfHub_Gen == myGen do
        task.wait(10.0) -- backstop only: the bag attributes above and the sell sliders already refresh the labels
        pcall(updateSellUiLabels)
    end
end)


local function getBarnabyNpc()
    local map = workspace:FindFirstChild("Map")
    local npcs = map and map:FindFirstChild("NPCs")
    if npcs then
        local merch = npcs:FindFirstChild("merchant") or npcs:FindFirstChild("Barnaby")
        if merch then
            local part = merch:FindFirstChild("Torso") or merch:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or merch:FindFirstChildOfClass("BasePart")
            if part then return merch, part.Position end
        end
    end
    local rootNpcs = workspace:FindFirstChild("NPCs")
    if rootNpcs then
        local merch = rootNpcs:FindFirstChild("merchant") or rootNpcs:FindFirstChild("Barnaby")
        if merch then
            local part = merch:FindFirstChild("Torso") or merch:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or merch:FindFirstChildOfClass("BasePart")
            if part then return merch, part.Position end
        end
    end
    -- Fallback static coordinates of Barnaby (Merchant) in Town
    return nil, TOWN_COORDS.BarnabyShop
end

-- What may never be sold or salvaged. The formats are the game's own (ShopScreenClient / DismantleT6Client):
--   LocksJson     = { [gear ref or item id] = true }   WeaponId = the weapon in hand (not part of EquipJson)
--   EquipJson     = { slot = ref }                     OtherWornJson = { [ref] = job }
--   LoadoutJson   = saved loadouts holding refs        RefineJson = { [ref] = +level } (gear.R is not the refine level)
-- Returns nil when the data cannot be read: the caller then sells / salvages nothing.

_G.GolfHub_STAR_SETS = {
    { "⭐ ขาว (Warrior)", "warrior" },
    { "⭐ ม่วง (Archmage)", "archmage" },
    { "⭐ น้ำเงิน (Guardian)", "guardian" },
    { "⭐ เขียว (Gale)", "gale" },
    { "⭐ เหลือง (Lucky Star)", "luckystar" },
    { "⭐ ฟ้า (Azure Star)", "azurestar" },
    { "⭐ ดำ (Black Star)", "blackstar" },
    { "⭐ แดง (Bloodlord)", "bloodlord" },
}

_G.GolfHub_StarBetter = function(a, b)
    if not a then return false end
    if not b then return true end
    local ta, tb = tonumber(a.T) or 0, tonumber(b.T) or 0
    if ta ~= tb then return ta > tb end
    local ma, mb = tonumber(a.M) or 0, tonumber(b.M) or 0
    if ma ~= mb then return ma > mb end
    local sa, sb = 0, 0
    for _, v in pairs(type(a.O) == "table" and a.O or {}) do sa = sa + (tonumber(v) or 0) end
    for _, v in pairs(type(b.O) == "table" and b.O or {}) do sb = sb + (tonumber(v) or 0) end
    return sa > sb
end

-- Star lock: for each chosen set ("star", gear.S) the BEST piece of each kind (gear Id without its _tN tier suffix:
-- mask, bow, ring_fire, ...). Better = higher tier, then higher main value (base damage / defence, gear.M), then higher
-- sum of its options (gear.O). With tiers chosen, only pieces of those tiers take part. Returns { [ref] = "set|kind" }.
_G.GolfHub_StarBest = function(gear)
    local sl = _G.GolfHub_StarLock
    if not (sl and sl.on and type(sl.sets) == "table" and next(sl.sets) and type(gear) == "table") then return {} end
    local tiers = (type(sl.tiers) == "table" and next(sl.tiers)) and sl.tiers or nil
    local better = _G.GolfHub_StarBetter
    local bestOf = {}
    for ref, g in pairs(gear) do
        if type(g) == "table" and g.S and sl.sets[g.S] and type(g.Id) == "string"
            and (not tiers or tiers[tonumber(g.T) or 0]) then
            local key = g.S .. "|" .. (g.Id:gsub("_t%d+$", ""))
            local cur = bestOf[key]
            if not cur or better(g, gear[cur]) then bestOf[key] = ref end
        end
    end
    local out = {}
    for key, ref in pairs(bestOf) do out[ref] = key end
    return out
end

_G.GolfHub_SafeGearList = function(maxTier, keepOtherWorn)
    local function read(name)
        local raw = lp:GetAttribute(name)
        if type(raw) ~= "string" then return nil end
        local ok, t = pcall(function() return HttpService:JSONDecode(raw) end)
        if ok and type(t) == "table" then return t end
        return nil
    end
    local gear, equip, locks, refine, other = read("GearJson"), read("EquipJson"), read("LocksJson"), read("RefineJson"), read("OtherWornJson")
    local weapon = lp:GetAttribute("WeaponId")
    if not (gear and equip and locks and refine and other) or type(weapon) ~= "string" or weapon == "" then return nil end

    local keep = { [weapon] = true }
    for _, ref in pairs(equip) do
        if type(ref) == "string" then keep[ref] = true end
    end
    for k, v in pairs(locks) do
        if v == true then keep[k] = true elseif type(v) == "string" then keep[v] = true end
    end
    if keepOtherWorn ~= false then
        for ref in pairs(other) do keep[ref] = true end
    end
    if lp:GetAttribute("LoadoutJson") ~= nil then
        local loadouts = read("LoadoutJson")
        if not loadouts then return nil end
        local function keepRefs(t)
            for k, v in pairs(t) do
                if type(k) == "string" and k:match("^g%d+$") then keep[k] = true end
                if type(v) == "table" then keepRefs(v)
                elseif type(v) == "string" and v:match("^g%d+$") then keep[v] = true end
            end
        end
        keepRefs(loadouts)
    end

    -- Star lock (see _G.GolfHub_StarBest): the best piece of each kind in the chosen sets is never sold or salvaged.
    -- The star-lock sync loop (Auto Sell tab) also puts the game's own Lock on these pieces; this keeps them safe
    -- in the moments before that lock has reached us.
    local starKeep = _G.GolfHub_StarBest(gear) or {}

    local out = {}
    for ref, g in pairs(gear) do
        if type(g) == "table" and not keep[ref] and not starKeep[ref] then
            local tier = tonumber(g.T)
            local refined = tonumber(refine[ref]) or 0
            -- a lock can also be set on the item id; gear of unknown tier or with any refine level is always kept
            if tier and tier <= math.min(maxTier, 5) and refined <= 0 and not (g.Id and locks[g.Id] == true) then
                out[#out + 1] = ref
            end
        end
    end
    return out
end

local function performSellAtBarnaby()
    local barnabyModel, barnabyPos = getBarnabyNpc()
    local root, hum = getCharRoot()
    if not root or not hum then return false end

    -- 1. Walk close to Barnaby (เดินอ้อมซุ้มกิจกรรม New Hunter Festival ผ่านถนนใต้)
    if (root.Position - barnabyPos).Magnitude > 6.0 then
        walkFromTownPillarToBarnaby()
    end

    -- 2. Open Barnaby's Shop (ProximityPrompt or OpenNpc remote)
    local prompt = nil
    if barnabyModel then
        for _, desc in ipairs(barnabyModel:GetDescendants()) do
            if desc:IsA("ProximityPrompt") then
                prompt = desc
                break
            end
        end
    end

    if prompt then
        triggerPromptSafe(prompt)
    end
    local Remotes = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("Remotes")
    if Remotes and Remotes:FindFirstChild("OpenNpc") then
        pcall(function() Remotes.OpenNpc:FireServer("merchant") end)
    end

    task.wait(1.0)

    -- 3. Sell through the two remotes the shop itself uses, nothing else. No searching the screen for buttons by
    -- text and no unfiltered sale (the old ShopSell("All") / BagSell calls).
    --   ShopSellJunk = the shop's "Sell junk" button: loot materials only, skips locked and kept items
    --   ShopSellMany = { { ref, count }, ... }: only refs that _G.GolfHub_SafeGearList allows
    local remotes = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("Remotes")
    _G.GolfHub_SellMark("sellSafe")
    local junkRemote = remotes and remotes:FindFirstChild("ShopSellJunk")
    if junkRemote then pcall(function() junkRemote:FireServer() end) end

    local manyRemote = remotes and remotes:FindFirstChild("ShopSellMany")
    local planned = manyRemote and _G.GolfHub_SafeGearList(sellTier5 and 5 or 4, true)
    if planned then
        for first = 1, #planned, 35 do
            -- read again right before each batch: something may have been equipped or locked meanwhile
            local nowSafe = _G.GolfHub_SafeGearList(sellTier5 and 5 or 4, true)
            if not nowSafe then break end
            local allowed, batch = {}, {}
            for _, ref in ipairs(nowSafe) do allowed[ref] = true end
            for i = first, math.min(#planned, first + 34) do
                if allowed[planned[i]] then batch[#batch + 1] = { planned[i], 1 } end
            end
            if #batch > 0 then pcall(function() manyRemote:FireServer(batch) end) end
            task.wait(0.25)
        end
    end

    task.wait(1.0)
    _G.GolfHub_SellMark("done")
    updateSellUiLabels()
    return true
end

_G.GolfHub_SellAtShop = performSellAtBarnaby -- sale only, no warp afterwards (class rotation)

executeSellRoutine = function(targetZoneId, farmActiveCheck, setStatusRef)
    if isSellingNow then return end
    isSellingNow = true

    local setStat = setStatusRef or setStatus
    if setStat then setStat("🎒 กระเป๋าเต็ม! เดินเข้าเสาวาร์ปกลับเมือง...") end
    if valSellStatus then pcall(function() valSellStatus:UpdateName("🏃 เดินไปเสาวาร์ป...") end) end

    -- 1. Pause bot combat
    setBotRunning(false)
    task.wait(0.2)

    local root = getCharRoot()

    -- Check if already in Town
    local curZone = getCurrentActualZone()
    if curZone ~= "Town" then
        if setStat then setStat("🌀 วาร์ปกลับเมืองไปหา Barnaby ผ่าน HomeWarp...") end
        if valSellStatus then pcall(function() valSellStatus:UpdateName("🌀 HomeWarp กลับเมือง...") end) end
        local okHome = castHomeWarp("merchant")
        if not okHome and getCurrentActualZone() ~= "Town" then
            -- Fallback to Warp Pillar
            if setStat then setStat("🌀 วาร์ปกลับเมืองผ่านเสาวาร์ป...") end
            if valSellStatus then pcall(function() valSellStatus:UpdateName("🌀 วาร์ปกลับเมือง...") end) end
            local okWarp = warpViaPillar("Town", setStat)
            if not okWarp then
                -- ถ้ายังอยู่นอกเมือง และเสาวาร์ปยังไม่สำเร็จ ให้รอคูลดาวน์ HomeWarp เพื่อวาร์ปกลับเมืองอย่างปลอดภัย (ห้ามเดินเท้าข้ามแมพเด็ดขาด!)
                local waitTownStart = tick()
                while isSellingNow and getCurrentActualZone() ~= "Town" and (tick() - waitTownStart < 65) do
                    local readyAt = lp:GetAttribute("HomeWarpReadyAt")
                    local serverTime = workspace:GetServerTimeNow()
                    local cdWait = (readyAt and type(readyAt) == "number") and math.max(0, math.ceil(readyAt - serverTime)) or 0
                    if cdWait <= 1 then
                        castHomeWarp("merchant")
                        task.wait(1.5)
                        if getCurrentActualZone() == "Town" then break end
                    else
                        if setStat then setStat(string.format("⏳ รอคูลดาวน์ HomeWarp กลับเมือง (%ds)... (ห้ามเดินเท้า)", cdWait)) end
                        task.wait(1.0)
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        task.wait(0.5)
    end

    -- กฎเหล็ก: ถ้าตัวละครไม่ได้อยู่ในเขตเมืองจริง (Town) ห้ามเดินเท้าหา Barnaby เด็ดขาด!
    if getCurrentActualZone() ~= "Town" then
        isSellingNow = false
        if setStat then setStat("⚠️ ตัวละครอยู่นอกเมือง -> ยกเลิกการเดินหา Barnaby กลับไปลูปปกติ") end
        return
    end

    -- 3. Walk to Barnaby and Sell
    if setStat then setStat("🛒 เดินไปหา Barnaby เพื่อขายของ...") end
    if valSellStatus then pcall(function() valSellStatus:UpdateName("🛒 ขายของที่ Barnaby...") end) end
    _G.GolfHub_SellMark("atBarnaby")
    performSellAtBarnaby()

    local used = tonumber(lp:GetAttribute("BagSlotsUsed"))
    local cap = tonumber(lp:GetAttribute("BagSlotsMax")) or 240
    local stillFull = used ~= nil and (used >= (tonumber(sellThreshold) or 220) or used >= cap - 2)
    if stillFull then _G.GolfHub_SellBlockedUntil = tick() + 600 end

    if setStat then
        setStat(stillFull and "⚠️ ของที่เหลือเป็นของที่ห้ามขาย — พัก Auto Sell 10 นาที" or "✅ ขายของเสร็จแล้ว! เดินไปเสาวาร์ปเมือง...")
    end
    if valSellStatus then pcall(function() valSellStatus:UpdateName("🏃 เดินไปเสาวาร์ป...") end) end

    -- 4. เดินจากร้าน Barnaby ไปยังเสาวาร์ปเมืองผ่านระเบียงถนนทิศใต้ (ไม่ตัดผ่านซุ้มกลางเมือง 100%)
    _G.GolfHub_SellMark("walkBack")
    walkFromBarnabyToTownPillar()
    task.wait(0.3)

    -- 5. วาร์ปออกจากเมืองผ่านเสาวาร์ป Town ไปยังจุดหมาย (Fast Travel) ห้ามเดินเท้าออกจากเมืองเด็ดขาด!
    local bDefCurrent = (selectedBossKey and BOSS_TARGET_DEFS[selectedBossKey])
    local destPillar = nil
    if targetZoneId and targetZoneId ~= "Town" then
        destPillar = getBestPillarForZone(targetZoneId)
    end
    if not destPillar then
        if bossFarmActive then
            destPillar = (bDefCurrent and bDefCurrent.pillar) or "Boss05"
        elseif farmActive then
            local lv = getLevel()
            local tz = resolveFarmTargetZone(lv)
            destPillar = (tz and getBestPillarForZone(tz.zone)) or "Boss01"
        end
    end

    if destPillar and WARP_PILLAR_DATA[destPillar] then
        if setStat then setStat(string.format("🌀 แตะเสาวาร์ปเมือง -> วาร์ปไป %s...", destPillar)) end
        if valSellStatus then pcall(function() valSellStatus:UpdateName(string.format("🌀 วาร์ปไป %s...", destPillar)) end) end

        local warpSuccess = false
        for attempt = 1, 3 do
            warpSuccess = warpViaPillar(destPillar, setStat)
            if warpSuccess or not _G.GolfHub_Pillars.canWarp(destPillar) then break end
            task.wait(1.0)
        end
    end

    -- 6. ตรวจสอบว่าออกจากเมืองสำเร็จแล้ว
    -- สำคัญมาก: หากกำลังรัน Auto Hunt Boss (bossFarmActive) อยู่ ห้ามเรียก travelToZoneSmart หรือเดิน Highway ท่องเที่ยวเด็ดขาด!
    -- ปล่อยให้ลูป runBossFarmLoop จัดการพาเดินเข้าห้องบอสเอง
    if bossFarmActive then
        if valSellStatus then pcall(function() valSellStatus:UpdateName("✅ ขายของเสร็จแล้ว -> ล็อคเป้าเข้าตีบอส!") end) end
        lastSellAttemptTime = tick()
        isSellingNow = false
        return
    end

    local afterZone = getCurrentActualZone()
    if afterZone ~= "Town" and targetZoneId and targetZoneId ~= "Town" then
        if farmActiveCheck and farmActiveCheck() then
            travelToZoneSmart(targetZoneId, farmActiveCheck, setStat)
        end
    end

    if valSellStatus then pcall(function() valSellStatus:UpdateName("✅ พร้อมฟาร์ม (Idle)") end) end
    lastSellAttemptTime = tick()
    isSellingNow = false
end

-- ====================================================================
-- CREATE WINDOW (PER-USER ISOLATED WORKSPACE FOLDER FOR MULTI-INSTANCE)
-- ====================================================================
do
    local safeUser = ((lp and lp.Name) or "Unknown"):gsub("[^%w_%-]", "_")
    if safeUser == "" then safeUser = "User_" .. tostring((lp and lp.UserId) or 0) end
    GOLFGUI.UserName        = safeUser
    GOLFGUI.AutoSaveName    = safeUser
    GOLFGUI.AutoSaveEnabled = true
    GOLFGUI:SetFolder("GolfHub_HuntingTogether/" .. safeUser)
end

Window = GOLFGUI:Window({
    Title     = "GOLF HUB",
    Subtitle  = "Hunting Together RPG",
    Size      = UDim2.fromOffset(868, 640),
    GuiName   = "GolfHubHuntingTogether",
    Keybind   = Enum.KeyCode.RightShift,
    StatusBar = false,
})
-- Build behind the mini bar. The window was shrunk to the mini bar 1.5 s after every start anyway; drawing the full
-- window once before that (layout of every row + first render of the custom fonts) was the last ~200 ms start-up
-- freeze. Open it from the mini bar or RightShift as before.
pcall(function() Window:SetState(false) end)

-- ====================================================================
-- TAB GROUP
-- ====================================================================
local mainGroup = Window:TabGroup({ Name = "Menu" })

-- Start-up stutter: everything from here to the end of the file used to run in ONE frame, a whole-game freeze
-- (590-750 ms measured live) every time the hub started in a new server. Building it one frame at a time spreads it
-- in ~40-90 ms slices instead (task.wait() between the big sections and before every tab).
task.wait() -- yield point, see the note below
-- ====================================================================
-- TAB: AUTO FARM
-- ====================================================================
local autoFarmTab = mainGroup:Tab({
    Name        = "Auto Farm",
    Title       = "Auto Farm & Combat",
    Description = "Configure monster targeting, legit walk farming, and attack distance.",
})

-- ---------- SECTION 0: Status & Progress ----------
local secStatus = autoFarmTab:Section()
secStatus:Header({
    Name        = "Status & Progress",
    Description = "สถานะการทำงานบอท โซนปัจจุบัน และเลเวล (Real-time)",
    Collapsible = false,
})
lblStatus       = secStatus:Status({ Name = "Bot Status", Text = "⏹ หยุดฟาร์มแล้ว", Tone = "off" })
lblCurrentZone  = secStatus:Value({ Name = "Current Zone", Text = getCurrentActualZone() })
lblCurrentLevel = secStatus:Value({ Name = "Current Level", Text = tostring(getLevel()) })

-- ---------- SECTION 1: Auto Attack & Targeting ----------
local secAttack = autoFarmTab:Section()
secAttack:Header({
    Name        = "Auto Attack & Targeting",
    Description = "Core combat automation settings (Legit Movement Only — No Teleport)",
    Collapsible = false,
})

toggleAutoFarm = secAttack:Toggle({
    Name        = "Auto Attack / Farm",
    Description = "Scan and attack targeted monsters within range — walks to target with wall avoidance",
    Default     = false,
    Callback    = function(val)
        farmActive = val
        if val then
            -- Mutual exclusivity: ปิดโหมดล่าบอสและโหมดลงดันเจี้ยนทันทีเพื่อไม่ให้ระบบแย่งกันเดินหรือปิดปุ่ม [F] AUTO!
            if bossFarmActive then
                bossFarmActive = false
                if toggleBossHunt then pcall(function() toggleBossHunt:Set(false) end) end
                if bossFarmThread then task.cancel(bossFarmThread) bossFarmThread = nil end
            end
            if dungeonFarmActive then
                dungeonFarmActive = false
                if toggleDungeonFarm then pcall(function() toggleDungeonFarm:Set(false) end) end
                if dungeonThread then task.cancel(dungeonThread) dungeonThread = nil end
            end

            -- ถ้าตัวละครอยู่ในโซนฟาร์ม (นอกเมือง) อยู่แล้ว -> เปิดปุ่ม [F] AUTO ทันทีไม่ต้องรอ!
            if not (isPlayerInDungeon and isPlayerInDungeon()) and getCurrentActualZone() ~= "Town" then
                setBotRunning(true, {
                    range        = farmRange,
                    loot         = farmLoot,
                    openBoxes    = farmOpenBoxes,
                    attackLeader = farmAtkLeader,
                    preset       = farmPreset,
                    hpPct        = farmHpPct,
                })
            end

            -- ปิดหน้าต่าง Dungeons / Modals ที่อาจเปิดค้างอยู่ให้หมด เพื่อไม่ให้บล็อกจอและการคลิก
            pcall(function()
                local pg = lp:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
                if pg then
                    local dngSelect = pg:FindFirstChild("HuntDungeonSelect")
                    if dngSelect then dngSelect.Enabled = false end
                    local dngWin = pg:FindFirstChild("LumiDungeonWindows")
                    if dngWin then
                        for _, w in ipairs(dngWin:GetChildren()) do
                            if w:IsA("GuiObject") then w.Visible = false end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)

            if autoEquipAlways and equipBestGearForCurrentJob then
                if GOLFGUI.IsLoadingConfig or isBootstrappingConfig then
                    task.delay(10.0, function()
                        if (dungeonFarmActive or farmActive) and equipBestGearForCurrentJob then
                            pcall(equipBestGearForCurrentJob, true)
                        end
                    end)
                else
                    task.spawn(function() equipBestGearForCurrentJob(true) end)
                end
            end
            if farmThread then task.cancel(farmThread) end
            farmThread = task.spawn(farmLoop)
        else
            if farmThread then task.cancel(farmThread) farmThread = nil end
            setBotRunning(false)
            local root, hum = getCharRoot()
            if hum and root then hum:MoveTo(root.Position) end
            pcall(function()
                local curTgt = lp:FindFirstChild("CurrentTarget")
                if curTgt then curTgt.Value = nil end
                local Remotes = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("Remotes")
                if Remotes and Remotes:FindFirstChild("SetTarget") then
                    Remotes.SetTarget:FireServer(nil, false)
                end
                if lp:GetAttribute("AutoOn") == true and Remotes and Remotes:FindFirstChild("BotAutoState") then
                    Remotes.BotAutoState:FireServer(false)
                end
            end)
            setStatus("⏹ หยุดฟาร์มแล้ว")
        end
    end,
}, "AutoFarm_AttackToggle")

do
    local A = _G.GolfHub_AllJobs
    A.toggle = secAttack:Toggle({
        Name        = "ฟาร์มทุกอาชีพถึง Lv.99",
        Description = "เปิดคู่กับ Auto Farm: ครบ 99 แล้วเดินเข้าเสากลับเมือง เปลี่ยนเป็นอาชีพที่ยังไม่ 99 แล้วฟาร์มต่อ ครบทุกอาชีพจะหยุดในเมือง · ใช้ Gold ปลดอาชีพที่ยังไม่เคยเล่นเมื่อถึงคิว (พื้นฐาน 50,000 · พิเศษ 200,000–500,000 เมื่อครบเงื่อนไข) เหลือกันไว้ 50,000 เสมอ · ไม่ใช้เพชร/Robux",
        Default     = false,
        Callback    = function(v)
            if A.on ~= v and A.log then A.log(v and "เปิดระบบฟาร์มทุกอาชีพ" or "ปิดระบบฟาร์มทุกอาชีพ") end
            A.on = v
            A.skip = {}
            _G.GolfHub_FarmNeedZoneReeval = true
            if v then A.show(A.describe()) task.spawn(A.unlockAll) else A.show("ปิดอยู่") end
        end,
    }, "AutoFarm_AllJobs")
    A.label = secAttack:Value({ Name = "อาชีพที่เหลือ", Text = "ปิดอยู่" })
    task.spawn(function()
        local gen = _G.GolfHub_Gen
        while gen == _G.GolfHub_Gen do
            task.wait(10)
            if A.on and not A.due() then pcall(function() A.show(A.describe()) end) end
            -- items locked again (by the player or a new drop) are unlocked once a minute
            if A.on and tick() - (A.unlockedAt or 0) > 60 then
                A.unlockedAt = tick()
                pcall(A.unlockAll)
            end
        end
    end)
    if getgenv then getgenv().GolfHub_ToggleAllJobs = function(v) A.toggle:Set(v) end end
end

secAttack:Dropdown({
    Name        = "Select Farm Zone / Map (เลือกแมพฟาร์ม)",
    Description = "เลือกพื้นที่ที่ต้องการให้ตัวละครเดินไปฟาร์ม หรือเลือก Auto เพื่อย้ายโซนตามเลเวลเอง",
    Options     = {

-- push the current Bot Settings to the game's native AUTO bot (only while farming and not walking to a pillar / selling)
_G.GolfHub_ApplyBot = function()
    if not farmActive or isSellingNow then return end
    if _G.GolfHub_isWarpingTravel and (tick() - (_G.GolfHub_warpStartedAt or 0)) < 45 then return end
    setBotRunning(true, {
        range        = farmRange,
        loot         = farmLoot,
        openBoxes    = farmOpenBoxes,
        attackLeader = farmAtkLeader,
        preset       = farmPreset,
        hpPct        = farmHpPct,
    })
end

-- Independent Chest Opener Loop: Auto Farm in the open world only. Boss Hunt opens the boss's chest itself and keeps
-- boxes off while fighting; the dungeon has its own chest logic and its Secret Chest switch. This loop used to run in
-- both and opened chests those modes had chosen to skip.
task.spawn(function()
    local myGen = _G.GolfHub_Gen
    task.wait(2.0) -- defer initial scan so join frame does not query CollectionService
    while _G.GolfHub_Gen == myGen do
        task.wait(0.35)
        local travelling = _G.GolfHub_isWarpingTravel and (tick() - (_G.GolfHub_warpStartedAt or 0)) < 45
        local inWb = lp:GetAttribute("InWorldBoss") == true
        if farmOpenBoxes and farmActive and not bossFarmActive and not dungeonFarmActive
            and not (isPlayerInDungeon and isPlayerInDungeon()) and not isSellingNow and not travelling
            and not inWb and lp:GetAttribute("InTown") ~= true then
            pcall(function()
                local root, hum = getCharRoot()
                if not root then return end

                local box = findNearestLootBox(farmRange or 70)
                if box and box.Parent then
                    local boxPos = box:IsA("BasePart") and box.Position or (box:IsA("Model") and box:GetPivot().Position)
                    if not boxPos and box:IsA("ProximityPrompt") and box.Parent then
                        local pp = box.Parent
                        boxPos = pp:IsA("BasePart") and pp.Position or (pp:IsA("Model") and pp:GetPivot().Position)
                    end

                    if boxPos then
                        local dist = (boxPos - root.Position).Magnitude
                        if dist <= 18 then
                            openLootBoxDirect(box)
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)
        end
    end
end)

local SkillUI = { farm = {}, boss = {}, dng = {} }
local isSyncingSkillUI = false
local isBootstrappingConfig = false

local function updateSkillToggleVisuals()
    if isSyncingSkillUI then return end
    isSyncingSkillUI = true
    pcall(function()
        for i = 1, 8 do
            local st = savedSkillSlots[i] == true
            local lbl = getSkillSlotLabel(i)
            if SkillUI.farm[i] then
                SkillUI.farm[i]:UpdateState(st, true)
                SkillUI.farm[i]:UpdateName(lbl)
            end
            if SkillUI.boss[i] then
                SkillUI.boss[i]:UpdateState(st, true)
                SkillUI.boss[i]:UpdateName(lbl)
            end
            if SkillUI.dng[i] then
                SkillUI.dng[i]:UpdateState(st, true)
                SkillUI.dng[i]:UpdateName(lbl)
            end
        end
    end)
    isSyncingSkillUI = false
end

-- Skill picker: one multi-select dropdown per tab instead of 8 toggles. It still fills SkillUI[key][1..8] with
-- objects that answer UpdateState / UpdateName, so every existing sync path (job change, hotbar change, the other
-- tabs) keeps working unchanged. State lives in savedSkillSlots (saved per job), so the dropdown has no config flag.
local function buildSkillPicker(section, key, title, afterChange)
    local function optName(i) return (getSkillSlotLabel(i):gsub("^⚡%s*", "")) end
    local names = {}
    for i = 1, 8 do names[i] = optName(i) end
    local function selectedList()
        local sel = {}
        for i = 1, 8 do if savedSkillSlots[i] ~= false then sel[#sel + 1] = names[i] end end
        return sel
    end
    local dd = section:Dropdown({
        Name        = title,
        Description = "กดเพื่อเปิดรายการ แล้วติ๊กเลือกสกิลที่จะใช้ (เลือกได้หลายสกิล · ซิงค์ทุกแท็บ)",
        Multi       = true,
        Options     = names,
        Default     = selectedList(),
        Callback    = function(sel)
            if isSyncingSkillUI then return end
            hasUserTouchedSkillSlots = true
            for i = 1, 8 do savedSkillSlots[i] = sel[names[i]] == true end
            saveCurrentJobSkillSlots()
            updateSkillToggleVisuals()
            if not isBootstrappingConfig then
                if afterChange then afterChange() else syncSkillSelectionToBotJson() end
            end
        end,
    })
    local pending = false
    local function refresh()
        if pending then return end
        pending = true
        task.defer(function()
            pending = false
            local changed = false
            for i = 1, 8 do
                local n = optName(i)
                if n ~= names[i] then names[i] = n; changed = true end
            end
            local was = isSyncingSkillUI
            isSyncingSkillUI = true
            pcall(function()
                if changed then dd:ClearOptions(); dd:InsertOptions(names) end
                dd:UpdateSelection(selectedList())
            end)
            isSyncingSkillUI = was
        end)
    end
    local shim = { UpdateState = function() refresh() end, UpdateName = function() refresh() end }
    for i = 1, 8 do SkillUI[key][i] = shim end
    return dd
end

buildSkillPicker(secSkill, "farm", "เลือกสกิลที่ใช้ (Auto Farm)")

        if updatingBotFromScript then return end
        syncSkillSlotsFromGame(false)
        updateSkillToggleVisuals()
    end)
    _G.GolfHub_Conn(lp:GetAttributeChangedSignal("HotbarJson"), function()
        task.defer(function()

            if hasUserTouchedSkillSlots then
                syncSkillSelectionToBotJson()
            end
        end)
    end)
    _G.GolfHub_Conn(lp:GetAttributeChangedSignal("Job"), function()
        task.wait(0.2)
        -- any job change (also by hand at the Master Class NPC): the "player edited the skills" mark was for the old
        -- class. A class with its own saved pattern still uses it; one without reads the game's hotbar instead of
        -- copying the previous class's on/off pattern.
        hasUserTouchedSkillSlots = false
        syncSkillSlotsFromGame(false)
        updateSkillToggleVisuals()
        if farmActive then
            setBotRunning(true)
        elseif dungeonFarmActive and isPlayerInDungeon and isPlayerInDungeon() then
            syncSkillSelectionToBotJson(true)
        end
    end)
end)

-- ====================================================================
-- BOSS 64-STUD LEASH & ARENA CLAMPING ENGINE
-- คำสั่งผู้ใช้: ห้ามเดินห่างเกิน 64 studs จากตัวบอสเด็ดขาด
-- ====================================================================
local function clampInsideBossLeash(pos, bossPos, maxLeashDist)
    if not pos or not bossPos then return pos end
    maxLeashDist = maxLeashDist or 64.0
    local diff = pos - bossPos
    local dist2D = math.sqrt(diff.X^2 + diff.Z^2)
    if dist2D > maxLeashDist then
        local dir2D = (dist2D > 0.1) and Vector3.new(diff.X, 0, diff.Z).Unit or Vector3.new(0, 0, -1)
        local clamped = bossPos + dir2D * maxLeashDist
        return Vector3.new(clamped.X, pos.Y, clamped.Z)
    end
    return pos
end

-- จำกัดตำแหน่งเวกเตอร์ให้อยู่ภายในขอบเขตห้องบอสเสมอ เพื่อป้องกันการถอยหลังหลุดออกประตู และล็อคไม่ให้เกิน 64 studs จากบอส
local function clampInsideArena(pos, arenaCenter, maxRadius, maxZ, bossPos)
    if not pos or not arenaCenter then return pos end
    local diff = pos - arenaCenter
    local dist2D = math.sqrt(diff.X^2 + diff.Z^2)
    local res = pos
    if dist2D > maxRadius then
        local dir2D = Vector3.new(diff.X, 0, diff.Z).Unit
        local clamped = arenaCenter + dir2D * maxRadius
        res = Vector3.new(clamped.X, pos.Y, clamped.Z)
    end
    -- ป้องกันการถอยเข้าใกล้ประตู: ต้องอยู่ห่างจากเส้นประตู (doorZ) อย่างน้อย 10 studs เสมอ
    if maxZ then
        local safeDoorLimit = maxZ - 10.0
        if res.Z > safeDoorLimit then
            res = Vector3.new(res.X, res.Y, safeDoorLimit)
        end
    end
    -- กฎเหล็กตามคำสั่งผู้ใช้: ห้ามเดินห่างเกิน 64 studs จากตัวบอส
    if bossPos then
        res = clampInsideBossLeash(res, bossPos, 64.0)
    end
    return res
end

-- ====================================================================
-- BOSS DETECTION ENGINE (Ground Truth 100% via Pivot & Workspace Scan)
-- ====================================================================
local function getModelPos(m)
    if not m then return nil end
    if m.PrimaryPart then return m.PrimaryPart.Position end
    local hrp = m:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if hrp and hrp:IsA("BasePart") then return hrp.Position end
    local bp = m:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)
    if bp then return bp.Position end
    local ok, piv = pcall(function() return m:GetPivot().Position end)
    if ok and piv then return piv end
    return nil
end

local function findBossModelInArena(bDef)
    if not bDef then return nil end
    local monsters = workspace:FindFirstChild("Monsters")
    local arenaCenter = bDef.arenaPos or Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
    local arenaRadius = (bDef.arenaRadius or 110) + 95

    local function checkModelCandidate(obj)
        if not obj or not obj:IsA("Model") then return nil end
        local objName = obj.Name:lower()
        local mId = obj:GetAttribute("MonsterId") or obj:GetAttribute("Monster") or obj:GetAttribute("Id")
        local exactIdMatch = (mId ~= nil and bDef.monsterId ~= nil and tostring(mId):lower() == bDef.monsterId:lower())

        if not exactIdMatch then
            local isMinion = objName:find("guard") or objName:find("mummy") or objName:find("beetle") or objName:find("servant") or objName:find("soldier") or objName:find("minion")
            if isMinion then return nil end
        end

        local matched = exactIdMatch
        if not matched then
            for _, pat in ipairs(bDef.patterns) do
                if string.find(objName, string.lower(pat)) then
                    matched = true
                    break
                end
            end
        end

        local attrBossName = obj:GetAttribute("BossName") or obj:GetAttribute("MonsterName")
        if not matched and attrBossName then
            for _, pat in ipairs(bDef.patterns) do
                if string.find(string.lower(tostring(attrBossName)), string.lower(pat)) then
                    matched = true
                    break
                end
            end
        end

        if not matched then return nil end

        local hp = obj:GetAttribute("HP") or obj:GetAttribute("Health")
        local hum = obj:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        local isDead = obj:GetAttribute("Dead") or obj:GetAttribute("IsDead") or obj:GetAttribute("AnimDead")
        local isAlive = (hp ~= nil and hp > 0) or (hp == nil and hum ~= nil and hum.Health > 0) or (hp == nil and hum == nil)
        if isAlive and (isDead ~= true) then
            return obj
        end
        return nil
    end

    if monsters then
        for _, obj in ipairs(monsters:GetChildren()) do
            local found = checkModelCandidate(obj)
            if found then return found end
        end
    end

    for _, obj in ipairs(workspace:GetChildren()) do
        if obj:IsA("Model") and obj ~= monsters then
            local found = checkModelCandidate(obj)
            if found then return found end
        end
    end

    return nil
end

local function getBossServerState(bDef)
    if not bDef or not bDef.monsterId then return nil end
    local ws = game:GetService("Workspace")

    -- 1. Ground Truth Check: ตรวจสอบโมเดลบอสตัวจริงในอารีน่าก่อนเสมอ (ความจริง 100%)
    -- ถ้ามีโมเดลบอสตัวจริงยืนอยู่ในห้องและยังมีชีวิตอยู่ ถือว่าเกิดอยู่ทันที ห้ามบอกว่าไม่พบเด็ดขาด!
    local liveModel = findBossModelInArena(bDef)
    if liveModel then
        return {
            alive = true,
            cooldown = 0,
            nextSpawn = 0,
            attributeLoaded = true,
            model = liveModel
        }
    end

    -- รอให้ Server Attributes โหลดเสร็จสมบูรณ์ ป้องกันการอ่านค่า nil ทันทีที่เข้าห้อง
    local aliveAttr = ws:GetAttribute("BossAlive_" .. bDef.monsterId)
    local nextSpawn = tonumber(ws:GetAttribute("BossNext_" .. bDef.monsterId)) or 0
    if aliveAttr == nil and nextSpawn == 0 then
        for _ = 1, 8 do
            task.wait(0.2)
            aliveAttr = ws:GetAttribute("BossAlive_" .. bDef.monsterId)
            nextSpawn = tonumber(ws:GetAttribute("BossNext_" .. bDef.monsterId)) or 0
            if aliveAttr ~= nil or nextSpawn > 0 then break end
        end
    end

    local nowTime = workspace:GetServerTimeNow()
    local cd = 0
    if nextSpawn > 0 then
        cd = math.max(0, math.floor(nextSpawn - nowTime))
    end

    -- ตรวจสอบ _BossTimers เฉพาะเมื่อ BillboardGui แสดงเวลานับถอยหลังเกิดใหม่จริง (Enabled == true)
    local bossTimers = ws:FindFirstChild("_BossTimers")
    if bossTimers then
        local pillarKey = bDef.pillar or ""
        for _, t in ipairs(bossTimers:GetChildren()) do
            if (pillarKey ~= "" and t.Name:find(pillarKey)) or (bDef.name and t.Name:lower():find(bDef.name:lower())) then
                local bg = t:FindFirstChild("BossTimer")
                if bg and bg:IsA("BillboardGui") and bg.Enabled then
                    if cd == 0 then
                        for _, l in ipairs(bg:GetDescendants()) do
                            if l:IsA("TextLabel") and l.Text:find(":") then
                                local m, s = l.Text:match("(%d+):(%d+)")
                                if m and s then cd = tonumber(m) * 60 + tonumber(s) end
                            end
                        end
                        if cd == 0 then cd = 600 end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end

    -- กำหนดสถานะความมีชีวิตของบอส
    local isAlive = false
    if cd == 0 then
        if aliveAttr == true then
            isAlive = true
        elseif aliveAttr == false then
            local recheck = findBossModelInArena(bDef)
            if recheck then isAlive = true end
        else
            local recheck = findBossModelInArena(bDef)
            if recheck then isAlive = true end
        end
    end

    local attrLoaded = (aliveAttr ~= nil or nextSpawn > 0)
    return {
        alive = isAlive,
        cooldown = cd,
        nextSpawn = nextSpawn,
        attributeLoaded = attrLoaded
    }
end
local bossCheckMode = "instant" -- "instant" (เช็คจากระบบเซิร์ฟเวอร์แบบ Real-Time ไม่ต้องลงแมพ) หรือ "walkin" (เดินเท้าเข้าห้องบอส)
local serverHopMode = "teleport" -- "teleport" (ย้ายผ่าน TeleportService) หรือ "kill" (ปิดเกมแล้วให้ Watchdog เปิดใหม่)

-- ====================================================================
-- BOSS 3D GUIDE LINE & 64-STUD LEASH VISUALIZER ENGINE
-- ระบบเส้นนำทาง 3D ชี้ตรงเข้าหาบอส + วงแหวนจำกัดระยะ 64 studs ตามคำสั่งผู้ใช้
-- ====================================================================
_G.GolfHub_GuideLineEnabled = true

local function removeBossGuideLine()
    pcall(function()
        local f = workspace:FindFirstChild("GolfHub_GuideSystem")
        if f then f:Destroy() end
        local tip = workspace:FindFirstChild("GuideArrowTip")
        if tip then tip:Destroy() end
        local root = lp and lp.Character and lp.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local att0 = root and root:FindFirstChild("GolfHub_GuideAtt0")
        if att0 then att0:Destroy() end
    end)
end

-- Guide arrow sprite: transparent PNG of the Kamen Rider arrow (320x166) laid flat along the path.
-- The PNG used to be embedded here as 117 KB of base64 (12% of this file, parsed on every launch) although it is only
-- written to disk once. It now ships as a file only (source copy: script/assets/guide_arrow_v1.png); without it the
-- guide line simply shows no arrow sprites.
local GuideArrow = {
    file        = "GolfHub_HuntingTogether/guide_arrow_v1.png",
    aspect      = 320 / 166, -- image width / height; the arrow tip points to the image's left edge
    spriteLen   = 12.0,      -- studs along the path
    spacing     = 15.0,      -- studs between sprite centers
    firstOffset = 10.0,      -- studs from the player to the first sprite
    count       = 7,
    asset       = nil,
}

function GuideArrow.getAsset()
    if GuideArrow.asset then return GuideArrow.asset end
    if not (getcustomasset and isfile and isfile(GuideArrow.file)) then return nil end
    local ok, asset = pcall(function() return getcustomasset(GuideArrow.file) end)
    if ok and type(asset) == "string" and asset ~= "" then
        GuideArrow.asset = asset
    end
    return GuideArrow.asset
end

local _lastGuideUpdate = 0
local function updateBossGuideLine(playerPos, targetPos, targetModel, forceMove)
    if _G.GolfHub_GuideLineEnabled == false then
        removeBossGuideLine()
        return
    end
    if not playerPos or not targetPos then return end

    local now = tick()
    if (now - _lastGuideUpdate) < 0.10 then return end
    _lastGuideUpdate = now

    pcall(function()
        local root, hum = getCharRoot()
        if not root then return end

        local folder = workspace:FindFirstChild("GolfHub_GuideSystem")
        if not folder then
            folder = Instance.new("Folder")
            folder.Name = "GolfHub_GuideSystem"
            folder.Parent = workspace
        end

        local bDef = BOSS_TARGET_DEFS[selectedBossKey]
        local bossName = (bDef and bDef.name) or "Boss"
        local dist2D = math.sqrt((playerPos.X - targetPos.X)^2 + (playerPos.Z - targetPos.Z)^2)
        local doorZ = bDef and bDef.doorZ

        local isFarFromTarget = (dist2D > 300.0) or (playerPos.Z > -25)
        local arrowTarget = targetPos

        if isFarFromTarget and bDef and bDef.pillar then
            -- ถ้าอยู่ต่างโซนหรืออยู่เมือง: ให้ลูกศรนำทางชี้ตรงไปที่เสาวาร์ปที่ใกล้ที่สุด!
            local nearestPillarPos = nil
            if playerPos.Z > -25 then
                nearestPillarPos = Vector3.new(22.4, 4.25, 68.0)
            else
                local pKey = _G.GolfHub_Pillars.start(playerPos, bDef.pillar) -- the pillar the warp will really use
                local pDat = pKey and WARP_PILLAR_DATA[pKey]
                if pDat then nearestPillarPos = pDat.standPos or pDat.pos end
            end
            if nearestPillarPos then
                arrowTarget = nearestPillarPos
            end
        end

        -- กฎเหล็ก 100%: ลูกศรและทิศทางการเดินต้องมุ่งหน้าไปหาบอส หรือเสาวาร์ป
        local goalX = arrowTarget.X
        local goalZ = arrowTarget.Z

        if not isFarFromTarget then
            if doorZ and playerPos.Z > doorZ then
                -- อยู่นอกประตูห้องบอส: มุ่งหน้าเข้าสู่กึ่งกลางประตู X=0.0, Z=doorZ
                goalX = (math.abs(playerPos.X) > 2.0) and (playerPos.X * 0.5) or 0.0
                goalZ = doorZ
            else
                -- อยู่ในห้องบอสแล้ว: มุ่งตรงเข้าหาพิกัดตัวบอส
                goalX = targetPos.X
                goalZ = targetPos.Z
            end

            -- บล็อกการหันหน้ากลับเมืองอย่างเด็ดขาด:
            if goalZ >= playerPos.Z then
                goalZ = playerPos.Z - 10.0
            end
        end

        local lookTarget = Vector3.new(goalX, playerPos.Y, goalZ)

        -- 2. สร้าง/อัปเดตโมเดล "Kamen Rider CycloneJoker Speed Arrow" ตามรูปต้นแบบ 100%
        local pStart = Vector3.new(playerPos.X, playerPos.Y - 1.8, playerPos.Z)
        local pEnd = Vector3.new(arrowTarget.X, (arrowTarget.Y or playerPos.Y) - 1.8, arrowTarget.Z)
        local fullVec = pEnd - pStart
        local totalDist = fullVec.Magnitude
        local dir = (totalDist > 0.001) and (fullVec / totalDist) or Vector3.new(0, 0, -1)

        -- คำนวณขนาดหัวธนูและก้านลูกศร (ไม่จำกัดระยะ: ก้านลากยาวจนถึงตัวบอส 100%)
        local headLen = math.clamp(totalDist * 0.15, 6.0, 12.0)
        local shaftLen = math.max(0.5, totalDist - headLen)
        local shaftCenter = pStart + dir * (shaftLen * 0.5)
        local shaftCF = CFrame.lookAt(shaftCenter, shaftCenter + dir)

        -- 2. Kamen Rider arrow sprites: the arrow image lies flat on the ground every few studs, tip pointing along the path
        local arrowAsset = GuideArrow.getAsset()
        if arrowAsset then
            if folder:GetAttribute("SpriteMode") ~= true then
                folder:SetAttribute("SpriteMode", true)
                for _, child in ipairs(folder:GetChildren()) do
                    if child.Name:sub(1, 10) == "GuideArrow" then child:Destroy() end
                end
            end

            local dirH = Vector3.new(fullVec.X, 0, fullVec.Z)
            local totalH = dirH.Magnitude
            dirH = (totalH > 0.01) and (dirH / totalH) or Vector3.new(0, 0, -1)
            local spriteLen = GuideArrow.spriteLen
            local spriteWid = spriteLen / GuideArrow.aspect
            local rayParams = RaycastParams.new()
            rayParams.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
            rayParams.FilterDescendantsInstances = { folder, root.Parent }

            for i = 1, GuideArrow.count do
                local along = GuideArrow.firstOffset + (i - 1) * GuideArrow.spacing
                local spriteName = "GuideSprite_" .. i
                local sprite = folder:FindFirstChild(spriteName)
                if along + spriteLen * 0.5 > totalH then
                    if sprite then sprite:Destroy() end
                else
                    if not sprite then
                        sprite = Instance.new("Part")
                        sprite.Name = spriteName
                        sprite.Anchored = true
                        sprite.CanCollide = false
                        sprite.CanQuery = false
                        sprite.CanTouch = false
                        sprite.CastShadow = false
                        sprite.Transparency = 1
                        sprite.Size = Vector3.new(spriteWid, 0.05, spriteLen)
                        local gui = Instance.new("SurfaceGui")
                        gui.Face = Enum.NormalId.Top
                        gui.SizingMode = Enum.SurfaceGuiSizingMode.PixelsPerStud
                        gui.PixelsPerStud = 40
                        gui.LightInfluence = 0
                        gui.Parent = sprite
                        local img = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
                        img.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                        img.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
                        img.Image = arrowAsset
                        img.Parent = gui
                        sprite.Parent = folder
                    end
                    local px = playerPos.X + dirH.X * along
                    local pz = playerPos.Z + dirH.Z * along
                    local groundY = playerPos.Y - 2.8
                    local hit = workspace:Raycast(Vector3.new(px, playerPos.Y + 8, pz), Vector3.new(0, -70, 0), rayParams)
                    if hit then groundY = hit.Position.Y end
                    local spritePos = Vector3.new(px, groundY + 0.15, pz)
                    -- on the Top face of a SurfaceGui the image's left edge (the arrow tip) faces local +Z, so look away from the path
                    sprite.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(spritePos, spritePos - dirH)
                end
            end
        else
        -- 2.1 ฐานแทร็กทางวิ่งหลักสีดำด้าน (Onyx Black Racing Track Chassis)
        local baseTrack = folder:FindFirstChild("GuideArrowTrack")
        if not baseTrack then
            baseTrack = Instance.new("Part")
            baseTrack.Name = "GuideArrowTrack"
            baseTrack.Anchored = true
            baseTrack.CanCollide = false
            baseTrack.Material = Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic
            baseTrack.Color = Color3.fromRGB(18, 18, 22)
            baseTrack.Parent = folder
        end
        baseTrack.Size = Vector3.new(2.4, 0.12, shaftLen)
        baseTrack.CFrame = shaftCF

        -- 2.2 ราวไฟนีออนสีเขียวมรกตเรืองแสง 2 ข้างแทร็ก (Dual Emerald Glow Rails)
        for _, side in ipairs({ -1, 1 }) do
            local rName = "GuideArrowRail_" .. (side == -1 and "L" or "R")
            local rail = folder:FindFirstChild(rName)
            if not rail then
                rail = Instance.new("Part")
                rail.Name = rName
                rail.Anchored = true
                rail.CanCollide = false
                rail.Material = Enum.Material.Neon
                rail.Color = Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 75)
                rail.Parent = folder
            end
            rail.Size = Vector3.new(0.35, 0.22, shaftLen)
            rail.CFrame = shaftCF * CFrame.new(side * 1.15, 0.05, 0)
        end

        -- 2.3 แกนลำแสงความเร็วนีออนเขียวตรงกลางแทร็ก (Cyclone Energy Pulse Beam)
        local centerBeam = folder:FindFirstChild("GuideArrowShaft")
        if not centerBeam then
            centerBeam = Instance.new("Part")
            centerBeam.Name = "GuideArrowShaft"
            centerBeam.Anchored = true
            centerBeam.CanCollide = false
            centerBeam.Material = Enum.Material.Neon
            centerBeam.Color = Color3.fromRGB(20, 255, 110)
            centerBeam.Transparency = 0.05
            centerBeam.Parent = folder
        end
        centerBeam.Size = Vector3.new(0.7, 0.24, shaftLen)
        centerBeam.CFrame = shaftCF * CFrame.new(0, 0.06, 0)

        -- 2.4 ลายธงหมากรุกขาว-ดำท้ายลูกศร (Checkered Racing Flag Accent) ที่หน้าเท้าผู้เล่น
        local cfBase = CFrame.lookAt(pStart, pStart + dir)
        for row = 0, 3 do
            for col = -1, 1, 2 do
                local chkName = string.format("GuideArrowCheck_%d_%d", row, col == -1 and 1 or 2)
                local chk = folder:FindFirstChild(chkName)
                if not chk then
                    chk = Instance.new("Part")
                    chk.Name = chkName
                    chk.Anchored = true
                    chk.CanCollide = false
                    chk.Material = Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic
                    chk.Parent = folder
                end
                chk.Color = ((row + (col > 0 and 1 or 0)) % 2 == 0) and Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255) or Color3.fromRGB(20, 20, 20)
                chk.Size = Vector3.new(1.0, 0.16, 0.8)
                chk.CFrame = cfBase * CFrame.new(col * 0.55, 0.08, -(row * 0.8 + 0.4))
            end
        end

        -- ฟังก์ชันสร้าง/อัปเดตหัวลูกศรสไตล์ Kamen Rider CycloneJoker 3D + Decal Plate
        local function ensureKamenHead(prefix, cf, len, isDestination)
            local w = len * 0.85

            -- 1. แผ่นภาพกราฟิก Kamen Rider Double ด้านบนหัวลูกศร
            local plateName = prefix .. "_Plate"
            local plate = folder:FindFirstChild(plateName)
            if not plate then
                plate = Instance.new("Part")
                plate.Name = plateName
                plate.Anchored = true
                plate.CanCollide = false
                plate.Transparency = 1
                plate.Parent = folder

                local decal = Instance.new("Decal")
                decal.Name = "KamenDecal"
                decal.Face = Enum.NormalId.Top
                pcall(function()
                    if getcustomasset then
                        decal.Texture = getcustomasset("kamen_rider_arrow_trans.png")
                    end
                end)
                decal.Parent = plate
            end
            plate.Size = Vector3.new(len * 1.5, 0.06, len * 0.75)
            plate.CFrame = cf * CFrame.new(0, 0.15, 0) * CFrame.Angles(0, math.rad(90), 0)

            -- 2. ปีกคมดาบเขียวนีออนทรงลูกศร (Left & Right Cyclone Blades)
            for _, side in ipairs({ -1, 1 }) do
                local bName = prefix .. "_Blade_" .. (side == -1 and "L" or "R")
                local blade = folder:FindFirstChild(bName)
                if not blade then
                    blade = Instance.new("Part")
                    blade.Name = bName
                    blade.Anchored = true
                    blade.CanCollide = false
                    blade.Material = Enum.Material.Neon
                    blade.Color = isDestination and Color3.fromRGB(255, 215, 0) or Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 75)
                    blade.Parent = folder
                end
                blade.Size = Vector3.new(0.48, 0.28, len * 0.85)
                blade.CFrame = cf * CFrame.new(side * (w * 0.45), 0.10, len * 0.1) * CFrame.Angles(0, math.rad(side * 32), 0)

                -- 3. ครีบความเร็วสีขาวตัดขอบ (White Racing Aero Fins)
                local fName = prefix .. "_Fin_" .. (side == -1 and "L" or "R")
                local fin = folder:FindFirstChild(fName)
                if not fin then
                    fin = Instance.new("Part")
                    fin.Name = fName
                    fin.Anchored = true
                    fin.CanCollide = false
                    fin.Material = Enum.Material.Neon
                    fin.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
                    fin.Parent = folder
                end
                fin.Size = Vector3.new(0.24, 0.29, len * 0.6)
                fin.CFrame = cf * CFrame.new(side * (w * 0.52), 0.11, len * 0.22) * CFrame.Angles(0, math.rad(side * 32), 0)
            end

            -- 4. หัวแหลมลิ่มหน้าลูกศร (Sharp Piercing Apex Wedge)
            local tipName = prefix .. "_Tip"
            local tip = folder:FindFirstChild(tipName)
            if not tip then
                tip = Instance.new("WedgePart")
                tip.Name = tipName
                tip.Anchored = true
                tip.CanCollide = false
                tip.Material = Enum.Material.Neon
                tip.Color = isDestination and Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 120) or Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 85)
                tip.Parent = folder
            end
            tip.Size = Vector3.new(0.95, 0.32, len * 0.7)
            tip.CFrame = cf * CFrame.new(0, 0.10, -len * 0.35) * CFrame.Angles(0, math.rad(90), 0)

            -- 5. เกราะฐานด้านในสีดำสนิท (Onyx Black Inner Armor Core)
            local coreName = prefix .. "_Core"
            local core = folder:FindFirstChild(coreName)
            if not core then
                core = Instance.new("Part")
                core.Name = coreName
                core.Anchored = true
                core.CanCollide = false
                core.Material = Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic
                core.Color = Color3.fromRGB(18, 18, 22)
                core.Parent = folder
            end
            core.Size = Vector3.new(w * 0.6, 0.22, len * 0.65)
            core.CFrame = cf * CFrame.new(0, 0.08, 0)
        end

        -- 2.5 อัปเดตหัวลูกศรปลายทาง ณ จุดตัวบอส (Far Arrowhead)
        local farHeadCenter = pStart + dir * (shaftLen + headLen * 0.5)
        local farHeadCF = CFrame.lookAt(farHeadCenter, farHeadCenter + dir)
        ensureKamenHead("GuideArrowHead_Far", farHeadCF, headLen, true)

        -- 2.6 อัปเดตหัวลูกศรหน้าเท้าผู้เล่น (Near Arrowhead)
        if totalDist > 14.0 then
            local nearDist = math.min(6.5, totalDist * 0.4)
            local nearHeadCenter = pStart + dir * nearDist
            local nearHeadCF = CFrame.lookAt(nearHeadCenter, nearHeadCenter + dir)
            ensureKamenHead("GuideArrowHead_Near", nearHeadCF, 5.0, false)
        else
            for _, sfx in ipairs({"_Plate", "_Blade_L", "_Blade_R", "_Fin_L", "_Fin_R", "_Tip", "_Core"}) do
                local oldNear = folder:FindFirstChild("GuideArrowHead_Near" .. sfx)
                if oldNear then oldNear:Destroy() end
            end
        end
        end

        -- ล้างชิ้นส่วนแบบเก่าที่ตกค้างทั้งหมด
        for _, oldName in ipairs({
            "GuideArrowHead_Near_L", "GuideArrowHead_Near_R",
            "GuideArrowHead_Far_L", "GuideArrowHead_Far_R",
            "GuideArrowHead"
        }) do
            local oldP = folder:FindFirstChild(oldName)
            if oldP then oldP:Destroy() end
        end

        -- ลบเส้นและจุด Tip เก่าทิ้ง 100% (ตัด GuideArrowTip ออกจากระบบทั้งหมด ห้ามมีอยู่เด็ดขาด)
        local oldBeam = folder:FindFirstChild("GuideArrowBeam")
        if oldBeam then oldBeam:Destroy() end
        local oldTip = folder:FindFirstChild("GuideArrowTip")
        if oldTip then oldTip:Destroy() end
        local oldTipWs = workspace:FindFirstChild("GuideArrowTip")
        if oldTipWs then oldTipWs:Destroy() end
        local oldAtt = root:FindFirstChild("GolfHub_GuideAtt0")
        if oldAtt then oldAtt:Destroy() end

        -- ลบป้ายบอกทางลอยฟ้าและเสาบีคอนทิ้งทั้งหมด 100% ตามคำสั่งผู้ใช้ ("ไม่เอาป้าย")
        local oldBeacon = folder:FindFirstChild("GuideBeacon")
        if oldBeacon then oldBeacon:Destroy() end
        local oldBeaconWs = workspace:FindFirstChild("GuideBeacon")
        if oldBeaconWs then oldBeaconWs:Destroy() end
        local oldBb = folder:FindFirstChild("GuideBillboard")
        if oldBb then oldBb:Destroy() end

        -- 4. วงแหวนจำกัดระยะ 64 studs บนพื้นห้องบอส (แสดงเฉพาะเมื่ออยู่ในโซนห้องบอส)
        local leashRing = folder:FindFirstChild("GuideLeashRing")
        if not leashRing then
            leashRing = Instance.new("Part")
            leashRing.Name = "GuideLeashRing"
            leashRing.Shape = Enum.PartType.Cylinder
            leashRing.Anchored = true
            leashRing.CanCollide = false
            leashRing.Material = Enum.Material.Neon
            leashRing.Color = Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 200)
            leashRing.Transparency = 0.88
            leashRing.Size = Vector3.new(0.18, 128, 128)
            leashRing.Parent = folder
        end
        if not isFarFromTarget then
            leashRing.Transparency = 0.88
            local arenaFloorY = (targetPos.Y or 172.4) - 2.8
            leashRing.CFrame = CFrame.new(targetPos.X, arenaFloorY, targetPos.Z) * CFrame.Angles(0, 0, math.rad(90))
        else
            leashRing.Transparency = 1.0
        end

        -- 5. "บังคับเดินตามลูกศรทันที เมื่อลูกศรชี้ไปทางไหน"
        -- ตัดการหันหน้ากลับเมือง / ตัดการเดินถอยหลังทั้งหมด 100%!
        -- เฉพาะเมื่ออยู่ในโซนบอส และไม่ได้กำลังเดินทางผ่านเสาวาร์ป
        local warpingNow = _G.GolfHub_isWarpingTravel and (tick() - (_G.GolfHub_warpStartedAt or 0)) < 45
        if forceMove and hum and root and not warpingNow then
            if not isFarFromTarget and playerPos.Z <= -25 then
                root.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(playerPos, Vector3.new(lookTarget.X, playerPos.Y, lookTarget.Z))
                hum:MoveTo(lookTarget)
            end
        end
    end)
end

-- ====================================================================
-- BOSS AOE DODGE & GREEN CIRCLE SAFE ZONE ENGINE (WALK ONLY, NO TELEPORT)
-- ====================================================================
local function isDangerRedColor(c)
    local r, g, b = c.R, c.G, c.B
    -- สีแดง / แสด / ส้มแดง (Telegraph พื้นที่โจมตีสีแดงของบอส u114 = Color3.fromRGB(240, 62, 56))
    return (r > 0.65 and g < 0.50 and b < 0.50) 
        or (r > 0.55 and (r - g) > 0.20 and (r - b) > 0.20)
        or (r > 0.70 and b < 0.45)
end

local function isSafeGreenColor(c)
    local r, g, b = c.R, c.G, c.B
    -- สีเขียวสว่างของเกม Hunting Together RPG (u119 = Color3.fromRGB(70, 255, 120))
    return (g > 0.85 and r < 0.45 and b < 0.60) or ((r - 70/255)^2 + (g - 1)^2 + (b - 120/255)^2 < 0.08)
end

-- ====================================================================
-- DIRECT BOSSFX REMOTE EVENT LISTENER (ZERO-LATENCY GEOMETRIC TELEGRAPH)
-- Full 6-Skill Tracker for Volcano Turtle King + All Field Bosses
-- ====================================================================
local activeBossDangerZones = {}
local activeBossSafeZones = {}
-- Follow a real walking path (Roblox PathfindingService) to a goal, one waypoint per call. Returns true while it is steering the humanoid.
_G.GolfHub_PathWalk = function(root, hum, goal)
    local st = _G.GolfHub_PathState
    if not st then st = { wps = nil, goal = nil, at = 0 } _G.GolfHub_PathState = st end
    local now = tick()
    -- ComputeAsync YIELDS (and can hang for a long time inside boss rooms), so it runs in its own thread: the caller (a fight loop) must never wait for it
    if (not st.wps or not st.goal or (st.goal - goal).Magnitude > 10 or now - st.at > 3) and not (st.computing and now - st.computeStart < 6) then
        st.at = now
        st.goal = goal
        st.computing = true
        st.computeStart = now
        local from = root.Position
        task.spawn(function()
            local ok, path = pcall(function()
                local p = game:GetService("PathfindingService"):CreatePath({ AgentRadius = 3, AgentHeight = 6, AgentCanJump = true, AgentCanClimb = false })
                p:ComputeAsync(from, goal)
                return p
            end)
            if ok and path and path.Status == Enum.PathStatus.Success then
                st.wps = path:GetWaypoints()
                st.i = 2 -- waypoint 1 is where the character stood
            else
                st.wps = nil
            end
            st.computing = false
        end)
    end
    if not st.wps then return false end
    -- only forward: searching from waypoint 1 every call sent the character back to the start as soon as it had left it
    for i = st.i or 2, #st.wps do
        local wp = st.wps[i]
        st.i = i
        if (Vector3.new(wp.Position.X, root.Position.Y, wp.Position.Z) - root.Position).Magnitude > 3.5 then
            hum:MoveTo(wp.Position)
            if wp.Action == Enum.PathWaypointAction.Jump then hum.Jump = true end
            return true
        end
        st.i = i + 1
    end
    return false
end

local activeBossFxSkill = nil
local activeBossFxExpire = 0

_G.GolfHub_BossExt = {
    -- Boss 5 (Volcano Turtle King)
    lavaPools          = {}, -- { { center, radius, expire } } (Lava Rain 3.0s burning pools)
    pendingPools       = {}, -- captured on Lava Rain warn -> promoted on hit
    spinUntil          = 0,  -- Inferno Spin active window (Warn 1.5s + Spin 5.0s)
    spinRadius         = 48, -- Size 72 body + 8 spin radius + margin
    stompStage1Until   = 0,  -- Earthquake Stomp stage 1 (R=72 circle_self)
    stompDonutUntil    = 0,  -- Earthquake Stomp stage 2 (Inner=72..118 donut — rush inside!)
    stompCenter        = nil,
    stompInnerR        = 72,
    flameSweepUntil    = 0,  -- Flame Breath 4-hit sweep (+55 -> +18 -> -18 -> -55)
    flameSweepFwd      = nil,
    shellFortressUntil = 0,  -- Shell Fortress (R=82, Warn=3.0s, 2.8x blast)
    shellFortressCenter = nil,
    shellFortressR     = 82,
    magmaBiteUntil     = 0,  -- Magma Bite (60 deg cone, R=100, Lunge=16)
    magmaBiteFwd       = nil,
    magmaBiteCenter    = nil,

    -- Boss 4 (Tempest Dragon King)
    stormEyeUntil      = 0,  -- Eye of the Storm (Donut: Inner=16 .. Outer=60)
    stormEyeCenter     = nil,
    stormEyeInnerR     = 16,
    frostBreathUntil   = 0,  -- Frost Breath (Cone: Angle=80, Radius=44)
    frostBreathFwd     = nil,

    -- Universal Skill Categories for All 5 Bosses
    crossUntil         = 0,  -- Cross of Ra / Tempest Cross (Cross: 45 deg diagonal step)
    crossCenter        = nil,
    coneAttackUntil    = 0,  -- Rending Claws / Scarab Swarm / Frost Breath (Frontal Cones)
    coneAttackFwd      = nil,
    chargeAttackUntil  = 0,  -- Rampage / Savage Rush / Tomb Rush / Dragon Dive (Linear Charges)
    chargeAttackFwd    = nil,
    lineAttackUntil    = 0,  -- Shockwave Punch / Sonic Roar / Sun Ray / Glacier Lance (Linear Beams)
    lineAttackFwd      = nil,
    roomUltimateUntil  = 0,  -- Jungle Roar / Apex Roar / Sandstorm Judgment / Blizzard Tempest (Green Safe Circles)
    roomSafeCenter     = nil,
    roomSafeRadius     = 9,
}

pcall(function()
    local Remotes = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):WaitForChild("Remotes", 5)
    if Remotes then
        local bossFx = Remotes:WaitForChild("BossFx", 5)
        if bossFx and bossFx:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
            _G.GolfHub_Conn(bossFx.OnClientEvent, function(action, bossName, skillName, zones, duration)
                if game.PlaceId == DUNGEON_PLACE_ID then return end
                local ext = _G.GolfHub_BossExt
                local sLower = tostring(skillName or ""):lower()
                local now = tick()

                if action == "cancel" then
                    activeBossDangerZones = {}
                    activeBossSafeZones = {}
                    activeBossFxSkill = nil
                    activeBossFxExpire = 0
                    if sLower:find("spin") then ext.spinUntil = 0 end
                    if sLower:find("flame") or sLower:find("breath") then ext.flameSweepUntil = 0 ext.frostBreathUntil = 0 end
                    if sLower:find("shell") then ext.shellFortressUntil = 0 end
                    if sLower:find("stomp") then ext.stompStage1Until = 0 ext.stompDonutUntil = 0 end
                    if sLower:find("bite") then ext.magmaBiteUntil = 0 end
                    if sLower:find("lava") then ext.pendingPools = {} end
                    if sLower:find("storm") or sLower:find("eye") then ext.stormEyeUntil = 0 end
                    if sLower:find("cross") then ext.crossUntil = 0 end
                    if sLower:find("charge") or sLower:find("rush") or sLower:find("dive") or sLower:find("rampage") then ext.chargeAttackUntil = 0 end
                    if sLower:find("ray") or sLower:find("lance") or sLower:find("punch") or sLower:find("sonic") then ext.lineAttackUntil = 0 end
                    if sLower:find("roar") or sLower:find("judgment") or sLower:find("tempest") then ext.roomUltimateUntil = 0 end
                    return
                end

                if action == "hit" then
                    -- Lava Rain: promote warn circles to 3.3s lingering burning pools!
