                    if sLower:find("lava") then
                        local srcZones = (#ext.pendingPools > 0) and ext.pendingPools or (type(zones) == "table" and zones or {})
                        for _, pz in ipairs(srcZones) do
                            local c = pz.Center or pz.center
                            local r = (pz.Radius or pz.radius or 13) + 2.2
                            if typeof(c) == "Vector3" then
                                table.insert(ext.lavaPools, { center = c, radius = r, expire = now + 3.35 })
                            end
                        end
                        ext.pendingPools = {}
                    end

                    -- Earthquake Stomp Stage 1 exploded -> Stage 2 Donut (72..118, Warn 0.8s) starts IMMEDIATELY!
                    if sLower:find("stomp") and now <= (ext.stompStage1Until + 0.4) then
                        ext.stompStage1Until = 0
                        ext.stompDonutUntil = now + 1.15
                    elseif sLower:find("stomp") then
                        ext.stompDonutUntil = 0
                    end

                    if sLower:find("shell") then ext.shellFortressUntil = 0 end
                    if sLower:find("bite") then ext.magmaBiteUntil = 0 end
                    if sLower:find("storm") or sLower:find("eye") then ext.stormEyeUntil = 0 end
                    if sLower:find("cross") then ext.crossUntil = 0 end
                    if sLower:find("dive") or sLower:find("rush") or sLower:find("rampage") then ext.chargeAttackUntil = 0 end
                    if sLower:find("lance") or sLower:find("ray") or sLower:find("punch") or sLower:find("sonic") then ext.lineAttackUntil = 0 end

                    activeBossDangerZones = {}
                    activeBossSafeZones = {}
                    activeBossFxSkill = nil
                    activeBossFxExpire = 0
                    return
                end

                if action == "warn" and type(zones) == "table" then
                    local dur = tonumber(duration) or 1.8
                    activeBossFxSkill = skillName
                    activeBossFxExpire = now + dur + 0.35
                    activeBossDangerZones = {}
                    activeBossSafeZones = {}

                    local hasDonut = false
                    local firstCenter, firstDir, firstR, firstInner = nil, nil, nil, nil
                    local newPools = {}

                    for _, z in ipairs(zones) do
                        if z.Safe then
                            table.insert(activeBossSafeZones, z)
                        else
                            table.insert(activeBossDangerZones, z)
                            if z.Center and not firstCenter then firstCenter = z.Center end
                            if z.Dir and not firstDir then firstDir = z.Dir end
                            if z.Radius and not firstR then firstR = z.Radius end
                            if z.Inner and z.Inner > 0 then
                                hasDonut = true
                                firstInner = z.Inner
                            end
                            if sLower:find("lava") or z.Fx == "lava" then
                                if typeof(z.Center) == "Vector3" then
                                    table.insert(newPools, { Center = z.Center, Radius = z.Radius or 13 })
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end

                    if #newPools > 0 then
                        ext.pendingPools = newPools
                    end

                    -- 1. Earthquake Stomp (Boss 5)
                    if sLower:find("stomp") then
                        ext.stompCenter = firstCenter or ext.stompCenter
                        if hasDonut or (firstR and firstR >= 100) then
                            ext.stompStage1Until = 0
                            ext.stompInnerR = firstInner or 72
                            ext.stompDonutUntil = now + dur + 0.25
                        else
                            ext.stompDonutUntil = 0
                            ext.stompInnerR = firstR or 72
                            ext.stompStage1Until = now + dur + 0.15
                        end
                    -- 2. Shell Fortress (Boss 5)
                    elseif sLower:find("shell") then
                        ext.shellFortressCenter = firstCenter
                        ext.shellFortressR = firstR or 82
                        ext.shellFortressUntil = now + dur + 0.3
                    -- 3. Inferno Spin (Boss 5)
                    elseif sLower:find("spin") then
                        ext.spinRadius = math.max((firstR or 8) + 38, 48)
                        ext.spinUntil = now + dur + 5.2
                    -- 4. Flame Breath (Boss 5)
                    elseif sLower:find("flame") and sLower:find("breath") then
                        local sweepDur = (dur >= 0.8) and (dur + 1.45) or (dur + 0.45)
                        ext.flameSweepUntil = math.max(ext.flameSweepUntil, now + sweepDur)
                        if firstDir then ext.flameSweepFwd = firstDir end
                    -- 5. Magma Bite (Boss 5)
                    elseif sLower:find("bite") then
                        ext.magmaBiteUntil = now + dur + 0.3
                        ext.magmaBiteCenter = firstCenter
                        ext.magmaBiteFwd = firstDir
                    -- 6. Eye of the Storm (Boss 4 Donut: Inner=16 .. Radius=60)
                    elseif sLower:find("eye of the storm") or (hasDonut and sLower:find("storm")) then
                        ext.stormEyeCenter = firstCenter
                        ext.stormEyeInnerR = firstInner or 16
                        ext.stormEyeUntil = now + dur + 0.3
                    -- 7. Frost Breath (Boss 4 Cone: Angle=80, Radius=44)
                    elseif sLower:find("frost") and sLower:find("breath") then
                        ext.frostBreathUntil = now + dur + 0.3
                        if firstDir then ext.frostBreathFwd = firstDir end
                    -- 8. Cross Skills (Cross of Ra - Boss 3 / Tempest Cross - Boss 4)
                    elseif sLower:find("cross") then
                        ext.crossUntil = now + dur + 0.3
                        ext.crossCenter = firstCenter
                    -- 9. Linear Charges (Rampage - Boss 1 / Savage Rush - Boss 2 / Tomb Rush - Boss 3 / Dragon Dive - Boss 4)
                    elseif sLower:find("rampage") or sLower:find("savage rush") or sLower:find("tomb rush") or sLower:find("dragon dive") or sLower:find("charge") then
                        ext.chargeAttackUntil = now + dur + 0.3
                        if firstDir then ext.chargeAttackFwd = firstDir end
                    -- 10. Linear Beams (Shockwave Punch - Boss 1 / Sonic Roar - Boss 2 / Sun Ray - Boss 3 / Glacier Lance - Boss 4)
                    elseif sLower:find("shockwave") or sLower:find("sonic roar") or sLower:find("sun ray") or sLower:find("glacier lance") or sLower:find("ray") or sLower:find("lance") then
                        ext.lineAttackUntil = now + dur + 0.3
                        if firstDir then ext.lineAttackFwd = firstDir end
                    -- 11. Frontal Cones (Rending Claws - Boss 2 / Scarab Swarm - Boss 3)
                    elseif sLower:find("rending") or sLower:find("scarab") or sLower:find("claw") or sLower:find("swarm") then
                        ext.coneAttackUntil = now + dur + 0.3
                        if firstDir then ext.coneAttackFwd = firstDir end
                    -- 12. Room Ultimates with Green Safe Circles (Jungle Roar - Boss 1 / Apex Roar - Boss 2 / Sandstorm Judgment - Boss 3 / Blizzard Tempest - Boss 4)
                    elseif sLower:find("jungle roar") or sLower:find("apex roar") or sLower:find("sandstorm") or sLower:find("blizzard") or sLower:find("roar") or sLower:find("judgment") then
                        ext.roomUltimateUntil = now + dur + 0.3
                        if #activeBossSafeZones > 0 then
                            ext.roomSafeCenter = activeBossSafeZones[1].Center or (activeBossSafeZones[1].CFrame and activeBossSafeZones[1].CFrame.Position)
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)
        end
    end
end)

-- ตรวจสอบว่าหน้าจอมีข้อความเตือนสกิลบอสขึ้นมาหรือไม่ (LumiBossWarn / ScreenGui)
local lastWarningBannerText = nil
local lastWarningBannerTick = 0

local function getBossWarningBanner()
    local pGui = lp:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
    if pGui then
        -- ตรวจสอบตรงจาก LumiBossWarn ของตัวเกม (O(1) Instant Check ไม่ทำให้เกมกระตุก)
        local lbw = pGui:FindFirstChild("LumiBossWarn")
        if lbw and lbw.Enabled then
            local lbl = lbw:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("TextLabel", true)
            if lbl and lbl.Visible and lbl.TextTransparency < 0.5 and lbl.Text and #lbl.Text > 0 then
                lastWarningBannerText = lbl.Text
                lastWarningBannerTick = tick()
                return lbl.Text
            end
        end
    end

    -- คงสถานะการแจ้งเตือนไว้ 2.2 วินาทีหลังจากแถบหายไป เพื่อให้ครอบคลุมช่วงเวลาร่ายสกิลทั้งหมด
    if lastWarningBannerText and (tick() - lastWarningBannerTick < 2.2) then
        return lastWarningBannerText
    end
    lastWarningBannerText = nil
    return nil
end

-- ตรวจสอบว่าตำแหน่งจุด P อยู่ในขอบเขต Part หรือไม่ (รองรับทั้งกล่องสี่เหลี่ยม แถบยาว Cross และทรงกระบอก)
local function isPointInsidePartOBB(pos, part, margin)
    margin = margin or 2.0
    local cf = part.CFrame
    local size = part.Size
    local flatPos = Vector3.new(pos.X, cf.Position.Y, pos.Z)
    local localPos = cf:PointToObjectSpace(flatPos)
    local hx = (size.X * 0.5) + margin
    local hz = (size.Z * 0.5) + margin
    return math.abs(localPos.X) <= hx and math.abs(localPos.Z) <= hz
end

-- คำนวณเวกเตอร์ผลัก (Push Vector) เพื่อหนีออกจาก Part แบบแม่นยำตามขอบ OBB
local function getEscapeVectorFromPart(pos, part, margin)
    margin = margin or 3.0
    local cf = part.CFrame
    local size = part.Size
    local flatPos = Vector3.new(pos.X, cf.Position.Y, pos.Z)
    local localPos = cf:PointToObjectSpace(flatPos)
    local hx = (size.X * 0.5) + margin
    local hz = (size.Z * 0.5) + margin

    if math.abs(localPos.X) > hx or math.abs(localPos.Z) > hz then
        return nil
    end

    local distToX = hx - math.abs(localPos.X)
    local distToZ = hz - math.abs(localPos.Z)

    local escapeLocal
    if distToX <= distToZ then
        -- หลบออกทางด้านข้างแกน X สั้นที่สุด (ฉากหลบแถบยาว เช่น Tomb Rush / Sun Ray)
        local signX = localPos.X >= 0 and 1 or -1
        escapeLocal = Vector3.new(signX * (hx + 5.0), 0, localPos.Z)
    else
        -- หลบออกทางด้านหน้า/หลังแกน Z สั้นที่สุด
        local signZ = localPos.Z >= 0 and 1 or -1
        escapeLocal = Vector3.new(localPos.X, 0, signZ * (hz + 5.0))
    end

    local escapeWorld = cf:PointToWorldSpace(escapeLocal)
    return Vector3.new(escapeWorld.X, pos.Y, escapeWorld.Z)
end

task.wait() -- yield point: spread boss farm setup over frames
local function scanBossArenaZonesRaw(arenaPos, maxRadius)
    arenaPos = arenaPos or Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
    maxRadius = maxRadius or 120

    local dangerZones = {}
    local safeZones = {}
    local seenParts = {}

    local function checkInstance(obj)
        if not obj or not obj.Parent then return end
        if not obj:IsA("BasePart") then return end
        if seenParts[obj] then return end
        seenParts[obj] = true

        local pPos = obj.Position
        local dist2D = math.sqrt((pPos.X - arenaPos.X)^2 + (pPos.Z - arenaPos.Z)^2)
        if dist2D > maxRadius then return end

        local size = obj.Size
        local maxHoriz = math.max(size.X, size.Z)
        -- ต้องเป็น Part ที่มีพื้นที่บนพื้นราบสมจริง (กว้างหรือยาวอย่างน้อย 3.5 studs)
        if math.max(maxHoriz, math.max(size.X, size.Y)) < 3.5 then return end

        local col = obj.Color
        local isCylinder = false; pcall(function() if obj:IsA("Part") and obj.Shape == Enum.PartType.Cylinder then isCylinder = true end end)




        local approxRad = maxHoriz * 0.5

        if isSafeGreenColor(col) and approxRad >= 4.0 then
            table.insert(safeZones, { pos = pPos, radius = approxRad + 2.0, part = obj, isCylinder = isCylinder })
        elseif isDangerRedColor(col) or obj.Name:lower():find("danger") or obj.Name:lower():find("telegraph") or obj.Name:lower():find("aoe") or obj.Name:lower():find("hazard") then
            table.insert(dangerZones, { pos = pPos, radius = approxRad + 3.0, part = obj, isCylinder = isCylinder })
        end
    end

    -- 1. สแกนทุกโฟลเดอร์สำหรับสกิลและเอฟเฟกต์บอสของเกม
    local candidateFolders = {
        "LumiBossFx", "LumiBossSkillFx", "BossFx", "Telegraph", "Telegraphs",
        "BossSkill", "SkillFx", "Effects", "Skills", "Debris", "Temp", "Projectiles"
    }
    for _, folderName in ipairs(candidateFolders) do
        local container = workspace:FindFirstChild(folderName)
        if container then
            for _, desc in ipairs(container:GetDescendants()) do
                if desc:IsA("BasePart") then
                    checkInstance(desc)
                end
            end
        end
    end

    -- 2. สแกนรอบตัวบอสโดยตรง (หากโมเดลบอสมี Part Telegraph ติดอยู่)
    local monsters = workspace:FindFirstChild("Monsters") or workspace:FindFirstChild("Bosses")
    if monsters then
        for _, m in ipairs(monsters:GetChildren()) do
            -- only monsters inside / near the arena can carry its telegraphs (workspace.Monsters holds every mob of the server)
            local okPv, pv = pcall(function() return m:GetPivot().Position end)
            if okPv and (pv - arenaPos).Magnitude > maxRadius + 80 then continue end
            for _, desc in ipairs(m:GetDescendants()) do
                if desc:IsA("BasePart") and (desc.Name:lower():find("telegraph") or desc.Name:lower():find("skill") or desc.Name:lower():find("fx")) then
                    checkInstance(desc)
                end
            end
        end
    end

    -- 3. สแกน Part สีแดงบนพื้น Workspace ในอารีน่า
    for _, child in ipairs(workspace:GetChildren()) do
        if child:IsA("BasePart") then
            local dist = (child.Position - arenaPos).Magnitude
            if dist < maxRadius then
                checkInstance(child)
            end
        end
    end

    return dangerZones, safeZones
end

-- cached wrapper: the scan walks several folders / mobs, so reuse the result for 0.2 s (callers run every frame)
local scanCache = { t = 0, key = nil, d = nil, s = nil }
local function scanBossArenaZones(arenaPos, maxRadius)
    local now = tick()
    local key = tostring(arenaPos) .. "|" .. tostring(maxRadius)
    if scanCache.key == key and now - scanCache.t < 0.2 and scanCache.d then
        return scanCache.d, scanCache.s
    end
    local d, s = scanBossArenaZonesRaw(arenaPos, maxRadius)
    scanCache.t, scanCache.key, scanCache.d, scanCache.s = now, key, d, s
    return d, s
end

local function isPositionInAnyDangerZone(pos, dangerZones, bossPos)
    local now = tick()
    local ext = _G.GolfHub_BossExt

    -- 0. ตรวจสอบบ่อลาวา 3 วิ (Lava Rain Pools) + Inferno Spin 5 วิ (Moving Spin Boss)
    if ext then
        if type(ext.lavaPools) == "table" and #ext.lavaPools > 0 then
            for i = #ext.lavaPools, 1, -1 do
                local pool = ext.lavaPools[i]
                if now >= pool.expire then
                    table.remove(ext.lavaPools, i)
                else
                    local dPool = math.sqrt((pool.center.X - pos.X)^2 + (pool.center.Z - pos.Z)^2)
                    if dPool < (pool.radius + 1.5) then
                        return true
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        if now < (ext.spinUntil or 0) and bossPos then
            local dSpin = math.sqrt((bossPos.X - pos.X)^2 + (bossPos.Z - pos.Z)^2)
            if dSpin < (ext.spinRadius or 48) then
                return true
            end
        end
    end

    -- 1. ตรวจสอบจาก RemoteEvent BossFx (Zero-latency Server Truth — รองรับ Donut Inner ด้วย!)
    local hasExactFx = (now < activeBossFxExpire) and (#activeBossDangerZones > 0)
    if hasExactFx then
        for _, dz in ipairs(activeBossDangerZones) do
            if dz.Inner and dz.Inner > 0 and dz.Center and dz.Radius then
                -- วงแหวนโดนัท (เช่น Earthquake Stomp จังหวะ 2: Inner=72 .. Radius=118) -> ข้างใน (< Inner-3) ปลอดภัย!
                local dist2D = math.sqrt((dz.Center.X - pos.X)^2 + (dz.Center.Z - pos.Z)^2)
                if dist2D >= (dz.Inner - 3.5) and dist2D <= (dz.Radius + 3.5) then
                    return true
                end
            elseif dz.Kind == "circle" and dz.Center and dz.Radius then
                local dist2D = math.sqrt((dz.Center.X - pos.X)^2 + (dz.Center.Z - pos.Z)^2)
                if dist2D < (dz.Radius + 3.0) then
                    return true
                end
            elseif dz.CFrame and dz.Width and dz.Length then
                local flatPos = Vector3.new(pos.X, dz.CFrame.Position.Y, pos.Z)
                local localPos = dz.CFrame:PointToObjectSpace(flatPos)
                local hx = (dz.Width * 0.5) + 2.5
                local hz = (dz.Length * 0.5) + 2.5
                if math.abs(localPos.X) <= hx and math.abs(localPos.Z) <= hz then
                    return true
                end
            elseif dz.Kind == "cone" and dz.Center and dz.Dir and dz.Radius and dz.Half then
                local diff = pos - dz.Center
                local dist2D = math.sqrt(diff.X^2 + diff.Z^2)
                if dist2D <= (dz.Radius + 3.5) then
                    local dir2D = dist2D > 0.1 and Vector3.new(diff.X, 0, diff.Z).Unit or Vector3.new(0, 0, 1)
                    local fwd2D = Vector3.new(dz.Dir.X, 0, dz.Dir.Z).Unit
                    local dot = math.clamp(fwd2D:Dot(dir2D), -1, 1)
                    local angle = math.acos(dot)
                    if angle <= (dz.Half + 0.14) then
                        return true
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end

    -- 2. ตรวจสอบจาก Physical Parts ใน Workspace (ข้าม WedgePart ของกรวยเมื่อมีข้อมูล BossFx แม่นยำแล้ว)
    if dangerZones then
        for _, dz in ipairs(dangerZones) do
            if hasExactFx and dz.part and dz.part:IsA("WedgePart") then
                continue
            end
            if dz.part and dz.part:IsA("BasePart") and not dz.isCylinder then
                if isPointInsidePartOBB(pos, dz.part, 2.5) then
                    return true
                end
            else
                local dist2D = math.sqrt((dz.pos.X - pos.X)^2 + (dz.pos.Z - pos.Z)^2)
                if dist2D < (dz.radius or 12) then
                    return true
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return false
end

local lastEvadeGoal = nil
local lastEvadeTick = 0

local function handleBossSkillEvade(root, hum, bDef, bossModel)
    if not root or not hum or not bDef then return false, nil end

    local now = tick()
    local myPos = root.Position
    local maxArenaRadius = bDef.arenaRadius or 38
    local maxSafeRad = maxArenaRadius - 2.5
    local doorZ = bDef.doorZ
    local ext = _G.GolfHub_BossExt

    -- เคลียร์ Zone ที่หมดอายุแล้วโดยอัตโนมัติ ป้องกันข้อมูลเก่าค้าง
    if now >= activeBossFxExpire then
        activeBossDangerZones = {}
        activeBossSafeZones = {}
        activeBossFxSkill = nil
    end

    -- เคลียร์บ่อลาวา 3 วิที่หมดอายุแล้ว
    local hasActiveLavaPools = false
    if ext and type(ext.lavaPools) == "table" then
        for i = #ext.lavaPools, 1, -1 do
            if now >= ext.lavaPools[i].expire then
                table.remove(ext.lavaPools, i)
            end
        end
        hasActiveLavaPools = (#ext.lavaPools > 0)
    end

    local dangerZones, safeZones = scanBossArenaZones(bDef.arenaPos, maxArenaRadius + 20)
    local warningBanner = getBossWarningBanner()
    local bPart = bossModel and (bossModel:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or bossModel.PrimaryPart or bossModel:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart"))
    local bossPos = bPart and bPart.Position or bDef.arenaPos

    local hasExtSkill = ext and (
        now < (ext.stompDonutUntil or 0)
        or now < (ext.stompStage1Until or 0)
        or now < (ext.shellFortressUntil or 0)
        or now < (ext.spinUntil or 0)
        or now < (ext.flameSweepUntil or 0)
        or now < (ext.magmaBiteUntil or 0)
        or now < (ext.stormEyeUntil or 0)
        or now < (ext.frostBreathUntil or 0)
        or now < (ext.crossUntil or 0)
        or now < (ext.coneAttackUntil or 0)
        or now < (ext.chargeAttackUntil or 0)
        or now < (ext.lineAttackUntil or 0)
        or now < (ext.roomUltimateUntil or 0)
        or hasActiveLavaPools
    )

    -- หากข้อความเตือนระบุ "get out of the red ground" ชัดเจนว่าไม่มีวงเขียว (ปลอดภัยเฉพาะอยู่นอกวงแดง)
    local isRedGroundSkill = warningBanner and warningBanner:lower():find("red ground") ~= nil
    local isGreenCircleSkill = warningBanner and warningBanner:lower():find("green circle") ~= nil

    -- วงเขียวที่แท้จริงต้องมาจาก Server BossFx (z.Safe == true) หรือมีแบนเนอร์สั่งชัดเจนเท่านั้น!
    local hasRealSafeZone = false
    if isGreenCircleSkill and not isRedGroundSkill then
        hasRealSafeZone = true
    elseif (now < activeBossFxExpire) and (#activeBossSafeZones > 0) and not isRedGroundSkill then
        hasRealSafeZone = true
    elseif ext and (now < (ext.roomUltimateUntil or 0)) and not isRedGroundSkill then
        hasRealSafeZone = true
    end

    -- ตรวจสอบว่าขณะนี้มีสกิลบอสกำลังร่ายอยู่หรือไม่
    local isBossSkillActive = (now < activeBossFxExpire) or (warningBanner ~= nil) or (#dangerZones > 0) or hasExtSkill
    if not isBossSkillActive then
        lastEvadeGoal = nil
        return false, nil
    end

    -- ====================================================================
    -- 0. DEDICATED TACTICAL WALK-DODGE FOR ALL 5 BOSSES (LEGIT WALK, NO TP)
    -- ====================================================================
    if ext and bPart then
        local flatAway = Vector3.new(myPos.X - bossPos.X, 0, myPos.Z - bossPos.Z)
        local distToBoss2D = flatAway.Magnitude
        local awayUnit = distToBoss2D > 0.2 and flatAway.Unit or (-bPart.CFrame.LookVector)
        awayUnit = Vector3.new(awayUnit.X, 0, awayUnit.Z)
        if awayUnit.Magnitude > 0.05 then awayUnit = awayUnit.Unit else awayUnit = Vector3.new(0, 0, 1) end

        -- 0A. Earthquake Stomp Stage 2 (Boss 5 Donut: Inner=72 .. Outer=118, Warn=0.8s) -> พุ่งเดินกลับเข้าชิดตัวบอส (< 64 studs)!
        if now < (ext.stompDonutUntil or 0) then
            local c = ext.stompCenter or bossPos
            local innerSafe = math.max(40, (ext.stompInnerR or 72) - 10)
            local dC = math.sqrt((myPos.X - c.X)^2 + (myPos.Z - c.Z)^2)
            if dC >= (innerSafe - 2) then
                local dirToC = Vector3.new(c.X - myPos.X, 0, c.Z - myPos.Z)
                dirToC = dirToC.Magnitude > 0.2 and dirToC.Unit or -awayUnit
                local bestIn = clampInsideArena(Vector3.new(c.X, myPos.Y, c.Z) - dirToC * math.min(46, innerSafe - 8), bDef.arenaPos, maxSafeRad, doorZ)
                for ang = 0, 315, 45 do
                    local rad = math.rad(ang)
                    local cand = clampInsideArena(Vector3.new(c.X + math.cos(rad) * 46, myPos.Y, c.Z + math.sin(rad) * 46), bDef.arenaPos, maxSafeRad, doorZ)
                    if not isPositionInAnyDangerZone(cand, nil, bossPos) then
                        if (cand - myPos).Magnitude < (bestIn - myPos).Magnitude or isPositionInAnyDangerZone(bestIn, nil, bossPos) then
                            bestIn = cand
                        end
                    end
                end
                lastEvadeGoal = bestIn
                lastEvadeTick = now
                hum:MoveTo(bestIn)
                return true, "🍩 หลบโดนัท Earthquake Stomp — เดินพุ่งเข้าชิดบอส!"
            end
        end

        -- 0B. Eye of the Storm (Boss 4 Donut: Inner=16 .. Outer=60, Warn=1.9s) -> พุ่งเข้าตาวายุชิดตัวมังกร (< 14 studs)!
        if now < (ext.stormEyeUntil or 0) then
            local c = ext.stormEyeCenter or bossPos
            local innerSafe = math.max(12, (ext.stormEyeInnerR or 16) - 2)
            local dC = math.sqrt((myPos.X - c.X)^2 + (myPos.Z - c.Z)^2)
            if dC >= innerSafe then
                local dirToC = Vector3.new(c.X - myPos.X, 0, c.Z - myPos.Z)
                dirToC = dirToC.Magnitude > 0.2 and dirToC.Unit or -awayUnit
                local bestIn = clampInsideArena(Vector3.new(c.X, myPos.Y, c.Z) - dirToC * math.min(10, innerSafe - 4), bDef.arenaPos, maxSafeRad, doorZ)
                lastEvadeGoal = bestIn
                lastEvadeTick = now
                hum:MoveTo(bestIn)
                return true, "🌀 หลบโดนัท Eye of the Storm — พุ่งเข้าตาวายุชิดตัวบอส!"
            end
        end

        -- 0C. Earthquake Stomp Stage 1 (Boss 5 Circle R=72, Warn=1.6s) -> เดินออกมารอที่ขอบนอกพอดี (~78 studs) เพื่อพร้อมพุ่งกลับเข้าโดนัท!
        if now < (ext.stompStage1Until or 0) then
            local c = ext.stompCenter or bossPos
            local edgeR = (ext.stompInnerR or 72) + 6.5
            local dC = math.sqrt((myPos.X - c.X)^2 + (myPos.Z - c.Z)^2)
            if dC < edgeR then
                local bestOut, bestOutDist = nil, 999999
                for ang = 0, 337.5, 22.5 do
                    local rad = math.rad(ang)
                    local cand = clampInsideArena(Vector3.new(c.X + math.cos(rad) * (edgeR + 2.5), myPos.Y, c.Z + math.sin(rad) * (edgeR + 2.5)), bDef.arenaPos, maxSafeRad, doorZ)
                    local dCandC = math.sqrt((cand.X - c.X)^2 + (cand.Z - c.Z)^2)
                    if dCandC >= edgeR and not isPositionInAnyDangerZone(cand, nil, bossPos) then
                        local stepD = (cand - myPos).Magnitude
                        if stepD < bestOutDist then
                            bestOutDist = stepD
                            bestOut = cand
                        end
                    end
                end
                bestOut = bestOut or clampInsideArena(Vector3.new(c.X, myPos.Y, c.Z) + awayUnit * (edgeR + 3.0), bDef.arenaPos, maxSafeRad, doorZ)
                lastEvadeGoal = bestOut
                lastEvadeTick = now
                hum:MoveTo(bestOut)
                return true, "💥 หลบ Earthquake Stomp (1/2) — เดินรอขอบนอก 78 studs!"
            end
        end

        -- 0D. Shell Fortress (Boss 5 Circle R=82, Warn=3.0s, Power=2.8x) -> เดินหนีออกนอกรัศมี 89+ studs ทันที!
        if now < (ext.shellFortressUntil or 0) then
            local c = ext.shellFortressCenter or bossPos
            local safeOutR = (ext.shellFortressR or 82) + 7.5
            local dC = math.sqrt((myPos.X - c.X)^2 + (myPos.Z - c.Z)^2)
            if dC < safeOutR then
                local bestOut, bestOutDist = nil, 999999
                for ang = 0, 337.5, 22.5 do
                    local rad = math.rad(ang)
                    local cand = clampInsideArena(Vector3.new(c.X + math.cos(rad) * (safeOutR + 3.0), myPos.Y, c.Z + math.sin(rad) * (safeOutR + 3.0)), bDef.arenaPos, maxSafeRad, doorZ)
                    local dCandC = math.sqrt((cand.X - c.X)^2 + (cand.Z - c.Z)^2)
                    if dCandC >= safeOutR and not isPositionInAnyDangerZone(cand, nil, bossPos) then
                        local stepD = (cand - myPos).Magnitude
                        if stepD < bestOutDist then
                            bestOutDist = stepD
                            bestOut = cand
                        end
                    end
                end
                bestOut = bestOut or clampInsideArena(Vector3.new(c.X, myPos.Y, c.Z) + awayUnit * (safeOutR + 3.0), bDef.arenaPos, maxSafeRad, doorZ)
                lastEvadeGoal = bestOut
                lastEvadeTick = now
                hum:MoveTo(bestOut)
                return true, "🐢 หลบ Shell Fortress (R=82) — เดินออกนอกรัศมีระเบิด!"
            end
        end

        -- 0E. Inferno Spin (Boss 5 Warn 1.5s + Spin Chase 5.0s) -> เดินไคท์วนรอบขอบห้องหนีการหมุนไล่ตลอด 5 วิ!
        if now < (ext.spinUntil or 0) then
            local minSpinSafe = (ext.spinRadius or 48) + 10.0
            if distToBoss2D < minSpinSafe then
                local bestKite, bestScore = nil, -999999
                for ang = 0, 337.5, 22.5 do
                    local rad = math.rad(ang)
                    local dir = Vector3.new(math.cos(rad), 0, math.sin(rad))
                    local cand = clampInsideArena(myPos + dir * 24.0, bDef.arenaPos, maxSafeRad, doorZ)
                    local dBoss = math.sqrt((cand.X - bossPos.X)^2 + (cand.Z - bossPos.Z)^2)
                    local dCenter = math.sqrt((cand.X - bDef.arenaPos.X)^2 + (cand.Z - bDef.arenaPos.Z)^2)
                    local inPool = isPositionInAnyDangerZone(cand, nil, nil)
                    local score = dBoss - (dCenter > maxSafeRad - 8 and 18 or 0) - (inPool and 80 or 0)
                    if score > bestScore then
                        bestScore = score
                        bestKite = cand
                    end
                end
                if bestKite then
                    lastEvadeGoal = bestKite
                    lastEvadeTick = now
                    hum:MoveTo(bestKite)
                    return true, "🌀 หลบ Inferno Spin — กำลังเดินไคท์วนหนีบอส!"
                end
            end
        end

        -- 0F. Cross Attacks (Cross of Ra - Boss 3 / Tempest Cross - Boss 4) -> สเต็ปเฉียง 45 องศาเข้าช่องปลอดภัย
        if now < (ext.crossUntil or 0) or (warningBanner and warningBanner:lower():find("cross")) then
            local flatPos = Vector3.new(myPos.X, bPart.Position.Y, myPos.Z)
            local localPos = bPart.CFrame:PointToObjectSpace(flatPos)
            local inZArm = (math.abs(localPos.X) < 14.0 and math.abs(localPos.Z) < 55.0)
            local inXArm = (math.abs(localPos.Z) < 14.0 and math.abs(localPos.X) < 55.0)
            if inZArm or inXArm then
                local signX = localPos.X >= 0 and 1 or -1
                local signZ = localPos.Z >= 0 and 1 or -1
                local safeDist = 20.0
                local safeLocal = Vector3.new(signX * safeDist, 0, signZ * safeDist)
                local safeWorld = bPart.CFrame:PointToWorldSpace(safeLocal)
                local targetSafe = clampInsideArena(Vector3.new(safeWorld.X, myPos.Y, safeWorld.Z), bDef.arenaPos, maxSafeRad, doorZ)
                lastEvadeGoal = targetSafe
                lastEvadeTick = now
                hum:MoveTo(targetSafe)
                return true, "✝️ หลบสกิลกางเขน (Cross of Ra / Tempest Cross) — ก้าวฉากเฉียง 45° เข้าช่องปลอดภัย!"
            end
        end

        -- 0G. Linear Charges & Beams (Shockwave Punch & Rampage - Boss 1 / Sonic Roar & Savage Rush - Boss 2 / Sun Ray & Tomb Rush - Boss 3 / Glacier Lance & Dragon Dive - Boss 4)
        if (now < (ext.chargeAttackUntil or 0) or now < (ext.lineAttackUntil or 0)) then
            local flatPos = Vector3.new(myPos.X, bPart.Position.Y, myPos.Z)
            local localPos = bPart.CFrame:PointToObjectSpace(flatPos)
            if localPos.Z < 4 and math.abs(localPos.Z) < 75.0 and math.abs(localPos.X) < 16.0 then
                local signX = localPos.X >= 0 and 1 or -1
                local safeLocal = Vector3.new(signX * 22.0, 0, localPos.Z)
                local safeWorld = bPart.CFrame:PointToWorldSpace(safeLocal)
                local targetSafe = clampInsideArena(Vector3.new(safeWorld.X, myPos.Y, safeWorld.Z), bDef.arenaPos, maxSafeRad, doorZ)
                lastEvadeGoal = targetSafe
                lastEvadeTick = now
                hum:MoveTo(targetSafe)
                return true, "⚡ หลบแนวพุ่ง/ลำแสง (Line/Charge) — ก้าวฉากหลบออกด้านข้าง!"
            end
        end

        -- 0H. Frontal Cones (Flame Breath & Magma Bite - Boss 5 / Frost Breath - Boss 4 / Scarab Swarm - Boss 3 / Rending Claws - Boss 2)
        local isAnyCone = now < (ext.flameSweepUntil or 0)
            or now < (ext.magmaBiteUntil or 0)
            or now < (ext.frostBreathUntil or 0)
            or now < (ext.coneAttackUntil or 0)

        if isAnyCone then
            local fwd = bPart.CFrame.LookVector
            if now < (ext.magmaBiteUntil or 0) and ext.magmaBiteFwd then
                fwd = ext.magmaBiteFwd
            elseif now < (ext.frostBreathUntil or 0) and ext.frostBreathFwd then
                fwd = ext.frostBreathFwd
            elseif now < (ext.coneAttackUntil or 0) and ext.coneAttackFwd then
                fwd = ext.coneAttackFwd
            end
            local fwd2D = Vector3.new(fwd.X, 0, fwd.Z)
            fwd2D = fwd2D.Magnitude > 0.1 and fwd2D.Unit or Vector3.new(0, 0, 1)
            local dotFront = fwd2D:Dot(awayUnit)
            local isFlame = now < (ext.flameSweepUntil or 0)
            local dangerDot = isFlame and -0.15 or 0.10
            local maxConeRange = (now < (ext.flameSweepUntil or 0) or now < (ext.magmaBiteUntil or 0)) and 122 or 52
            if dotFront > dangerDot and distToBoss2D < maxConeRange then
                local right2D = Vector3.new(-fwd2D.Z, 0, fwd2D.X)
                local sideSign = (right2D:Dot(awayUnit) >= 0) and 1 or -1
                local orbitR = math.max(bDef.bodyPad or 22, 26)
                local goalBehind
                if dotFront > 0.55 then
                    goalBehind = clampInsideArena(Vector3.new(bossPos.X, myPos.Y, bossPos.Z) + (right2D * sideSign * 0.85 - fwd2D * 0.55).Unit * orbitR, bDef.arenaPos, maxSafeRad, doorZ)
                else
                    goalBehind = clampInsideArena(Vector3.new(bossPos.X, myPos.Y, bossPos.Z) - fwd2D * orbitR + right2D * sideSign * 14, bDef.arenaPos, maxSafeRad, doorZ)
                end
                if isPositionInAnyDangerZone(goalBehind, nil, nil) then
                    goalBehind = clampInsideArena(Vector3.new(bossPos.X, myPos.Y, bossPos.Z) - fwd2D * orbitR - right2D * sideSign * 14, bDef.arenaPos, maxSafeRad, doorZ)
                end
                lastEvadeGoal = goalBehind
                lastEvadeTick = now
                hum:MoveTo(goalBehind)
                return true, "🐾 หลบสกิลหน้ากรวย (Cone) — เดินฉากอ้อมข้าง/หลังบอส!"
            end
        end
    end

    -- 1. กรณีเป็นสกิลที่มี Safe Zone จริงๆ (วงเขียว เช่น Sandstorm Judgment / Apex Roar / Jungle Roar / Blizzard Tempest)
    if hasRealSafeZone then
        local bestSafe, bestSafeDist = nil, 999999
        for _, sz in ipairs(activeBossSafeZones) do
            local sCenter = sz.Center or (sz.CFrame and sz.CFrame.Position)
            if sCenter then
                local d = (sCenter - myPos).Magnitude
                if d < bestSafeDist then
                    bestSafeDist = d
                    bestSafe = { pos = sCenter, radius = sz.Radius or 9.0 }
                end
            end
        end
        if not bestSafe and ext and ext.roomSafeCenter then
            local d = (ext.roomSafeCenter - myPos).Magnitude
            bestSafe = { pos = ext.roomSafeCenter, radius = ext.roomSafeRadius or 9.0 }
            bestSafeDist = d
        end
        if not bestSafe and isGreenCircleSkill then
            for _, sz in ipairs(safeZones) do
                local d = (sz.pos - myPos).Magnitude
                if d < bestSafeDist then
                    bestSafeDist = d
                    bestSafe = sz
                end
            end
        end

        if bestSafe then
            if bestSafeDist > (bestSafe.radius - 2.5) then
                hum:MoveTo(bestSafe.pos)
                lastEvadeGoal = bestSafe.pos
                lastEvadeTick = now
                return true, string.format("🛡️ วิ่งเข้าวงเขียวหลบสกิลห้อง (%s)!", tostring(activeBossFxSkill or "Room Wipe"))
            else
                lastEvadeGoal = myPos
                lastEvadeTick = now
                return false, nil -- อยู่ในวงเขียวปลอดภัยแล้ว สามารถยิงโจมตีต่อเนื่องได้ทันที
            end
        end
    end

    -- 2. ตรวจสอบพื้นที่อันตรายสีแดง (Red Danger Zones + Lava Pools 3 วิ) ทั้งหมด
    local inDanger = false
    local candidateEscapes = {}

    -- 2.0 บ่อลาวาค้างพื้น 3 วิ (Lava Rain Pools)
    if ext and type(ext.lavaPools) == "table" then
        for _, pool in ipairs(ext.lavaPools) do
            if now < pool.expire then
                local dist2D = math.sqrt((myPos.X - pool.center.X)^2 + (myPos.Z - pool.center.Z)^2)
                if dist2D < (pool.radius + 1.5) then
                    inDanger = true
                    local diff = myPos - pool.center
                    local radial = Vector3.new(diff.X, 0, diff.Z)
                    radial = radial.Magnitude > 0.1 and radial.Unit or Vector3.new(1, 0, 0)
                    local sideL = Vector3.new(-radial.Z, 0, radial.X)
                    table.insert(candidateEscapes, pool.center + radial * (pool.radius + 5.0))
                    table.insert(candidateEscapes, myPos + sideL * 16.0)
                    table.insert(candidateEscapes, myPos - sideL * 16.0)
                end
            end
        end
    end

    -- 2.1 Danger Zones จาก BossFx RemoteEvent (รองรับ Donut Inner, Circle, OBB, Cone)
    if now < activeBossFxExpire then
        for _, dz in ipairs(activeBossDangerZones) do
            if dz.Inner and dz.Inner > 0 and dz.Center and dz.Radius then
                local dist2D = math.sqrt((myPos.X - dz.Center.X)^2 + (myPos.Z - dz.Center.Z)^2)
                if dist2D >= (dz.Inner - 3.5) and dist2D <= (dz.Radius + 3.5) then
                    inDanger = true
                    local toC = Vector3.new(dz.Center.X - myPos.X, 0, dz.Center.Z - myPos.Z)
                    toC = toC.Magnitude > 0.1 and toC.Unit or Vector3.new(0, 0, -1)
                    table.insert(candidateEscapes, dz.Center - toC * math.max(36, dz.Inner - 14))
                end
            elseif dz.Kind == "circle" and dz.Center and dz.Radius then
                local dist2D = math.sqrt((myPos.X - dz.Center.X)^2 + (myPos.Z - dz.Center.Z)^2)
                local safeRad = dz.Radius + 3.5
                if dist2D < safeRad then
                    inDanger = true
                    local diff = myPos - dz.Center
                    local radial = Vector3.new(diff.X, 0, diff.Z)
                    if radial.Magnitude < 0.1 then
                        if bPart then
                            local away = (myPos - bPart.Position)
                            radial = Vector3.new(away.X, 0, away.Z)
                        end
                        if radial.Magnitude < 0.1 then radial = -root.CFrame.LookVector else radial = radial.Unit end
                    else
                        radial = radial.Unit
                    end

                    local sideL = Vector3.new(-radial.Z, 0, radial.X)
                    local sideR = Vector3.new(radial.Z, 0, -radial.X)
                    table.insert(candidateEscapes, dz.Center + radial * (safeRad + 5.0))
                    table.insert(candidateEscapes, myPos + sideL * 16.0)
                    table.insert(candidateEscapes, myPos + sideR * 16.0)
                    table.insert(candidateEscapes, myPos + (radial + sideL).Unit * (safeRad - dist2D + 6.0))
                    table.insert(candidateEscapes, myPos + (radial + sideR).Unit * (safeRad - dist2D + 6.0))
                end
            elseif dz.CFrame and dz.Width and dz.Length then
                local flatPos = Vector3.new(myPos.X, dz.CFrame.Position.Y, myPos.Z)
                local localPos = dz.CFrame:PointToObjectSpace(flatPos)
                local hx = (dz.Width * 0.5) + 3.0
                local hz = (dz.Length * 0.5) + 3.0
                if math.abs(localPos.X) <= hx and math.abs(localPos.Z) <= hz then
                    inDanger = true
                    local escLocalL = Vector3.new(hx + 6.0, 0, localPos.Z)
                    local escLocalR = Vector3.new(-(hx + 6.0), 0, localPos.Z)
                    table.insert(candidateEscapes, dz.CFrame:PointToWorldSpace(escLocalL))
                    table.insert(candidateEscapes, dz.CFrame:PointToWorldSpace(escLocalR))
                end
            elseif dz.Kind == "cone" and dz.Center and dz.Dir and dz.Radius and dz.Half then
                local diff = myPos - dz.Center
                local dist2D = math.sqrt(diff.X^2 + diff.Z^2)
                if dist2D <= (dz.Radius + 3.5) then
                    local dir2D = dist2D > 0.1 and Vector3.new(diff.X, 0, diff.Z).Unit or Vector3.new(0, 0, 1)
                    local fwd2D = Vector3.new(dz.Dir.X, 0, dz.Dir.Z).Unit
                    local angle = math.acos(math.clamp(fwd2D:Dot(dir2D), -1, 1))
                    if angle <= (dz.Half + 0.15) then
                        inDanger = true
                        local sideL = Vector3.new(-fwd2D.Z, 0, fwd2D.X)
                        local sideR = Vector3.new(fwd2D.Z, 0, -fwd2D.X)
                        table.insert(candidateEscapes, dz.Center - fwd2D * math.max(bDef.bodyPad or 24, 28))
                        table.insert(candidateEscapes, myPos + sideL * 20.0)
                        table.insert(candidateEscapes, myPos + sideR * 20.0)
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end

    -- 2.2 ตรวจสอบข้อความเตือนสกิล (Warning Banner)
    if warningBanner and bPart then
        local lowerTxt = warningBanner:lower()
        local distToBoss = (bPart.Position - myPos).Magnitude

        local toCenter = (bDef.arenaPos - myPos)
        local toCenter2D = Vector3.new(toCenter.X, 0, toCenter.Z)
        if toCenter2D.Magnitude > 0.1 then toCenter2D = toCenter2D.Unit else toCenter2D = Vector3.new(0, 0, -1) end
        local sideDirCenter = Vector3.new(-toCenter2D.Z, 0, toCenter2D.X)

        if lowerTxt:find("breath") or lowerTxt:find("cone") or lowerTxt:find("frost breath") or lowerTxt:find("flame breath") or lowerTxt:find("claw") or lowerTxt:find("rending") or lowerTxt:find("scarab") then
            local flatPos = Vector3.new(myPos.X, bPart.Position.Y, myPos.Z)
            local localPos = bPart.CFrame:PointToObjectSpace(flatPos)
            if localPos.Z < 5 and math.abs(localPos.Z) < 120.0 then
                inDanger = true
                local bossFwd = bPart.CFrame.LookVector
                local bossRight = bPart.CFrame.RightVector
                local signRight = (localPos.X >= 0) and 1 or -1
                local escapeDist = math.max(28.0, math.abs(localPos.Z) * 0.85 + 12.0)
                table.insert(candidateEscapes, bPart.Position - bossFwd * math.max(bDef.bodyPad or 24, 30))
                table.insert(candidateEscapes, myPos + (bossRight * signRight) * escapeDist)
                table.insert(candidateEscapes, myPos - (bossRight * signRight) * escapeDist)
            end
        elseif lowerTxt:find("cross") then
            local flatPos = Vector3.new(myPos.X, bPart.Position.Y, myPos.Z)
            local localPos = bPart.CFrame:PointToObjectSpace(flatPos)
            local inZArm = (math.abs(localPos.X) < 14.0 and math.abs(localPos.Z) < 55.0)
            local inXArm = (math.abs(localPos.Z) < 14.0 and math.abs(localPos.X) < 55.0)
            if inZArm or inXArm then
                inDanger = true
                local signX = localPos.X >= 0 and 1 or -1
                local signZ = localPos.Z >= 0 and 1 or -1
                local safeDist = 20.0
                local safeLocal = Vector3.new(signX * safeDist, 0, signZ * safeDist)
                local safeWorld = bPart.CFrame:PointToWorldSpace(safeLocal)
                table.insert(candidateEscapes, Vector3.new(safeWorld.X, myPos.Y, safeWorld.Z))
            end
        elseif lowerTxt:find("lance") or lowerTxt:find("ray") or lowerTxt:find("line") or lowerTxt:find("rush") or lowerTxt:find("charge") or lowerTxt:find("punch") or lowerTxt:find("rampage") or lowerTxt:find("sonic") or lowerTxt:find("dive") then
            local flatPos = Vector3.new(myPos.X, bPart.Position.Y, myPos.Z)
            local localPos = bPart.CFrame:PointToObjectSpace(flatPos)
            if localPos.Z < 0 and math.abs(localPos.Z) < 75.0 and math.abs(localPos.X) < 16.0 then
                inDanger = true
                local signX = localPos.X >= 0 and 1 or -1
                local safeLocal = Vector3.new(signX * 22.0, 0, localPos.Z)
                local safeWorld = bPart.CFrame:PointToWorldSpace(safeLocal)
                table.insert(candidateEscapes, Vector3.new(safeWorld.X, myPos.Y, safeWorld.Z))
            end
        elseif lowerTxt:find("meteor") or lowerTxt:find("rain") or lowerTxt:find("pillar") or lowerTxt:find("cursed") or lowerTxt:find("mark") or lowerTxt:find("boulder") or lowerTxt:find("lava") then
            if isPositionInAnyDangerZone(myPos, dangerZones, bossPos) then
                inDanger = true
                table.insert(candidateEscapes, myPos + sideDirCenter * 18.0)
                table.insert(candidateEscapes, myPos - sideDirCenter * 18.0)
                table.insert(candidateEscapes, myPos + toCenter2D * 15.0)
            end
        elseif lowerTxt:find("stomp") or lowerTxt:find("slam") or lowerTxt:find("crash") or lowerTxt:find("titan") or lowerTxt:find("sky crash") then
            if distToBoss < 36.0 and not (ext and (now < (ext.stompDonutUntil or 0) or now < (ext.stompStage1Until or 0))) then
                inDanger = true
                table.insert(candidateEscapes, myPos + sideDirCenter * 20.0)
                table.insert(candidateEscapes, myPos - sideDirCenter * 20.0)
                table.insert(candidateEscapes, myPos + toCenter2D * 18.0)
            end
        elseif lowerTxt:find("wrath") or lowerTxt:find("leap") or lowerTxt:find("pounce") or lowerTxt:find("saber") or lowerTxt:find("pharaoh") then
            if distToBoss < 28.0 then
                inDanger = true
                table.insert(candidateEscapes, myPos + sideDirCenter * 16.0)
                table.insert(candidateEscapes, myPos - sideDirCenter * 16.0)
                table.insert(candidateEscapes, myPos + toCenter2D * 14.0)
            end
        elseif lowerTxt:find("red ground") and isPositionInAnyDangerZone(myPos, dangerZones, bossPos) then
            inDanger = true
            table.insert(candidateEscapes, myPos + sideDirCenter * 18.0)
            table.insert(candidateEscapes, myPos - sideDirCenter * 18.0)
            table.insert(candidateEscapes, myPos + toCenter2D * 14.0)
        end
    end

    -- 2.3 ตรวจสอบ Part สีแดงใน LumiBossFx (Physical Indicators — ข้าม WedgePart เมื่อมี BossFx แม่นยำแล้ว)
    local hasExactFx = (now < activeBossFxExpire) and (#activeBossDangerZones > 0)
    for _, dz in ipairs(dangerZones) do
        if hasExactFx and dz.part and dz.part:IsA("WedgePart") then
            continue
        end
        local inside = false
        if dz.part and dz.part:IsA("BasePart") and not dz.isCylinder then
            inside = isPointInsidePartOBB(myPos, dz.part, 2.5)
            if inside then
                local esc = getEscapeVectorFromPart(myPos, dz.part, 5.0)
                if esc then table.insert(candidateEscapes, esc) end
            end
        else
            local dist2D = math.sqrt((dz.pos.X - myPos.X)^2 + (dz.pos.Z - myPos.Z)^2)
            if dist2D < dz.radius then
                inside = true
                local diff = myPos - dz.pos
                local dir2D = Vector3.new(diff.X, 0, diff.Z)
                if dir2D.Magnitude < 0.1 then
                    if bPart then
                        local away = (myPos - bPart.Position)
                        dir2D = Vector3.new(away.X, 0, away.Z)
                    end
                    if dir2D.Magnitude < 0.1 then dir2D = -root.CFrame.LookVector else dir2D = dir2D.Unit end
                else
                    dir2D = dir2D.Unit
                end
                local sideDir = Vector3.new(-dir2D.Z, 0, dir2D.X)
                table.insert(candidateEscapes, myPos + dir2D * (dz.radius - dist2D + 6.0))
                table.insert(candidateEscapes, myPos + sideDir * 15.0)
                table.insert(candidateEscapes, myPos - sideDir * 15.0)
            end
        end
        if inside then inDanger = true end
    end

    -- 3. ถ้าอยู่ในเขตอันตรายสีแดง (inDanger == true) -> วิ่งหลบออกนอกเขตทันที
    if inDanger then
        if lastEvadeGoal and (now - lastEvadeTick < 0.35) then
            local dGoal = (lastEvadeGoal - myPos).Magnitude
            if dGoal > 1.8 and not isPositionInAnyDangerZone(lastEvadeGoal, dangerZones, bossPos) then
                hum:MoveTo(lastEvadeGoal)
                return true, hasActiveLavaPools and "🌋 เดินหลบบ่อลาวา / วงแดง AOE!" or "⚠ กำลังก้าวเท้าออกจากแถบแดง (AOE)..."
            end
        end

        local bestEscape = nil
        local bestEscapeDist = 999999

        for _, esc in ipairs(candidateEscapes) do
            local escPos = Vector3.new(esc.X, myPos.Y, esc.Z)
            local clamped = clampInsideArena(escPos, bDef.arenaPos, maxSafeRad, doorZ)
            if not isPositionInAnyDangerZone(clamped, dangerZones, bossPos) then
                local dStep = (clamped - myPos).Magnitude
                if dStep < bestEscapeDist then
                    bestEscapeDist = dStep
                    bestEscape = clamped
                end
            end
        end

        -- 3.3 หากไม่มี Candidate ที่ปลอดภัย ให้สแกน 16 ทิศทางรอบตัว (รองรับ 15 วงของ Lava Rain!)
        if not bestEscape then
            for stepDist = 10, 35, 5 do
                for angleDeg = 0, 337.5, 22.5 do
                    local rad = math.rad(angleDeg)
                    local testDir = Vector3.new(math.cos(rad), 0, math.sin(rad))
                    local testPos = clampInsideArena(myPos + testDir * stepDist, bDef.arenaPos, maxSafeRad, doorZ)
                    if not isPositionInAnyDangerZone(testPos, dangerZones, bossPos) then
                        local minBodyClear = math.min(bDef.bodyPad or 18, 22)
                        if not bPart or (testPos - bPart.Position).Magnitude >= minBodyClear then
                            bestEscape = testPos
                            break
                        end
                    end
                end
                if bestEscape then break end
            end
        end

        if bestEscape then
            bestEscape = clampInsideBossLeash(bestEscape, bossPos, 64.0)
            lastEvadeGoal = bestEscape
            lastEvadeTick = now
            hum:MoveTo(bestEscape)
            return true, hasActiveLavaPools and "🌋 เดินหลบบ่อลาวา / วงแดง AOE!" or "⚠ เดินเท้าฉากหลบแถบสกิลสีแดง (AOE)!"
        end

        -- 3.4 Fallback ฉุกเฉิน: ฉากหลบ 90 องศาไปทางด้านข้างภายในอารีน่า (จำกัดไม่เกิน 64 studs จากบอส)
        if bPart then
            local away = (myPos - bPart.Position)
            local away2D = Vector3.new(away.X, 0, away.Z)
            if away2D.Magnitude < 0.1 then away2D = -bPart.CFrame.LookVector else away2D = away2D.Unit end
            local side1 = Vector3.new(-away2D.Z, 0, away2D.X)
            local side2 = Vector3.new(away2D.Z, 0, -away2D.X)
            local p1 = clampInsideArena(myPos + side1 * 14.0, bDef.arenaPos, maxSafeRad, doorZ, bossPos)
            local p2 = clampInsideArena(myPos + side2 * 14.0, bDef.arenaPos, maxSafeRad, doorZ, bossPos)
            local fallbackPos = ((p1 - bDef.arenaPos).Magnitude <= (p2 - bDef.arenaPos).Magnitude) and p1 or p2
            fallbackPos = clampInsideBossLeash(fallbackPos, bossPos, 64.0)
            lastEvadeGoal = fallbackPos
            lastEvadeTick = now
            hum:MoveTo(fallbackPos)
            return true, "⚠ ฉากหลบฉุกเฉินภายในอารีน่า!"
        end
    end

    -- 4. ตัวละครอยู่นอกเขตอันตรายแล้ว (inDanger == false) -> พร้อมโจมตีบอสทันที
    lastEvadeGoal = nil
    return false, nil
end

local function getAllDropsInArena(arenaPos, maxRadius)
    arenaPos = arenaPos or Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
    maxRadius = maxRadius or 85
    local root = getCharRoot()
    local myPos = root and root.Position or arenaPos
    local cs = cloneref and cloneref(game:GetService("CollectionService")) or game:GetService("CollectionService")

    local items = {}
    local seen = {}

    local function addDropCandidate(inst)
        if not inst or not inst.Parent or seen[inst] then return end
        local nameLower = inst.Name:lower()
        -- กรองทิ้งเด็ดขาด: ห้ามเป็นกล่องสมบัติ, relic box, lootbox, chest, ประตู, เสา, timer, spawner
        if nameLower:find("lootbox") or nameLower:find("relic") or nameLower:find("chest") 
           or nameLower:find("box") or nameLower:find("door") or nameLower:find("timer") 
           or nameLower:find("spawner") or nameLower:find("pillar") then
            return
        end

        local part = inst:IsA("BasePart") and inst or (inst:IsA("Model") and (inst.PrimaryPart or inst:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart")))
        if not part or seen[part] then return end

        local distFromArena = (part.Position - arenaPos).Magnitude
        -- ต้องอยู่ในระยะอารีน่าเท่านั้น ไม่หลุดออกไปนอกพีระมิด
        if distFromArena <= maxRadius then
            seen[inst] = true
            seen[part] = true
            table.insert(items, part)
        end
    end

    -- 1. Scan Workspace.Drops (แหล่งเก็บไอเทมดรอปหลักของเกม Hunting Together RPG)
    local dropsFolder = workspace:FindFirstChild("Drops")
    if dropsFolder then
        for _, d in ipairs(dropsFolder:GetChildren()) do
            addDropCandidate(d)
        end
    end

    -- 2. Scan CollectionService Tag "Drop"
    pcall(function()
        for _, d in ipairs(cs:GetTagged("Drop")) do
            addDropCandidate(d)
        end
    end)

    -- 3. Scan Workspace Folders (LootDrops, Debris, Drop, Items)
    for _, containerName in ipairs({ "LootDrops", "Drop", "Items" }) do
        local folder = workspace:FindFirstChild(containerName)
        if folder then
            for _, d in ipairs(folder:GetChildren()) do
                addDropCandidate(d)
            end
        end
    end

    -- 4. Scan Workspace Children ที่มีชื่อบ่งบอกเป็นไอเทมดรอป หรือมี attribute ของไอเทม (ไม่เอากล่อง)
    pcall(function()
        for _, obj in ipairs(workspace:GetChildren()) do
            if obj:IsA("BasePart") or obj:IsA("Model") then
                local hasDropAttr = obj:GetAttribute("DropKind") ~= nil or obj:GetAttribute("DropRarity") ~= nil or obj:GetAttribute("ItemId") ~= nil
                local nameLower = obj.Name:lower()
                local isDropName = (nameLower:sub(1, 5) == "drop_" or nameLower:find("^item_") or nameLower:find("drop"))
                if (hasDropAttr or isDropName) and not (nameLower:find("box") or nameLower:find("chest") or nameLower:find("relic")) then
                    addDropCandidate(obj)
                end
            end
        end
    end)

    -- เรียงลำดับจากชิ้นที่ใกล้ตัวละครมากที่สุดไปหาน้อ·ี่สุด
    table.sort(items, function(a, b)
        return (a.Position - myPos).Magnitude < (b.Position - myPos).Magnitude
    end)

    return items
end

local function getAllLootBoxesInArena(arenaPos, maxRadius)
    arenaPos = arenaPos or Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
    maxRadius = maxRadius or 80
    local root = getCharRoot()
    local myPos = root and root.Position or arenaPos
    local cs = cloneref and cloneref(game:GetService("CollectionService")) or game:GetService("CollectionService")
    local boxes = {}
    local seen = {}

    local function addBox(b)
        if not b or not b.Parent or seen[b] then return end
        local nameLower = b.Name:lower()
        if nameLower:find("relic") then return end -- ห้ามเป็น relic box หรือ world chest เด็ดขาด
        local p = b:IsA("BasePart") and b.Position or (b:IsA("Model") and (b.PrimaryPart and b.PrimaryPart.Position or b:GetPivot().Position))
        if not p then return end
        local distFromArena = (p - arenaPos).Magnitude
        -- เฉพาะกล่องที่อยู่ในห้องบอสจริงเท่านั้น (ห้ามออกนอกห้องบอส)
        if distFromArena <= maxRadius then
            seen[b] = true
            table.insert(boxes, b)
        end
    end

    for _, b in ipairs(cs:GetTagged("LootBox")) do addBox(b) end
    for _, ch in ipairs(workspace:GetChildren()) do
        local n = ch.Name:lower()
        if (n:find("bosschest") or n:find("boss_chest")) and not n:find("relic") then
            addBox(ch)
        end
    end

    table.sort(boxes, function(a, b)
        local pa = a:IsA("BasePart") and a.Position or (a:IsA("Model") and (a.PrimaryPart and a.PrimaryPart.Position or a:GetPivot().Position))
        local pb = b:IsA("BasePart") and b.Position or (b:IsA("Model") and (b.PrimaryPart and b.PrimaryPart.Position or b:GetPivot().Position))
        if pa and pb then
            return (pa - myPos).Magnitude < (pb - myPos).Magnitude
        end
        return false
    end)

    return boxes
end

local function collectAllBossLootAndBoxes(bDef, maxWaitSec)
    maxWaitSec = maxWaitSec or 6
    local Remotes = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("Remotes")
    local startCollect = tick()
    local hadItems = false

    if valBossStatus then pcall(function() valBossStatus:UpdateName("🎁 ตรวจสอบและเก็บไอเทมดรอปบอส...") end) end

    -- หยุดระบบต่อสู้ชั่วคราวขณะเดินเก็บไอเทม
    setBotRunning(false)
    task.wait(0.25)

    local arenaRadius = (bDef and bDef.arenaRadius) or 75
    local scanRadius = arenaRadius + 15

    while bossFarmActive and (tick() - startCollect < maxWaitSec) do
        local root, hum = getCharRoot()
        if not root or not hum then break end

        -- ตรวจสอบหากกระเป๋าเต็ม 240/240 ให้ยกเลิกการรอเก็บของเพื่อวาร์ปไปขายทันที
        local maxS = lp:GetAttribute("BagSlotsMax") or 240
        local usedS = lp:GetAttribute("BagSlotsUsed") or 0
        if usedS >= maxS then
            if valBossStatus then pcall(function() valBossStatus:UpdateName("🎒 กระเป๋าเต็ม 240/240! ข้ามการเก็บเพื่อไปขายของ...") end) end
            break
        end

        local rawDrops = getAllDropsInArena(bDef.arenaPos, scanRadius)
        local rawBoxes = getAllLootBoxesInArena(bDef.arenaPos, scanRadius)

        -- กรองเฉพาะของดรอปและกล่องที่อยู่ในระยะเอื้อมถึงจริง (ไม่เกิน 50 studs จากตัวละคร และอยู่ในห้องบอส)
        local drops = {}
        for _, d in ipairs(rawDrops) do
            local dPos = d.Position
            local dFromPlayer = (dPos - root.Position).Magnitude
            local dFromArena = (dPos - bDef.arenaPos).Magnitude
            if dFromPlayer <= 50 and dFromArena <= arenaRadius + 5 then
                table.insert(drops, d)
            end
        end

        local boxes = {}
        for _, b in ipairs(rawBoxes) do
            local bPos = b:IsA("BasePart") and b.Position or (b:IsA("Model") and (b.PrimaryPart and b.PrimaryPart.Position or b:GetPivot().Position))
            if bPos then
                local bFromPlayer = (bPos - root.Position).Magnitude
                local bFromArena = (bPos - bDef.arenaPos).Magnitude
                if bFromPlayer <= 50 and bFromArena <= arenaRadius + 5 then
                    table.insert(boxes, b)
                end
            end
        end

        if #drops == 0 and #boxes == 0 then
            -- ถ้าไม่มีของดรอปในห้อง หรือเก็บครบแล้ว ออกทันที ย้ายเซิร์ฟเวอร์โดยไม่ต้องรอ!
            task.wait(0.2)
            break
        else
            hadItems = true
        end

        -- A. เดินเก็บไอเทมดรอป (เน้นของดรอปจริงในอารีน่า)
        if #drops > 0 then
            local targetDrop = drops[1]
            if targetDrop and targetDrop.Parent then
                local dPos = targetDrop.Position
                local diff = (dPos - root.Position)
                local dist2D = math.sqrt(diff.X^2 + diff.Z^2)
                local itemName = targetDrop:GetAttribute("Label") or targetDrop.Name

                if valBossStatus then
                    pcall(function()
                        valBossStatus:UpdateName(string.format("🎁 เดินเก็บไอเทม: %s (%.1f studs, เหลืออีก %d ชิ้น)...", tostring(itemName), dist2D, #drops))
                    end)
                end

                -- เดินตรงเข้าไปเหยียบไอเทม
                hum:MoveTo(Vector3.new(dPos.X, root.Position.Y, dPos.Z))

                -- เรียก PickupDrop
                if Remotes and Remotes:FindFirstChild("PickupDrop") then
                    pcall(function() Remotes.PickupDrop:FireServer(true) end)
                end
                if dist2D <= 15.0 then
                    pcall(function()
                        if Remotes and Remotes:FindFirstChild("PickupDrop") then
                            Remotes.PickupDrop:FireServer(true)
                        end
                        if firetouchinterest then
                            firetouchinterest(root, targetDrop, 0)
                            firetouchinterest(root, targetDrop, 1)
                        end
                    end)
                end
            end
        elseif #boxes > 0 then
            -- B. เปิดกล่องสมบัติในห้องบอส (ถ้ามี)
            local targetBox = boxes[1]
            if targetBox and targetBox.Parent then
                local bPos = targetBox:IsA("BasePart") and targetBox.Position or (targetBox:IsA("Model") and (targetBox.PrimaryPart and targetBox.PrimaryPart.Position or targetBox:GetPivot().Position))
                if bPos then
                    local bDist = (bPos - root.Position).Magnitude
                    if valBossStatus then
                        pcall(function()
                            valBossStatus:UpdateName(string.format("📦 เปิดกล่องสมบัติบอส: %s (%.1f studs)...", targetBox.Name, bDist))
                        end)
                    end
                    if bDist > 6.0 then
                        hum:MoveTo(Vector3.new(bPos.X, root.Position.Y, bPos.Z))
                    else
                        openLootBoxDirect(targetBox)
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        task.wait(0.1)
    end

    if valBossStatus then pcall(function() valBossStatus:UpdateName("✅ เก็บของดรอปครบทุกชิ้นแล้ว! สลับห้องทันที...") end) end
    task.wait(0.2)
end

-- findBossModelInArena is defined above at Boss Detection Engine (line ~6252)

local isServerHopping = false
local lastHopTick = 0
local lastAttemptedServerId = nil
local failedServerIds = {} -- เก็บ blacklist เซิร์ฟเวอร์ที่ตาย/เต็ม ไม่ให้เข้าซ้ำ
local hopInFlightUntil = 0 -- blocks stacked teleports while a hop is searching or the game is loading the new server
local HOP_SEARCH_SEC = 25
local HOP_INFLIGHT_SEC = 60
local performServerHop

-- ดักจับเหตุการณ์ Teleport ล้มเหลวเพื่อป้องกันตัวเกม Crash / Disconnect และค้นหาห้องใหม่ทดแทนอัตโนมัติ
pcall(function()
    _G.GolfHub_Conn(TeleportService.TeleportInitFailed, function(player, teleportResult, errorMessage)
        isServerHopping = false
        hopInFlightUntil = 0
        _G.GolfHub_HopInFlightUntil = 0
        pcall(function() GuiService:ClearError() end)
        if lastAttemptedServerId then
            failedServerIds[lastAttemptedServerId] = tick()
            if _G.GolfHub_Scout and _G.GolfHub_Scout.markFailed then pcall(_G.GolfHub_Scout.markFailed, lastAttemptedServerId) end
        end
        if valHopStatus then
            pcall(function()
                valHopStatus:UpdateName(string.format("⚠ ย้ายเซิร์ฟขัดข้อง (%s) — เคลียร์หน้าต่าง & ค้นหาใหม่ใน 2 วิ...", tostring(errorMessage or teleportResult)))
            end)
        end
        task.wait(1.5)
        pcall(function() GuiService:ClearError() end)
        if bossFarmActive and performServerHop then
            task.spawn(performServerHop)
        end
    end)
end)

performServerHop = function(statusCb)
    -- Guard: หากบอสเป้าหมายกำลังเกิดอยู่ (🟢 เกิดอยู่) หรือพบตัวในห้องบอส ให้ล็อคห้องและยกเลิกการ Hop ทันทีแบบไม่มีเงื่อนไข!
    if bossFarmActive then
        local currentTargetKey = selectedBossKey
        local bDef = BOSS_TARGET_DEFS[currentTargetKey]
        if bDef then
            local sState = getBossServerState(bDef)
            local isBossAlive = (sState and sState.alive == true) or (findBossModelInArena(bDef) ~= nil)
            if isBossAlive then
                if statusCb then statusCb(string.format("🔒 %s เกิดอยู่ (พร้อมสู้)! ล็อคเซิร์ฟเวอร์นี้ (ยกเลิกการย้าย)", bDef.name)) end
                if valHopStatus then pcall(function() valHopStatus:UpdateName(string.format("🔒 พบบอส %s เกิดอยู่! ล็อคเซิร์ฟเวอร์นี้ (หยุดย้ายเซิร์ฟ)", bDef.name)) end) end
                isServerHopping = false
                return
            end
        end
    end

    if tick() < hopInFlightUntil then return end
    if isServerHopping and (tick() - lastHopTick < 8) then return end
    hopInFlightUntil = tick() + HOP_SEARCH_SEC
    _G.GolfHub_HopInFlightUntil = hopInFlightUntil
    _G.GolfHub_LastTeleportStartedAt = os.clock()
    isServerHopping = true
    lastHopTick = tick()

    -- เคลียร์หน้าต่าง Error Popup ที่อาจค้างอยู่ก่อนเริ่ม Hop ใหม่
    pcall(function() GuiService:ClearError() end)

    -- หยุดการเคลื่อนที่และระบบต่อสู้ก่อนเริ่มย้ายเซิร์ฟ เพื่อความเสถียร
    local root, hum = getCharRoot()
    if hum and root then
        pcall(function() hum:MoveTo(root.Position) end)
    end
    setBotRunning(false)
    task.wait(0.2)

    -- Leave the party before hopping. Seen live: every boss hop took the whole party along, so the other accounts
    -- were pulled out of their farm servers every 30-40 s while the boss was dead in server after server.
    pcall(function()
        local pj = lp:GetAttribute("PartyJson")
        if type(pj) == "string" and #pj > 4 then
            local R = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("Remotes")
            local pa = R and R:FindFirstChild("PartyAction")
            if pa then
                pa:FireServer("leave")
                local t0 = tick()
                while tick() - t0 < 4 do
                    local j = lp:GetAttribute("PartyJson")
                    if not (type(j) == "string" and #j > 4) then break end
                    task.wait(0.2)
                end
            end
        end
    end)

    -- โหมด Kill Game & Watchdog Relaunch (ถ้าผู้ใช้เลือก)
    if serverHopMode == "kill" then
        if statusCb then statusCb("💀 บอสตายแล้ว! กำลังปิดเกมเพื่อให้ Watchdog เปิดเข้าห้องใหม่ทันที (กันค้าง 100%)...") end
        if valHopStatus then pcall(function() valHopStatus:UpdateName("💀 ปิดเกมเพื่อสลับห้องใหม่ (Auto-Watchdog Relaunch)...") end) end
        task.wait(1.0)
        pcall(function() game:Shutdown() end)
        task.wait(1.0)
        pcall(function()
            local plrs = game:GetService("Players")
            if plrs.LocalPlayer then
                plrs.LocalPlayer:Kick("GOLF HUB: Server hopping via clean relaunch...")
            end
        end)
        return
    end

    if statusCb then statusCb("🌐 กำลังค้นหาเซิร์ฟเวอร์สด (Live Server Hopping)...") end

    local currentJobId = game.JobId
    local placeId = game.PlaceId
    local targetServerId = nil

    -- Servers to stay away from, kept on disk per account because everything in memory is lost on each join.
    -- Seen live: a boss hunt went back to servers it had just left (same job id twice in a few hops), where the boss
    -- it had just killed was still on its respawn timer. Each entry is "<boss>|<jobId>" = the time (os.time) until
    -- which that server is useless for that boss: leaving a server stores its boss's real respawn time (BossNext_<id>),
    -- so a server comes back into play as soon as its boss is up again instead of after a fixed wait.
    local recentFile = (GOLFGUI.Folder or "GolfHub_HuntingTogether") .. "/recent_servers.json"
    local bossTag = tostring(selectedBossKey or "any")
    local nowT = os.time()
    local recent = {}
    pcall(function()
        if isfile(recentFile) then
            local data = HttpService:JSONDecode(readfile(recentFile))
            if type(data) == "table" then
                for k, untilT in pairs(data) do
                    if type(k) == "string" and tonumber(untilT) and tonumber(untilT) > nowT then recent[k] = tonumber(untilT) end
                end
            end
        end
    end)
    do
        local untilT = nowT + 5 * 60 -- respawn unknown: about one boss cycle
        local bDef = BOSS_TARGET_DEFS and BOSS_TARGET_DEFS[selectedBossKey]
        local nextAt = bDef and tonumber(workspace:GetAttribute("BossNext_" .. bDef.monsterId))
        if nextAt then
            local left = nextAt - workspace:GetServerTimeNow()
            if left > 0 then untilT = nowT + math.ceil(left) end
        end
        recent[bossTag .. "|" .. currentJobId] = math.min(untilT, nowT + 20 * 60)
    end
    local function isRecent(id)
        id = tostring(id)
        return id == currentJobId or recent[bossTag .. "|" .. id] ~= nil
    end

    -- Shared server list: the scout keeps one cached, use-once list on disk; use it first so the hub does not poll the servers API on every hop
    if _G.GolfHub_Scout and _G.GolfHub_Scout.pickServer then
        for _ = 1, 5 do -- use-once list: each call hands out the next server, so skipping a recent one costs nothing
            local okPick, picked = pcall(_G.GolfHub_Scout.pickServer)
            if not (okPick and type(picked) == "string") then break end
            if not isRecent(picked) then
                targetServerId = picked
                break
            end
        end
    end

    -- 1. ค้นหาเซิร์ฟเวอร์สดจาก Live HTTP API เป็นลำดับแรก (Real-Time 100% ไม่เสี่ยงเจอห้องตาย)
    local httpReq = (syn and syn.request) or (http and http.request) or http_request or request
    -- Page through the list (100 servers a page) until one is usable, instead of giving up after the first page:
    -- with recent servers skipped, the first page can run out while later pages still have fresh servers.
    local cursor = nil
    local pages, tries = 0, 0
    while not targetServerId and pages < 5 and tries < 8 do
        tries = tries + 1
        local url = string.format("https://games.roblox.com/v1/games/%d/servers/Public?sortOrder=Asc&limit=100%s", placeId,
            cursor and ("&cursor=" .. HttpService:UrlEncode(cursor)) or "")
        local success, res = pcall(function()
            if httpReq then
                local response = httpReq({ Url = url, Method = "GET" })
                return response and (response.Body or response.body)
            else
                return game:HttpGet(url)
            end
        end)

        local gotPage = false
        if success and res and type(res) == "string" and #res > 0 then
            local okDecode, data = pcall(function() return HttpService:JSONDecode(res) end)
            if okDecode and data and data.data then
                gotPage = true
                pages = pages + 1
                local validServers = {}
                for _, s in ipairs(data.data) do
                    local maxP = s.maxPlayers or 16
                    local curP = s.playing or 0
                    local isBlacklisted = failedServerIds[s.id] and (tick() - failedServerIds[s.id] < 180)
                    if s.id and not isRecent(s.id) and curP > 0 and (maxP - curP) >= 2 and not isBlacklisted then
                        table.insert(validServers, { id = s.id, p = curP })
                    end
                end
                if #validServers > 0 then
                    -- fewest players first, random among the quietest few (see scoutFetchServers)
                    table.sort(validServers, function(a, b) return a.p < b.p end)
                    targetServerId = validServers[math.random(1, math.min(#validServers, 8))].id
                    break
                end
                if type(data.nextPageCursor) == "string" and data.nextPageCursor ~= "" then
                    cursor = data.nextPageCursor
                else
                    break -- last page: nothing usable anywhere
                end
            end
        end
        task.wait(gotPage and 0.2 or 0.6) -- a failed request is retried on the same page
    end

    -- 2. สำรอง: ถ้า Live API ไม่ตอบสนอง ลองตรวจ servers.json (เฉพาะกรณีไม่ว่าง)
    if not targetServerId and isfile and isfile("servers.json") then
        pcall(function()
            local content = readfile("servers.json")
            local list = HttpService:JSONDecode(content)
            if list and type(list) == "table" and #list > 0 then
                local validServers = {}
                for _, sId in ipairs(list) do
                    local sidStr = tostring(sId)
                    local isBlacklisted = failedServerIds[sidStr] and (tick() - failedServerIds[sidStr] < 180)
                    if not isRecent(sidStr) and not isBlacklisted then
                        table.insert(validServers, sidStr)
                    end
                end
                if #validServers > 0 then
                    targetServerId = validServers[math.random(1, #validServers)]
                end
            end
        end)
    end

    -- No usable server anywhere: stay here and search again shortly. A random public teleport would let Roblox pick,
    -- and it can pick a server whose boss we just killed.
    if not targetServerId then
        pcall(function() writefile(recentFile, HttpService:JSONEncode(recent)) end)
        if statusCb then statusCb("⏳ ทุกเซิร์ฟที่เจอบอสยังไม่เกิด — รอแล้วค้นหาใหม่...") end
        isServerHopping = false
        hopInFlightUntil = tick() + 20
        return
    end

    -- AutoExec ของ Real โหลดสคริปต์ให้อัตโนมัติอยู่แล้ว จึงไม่ใช้ queue_on_teleport ซ้ำ เพื่อป้องกันการรันสคริปต์ซ้อน 2 ชุดกินแรมจนเครื่องค้าง
    lastAttemptedServerId = targetServerId

    -- Crash guard: the client crashed every ~5-10 back-to-back teleports (~20 s apart, ~3.7 GB at teleport time).
    -- Space teleports at least HOP_MIN_GAP seconds apart (stamp kept in a file, since globals reset on each join)
    -- and free what the hub holds before leaving, so each teleport starts from a smaller footprint.
    local HOP_MIN_GAP = 35
    -- per account, so several open windows (different accounts) do not delay each other's hops
    local stampFile = (GOLFGUI.Folder or "GolfHub_HuntingTogether") .. "/last_hop.txt"
    local lastHop = 0
    pcall(function() if isfile(stampFile) then lastHop = tonumber(readfile(stampFile)) or 0 end end)
    local waitLeft = HOP_MIN_GAP - (os.time() - lastHop)
    if waitLeft > 0 and waitLeft <= HOP_MIN_GAP then
        if statusCb then statusCb(string.format("⏳ พักก่อนย้ายเซิร์ฟ %d วินาที (กันเกมแครช)...", waitLeft)) end
        task.wait(waitLeft)
    end
    pcall(writefile, stampFile, tostring(os.time()))
    -- remember the server we leave and the one we go to, before anything can stop us
    -- the server we go to: unknown state, keep it off the list a little while (we write its real respawn when we leave it)
    recent[bossTag .. "|" .. targetServerId] = os.time() + 3 * 60
    pcall(function() writefile(recentFile, HttpService:JSONEncode(recent)) end)
    pcall(function() if removeBossGuideLine then removeBossGuideLine() end end)
    pcall(function() collectgarbage("collect") end)

    -- 3. สลับไปยัง Server เป้าหมาย
    hopInFlightUntil = tick() + HOP_INFLIGHT_SEC
    _G.GolfHub_HopInFlightUntil = hopInFlightUntil
    _G.GolfHub_LastTeleportStartedAt = os.clock()
    if targetServerId then
        if statusCb then statusCb(string.format("🚀 กำลังเชื่อมต่อไปยังเซิร์ฟเวอร์ใหม่: %s...", string.sub(targetServerId, 1, 8))) end
    else
        if statusCb then statusCb("⚠ สุ่มเซิร์ฟเวอร์สาธารณะใหม่...") end
    end

    -- Unload the hub BEFORE teleporting. Seen live (5 uploaded crash reports in one evening, every one at the moment
    -- the client left for the new server): "RBXCRASH: HardAssert (Invalid Facet Access)" while the old server was being
    -- torn down and the hub's loops and signal handlers were still touching its instances. With the hub stopped first,
    -- nothing of ours runs during the teardown; autoexec loads a fresh copy in the new server as usual.
    -- Deferred, so the boss thread that called us has yielded before Unload cancels it (a thread cannot cancel itself).
    local fromJob = game.JobId
    task.defer(function()
        pcall(function()
            if getgenv and getgenv().GolfHub_Unload then getgenv().GolfHub_Unload() end
        end)
        task.wait(0.5)
        pcall(function() collectgarbage("collect") end)
        pcall(function()
            _G.GolfHub_LastTeleportStartedAt = os.clock()
            if targetServerId then
                TeleportService:TeleportToPlaceInstance(placeId, targetServerId)
            else
                TeleportService:Teleport(placeId)
            end
        end)
        -- Still in the same server after 25 s: the teleport failed (full server, rejected). Load the hub again from the
        -- deployed file so the hunt carries on here and hops again, instead of leaving the character idle with no hub.
        local waitStart = os.clock()
        while os.clock() - waitStart < 25 do task.wait(1) end
        if game.JobId == fromJob then
            pcall(function()
                local src = readfile("GolfHub_HuntingTogether/golf-hub-main.lua")
                local fn = src and loadstring(src)
                if fn then task.spawn(fn) end
            end)
        end
    end)

    task.delay(10, function()
        isServerHopping = false
    end)
end

-- Pillar nearest the selected boss arena: the boss's own pillar if unlocked, otherwise the unlocked pillar closest to the arena
local function resolveBossPillar(bDef)
    if not bDef then return nil end
    if bDef.pillar and WARP_PILLAR_DATA[bDef.pillar] and _G.GolfHub_Pillars.canWarp(bDef.pillar) then
        return bDef.pillar
    end
    local bestKey = getBestPillarForZone(bDef.arenaPos)
    if bestKey == "Town" then return nil end -- nothing usable near this boss: going to town would not bring it closer
    return bestKey
end

task.wait() -- yield point: spread boss farm setup over frames
local function runBossFarmLoop()
    local checkedCenterKey = nil
    local emptyCenterChecks = 0
    local myGen = _G.GolfHub_Gen -- a hub reload bumps the generation: an older copy of this loop must stop instead of running beside the new one

    while bossFarmActive and myGen == _G.GolfHub_Gen do
        _G.GolfHub_BossPhase = 'loop-top ' .. string.format('%.0f', tick())
        -- 0A. วนรอหากขั้นตอนขายของกำลังทำงานอยู่
        while isSellingNow and bossFarmActive do
            task.wait(1)
        end
        if not bossFarmActive then break end

        local currentTargetKey = selectedBossKey
        local bDef = BOSS_TARGET_DEFS[currentTargetKey]
        if not bDef then
            task.wait(1)
            continue
        end

        -- 0A. ตรวจสอบสถานะบอสแบบ Real-Time จากระบบเกมทันที (Instant Pre-Flight Boss Check)
        -- ตรวจสอบจาก Workspace Attributes ก่อนเดินลงแมพ/ก่อนขยับตัว: BossAlive_<monsterId> และ BossNext_<monsterId>
        local ws = game:GetService("Workspace")
        local attrAliveKey = "BossAlive_" .. (bDef.monsterId or "")
        local attrNextKey = "BossNext_" .. (bDef.monsterId or "")

        -- ล้างเควสและการเดินของตัวเกมทันที ป้องกันตัวละครเดินออกนอกเส้นทางหรือเดินถอยหลัง
        pcall(function()
            lp:SetAttribute("QuestNavId", "")
            local Remotes = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("Remotes")
            if Remotes and Remotes:FindFirstChild("QuestAction") then Remotes.QuestAction:FireServer("nav", "") end
            if Remotes and Remotes:FindFirstChild("BotAutoState") then Remotes.BotAutoState:FireServer(false) end
        end)

        -- วาดและอัปเดตระบบลูกศรลากยาว 3D ทันทีตั้งแต่เฟรมแรก และบังคับหันหน้าเดินตามลูกศร
        local directBoss = bDef and findBossModelInArena(bDef) or nil
        local rPreInit = getCharRoot()
        if rPreInit and bDef then
