    -- 2. กฎเหล็ก: ถ้าตัวละครอยู่นอกเมือง หรือหลุดไปแถวสะพานโซน 1 (Z < -5 หรือ |X| > 95) ให้กลับเข้าเมืองก่อน
    local curZ = getCurrentActualZone()
    local posNow = root.Position
    if curZ ~= "Town" or posNow.Z < -5 or math.abs(posNow.X) > 95 then
        if setStatCb then setStatCb("🌀 อยู่นอกเมือง/โซนฟาร์ม -> ปิด Auto แล้วกลับเข้าเมือง...") end
        if forceStopNativeAutoBot then forceStopNativeAutoBot() end
        local arrivedInTown = castHomeWarp()
        if not arrivedInTown and dungeonFarmActive then
            root = getCharRoot()
            -- กรณี HomeWarp ติดคูลดาวน์ แต่ตัวละครอยู่แค่โซน 1 ใกล้สะพานเมือง: เดินย้อนกลับข้ามสะพานเข้าเมืองตรงๆ ได้เลย
            if root and root.Position.Z > -220 and math.abs(root.Position.X) < 140 then
                if setStatCb then setStatCb("🏃 HomeWarp ติดคูลดาวน์ -> เดินย้อนกลับเข้าเมืองทางสะพาน...") end
                walkTo(Vector3.new(18.0, 4.3, 4.0), 18, 5, dngWalkAbort)
                root = getCharRoot()
                if root and root.Position.Z >= -5 and math.abs(root.Position.X) <= 95 then
                    arrivedInTown = true
                end
            end
        end
        if not arrivedInTown and dungeonFarmActive then
            for attempt = 1, 3 do
                if not dungeonFarmActive then return false end
                if forceStopNativeAutoBot then forceStopNativeAutoBot() end
                warpViaPillar("Town", setStatCb)
                task.wait(1.0)
                root = getCharRoot()
                if root and (getCurrentActualZone() == "Town" or (root.Position - TOWN_COORDS.TownPillarStand).Magnitude < 60) then
                    arrivedInTown = true
                    break
                end
            end
        end
        if not arrivedInTown then
            if setStatCb then setStatCb("⚠️ กลับเมืองไม่สำเร็จ กำลังลองใหม่...") end
            return false
        end
        task.wait(0.25)
    end

    if not dungeonFarmActive then return false end
    if forceStopNativeAutoBot then forceStopNativeAutoBot() end
    root, hum = getCharRoot()
    if not root or not hum then return false end

    -- 3. ล้างหน้าต่าง LeaveConfirm (ออกจากดันเจี้ยนเก่า) ก่อนเสมอ เพื่อไม่ให้ขัดขวางการคุยกับ GateKeeper
    local pg = lp:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
    local dw = pg and pg:FindFirstChild("LumiDungeonWindows")
    if dw and dw:FindFirstChild("LeaveConfirm") and dw.LeaveConfirm.Visible then
        local btnRed = dw.LeaveConfirm:FindFirstChild("BtnRed", true)
        if btnRed then simulateButtonClick(btnRed) end
        local Remotes = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("Remotes")
        if Remotes and Remotes:FindFirstChild("DungeonAction") then
            pcall(function() Remotes.DungeonAction:FireServer("leave") end)
        end
        dw.LeaveConfirm.Visible = false
        task.wait(0.3)
    end

    -- 4. หลุดจากซุ้มกิจกรรมกลางเมืองก่อน หากตัวละครติดอยู่ในเขตซุ้ม (X: -22..22, Z: 15..56)
    escapeFestivalBoothIfStuck()

    -- 5. อ่านพิกัดจริงของ GateKeeper NPC แบบ Dynamic (รองรับหากแผนที่ขยับ)
    local gate = workspace:FindFirstChild("Map") and workspace.Map:FindFirstChild("Village") and workspace.Map.Village:FindFirstChild("DungeonGate_Village")
    local gk = gate and gate:FindFirstChild("GateKeeper")
    local gkTorso = gk and (gk:FindFirstChild("Torso") or gk:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or gk.PrimaryPart)
    local gkPos = gkTorso and gkTorso.Position or Vector3.new(4.8, 4.8, 112.4)
    local gateStandPos = Vector3.new(gkPos.X, 4.3, gkPos.Z - 3.2)

    if setStatCb then setStatCb("🚶 กำลังเดินเลียบทางเท้าไปหา GateKeeper NPC หน้าประตูดันเจี้ยน...") end

    -- 6. คำนวณเส้นทางอ้อมซุ้มกิจกรรม New Hunter Festival 100% จากทุกทิศในเมือง (รวมถึงฝั่งเหนือ/สะพานโซน 1)
    root, hum = getCharRoot()
    if not root or not hum then return false end
    local myPos = root.Position
    local waypoints = {}

    if (Vector3.new(myPos.X, 0, myPos.Z) - Vector3.new(gateStandPos.X, 0, gateStandPos.Z)).Magnitude <= 14 then
        table.insert(waypoints, gateStandPos)
    elseif myPos.Z < 62 then
        -- อยู่ฝั่งเหนือของซุ้มกิจกรรม (เช่น ใกล้ TownExit/สะพานโซน 1 หรือ Barnaby) -> ห้ามเดินผ่ากลางซุ้มเด็ดขาด
        if myPos.X < -22 then
            -- ฝั่งตะวันตก (ใกล้ Barnaby): เดินอ้อมผ่านถนนทิศใต้
            table.insert(waypoints, TOWN_COORDS.BarnabySouthJunc)
            table.insert(waypoints, TOWN_COORDS.SouthAlleyWest)
            table.insert(waypoints, TOWN_COORDS.SouthAlleyCenter)
            table.insert(waypoints, Vector3.new(4.8, 4.3, 85.0))
        else
            -- ฝั่งเหนือ / ตะวันออก (รวมถึงหน้าสะพานโซน 1): เดินอ้อมถนนตะวันออก (TownEastAvenue -> TownPillarStand)
            if myPos.Z < 18 then
                table.insert(waypoints, Vector3.new(18.0, 4.3, 4.0))
            end
            table.insert(waypoints, TOWN_COORDS.TownEastAvenue)
            table.insert(waypoints, TOWN_COORDS.TownPillarStand)
            table.insert(waypoints, Vector3.new(12.0, 4.3, 82.0))
        end
        table.insert(waypoints, Vector3.new(4.8, 4.3, 100.0))
        table.insert(waypoints, gateStandPos)
    else
        -- อยู่ฝั่งใต้ของซุ้มกิจกรรมแล้ว (Z >= 62) -> เดินตรงไปหน้า GateKeeper ได้เลย
        if myPos.Z < 85 then
            if myPos.X < -10 then
                table.insert(waypoints, TOWN_COORDS.SouthAlleyCenter)
                table.insert(waypoints, Vector3.new(4.8, 4.3, 85.0))
            elseif myPos.X > 14 then
                table.insert(waypoints, Vector3.new(20.0, 4.3, 82.0))
                table.insert(waypoints, Vector3.new(4.8, 4.3, 88.0))
            else
                table.insert(waypoints, Vector3.new(4.8, 4.3, 85.0))
            end
        end
        if myPos.Z < 102 then
            table.insert(waypoints, Vector3.new(4.8, 4.3, 100.0))
        end
        table.insert(waypoints, gateStandPos)
    end

    -- Smooth walk: one navmesh path straight to the NPC, followed without stopping at each point (the old
    -- fixed-waypoint walk stopped, turned and re-routed at every corner, which looked confused). The fixed
    -- waypoints above stay as the fallback when the path cannot be computed.
    local function smoothWalk(goal)
        local wps = computeSubPath(root.Position, goal)
        if not wps or #wps < 2 then return false end
        local lastPos, lastMoveT = root.Position, tick()
        local t0 = tick()
        local i = 2
        while i <= #wps and tick() - t0 < 25 do
            if dngWalkAbort() then return false end
            root, hum = getCharRoot()
            if not root or not hum then return false end
            local wp = wps[i]
            if wp.Action == Enum.PathWaypointAction.Jump then hum.Jump = true end
            hum:MoveTo(wp.Position)
            local flat = Vector3.new(wp.Position.X - root.Position.X, 0, wp.Position.Z - root.Position.Z).Magnitude
            if flat < 3.5 then
                i = i + 1
            else
                if (root.Position - lastPos).Magnitude > 1.5 then
                    lastPos, lastMoveT = root.Position, tick()
                elseif tick() - lastMoveT > 1.5 then
                    -- blocked: re-plan from here once
                    hum.Jump = true
                    local again = computeSubPath(root.Position, goal)
                    if not again or #again < 2 then return false end
                    wps, i, lastMoveT = again, 2, tick()
                end
                task.wait(0.05)
            end
        end
        root = getCharRoot()
        return root and (Vector3.new(root.Position.X - goal.X, 0, root.Position.Z - goal.Z).Magnitude <= 6) or false
    end

    -- North of the festival booth (red no-go circle at 0,37) keep the booth detour, then path smoothly from the south side
    if myPos.Z < 62 then
        for idx = 1, math.max(0, #waypoints - 2) do
            if dngWalkAbort() then return false end
            root, hum = getCharRoot()
            if not root or not hum then return false end
            if (root.Position - waypoints[idx]).Magnitude > 4.5 then walkTo(waypoints[idx], 12, 4, dngWalkAbort) end
        end
        root, hum = getCharRoot()
        if not root or not hum then return false end
    end
    local smoothOk = false
    pcall(function() smoothOk = smoothWalk(gateStandPos) end)
    if not smoothOk then
        for _, wp in ipairs(waypoints) do
            if dngWalkAbort() then return false end
            root, hum = getCharRoot()
            if not root or not hum then return false end
            if (root.Position - wp).Magnitude > 4.5 then
                walkTo(wp, 12, 4, dngWalkAbort)
            end
        end
    end

    if dngWalkAbort() then return false end
    root, hum = getCharRoot()
    if not root or not hum then return false end

    -- 7. ตรวจสอบระยะจริงถึง GateKeeper NPC (MaxActivationDistance = 10 studs)
    local distToNpc = (Vector3.new(root.Position.X, 0, root.Position.Z) - Vector3.new(gkPos.X, 0, gkPos.Z)).Magnitude
    if distToNpc > 8.0 then
        if forceStopNativeAutoBot then forceStopNativeAutoBot() end
        walkTo(gateStandPos, 10, 2.5, dngWalkAbort)
        root, hum = getCharRoot()
        if not root or not hum then return false end
        distToNpc = (Vector3.new(root.Position.X, 0, root.Position.Z) - Vector3.new(gkPos.X, 0, gkPos.Z)).Magnitude
    end

    if distToNpc > 10.5 then
        if setStatCb then
            setStatCb(string.format("⚠️ ยังเดินไม่ถึง GateKeeper (ห่าง %.0f studs) — กำลังเดินเข้าหาใหม่...", distToNpc))
        end
        return false
    end

    -- หยุดการเคลื่อนที่ก่อนคุยกับ NPC
    pcall(function() hum:MoveTo(root.Position) end)

    -- 8. หันหน้าเข้าหา GateKeeper Torso (โดยไม่ Teleport นอกดันเจี้ยน)
    pcall(function()
        root.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(root.Position, Vector3.new(gkPos.X, root.Position.Y, gkPos.Z))
        if workspace.CurrentCamera then
            workspace.CurrentCamera.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(workspace.CurrentCamera.CFrame.Position, gkPos)
        end
    end)
    task.wait(0.25)

    if setStatCb then setStatCb("💬 เข้าประชิดและคุยกับ GateKeeper NPC...") end

    -- 9. ค้นหา ProximityPrompt บนตัว GateKeeper หรือซุ้มประตูดันเจี้ยน
    local prompt = nil
    if gate then
        if gk then
            local t = gk:FindFirstChild("Torso")
            if t and t:FindFirstChild("DungeonPrompt") and t.DungeonPrompt.Enabled then
                prompt = t.DungeonPrompt
            end
            if not prompt then
                for _, desc in ipairs(gk:GetDescendants()) do
                    if desc:IsA("ProximityPrompt") and desc.Enabled then
                        if desc.Name == "DungeonPrompt" then
                            prompt = desc
                            break
                        elseif not prompt then
                            prompt = desc
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        if not prompt then
            for _, desc in ipairs(gate:GetDescendants()) do
                if desc:IsA("ProximityPrompt") and desc.Enabled and (desc.Name == "DungeonPrompt" or desc.Name:find("Prompt")) then
                    prompt = desc
                    break
                end
            end
        end
    end
    if not prompt and gk and gk:FindFirstChild("Torso") then
        prompt = gk.Torso:FindFirstChild("DungeonPrompt")
    end

    -- 10. เรียกใช้งาน ProximityPrompt เพื่อคุยและเปิดหน้าต่างดันเจี้ยน HuntDungeonSelect
    local opened = false
    for attempt = 1, 3 do
        if dngWalkAbort() then return false end
        if prompt then
            triggerPromptSafe(prompt)
        end

        local waitOpen = tick()
        while tick() - waitOpen < 2.5 do
            if dngWalkAbort() then return false end
            pg = lp:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
            if pg then
                local card = pg:FindFirstChild("Card_" .. (dungeonSelectedId or "halloween"), true)
                if card and card.Visible then opened = true break end
                local dngGui = pg:FindFirstChild("HuntDungeonSelect")
                if dngGui and dngGui.Enabled and #dngGui:GetChildren() > 2 then opened = true break end
            end
            task.wait(0.2)
        end
        if opened then break end
        task.wait(0.4)
    end

    return true
end


-- ตอบรับ Ready check อัตโนมัติเมื่อปาร์ตี้ส่งคำขอ
task.spawn(function()
    local Remotes = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):WaitForChild("Remotes", 10)
    if Remotes and Remotes:FindFirstChild("DungeonPrompt") then
        _G.GolfHub_Conn(Remotes.DungeonPrompt.OnClientEvent, function(action, data)
            if dungeonFarmActive and dungeonAutoReady and action == "ready" then
                task.wait(0.3)
                local readyId = (type(data) == "table" and data.Id) or nil
                if Remotes:FindFirstChild("DungeonAction") then
                    if readyId then
                        pcall(function() Remotes.DungeonAction:FireServer("ready", readyId, true) end)
                    else
                        pcall(function() Remotes.DungeonAction:FireServer("ready", true) end)
                    end
                end
                -- กดปุ่ม Ready บน UI ReadyCheck ด้วย
                local pg = lp:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
                local dw = pg and pg:FindFirstChild("LumiDungeonWindows")
                local rc = dw and dw:FindFirstChild("ReadyCheck")
                if rc and rc.Visible then
                    for _, desc in ipairs(rc:GetDescendants()) do
                        if desc:IsA("TextButton") or desc:IsA("ImageButton") then
                            local txt = desc:FindFirstChildOfClass("TextLabel") and desc:FindFirstChildOfClass("TextLabel").Text or (desc:IsA("TextButton") and desc.Text or desc.Name)
                            if txt:lower():find("ready") and not txt:lower():find("not") then
                                simulateButtonClick(desc)
                                break
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end)
    end
end)

-- ---------- IN-DUNGEON COMBAT ENGINE (100% Isolated & Full-Featured) ----------

-- ฟังก์ชันดึงพิกัดที่ปลอดภัยของ Object (ป้องกัน Folder หรือ Model ไม่มี PrimaryPart)
local function getObjectSafePos(obj)
    if not obj then return nil end
    if obj:IsA("BasePart") then
        return obj.Position
    elseif obj:IsA("Model") then
        local pp = obj.PrimaryPart
        if pp then return pp.Position end
        local ok, piv = pcall(function() return obj:GetPivot().Position end)
        if ok and piv then return piv end
    end
    return nil
end

task.wait() -- yield point: spread dungeon setup over frames
-- ====================================================================
-- REAL-TIME 360° AI HAZARD TRACKER & GEOMETRIC EVASION ENGINE (100+ FPS)
-- ====================================================================
local activeDungeonHazards   = {} -- [key] = { center, cframe, radius, width, length, angle, dir, kind, room, oneHit, isPool, expire, skill }
local activeDungeonSafeZones = {} -- list of { center, radius, expire, prefix }
local activeBrazierUntil     = 0  -- Inferno Judgment (4 braziers) active window (8s warn -> StunOnFail 4s)
local activeBrazierZones     = {} -- fallback positions from BossFx for Inferno Judgment
local visitedBraziers        = {} -- [obj/key] = tick() cooldown after lighting
local activeBossSpinUntil    = 0  -- Fire Sword Spin active window (R=13.5, Duration=4s, TickEvery=0.33s)
local activeBossSpinRadius   = 15.0
local lastDungeonDodgeTick   = 0
local lastSkillCastTime      = {} -- per-slot debounce ป้องกันยิง firesignal รัวจน FPS ตก
local cachedHotbarStr        = nil
local cachedHotbarTbl        = {}

-- พิกัดจริงทั้ง 4 แท่นกระถางไฟ (workspace.Halloween.Braziers -> Bowl) สำหรับตัดท่า Inferno Judgment ของ Skull King
local SKULL_KING_BRAZIER_SPOTS = {
    { id = "brazier_NE", pos = Vector3.new(-3988.0, 63.9, -296.0) },
    { id = "brazier_NW", pos = Vector3.new(-4052.0, 63.9, -296.0) },
    { id = "brazier_SW", pos = Vector3.new(-4052.0, 63.9, -224.0) },
    { id = "brazier_SE", pos = Vector3.new(-3988.0, 63.9, -224.0) },
}

local DngExt = {
    activeBraziers      = {},
    braziersCompletedUntil = 0,
    bossStunRushUntil   = 0,
    activeDemonSkulls   = {},
    kickedDemonSkulls   = {},
    lastSkullScanTick   = 0,
    activeChests        = {},
    openedChests        = {},
    lastChestScanTick   = 0,
    lastChestOpenedTick = 0,
    clearFirstSeenTick  = 0,
    lastAliveChar       = nil,
    wasDownedOrDead     = false,
    lastCombatPos       = nil,
    lastCombatStep      = 0,
    lastRespawnRetTick  = 0,
}

-- DUNGEON SKILL DATABASE: every Skills / LeaderMoves entry of every dgn_* monster, read from the game's own MonsterData
-- (source checked in Skull King's Crypt). Used to fill in OneHit / geometry when a BossFx zone does not carry it.
DngExt.skillDb = { byName = {}, ready = false }
pcall(function()
    local shared = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):WaitForChild("Shared", 5)
    local MonsterData = require(shared:WaitForChild("MonsterData", 5))
    for monsterId, def in pairs(MonsterData) do
        if type(monsterId) == "string" and monsterId:find("^dgn_") and type(def) == "table" then
            for _, listKey in ipairs({ "Skills", "LeaderMoves" }) do
                local list = def[listKey]
                if type(list) == "table" then
                    for _, sk in pairs(list) do
                        if type(sk) == "table" and type(sk.Name) == "string" and not DngExt.skillDb.byName[sk.Name] then
                            DngExt.skillDb.byName[sk.Name] = {
                                monster = monsterId,
                                shape   = sk.Shape,
                                radius  = sk.Radius,
                                width   = sk.Width,
                                length  = sk.Length,
                                angle   = sk.Angle,
                                warn    = sk.Warn,
                                duration = sk.Duration,
                                poolDur = (type(sk.Pool) == "table" and tonumber(sk.Pool.Duration)) or nil,
                                oneHit  = (sk.OneHit == true),
                                magic   = (sk.Magic == true),
                            }
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    DngExt.skillDb.ready = true
end)
-- Fallback for the three skills MonsterData marks OneHit, in case the module cannot be required
for _, name in ipairs({ "Doom Nova", "Execution Cleave", "Hex Bolt" }) do
    if not DngExt.skillDb.byName[name] then
        DngExt.skillDb.byName[name] = { oneHit = true }
    end
end
-- BossFx skill names are not always clean ("Skull Star Rush " with a trailing space, "Inferno Judgment (light all 4 braziers!)")
function DngExt.lookupSkill(name)
    local key = tostring(name or ""):gsub("%s*%b()", ""):gsub("^%s+", ""):gsub("%s+$", "")
    return DngExt.skillDb.byName[key]
end
_G.GolfHub_DngExt = DngExt
DngExt.retreatHp       = 0.35 -- start a low-HP retreat below this health ratio
DngExt.retreatResumeHp = 0.60 -- go back to fighting at or above this ratio
DngExt.retreatMaxSec   = 5.0  -- never stay out longer than this
DngExt.retreatCooldown = 12.0 -- minimum gap between two retreats
DngExt.retreatUntil    = 0
DngExt.retreatReadyAt  = 0
DngExt.spinSeenAt      = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" }) -- model -> first time SpinShowZone was seen continuously true
if getgenv then
    getgenv().GolfHub_Debug = getgenv().GolfHub_Debug or {}
    getgenv().GolfHub_Debug.dng = DngExt
end
-- 1. Real-time RemoteEvent BossFx + Notify Listener (ครบทุกท่าจาก MonsterData:
--    Skull King: Rise of the Skull King R=27, Flame Slam Cone 70° R=32, Fire Sword Spin R=13.5 4s, Skull Star Rush W=10, Doom Nova Safe×2 R=8.75 OneHit, Execution Cleave 11×90 OneHit, Skull Bomb Rain R=12, Inferno Judgment 4 Braziers 8s
--    Potion Witch: Poison Potion R=14.4 + Pool 4s, Cauldron Splash Cone 80° R=26, Crow Peck R=11.2, Broom Dash 7×48, Vanish Clones×2, Crow Mark R=17.6, Hex Bolt R=7 OneHit)
pcall(function()
    local Remotes = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):WaitForChild("Remotes", 5)
    if Remotes then
        local bossFx = Remotes:WaitForChild("BossFx", 5)
        if bossFx and bossFx:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
            _G.GolfHub_Conn(bossFx.OnClientEvent, function(action, bossName, skillName, zones, duration)
                if game.PlaceId ~= DUNGEON_PLACE_ID then return end
                if getgenv and getgenv().GolfHub_DngDebug and writefile then
                    pcall(function()
                        local z1 = type(zones) == "table" and zones[1] or nil
                        local row = string.format("%.2f|%s|%s|%s|zones=%d|dur=%s|kind=%s|r=%s|w=%s|l=%s|a=%s|oneHit=%s",
                            tick(), tostring(action), tostring(bossName), tostring(skillName), type(zones) == "table" and #zones or 0, tostring(duration),
                            tostring(z1 and (z1.Kind or z1.Shape)), tostring(z1 and z1.Radius), tostring(z1 and z1.Width), tostring(z1 and z1.Length), tostring(z1 and z1.Angle), tostring(z1 and z1.OneHit))
                        local logPath = "GolfHub_HuntingTogether/dungeon_bossfx.log"
                        if appendfile then
                            appendfile(logPath, row .. "\n")
                        else
                            writefile(logPath, ((isfile and isfile(logPath)) and readfile(logPath) or "") .. row .. "\n")
                        end
                    end)
                end
                local now = tick()
                if action == "cancel" then
                    -- cancel names one skill (for example "Inferno Judgment"); clearing every hazard also dropped unrelated active zones
                    local cancelled = tostring(skillName or ""):gsub("%s*%b()", ""):gsub("^%s+", ""):gsub("%s+$", "")
                    if cancelled ~= "" then
                        for key, hz in pairs(activeDungeonHazards) do
                            local hzName = tostring(hz.skill or ""):gsub("%s*%b()", ""):gsub("^%s+", ""):gsub("%s+$", "")
                            if hzName == cancelled then activeDungeonHazards[key] = nil end
                        end
                    else
                        table.clear(activeDungeonHazards)
                        table.clear(activeDungeonSafeZones)
                    end
                    return
                end
                local skillInfo = DngExt.lookupSkill(skillName)
                local poolDur = skillInfo and tonumber(skillInfo.poolDur) or 0
                local cleanSkill = tostring(skillName or ""):gsub("%s*%b()", ""):gsub("^%s+", ""):gsub("%s+$", "")
                if action == "warn" and skillInfo and skillInfo.shape == "spin" then
                    activeBossSpinUntil = math.max(activeBossSpinUntil, now + (tonumber(duration) or 1.2) + (tonumber(skillInfo.duration) or 4) + 0.3)
                end
                if action == "hit" then
                    if poolDur > 0 then
                        if type(zones) ~= "table" or #zones == 0 then
                            for _, hz in pairs(activeDungeonHazards) do
                                local hzName = tostring(hz.skill or ""):gsub("%s*%b()", ""):gsub("^%s+", ""):gsub("%s+$", "")
                                if hzName == cleanSkill then
                                    hz.isPool = true
                                    hz.expire = math.max(hz.expire, now + poolDur + 0.35)
                                end
                            end
                            return
                        end
                    elseif not (skillInfo and skillInfo.shape == "spin") then
                        local hitCenters = {}
                        if type(zones) == "table" then
                            for _, z in ipairs(zones) do
                                if type(z) == "table" then
                                    local cf = z.CFrame or z.cf
                                    local c = z.Center or (cf and cf.Position)
                                    if typeof(c) == "Vector3" then hitCenters[#hitCenters + 1] = c end
                                end
                            end
                        end
                        for _, hz in pairs(activeDungeonHazards) do
                            local hzName = tostring(hz.skill or ""):gsub("%s*%b()", ""):gsub("^%s+", ""):gsub("%s+$", "")
                            if hzName == cleanSkill then
                                if #hitCenters > 0 and typeof(hz.center) == "Vector3" then
                                    for _, hc in ipairs(hitCenters) do
                                        local dx, dz = hz.center.X - hc.X, hz.center.Z - hc.Z
                                        if (dx * dx + dz * dz) <= 12.25 then
                                            hz.expire = math.min(hz.expire, now + 0.15)
                                            break
                                        end
                                    end
                                elseif #hitCenters == 0 and (hz.expire - now) <= 0.45 then
                                    hz.expire = math.min(hz.expire, now + 0.15)
                                end
                            end
                        end
                        return
                    end
                end
                if type(zones) == "table" then
                    local isHitPool = (action == "hit") and (poolDur > 0)
                    local dur = isHitPool and (poolDur + 0.35) or (tonumber(duration) or 2.5)
                    for idx, z in ipairs(zones) do
                        if type(z) == "table" then
                            local cf = z.CFrame or z.cf
                            local center = z.Center or (cf and cf.Position)
                            local radius = tonumber(z.Radius) or (skillInfo and tonumber(skillInfo.radius)) or 10
                            local kindStr = tostring(z.Kind or z.Shape or "Circle")
                            local zHalf = tonumber(z.Half)
                            local angleDeg = tonumber(z.Angle) or (skillInfo and tonumber(skillInfo.angle)) or (zHalf and (zHalf <= math.pi and math.deg(zHalf) * 2 or zHalf * 2))
                            local hzKey = string.format("%s_%s_%d_%d_%d", tostring(skillName), tostring(kindStr), idx, center and math.floor(center.X + 0.5) or 0, center and math.floor(center.Z + 0.5) or 0)
                            local isOneHitSkill = (skillInfo ~= nil and skillInfo.oneHit == true)
                            activeDungeonHazards[hzKey] = {
                                center = center,
                                cframe = cf,
                                radius = radius,
                                width  = tonumber(z.Width) or (skillInfo and tonumber(skillInfo.width)),
                                length = tonumber(z.Length) or (skillInfo and tonumber(skillInfo.length)),
                                angle  = angleDeg,
                                dir    = (typeof(z.Dir) == "Vector3" and z.Dir) or (cf and cf.LookVector) or nil,
                                kind   = kindStr,
                                room   = (z.Room == true),
                                oneHit = (z.OneHit == true) or isOneHitSkill,
                                isPool = isHitPool,
                                expire = now + dur,
                                skill  = skillName or "Telegraph",
                            }
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)
        end

        -- ฟัง Notify เพื่อตรวจจับเริ่ม/จบ Inferno Judgment (ห้ามรีทริกเกอร์ตอนมีข้อความ "Brazier lit!" หรือ "stunned"!)
        local notifyRem = Remotes:FindFirstChild("Notify")
        if notifyRem and notifyRem:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
            _G.GolfHub_Conn(notifyRem.OnClientEvent, function(msg)
                local s = tostring(msg or "")
                local sl = s:lower()
                if (sl:find("kicked a skull") or sl:find("stun") or sl:find("stopped") or sl:find("interrupt") or sl:find("4/4") or sl:find("all braziers lit")) then
                    DngExt.bossStunRushUntil = tick() + 3.5
                    if not sl:find("kicked a skull") then
                        activeBrazierUntil = 0
                        table.clear(activeBrazierZones)
                        DngExt.braziersCompletedUntil = tick() + 4.5
                    end
                    -- บอสติดสตัน/โดนขัดจังหวะ -> ล้างวงแดงสกิลบอสที่ถูกตัดทิ้ง (เก็บเฉพาะบ่อพิษ Poison Pool ไว้) เพื่อพุ่งซ้ำบอสทันที!
                    for k, hz in pairs(activeDungeonHazards) do
                        if not hz.isPool then
                            if sl:find("stun") or sl:find("stopped") or sl:find("interrupt") or hz.room or tostring(hz.skill or ""):lower():find("inferno") then
                                activeDungeonHazards[k] = nil
                            end
                        end
                    end
                    if sl:find("stun") or sl:find("stopped") or sl:find("interrupt") then
                        activeBossSpinUntil = 0
                    end
                elseif sl:find("relight") or sl:find("light all") or (sl:find("brazier") and not sl:find("lit")) or s:find("จุดไฟ") then
                    if tick() >= (DngExt.braziersCompletedUntil or 0) then
                        activeBrazierUntil = tick() + 8.5
                        table.clear(visitedBraziers)
                    end
                end
            end)
        end
    end
end)

-- 1.5 ค้นหากระถางไฟที่ยังดับอยู่ระหว่างท่า Inferno Judgment (4 Braziers — Event-Driven + workspace.Halloween.Braziers)
function DngExt.isBrazierPrompt(inst)
    if not inst or not inst:IsA("ProximityPrompt") then return false end
    local objTxt = tostring(inst.ObjectText or ""):lower()
    local actTxt = tostring(inst.ActionText or ""):lower()
    local pName  = tostring(inst.Name or ""):lower()
    local parentName = inst.Parent and tostring(inst.Parent.Name or ""):lower() or ""
    if objTxt:find("brazier") or (actTxt == "light" and (objTxt == "" or objTxt:find("brazier") or parentName:find("bowl") or parentName:find("brazier") or pName:find("light") or pName:find("brazier"))) then
        return true
    end
    return false
end

local function findUnlitDungeonBraziers(ignoreVisited)
    local now = tick()
    local results = {}
    local seen = {}

    local function tryAddBrazier(inst, promptOpt)
        if not inst or not inst.Parent or seen[inst] then return end
        local pr = promptOpt or (inst:IsA("ProximityPrompt") and inst or inst:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("ProximityPrompt", true))
        if pr and not pr.Enabled then return end
        -- กฎเหล็ก: ถ้าเป็น ProximityPrompt จริงที่ยัง Enabled อยู่ ห้ามซ่อนด้วย visitedBraziers เด็ดขาด (กันข้ามแท่นที่ไฟยังไม่ติด!)
        if (not ignoreVisited) and (not pr) and visitedBraziers[inst] and (now - visitedBraziers[inst]) < 0.25 then return end
        if inst:GetAttribute("Lit") == true or inst:GetAttribute("IsLit") == true or inst:GetAttribute("Active") == true then
            return
        end
        local posObj = inst:IsA("ProximityPrompt") and inst.Parent or inst
        local pos = getObjectSafePos(posObj)
        if not pos and posObj and posObj.Parent then
            pos = getObjectSafePos(posObj.Parent)
        end
        if not pos or pos.X > -3500 then return end
        seen[inst] = true
        if posObj then seen[posObj] = true end
        table.insert(results, {
            obj = posObj or inst,
            pos = pos,
            prompt = pr,
        })
    end

    -- (ก) Event Tracker (O(1) จับ Prompt "Brazier / Light [E]" ทุกตัวที่ Server สปอนไม่ว่าอยู่โฟลเดอร์ไหน!)
    for pr in pairs(DngExt.activeBraziers) do
        if not pr or not pr.Parent then
            DngExt.activeBraziers[pr] = nil
        elseif pr.Enabled then
            tryAddBrazier(pr.Parent, pr)
        end
    end

    -- (ข) สแกน workspace.Halloween.Braziers โดยตรง (โฟลเดอร์เล็ก ~29 ชิ้น — แม่นยำ 100%)
    pcall(function()
        local hw = workspace:FindFirstChild("Halloween")
        local brFolder = hw and hw:FindFirstChild("Braziers")
        if brFolder then
            for _, d in ipairs(brFolder:GetDescendants()) do
                if d:IsA("ProximityPrompt") and d.Enabled and d.Parent then
                    DngExt.activeBraziers[d] = true
                    tryAddBrazier(d.Parent, d)
                end
            end
        end
    end)

    -- (ค) สแกน CollectionService (สำรอง)
    if #results == 0 then
        pcall(function()
            local cs = cloneref and cloneref(game:GetService("CollectionService")) or game:GetService("CollectionService")
            for _, tag in ipairs({"Brazier", "BossBrazier", "InfernoBrazier", "DungeonBrazier"}) do
                for _, inst in ipairs(cs:GetTagged(tag)) do
                    local pr = inst:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("ProximityPrompt", true)
                    if pr and pr.Enabled then
                        DngExt.activeBraziers[pr] = true
                        tryAddBrazier(inst, pr)
                    end
                end
            end
        end)
    end

    -- (ง) เช็คลูกศรสีเหลืองบนพื้น (LumiGuideGoal) — ถ้าลูกศรยังชี้ไปที่ 1 ใน 4 กระถางไฟ แปลว่ายังมีแท่นที่ไฟยังไม่ติดแน่นอน!
    if #results == 0 and now >= (DngExt.braziersCompletedUntil or 0) then
        local guideGoal = lp:GetAttribute("LumiGuideGoal")
        if typeof(guideGoal) == "Vector3" and guideGoal.X <= -3500 then
            for _, sp in ipairs(SKULL_KING_BRAZIER_SPOTS) do
                local fakeKey = string.format("FB_%.0f_%.0f", sp.pos.X, sp.pos.Z)
                if ignoreVisited or (not visitedBraziers[fakeKey]) or (now - visitedBraziers[fakeKey]) > 3.0 then
                    table.insert(results, {
                        obj = fakeKey,
                        pos = sp.pos,
                        prompt = nil,
                        isZoneFallback = true,
                    })
                end
            end
        end
    end

    return results
end

local function lightDungeonBrazierWithKeyE(root, hum, bestB, totalUnlit, tryNum)
    if not bestB or not bestB.pos or not root or not hum then return false end
    -- ยืนบนพื้นห้องบอส (Y = 62.8) ห่างจากแท่นกระถาง 3.8 studs โดยเยื้องเข้าหากึ่งกลางห้อง (-4020, -260) กันตกขอบ/ตกแมพ 100%!
    local bPos = bestB.pos
    local toCenter = Vector3.new(-4020.0 - bPos.X, 0, -260.0 - bPos.Z)
    local dirU = toCenter.Magnitude > 0.5 and toCenter.Unit or Vector3.new(0, 0, 1)
    local standX = math.clamp(bPos.X + dirU.X * 3.8, -4065, -3955)
    local standZ = math.clamp(bPos.Z + dirU.Z * 3.8, -315, -205)
    local standPos = Vector3.new(standX, 62.8, standZ)
    local lookTarget = Vector3.new(bPos.X, 62.8, bPos.Z)

    if teleportDungeonTo then
        teleportDungeonTo(root, hum, standPos, lookTarget)
    else
        root.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(standPos, lookTarget)
        if root:IsA("BasePart") then
            root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
            root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
        end
    end

    local prompt = bestB.prompt
    if not prompt or not prompt.Parent or not prompt.Enabled then
        local nearD = 15.0
        pcall(function()
            local hw = workspace:FindFirstChild("Halloween")
            local brFolder = hw and hw:FindFirstChild("Braziers")
            if brFolder then
                for _, d in ipairs(brFolder:GetDescendants()) do
                    if d:IsA("ProximityPrompt") and d.Enabled and d.Parent then
                        local pp = getObjectSafePos(d.Parent)
                        if pp and (pp - bestB.pos).Magnitude < nearD then
                            nearD = (pp - bestB.pos).Magnitude
                            prompt = d
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end)
    end

    local holdSec = math.clamp((prompt and prompt.HoldDuration or 0) + 0.45, 0.30, 1.3)
    local vim = nil
    pcall(function() vim = game:GetService("VirtualInputManager") end)

    -- ยิงครบทั้ง 3 ช่องทางพร้อมกัน: (1) กดคีย์ 'E' จริงผ่าน VirtualInputManager (2) InputHoldBegin/End (3) fireproximityprompt
    if vim then
        pcall(function() vim:SendKeyEvent(true, Enum.KeyCode.E, false, game) end)
    end
    if prompt and prompt.Parent and prompt.Enabled then
        pcall(function() prompt:InputHoldBegin() end)
        if fireproximityprompt then
            pcall(fireproximityprompt, prompt)
        end
    end

    -- รอยืนยันจนกว่าไฟจะติดจริง (prompt หายหรือ Enabled == false) พร้อมยิงซ้ำทุก 0.12 วิ
    local waitStart = tick()
    local lastRetry = waitStart
    local litConfirmed = false
    while tick() - waitStart < holdSec do
        if not isPlayerInDungeon() or lp:GetAttribute("DungeonDowned") == true then break end
        if prompt then
            if not prompt.Parent or not prompt.Enabled then
                litConfirmed = true
                break
            end
            if (tick() - lastRetry) >= 0.12 then
                lastRetry = tick()
                if fireproximityprompt then
                    pcall(fireproximityprompt, prompt)
                end
            end
        elseif (tick() - waitStart) >= 0.22 then
            break
        end
        task.wait(0.025)
    end

    if vim then
        pcall(function() vim:SendKeyEvent(false, Enum.KeyCode.E, false, game) end)
    end
    if prompt and prompt.Parent then
        pcall(function() prompt:InputHoldEnd() end)
        if prompt.Enabled and fireproximityprompt then
            pcall(fireproximityprompt, prompt)
            task.wait(0.04)
            if not prompt.Parent or not prompt.Enabled then
                litConfirmed = true
            end
        end
    end

    -- บันทึก visitedBraziers เฉพาะตอนเป็น fallback หรือไฟติดยืนยันแล้วเท่านั้น (ห้ามซ่อนแท่นที่ยังจุดไม่ติด!)
    if bestB.isZoneFallback or litConfirmed then
        visitedBraziers[bestB.obj] = tick()
    end
    return litConfirmed
end

-- วนวาร์ปจุดกระถางไฟทั้งหมดให้ครบ 4 แท่นจนยืนยันว่าไม่มีแท่นดับเหลืออยู่เลย -> ล้างวงแดง -> วาร์ปกลับรัวสกิลใส่บอสทันที!
function DngExt.lightAllBraziersAndResume(root, hum, target, tPos, Remotes)
    local sweepStart = tick()
    local litAny = false
    local perBrazierTries = {}

    for stepIdx = 1, 12 do
        if (tick() - sweepStart) > 7.6 then break end
        if not dungeonFarmActive or not isPlayerInDungeon() or lp:GetAttribute("DungeonDowned") == true then break end
        root, hum = getCharRoot()
        if not root or not hum or hum.Health <= 0 then break end

        local unlitNow = findUnlitDungeonBraziers(true)
        if #unlitNow == 0 then
            break
        end

        local bestB, bestBD = nil, math.huge
        for _, b in ipairs(unlitNow) do
            local bd = (b.pos - root.Position).Magnitude
            if bd < bestBD then
                bestBD = bd
                bestB = b
            end
        end
        if not bestB then break end

        local tryNum = (perBrazierTries[bestB.obj] or 0) + 1
        perBrazierTries[bestB.obj] = tryNum
        lightDungeonBrazierWithKeyE(root, hum, bestB, #unlitNow, tryNum)
        litAny = true
        task.wait(0.04)
    end

    -- เช็คซ้ำแบบเข้มงวด (ignoreVisited = true): ต้องไม่มีกระถางไฟที่ยังดับเหลืออยู่เลยแม้แต่แท่นเดียว!
    local remainingAfter = findUnlitDungeonBraziers(true)
    if #remainingAfter == 0 then
        activeBrazierUntil = 0
        table.clear(activeBrazierZones)
        DngExt.braziersCompletedUntil = tick() + 4.5
        for k, hz in pairs(activeDungeonHazards) do
            if hz.room or tostring(hz.skill or ""):lower():find("inferno") then
                activeDungeonHazards[k] = nil
            end
        end

        -- จุดครบทุกแท่นแล้ว! วาร์ปกลับด้านหลังบอส -> ล็อคเป้า -> เปิด [F] AUTO -> ยิงชุดสกิล 1-5 รัวทันที!
        root, hum = getCharRoot()
        if root and hum and hum.Health > 0 and target and target.Parent then
            local bossPosNow = getObjectSafePos(target) or tPos
            if bossPosNow then
                if lblDungeonStatus then
                    pcall(function()
                        lblDungeonStatus:Set("⚡ [จุดครบทั้ง 4 กระถางแล้ว!] วาร์ปกลับรัวสกิลโจมตีบอสทันที!", "run")
                    end)
                end
                if teleportDungeonTo then
                    local tLook = (target:IsA("Model") and target.PrimaryPart and target.PrimaryPart.CFrame.LookVector) or nil
                    local backDir = tLook and Vector3.new(-tLook.X, 0, -tLook.Z) or Vector3.new(root.Position.X - bossPosNow.X, 0, root.Position.Z - bossPosNow.Z)
                    local dirU = backDir.Magnitude > 0.2 and backDir.Unit or Vector3.new(0, 0, 1)
                    local resumePos = Vector3.new(bossPosNow.X + dirU.X * 5.0, 62.8, bossPosNow.Z + dirU.Z * 5.0)
                    teleportDungeonTo(root, hum, resumePos, bossPosNow)
                end
                pcall(function()
                    local lockEv = lp:FindFirstChild("LumiLockTarget")
                    if lockEv and lockEv:IsA("BindableEvent") then lockEv:Fire(target) end
                    if Remotes and Remotes:FindFirstChild("SetTarget") then
                        Remotes.SetTarget:FireServer(target, false, true)
                    end
                end)
                return false
            end
        end
    end

    return litAny
end

-- 1.7 ระบบตรวจจับและ Teleport + กดปุ่ม 'E' เตะ Demon Skull (Skull Bomb Rain Duds) & เปิดกล่องสมบัติ/Item Crate แบบ Event-Driven (0ms/frame ไม่กิน FPS!)
function DngExt.isDemonSkullPrompt(inst)
    if not inst or not inst:IsA("ProximityPrompt") then return false end
    local objTxt = tostring(inst.ObjectText or ""):lower()
    local actTxt = tostring(inst.ActionText or ""):lower()
    local pName  = tostring(inst.Name or ""):lower()
    local parentName = inst.Parent and tostring(inst.Parent.Name or ""):lower() or ""
    if objTxt:find("demon skull") or (objTxt:find("skull") and actTxt:find("kick")) then
        return true
    end
    if actTxt == "kick" and (objTxt:find("skull") or parentName:find("skull") or parentName:find("dud") or parentName:find("bomb") or pName:find("kick") or pName:find("skull")) then
        return true
    end
    return false
end

function DngExt.isRewardChestPrompt(inst)
    if not inst or not inst:IsA("ProximityPrompt") then return false end
    local objTxt = tostring(inst.ObjectText or ""):lower()
    local actTxt = tostring(inst.ActionText or ""):lower()
    local pName  = tostring(inst.Name or ""):lower()
    local parentName = inst.Parent and tostring(inst.Parent.Name or ""):lower() or ""

    -- ยกเว้นประตูห้องบอส, กระถางไฟ, หัวกะโหลก Demon Skull, NPC หน้าดันเจี้ยน
    if pName:find("door") or objTxt:find("door") or parentName:find("door") or parentName:find("slab") then return false end
    if objTxt:find("brazier") or actTxt:find("light") or actTxt:find("kick") or objTxt:find("demon skull") then return false end
    if pName:find("dungeonprompt") or parentName:find("gatekeeper") then return false end
    -- small Secret Chests along the route (a few potions): skipped unless the user turns them on
    if DngExt.skipSecretChests ~= false and (objTxt:find("secret", 1, true) or pName:find("secretchest", 1, true)) then return false end

    -- ถ้าเป็นกล่องระบุชื่อเจ้าของ ("xxx's chest") ให้เปิดเฉพาะกล่องของเราเองเท่านั้น!
    if objTxt:find("'s chest", 1, true) or objTxt:find("'s crate", 1, true) or objTxt:find("'s box", 1, true) then
        local myName = tostring(lp.Name or ""):lower()
        local myDisp = tostring(lp.DisplayName or ""):lower()
        local isMine = (#myName > 0 and objTxt:find(myName, 1, true) ~= nil)
            or (#myDisp > 0 and objTxt:find(myDisp, 1, true) ~= nil)
        if not isMine then return false end
        return true
    end

    if actTxt:find("open") and (
        objTxt:find("chest") or objTxt:find("crate") or objTxt:find("box") or objTxt:find("loot") or objTxt:find("reward")
        or parentName:find("chest") or parentName:find("crate") or parentName:find("loot") or parentName:find("reward")
        or pName:find("chest") or pName:find("crate") or pName:find("open")
    ) then
        return true
    end
    return false
end

-- The tracking tables are only cleaned by the dungeon finders, which never run while farming the open world, so
-- destroyed loot boxes and prompts piled up for hours and kept the dead instances in memory (770X880, 30 s after a
-- reload: 50 entries in activeChests, 44 of them already destroyed). Every 64 additions, drop the destroyed ones.
-- Only Instance keys whose Parent is gone are removed; any other key is left alone.
function DngExt.noteAdded()
    DngExt.addsSincePrune = (DngExt.addsSincePrune or 0) + 1
    if DngExt.addsSincePrune < 64 then return end
    DngExt.addsSincePrune = 0
    for _, t in ipairs({ DngExt.activeChests, DngExt.openedChests, DngExt.activeBraziers, DngExt.activeDemonSkulls, DngExt.kickedDemonSkulls }) do
        for inst in pairs(t) do
            if typeof(inst) == "Instance" and not inst.Parent then t[inst] = nil end
        end
    end
end

function DngExt.registerPrompt(inst)
    if not inst or not inst:IsA("ProximityPrompt") then return end
    DngExt.noteAdded()
    if DngExt.isBrazierPrompt(inst) then
        DngExt.activeBraziers[inst] = true
    elseif DngExt.isDemonSkullPrompt(inst) then
        DngExt.activeDemonSkulls[inst] = true
    elseif DngExt.isRewardChestPrompt(inst) then
        DngExt.activeChests[inst] = true
    end
    -- กรณี Server สร้าง ProximityPrompt ก่อนแล้วค่อยกำหนด ObjectText/ActionText ในบรรทัดถัดไป
    task.defer(function()
        if inst and inst.Parent then
            if DngExt.isBrazierPrompt(inst) then
                DngExt.activeBraziers[inst] = true
            elseif DngExt.isDemonSkullPrompt(inst) then
                DngExt.activeDemonSkulls[inst] = true
            elseif DngExt.isRewardChestPrompt(inst) then
                DngExt.activeChests[inst] = true
            end
        end
    end)
end

pcall(function()
    if _G.GolfHub_DemonSkullConns then
        for _, c in ipairs(_G.GolfHub_DemonSkullConns) do
            pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end)
        end
    end
    _G.GolfHub_DemonSkullConns = {}

    table.insert(_G.GolfHub_DemonSkullConns, workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(inst)
        if inst:IsA("ProximityPrompt") then
            DngExt.registerPrompt(inst)
        end
    end))

    local pps = cloneref and cloneref(game:GetService("ProximityPromptService")) or game:GetService("ProximityPromptService")
    if pps and pps.PromptShown then
        table.insert(_G.GolfHub_DemonSkullConns, pps.PromptShown:Connect(function(prompt)
            if prompt then
                if DngExt.isBrazierPrompt(prompt) then
                    DngExt.activeBraziers[prompt] = true
                elseif DngExt.isDemonSkullPrompt(prompt) then
                    DngExt.activeDemonSkulls[prompt] = true
                elseif DngExt.isRewardChestPrompt(prompt) then
                    DngExt.activeChests[prompt] = true
                end
            end
        end))
    end

    local cs = cloneref and cloneref(game:GetService("CollectionService")) or game:GetService("CollectionService")
    if cs and cs.GetInstanceAddedSignal then
        table.insert(_G.GolfHub_DemonSkullConns, cs:GetInstanceAddedSignal("LootBox"):Connect(function(box)
            if box and box.Parent then
                DngExt.noteAdded()
                DngExt.activeChests[box] = true
            end
        end))
    end
end)

function DngExt.findDemonSkulls()
    local now = tick()
    local results = {}
    local seen = {}

    local function tryAddSkull(pr)
        if not pr or seen[pr] or not pr.Parent or not pr.Enabled then return end
        if not DngExt.isDemonSkullPrompt(pr) then return end
        if DngExt.kickedDemonSkulls[pr] and now < DngExt.kickedDemonSkulls[pr] then return end
        local pos = getObjectSafePos(pr.Parent)
        if not pos and pr.Parent and pr.Parent.Parent then
            pos = getObjectSafePos(pr.Parent.Parent)
        end
        if pos and pos.X <= -3000 then
            seen[pr] = true
            table.insert(results, { prompt = pr, obj = pr.Parent, pos = pos })
        end
    end

    -- 1. ดึงจาก Event Tracker (O(1) แม่นยำและไวที่สุดในเฟรมเดียวกับที่ Demon Skull เกิด!)
    for pr in pairs(DngExt.activeDemonSkulls) do
        if not pr or not pr.Parent then
            DngExt.activeDemonSkulls[pr] = nil
            DngExt.kickedDemonSkulls[pr] = nil
        else
            tryAddSkull(pr)
        end
    end

    -- 2. Fallback scan เบาๆ (ทุก 1.5 วิ เฉพาะวัตถุเล็ก/Effect โดยไม่สแกนแผนที่ workspace.Halloween ทั้งแมพ!)
    if #results == 0 and (now - DngExt.lastSkullScanTick >= 1.5) then
        DngExt.lastSkullScanTick = now
        for _, child in ipairs(workspace:GetChildren()) do
            local cn = child.Name:lower()
            if cn:find("skull") or cn:find("dud") or cn:find("bomb") or cn:find("bossfx") or cn:find("projectile") then
                local pr = child:IsA("ProximityPrompt") and child or child:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("ProximityPrompt", true)
                if pr and pr.Enabled and DngExt.isDemonSkullPrompt(pr) then
                    DngExt.activeDemonSkulls[pr] = true
                    tryAddSkull(pr)
                end
            end
        end
    end

    return results
end

function DngExt.kickDemonSkull(root, hum, skullInfo, bossPos)
    if not isPlayerInDungeon or not isPlayerInDungeon() then return false end
    if not skullInfo or not root then return false end
    local prompt = skullInfo.prompt
    if not prompt or not prompt.Parent or not prompt.Enabled then return false end

    dngWatchSince = tick()
    local bv = root:FindFirstChild("GolfHub_AirHoverBV")
    if bv then bv:Destroy() end

    if lblDungeonStatus then
        pcall(function()
            lblDungeonStatus:Set("💀 [Demon Skull] Teleport เข้าไปกด [E] เตะหัวกะโหลกทันที!", "run")
        end)
    end

    -- Teleport ประชิด Demon Skull ทันที (หันหน้าเข้าหาบอสถ้ามี เพื่อทิศทางเตะแม่นยำ 100%)
    local sPos = getObjectSafePos(skullInfo.obj) or skullInfo.pos
    local standPos = Vector3.new(sPos.X, math.max(62.4, sPos.Y + 1.2), sPos.Z)
    local lookAtPos = (bossPos and (Vector3.new(bossPos.X, 0, bossPos.Z) - Vector3.new(standPos.X, 0, standPos.Z)).Magnitude > 1.0) and bossPos or sPos
    if teleportDungeonTo then
        teleportDungeonTo(root, hum, standPos, lookAtPos)
    else
        if isPlayerInDungeon and isPlayerInDungeon() then root.CFrame = CFrame.new(standPos) end
        if root:IsA("BasePart") then
            root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
            root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
        end
    end
    task.wait(0.03)

    local holdSec = math.clamp((prompt.HoldDuration or 0) + 0.06, 0.06, 0.35)
    local vim = nil
    pcall(function() vim = game:GetService("VirtualInputManager") end)

    -- ยิงครบทั้ง 3 ช่องทางทันที: (1) fireproximityprompt (2) InputHoldBegin/End (3) กดปุ่ม 'E' จริงผ่าน VirtualInputManager
    if prompt.Parent and prompt.Enabled then
        if fireproximityprompt then
            pcall(fireproximityprompt, prompt)
        end
        pcall(function() prompt:InputHoldBegin() end)
    end
    if vim then
        pcall(function() vim:SendKeyEvent(true, Enum.KeyCode.E, false, game) end)
    end

    local waitStart = tick()
    while tick() - waitStart < holdSec do
        if not prompt.Parent or not prompt.Enabled then
            break
        end
        task.wait(0.02)
    end

    if vim then
        pcall(function() vim:SendKeyEvent(false, Enum.KeyCode.E, false, game) end)
    end
    local kickSucceeded = (not prompt.Parent or not prompt.Enabled)
    if prompt.Parent then
        pcall(function() prompt:InputHoldEnd() end)
        if prompt.Enabled and fireproximityprompt then
            pcall(fireproximityprompt, prompt)
            task.wait(0.02)
            if not prompt.Parent or not prompt.Enabled then
                kickSucceeded = true
            end
        end
    end

    DngExt.kickedDemonSkulls[prompt] = tick() + 0.35
    if kickSucceeded then
        -- เตะหัวกะโหลกโดนบอสแล้ว! บอสจะติดสตัน -> ตั้งโหมดพุ่งซ้ำบอส + ล้างสกิลบอสที่ถูกขัดทันที แล้วปล่อยให้ dungeonCombatStep วาร์ปอ้อมหลังบอสรัวสกิลในเฟรมเดียวกัน!
        DngExt.bossStunRushUntil = tick() + 3.8
        for k, hz in pairs(activeDungeonHazards) do
            if not hz.isPool and not hz.oneHit then
                activeDungeonHazards[k] = nil
            end
        end
        activeBossSpinUntil = 0
        if lblDungeonStatus then
            pcall(function()
                lblDungeonStatus:Set("💥 [เตะหัวกะโหลกสำเร็จ! บอสติดสตัน] วาร์ปพุ่งเข้าหลังบอสรัวสกิลทันที!", "run")
            end)
        end
    end
    -- คืนค่า false เพื่อให้ dungeonCombatStep เดินหน้าต่อทันที (วาร์ปไปหลังบอส + โจมตี + รัวสกิล 1-5 ไม่ยืนดูเฉย!)
    return false
end

-- 1.8 ระบบตรวจจับกล่องสมบัติ / Item Crate (<Player>'s Chest / LootBox) -> วาร์ปทันที + กดค้างปุ่ม 'E' เปิดกล่อง + เก็บของดรอปทั้งหมด!
function DngExt.findChests()
    local now = tick()
    local results = {}
    local seen = {}

    local function tryAddChestInst(inst)
        if not inst or not inst.Parent then return end
        local prompt, obj = nil, nil
        if inst:IsA("ProximityPrompt") then
            if not inst.Enabled or not DngExt.isRewardChestPrompt(inst) then return end
            prompt = inst
            obj = inst.Parent
        else
            obj = inst
            local pr = inst:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("ProximityPrompt", true)
            if pr then
                if not pr.Enabled then return end
                local oTxt = tostring(pr.ObjectText or ""):lower()
                if oTxt:find("'s chest", 1, true) or oTxt:find("'s crate", 1, true) or oTxt:find("'s box", 1, true) then
                    local myName = tostring(lp.Name or ""):lower()
                    local myDisp = tostring(lp.DisplayName or ""):lower()
                    if not ((#myName > 0 and oTxt:find(myName, 1, true)) or (#myDisp > 0 and oTxt:find(myDisp, 1, true))) then
                        return
                    end
                end
                prompt = pr
            end
        end

        if DngExt.skipSecretChests ~= false then
            local oT = prompt and tostring(prompt.ObjectText or ""):lower() or ""
            local nm = obj and tostring(obj.Name or ""):lower() or ""
            if oT:find("secret", 1, true) or nm:find("secret", 1, true) then return end
        end
        local dedupKey = prompt or obj
        if not dedupKey or seen[dedupKey] then return end
        if DngExt.openedChests[dedupKey] and now < DngExt.openedChests[dedupKey] then return end

        local pos = getObjectSafePos(obj)
        if not pos and obj and obj.Parent then
            pos = getObjectSafePos(obj.Parent)
        end
        if pos and pos.X <= -3000 then
            seen[dedupKey] = true
            table.insert(results, { key = dedupKey, prompt = prompt, obj = obj, pos = pos })
        end
    end

    -- (ก) Event Tracker (O(1) ทันทีที่กล่องสมบัติเกิด!)
    for inst in pairs(DngExt.activeChests) do
        if not inst or not inst.Parent then
            DngExt.activeChests[inst] = nil
            DngExt.openedChests[inst] = nil
        else
            tryAddChestInst(inst)
        end
    end

    -- (ข) CollectionService Tag "LootBox"
    pcall(function()
        local cs = cloneref and cloneref(game:GetService("CollectionService")) or game:GetService("CollectionService")
        for _, box in ipairs(cs:GetTagged("LootBox")) do
            if box and box.Parent then
                tryAddChestInst(box)
            end
        end
    end)

    -- (ค) Fallback Scan เบาๆ ทุก 1.5 วิ (เฉพาะวัตถุกล่องโดยตรงใน workspace หรือโฟลเดอร์กล่องย่อย — ห้ามสแกน workspace.Halloween:GetDescendants() ทั้งแมพ!)
    if #results == 0 and (now - DngExt.lastChestScanTick >= 1.5) then
        DngExt.lastChestScanTick = now
        local function checkSmallNode(node)
            if not node then return end
            if node:IsA("ProximityPrompt") then
                if node.Enabled and DngExt.isRewardChestPrompt(node) then
                    DngExt.activeChests[node] = true
                    tryAddChestInst(node)
                end
            else
                local pr = node:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("ProximityPrompt", true)
                if pr and pr.Enabled and DngExt.isRewardChestPrompt(pr) then
                    DngExt.activeChests[pr] = true
                    tryAddChestInst(pr)
                end
            end
        end
        for _, child in ipairs(workspace:GetChildren()) do
            local cn = child.Name:lower()
            if cn:find("chest") or cn:find("crate") or cn:find("loot") or cn:find("box") or cn:find("reward") or cn:find("drop") then
                checkSmallNode(child)
            end
        end
        local hw = workspace:FindFirstChild("Halloween")
        if hw then
            for _, sub in ipairs(hw:GetChildren()) do
                local sn = sub.Name:lower()
                if sn:find("chest") or sn:find("crate") or sn:find("loot") or sn:find("box") or sn:find("reward") then
                    checkSmallNode(sub)
                end
            end
        end
    end

    return results
end

-- เก็บไอเทมดรอปทั้งหมดรอบกล่องสมบัติ/ห้องดันเจี้ยน พร้อมกดค้างปุ่ม 'E' + PickupDrop จนกว่าจะเก็บครบ 100%
function DngExt.collectAllDrops(root, hum, centerPos, Remotes, maxDurationSec, vim)
    if not isPlayerInDungeon or not isPlayerInDungeon() then return 0 end
    if not root or not hum or hum.Health <= 0 then return 0 end
    local cs = cloneref and cloneref(game:GetService("CollectionService")) or game:GetService("CollectionService")
    local maxDur = maxDurationSec or 4.5
    local startT = tick()
    local emptySince = nil
    local collectedCount = 0
    local dropTries = {}

    if vim then
        pcall(function() vim:SendKeyEvent(true, Enum.KeyCode.E, false, game) end)
    end

    while (tick() - startT) < maxDur do
        if not dungeonFarmActive or not isPlayerInDungeon() or lp:GetAttribute("DungeonDowned") == true then break end
        root, hum = getCharRoot()
        if not root or not hum or hum.Health <= 0 then break end

        local maxSlots = lp:GetAttribute("BagSlotsMax") or 240
        local usedSlots = lp:GetAttribute("BagSlotsUsed") or 0
        if (maxSlots - usedSlots) <= 0 then
            break
        end

        local srvNow = workspace:GetServerTimeNow()
        local myPos = root.Position
        local bestDrop, bestDist = nil, 150
        for _, d in ipairs(cs:GetTagged("Drop")) do
            if d:IsA("BasePart") and d.Parent and d.Position.X <= -3000 then
                if (dropTries[d] or 0) < 4 then
                    local owner = d:GetAttribute("OwnerUserId") or 0
                    local freeAt = d:GetAttribute("FreeAt") or 0
                    if owner == 0 or owner == lp.UserId or freeAt <= srvNow then
                        local refDist = centerPos and (d.Position - centerPos).Magnitude or 0
                        local dist = (d.Position - myPos).Magnitude
                        if refDist <= 150 and dist < bestDist then
                            bestDist = dist
                            bestDrop = d
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        if bestDrop then
            emptySince = nil
            dropTries[bestDrop] = (dropTries[bestDrop] or 0) + 1
            if lblDungeonStatus then
                pcall(function()
                    lblDungeonStatus:Set(string.format("💎 [Collect All Drops] วาร์ปเก็บของดรอปทั้งหมด [E] (%d ชิ้น)...", collectedCount + 1), "run")
                end)
            end
            if teleportDungeonTo then
                teleportDungeonTo(root, hum, bestDrop.Position + Vector3.new(0, 1.8, 0))
            end
            if Remotes and Remotes:FindFirstChild("PickupDrop") then
                pcall(function() Remotes.PickupDrop:FireServer(true) end)
            end
            if firetouchinterest then
                pcall(function()
                    firetouchinterest(root, bestDrop, 0)
                    task.wait(0.02)
                    firetouchinterest(root, bestDrop, 1)
                end)
            end
            task.wait(0.06)
            if not bestDrop.Parent then
                collectedCount = collectedCount + 1
            end
        else
            if Remotes and Remotes:FindFirstChild("PickupDrop") then
                pcall(function() Remotes.PickupDrop:FireServer(true) end)
            end
            if not emptySince then
                emptySince = tick()
            elseif (tick() - emptySince) >= 0.55 then
                break
            end
            task.wait(0.07)
        end
    end

    if vim then
        pcall(function() vim:SendKeyEvent(false, Enum.KeyCode.E, false, game) end)
    end
    return collectedCount
end

function DngExt.openChestAndCollectAll(root, hum, chestInfo, Remotes)
    if not isPlayerInDungeon or not isPlayerInDungeon() then return false end
    if not chestInfo or not root or not hum then return false end

    dngWatchSince = tick()
    local bv = root:FindFirstChild("GolfHub_AirHoverBV")
    if bv then bv:Destroy() end

    local cPos = getObjectSafePos(chestInfo.obj) or chestInfo.pos
    if not cPos then return false end

    if lblDungeonStatus then
        pcall(function()
            lblDungeonStatus:Set("🎁 [Item Crate] วาร์ปไปกล่องสมบัติ + กดค้างปุ่ม [E] เพื่อเปิดและเก็บของทั้งหมด!", "run")
        end)
    end

    -- 1. วาร์ปตรงไปหน้ากล่องสมบัติทันที (ถ้าไกล > 170 studs ใช้ DngExt.teleportPathTo สตรีมฉากให้ครบก่อน)
    local standPos = Vector3.new(cPos.X, math.max(62.4, cPos.Y + 1.2), cPos.Z)
    local distToChest = (Vector3.new(root.Position.X, 0, root.Position.Z) - Vector3.new(cPos.X, 0, cPos.Z)).Magnitude
    if distToChest > 170 and DngExt.teleportPathTo then
        DngExt.teleportPathTo(root, hum, standPos, cPos)
    elseif teleportDungeonTo then
        teleportDungeonTo(root, hum, standPos, cPos)
    end
    task.wait(0.05)

    local vim = nil
    pcall(function() vim = game:GetService("VirtualInputManager") end)

    local prompt = chestInfo.prompt
    if (not prompt or not prompt.Parent) and chestInfo.obj and chestInfo.obj.Parent then
        prompt = chestInfo.obj:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("ProximityPrompt", true)
    end

    -- 2. กดค้างปุ่ม 'E' จริง + InputHoldBegin + fireproximityprompt + OpenLootBox จนกว่ากล่องจะเปิด!
    local holdSec = math.clamp((prompt and prompt.HoldDuration or 0.5) + 0.9, 1.0, 3.0)
    if vim then
        pcall(function() vim:SendKeyEvent(true, Enum.KeyCode.E, false, game) end)
    end
    if prompt and prompt.Parent and prompt.Enabled then
        pcall(function() prompt:InputHoldBegin() end)
        if fireproximityprompt then
            pcall(fireproximityprompt, prompt)
        end
    end
    if Remotes and Remotes:FindFirstChild("OpenLootBox") and chestInfo.obj and chestInfo.obj.Parent then
        pcall(function() Remotes.OpenLootBox:FireServer(chestInfo.obj) end)
    end

    local openStart = tick()
    local lastRefire = openStart
    while (tick() - openStart) < holdSec do
        if not dungeonFarmActive or not isPlayerInDungeon() or lp:GetAttribute("DungeonDowned") == true then break end
        if prompt then
            if not prompt.Parent or not prompt.Enabled then
                break
            end
        elseif not chestInfo.obj or not chestInfo.obj.Parent then
            break
        end
        if (tick() - lastRefire) >= 0.35 then
            lastRefire = tick()
            if prompt and prompt.Parent and prompt.Enabled and fireproximityprompt then
                pcall(fireproximityprompt, prompt)
            end
            if Remotes and Remotes:FindFirstChild("OpenLootBox") and chestInfo.obj and chestInfo.obj.Parent then
                pcall(function() Remotes.OpenLootBox:FireServer(chestInfo.obj) end)
            end
        end
        task.wait(0.04)
    end

    if prompt and prompt.Parent then
        pcall(function() prompt:InputHoldEnd() end)
    end

    -- A chest that is still there with its prompt enabled after the full hold did not open (a Secret Chest without its pieces, for
    -- example). It used to be retried every 3.5s forever, so the character stayed on it and never went back to the monsters.
    -- After two failed holds leave it alone for 10 minutes.
    DngExt.chestFails = DngExt.chestFails or {}
    local chestStillClosed = prompt and prompt.Parent and prompt.Enabled
    if chestStillClosed then
        DngExt.chestFails[chestInfo.key] = (DngExt.chestFails[chestInfo.key] or 0) + 1
    else
        DngExt.chestFails[chestInfo.key] = nil
    end
    local ignoreFor = (chestStillClosed and (DngExt.chestFails[chestInfo.key] or 0) >= 2) and 600 or 3.5
    DngExt.openedChests[chestInfo.key] = tick() + ignoreFor
    DngExt.lastChestOpenedTick = tick()

    -- 3. รอไอเทมระเบิดกระจายออกจากกล่องสักครู่ (ระหว่างยังกดค้าง 'E' อยู่) แล้ววาร์ปเก็บไอเทมดรอปทั้งหมดทุกชิ้น!
    task.wait(0.35)
    root, hum = getCharRoot()
    if root and hum and hum.Health > 0 then
        DngExt.collectAllDrops(root, hum, cPos, Remotes, 4.5, vim)
    elseif vim then
        pcall(function() vim:SendKeyEvent(false, Enum.KeyCode.E, false, game) end)
    end

    return true
end

-- 2. รวบรวมข้อมูลพื้นที่อันตรายแบบ Real-time (BossFx + Moving Spin Boss + Physical Parts แบบไม่ขยายหลอก)
local function getRealtimeDungeonHazards()
    local now = tick()
    local hazards = {}
    local safeZones = {}

    -- 2.1 ดึงจาก Server BossFx (แม่นยำ 100%)
    for key, hz in pairs(activeDungeonHazards) do
        if now < hz.expire then
            table.insert(hazards, hz)
        else
            activeDungeonHazards[key] = nil
        end
    end
    for i = #activeDungeonSafeZones, 1, -1 do
        local sz = activeDungeonSafeZones[i]
        if now < sz.expire then
            table.insert(safeZones, sz)
        else
            table.remove(activeDungeonSafeZones, i)
        end
    end

    -- 2.2 ดึง Moving Spin Hazard ของบอส (เช่น Skull King Fire Sword Spin / SpinShowZone / AnimSkillRadius)
    local monFolder = workspace:FindFirstChild("Monsters")
    if monFolder then
        for _, m in ipairs(monFolder:GetChildren()) do
            if m:IsA("Model") then
                local isSpinAttr = (m:GetAttribute("SpinShowZone") == true)
                -- A real Fire Sword Spin lasts about 5.2s (1.2s warn + 4s spin). The flag can stay true on the boss for minutes
                -- (seen live: SpinShowZone=true with the boss idle), which kept a r=24.5 circle on the boss forever, blocked every
                -- standing spot around it and left the character idle far away. Ignore the flag once it has been on too long.
                if isSpinAttr then
                    local firstSeen = DngExt.spinSeenAt[m]
                    if not firstSeen then
                        firstSeen = now
                        DngExt.spinSeenAt[m] = firstSeen
                    end
                    if (now - firstSeen) > 6.5 then isSpinAttr = false end
                else
                    DngExt.spinSeenAt[m] = nil
                end
                local isBossSpinActive = (now < activeBossSpinUntil) and (m:GetAttribute("IsBoss") == true or tostring(m:GetAttribute("MonsterId") or ""):lower():find("crypt_boss"))
                if isSpinAttr or isBossSpinActive then
                    local mPos = getObjectSafePos(m)
                    if mPos and mPos.X <= -3500 then
                        -- MonsterData says Fire Sword Spin hits within 13.5; the model's SpinReach / AnimSkillRadius
                        -- attributes read larger (24.5 seen live) and kept the character standing far out of reach
                        local spinR = math.min(tonumber(m:GetAttribute("SpinReach")) or tonumber(m:GetAttribute("AnimSkillRadius")) or activeBossSpinRadius, activeBossSpinRadius)
                        table.insert(hazards, {
                            center = mPos,
                            radius = spinR,
                            kind   = "circle",
                            expire = now + 0.3,
                            skill  = tostring(m:GetAttribute("AnimSkillName") or "Fire Sword Spin"),
                        })
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end

    -- 2.3 ดึงจาก Physical Parts ใน workspace.LumiBossFx
    -- กฎสำคัญ:
    --   (ก) ถ้ามีข้อมูลเรขาคณิตแม่นยำจาก RemoteEvent BossFx (exact cone / exact OBB line / exact circle) อยู่แล้ว -> ห้ามเอา Physical Part ใน LumiBossFx มาสร้างวงกลมหลอกทับด้านหลังบอส! (แก้บั๊ก Flame Slam cone ถูก WedgePart กลายเป็นวงกลมจนวิ่งหนีไม่ยอมวาร์ปตีหลังบอส!)
    --   (ข) ข้าม WedgePart เสมอ (BossClient ใช้ WedgePart สร้างกรวย Cone เช่น Flame Slam / Cauldron Splash)
    local hasExactRemoteHazards = (#hazards > 0)
    local bossFx = workspace:FindFirstChild("LumiBossFx")
    if bossFx then
        local seenTiles = {}
        for _, part in ipairs(bossFx:GetChildren()) do
            if part:IsA("BasePart") and (not part:IsA("WedgePart")) and part.Transparency < 0.82 then
                local col = part.Color
                local pos = part.Position
                local maxDim = math.max(part.Size.X, part.Size.Z)
                local gx = math.floor(pos.X / 4)
                local gz = math.floor(pos.Z / 4)
                local gKey = gx .. "_" .. gz
                if not seenTiles[gKey] then
                    seenTiles[gKey] = true
                    local r = maxDim * 0.5 + 0.8

                    -- วงเขียว Safe Zone (BossClient สร้างเป็นรูปทรง Cylinder เสมอ ไม่สับสนกับบ่อพิษ!)
                    if part:IsA("Part") and part.Shape == Enum.PartType.Cylinder
                        and col.G > 0.62 and col.G > (col.R * 1.45) and col.G > (col.B * 1.3) and col.B < 0.6 then
                        table.insert(safeZones, { center = pos, radius = math.max(r, 6), expire = now + 0.5, prefix = "_phys_" })
                    -- วงแดง Red Zone (ใช้เป็น Fallback เฉพาะตอนไม่มีข้อมูลแม่นยำจาก BossFx RemoteEvent เช่น เพิ่งโหลดสคริปต์กลางสกิล)
                    elseif (not hasExactRemoteHazards) and maxDim <= 85 and part.Transparency < 0.76 and col.R > 0.55 and col.R > (col.G * 1.35) and col.R > (col.B * 1.35) then
                        table.insert(hazards, {
                            center = pos,
                            cframe = part.CFrame,
                            width  = part.Size.X,
                            length = part.Size.Z,
                            radius = r,
                            kind   = (math.abs(part.Size.X - part.Size.Z) > 3) and "rect" or "circle",
                            expire = now + 0.45,
                            skill  = "AoE Red Zone",
                        })
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end

    return hazards, safeZones
end

-- 3. ตรวจสอบว่าจุดที่กำหนดอยู่ในพื้นที่อันตรายหรือไม่ (Exact Circle + OBB Rect/Line/StarDash/Charge + Exact Cone Half-Angle + OneHit Margin + SafeZone)
local function isPointInDungeonDanger(pos, hazards, margin, safeZones)
    margin = margin or 3.0

    -- ถ้ามีวงเขียว Safe Zone (Doom Nova): จุดที่อยู่นอกวงเขียวถือว่าอันตรายทันที
    if safeZones and #safeZones > 0 then
        local insideAnySafe = false
        for _, sz in ipairs(safeZones) do
            local c = sz.center
            if c then
                local dx = pos.X - c.X
                local dz = pos.Z - c.Z
                if (dx * dx + dz * dz) <= ((math.max(2, (sz.radius or 8) - 1.5)) ^ 2) then
                    insideAnySafe = true
                    break
                end
            end
        end
        if not insideAnySafe then
            return true, { skill = "Doom Nova (เข้าวงเขียว Safe Zone)", oneHit = true }, 999
        end
        return false, nil, 0
    end

    for _, hz in ipairs(hazards) do
        local kind = hz.kind or "circle"
        local effMargin = margin + (hz.oneHit and 0.8 or 0)
        -- ตรวจจับ OBB ทุกทรงเส้น/สี่เหลี่ยม (Execution Cleave line, Skull Star Rush stardash, Broom Dash charge, rect, box)
        if kind ~= "circle" and kind ~= "cone" and hz.cframe and hz.width and hz.length then
            local localPos = hz.cframe:PointToObjectSpace(pos)
            local halfW = (hz.width * 0.5) + effMargin
            local halfL = (hz.length * 0.5) + effMargin
            if math.abs(localPos.X) <= halfW and math.abs(localPos.Z) <= halfL then
                return true, hz, math.abs(localPos.X)
            end
        elseif kind == "cone" and hz.center then
            local dx = pos.X - hz.center.X
            local dz = pos.Z - hz.center.Z
            local dist2D = math.sqrt(dx * dx + dz * dz)
            local r = (hz.radius or 24) + effMargin
            if dist2D <= r then
                if dist2D < 0.6 or not hz.dir then
                    return true, hz, dist2D
                end
                local toPt = Vector3.new(dx, 0, dz).Unit
                local fDir = Vector3.new(hz.dir.X, 0, hz.dir.Z)
                if fDir.Magnitude > 0.01 then
                    local dot = math.clamp(toPt:Dot(fDir.Unit), -1, 1)
                    local halfAng = math.rad(((hz.angle or 70) * 0.5) + 4)
                    if math.acos(dot) <= halfAng then
                        return true, hz, dist2D
                    end
                else
                    return true, hz, dist2D
                end
            end
        else
            local c = hz.center
            if c then
                local dx = pos.X - c.X
                local dz = pos.Z - c.Z
                local dist2D = math.sqrt(dx * dx + dz * dz)
                if dist2D < ((hz.radius or 18) + effMargin) then
                    return true, hz, dist2D
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return false, nil, 0
end

function DngExt.isDangerPos(pos, margin)
    local hz, sz = getRealtimeDungeonHazards()
    return isPointInDungeonDanger(pos, hz, margin or 1.5, sz)
end

function DngExt.snapDungeonFloor(pos, fallbackY)
    local rp = RaycastParams.new()
    rp.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
    local fl = {}
    for _, inst in ipairs({ lp.Character, workspace:FindFirstChild("Monsters"), workspace:FindFirstChild("LumiBossFx"), workspace:FindFirstChild("LumiBossSkillFx") }) do
        if inst then fl[#fl + 1] = inst end
    end
    rp.FilterDescendantsInstances = fl
    local refY = math.clamp(fallbackY or pos.Y, 55, 66)
    local hit = workspace:Raycast(Vector3.new(pos.X, refY + 8, pos.Z), Vector3.new(0, -24, 0), rp)
    if hit and hit.Position.Y >= 54 and hit.Position.Y <= 70 then
        return Vector3.new(pos.X, hit.Position.Y + 2.8, pos.Z)
    end
    return Vector3.new(pos.X, refY, pos.Z)
end

-- 4. อัลกอริทึม AI คำนวณหลบแบบ 360 องศา (< 0.3ms, 16 ทิศทาง, ไม่ทำให้ FPS ตก!)
local function calculate360OptimalSafeSpot(myPos, targetPos, hazards, safeZones)
    local rayParams = RaycastParams.new()
    rayParams.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
    local filterList = {}
    if lp.Character then table.insert(filterList, lp.Character) end
    local bFx = workspace:FindFirstChild("LumiBossFx")
    if bFx then table.insert(filterList, bFx) end
    local bSFx = workspace:FindFirstChild("LumiBossSkillFx")
    if bSFx then table.insert(filterList, bSFx) end
    local monF = workspace:FindFirstChild("Monsters")
    if monF then table.insert(filterList, monF) end
    local hw = workspace:FindFirstChild("Halloween")
    if hw then
        local sp = hw:FindFirstChild("Spawns")
        if sp then table.insert(filterList, sp) end
        local at = hw:FindFirstChild("Atmos")
        if at then table.insert(filterList, at) end
    end
    rayParams.FilterDescendantsInstances = filterList

    -- อันดับ 1: ถ้ามีวงเขียว (Safe Zone) ให้เลือกวงเขียวที่ใกล้บอส/เป้าหมายมากที่สุด แล้วชิดขอบในฝั่งบอสเพื่อให้ตีและสกิลถึง!
    if safeZones and #safeZones > 0 then
        local refPos = targetPos or myPos
        local bestSz, bestSzDist = nil, math.huge
        for _, sz in ipairs(safeZones) do
            if sz.center then
                local d = (Vector3.new(refPos.X, 0, refPos.Z) - Vector3.new(sz.center.X, 0, sz.center.Z)).Magnitude
                if d < bestSzDist then
                    bestSzDist = d
                    bestSz = sz
                end
            end
        end
        if bestSz and bestSz.center then
            local c = bestSz.center
            local spotX, spotZ = c.X, c.Z
            -- ขยับชิดขอบด้านในของวงเขียวฝั่งที่หันเข้าหาบอส (เว้นระยะปลอดภัยจากขอบนอก 2.4 studs) เพื่อเพิ่มระยะโจมตีและสกิล
            if targetPos then
                local toBoss = Vector3.new(targetPos.X - c.X, 0, targetPos.Z - c.Z)
                local distCenterToBoss = toBoss.Magnitude
                local safeInnerR = math.max(0, (bestSz.radius or 8) - 2.4)
                if distCenterToBoss > 0.5 and safeInnerR > 0.5 then
                    local stepTowardBoss = math.min(safeInnerR, math.max(0, distCenterToBoss - 3.0))
                    local dir = toBoss.Unit
                    spotX = c.X + dir.X * stepTowardBoss
                    spotZ = c.Z + dir.Z * stepTowardBoss
                end
            end
            local floorRay = workspace:Raycast(Vector3.new(spotX, myPos.Y + 8, spotZ), Vector3.new(0, -35, 0), rayParams)
            local floorY = floorRay and floorRay.Position.Y or myPos.Y
            return Vector3.new(spotX, floorY + 2.5, spotZ), "🟢 Safe Zone (วงเขียวใกล้บอส)", 0, math.floor(bestSzDist)
        end
    end

    local bestSpot = nil
    local bestScore = math.huge
    local bestAngleDeg = 0
    local bestDist = 0

    -- สแกน 16 ทิศทางรอบตัว 360° (ทุกๆ 22.5°) ด้วยระยะขั้นแบบ Sparse -> ใช้ Raycast รวมไม่เกิน ~25 ครั้ง/รอบ (เร็วกว่าเดิม 15 เท่า!)
    local NUM_RAYS = 16
    local DIST_STEPS = { 8, 13, 19, 26, 34, 42 }
    -- Skull King room (Halloween step 4): never dodge out through the west doorway. dungeonCombatStep treats
    -- X < -4066 there as "knocked out of the room" and puts us straight back, so a dodge spot out there would flip
    -- between the two every tick (DngExt.findRetreatSpot already has the same -4062 limit).
    -- (step/kind come from dungeonCombatStep via DngExt: getRealtimeDungeonStep is declared further down the file)
    local keepInSkullRoom = (DngExt.kindNow == "halloween" and DngExt.stepNow == 4)
    for i = 0, NUM_RAYS - 1 do
        local angleRad = (i / NUM_RAYS) * math.pi * 2
        local dirX = math.cos(angleRad)
        local dirZ = math.sin(angleRad)
        local dirVector = Vector3.new(dirX, 0, dirZ)

        local maxDist = 44
        local wallHit = workspace:Raycast(myPos + Vector3.new(0, 2.5, 0), dirVector * 46, rayParams)
        if wallHit then
            maxDist = math.max(0, wallHit.Distance - 3.0)
        end

        if maxDist >= 6 then
            for _, stepD in ipairs(DIST_STEPS) do
                local dist = math.min(stepD, maxDist)
                local candPos = Vector3.new(myPos.X + (dirX * dist), myPos.Y, myPos.Z + (dirZ * dist))

                if not (keepInSkullRoom and candPos.X < -4062) and not isPointInDungeonDanger(candPos, hazards, 3.2) then
