                    local floorHit = workspace:Raycast(candPos + Vector3.new(0, 8, 0), Vector3.new(0, -30, 0), rayParams)
                    if floorHit then
                        local finalPos = Vector3.new(candPos.X, floorHit.Position.Y + 2.5, candPos.Z)
                        local distToTgt = targetPos and (Vector3.new(finalPos.X, 0, finalPos.Z) - Vector3.new(targetPos.X, 0, targetPos.Z)).Magnitude or 0
                        local score = (dist * 0.9) + (distToTgt * 0.75)

                        if score < bestScore then
                            bestScore = score
                            bestSpot = finalPos
                            bestAngleDeg = math.floor(math.deg(angleRad))
                            bestDist = dist
                        end
                        break -- เจอจุดปลอดภัยที่ใกล้ที่สุดในทิศนี้แล้ว
                    end
                end
                if dist >= maxDist then break end
            end
        end
    end

    -- Fallback สำรองหากวงแดงปิดเต็มทางเดิน: ถอยออกจากศูนย์กลางวงแดงโดยไม่ทะลุกำแพง
    if not bestSpot then
        local hz = hazards[1]
        local awayDir = Vector3.new(0, 0, 1)
        if hz and hz.center then
            local diff = Vector3.new(myPos.X - hz.center.X, 0, myPos.Z - hz.center.Z)
            if diff.Magnitude > 0.5 then awayDir = diff.Unit end
        end
        local wallHit = workspace:Raycast(myPos + Vector3.new(0, 2.5, 0), awayDir * 35, rayParams)
        local safeDist = wallHit and math.max(0, wallHit.Distance - 3.0) or 22
        if safeDist >= 5 then
            local candPos = myPos + (awayDir * safeDist)
            local floorHit = workspace:Raycast(candPos + Vector3.new(0, 8, 0), Vector3.new(0, -30, 0), rayParams)
            if floorHit then
                bestSpot = Vector3.new(candPos.X, floorHit.Position.Y + 2.5, candPos.Z)
                bestAngleDeg = math.floor(math.deg(math.atan2(awayDir.Z, awayDir.X)))
                bestDist = safeDist
            end
        end
        if not bestSpot then
            bestSpot = myPos
        end
    end

    return bestSpot, string.format("🏃 วิ่งหลบสกิล (%d° - %.0f st)", bestAngleDeg, bestDist), bestAngleDeg, bestDist
end

-- ยิงสกิลตามช่องที่เปิดใช้งานทันทีที่มีเป้าหมาย (HotbarJson + UseSkill Remote / LumiActionCast)
local function burstDungeonSkills(target)
    if not dngAutoSkill or not target then return end
    local Remotes = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("Remotes")
    local skillRem = Remotes and Remotes:FindFirstChild("UseSkill")
    local rawHotbar = lp:GetAttribute("HotbarJson")
    if type(rawHotbar) == "string" and rawHotbar ~= cachedHotbarStr then
        cachedHotbarStr = rawHotbar
        local ok, parsed = pcall(HttpService.JSONDecode, HttpService, rawHotbar)
        if ok and type(parsed) == "table" then
            cachedHotbarTbl = parsed
        end
    end

    local isActionMode = (lp:GetAttribute("ActionMode") == true) or (lp:GetAttribute("ControlMode") == "Action")
    local actionCast = lp:FindFirstChild("LumiActionCast") or lp:FindFirstChild("ActionCast")
    local pg = lp:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
    local hotbarGui = pg and (pg:FindFirstChild("LumiHotbar") or pg:FindFirstChild("Hotbar"))
    local slotCache = DngExt.slotBtnCache
    if not slotCache or DngExt.slotBtnGui ~= hotbarGui then
        slotCache = {}
        DngExt.slotBtnCache = slotCache
        DngExt.slotBtnGui = hotbarGui
    end
    local now = tick()

    for slot = 1, 8 do
        if isSkillSlotEnabled(slot) and (now - (lastSkillCastTime[slot] or 0)) >= 0.12 then
            local onCd = false
            local slotBtn = slotCache[slot]
            if not (slotBtn and slotBtn.Parent and hotbarGui and slotBtn:IsDescendantOf(hotbarGui)) then
                slotBtn = hotbarGui and (hotbarGui:FindFirstChild("Slot" .. slot, true) or hotbarGui:FindFirstChild("Skill" .. slot, true))
                slotCache[slot] = slotBtn
            end
            if slotBtn then
                local cdLabel = slotBtn:FindFirstChild("Cooldown")
                local shade = slotBtn:FindFirstChild("CooldownShade")
                local cdTxt = cdLabel and tostring(cdLabel.Text or "") or ""
                local cdNum = tonumber(cdTxt)
                if (cdNum and cdNum > 0.12) or (not cdNum and cdTxt ~= "") then
                    onCd = true
                elseif shade and shade.Visible and shade.Size.Y.Scale > 0.10 then
                    onCd = true
                end
            end

            if not onCd then
                local rawEntry = cachedHotbarTbl[tostring(slot)]
                local skillId = (type(rawEntry) == "string") and string.match(rawEntry, "^skill:(.+)$") or nil
                if not skillId then
                    -- Fallback เฉพาะ string skillId จาก BotJson.Skills[curJob] (ห้ามดึง boolean table!)
                    local curJob = getCurrentJobName()
                    local parsed = getSavedBotSettings()
                    local jobSkills = parsed.Skills and parsed.Skills[curJob]
                    if type(jobSkills) == "table" and type(jobSkills[slot]) == "string" and #jobSkills[slot] > 0 then
                        skillId = jobSkills[slot]
                    end
                end

                if type(skillId) == "string" and #skillId > 0 and now >= (DngExt.skillReadyAt[skillId] or 0)
                    and (not DngExt.rangedNow or DngExt.isLongRangeSkill(skillId)) then
                    lastSkillCastTime[slot] = now
                    pcall(function()
                        if isActionMode and actionCast and actionCast:IsA("BindableEvent") then
                            actionCast:Fire(skillId, target)
                        else
                            skillRem:FireServer(skillId, target)
                        end
                        if lp:GetAttribute("MoveLockUntil") and lp:GetAttribute("MoveLockUntil") > 0 then
                            lp:SetAttribute("MoveLockUntil", 0)
                        end
                        if lp:GetAttribute("HardLockUntil") and lp:GetAttribute("HardLockUntil") > 0 then
                            lp:SetAttribute("HardLockUntil", 0)
                        end
                    end)
                end
            end
        end
    end
end

-- ลิสต์ Checkpoints แยกตาม Objective Step จริงของดันเจี้ยน (Speedrun ข้ามมอนไม่จำเป็นทันทีเมื่อ Step ได้ ✓)
local DUNGEON_STEP_WAYPOINTS = {
    -- Step 1: Clear all monsters 0/5 (Pack 1–5 หน้า SealCorridor)
    [1] = {
        Vector3.new(-3842, 60, 214), -- Pack 1
        Vector3.new(-3842, 60, 150), -- Pack 2
        Vector3.new(-3842, 60, 118), -- Ambush 1
        Vector3.new(-3842, 60, 86),  -- Pack 3
        Vector3.new(-3842, 60, 18),  -- Pack 4
        Vector3.new(-3890, 60, -20), -- Pack 5
    },
    -- Step 2: Destroy the ritual pillars (พุ่งตรงเสา 3 ต้นในลาน Plaza ข้ามมอน Stage 1 ที่เหลือทั้งหมด)
    [2] = {
        Vector3.new(-3994, 60, -46), -- PillarSpot 1
        Vector3.new(-3971, 60, -7),  -- PillarSpot 2
        Vector3.new(-4017, 60, -7),  -- PillarSpot 3
        Vector3.new(-3994, 60, -20), -- Plaza Center
    },
    -- Step 3: Defeat the mini boss (เหยียบ Zone_Witch trigger ก่อนทันทีเพื่อให้แม่มดเกิด ไม่ต้องยืนรอ!)
    [3] = {
        Vector3.new(-4215, 60, -20), -- Zone_Witch trigger (สปอนมินิบอสทันที!)
        Vector3.new(-4236, 60, -20), -- WitchSpot
        Vector3.new(-4230, 60, -35), -- Witch sweep
    },
    -- Step 4: Defeat the boss (ข้าม Pack 9-11 เปิด BossDoor แล้วพุ่งเหยียบ Zone_Boss ตรง Skull King ทันที!)
    [4] = {
        Vector3.new(-4065, 60, -260), -- Boss room entrance trigger
        Vector3.new(-4010, 60, -260), -- Zone_Boss trigger
        Vector3.new(-3966, 60, -260), -- BossSpot (Skull King)
        Vector3.new(-3985, 60, -260), -- Boss room sweep
    },
    -- Step 5: Cleared (กล่องสมบัติทางออก)
    [5] = {
        Vector3.new(-4038, 60, -260), -- ExitChestSpot
    },
}
-- เก็บ DUNGEON_CHECKPOINTS เดิมไว้สำหรับ fallback กู้ชีพตกเหว
local DUNGEON_CHECKPOINTS = {
    Vector3.new(-3842, 60, 214),
    Vector3.new(-3994, 60, -20),
    Vector3.new(-4236, 60, -20),
    Vector3.new(-3966, 60, -260),
    Vector3.new(-4038, 60, -260),
}
local currentDungeonCheckpointIdx = 1
local lastDungeonStepSeen         = 0
local lastCheckpointAdvanceTick   = 0
local lastBossDoorTryTick         = 0
local CHECKPOINT_DWELL_SEC        = 0.25 -- สแกนไว 0.25 วิ แล้วพุ่งต่อทันที ไม่ยืนแช่นิ่ง!

-- อ่านเงื่อนไขผ่านดันเจี้ยนแบบ Real-Time จาก Server Attribute (workspace.DungeonStep / DungeonState / DungeonObjective)
local function getRealtimeDungeonStep()
    local dngId = tostring(workspace:GetAttribute("DungeonId") or ""):lower()
    local dngState = tostring(workspace:GetAttribute("DungeonState") or ""):lower()
    local objText = tostring(workspace:GetAttribute("DungeonObjective") or "")

    if dngId == "gold" then
        return 0, "gold", dngState, objText
    end
    if dngState == "cleared" then
        return 5, "halloween", dngState, objText
    end

    local srvStep = tonumber(workspace:GetAttribute("DungeonStep"))
    if srvStep and srvStep >= 1 and srvStep <= 5 then
        return math.floor(srvStep), "halloween", dngState, objText
    end

    -- Fallback จาก Barriers กรณี Attribute ยังไม่ replicate
    local hw = workspace:FindFirstChild("Halloween")
    local barriers = hw and hw:FindFirstChild("Barriers")
    if barriers then
        local sealCorridor = barriers:FindFirstChild("SealCorridor")
        if sealCorridor and sealCorridor:IsA("BasePart") and sealCorridor.CanCollide then
            return 1, "halloween", dngState, objText
        end
        local sealPlaza = barriers:FindFirstChild("SealPlaza")
        if sealPlaza and sealPlaza:IsA("BasePart") and sealPlaza.CanCollide then
            return 2, "halloween", dngState, objText
        end
        local sealWitch = barriers:FindFirstChild("SealWitch")
        if sealWitch and sealWitch:IsA("BasePart") and sealWitch.CanCollide then
            return 3, "halloween", dngState, objText
        end
    end
    return 4, "halloween", dngState, objText
end

local function getDungeonStageBounds()
    local step = getRealtimeDungeonStep()
    local idx = math.clamp(step, 1, #DUNGEON_CHECKPOINTS)
    return idx, idx
end

-- ฟังก์ชัน Teleport กลางสำหรับทุกการเคลื่อนที่ภายในดันเจี้ยน 100% (ปลอดภัยเฉพาะในดันเจี้ยน ห้ามใช้ข้างนอกเด็ดขาด!)
teleportDungeonTo = function(root, hum, destPos, lookPos)
    if not isPlayerInDungeon or not isPlayerInDungeon() then return false end
    if not root or not destPos then return false end
    lastDngTeleportTick = tick()
    if root:IsA("BasePart") then
        -- ล้างแรงผลัก Knockback จากบอสก่อนวางพิกัดใหม่ กันถูกดีดกระเด็นซ้ำหลังวาร์ป
        for _, child in ipairs(root:GetChildren()) do
            if child.Name ~= "GolfHub_AirHoverBV" and (child:IsA("BodyVelocity") or child:IsA("LinearVelocity") or child:IsA("VectorForce")) then
                pcall(function() child:Destroy() end)
            end
        end
        root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
        root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
    end
    if lookPos then
        local flatLook = Vector3.new(lookPos.X, destPos.Y, lookPos.Z)
        if (flatLook - destPos).Magnitude >= 0.25 then
            root.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(destPos, flatLook)
        else
            root.CFrame = CFrame.new(destPos)
        end
    else
        root.CFrame = CFrame.new(destPos)
    end
    if hum then
        pcall(function()
            if hum.Sit then hum.Sit = false end
            if hum:GetState() == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics or hum:GetState() == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll then
                hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running)
            end
            hum:MoveTo(destPos)
        end)
    end
    return true
end

-- ตรวจสอบและกดเปิดประตูหินหน้าห้องบอส (Halloween BossDoor) เมื่อถึงด่านบอส (Step 4)
local function tryOpenBossDoorIfNeeded(root, hum)
    if not (isPlayerInDungeon and isPlayerInDungeon()) then return false end
    if not root or not hum or hum.Health <= 0 then return false end
    local now = tick()
    if (now - lastBossDoorTryTick) < 0.65 then return false end

    local hw = workspace:FindFirstChild("Halloween")
    local bd = hw and hw:FindFirstChild("BossDoor", true)
    if not bd then return false end

    local prompt = bd:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("ProximityPrompt", true)
    local slab = bd:FindFirstChild("Slab", true)
    local isOpen = (prompt and not prompt.Enabled) or (slab and not slab.CanCollide)
    if isOpen then return false end

    -- Already inside the Skull King room (east of X -4062, the same room line findRetreatSpot uses) with the boss
    -- up: the door behind us does not matter. Seen live: DoorPrompt stayed Enabled during the whole boss fight, so
    -- every 0.65 s this teleported out to the door at X -4102 and section 6 teleported straight back to the boss
    -- (the "teleport flicker"), 19 jumps in 15 s.
    if root.Position.X > -4062 then
        local mf = workspace:FindFirstChild("Monsters")
        for _, m in ipairs(mf and mf:GetChildren() or {}) do
            local nm = tostring(m:GetAttribute("DisplayName") or m.Name)
            if nm:find("Skull King") and (tonumber(m:GetAttribute("HP")) or 1) > 0 then return false end
        end
    end

    if prompt and prompt.Enabled and prompt.Parent then
        lastBossDoorTryTick = now
        local pPos = getObjectSafePos(prompt.Parent) or (slab and slab.Position)
        if pPos then
            if lblDungeonStatus then
                pcall(function() lblDungeonStatus:Set("🚪 [เปิดประตูห้องบอส] Teleport กดเปิด BossDoor...", "run") end)
            end
            teleportDungeonTo(root, hum, pPos + Vector3.new(0, 1.8, 0), pPos)
            task.wait(0.03)
            if fireproximityprompt then
                pcall(fireproximityprompt, prompt)
            end
            pcall(function() prompt:InputHoldBegin() end)
            local vim = nil
            pcall(function() vim = game:GetService("VirtualInputManager") end)
            if vim then
                pcall(function() vim:SendKeyEvent(true, Enum.KeyCode.E, false, game) end)
                task.delay(0.08, function()
                    pcall(function() vim:SendKeyEvent(false, Enum.KeyCode.E, false, game) end)
                end)
            end
            task.wait(0.08)
            pcall(function() prompt:InputHoldEnd() end)
            return true
        end
    end
    return false
end

-- ระบบวาปแบบเป็นขั้นตอน (Stepped Corridor Teleport + Stream Preload) สำหรับระยะไกลในดันเจี้ยน
function DngExt.teleportPathTo(root, hum, destPos, lookPos)
    if not isPlayerInDungeon or not isPlayerInDungeon() then return false end
    if not root or not hum or not destPos then return false end

    local function snapFloorPos(p)
        local rp = RaycastParams.new()
        rp.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
        local fl = {}
        if lp.Character then table.insert(fl, lp.Character) end
        local hw = workspace:FindFirstChild("Halloween")
        if hw then
            local sp = hw:FindFirstChild("Spawns")
            if sp then table.insert(fl, sp) end
        end
        rp.FilterDescendantsInstances = fl
        local hit = workspace:Raycast(Vector3.new(p.X, p.Y + 8, p.Z), Vector3.new(0, -28, 0), rp)
        if hit then
            return Vector3.new(p.X, hit.Position.Y + 2.8, p.Z)
        end
        return Vector3.new(p.X, math.max(p.Y, 62.5), p.Z)
    end

    local startPos = root.Position
    local totalDist = (Vector3.new(destPos.X, 0, destPos.Z) - Vector3.new(startPos.X, 0, startPos.Z)).Magnitude

    -- ระยะใกล้ (<= 170 studs): Teleport ตรงทีเดียวทันที (สตรีมก่อนถ้าไกล > 90 studs)
    if totalDist <= 170 then
        if workspace.StreamingEnabled and totalDist > 90 then
            pcall(_G.GolfHub_StreamAround, destPos, 1.0)
        end
        return teleportDungeonTo(root, hum, snapFloorPos(destPos), lookPos)
    end

    -- ระยะไกล (> 170 studs เช่น เกิดใหม่หน้าทางเข้า Z≈214 แล้วต้องกลับห้องบอส Z≈-260)
    local hops = {}
    local _, dngKind = getRealtimeDungeonStep()
    if dngKind == "halloween" and startPos.Z > 40 and destPos.Z < -160 and destPos.X < -3920 then
        if startPos.Z > 110 then
            table.insert(hops, Vector3.new(-3842, 62.5, 86))
        end
        table.insert(hops, Vector3.new(-3890, 62.5, -20))
        table.insert(hops, Vector3.new(-3994, 62.5, -20))
        table.insert(hops, Vector3.new(-4140, 62.5, -20))
        table.insert(hops, Vector3.new(-4220, 62.5, -35))
        table.insert(hops, Vector3.new(-4225, 62.5, -145))
        table.insert(hops, Vector3.new(-4155, 62.5, -260))
        table.insert(hops, Vector3.new(-4065, 62.5, -260))
        table.insert(hops, destPos)
    elseif dngKind == "halloween" and startPos.Z > 40 and destPos.X < -4100 and destPos.Z >= -100 then
        if startPos.Z > 110 then
            table.insert(hops, Vector3.new(-3842, 62.5, 86))
        end
        table.insert(hops, Vector3.new(-3890, 62.5, -20))
        table.insert(hops, Vector3.new(-3994, 62.5, -20))
        table.insert(hops, Vector3.new(-4140, 62.5, -20))
        table.insert(hops, destPos)
    else
        local steps = math.max(2, math.ceil(totalDist / 140))
        for i = 1, steps do
            local alpha = i / steps
            local p = startPos:Lerp(destPos, alpha)
            table.insert(hops, Vector3.new(p.X, math.max(p.Y, 62.5), p.Z))
        end
    end

    for i, hopPos in ipairs(hops) do
        if not isPlayerInDungeon() or lp:GetAttribute("DungeonDowned") == true then return false end
        local curRoot, curHum = getCharRoot()
        if not curRoot or not curHum or curHum.Health <= 0 then return false end

        -- โหลดฉากและมอน/บอสรอบจุดหมายก่อนวาร์ปลง (ป้องกัน StreamingEnabled หลุดโหลดพื้น/บอสหาย)
        if workspace.StreamingEnabled then
            pcall(_G.GolfHub_StreamAround, hopPos, 1.2)
        end

        -- ถ้ากำลังจะเข้าห้องบอส (จุดก่อนประตู -4155, -260) และประตูยังปิดอยู่ ให้เปิดประตูก่อน
        if dngKind == "halloween" and hopPos.Z < -200 and hopPos.X > -4110 then
            if tryOpenBossDoorIfNeeded(curRoot, curHum) then
                return true
            end
        end

        local safeHop = snapFloorPos(hopPos)
        teleportDungeonTo(curRoot, curHum, safeHop, (i == #hops) and lookPos or nil)
        if i < #hops then
            task.wait(0.05)
        else
            task.wait(0.08)
        end
    end
    return true
end

local lastGoldSweepIdx = 0
local function advanceDungeonPath(root, hum)
    -- กฎเหล็ก: ป้องกันการเทเลพอร์ตทุกกรณีถ้าอยู่นอกดันเจี้ยน
    if not isPlayerInDungeon() then return end
    if not root or not hum then return end

    -- เคลียร์ Hover BV ชั่วคราวตอนเคลื่อนย้ายจุด เพื่อให้เหยียบพื้น Trigger สปอนมอน/บอสได้ 100%
    local bv = root:FindFirstChild("GolfHub_AirHoverBV")
    if bv then bv:Destroy() end

    local step, dngKind, dngState, objText = getRealtimeDungeonStep()

    -- Gold Dungeon: ใช้ระบบ Teleport กวาดทุกจุดเกิด/ถังทอง/กล่องใน GoldDungeon อัตโนมัติ
    if dngKind == "gold" then
        local gd = workspace:FindFirstChild("GoldDungeon")
        local spots = {}
        if gd then
            local spFolder = gd:FindFirstChild("Spawns") or gd
            for _, d in ipairs(spFolder:GetDescendants()) do
                if d:IsA("BasePart") and d.Position.X <= -3000 then
                    local dn = d.Name:lower()
                    if dn:find("spawn") or dn:find("barrel") or dn:find("gold") or dn:find("spot") or dn:find("zone") then
                        table.insert(spots, d.Position)
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        if #spots > 0 then
            lastGoldSweepIdx = (lastGoldSweepIdx % #spots) + 1
            local targetPos = spots[lastGoldSweepIdx]
            teleportDungeonTo(root, hum, Vector3.new(targetPos.X, targetPos.Y + 2.5, targetPos.Z))
            if lblDungeonStatus then
                pcall(function()
                    lblDungeonStatus:Set(string.format("💰 [Gold Dungeon] Teleport กวาดจุด %d/%d...", lastGoldSweepIdx, #spots), "run")
                end)
            end
        end
        return
    end

    -- ถ้า Step เปลี่ยน (ได้ติ๊กถูก ✓ ในเงื่อนไขเดิมแล้ว!) -> รีเซ็ต index แล้ว Teleport ข้ามลัดไป Step ใหม่ทันที!
    if step ~= lastDungeonStepSeen then
        lastDungeonStepSeen = step
        currentDungeonCheckpointIdx = 1
        lastCheckpointAdvanceTick = 0
    end

    -- Step 4: ถ้าประตูหินห้องบอสยังไม่เปิด ให้ Teleport ไปกดเปิดทันที
    if step == 4 then
        if tryOpenBossDoorIfNeeded(root, hum) then
            return
        end
    end

    -- Step 5 (Cleared): วาร์ปไปที่กล่องสมบัติ (<Player>'s Chest / Item Crate) กดค้าง 'E' เปิด และเก็บของดรอปทั้งหมดทันที!
    if step == 5 then
        local RemotesForChest = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("Remotes")
        local chestsNow = DngExt.findChests()
        if #chestsNow > 0 then
            local bestC, bestCD = nil, math.huge
            for _, c in ipairs(chestsNow) do
                local cd = (c.pos - root.Position).Magnitude
                if cd < bestCD then
                    bestCD = cd
                    bestC = c
                end
            end
            if bestC and DngExt.openChestAndCollectAll(root, hum, bestC, RemotesForChest) then
                return
            end
        end
        -- ถ้าเปิดกล่องไปแล้วแต่ยังมีของดรอปตกเหลืออยู่รอบๆ -> กดค้าง 'E' วาร์ปเก็บให้เกลี้ยง!
        local remDrop = findNearestDrop(150)
        if remDrop and remDrop.Position and remDrop.Position.X <= -3000 then
            local vim = nil
            pcall(function() vim = game:GetService("VirtualInputManager") end)
            DngExt.collectAllDrops(root, hum, remDrop.Position, RemotesForChest, 3.0, vim)
            return
        end
    end

    local waypoints = DUNGEON_STEP_WAYPOINTS[step] or DUNGEON_STEP_WAYPOINTS[1]
    if currentDungeonCheckpointIdx < 1 or currentDungeonCheckpointIdx > #waypoints then
        currentDungeonCheckpointIdx = 1
    end

    local myPos = root.Position
    local cp = waypoints[currentDungeonCheckpointIdx]
    local distToCp = cp and (Vector3.new(myPos.X, 0, myPos.Z) - Vector3.new(cp.X, 0, cp.Z)).Magnitude or 999

    -- ถ้ายืนถึงจุด Waypoint แล้ว รอ Dwell สั้นๆ (0.25s) โดยไม่กระตุกสุ่มซ้ายขวา แล้ว Teleport ไปจุดถัดไปทันที!
    if distToCp < 12 then
        if lastCheckpointAdvanceTick == 0 then
            lastCheckpointAdvanceTick = tick()
            return
        end
        if tick() - lastCheckpointAdvanceTick < CHECKPOINT_DWELL_SEC then
            if lblDungeonStatus then
                pcall(function()
                    lblDungeonStatus:Set(string.format("🔎 [Step %d: %s] Teleport สแกนจุด %d/%d...", step, objText ~= "" and objText or "Objective", currentDungeonCheckpointIdx, #waypoints), "run")
                end)
            end
            return
        end
        currentDungeonCheckpointIdx = (currentDungeonCheckpointIdx % #waypoints) + 1
        lastCheckpointAdvanceTick = tick()
    end

    local nextCp = waypoints[currentDungeonCheckpointIdx]
    if not nextCp then return end

    local stepLabels = {
        [1] = "Step 1: เคลียร์มอน 0/5",
        [2] = "Step 2: ข้ามลัดตีเสาพิธีกรรม 3 ต้น",
        [3] = "Step 3: ข้ามลัดล่ามินิบอส Witch",
        [4] = "Step 4: ข้ามลัดล่าบอสใหญ่ Skull King",
        [5] = "Step 5: เคลียร์ครบแล้ว เก็บกล่องออก",
    }
    if lblDungeonStatus then
        pcall(function()
            lblDungeonStatus:Set(string.format("⚡ [%s] Teleport จุด %d/%d...", stepLabels[step] or ("Step " .. step), currentDungeonCheckpointIdx, #waypoints), "run")
        end)
    end

    -- ใช้ระบบ Teleport เต็มรูปแบบ 100% ภายในดันเจี้ยน (รองรับการสตรีมฉากและแบ่งช่วงเมื่อข้ามห้องไกล เช่น เกิดใหม่หน้าทางเข้า)
    DngExt.teleportPathTo(root, hum, nextCp)
    lastCheckpointAdvanceTick = tick()
end

-- ฟังก์ชันตรวจสอบประเภทมอนสเตอร์สำหรับ Speedrun Objective Filtering
local dungeonTargetBlacklist = {} -- [model] = expireTick (กันค้างลอยข้างมอนที่ตีไม่เข้า/บั๊ก)
local attemptedDungeonLoot   = {} -- [obj] = { tries, skipUntil } (กันค้าง Teleport วนเก็บดรอป/กล่องเดิมซ้ำ)

local function isMonsterAliveObj(m)
    if not m or not m.Parent then return false end
    if m:GetAttribute("AnimDead") == true then return false end
    if m:GetAttribute("IsDummy") == true then return false end
    local hp = m:GetAttribute("HP")
    if hp == nil then hp = m:GetAttribute("Health") end
    local hum = m:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if hp ~= nil then return hp > 0 end
    if hum ~= nil then return hum.Health > 0 end
    return true
end

local function isTargetBlacklisted(m)
    local exp = dungeonTargetBlacklist[m]
    if not exp then return false end
    if tick() >= exp then
        dungeonTargetBlacklist[m] = nil
        return false
    end
    return true
end

local function isPillarObj(m)
    local name = (m:GetAttribute("DisplayName") or m.Name):lower()
    local mId = tostring(m:GetAttribute("MonsterId") or ""):lower()
    return (name:find("pillar") or name:find("totem") or name:find("เสา") or mId:find("pillar") or mId:find("totem")) and not name:find("brazier")
end

local function isMiniBossObj(m)
    local name = (m:GetAttribute("DisplayName") or m.Name):lower()
    local mId = tostring(m:GetAttribute("MonsterId") or ""):lower()
    -- ข้ามร่างปลอม Vanish (dgn_witch_clone / Clone = true) ของ Potion Witch เพื่อโฟกัสตีตัวจริง 100%
    if m:GetAttribute("Clone") == true or mId:find("clone") or name:find("clone") then return false end
    if m:GetAttribute("IsMiniBoss") == true then return true end
    return name:find("witch") or mId:find("witch") or name:find("แม่มด")
end

local function isFinalBossObj(m)
    if m:GetAttribute("IsBoss") == true then return true end
    local name = (m:GetAttribute("DisplayName") or m.Name):lower()
    local mId = tostring(m:GetAttribute("MonsterId") or ""):lower()
    return name:find("skull king") or mId:find("crypt_boss") or name:find("ราชัน")
end

-- ฟังก์ชันค้นหาเป้าหมายแบบ Real-Time Objective Speedrun (ล็อกตรงเงื่อนไข Step ปัจจุบัน ข้ามมอนไม่จำเป็น 100%)
local function getDungeonTarget()
    local root = getCharRoot()
    if not root then return nil, 99999, false end
    local myPos = root.Position
    local step, dngKind = getRealtimeDungeonStep()

    -- กรณีเปลี่ยน Step (ได้ติ๊กถูก ✓ แล้ว) รีเซ็ต Checkpoint ทันที
    if dngKind == "halloween" and step ~= lastDungeonStepSeen then
        lastDungeonStepSeen = step
        currentDungeonCheckpointIdx = 1
        lastCheckpointAdvanceTick = 0
    end

    -- เก็บมอนสเตอร์ที่มีชีวิตทั้งหมดในดันเจี้ยนรอบเดียว O(N)
    local aliveList = {}
    local seen = {}
    local function addCandidate(m)
        if not m or seen[m] or m == lp.Character then return end
        if not (m:IsA("Model") or m:IsA("BasePart")) then return end
        if m.Name:find("Dummy") or m.Name:find("Pet") then return end
        if not isMonsterAliveObj(m) then return end
        local pPos = getObjectSafePos(m)
        if not pPos then return end
        if dngKind == "halloween" and pPos.X > -3500 then return end
        local dist = (pPos - myPos).Magnitude
        if dist < 1200 then
            seen[m] = true
            table.insert(aliveList, { obj = m, pos = pPos, dist = dist })
        end
    end

    local monFolder = workspace:FindFirstChild("Monsters")
    if monFolder then
        for _, m in ipairs(monFolder:GetChildren()) do addCandidate(m) end
    end
    local cs = cloneref and cloneref(game:GetService("CollectionService")) or game:GetService("CollectionService")
    for _, tag in ipairs({"Pillar", "Totem", "Monster"}) do
        for _, obj in ipairs(cs:GetTagged(tag)) do addCandidate(obj) end
    end

    -- ==================== HALLOWEEN SPEEDRUN OBJECTIVE FILTER ====================
    if dngKind == "halloween" then
        -- STEP 5: เคลียร์ครบแล้ว ไม่ต้องเสียเวลาตีมอน
        if step >= 5 then
            return nil, 99999, false
        end

        -- STEP 4: ล้มบอสใหญ่ (Skull King) — ถ้ามีเสา Seal (BossSealPillars ตอนเลือด 50%) ต้องตีเสาก่อนไม่งั้นบอสอมตะ (SealShield = true)!
        if step == 4 then
            local bestPillar, bestPD = nil, math.huge
            local fbPillar, fbPD = nil, math.huge
            for _, item in ipairs(aliveList) do
                if isPillarObj(item.obj) then
                    if not isTargetBlacklisted(item.obj) and item.dist < bestPD then
                        bestPD = item.dist
                        bestPillar = item.obj
                    elseif item.dist < fbPD then
                        fbPD = item.dist
                        fbPillar = item.obj
                    end
                end
            end
            if bestPillar then
                return bestPillar, bestPD, true
            end
            if fbPillar then
                return fbPillar, fbPD, true
            end

            local bestBoss, bestD = nil, math.huge
            local fallbackBoss, fallbackD = nil, math.huge
            for _, item in ipairs(aliveList) do
                if isFinalBossObj(item.obj) then
                    if item.obj:GetAttribute("SealShield") ~= true and not isTargetBlacklisted(item.obj) then
                        if item.dist < bestD then
                            bestD = item.dist
                            bestBoss = item.obj
                        end
                    elseif item.dist < fallbackD then
                        fallbackD = item.dist
                        fallbackBoss = item.obj
                    end
                end
            end
            if bestBoss then return bestBoss, bestD, false end
            return fallbackBoss, fallbackD, false
        end

        -- STEP 3: ล้มมินิบอส (Potion Witch) — ตี Witch เท่านั้น ข้าม Pack 6-8 และลูกน้องทั้งหมด!
        if step == 3 then
            local bestMini, bestD = nil, math.huge
            for _, item in ipairs(aliveList) do
                if isMiniBossObj(item.obj) and item.obj:GetAttribute("SealShield") ~= true and item.dist < bestD then
                    bestD = item.dist
                    bestMini = item.obj
                end
            end
            -- The Witch's summons used to be ignored completely. When she broom-dashed or vanished away ("Target is
            -- too far away") the character chased her while the Scarecrows / Bone Hounds / Skull Bats around it hit it
            -- unanswered — He kept dying here (Pahtzx). Hit the nearest summon first when she is out of reach, when
            -- 2+ of them are on us at < 70 % HP, or when 3+ are on us; then back to her. Her Vanish clones are not
            -- summons (isMiniBossObj already skips them; they are skipped here too).
            -- Before she spawns: clear the room's pack first, THEN step on Zone_Witch. The route used to run straight
            -- onto the trigger ("skip Pack 6-8"), so she spawned in the middle of an uncleared pack, and the pack is
            -- what downed us (8 of 11 downs). Clearing the pack while she was up was tried and kept us in Cauldron
            -- Splash's cone; with her not spawned yet there is no cone to stand in. Room = within 60 studs of WitchSpot.
            if not bestMini then
                local roomC = Vector3.new(-4228, 60, -25)
                local preMob, preD = nil, math.huge
                for _, item in ipairs(aliveList) do
                    local o = item.obj
                    if (item.pos - roomC).Magnitude <= 60 and not isMiniBossObj(o) and not isFinalBossObj(o)
                        and not isPillarObj(o) and not isTargetBlacklisted(o) and o:GetAttribute("Clone") ~= true
                        and not tostring(o:GetAttribute("MonsterId") or ""):lower():find("clone", 1, true)
                        and item.dist < preD then
                        preD, preMob = item.dist, o
                    end
                end
                if preMob then
                    _G.GolfHub_WitchPreClearAt = tick() -- read by live checks
                    return preMob, preD, false
                end
            end
            -- Summons / pack mobs on us are hit first only when they really crowd us; otherwise her.
            -- A "clear every pack mob within 30 studs of her first" rule was tried (2026-11-25): pack downs fell (8 in
            -- 11 runs -> 2 in 6) but Cauldron Splash downs rose (0 -> 5; fighting next to her keeps us in her cone)
            -- and runs took 813 s instead of 411 s. Reverted to this rule.
            local hum3 = lp.Character and lp.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            local hp3 = (hum3 and hum3.MaxHealth > 0) and (hum3.Health / hum3.MaxHealth) or 1
            local nearAdd, nearAddD, nearCount = nil, math.huge, 0
            for _, item in ipairs(aliveList) do
                local o = item.obj
                if item.dist <= 14 and not isMiniBossObj(o) and not isFinalBossObj(o) and not isPillarObj(o)
                    and not isTargetBlacklisted(o)
                    and o:GetAttribute("SealShield") ~= true and o:GetAttribute("Clone") ~= true
                    and not tostring(o:GetAttribute("MonsterId") or ""):lower():find("clone", 1, true) then
                    nearCount = nearCount + 1
                    if item.dist < nearAddD then nearAddD, nearAdd = item.dist, o end
                end
            end
            if nearAdd and (bestMini == nil or bestD > 20 or (nearCount >= 2 and hp3 < 0.7) or nearCount >= 3) then
                _G.GolfHub_WitchAddsAt = tick() -- read by live checks
                return nearAdd, nearAddD, false
            end
            return bestMini, bestD, false
        end

        -- STEP 2: ทำลายเสาพิธีกรรม 3 ต้น — โฟกัสตีเสาเท่านั้น ข้ามมอนลาน Plaza ทั้งหมด!
        if step == 2 then
            local bestPillar, bestD = nil, math.huge
            for _, item in ipairs(aliveList) do
                if isPillarObj(item.obj) and not isTargetBlacklisted(item.obj) and item.dist < bestD then
                    bestD = item.dist
                    bestPillar = item.obj
                end
            end
            if bestPillar then
                return bestPillar, bestD, true
            end
            -- เช็คเพิ่มเติมใน workspace.Halloween เผื่อเสาไม่ได้อยู่ใน Monsters
            local hw = workspace:FindFirstChild("Halloween")
            if hw then
                for _, c in ipairs(hw:GetChildren()) do
                    if (c:IsA("Model") or c:IsA("BasePart")) and isPillarObj(c) and isMonsterAliveObj(c) and not isTargetBlacklisted(c) then
                        local pPos = getObjectSafePos(c)
                        if pPos then
                            local d = (pPos - myPos).Magnitude
                            if d < bestD then bestD = d bestPillar = c end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
            return bestPillar, bestD, (bestPillar ~= nil)
        end

        -- STEP 1: กำจัดมอนสเตอร์ทั้งหมด 0/5 — โฟกัสเฉพาะมอนในโซน Stage 1 (X > -3945) ก่อน SealCorridor!
        if step == 1 then
            local bestMob, bestWeight = nil, math.huge
            local fallbackMob, fallbackWeight = nil, math.huge
            for _, item in ipairs(aliveList) do
                if item.pos.X > -3945 and not isPillarObj(item.obj) and item.obj:GetAttribute("SealShield") ~= true then
                    local mName = (item.obj:GetAttribute("DisplayName") or item.obj.Name):lower()
                    local w = item.dist + (mName:find("bat") and 25 or 0)
                    if not isTargetBlacklisted(item.obj) then
                        if w < bestWeight then
                            bestWeight = w
                            bestMob = item.obj
                        end
                    elseif w < fallbackWeight then
                        fallbackWeight = w
                        fallbackMob = item.obj
                    end
                end
            end
            if bestMob then return bestMob, bestWeight, false end
            return fallbackMob, fallbackWeight, false
        end
    end

    -- ==================== DEFAULT / GOLD DUNGEON: ตีมอนที่ใกล้ที่สุด ====================
    local bestTarget, bestWeight, bestIsPillar = nil, math.huge, false
    local fbTarget, fbWeight, fbIsPillar = nil, math.huge, false
    for _, item in ipairs(aliveList) do
        if item.obj:GetAttribute("SealShield") ~= true then
            local isP = isPillarObj(item.obj)
            local mName = (item.obj:GetAttribute("DisplayName") or item.obj.Name):lower()
            local w = item.dist + (mName:find("bat") and 25 or 0) - (dngPillarPriority and isP and 500 or 0)
            if not isTargetBlacklisted(item.obj) then
                if w < bestWeight then
                    bestWeight = w
                    bestTarget = item.obj
                    bestIsPillar = isP
                end
            elseif w < fbWeight then
                fbWeight = w
                fbTarget = item.obj
                fbIsPillar = isP
            end
        end
    end
    if bestTarget then return bestTarget, bestWeight, bestIsPillar end
    return fbTarget, fbWeight, fbIsPillar
end


local lastDngClearCheck       = 0
local lastDngSetTargetObj     = nil
local lastDngSetTargetTick    = 0
local lastDngUnsitTick        = 0
local lastDngBotNormalFixTick = 0
local dngLockedStandTarget    = nil
local dngLockedStandDir       = Vector3.new(0, 0, 1)
local lastBossBackAlignTick   = 0

-- Active Zero-Damage / Stall Watchdog State
local dngWatchTarget          = nil
local dngWatchHp              = nil
local dngWatchSince           = 0
local dngWatchRecoveryStage   = 0
local dngForceGroundUntil     = 0
local dngLastTooFarTick       = 0
local dngLastRealHitTick      = 0
local dngWatchdogConns        = {}

local function getPlayerMeleeAttackRange()
    local ok, val = pcall(function()
        local sh = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("Shared")
        local cr = sh and sh:FindFirstChild("ClassRange")
        if cr then
            return require(cr).Attack(lp)
        end
        return nil
    end)
    if ok and type(val) == "number" and val > 0 then
        return val
    end
    return tonumber(lp:GetAttribute("AttackRange")) or 7
end

-- ติดตั้ง Listener น้ำหนักเบาสำหรับตรวจจับ "Target is too far away" และดาเมจจริงในดันเจี้ยน
pcall(function()
    if _G.GolfHub_DngWatchdogConns then
        for _, c in ipairs(_G.GolfHub_DngWatchdogConns) do
            pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end)
        end
    end
    _G.GolfHub_DngWatchdogConns = {}
    dngWatchdogConns = _G.GolfHub_DngWatchdogConns
    local Remotes = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("Remotes")
    if not Remotes then return end
    local notifyRem = Remotes:FindFirstChild("Notify")
    if notifyRem and notifyRem:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
        table.insert(dngWatchdogConns, notifyRem.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(msg)
            if not dungeonFarmActive then return end
            if type(msg) == "string" and msg:lower():find("too far") then
                dngLastTooFarTick = tick()
            end
        end))
    end
    local atkRem = Remotes:FindFirstChild("AttackAnim")
    if atkRem and atkRem:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
        table.insert(dngWatchdogConns, atkRem.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(who)
            if not dungeonFarmActive then return end
            if tostring(who) == lp.Name then
                dngLastRealHitTick = tick()
            end
        end))
    end
    local cfxRem = Remotes:FindFirstChild("CombatFx")
    if cfxRem and cfxRem:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
        table.insert(dngWatchdogConns, cfxRem.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(a1, _, a3)
            if not dungeonFarmActive then return end
            if a1 == "__batch" or a3 == "damage" or a3 == "crit" or a3 == "magic" or a3 == "double" then
                dngLastRealHitTick = tick()
            end
        end))
    end
end)

-- ตรวจ Line-of-Sight กับ DungeonLosWall (Workspace.Halloween.Wall) เพื่อไม่ให้ลอยทะลุหรือหลังกำแพงจนเซิร์ฟบล็อกการโจมตี
local function hasDungeonLineOfSight(fromPos, toPos)
    local cs = cloneref and cloneref(game:GetService("CollectionService")) or game:GetService("CollectionService")
    local losWalls = cs:GetTagged("DungeonLosWall")
    if not losWalls or #losWalls == 0 then return true end
    local diff = toPos - fromPos
    if diff.Magnitude < 0.8 then return true end
    local rp = RaycastParams.new()
    rp.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Include
    rp.FilterDescendantsInstances = losWalls
    return workspace:Raycast(fromPos, diff, rp) == nil
end

-- คำนวณจุดยืน/ลอยที่ปลอดภัยในระยะโจมตีจริง: ไม่สั่น 180° สลับฝั่ง, ไม่ทะลุกำแพง LOS, ความสูงไม่หลุดระยะตี
-- Line aim (He asked: "find the angle where hitting the minions goes through to the target"): Quick Draw is a line
-- wave from us toward the target, 50 studs wide. Standing on the far side of the target from the mobs around it puts
-- them behind the target, inside the same wave. Returns the stand direction (from the target, away from the mobs'
-- centre) when 2+ mobs are within 30 studs of the target, else nil.
function DngExt.lineAimDir(target, tPos)
    local mf = workspace:FindFirstChild("Monsters")
    local sx, sz, n = 0, 0, 0
    for _, m in ipairs(mf and mf:GetChildren() or {}) do
        if m ~= target and m:IsA("Model") and m:GetAttribute("Clone") ~= true and not isPillarObj(m)
            and not isFinalBossObj(m) and isMonsterAliveObj(m) then
            local p = getObjectSafePos(m)
            if p and Vector3.new(p.X - tPos.X, 0, p.Z - tPos.Z).Magnitude <= 30 then
                sx, sz, n = sx + p.X, sz + p.Z, n + 1
            end
        end
    end
    if n < 2 then return nil end
    local away = Vector3.new(tPos.X - sx / n, 0, tPos.Z - sz / n)
    if away.Magnitude < 1 then return nil end
    return away.Unit
end

function DngExt.getStandRaycastParams()
    local now = tick()
    local char = lp.Character
    if DngExt._standRp and DngExt._standWallRp and DngExt._standRpChar == char and (now - (DngExt._standRpAt or 0)) < 1.5 then
        return DngExt._standRp, DngExt._standWallRp, DngExt._standLosCount or 0
    end
    local cs = cloneref and cloneref(game:GetService("CollectionService")) or game:GetService("CollectionService")
    local rayParams = RaycastParams.new()
    rayParams.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
    local filterList = {}
    if char then table.insert(filterList, char) end
    local bFx = workspace:FindFirstChild("LumiBossFx")
    if bFx then table.insert(filterList, bFx) end
    local bSFx = workspace:FindFirstChild("LumiBossSkillFx")
    if bSFx then table.insert(filterList, bSFx) end
    local monF = workspace:FindFirstChild("Monsters")
    if monF then table.insert(filterList, monF) end
    local hw = workspace:FindFirstChild("Halloween")
    if hw then
        local sp = hw:FindFirstChild("Spawns")
        if sp then table.insert(filterList, sp) end
        local at = hw:FindFirstChild("Atmos")
        if at then table.insert(filterList, at) end
    end
    rayParams.FilterDescendantsInstances = filterList
    local losWalls = cs:GetTagged("DungeonLosWall")
    local wallRp = RaycastParams.new()
    wallRp.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Include
    wallRp.FilterDescendantsInstances = losWalls
    DngExt._standRp = rayParams
    DngExt._standWallRp = wallRp
    DngExt._standLosCount = #losWalls
    DngExt._standRpChar = char
    DngExt._standRpAt = now
    return rayParams, wallRp, #losWalls
end

local function findOptimalDungeonStandPos(root, target, tPos, forceGround, rotateStepDeg, overrideDist)
    local rayParams, wallRp, losWallCount = DngExt.getStandRaycastParams()

    -- 1. หาพื้นจริงใต้ตัวมอนสเตอร์เป็นฐานอ้างอิง (กัน floorRay ไปชนหัวเสา/ขอบกำแพงจนลอยสูงหลุดระยะ)
    local mobFloorRay = workspace:Raycast(Vector3.new(tPos.X, tPos.Y + 4, tPos.Z), Vector3.new(0, -30, 0), rayParams)
    local mobFloorY = mobFloorRay and mobFloorRay.Position.Y or (tPos.Y - 3.0)

    -- 2. คำนวณระยะแนวนอนและแนวตั้งให้การันตีอยู่ใน AttackRange + MobRadius 100%
    local atkRange = getPlayerMeleeAttackRange()
    local mobRadius = tonumber(target:GetAttribute("Radius")) or 1.5
    local maxSafe3D = math.max(3.6, (atkRange + mobRadius) - 1.8)

    local useHover = (dngAirHoverBackstab == true) and (not forceGround)
    local combatDist
    if type(overrideDist) == "number" and overrideDist > 0 then
        combatDist = math.clamp(overrideDist, 2.2, math.max(11.5, maxSafe3D))
    elseif forceGround then
        combatDist = math.clamp(math.min(dngAttackDistance or 3.5, 2.8), 2.0, math.min(3.2, maxSafe3D * 0.65))
    elseif useHover then
        combatDist = math.clamp(dngAttackDistance or 3.5, 2.5, math.min(4.0, maxSafe3D * 0.60))
    else
        combatDist = math.clamp(dngAttackDistance or 3.5, 2.5, math.min(4.2, maxSafe3D * 0.70))
    end

    local standY
    if useHover then
        local hoverOff = math.clamp(dngHoverHeight or 4.5, 2.0, 6.2)
        -- จำกัดไม่ให้ระยะ 3D (sqrt(combatDist^2 + dy^2)) เกิน maxSafe3D
        local maxAllowedDy = math.sqrt(math.max(1.0, (maxSafe3D * maxSafe3D) - (combatDist * combatDist)))
        local rawY = mobFloorY + hoverOff
        local dy = rawY - tPos.Y
        if math.abs(dy) > maxAllowedDy then
            rawY = tPos.Y + (dy > 0 and maxAllowedDy or -maxAllowedDy)
            hoverOff = math.max(2.0, rawY - mobFloorY)
        end
        standY = mobFloorY + hoverOff
    else
        -- โหมดยืนพื้น: ต้องสูงจากพื้น +2.8 studs (สูงระดับ HumanoidRootPart ปกติ ไม่จมลงดิน!)
        standY = mobFloorY + 2.8
    end

    -- 3. ล็อกทิศทางด้านหลังเป้าหมาย (สำหรับบอส Skull King ที่มี BackWeak 1.5x จะค่อยๆ อ้อมหลังใหม่เมื่อบอสหันเกิน 35° เพื่อตีแรงขึ้น +50% และหลบ Flame Slam อัตโนมัติ)
    local isBossBackWeak = isFinalBossObj(target)
    local tLook = (target:IsA("Model") and target.PrimaryPart and target.PrimaryPart.CFrame.LookVector)
        or (target:IsA("BasePart") and target.CFrame.LookVector)
    local backDir2D = nil
    if tLook then
        local b2d = Vector3.new(-tLook.X, 0, -tLook.Z)
        if b2d.Magnitude > 0.1 then backDir2D = b2d.Unit end
    end

    if dngLockedStandTarget ~= target or not dngLockedStandDir or dngLockedStandDir.Magnitude < 0.1 then
        dngLockedStandTarget = target
        -- the boss keeps his back-first rule (BackWeak); pillars, the Witch and mobs get the line-aim side
        local prefDir = (not isBossBackWeak and DngExt.lineAimDir(target, tPos)) or backDir2D
        if not prefDir then
            local diff = Vector3.new(root.Position.X - tPos.X, 0, root.Position.Z - tPos.Z)
            prefDir = diff.Magnitude > 0.2 and diff.Unit or Vector3.new(0, 0, 1)
        end
        dngLockedStandDir = prefDir
    elseif rotateStepDeg and rotateStepDeg ~= 0 then
        local rad = math.rad(rotateStepDeg)
        local c, s = math.cos(rad), math.sin(rad)
        local d = dngLockedStandDir
        dngLockedStandDir = Vector3.new(d.X * c - d.Z * s, 0, d.X * s + d.Z * c).Unit
    elseif isBossBackWeak and backDir2D and (tick() - (lastBossBackAlignTick or 0) >= 2.0) then
        -- Re-circle behind the boss (BackWeak 1.5x) only when he has turned well past us (> ~70°) and at most every
        -- 2 s. The boss keeps turning to face his target, so the old 35° / 0.35 s rule teleported us around him
        -- ~1.5 times a second: it looked confused and kept interrupting the attack.
        -- Measured on Pahtzx (Skull King): the bonus only applies within ~45° of his back (median hit 58.7k there
        -- vs ~40k at 45-90° and in front), but he snaps to face his target when he attacks (turns up to ~1550°/s;
        -- a stint behind him lasts ~0.55 s median). A 45° trigger was tried: more jumps (50 in 60 s) and no more
        -- time behind him (31 %). So 70° stays; the back-first pocket search below is what keeps us behind.
        if dngLockedStandDir:Dot(backDir2D) < 0.34 then
            lastBossBackAlignTick = tick()
            dngLockedStandDir = backDir2D
        end
    elseif not isBossBackWeak and tick() - (DngExt.lineAimAt or 0) >= 1.5 then
        -- the mobs move: re-aim every 1.5 s, and only when the side is more than ~30° off (no hopping)
        DngExt.lineAimAt = tick()
        local d = DngExt.lineAimDir(target, tPos)
        if d and dngLockedStandDir:Dot(d) < 0.87 then
            dngLockedStandDir = d
            DngExt.lineAimMoves = (DngExt.lineAimMoves or 0) + 1 -- read by live checks
        end
    end

    -- 4. ทดสอบมุมรอบตัวมอนเพื่อหาจุดที่: (a) ไม่มี DungeonLosWall กั้น (b) มีพื้นรองรับจริง (c) ไม่ชนเพดาน
    local angleCandidates = { 0, 45, -45, 90, -90, 135, -135, 180 }

    local baseDir = dngLockedStandDir
    for _, deg in ipairs(angleCandidates) do
        local rad = math.rad(deg)
        local c, s = math.cos(rad), math.sin(rad)
        local dir = Vector3.new(baseDir.X * c - baseDir.Z * s, 0, baseDir.X * s + baseDir.Z * c).Unit
        local cx = tPos.X + (dir.X * combatDist)
        local cz = tPos.Z + (dir.Z * combatDist)

        -- ตรวจว่าจากมอนออกไปยังจุดยืนไม่ทะลุกำแพง DungeonLosWall
        local clearWall = true
        if losWallCount > 0 then
            local checkFrom = Vector3.new(tPos.X, mobFloorY + 3.0, tPos.Z)
            local checkVec = Vector3.new(cx - tPos.X, 0, cz - tPos.Z)
            if checkVec.Magnitude > 0.5 then
                local wHit = workspace:Raycast(checkFrom, checkVec.Unit * (checkVec.Magnitude + 1.2), wallRp)
                if wHit then clearWall = false end
            end
        end

        if clearWall then
            local candFloorRay = workspace:Raycast(Vector3.new(cx, mobFloorY + 6.0, cz), Vector3.new(0, -20, 0), rayParams)
            if candFloorRay and math.abs(candFloorRay.Position.Y - mobFloorY) <= 4.5 then
                local finalY = standY
                if useHover then
                    local ceilRay = workspace:Raycast(Vector3.new(cx, candFloorRay.Position.Y + 1.5, cz), Vector3.new(0, (finalY - candFloorRay.Position.Y) + 2.5, 0), rayParams)
                    if ceilRay then
                        finalY = math.max(candFloorRay.Position.Y + 2.8, candFloorRay.Position.Y + ceilRay.Distance - 1.5)
                    end
                else
                    finalY = candFloorRay.Position.Y + 2.8
                end
                local candPos = Vector3.new(cx, finalY, cz)
                if hasDungeonLineOfSight(candPos, tPos) then
                    if deg ~= 0 then
                        dngLockedStandDir = dir
                    end
                    return candPos, useHover
                end
            end
        end
    end

    -- Fallback ปลอดภัยสุด: ชิดตัวมอนสเตอร์ตรงตำแหน่งที่มอนยืนอยู่ (การันตี LOS 100% และมีพื้น 100%)
    return Vector3.new(tPos.X, useHover and standY or (mobFloorY + 2.8), tPos.Z), useHover
end

local function isCurrentDungeonExpired()
    -- ห้ามเช็คหมดเวลาจนกว่าจะอยู่ในเซิร์ฟเวอร์ดันเจี้ยนจริง และตัวละครถูกส่งเข้าสนามดันเจี้ยน (X <= -3000) แล้ว
    if workspace:GetAttribute("PlaceRole") ~= "Dungeon" then
        return false, nil
    end
    local root = getCharRoot and getCharRoot()
    if not root or root.Position.X > -3000 then
        return false, nil
    end
    local dngState = workspace:GetAttribute("DungeonState")
    local endsAt = tonumber(workspace:GetAttribute("DungeonEndsAt"))
    local serverNow = workspace:GetServerTimeNow()
    -- เช็คเหมือน DungeonTimeoutClient ของเกม: เช็คเฉพาะตอน DungeonState == "running" และ endsAt เป็น Unix timestamp จริง (> 1e9 ไม่ใช่ 0)
    -- เผื่อเวลา 15 วินาทีหลังหมดเวลา เพื่อให้จับเฉพาะห้องที่ค้างจริงหลังหมดเวลาเท่านั้น (ไม่เด้งออกตอนเพิ่งเข้าหรือตอนเคลียร์บอส)
    if dngState == "running" and endsAt and endsAt > 1000000000 and serverNow and serverNow > (endsAt + 15) then
        return true, "timeout"
    end
    return false, nil
end

local lastDngNativeAutoTryTick = 0
local dngNativeSyncedThisRun   = false

-- เปิดปุ่ม [F] AUTO หลักของตัวเกมส์ (LumiAuto.AutoButton / AutoOn = true) ค้างไว้ตลอดเวลาเมื่อเข้ามาในดันเจี้ยน!
-- ใช้การยิงสัญญาณเดี่ยวแบบไม่ชนกัน (กัน Double-Toggle ปิดกลับเป็น OFF!) + เปิด RespawnKeepBot ให้ AUTO ค้างไว้ตอนเกิดใหม่ด้วย
local function ensureNativeDungeonAttackOn(Remotes)
    if not (isPlayerInDungeon and isPlayerInDungeon()) then return end
    if lp:GetAttribute("DungeonDowned") == true then return end
    local root, hum = getCharRoot()
    if not root or not hum or hum.Health <= 0 then return end

    local now = tick()

    -- คงสถานะ RespawnKeepBot = true ตลอดเวลาที่อยู่ในดันเจี้ยน เพื่อให้ CombatClient ไม่ปิดปุ่ม [F] AUTO ตอนตัวละครตาย/เกิดใหม่
    if lp:GetAttribute("RespawnKeepBot") ~= true then
        pcall(function() lp:SetAttribute("RespawnKeepBot", true) end)
    end

    -- 1. ซิงค์ BotJson (NormalAttack = true, UseSkills = dngAutoSkill, SkillOff ตรงกับช่อง 1-5) ทันทีเมื่อเข้าดัน และเช็คซ้ำทุก 10 วิ
    if (not dngNativeSyncedThisRun) or (now - lastDngBotNormalFixTick >= 10) then
        dngNativeSyncedThisRun = true
        lastDngBotNormalFixTick = now
        syncSkillSelectionToBotJson(dngAutoSkill == true)
    end

    -- 2. เปิดปุ่ม [F] AUTO ของตัวเกมส์ (LumiAuto.AutoButton -> AutoOn = true) แบบยิงครั้งเดียวต่อรอบ (กัน Double-Toggle 100%)
    if lp:GetAttribute("AutoOn") ~= true and (now - lastDngNativeAutoTryTick >= 0.45) then
        lastDngNativeAutoTryTick = now
        pcall(function()
            local menuToggle = lp:FindFirstChild("LumiMenuToggle")
            if menuToggle and menuToggle:IsA("BindableEvent") then
                menuToggle:Fire("autoToggle")
            else
                local vim = game:GetService("VirtualInputManager")
                if vim then
                    vim:SendKeyEvent(true, Enum.KeyCode.F, false, game)
                    task.delay(0.03, function()
                        pcall(function() vim:SendKeyEvent(false, Enum.KeyCode.F, false, game) end)
                    end)
                end
            end
        end)
    end

    -- 3. เปิด/ปิด AutoSkill attribute ให้ตรงกับสวิตช์ dngAutoSkill
    Remotes = Remotes or game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("Remotes")
    if Remotes and Remotes:FindFirstChild("SetAutoSkill") then
        local wantAutoSkill = (dngAutoSkill == true)
        if lp:GetAttribute("AutoSkill") ~= wantAutoSkill then
            pcall(function()
                Remotes.SetAutoSkill:FireServer(wantAutoSkill)
                lp:SetAttribute("AutoSkill", wantAutoSkill)
            end)
        end
    end
end

-- ระบบล็อกปุ่ม [F] AUTO ให้เปิดค้างเป็นสีเขียวตลอดเวลาทันทีที่เข้ามาในดันเจี้ยน (Event-Driven + 0.5s Watchdog, ไม่เปิดค้างนอกดัน!)
pcall(function()
    if _G.GolfHub_DngAutoLockConns then
        for _, c in ipairs(_G.GolfHub_DngAutoLockConns) do
            pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end)
        end
    end
    if _G.GolfHub_DngAutoLockThread then
        pcall(task.cancel, _G.GolfHub_DngAutoLockThread)
    end
    _G.GolfHub_DngAutoLockConns = {}

    -- ดักจับทันทีถ้าปุ่ม [F] AUTO ถูกปิดขณะอยู่ในดันเจี้ยน -> ดีดเปิดกลับทันที
    table.insert(_G.GolfHub_DngAutoLockConns, lp:GetAttributeChangedSignal("AutoOn"):Connect(function()
        if lp:GetAttribute("AutoOn") ~= true and isPlayerInDungeon and isPlayerInDungeon() then
            task.delay(0.15, function()
                if isPlayerInDungeon and isPlayerInDungeon() and lp:GetAttribute("AutoOn") ~= true then
                    ensureNativeDungeonAttackOn()
                end
            end)
        end
    end))

    -- ดักจับตอนฟื้นคืนชีพหลังล้มในดันเจี้ยน -> เปิด [F] AUTO กลับทันที
    table.insert(_G.GolfHub_DngAutoLockConns, lp:GetAttributeChangedSignal("DungeonDowned"):Connect(function()
        if lp:GetAttribute("DungeonDowned") ~= true and isPlayerInDungeon and isPlayerInDungeon() then
            task.delay(0.20, function()
                ensureNativeDungeonAttackOn()
            end)
        end
    end))

    -- ดักจับตอนเกิดตัวละครใหม่ในดันเจี้ยน -> เปิด [F] AUTO กลับทันที
    table.insert(_G.GolfHub_DngAutoLockConns, lp.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
        if isPlayerInDungeon and isPlayerInDungeon() then
            pcall(function() lp:SetAttribute("RespawnKeepBot", true) end)
            task.delay(0.35, function()
                ensureNativeDungeonAttackOn()
            end)
        end
    end))

    -- ลูปเฝ้าเบาๆ ทุก 0.5 วิ เผื่อเข้ามาในดันเจี้ยนใหม่หรือมีสคริปต์เกมแอบปิด AutoOn
    _G.GolfHub_DngAutoLockThread = task.spawn(function()
        local myGen = _G.GolfHub_Gen
        while _G.GolfHub_Gen == myGen do
            if isPlayerInDungeon and isPlayerInDungeon() then
                pcall(ensureNativeDungeonAttackOn)
            end
            task.wait(0.5)
        end
    end)
end)

-- ---------- Ranged mode (Skull King): stand far, cast only long-range skills ----------
-- Samurai's skill 1 Quick Draw (iaigiri) is a 176-stud line wave, 50 wide, cooldown = attack speed, 7 SP (SkillData,
-- read live). From 34-52 studs we are outside Flame Slam (cone R32), Rise of the Skull King (R27) and Fire Sword
-- Spin (R13.5 / reach 24.5); Execution Cleave (OneHit strip 11x90) and Skull Bomb Rain still reach us and are dodged
-- through the red-zone check below; Doom Nova (green safe circles) is left to the normal dodge code (the caller skips
-- this mode while safe circles exist).
DngExt.longRangeCache = {}
function DngExt.isLongRangeSkill(skillId)
    local c = DngExt.longRangeCache[skillId]
    if c ~= nil then return c end
    local ok, sd = pcall(function() return require(game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").Shared.SkillData) end)
    local def
    if ok and type(sd) == "table" then
        def = sd[skillId]
        if type(def) ~= "table" then
            for _, v in pairs(sd) do
                if type(v) == "table" and type(v[skillId]) == "table" then def = v[skillId] break end
            end
        end
    end
    local yes = type(def) == "table" and (tonumber(def.Range) or 0) >= 60
    DngExt.longRangeCache[skillId] = yes
    return yes
end

-- backDir (optional, the boss's back as a flat unit vector): when given, a spot counts only inside ±45° of his back
-- (BackWeak 1.5x: median hit 58.7k there vs ~40k elsewhere, measured on Pahtzx) unless no back spot is safe; the
-- back sector is searched at every distance before any other angle. Without backDir (swarm kiting) any side will do.
function DngExt.findRangedSpot(root, tPos, hazards, safeZones, step, kind, backDir, rings)
    local function usable(p)
        if kind == "halloween" and step == 4 and p.X < -4062 then return false end -- never out in the hallway
        if isPointInDungeonDanger(p, hazards, 2.5, safeZones) then return false end
        return hasDungeonLineOfSight(p, tPos)
    end
    local minR = rings and 23 or 33
    local maxR = rings and 31 or 36
    local my = root.Position
    local flatD = Vector3.new(my.X - tPos.X, 0, my.Z - tPos.Z).Magnitude
    -- 33-36 studs, a thin band on purpose. Quick Draw's SkillData says Range 176, but on the server it only landed
    -- within ~35 studs (measured on Pahtzx: boss HP dropped at 16 and 35 studs, not at 40-51; the first version held
    -- 34-52 and stood at 51 studs casting for nothing). 26-32 was tried next and Flame Slam (cone, R 32) downed us.
    -- 33+ is outside Flame Slam, Rise of the Skull King (R 27) and Fire Sword Spin (reach 24.5).
    local inBack = true
    if backDir and flatD > 0.1 then
        inBack = Vector3.new(my.X - tPos.X, 0, my.Z - tPos.Z).Unit:Dot(backDir) >= 0.707
    end
    if flatD >= minR and flatD <= maxR and inBack and usable(my) then return my end -- already fine: do not move

    local rp = RaycastParams.new()
    rp.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
    local filter = {}
    for _, inst in ipairs({ lp.Character, workspace:FindFirstChild("Monsters"), workspace:FindFirstChild("LumiBossFx"), workspace:FindFirstChild("LumiBossSkillFx") }) do
        if inst then filter[#filter + 1] = inst end
    end
    rp.FilterDescendantsInstances = filter
    local mobFloor = workspace:Raycast(tPos + Vector3.new(0, 4, 0), Vector3.new(0, -30, 0), rp)
    local floorY = mobFloor and mobFloor.Position.Y or (tPos.Y - 3)

    local base = backDir
    if not base then
        base = Vector3.new(my.X - tPos.X, 0, my.Z - tPos.Z)
        base = base.Magnitude > 0.1 and base.Unit or Vector3.new(0, 0, 1)
    end
    local passes = {
        { 0, 22.5, -22.5, 45, -45 },
        { 67.5, -67.5, 90, -90, 112.5, -112.5, 135, -135, 157.5, -157.5, 180 },
    }
    for _, angles in ipairs(passes) do
        for _, ringR in ipairs(rings or { 34, 35, 33.5, 36 }) do
            for _, deg in ipairs(angles) do
                local rad = math.rad(deg)
                local c, s = math.cos(rad), math.sin(rad)
                local d = Vector3.new(base.X * c - base.Z * s, 0, base.X * s + base.Z * c)
                local fx, fz = tPos.X + d.X * ringR, tPos.Z + d.Z * ringR
                local hit = workspace:Raycast(Vector3.new(fx, floorY + 10, fz), Vector3.new(0, -30, 0), rp)
                if hit and math.abs(hit.Position.Y - floorY) <= 6 then
                    local cand = Vector3.new(fx, hit.Position.Y + 2.8, fz)
                    if usable(cand) then return cand end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    -- nothing safe behind him and the current spot is safe and in range: stay rather than jump to his front
    if flatD >= minR and flatD <= maxR and usable(my) then return my end
    return nil
end

-- ค้นหาจุดปลอดภัยที่ "ใกล้บอสที่สุดและอยู่ด้านหลังบอสก่อนเสมอ" (Direct 360° Concentric Safe-Pocket Finder)
-- หาช่องว่างนอกวงแดงรอบบอสตั้งแต่ระยะ 3.8 -> 5.8 -> 7.8 -> 9.8 -> 11.8 -> 13.8 -> 15.8 studs ทั้ง 16 ทิศทาง (เริ่มจากด้านหลังบอสก่อนเพื่อตีแรงขึ้น 1.5x BackWeak!)
-- ใช้ได้กับทุกสกิล: Flame Slam (Cone 70°), Execution Cleave (11x90), Skull Bomb Rain (วงกลมหลายจุด), Cauldron Splash (Cone 80°), Broom Dash (7x48)!
local function findSafeMeleeStandPos(root, target, tPos, hazards, safeZones, forceGround)
    if not target or not tPos then return nil, false end

    -- 1. คำนวณทิศด้านหลังบอสที่แม่นยำที่สุด (ตรงข้าม Cone / ตรงข้ามแถบแดง Execution Cleave / ด้านหลังโมเดลบอส)
    local preferredBackDir = nil
    if hazards then
        for _, hz in ipairs(hazards) do
            if hz.kind == "cone" and hz.dir then
                local flatDir = Vector3.new(-hz.dir.X, 0, -hz.dir.Z)
                if flatDir.Magnitude > 0.05 then
                    preferredBackDir = flatDir.Unit
                    break
                end
            elseif hz.kind ~= "circle" and hz.cframe and hz.width and hz.length then
                local awayFromStrip = Vector3.new(tPos.X - hz.cframe.Position.X, 0, tPos.Z - hz.cframe.Position.Z)
                if awayFromStrip.Magnitude > 2.0 then
                    preferredBackDir = awayFromStrip.Unit
                elseif hz.dir then
                    local flatBack = Vector3.new(-hz.dir.X, 0, -hz.dir.Z)
                    if flatBack.Magnitude > 0.05 then preferredBackDir = flatBack.Unit end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    if not preferredBackDir then
        local tRoot = target:IsA("Model") and (target.PrimaryPart or target:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or target:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart")) or nil
        if tRoot then
            local b2d = Vector3.new(-tRoot.CFrame.LookVector.X, 0, -tRoot.CFrame.LookVector.Z)
            if b2d.Magnitude > 0.05 then preferredBackDir = b2d.Unit end
        end
    end

    local baseDir = preferredBackDir or dngLockedStandDir
    if not baseDir or baseDir.Magnitude < 0.05 then
        local diff = Vector3.new(root.Position.X - tPos.X, 0, root.Position.Z - tPos.Z)
        baseDir = diff.Magnitude > 0.2 and diff.Unit or Vector3.new(0, 0, 1)
    else
        baseDir = baseDir.Unit
    end

    -- 2. เตรียม Raycast พื้นและกำแพง LOS ของดันเจี้ยน (ใช้ตัวแคชเพื่อลด GC)
    local rayParams, wallRp, losWallCount = DngExt.getStandRaycastParams()

    local mobFloorRay = workspace:Raycast(Vector3.new(tPos.X, tPos.Y + 4, tPos.Z), Vector3.new(0, -30, 0), rayParams)
    local mobFloorY = mobFloorRay and mobFloorRay.Position.Y or (tPos.Y - 3.0)

    local isBossObj = isFinalBossObj(target) or isMiniBossObj(target)
    local useHover = (dngAirHoverBackstab == true) and (not forceGround) and (not isBossObj)
    -- วงรัศมีไล่จากชิดบอสที่สุด -> ขอบวงฟ้าของบอส (15.8 studs ยังอยู่ในระยะตี+สกิลครบทุกช่อง!)
    local testDists = isBossObj and { 4.2, 6.0, 8.0, 10.0, 12.0, 14.0, 15.8 } or { 3.5, 5.5, 7.5, 9.5 }
    -- Two passes: first only the back sector (±45° — measured on Pahtzx, the BackWeak bonus did not reach 45-90°:
    -- median hit 58.8k behind vs 40.6k at 45-90° vs 40.5k in front) at every ring, then every other angle.
    -- Before, the ring loop ran outside the angle loop, so a front spot at 4.2 studs won over a back spot at 6.0,
    -- and the chosen front direction was then locked for 2 s. The red-zone, wall, floor and LOS checks are unchanged.
    local passAngles = {
        { 0, 22.5, -22.5, 45, -45 },
        { 67.5, -67.5, 90, -90, 112.5, -112.5, 135, -135, 157.5, -157.5, 180 },
    }

    for _, testAngles in ipairs(passAngles) do
    for _, distTry in ipairs(testDists) do
        for _, deg in ipairs(testAngles) do
            local rad = math.rad(deg)
            local c, s = math.cos(rad), math.sin(rad)
            local dir = Vector3.new(baseDir.X * c - baseDir.Z * s, 0, baseDir.X * s + baseDir.Z * c).Unit
            local cx = tPos.X + dir.X * distTry
            local cz = tPos.Z + dir.Z * distTry
            local candPos = Vector3.new(cx, mobFloorY + 2.8, cz)

            -- เช็คว่าพ้นวงแดง 100% (ใช้ margin กระชับ 1.0 stud เพื่อเข้าใกล้บอสได้ชิดที่สุดโดยไม่เหยียบวงแดง!)
            if not isPointInDungeonDanger(candPos, hazards, 1.0, safeZones) then
                -- เช็คว่าไม่ทะลุกำแพง DungeonLosWall และมีพื้นจริงรองรับ
                local clearWall = true
                if losWallCount > 0 then
                    local checkFrom = Vector3.new(tPos.X, mobFloorY + 3.0, tPos.Z)
                    local checkVec = Vector3.new(cx - tPos.X, 0, cz - tPos.Z)
                    if checkVec.Magnitude > 0.5 then
                        if workspace:Raycast(checkFrom, checkVec.Unit * (checkVec.Magnitude + 1.0), wallRp) then
                            clearWall = false
                        end
                    end
                end
                if clearWall then
                    local candFloor = workspace:Raycast(Vector3.new(cx, mobFloorY + 6.0, cz), Vector3.new(0, -18, 0), rayParams)
                    if candFloor and math.abs(candFloor.Position.Y - mobFloorY) <= 4.5 then
                        local finalY = candFloor.Position.Y + 2.8
                        local finalPos = Vector3.new(cx, finalY, cz)
                        if hasDungeonLineOfSight(finalPos, tPos) then
                            dngLockedStandTarget = target
                            dngLockedStandDir = dir
                            lastBossBackAlignTick = tick()
                            return finalPos, useHover
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    end

    return nil, false
end

-- รีเซ็ตสถานะการต่อสู้ทั้งหมดหลังเกิดใหม่ และ Teleport กลับไปหาบอส/มินิบอสต่อทันที (รองรับ StreamingEnabled + Anti-Snapback)
function DngExt.handleRespawnReturn(root, hum, Remotes, fromDeathEvent)
    if not isPlayerInDungeon() then return false end
    if not root or not hum or hum.Health <= 0 or lp:GetAttribute("DungeonDowned") == true then return false end

    DngExt.wasDownedOrDead = false
    DngExt.lastAliveChar = lp.Character
    DngExt.lastRespawnRetTick = tick()

    table.clear(activeDungeonHazards)
    table.clear(activeDungeonSafeZones)
    table.clear(activeBrazierZones)
    table.clear(visitedBraziers)
    table.clear(DngExt.kickedDemonSkulls)
    table.clear(DngExt.openedChests)
    table.clear(dungeonTargetBlacklist)
    table.clear(attemptedDungeonLoot)
    activeBrazierUntil = 0
    DngExt.braziersCompletedUntil = 0
    activeBossSpinUntil = 0
    lastDngSetTargetObj = nil
    lastDngSetTargetTick = 0
    dngLockedStandTarget = nil
    dngLockedStandDir = nil
    dngWatchTarget = nil
    dngWatchHp = nil
    dngWatchRecoveryStage = 0
    dngNativeSyncedThisRun = false
    lastDngNativeAutoTryTick = 0
    lastCheckpointAdvanceTick = 0
    currentDungeonCheckpointIdx = 1

    pcall(function()
        hum.Sit = false
        hum.PlatformStand = false
        if lp:GetAttribute("MoveLockUntil") and lp:GetAttribute("MoveLockUntil") > 0 then
            lp:SetAttribute("MoveLockUntil", 0)
        end
        if lp:GetAttribute("HardLockUntil") and lp:GetAttribute("HardLockUntil") > 0 then
            lp:SetAttribute("HardLockUntil", 0)
        end
        local bv = root:FindFirstChild("GolfHub_AirHoverBV")
        if bv then bv:Destroy() end
    end)

    if fromDeathEvent then
        task.wait(0.25)
    end

    if not isPlayerInDungeon() or lp:GetAttribute("DungeonDowned") == true then return false end
    root, hum = getCharRoot()
    if not root or not hum or hum.Health <= 0 or root.Position.X > -3000 then return false end

    local step, dngKind = getRealtimeDungeonStep()
    local returnPos = nil
    if dngKind == "halloween" then
        if DngExt.lastCombatPos and DngExt.lastCombatStep == step then
            if step ~= 4 or DngExt.lastCombatPos.Z < -180 then
                returnPos = DngExt.lastCombatPos
            end
        end
        if not returnPos then
            if step == 4 then
                returnPos = Vector3.new(-3985, 62.5, -260)
            elseif step == 3 then
                returnPos = Vector3.new(-4215, 62.5, -20)
            elseif step == 2 then
                returnPos = Vector3.new(-3994, 62.5, -20)
            elseif step == 5 then
                returnPos = Vector3.new(-4038, 62.5, -260)
            elseif DUNGEON_STEP_WAYPOINTS[step] and DUNGEON_STEP_WAYPOINTS[step][1] then
                returnPos = DUNGEON_STEP_WAYPOINTS[step][1]
            end
        end
    end

    if returnPos and (Vector3.new(root.Position.X, 0, root.Position.Z) - Vector3.new(returnPos.X, 0, returnPos.Z)).Magnitude > 35 then
        if lblDungeonStatus then
            pcall(function()
                lblDungeonStatus:Set(string.format("🔄 [ฟื้นคืนชีพแล้ว!] Teleport กลับห้องสู้บอส (Step %d) ต่อทันที...", step), "run")
            end)
        end

        DngExt.teleportPathTo(root, hum, returnPos)

        task.wait(0.18)
        if isPlayerInDungeon() and lp:GetAttribute("DungeonDowned") ~= true then
            root, hum = getCharRoot()
            if root and hum and hum.Health > 0 and root.Position.X <= -3000 then
                local remainDist = (Vector3.new(root.Position.X, 0, root.Position.Z) - Vector3.new(returnPos.X, 0, returnPos.Z)).Magnitude
                if remainDist > 45 then
                    DngExt.teleportPathTo(root, hum, returnPos)
                end
            end
        end
    end

    ensureNativeDungeonAttackOn(Remotes)
    return true
end

-- Low-HP retreat spot: a hazard-free floor point away from every nearby monster (no walls in between, no ledges, inside the boss room)
function DngExt.findRetreatSpot(root, hazards, safeZones)
    local myPos = root.Position
    local monFolder = workspace:FindFirstChild("Monsters")
    local threats = {}
    if monFolder then
        for _, m in ipairs(monFolder:GetChildren()) do
            if m:IsA("Model") then
                local p = getObjectSafePos(m)
                if p and (p - myPos).Magnitude < 90 then
                    threats[#threats + 1] = p
                end
            end
        end
    end

    local rayParams = RaycastParams.new()
    rayParams.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
    local filter = {}
    for _, inst in ipairs({ lp.Character, monFolder, workspace:FindFirstChild("LumiBossFx"), workspace:FindFirstChild("LumiBossSkillFx") }) do
        if inst then filter[#filter + 1] = inst end
    end
    rayParams.FilterDescendantsInstances = filter

    local step, kind = getRealtimeDungeonStep()
    local bestSpot, bestScore = nil, -math.huge
    for i = 0, 15 do
        local ang = (i / 16) * math.pi * 2
        local dir = Vector3.new(math.cos(ang), 0, math.sin(ang))
        for _, dist in ipairs({ 14, 22, 30 }) do
            local flat = myPos + dir * dist
            if not (kind == "halloween" and step == 4 and flat.X < -4062) then
                local wall = workspace:Raycast(myPos + Vector3.new(0, 2.5, 0), dir * dist, rayParams)
                if not wall and not isPointInDungeonDanger(flat, hazards, 3.5, safeZones) then
                    local floorHit = workspace:Raycast(flat + Vector3.new(0, 8, 0), Vector3.new(0, -30, 0), rayParams)
                    if floorHit and math.abs(floorHit.Position.Y + 2.5 - myPos.Y) <= 6 then
                        local nearest = math.huge
                        for _, tp in ipairs(threats) do
                            local d = Vector3.new(flat.X - tp.X, 0, flat.Z - tp.Z).Magnitude
                            if d < nearest then nearest = d end
                        end
                        local score = math.min(nearest, 60) - dist * 0.15
                        if score > bestScore then
                            bestScore = score
                            bestSpot = Vector3.new(flat.X, floorHit.Position.Y + 2.5, flat.Z)
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return bestSpot
end
-- ====================================================================
-- SKILL PUMP: casts every hotbar skill the moment the SERVER says its cooldown is over.
-- The server owns the cooldowns: a 7s test that fired Blossom Dance 99 times (15/s) was accepted exactly 3 times, 3.15s apart,
-- so a "no cooldown" spam does not exist. What does exist is idle time: over 40s of dungeon packs Quick Draw (0.24s cooldown) was
-- cast 26 times out of ~164 possible. The server answers every accepted cast with SkillUsed(skillId, cooldownSeconds), so the
-- next cast can be sent at exactly that moment instead of waiting for the hotbar shading or for a branch of the combat step.
-- ====================================================================
DngExt.skillReadyAt = {}
DngExt.pumpRun = 0
do
    local okSD, SkillData = pcall(function()
        local shared = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):WaitForChild("Shared", 5)
        return require(shared:WaitForChild("SkillData", 5))
    end)
    DngExt.skillDef = (okSD and type(SkillData) == "table" and type(SkillData.Skills) == "table") and SkillData.Skills or {}
    DngExt.studsPerPixel = (okSD and type(SkillData) == "table" and tonumber(SkillData.StudsPerPixel)) or (1 / 9)
end

-- reach in studs: Range / Radius / Length are stored in hotbar pixels (SkillData.StudsPerPixel), plus a small margin
function DngExt.skillReach(skillId)
    local def = DngExt.skillDef[skillId]
    local px = def and (tonumber(def.Range) or tonumber(def.Radius) or tonumber(def.Length))
    local r = px and (px * DngExt.studsPerPixel + 3) or 24
    if def and def.RangedFire == true then
        -- RangedFire wave projectiles (e.g. Quick Draw Range 176) land out to ~38 studs while px * 1/9 reads ~22.5
        r = math.max(r, 38)
    end
    return r
end

-- highest SP cost listed for the skill (the table holds the per-level costs)
function DngExt.skillSpCost(skillId)
    local def = DngExt.skillDef[skillId]
    local best = 0
    if def and type(def.SP) == "table" then
        for _, v in pairs(def.SP) do
            if type(v) == "number" and v > best then best = v end
        end
    end
    return best
end

pcall(function()
    local Remotes = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):WaitForChild("Remotes", 5)
    local used = Remotes and Remotes:FindFirstChild("SkillUsed")
    if used and used:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
        _G.GolfHub_Conn(used.OnClientEvent, function(skillId, cooldown)
            if type(skillId) == "string" then
                DngExt.skillReadyAt[skillId] = tick() + math.max(0, (tonumber(cooldown) or 0) - 0.015)
            end
        end)
    end
end)

function DngExt.skillPump(runId, gen)
    local Remotes = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("Remotes")
    local skillRem = Remotes and Remotes:FindFirstChild("UseSkill")
    if not skillRem then return end
    while DngExt.pumpRun == runId and _G.GolfHub_Gen == gen and dungeonFarmActive and isPlayerInDungeon() do
        task.wait(0.02)
        local root, hum = getCharRoot()
        if dngAutoSkill and dngCombatActive and root and hum and hum.Health > 0 and lp:GetAttribute("DungeonDowned") ~= true then
            local targetVal = lp:FindFirstChild("CurrentTarget")
            local target = targetVal and targetVal.Value
            local tPos = target and target.Parent and getObjectSafePos(target)
            if tPos then
                local dist = (Vector3.new(tPos.X, 0, tPos.Z) - Vector3.new(root.Position.X, 0, root.Position.Z)).Magnitude
                local sp = tonumber(lp:GetAttribute("SP"))
                local raw = lp:GetAttribute("HotbarJson")
                if type(raw) == "string" and raw ~= cachedHotbarStr then
                    cachedHotbarStr = raw
                    local okJson, parsed = pcall(HttpService.JSONDecode, HttpService, raw)
                    if okJson and type(parsed) == "table" then cachedHotbarTbl = parsed end
                end
                local now = tick()
                for slot = 1, 8 do
                    if isSkillSlotEnabled(slot) then
                        local entry = cachedHotbarTbl[tostring(slot)]
                        local skillId = (type(entry) == "string") and string.match(entry, "^skill:(.+)$") or nil
                        if skillId
                            and now >= (DngExt.skillReadyAt[skillId] or 0)
                            and dist <= DngExt.skillReach(skillId)
                            and (not sp or sp >= DngExt.skillSpCost(skillId)) then
                            DngExt.skillReadyAt[skillId] = now + 0.035 -- retry window if the server rejects; SkillUsed overwrites it with the real cooldown
                            pcall(function() skillRem:FireServer(skillId, target) end)
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
end

-- Stage marker for diagnosing where a combat step stops (read DngExt.lastStage through getgenv().GolfHub_Debug.dng)
DngExt.lastStage, DngExt.lastStageAt = "none", 0
function DngExt.t(stage) DngExt.lastStage = stage DngExt.lastStageAt = tick() end

local function dungeonCombatStep()
    -- กฎเหล็ก: ป้องกันการเทเลพอร์ตทุกกรณีถ้าอยู่นอกดันเจี้ยน
    if not isPlayerInDungeon() then return end
    DngExt.t("enter")
    local root, hum = getCharRoot()
    local isDownedNow = (lp:GetAttribute("DungeonDowned") == true)
    if isDownedNow or not root or not hum or hum.Health <= 0 then
        DngExt.wasDownedOrDead = true
        if root then
            local bv = root:FindFirstChild("GolfHub_AirHoverBV")
            if bv then bv:Destroy() end
        end
        if lblDungeonStatus and isDownedNow then
            pcall(function()
                lblDungeonStatus:Set("💀 ตัวละครล้มในดันเจี้ยน กำลังรอเกิดใหม่แล้ว Teleport กลับไปสู้บอสต่อ...", "idle")
            end)
        end
        return
    end

    -- กฎเหล็ก: รอจนกว่า Server จะส่งตัวละครเข้าพิกัดดันเจี้ยนจริง (X <= -3000) ป้องกัน Anti-Teleport ตอนเพิ่งโหลดเข้าแมพ
    if root.Position.X > -3000 then
        if lblDungeonStatus then
