            pcall(function() lblDungeonStatus:Set("⏳ รอ Server ส่งตัวละครเข้าดันเจี้ยน...", "idle") end)
        end
        task.wait(0.08)
        return
    end

    -- Emergency Auto Potion runs before early-return dodge/retreat branches so HP/SP heal during 1-5s dodges and retreats
    do
        local remPot = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("Remotes")
        local useItem = remPot and remPot:FindFirstChild("UseItem")
        if useItem then
            -- HP potions have a 3 s cooldown (lp HpPotCdLen = 3, HpPotCdEnd = server time it ends): ask only once it has
            -- ended, instead of every 0.35-0.9 s, where most requests were refused
            local potReady = workspace:GetServerTimeNow() >= (tonumber(lp:GetAttribute("HpPotCdEnd")) or 0) - 0.05
            if potReady and hum.Health > 0 and (hum.Health / hum.MaxHealth) < 0.85 and (tick() - (DngExt.lastHpPotAt or 0)) >= (((hum.Health / hum.MaxHealth) < 0.5) and 0.35 or 0.9) then
                DngExt.lastHpPotAt = tick()
                local hpPot = lp:GetAttribute("PotionHpNow") or "white_potion"
                pcall(function()
                    useItem:FireServer("red_potion_l")
                    useItem:FireServer(hpPot)
                end)
            end
            local sp = lp:GetAttribute("SP")
            local maxSp = lp:GetAttribute("MaxSP") or 500
            if sp and type(sp) == "number" and (sp / maxSp) < 0.50 and (tick() - (DngExt.lastSpPotAt or 0)) >= 1.0 then
                DngExt.lastSpPotAt = tick()
                local spPot = lp:GetAttribute("PotionSpNow") or "blue_potion"
                pcall(function() useItem:FireServer(spPot) end)
            end
        end
    end

    -- Telegraphed skill in progress (planned from the server warning): get to the safe spot first, attack after
    local dd = _G.GolfHub_DngDodge
    -- during Inferno Judgment only one-hit skills (or low/mid HP < 65%) may pull us off the braziers
    -- In Step 4 RangedKite, if we're already safe from all hazards (non-OneHit, no SafeZones, >=65% HP), let RangedKite keep BackWeak 1.5x instead of dragging us to 7-stud melee
    local skipRangedDd = (_G.GolfHub_DngRanged == true) and (DngExt.stepNow == 4) and dd and (not dd.oneHit) and (not dd.safes)
        and (hum.MaxHealth <= 0 or (hum.Health / hum.MaxHealth) >= 0.65)
        and tick() >= (activeBrazierUntil or 0) and tick() >= (DngExt.rangedPauseUntil or 0)
        and DngExt.isDangerPos and (not DngExt.isDangerPos(root.Position, 1.5))
    if (not skipRangedDd) and dd and tick() < dd.untilT and dd.goal and (dd.oneHit or tick() >= (activeBrazierUntil or 0) or (hum.MaxHealth > 0 and (hum.Health / hum.MaxHealth) < 0.65)) then
        if dd.safes and dd.bossPart and dd.bossPart.Parent then
            local bpos = dd.bossPart.Position
            local brad = tonumber(dd.bossPart.Parent:GetAttribute("Radius")) or 1.5
            local bestP, bestD = nil, math.huge
            for _, s in ipairs(dd.safes) do
                local toward = Vector3.new(bpos.X - s.c.X, 0, bpos.Z - s.c.Z)
                local step = 0
                if toward.Magnitude > 0.5 then step = math.min(math.max(0, s.r - 3.5), math.max(0, toward.Magnitude - (brad + 1))) end
                local p = Vector3.new(s.c.X, 0, s.c.Z) + (toward.Magnitude > 0.5 and toward.Unit * step or Vector3.zero)
                local dB = (p - Vector3.new(bpos.X, 0, bpos.Z)).Magnitude
                if dB < bestD then bestD, bestP = dB, p end
            end
            if bestP then dd.goal = bestP end
        elseif dd.spin and dd.bossPart and dd.bossPart.Parent then
            -- keep just outside the spin ring around the boss's live position (he walks after us while spinning)
            local bpos = dd.bossPart.Position
            local away = Vector3.new(root.Position.X - bpos.X, 0, root.Position.Z - bpos.Z)
            if away.Magnitude < 0.5 then away = Vector3.new(1, 0, 0) end
            if away.Magnitude < dd.radius then
                local candSpin = Vector3.new(bpos.X, 0, bpos.Z) + away.Unit * (dd.radius + 1.5)
                if DngExt.kindNow == "halloween" and DngExt.stepNow == 4 and candSpin.X < -4062 then
                    candSpin = Vector3.new(bpos.X + (dd.radius + 1.5), 0, bpos.Z)
                end
                dd.goal = candSpin
            else
                dd.goal = root.Position
            end
        end
        local g = Vector3.new(dd.goal.X, root.Position.Y, dd.goal.Z)
        -- one-hit skills: snap back as soon as anything (the game's auto-bot) drags us 1 stud off the safe spot
        if (Vector3.new(root.Position.X, 0, root.Position.Z) - Vector3.new(g.X, 0, g.Z)).Magnitude > (dd.oneHit and 1 or 2) then
            if teleportDungeonTo then teleportDungeonTo(root, hum, g) else hum:MoveTo(g) end
        end
        if lblDungeonStatus then
            pcall(function() lblDungeonStatus:Set("🛡️ หลบ " .. tostring(dd.skill), "run") end)
        end
        -- keep casting while dodging: skills only fired from the main path, so 30-40% of the fight cast nothing
        -- (Blossom Dance's 24-stud radius still reaches from most dodge spots)
        pcall(function()
            local t = getDungeonTarget()
            if t then burstDungeonSkills(t) end
        end)
        task.wait(0.05)
        return
    end

    -- Free-hit window: all 4 braziers lit (or a skull kicked into him) stuns Skull King. Stand right behind him
    -- (BackWeak 1.5x) and let the attack + skills run; nothing else (retreat / wandering) may pull us away.
    -- never while Inferno Judgment is up: lighting all 4 braziers comes first (a skull kick also opens this window)
    -- ...and not when HP is already low: the free-hit spot is often inside the add pack (299 HP seen live)
    local inStunWindow = tick() < (DngExt.bossStunRushUntil or 0) and tick() >= (activeBrazierUntil or 0)
        and (hum.MaxHealth <= 0 or hum.Health / hum.MaxHealth >= 0.45)
    -- ranged mode: switch normal attacks back on for the window and off again after it (one bot-settings sync each way)
    if _G.GolfHub_DngRanged == true and (inStunWindow == true) ~= (_G.GolfHub_StunMelee == true) then
        _G.GolfHub_StunMelee = inStunWindow == true
        pcall(syncSkillSelectionToBotJson, dngAutoSkill == true)
    end
    if inStunWindow then
        local mf = workspace:FindFirstChild("Monsters")
        local bossPart
        for _, m in ipairs(mf and mf:GetChildren() or {}) do
            if tostring(m:GetAttribute("MonsterId") or "") == "dgn_crypt_boss" then
                bossPart = m:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or m.PrimaryPart
                break
            end
        end
        if bossPart then
            local look = Vector3.new(bossPart.CFrame.LookVector.X, 0, bossPart.CFrame.LookVector.Z)
            if look.Magnitude < 0.1 then look = Vector3.new(0, 0, 1) end
            -- melee reach from his centre = AttackRange + his Radius (game's own rule); stand a bit inside it
            local brad = tonumber(bossPart.Parent:GetAttribute("Radius")) or 1.5
            local reach = (tonumber(lp:GetAttribute("AttackRange")) or 7) + brad
            local spot = bossPart.Position - look.Unit * math.max(3, reach - 2)
            spot = Vector3.new(spot.X, root.Position.Y, spot.Z)
            -- move when out of reach, or when not within ±45° of his back: stunned he cannot turn, so one jump
            -- gives the whole window at BackWeak 1.5x (in reach but in front used to stay in front)
            local fromBoss = Vector3.new(root.Position.X - bossPart.Position.X, 0, root.Position.Z - bossPart.Position.Z)
            local distBoss = fromBoss.Magnitude
            local behindNow = distBoss > 0.1 and fromBoss.Unit:Dot(-look.Unit) >= 0.707
            if distBoss > reach - 0.5 or not behindNow then
                if teleportDungeonTo then teleportDungeonTo(root, hum, spot, bossPart.Position) else hum:MoveTo(spot) end
            end
            pcall(function()
                local rem = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("Remotes")
                if rem and rem:FindFirstChild("SetTarget") then rem.SetTarget:FireServer(bossPart.Parent, false, true) end
            end)
            if lblDungeonStatus then pcall(function() lblDungeonStatus:Set("💥 บอสติดสตัน — ตีฟรีจากด้านหลัง!", "run") end) end
            pcall(burstDungeonSkills, bossPart.Parent)
            task.wait(0.1)
            return
        end
    end

    -- Low-HP retreat (Hard mode deaths came from stacked mob hits + poison while the bot kept trading blows at
    -- ~15% HP): below 40% HP stand at the spot farthest from every monster until HP is back to 75% (max 10 s).
    do
        local pct = hum.MaxHealth > 0 and hum.Health / hum.MaxHealth or 1
        DngExt.lowHpUntil = DngExt.lowHpUntil or 0
        -- same thresholds as the older DngExt retreat (0.35 -> 0.60) so the two never stack into long idle spells
        if pct < (DngExt.retreatHp or 0.35) and tick() > (DngExt.lowHpCooldown or 0) then
            DngExt.lowHpSpot = nil
            DngExt.lowHpUntil = tick() + 6
            DngExt.lowHpCooldown = tick() + 12
            DngExt.retreatReadyAt = math.max(DngExt.retreatReadyAt or 0, DngExt.lowHpCooldown)
        end
        if tick() < DngExt.lowHpUntil and pct < (DngExt.retreatResumeHp or 0.60) and tick() >= (activeBrazierUntil or 0) then
            local hzL, szL = {}, {}
            if dngAutoDodge then
                hzL, szL = getRealtimeDungeonHazards()
            end
            local stL, kdL = getRealtimeDungeonStep()
            local mons = {}
            local mf = workspace:FindFirstChild("Monsters")
            if mf then
                for _, m in ipairs(mf:GetChildren()) do
                    local mp = m:IsA("Model") and (m:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or m.PrimaryPart)
                    local mh = m:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                    if mp and (not mh or mh.Health > 0) and (mp.Position - root.Position).Magnitude < 160 then mons[#mons + 1] = mp.Position end
                end
            end
            local rpL = RaycastParams.new()
            rpL.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
            local flL = {}
            for _, inst in ipairs({ lp.Character, mf, workspace:FindFirstChild("LumiBossFx"), workspace:FindFirstChild("LumiBossSkillFx") }) do
                if inst then flL[#flL + 1] = inst end
            end
            rpL.FilterDescendantsInstances = flL
            -- pick the spot once per retreat and stay there (re-picking every tick from the new position made the
            -- character hop across the room); only move again if a monster gets within 12 studs or a hazard lands on it
            local keep = DngExt.lowHpSpot
            if keep then
                if isPointInDungeonDanger(keep, hzL, 2.5, szL) or (kdL == "halloween" and stL == 4 and keep.X < -4062) then
                    keep = nil
                else
                    for _, mp in ipairs(mons) do
                        if (Vector3.new(mp.X, 0, mp.Z) - Vector3.new(keep.X, 0, keep.Z)).Magnitude < 12 then keep = nil break end
                    end
                end
            end
            local best, bestScore = keep, keep and math.huge or -1
            for _, dist in ipairs(keep and {} or { 25, 40, 55 }) do
                for a = 0, 330, 30 do
                    local cand = root.Position + Vector3.new(math.cos(math.rad(a)) * dist, 0, math.sin(math.rad(a)) * dist)
                    if not (kdL == "halloween" and stL == 4 and cand.X < -4062) then
                        local wall = workspace:Raycast(root.Position + Vector3.new(0, 2.5, 0), Vector3.new(cand.X - root.Position.X, 0, cand.Z - root.Position.Z), rpL)
                        if not wall then
                            local hit = workspace:Raycast(cand + Vector3.new(0, 12, 0), Vector3.new(0, -40, 0), rpL)
                            if hit and math.abs(hit.Position.Y - (root.Position.Y - 3)) < 8 then
                                local candPos = Vector3.new(cand.X, hit.Position.Y + 3, cand.Z)
                                if not isPointInDungeonDanger(candPos, hzL, 2.5, szL) then
                                    local minD = math.huge
                                    for _, mp in ipairs(mons) do minD = math.min(minD, (Vector3.new(mp.X, 0, mp.Z) - Vector3.new(cand.X, 0, cand.Z)).Magnitude) end
                                    if minD > bestScore then bestScore, best = minD, candPos end
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
            DngExt.lowHpSpot = best
            if best then
                if (best - root.Position).Magnitude > 3 then
                    if teleportDungeonTo then teleportDungeonTo(root, hum, best) else hum:MoveTo(best) end
                end
                if lblDungeonStatus then
                    pcall(function() lblDungeonStatus:Set(string.format("❤️ เลือดต่ำ %.0f%% — ถอยรอฟื้นเลือด", pct * 100), "run") end)
                end
                task.wait(0.15)
                return
            end
        end
    end

    -- ตรวจจับการฟื้นคืนชีพหลังตายในดันเจี้ยน (หรือยืนค้างอยู่หน้าทางเข้า Z > 110 ทั้งที่ด่านเดินหน้าไป Step >= 2 แล้ว) -> Teleport กลับไปสู้บอสต่อทันที!
    local curChar = lp.Character
    local charChangedAfterAlive = (DngExt.lastAliveChar ~= nil and curChar ~= nil and curChar ~= DngExt.lastAliveChar)
    local stepChk, kindChk = getRealtimeDungeonStep()
    local stuckAtEntranceAfterAdvance = (kindChk == "halloween" and stepChk >= 2 and stepChk <= 4 and root.Position.Z > 110 and (tick() - DngExt.lastRespawnRetTick) >= 2.2)
    if DngExt.wasDownedOrDead or charChangedAfterAlive or stuckAtEntranceAfterAdvance then
        local RemotesForRespawn = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("Remotes")
        DngExt.handleRespawnReturn(root, hum, RemotesForRespawn, DngExt.wasDownedOrDead or charChangedAfterAlive)
        root, hum = getCharRoot()
        if not root or not hum or hum.Health <= 0 or lp:GetAttribute("DungeonDowned") == true then return end
    else
        DngExt.lastAliveChar = curChar
    end

    -- ตรวจสอบสถานะดันเจี้ยนหมดเวลาจริง (เฉพาะห้องที่ค้างเกินเวลาที่กำหนด > 15 วิ)
    local isExpired, expireReason = isCurrentDungeonExpired()
    if isExpired then
        if lblDungeonStatus then
            pcall(function() lblDungeonStatus:Set("⏰ ดันเจี้ยนหมดเวลา / สิ้นสุดเซสชัน! กำลังออกจากดันเจี้ยน...", "idle") end)
        end
        if forceStopNativeAutoBot and not farmActive and not bossFarmActive then
            forceStopNativeAutoBot()
        end
        local bv = root:FindFirstChild("GolfHub_AirHoverBV")
        if bv then bv:Destroy() end
        table.clear(dungeonTargetBlacklist)
        dngWatchTarget = nil
        dngWatchHp = nil
        dngWatchRecoveryStage = 0
        local Remotes = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("Remotes")
        if Remotes and Remotes:FindFirstChild("DungeonAction") then
            pcall(function() Remotes.DungeonAction:FireServer("leave") end)
        end
        task.wait(1.5)
        return
    end

    -- ระบบกู้ชีพฉุกเฉินเฉพาะเมื่อตัวละครอยู่ในดันเจี้ยนแล้ว (X <= -3000) แต่ตกเหว (Y < 30)
    if root.Position.Y < 30 and lp:GetAttribute("DungeonDowned") ~= true then
        local minIdx, maxIdx = getDungeonStageBounds()
        local rescueIdx = math.clamp(currentDungeonCheckpointIdx, minIdx, maxIdx)
        local rescueCp = DUNGEON_CHECKPOINTS[rescueIdx]
        root.CFrame = CFrame.new(rescueCp + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0))
        if root:IsA("BasePart") then
            root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
            root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
        end
        task.wait(0.12)
        return
    end

    local Remotes = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("Remotes")

    -- ปลดสถานะนั่งอัตโนมัติ (ถ้านั่งอยู่ เซิร์ฟเวอร์จะบล็อกการโจมตีและสกิลทั้งหมด!)
    if (hum.Sit or lp:GetAttribute("Sitting") == true) and (tick() - lastDngUnsitTick > 0.4) then
        lastDngUnsitTick = tick()
        hum.Sit = false
        if Remotes and Remotes:FindFirstChild("SitToggle") then
            pcall(function() Remotes.SitToggle:FireServer(false) end)
        end
    end

    -- เปิดปุ่ม [F] AUTO หลักของเกม + ซิงค์สกิลช่อง 1-5 เฉพาะในดันเจี้ยน
    ensureNativeDungeonAttackOn(Remotes)

    DngExt.t("retreatSection")
    -- Low-HP retreat: below retreatHp, jump (dungeon Teleport only) to a safe spot away from the monsters and let the potions above
    -- work until retreatResumeHp, for at most retreatMaxSec; a cooldown stops it from looping during a long fight.
    if dngAutoDodge then
        local hpRatio = hum.Health / math.max(1, hum.MaxHealth)
        local nowRetreat = tick()
        if nowRetreat < DngExt.retreatUntil and hpRatio < DngExt.retreatResumeHp then
            local hzR, szR = getRealtimeDungeonHazards()
            -- Move on again when a monster has followed us (closer than 10 studs), not only when we stand in a red
            -- zone: summons (Bone Hounds) caught up within the 5 s retreat and kept hitting while we only waited.
            local chased = false
            if nowRetreat - (DngExt.retreatRejumpAt or 0) >= 1.0 then
                local mfR = workspace:FindFirstChild("Monsters")
                for _, m in ipairs(mfR and mfR:GetChildren() or {}) do
                    if m:IsA("Model") and isMonsterAliveObj(m) then
                        local p = getObjectSafePos(m)
                        if p and Vector3.new(p.X - root.Position.X, 0, p.Z - root.Position.Z).Magnitude < 10 then
                            chased = true
                            break
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
            if chased or isPointInDungeonDanger(root.Position, hzR, 2.0, szR) then
                local spotR = DngExt.findRetreatSpot(root, hzR, szR)
                if spotR then
                    DngExt.retreatRejumpAt = nowRetreat
                    teleportDungeonTo(root, hum, spotR)
                end
            end
            if lblDungeonStatus then
                pcall(function()
                    lblDungeonStatus:Set(string.format("🩹 [HP %.0f%%] ถอยพักฟื้นก่อนสู้ต่อ (กลับที่ %.0f%%)", hpRatio * 100, DngExt.retreatResumeHp * 100), "idle")
                end)
            end
            task.wait(0.1)
            return
        elseif nowRetreat >= DngExt.retreatUntil and hpRatio < DngExt.retreatHp and nowRetreat >= DngExt.retreatReadyAt then
            local hzR, szR = getRealtimeDungeonHazards()
            local spotR = DngExt.findRetreatSpot(root, hzR, szR)
            if spotR then
                DngExt.retreatUntil = nowRetreat + DngExt.retreatMaxSec
                DngExt.retreatReadyAt = nowRetreat + DngExt.retreatCooldown
                DngExt.lowHpCooldown = math.max(DngExt.lowHpCooldown or 0, DngExt.retreatReadyAt)
                teleportDungeonTo(root, hum, spotR)
                task.wait(0.1)
                return
            end
        end
    end
    -- 0. เช็คกล่องสมบัติ / Item Crate (<Player>'s Chest / LootBox) ก่อนเสมอ -> วาร์ปทันที + กดค้าง 'E' เปิดและเก็บของดรอปทั้งหมด!
    local curStepNow, curDngKindNow = getRealtimeDungeonStep()
    DngExt.stepNow, DngExt.kindNow = curStepNow, curDngKindNow -- read by calculate360OptimalSafeSpot
    _G.GolfHub_DngStepNow = curStepNow -- read by the dungeon run stats (per-step seconds)
    if curStepNow == 5 then
        if DngExt.clearFirstSeenTick == 0 then
            DngExt.clearFirstSeenTick = tick()
        end
    else
        DngExt.clearFirstSeenTick = 0
    end

    local chestsInstant = DngExt.findChests()
    if #chestsInstant > 0 then
        local bestC, bestCD = nil, math.huge
        for _, c in ipairs(chestsInstant) do
            local cd = (c.pos - root.Position).Magnitude
            if cd < bestCD then
                bestCD = cd
                bestC = c
            end
        end
        if bestC and DngExt.openChestAndCollectAll(root, hum, bestC, Remotes) then
            return
        end
    end

    -- ถ้าเคลียร์ดันแล้ว (Step 5) และยังมีของดรอปตกอยู่ -> วาร์ปเก็บให้ครบทุกชิ้นก่อนกด Replay!
    local hasUncollectedClearLoot = false
    if curStepNow == 5 then
        local maxSlots = lp:GetAttribute("BagSlotsMax") or 240
        local usedSlots = lp:GetAttribute("BagSlotsUsed") or 0
        if (maxSlots - usedSlots) > 0 then
            local nearD = findNearestDrop(150)
            if nearD and nearD.Position and nearD.Position.X <= -3000 then
                hasUncollectedClearLoot = true
                local vim = nil
                pcall(function() vim = game:GetService("VirtualInputManager") end)
                DngExt.collectAllDrops(root, hum, nearD.Position, Remotes, 3.5, vim)
                return
            end
        end
        if DngExt.lastChestOpenedTick < DngExt.clearFirstSeenTick and (tick() - DngExt.clearFirstSeenTick) < 2.5 then
            hasUncollectedClearLoot = true
        end
    end

    DngExt.t("chestSection")
    -- 0.2 ตรวจสอบหน้าต่างจบดันเจี้ยน (LumiDungeonClear / LumiDungeonReplay) — กดต่อเมื่อเปิดกล่องและเก็บของดรอปครบแล้วเท่านั้น!
    if (not hasUncollectedClearLoot) and (tick() - lastDngClearCheck > 1.5) then
        lastDngClearCheck = tick()
        local pg = lp:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
        if pg then
            local dngClear = pg:FindFirstChild("LumiDungeonClear")
            if dngClear and dngClear.Enabled then
                local replayBtn = dngClear:FindFirstChild("ReplayDungeon", true)
                if replayBtn and replayBtn.Visible and dungeonAutoRepeat then
                    simulateButtonClick(replayBtn)
                end
            end

            local dngReplay = pg:FindFirstChild("LumiDungeonReplay")
            if dngReplay and dngReplay.Enabled then
                local acceptBtn = dngReplay:FindFirstChild("ReplayAccept", true)
                if acceptBtn and acceptBtn.Visible and dungeonAutoRepeat then
                    simulateButtonClick(acceptBtn)
                elseif not dungeonAutoRepeat then
                    local leaveBtn = dngReplay:FindFirstChild("ReplayLeave", true)
                    if leaveBtn and leaveBtn.Visible then
                        simulateButtonClick(leaveBtn)
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end

    -- 0.5 Step 4 กฎเหล็ก: ถ้าประตูหินห้องบอส (BossDoor.Slab.DoorPrompt) ยังปิดอยู่ ให้ Teleport ไปเปิดประตูก่อนเสมอ!
    if curDngKindNow == "halloween" and curStepNow == 4 then
        if tryOpenBossDoorIfNeeded(root, hum) then
            return
        end
    end

    -- 1. ค้นหาเป้าหมายก่อน: ตีเสาก่อน (Priority 1) -> เคลียร์มอนสเตอร์ / มินิบอส / บอส (Priority 2)
    DngExt.t("afterChests")
    local target, dist, isPillar = getDungeonTarget()
    local tPos = (target and getObjectSafePos(target))

    -- 1.5 กู้ชีพทันทีเมื่อบอสผลักกระเด็นออกมานอกเส้นขาวหน้าโถงห้องบอส (X < -4066 ใน Step 4) -> วาร์ปกลับเข้าหลังบอสโจมตีต่อทันที!
    if curDngKindNow == "halloween" and curStepNow == 4 and target and tPos and tPos.X >= -4068 and root.Position.X < -4066 and root.Position.Z < -160 then
        local hzPush, szPush = {}, {}
        if dngAutoDodge then
            hzPush, szPush = getRealtimeDungeonHazards()
        end
        if #szPush == 0 then
            pcall(function()
                if lp:GetAttribute("MoveLockUntil") and lp:GetAttribute("MoveLockUntil") > 0 then lp:SetAttribute("MoveLockUntil", 0) end
                if lp:GetAttribute("HardLockUntil") and lp:GetAttribute("HardLockUntil") > 0 then lp:SetAttribute("HardLockUntil", 0) end
            end)
            local snapSpot = findSafeMeleeStandPos(root, target, tPos, hzPush, szPush, true)
            if not snapSpot then
                local tRoot = target:IsA("Model") and (target.PrimaryPart or target:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart")) or nil
                local backDir = tRoot and Vector3.new(-tRoot.CFrame.LookVector.X, 0, -tRoot.CFrame.LookVector.Z) or Vector3.new(1, 0, 0)
                local dirU = backDir.Magnitude > 0.1 and backDir.Unit or Vector3.new(1, 0, 0)
                snapSpot = Vector3.new(math.max(-4058, tPos.X + dirU.X * 6.5), 62.8, tPos.Z + dirU.Z * 6.5)
            end
            if lblDungeonStatus then
                pcall(function()
                    lblDungeonStatus:Set("⚡ [โดนบอสผลักออกโถง!] วาร์ปกลับเข้าห้องบอสโจมตีต่อทันที!", "run")
                end)
            end
            teleportDungeonTo(root, hum, snapSpot, tPos)
            pcall(function()
                local lockEv = lp:FindFirstChild("LumiLockTarget")
                if lockEv and lockEv:IsA("BindableEvent") then lockEv:Fire(target) end
                if Remotes and Remotes:FindFirstChild("SetTarget") then
                    Remotes.SetTarget:FireServer(target, false, true)
                end
            end)
            ensureNativeDungeonAttackOn(Remotes)
        end
    end

    -- 2. Real-time 360° AI Red Zone Hazard, Safe Zone, Inferno Judgment Braziers, Demon Skull Kick & Behind-Boss Attack
    local hazards, safeZones = {}, {}
    local precomputedSafeMeleePos, precomputedUseHover = nil, false
    if dngAutoDodge then
        hazards, safeZones = getRealtimeDungeonHazards()
    end

    -- ลำดับความสำคัญสูงสุด (เมื่อไม่มีวงเขียว Doom Nova):
    -- (1) วาร์ปจุดกระถางไฟทั้ง 4 แท่นด้วยปุ่ม 'E' ให้ครบต่อเนื่อง -> ล้างวงแดง Inferno Judgment -> กลับไปโจมตีบอสต่อทันที!
    -- (2) Teleport เข้าไปกดปุ่ม 'E' เตะ Demon Skull ทันทีเมื่อเจอ!
    if #safeZones == 0 then
        local unlitNow = findUnlitDungeonBraziers()
        if #unlitNow > 0 then
            local stillPending = DngExt.lightAllBraziersAndResume(root, hum, target, tPos, Remotes)
            if stillPending then
                return
            end
            root, hum = getCharRoot()
            if not root or not hum or hum.Health <= 0 then return end
            if dngAutoDodge then
                hazards, safeZones = getRealtimeDungeonHazards()
            end
        end

        local demonSkullsNow = DngExt.findDemonSkulls()
        if #demonSkullsNow > 0 then
            local bestSkull, bestSD = nil, math.huge
            for _, sk in ipairs(demonSkullsNow) do
                local sd = (sk.pos - root.Position).Magnitude
                if sd < bestSD then
                    bestSD = sd
                    bestSkull = sk
                end
            end
            if bestSkull then
                local stayHere = DngExt.kickDemonSkull(root, hum, bestSkull, tPos)
                if stayHere then
                    return
                end
                -- เตะหัวกะโหลกเสร็จแล้ว! รีเฟรช root + hazards แล้วพุ่งอ้อมหลังบอสรัวสกิลทันทีในเฟรมเดียวกัน!
                root, hum = getCharRoot()
                if not root or not hum or hum.Health <= 0 then return end
                if dngAutoDodge then
                    hazards, safeZones = getRealtimeDungeonHazards()
                end
            end
        end
    end

    -- Ranged mode (switch "ยืนไกลยิงสกิล", Skull King only): hold 34-52 studs, outside red zones, with line of sight,
    -- and cast only long-range skills (Quick Draw). Braziers / Demon Skulls above still come first, and while Doom
    -- Nova's green safe circles are up (#safeZones > 0) the normal dodge code below takes over.
    -- Swarm kiting (any time, mainly the Potion Witch's summons): seen live on Pahtzx, 10+ summons (6 Bone Hounds,
    -- Skull Bats, Sheet Ghost, Scarecrow) within 2-7 studs, ~750 per hit each, downed us in 6 s; hitting them one by
    -- one could not keep up. With 4+ alive monsters within 12 studs: step out to 34-52 studs from the pack's centre and
    -- fire long-range skills (Quick Draw is a 50-wide line, it hits the pack together); when they catch up, step again.
    -- OFF by default since 2026-11-25: with it on (and the other changes of that night) runs went from ~250 s
    -- (2026-11-22/23) to 800-900 s with ~100 kites a run and 1.3-2.8 downs a run. Set _G.GolfHub_SwarmKiteOn = true
    -- to try it again.
    if (_G.GolfHub_SwarmKiteOn == true or _G.GolfHub_DngRanged == true) and target and tPos and #safeZones == 0 then
        local packN, sx, sz, nearest, nearestD = 0, 0, 0, nil, math.huge
        local mfP = workspace:FindFirstChild("Monsters")
        for _, m in ipairs(mfP and mfP:GetChildren() or {}) do
            if m:IsA("Model") and m:GetAttribute("Clone") ~= true and not isFinalBossObj(m) and not isPillarObj(m)
                and isMonsterAliveObj(m) then
                local p = getObjectSafePos(m)
                if p then
                    local d = Vector3.new(p.X - root.Position.X, 0, p.Z - root.Position.Z).Magnitude
                    if d < 12 then
                        packN = packN + 1
                        sx, sz = sx + p.X, sz + p.Z
                        if d < nearestD then nearestD, nearest = d, m end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        if packN >= 4 and nearest then
            local center = Vector3.new(sx / packN, tPos.Y, sz / packN)
            local spot = DngExt.findRangedSpot(root, center, hazards, safeZones, curStepNow, curDngKindNow, nil, { 26, 28, 24, 30 })
            if spot then
                teleportDungeonTo(root, hum, spot, center)
                _G.GolfHub_SwarmKiteAt = tick() -- read by live checks
                _G.GolfHub_SwarmKites = (_G.GolfHub_SwarmKites or 0) + 1 -- per-run count in dungeon_stats.jsonl
                pcall(function()
                    local curTgt = lp:FindFirstChild("CurrentTarget")
                    if curTgt and curTgt.Value ~= nearest then curTgt.Value = nearest end
                    if Remotes and Remotes:FindFirstChild("SetTarget") then Remotes.SetTarget:FireServer(nearest, false, true) end
                end)
                DngExt.rangedNow = true
                pcall(burstDungeonSkills, nearest)
                DngExt.rangedNow = false
                if lblDungeonStatus then
                    pcall(function() lblDungeonStatus:Set(string.format("🏃 โดนรุม %d ตัว — ถอยออกมายิงสกิลระยะไกลใส่ฝูง", packN), "run") end)
                end
                return
            end
        end
    end

    -- Stall guard: if the boss's HP has not dropped for 6 s while in ranged mode, fight the normal way for 8 s.
    -- A wrong stand distance must never again leave us standing and casting into nothing.
    local rangedOk = true
    if _G.GolfHub_DngRanged == true and target and isFinalBossObj(target) then
        local bhp = tonumber(target:GetAttribute("HP")) or 0
        local maxBhp = tonumber(target:GetAttribute("MaxHP")) or 0
        local nowR = tick()
        local ddR = _G.GolfHub_DngDodge
        local invulnOrBusy = (#safeZones > 0) or (nowR < (activeBrazierUntil or 0)) or (maxBhp > 0 and math.abs(bhp - maxBhp * 0.5) < 2) or (ddR and nowR < (ddR.untilT or 0) and (ddR.skill == "Rise of the Skull King" or tostring(ddR.skill or ""):find("Skull Star Rush", 1, true) ~= nil))
        if DngExt.rangedHpTarget ~= target or bhp < (DngExt.rangedLastHp or math.huge) or invulnOrBusy then
            DngExt.rangedHpTarget, DngExt.rangedLastHp, DngExt.rangedLastDropAt = target, bhp, nowR
        end
        if nowR < (DngExt.rangedPauseUntil or 0) then
            rangedOk = false
        elseif nowR - (DngExt.rangedLastDropAt or nowR) > 8 then
            DngExt.rangedPauseUntil = nowR + 8
            DngExt.rangedLastDropAt = nowR + 8
            DngExt.rangedStalls = (DngExt.rangedStalls or 0) + 1 -- read by live checks
            _G.GolfHub_RangedStalls = (_G.GolfHub_RangedStalls or 0) + 1 -- per-run count in dungeon_stats.jsonl
            rangedOk = false
        end
    end

    if rangedOk and _G.GolfHub_DngRanged == true and target and tPos and isFinalBossObj(target) and #safeZones == 0 then
        -- Stay behind him: he snaps to face his target when he attacks (a stint behind lasted ~0.55 s), so jump back
        -- behind as soon as we fall outside ±45° of his back, at most every 0.3 s. Quick Draw is a ranged cast with no
        -- melee combo to break, so frequent jumps cost little (He asked for full use of teleport here).
        local bRoot = target:IsA("Model") and target.PrimaryPart or nil
        local bLook = bRoot and bRoot.CFrame.LookVector
        local backDirR = bLook and Vector3.new(-bLook.X, 0, -bLook.Z)
        if backDirR and backDirR.Magnitude > 0.1 then backDirR = backDirR.Unit else backDirR = nil end
        local spot = DngExt.findRangedSpot(root, tPos, hazards, safeZones, curStepNow, curDngKindNow, backDirR)
        if spot then
            local aimPos = Vector3.new(tPos.X, root.Position.Y, tPos.Z)
            if (Vector3.new(root.Position.X, 0, root.Position.Z) - Vector3.new(spot.X, 0, spot.Z)).Magnitude > 2.5
                and tick() - (DngExt.rangedMoveAt or 0) >= 0.3 then
                DngExt.rangedMoveAt = tick()
                teleportDungeonTo(root, hum, spot, aimPos)
                DngExt.rangedMoves = (DngExt.rangedMoves or 0) + 1 -- read by live checks
            end
            pcall(function()
                local lockEv = lp:FindFirstChild("LumiLockTarget")
                if lockEv and lockEv:IsA("BindableEvent") then lockEv:Fire(target) end
                local curTgt = lp:FindFirstChild("CurrentTarget")
                if curTgt and curTgt.Value ~= target then curTgt.Value = target end
                if Remotes and Remotes:FindFirstChild("SetTarget") and (tick() - lastDngSetTargetTick >= 0.35 or lastDngSetTargetObj ~= target) then
                    lastDngSetTargetObj = target
                    lastDngSetTargetTick = tick()
                    Remotes.SetTarget:FireServer(target, false, true)
                end
            end)
            DngExt.rangedNow = true
            pcall(burstDungeonSkills, target)
            DngExt.rangedNow = false
            if lblDungeonStatus then
                pcall(function()
                    lblDungeonStatus:Set(string.format("🏹 ยืนไกล %.0f studs ยิงสกิลระยะไกลใส่ %s", (Vector3.new(spot.X, 0, spot.Z) - Vector3.new(tPos.X, 0, tPos.Z)).Magnitude, tostring(target:GetAttribute("DisplayName") or target.Name)), "run")
                end)
            end
            return
        end
    end

    if dngAutoDodge then
        if #hazards > 0 or #safeZones > 0 then
            local inDanger, nearHz = isPointInDungeonDanger(root.Position, hazards, 1.2, safeZones)
            local hasOneHitHazard = false
            for _, hz in ipairs(hazards) do
                if hz.oneHit then hasOneHitHazard = true break end
            end

            -- ค้นหาช่องปลอดภัยที่ใกล้บอสที่สุด (ด้านหลังบอสก่อนเสมอ!) -> วาร์ปเข้าไปกดดันรัวสกิลต่อเนื่องไม่หยุด!
            local safeMeleePos, safeMeleeHover = nil, false
            if target and tPos then
                safeMeleePos, safeMeleeHover = findSafeMeleeStandPos(root, target, tPos, hazards, safeZones, false)
            end

            if safeMeleePos then
                precomputedSafeMeleePos = safeMeleePos
                precomputedUseHover = safeMeleeHover
            else
                -- ไม่มีจุดประชิดที่ปลอดภัยเลย (เช่น วงเขียว Doom Nova หรือ บอสหมุนรอบตัว 360° Spin)
                if inDanger then
                    lastDungeonDodgeTick = tick()
                    dngWatchSince = tick()
                    local bv = root:FindFirstChild("GolfHub_AirHoverBV")
                    if bv then bv:Destroy() end

                    local aimPos = tPos or root.Position
                    local safeSpot, dodgeDesc = calculate360OptimalSafeSpot(root.Position, aimPos, hazards, safeZones)
                    if safeSpot then
                        teleportDungeonTo(root, hum, safeSpot, aimPos)
                    end

                    if lblDungeonStatus then
                        pcall(function()
                            local hzName = nearHz and nearHz.skill or "Red Zone"
                            lblDungeonStatus:Set(string.format("🛡️ [%s | %s] Teleport หลบพ้นแล้ว รอดักสวน...", hzName, dodgeDesc), "idle")
                        end)
                    end

                    if target and tPos and (root.Position - tPos).Magnitude <= 32 then
                        pcall(function()
                            local lockEv = lp:FindFirstChild("LumiLockTarget")
                            if lockEv and lockEv:IsA("BindableEvent") then lockEv:Fire(target) end
                            local curTgt = lp:FindFirstChild("CurrentTarget")
                            if curTgt and curTgt.Value ~= target then curTgt.Value = target end
                            if Remotes and Remotes:FindFirstChild("SetTarget") and (tick() - lastDngSetTargetTick >= 0.35 or lastDngSetTargetObj ~= target) then
                                lastDngSetTargetObj = target
                                lastDngSetTargetTick = tick()
                                Remotes.SetTarget:FireServer(target, false, true)
                            end
                        end)
                        burstDungeonSkills(target)
                    end

                    return
                end

                if target and tPos then
                    lastDungeonDodgeTick = tick()
                    dngWatchSince = tick()
                    local bv = root:FindFirstChild("GolfHub_AirHoverBV")
                    if bv then bv:Destroy() end

                    local aimPos = Vector3.new(tPos.X, root.Position.Y, tPos.Z)

                    if #safeZones > 0 then
                        local safeSpot = calculate360OptimalSafeSpot(root.Position, aimPos, hazards, safeZones)
                        if safeSpot and (Vector3.new(root.Position.X, 0, root.Position.Z) - Vector3.new(safeSpot.X, 0, safeSpot.Z)).Magnitude > 1.8 then
                            teleportDungeonTo(root, hum, safeSpot, tPos)
                        else
                            teleportDungeonTo(root, hum, root.Position, aimPos)
                        end
                    else
                        -- ขยับชิดขอบนอกที่ปลอดภัยให้ใกล้บอสที่สุด (15 -> 18 -> 22 studs รอบบอส 16 ทิศทาง และไม่ปล่อยให้ติดค้างนอกโถง X < -4066!)
                        local curDist = (root.Position - tPos).Magnitude
                        local pushedOutHallway = (curDngKindNow == "halloween" and curStepNow == 4 and root.Position.X < -4066)
                        if curDist > 17 or pushedOutHallway then
                            local bestRingSpot = nil
                            local baseDir2D = Vector3.new(root.Position.X - tPos.X, 0, root.Position.Z - tPos.Z)
                            local unitBase = baseDir2D.Magnitude > 0.1 and baseDir2D.Unit or Vector3.new(0, 0, 1)
                            for _, ringR in ipairs({ 15.0, 18.0, 22.0, 27.0, 31.0, 36.0 }) do
                                for _, deg in ipairs({ 0, 30, -30, 60, -60, 90, -90, 120, -120, 150, -150, 180 }) do
                                    local rad = math.rad(deg)
                                    local c, s = math.cos(rad), math.sin(rad)
                                    local d = Vector3.new(unitBase.X * c - unitBase.Z * s, 0, unitBase.X * s + unitBase.Z * c).Unit
                                    local candP = Vector3.new(tPos.X + d.X * ringR, 62.8, tPos.Z + d.Z * ringR)
                                    local insideRoomOk = (curStepNow ~= 4) or (candP.X >= -4062)
                                    if insideRoomOk and not isPointInDungeonDanger(candP, hazards, 1.2, safeZones) then
                                        bestRingSpot = candP
                                        break
                                    end
                                end
                                if bestRingSpot then break end
                            end
                            if bestRingSpot then
                                teleportDungeonTo(root, hum, bestRingSpot, aimPos)
                            else
                                teleportDungeonTo(root, hum, root.Position, aimPos)
                            end
                        else
                            teleportDungeonTo(root, hum, root.Position, aimPos)
                        end
                    end

                    if lblDungeonStatus then
                        pcall(function()
                            local tName = target:GetAttribute("DisplayName") or target.Name
                            lblDungeonStatus:Set(string.format("🛡️ [%s] ปักหลักชิดขอบปลอดภัย รัวสกิลกดดัน %s...", (#safeZones > 0 and "Safe Zone วงเขียวใกล้บอส" or (hasOneHitHazard and "OneHit หลบด่วน!" or "ชิดขอบวงแดงรัวสกิล")), tName), "run")
                        end)
                    end

                    if (root.Position - tPos).Magnitude <= 32 then
                        pcall(function()
                            local lockEv = lp:FindFirstChild("LumiLockTarget")
                            if lockEv and lockEv:IsA("BindableEvent") then lockEv:Fire(target) end
                            local curTgt = lp:FindFirstChild("CurrentTarget")
                            if curTgt and curTgt.Value ~= target then curTgt.Value = target end
                            if Remotes and Remotes:FindFirstChild("SetTarget") and (tick() - lastDngSetTargetTick >= 0.35 or lastDngSetTargetObj ~= target) then
                                lastDngSetTargetObj = target
                                lastDngSetTargetTick = tick()
                                Remotes.SetTarget:FireServer(target, false, true)
                            end
                        end)
                        burstDungeonSkills(target)
                    end

                    return
                end
            end
        end
    end

    -- 3. ตรวจสอบว่ามีเป้าหมายหรือไม่
    DngExt.t(target and "hasTarget" or "noTarget")
    if not target then
        dngWatchTarget = nil
        dngWatchHp = nil
        dngWatchRecoveryStage = 0
        pcall(function()
            local curTgt = lp:FindFirstChild("CurrentTarget")
            if curTgt and curTgt.Value ~= nil then
                curTgt.Value = nil
            end
            local actTgt = lp:FindFirstChild("LumiActionTarget")
            if actTgt and actTgt:IsA("BindableEvent") then
                actTgt:Fire(nil, false)
            end
        end)

        if dngAutoLoot then
            local nowLoot = tick()
            local maxSlots = lp:GetAttribute("BagSlotsMax") or 240
            local usedSlots = lp:GetAttribute("BagSlotsUsed") or 0
            local bagHasSpace = (maxSlots - usedSlots) > 2

            if bagHasSpace then
                local drop = findNearestDrop(90)
                if drop and drop.Position and drop.Position.X <= -3000 then
                    local rec = attemptedDungeonLoot[drop]
                    if not rec or (rec.skipUntil or 0) <= nowLoot then
                        rec = rec or { tries = 0, skipUntil = 0 }
                        rec.tries = rec.tries + 1
                        if rec.tries >= 2 then
                            rec.skipUntil = nowLoot + 30
                        end
                        attemptedDungeonLoot[drop] = rec
                        teleportDungeonTo(root, hum, drop.Position + Vector3.new(0, 2, 0))
                        if Remotes and Remotes:FindFirstChild("PickupDrop") then
                            pcall(function() Remotes.PickupDrop:FireServer(true) end)
                        end
                        task.wait(0.03)
                        return
                    end
                end
            end

            local cs = cloneref and cloneref(game:GetService("CollectionService")) or game:GetService("CollectionService")
            local bestBox, bestBoxDist = nil, 90
            for _, box in ipairs(cs:GetTagged("LootBox")) do
                if box and box.Parent then
                    local rec = attemptedDungeonLoot[box]
                    if not rec or (rec.skipUntil or 0) <= nowLoot then
                        local bp = getObjectSafePos(box)
                        if bp and bp.X <= -3000 then
                            local bd = (bp - root.Position).Magnitude
                            if bd < bestBoxDist then
                                bestBoxDist = bd
                                bestBox = box
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
            if bestBox then
                local bp = getObjectSafePos(bestBox)
                if bp then
                    local rec = attemptedDungeonLoot[bestBox] or { tries = 0, skipUntil = 0 }
                    rec.tries = rec.tries + 1
                    if rec.tries >= 2 then
                        rec.skipUntil = nowLoot + 30
                    end
                    attemptedDungeonLoot[bestBox] = rec
                    teleportDungeonTo(root, hum, bp + Vector3.new(0, 2.2, 0))
                    if Remotes and Remotes:FindFirstChild("OpenLootBox") then
                        pcall(function() Remotes.OpenLootBox:FireServer(bestBox) end)
                    end
                    task.wait(0.04)
                    return
                end
            end
        end

        advanceDungeonPath(root, hum)
        return
    end

    if not tPos then DngExt.t("noTPos") return end

    -- จดจำพิกัดบอส/จุดสู้ล่าสุดของด่านปัจจุบัน เผื่อตัวละครตายจะได้ Teleport กลับจุดสู้บอสได้แม่นยำ 100%
    if tPos.X <= -3500 then
        DngExt.lastCombatPos = Vector3.new(tPos.X, math.max(tPos.Y, 62.5), tPos.Z)
        DngExt.lastCombatStep = curStepNow or 0
    end

    -- 4. Active Zero-Damage Watchdog
    local now = tick()
    local curMobHp = target:GetAttribute("HP")
    if curMobHp == nil then curMobHp = target:GetAttribute("Health") end
    if dngWatchTarget ~= target then
        dngWatchTarget = target
        dngWatchHp = curMobHp
        dngWatchSince = now
        dngWatchRecoveryStage = 0
    elseif curMobHp ~= dngWatchHp then
        dngWatchHp = curMobHp
        dngWatchSince = now
        dngWatchRecoveryStage = 0
    end

    local stallDuration = now - (dngWatchSince or now)
    local tooFarRecently = (now - dngLastTooFarTick) < 0.85
    local forceGroundNow = (now < dngForceGroundUntil) or tooFarRecently or (stallDuration >= 0.85)
    local rotateAngle = 0
    local needHardRetarget = false

    if stallDuration >= 4.2 and target:GetAttribute("IsBoss") ~= true and target:GetAttribute("IsMiniBoss") ~= true and not isPillar then
        dungeonTargetBlacklist[target] = now + 6.0
        dngWatchTarget = nil
        dngWatchRecoveryStage = 0
        if Remotes and Remotes:FindFirstChild("SetTarget") then
            pcall(function() Remotes.SetTarget:FireServer(nil, false) end)
        end
        return
    elseif stallDuration >= 2.0 and dngWatchRecoveryStage < 2 then
        dngWatchRecoveryStage = 2
        dngForceGroundUntil = now + 1.6
        rotateAngle = 90
        needHardRetarget = true
    elseif (stallDuration >= 0.85 or tooFarRecently) and dngWatchRecoveryStage < 1 then
        dngWatchRecoveryStage = 1
        dngForceGroundUntil = now + 1.2
        needHardRetarget = true
    end

    -- 5. อัปเดตสถานะบนหัว UI
    if lblDungeonStatus then
        pcall(function()
            local tName = target:GetAttribute("DisplayName") or target.Name
            local hpText = tostring(curMobHp or "?")
            if needHardRetarget or (forceGroundNow and dngAirHoverBackstab) then
                lblDungeonStatus:Set(string.format("🔄 [Auto-Sync โจมตี] Teleport ลงพื้นประชิด %s (HP: %s)", tName, hpText), "run")
            elseif precomputedSafeMeleePos then
                lblDungeonStatus:Set(string.format("⚡ [อ้อมหลังหลบสกิล!] Teleport ประชิดรัวสกิลใส่ %s (HP: %s)", tName, hpText), "run")
            elseif now - lastDungeonDodgeTick < 1.2 then
                lblDungeonStatus:Set(string.format("⚡ [สกิลแดงหายแล้ว!] Teleport คลุกวงใน รัวสกิลใส่ %s ต่อเนื่อง!", tName), "run")
            elseif isPillar then
                lblDungeonStatus:Set(string.format("🗼 [ตีเสาก่อน] Teleport โจมตี %s (HP: %s)", tName, hpText), "run")
            elseif dngAirHoverBackstab then
                lblDungeonStatus:Set(string.format("🦅 [Teleport Air-Hover +%.1fs] กำจัด %s (HP: %s)", dngHoverHeight or 4.5, tName, hpText), "run")
            else
                lblDungeonStatus:Set(string.format("⚔️ [Full Teleport] กำจัด %s (HP: %s)", tName, hpText), "run")
            end
        end)
    end

    -- 6. ใช้ระบบ Teleport เต็มรูปแบบ 100% ภายในดันเจี้ยน! (Position FIRST -> Target SECOND ป้องกัน "Target is too far away")
    DngExt.t("section6")
    local jumpedFar = false
    if isPlayerInDungeon() then
        local standPos, useHover
        if precomputedSafeMeleePos then
            standPos, useHover = precomputedSafeMeleePos, precomputedUseHover
        else
            standPos, useHover = findOptimalDungeonStandPos(root, target, tPos, forceGroundNow, rotateAngle)
        end
        local jumpDist = (standPos - root.Position).Magnitude
        jumpedFar = jumpDist > 12

        if useHover then
            local bv = root:FindFirstChild("GolfHub_AirHoverBV")
            if not bv then
                bv = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
                bv.Name = "GolfHub_AirHoverBV"
                bv.MaxForce = Vector3.new(0, 1e6, 0)
                bv.Velocity = Vector3.zero
                bv.Parent = root
            else
                bv.MaxForce = Vector3.new(0, 1e6, 0)
                bv.Velocity = Vector3.zero
            end
        else
            local bv = root:FindFirstChild("GolfHub_AirHoverBV")
            if bv then bv:Destroy() end
        end

        if jumpDist > 170 then
            DngExt.teleportPathTo(root, hum, standPos, tPos)
        elseif useHover or needHardRetarget or precomputedSafeMeleePos or jumpDist > 1.8 then
            teleportDungeonTo(root, hum, standPos, tPos)
        else
            local flatLook = Vector3.new(tPos.X, root.Position.Y, tPos.Z)
            local toTgt = flatLook - root.Position
            if toTgt.Magnitude >= 0.5 then
                local curLook = Vector3.new(root.CFrame.LookVector.X, 0, root.CFrame.LookVector.Z)
                if curLook.Magnitude < 0.1 or curLook.Unit:Dot(toTgt.Unit) < 0.90 then
                    root.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(root.Position, flatLook)
                end
            end
        end

        if needHardRetarget then
            task.wait(0.04)
        elseif jumpedFar then
            task.wait(0.03)
        end
    end

    -- 7. ล็อคเป้าผ่านระบบหลักของเกม (LumiLockTarget + SetTarget) เพื่อให้ปุ่ม Attack หลักของเกมตีประสาน 100%
    local targetChanged = (lastDngSetTargetObj ~= target)
    pcall(function()
        local curTgt = lp:FindFirstChild("CurrentTarget")
        if needHardRetarget and Remotes and Remotes:FindFirstChild("SetTarget") then
            Remotes.SetTarget:FireServer(nil, false)
            if curTgt then curTgt.Value = nil end
            task.wait(0.03)
        end

        if targetChanged or needHardRetarget or (curTgt and curTgt.Value ~= target) then
            local lockEv = lp:FindFirstChild("LumiLockTarget")
            if lockEv and lockEv:IsA("BindableEvent") then
                lockEv:Fire(target)
            elseif curTgt then
                curTgt.Value = target
            end
        end

        if Remotes and Remotes:FindFirstChild("SetTarget") and (targetChanged or needHardRetarget or jumpedFar or (tick() - lastDngSetTargetTick >= 0.35)) then
            lastDngSetTargetObj = target
            lastDngSetTargetTick = tick()
            Remotes.SetTarget:FireServer(target, false, true)
        end

        if not dngSkillOnly and lp:GetAttribute("ActionMode") == true then
            local actAtk = lp:FindFirstChild("LumiActionAttack")
            if actAtk and actAtk:IsA("BindableEvent") then
                actAtk:Fire()
            end
        end

        if lp:GetAttribute("MoveLockUntil") and lp:GetAttribute("MoveLockUntil") > 0 then
            lp:SetAttribute("MoveLockUntil", 0)
        end
        if lp:GetAttribute("HardLockUntil") and lp:GetAttribute("HardLockUntil") > 0 then
            lp:SetAttribute("HardLockUntil", 0)
        end
    end)

    -- 8. รัวสกิลเฉพาะช่อง 1-5 ที่เลือกเปิดไว้ (Instant Rapid Burst Continuous Combat)
    burstDungeonSkills(target)
end

-- ลูปหลักของ In-Dungeon Combat (Smooth Stable Loop — Clamp >= 0.03s ป้องกัน 200Hz Remote Flood)
local function runDungeonCombatLoop()
    lastDngSetTargetObj = nil
    lastDngSetTargetTick = 0
    lastDngNativeAutoTryTick = 0
    dngNativeSyncedThisRun = false
    dngLockedStandTarget = nil
    dngWatchTarget = nil
    dngWatchRecoveryStage = 0
    DngExt.lastAliveChar = lp.Character
    DngExt.wasDownedOrDead = false
    DngExt.lastCombatPos = nil
    DngExt.lastCombatStep = 0
    DngExt.lastRespawnRetTick = 0
    DngExt.lastChestOpenedTick = 0
    DngExt.clearFirstSeenTick = 0
    DngExt.braziersCompletedUntil = 0
    DngExt.bossStunRushUntil = 0
    table.clear(DngExt.kickedDemonSkulls)
    table.clear(DngExt.openedChests)
    table.clear(attemptedDungeonLoot)

    -- เปิดปุ่ม [F] AUTO หลักของตัวเกมส์ทันทีที่เริ่มลุยดันเจี้ยน!
    pcall(ensureNativeDungeonAttackOn)
    DngExt.pumpRun = DngExt.pumpRun + 1
    task.spawn(DngExt.skillPump, DngExt.pumpRun, _G.GolfHub_Gen)

    while dungeonFarmActive and isPlayerInDungeon() do
        if isCurrentDungeonExpired() then
            break
        end
        pcall(ensureNativeDungeonAttackOn)
        if dngCombatActive then
            local ok, err = pcall(dungeonCombatStep)
            if not ok then
                warn("[DUNGEON COMBAT ERROR]:", err)
            end
        else
            local r = getCharRoot()
            local bv = r and r:FindFirstChild("GolfHub_AirHoverBV")
            if bv then bv:Destroy() end
        end
        task.wait(math.max(0.03, tonumber(dngCombatTick) or 0.05))
    end
    -- เมื่อออกจากดันเจี้ยนแล้วเท่านั้น -> ปิด RespawnKeepBot + ปิดปุ่ม [F] AUTO อัตโนมัติ (ถ้ายังอยู่ในดันเจี้ยนให้เปิดค้างตลอดเวลา!)
    if not (isPlayerInDungeon and isPlayerInDungeon()) then
        pcall(function() lp:SetAttribute("RespawnKeepBot", false) end)
        if forceStopNativeAutoBot and not farmActive and not bossFarmActive then
            forceStopNativeAutoBot()
        end
    end
    local r = getCharRoot()
    local bv = r and r:FindFirstChild("GolfHub_AirHoverBV")
    if bv then bv:Destroy() end
end

-- ลูปควบคุมการลงดันเจี้ยนหลัก (Dungeon Master Loop)
if getgenv then
    getgenv().GolfHub_Debug = getgenv().GolfHub_Debug or {}
    getgenv().GolfHub_Debug.dngStep = dungeonCombatStep
    getgenv().GolfHub_Debug.dngParts = {
        getDungeonTarget = getDungeonTarget, tryOpenBossDoorIfNeeded = tryOpenBossDoorIfNeeded,
        getRealtimeDungeonHazards = getRealtimeDungeonHazards, findOptimalDungeonStandPos = findOptimalDungeonStandPos,
        isCurrentDungeonExpired = isCurrentDungeonExpired, getRealtimeDungeonStep = getRealtimeDungeonStep,
        findUnlitDungeonBraziers = findUnlitDungeonBraziers, teleportDungeonTo = teleportDungeonTo,
    }
    getgenv().GolfHub_Debug.dngState = function()
        return {
            farmActive = dungeonFarmActive, combatActive = dngCombatActive, teleportAttack = dngTeleportAttack,
            autoDodge = dngAutoDodge, autoSkill = dngAutoSkill, skillOnly = dngSkillOnly, tick = dngCombatTick,
        }
    end
end

function dungeonMainLoop()
    while dungeonFarmActive do
        local root, hum = getCharRoot()
        if not root or not hum or hum.Health <= 0 or lp:GetAttribute("DungeonDowned") == true then
            if not isPlayerInDungeon() then
                local Remotes = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("Remotes")
                if Remotes and Remotes:FindFirstChild("RespawnChoice") then
                    pcall(function() Remotes.RespawnChoice:FireServer("town") end)
                end
                -- no clicking by button name here: next to the free button are the Robux and Diamond ones
            else
                -- อยู่ในดันเจี้ยน: รอฟื้นคืนชีพที่หน้าทางเข้าดันเจี้ยน (ห้ามกดยอมแพ้กลับเมือง)
                if lblDungeonStatus then
                    pcall(function() lblDungeonStatus:Set("⏳ ตัวละครล้มในดันเจี้ยน กำลังรอเกิดใหม่หน้าทางเข้า...", "idle") end)
                end
            end
            task.wait(1.0)
            continue
        end

        updateDungeonUiLabels()

        local inDng, curDng = isPlayerInDungeon()

        if inDng then
            -- ==================== CASE A: อยู่ในดันเจี้ยน ====================
            if isCurrentDungeonExpired() then
                if lblDungeonStatus then
                    pcall(function()
                        lblDungeonStatus:Set("⏰ ดันเจี้ยนเดิมหมดเวลา / สิ้นสุดเซสชัน! กำลังออกจากห้อง...", "idle")
                    end)
                end
                local r = getCharRoot()
                local bv = r and r:FindFirstChild("GolfHub_AirHoverBV")
                if bv then bv:Destroy() end
                table.clear(dungeonTargetBlacklist)
                dngWatchTarget = nil
                dngWatchHp = nil
                dngWatchRecoveryStage = 0
                local Remotes = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("Remotes")
                if Remotes and Remotes:FindFirstChild("DungeonAction") then
                    pcall(function() Remotes.DungeonAction:FireServer("leave") end)
                end
                task.wait(2.5)
                continue
            end

            currentDungeonCheckpointIdx = 1
            if lblDungeonStatus then
                pcall(function()
                    lblDungeonStatus:Set(string.format("⚔️ กำลังลุยดันเจี้ยน: %s [%s]", dungeonSelectedName, dungeonSelectedDiffName), "run")
                end)
            end
            local loopOk, loopErr = pcall(runDungeonCombatLoop)
            if not loopOk then
                warn("[DUNGEON LOOP ERROR]:", loopErr)
            end

            -- เมื่อออกจากดันเจี้ยนกลับมาเมือง
            task.wait(2.0)
            if not dungeonFarmActive then break end

            if not dungeonAutoRepeat then
                if lblDungeonStatus then pcall(function() lblDungeonStatus:Set("✅ ลงดันเจี้ยนจบรอบเรียบร้อย!", "idle") end) end
                dungeonFarmActive = false
                if toggleDungeonFarm then pcall(function() toggleDungeonFarm:Set(false) end) end
                break
            end
        else
            -- ==================== CASE B: อยู่นอกดันเจี้ยน (เมือง) ====================
            -- ปิดบอท Auto ฝั่ง Client ทันที กันบอทเกมลากออกไปโซน 1 ตีกระต่าย
            if forceStopNativeAutoBot then forceStopNativeAutoBot() end

            if lblDungeonStatus then
                pcall(function()
                    lblDungeonStatus:Set("🚶 เดินทางเข้าหา GateKeeper NPC หน้าประตูดันเจี้ยน...", "run")
                end)
            end

            -- เดินไปยังหน้าซุ้มประตูวาร์ปและคุยกับ GateKeeper NPC
            local reachedNpc = walkToDungeonNpcAndInteract(function(msg)
                if lblDungeonStatus then pcall(function() lblDungeonStatus:Set(msg, "run") end) end
            end)

            if reachedNpc and dungeonFarmActive then
                if lblDungeonStatus then
                    pcall(function()
                        lblDungeonStatus:Set(string.format("🚪 คุยกับ GateKeeper แล้ว! กำลังกดเข้า %s (%s)...", dungeonSelectedName, dungeonSelectedDiffName), "run")
                    end)
                end

                task.wait(0.4)
                enterSelectedDungeon()

                -- รอดันเจี้ยนโหลด (Timeout 15 วินาที)
                local waitStart = tick()
                while dungeonFarmActive and (tick() - waitStart < 15) do
                    task.wait(0.5)
                    if isPlayerInDungeon() then
                        break
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        task.wait(1.0)
    end

    if lblDungeonStatus then pcall(function() lblDungeonStatus:Set("⏹ หยุดลงดันเจี้ยนแล้ว", "off") end) end
end

task.wait() -- yield point: spread dungeon setup over frames
-- ---------- GUI CONSTRUCTION: TAB FARM & DUNGEON ----------
dungeonFarmTab = mainGroup:Tab({
    Name        = "Farm&Dungeon",
    Title       = "Dungeon Farm & Auto Join",
    Description = "ระบบลงดันเจี้ยนอัตโนมัติ (Skull King / Gold Dungeon) — แยกการทำงานเป็นอิสระจากแมพด้านนอก 100%",
})

-- ---------- SECTION 0: Status & Progress ----------
local secDngStatus = dungeonFarmTab:Section()
secDngStatus:Header({
    Name        = "Dungeon Status & Progress",
    Description = "สถานะการทำงานบอทดันเจี้ยน จำนวนรอบที่เหลือ และจำนวนกุญแจ (Real-time)",
    Collapsible = false,
})
lblDungeonStatus   = secDngStatus:Status({ Name = "Dungeon Status", Text = "⏹ ปิดระบบดันเจี้ยน (Idle)", Tone = "off" })
lblDungeonState    = secDngStatus:Value({ Name = "Current State", Text = "🏡 อยู่นอกดันเจี้ยน" })
lblDungeonRunsLeft = secDngStatus:Value({ Name = "Runs Left Today", Text = "กำลังตรวจสอบ..." })
lblDungeonKeys     = secDngStatus:Value({ Name = "Dungeon Keys", Text = "กำลังตรวจสอบ..." })

-- ---------- SECTION 1: Dungeon Selection & Entry ----------
local secDngConfig = dungeonFarmTab:Section()
secDngConfig:Header({
    Name        = "Dungeon Selection & Auto Join",
    Description = "เลือกดันเจี้ยนและระดับความยากที่ต้องการลง พร้อมระบบกดยืนยันอัตโนมัติ",
    Collapsible = false,
})

toggleDungeonFarm = secDngConfig:Toggle({
    Name        = "Auto Farm Dungeon",
    Description = "เปิด/ปิด ระบบลงดันเจี้ยนอัตโนมัติ (เมื่อเปิดจะหยุด Auto Farm และ Farm&Boss ภายนอกทันที)",
    Default     = false,
    Flag        = "Dungeon_AutoFarmToggle",
    Callback    = function(val)
        dungeonFarmActive = val
        if val then
            -- Mutual exclusivity: ปิดโหมดฟาร์มอื่นเพื่อป้องกันการแย่งควบคุมตัวละคร
            if farmActive then
                farmActive = false
                if toggleAutoFarm then pcall(function() toggleAutoFarm:Set(false) end) end
                if farmThread then task.cancel(farmThread) farmThread = nil end
            end
            if bossFarmActive then
                bossFarmActive = false
                if toggleBossHunt then pcall(function() toggleBossHunt:Set(false) end) end
                if bossFarmThread then task.cancel(bossFarmThread) bossFarmThread = nil end
            end

            -- เปิดปุ่ม [F] AUTO หลักของตัวเกมส์ทันทีเมื่ออยู่ในดันเจี้ยน (หรือปิดไว้ก่อนหากยังเดินคุย NPC ในเมือง แล้วเปิดทันทีที่เข้าดัน!)
            dngCombatActive = true
            if toggleDungeonCombat then
                pcall(function() toggleDungeonCombat:Set(true) end)
            end
            lastDngNativeAutoTryTick = 0
            dngNativeSyncedThisRun = false
            if isPlayerInDungeon and isPlayerInDungeon() then
                ensureNativeDungeonAttackOn()
                task.delay(0.55, function()
                    if dungeonFarmActive and isPlayerInDungeon and isPlayerInDungeon() then
                        ensureNativeDungeonAttackOn()
                    end
                end)
            else
                if forceStopNativeAutoBot then forceStopNativeAutoBot() end
            end

            if autoEquipAlways and equipBestGearForCurrentJob then
                if GOLFGUI.IsLoadingConfig or isBootstrappingConfig then
                    task.delay(10.0, function()
                        if (dungeonFarmActive or farmActive) and equipBestGearForCurrentJob then
                            pcall(equipBestGearForCurrentJob, true)
                        end
                    end)
                else
                    task.spawn(function() equipBestGearForCurrentJob(true) end)
                end
            end

            if dungeonThread then task.cancel(dungeonThread) end
            dungeonThread = task.spawn(dungeonMainLoop)
        else
            if dungeonThread then task.cancel(dungeonThread) dungeonThread = nil end
            if not (isPlayerInDungeon and isPlayerInDungeon()) then
                pcall(function() lp:SetAttribute("RespawnKeepBot", false) end)
                setBotRunning(false)
            end
            if lblDungeonStatus then pcall(function() lblDungeonStatus:Set("⏹ หยุดลงดันเจี้ยนแล้ว", "off") end) end
            local root, hum = getCharRoot()
            if root then
                local bv = root:FindFirstChild("GolfHub_AirHoverBV")
                if bv then bv:Destroy() end
            end
            if hum and root then hum:MoveTo(root.Position) end
            pcall(function()
                local Remotes = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("Remotes")
                if Remotes and Remotes:FindFirstChild("SetTarget") then
                    Remotes.SetTarget:FireServer(nil, false)
                end
                local curTgt = lp:FindFirstChild("CurrentTarget")
                if curTgt then curTgt.Value = nil end
            end)
        end
    end,
})

dropdownDungeonSelect = secDngConfig:Dropdown({
    Name        = "Select Dungeon",
    Description = "เลือกดันเจี้ยนที่ต้องการลง",
    Options     = {
        "Skull King's Crypt (Lv.90+)",
        "Gold Dungeon (Lv.90+)",
        "Endless Tower (เร็วๆ นี้)",
    },
    Default     = "Skull King's Crypt (Lv.90+)",
    Flag        = "Dungeon_SelectedDungeon",
    Callback    = function(opt)
        dungeonSelectedName = opt
        if opt:find("Skull King") then
            dungeonSelectedId = "halloween"
        elseif opt:find("Gold") then
            dungeonSelectedId = "gold"
        end
        updateDungeonUiLabels()
    end,
})

dropdownDungeonDiff = secDngConfig:Dropdown({
    Name        = "Dungeon Difficulty",
    Description = "เลือกระดับความยาก (Gold Dungeon จะปรับเป็น Normal อัตโนมัติ)",
    Options     = {
        "Easy (ง่าย - มอนสเตอร์อ่อนลง รางวัลลดลง)",

    },
    Default     = "Easy (ง่าย - มอนสเตอร์อ่อนลง รางวัลลดลง)",
    Flag        = "Dungeon_SelectedDifficulty",
    Callback    = function(opt)
        dungeonSelectedDiffName = opt

        else
            dungeonSelectedDiffId = "easy"
        end
    end,
})

        end

--   Sky Pillars (circles on players + 8-62 extra) · Heaven's Judgment (room wipe: stand in the safe circle r9, 2.4s warning)

;(function()
local worldTab = mainGroup:Tab({
    Name        = "Farm&World",
    Title       = "GOLF HUB — Farm & World Boss",
    Description = "ตีบอสโลก (World Boss) อัตโนมัติ — เดิน + โจมตี + สกิล + หลบ ด้วยการเดินเท่านั้น ห้ามวาร์ป",
})
local wbOn, wbResumeFarm, wbAutoEnter = false, true, true
local wbEntries, wbMaxTries, wbCounted = 0, 3, false
local wbPausedFarm = false -- true while the World Boss holds Auto Farm / Boss Hunt / Dungeon Farm off
local wbBusy = false
-- Pause every hub mode that moves the character (they would fight the World Boss walker for the legs), and keep
-- the player's own choice in the config file (SaveAs) so a reload during the fight does not leave them off.
local wbHeld = {}
local function wbPauseAll()
    for _, t in ipairs({ toggleAutoFarm, toggleBossHunt, toggleDungeonFarm }) do
        if t and t.State == true then
            table.insert(wbHeld, t)
            t.SaveAs = true
            pcall(function() t:Set(false) end)
        end
    end
    if #wbHeld > 0 then wbPausedFarm = true end
end
local function wbReleaseAll()
    wbPausedFarm = false
    local held = wbHeld
    wbHeld = {}
    for _, t in ipairs(held) do
        local stillHeld = t.SaveAs == true -- a click by the player meanwhile cleared it: their choice wins
        t.SaveAs = nil
        if stillHeld and wbResumeFarm and t.State ~= true then pcall(function() t:Set(true) end) end
    end
    -- "resume after World Boss" off: the modes stay off, and now the file says so too
    if not wbResumeFarm and #held > 0 and GOLFGUI.TriggerAutoSave then GOLFGUI:TriggerAutoSave() end
end
local valWbStatus, valWbBoss, valWbTime

local function wbFindBoss()
    local mon = workspace:FindFirstChild("Monsters")
    if not mon then return nil end
    for _, m in ipairs(mon:GetChildren()) do
        if m:IsA("Model") and tostring(m:GetAttribute("MonsterId") or ""):find("^worldboss_") then return m end
    end
    return nil
end

local function wbTimerText()
    -- 1) the server's own end time (workspace attribute WorldBossEndsAt, server clock)
    local ends = tonumber(workspace:GetAttribute("WorldBossEndsAt"))
    if ends then
        local left = ends - workspace:GetServerTimeNow()
        if left > 0 then return string.format("%d:%02d", math.floor(left / 60), math.floor(left % 60)) end
    end
    -- 2) the sign on the town gate ("Stormhorn  -  2:06 left")
    local gate = workspace:FindFirstChild("WorldBossGate")
    local cd = gate and gate:FindFirstChild("Countdown", true)
    if cd and cd:IsA("TextLabel") then
        local t = cd.Text:match("(%d+:%d%d)")
        if t then return t end
    end
    -- 3) old timer window
    local g = lp.PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("HuntWorldBossTimer")
    if not g then return nil end
    -- the banner now reads "Stormhorn - 3:11 left", so pick the m:ss out of any label instead of a bare "3:11"
    for _, x in ipairs(g:GetDescendants()) do
        if x:IsA("TextLabel") and x.Visible then
            local t = x.Text:match("(%d+:%d%d)")
            if t then return t end
        end
    end
    return nil
end

local function wbSetStatus(msg)
    if valWbStatus then pcall(function() valWbStatus:UpdateName(msg) end) end
end

local sW1 = worldTab:Section()
sW1:Header({ Name = "World Boss (ทุกตัว เช่น Stormhorn / Vesperbloom)", Description = "เดินเข้าหาบอส ยืนด้านหลังบอส โจมตี ใช้สกิล และเดินหลบสกิลตามสัญญาณเตือนของเซิร์ฟเวอร์ (ไม่ใช้การวาร์ปเลย)", Collapsible = false })
valWbStatus = sW1:Value({ Name = "Status", Text = "ปิดอยู่" })
valWbBoss   = sW1:Value({ Name = "Boss", Text = "-" })
valWbTime   = sW1:Value({ Name = "เวลาที่เหลือ", Text = "-" })
sW1:Toggle({
    Name = "Auto World Boss", Description = "เมื่อมี World Boss ในห้องที่คุณอยู่ จะเดินเข้าไปตีและหลบสกิลให้อัตโนมัติ (ต้องเข้าด่านบอสโลกเอง)",
    Default = false, Callback = function(v) wbOn = v if not v then wbSetStatus("ปิดอยู่") end end,
}, "World_Auto")
sW1:Toggle({
    Name = "Auto Enter ประตูบอสโลก", Description = "เมื่อมีประตู World Boss ในเมือง จะเดินเข้าประตูให้เอง (ถ้าอยู่นอกเมืองจะใช้เสาวาร์ปของเกมกลับเมืองก่อน)",
    Default = true, Callback = function(v) wbAutoEnter = v end,
}, "World_AutoEnter")
sW1:Slider({
    Name = "เข้าสู้ได้สูงสุดกี่ครั้งต่อรอบบอส", Description = "ถ้าตายแล้วเข้าซ้ำครบจำนวนนี้ จะหยุดจนกว่าบอสรอบใหม่ (กันตายวน)",
    Minimum = 1, Maximum = 10, Default = 3, Decimals = 0, Precision = 0,
    Callback = function(v) wbMaxTries = math.floor(tonumber(v) or 3) end,
}, "World_MaxTries")
sW1:Toggle({
    Name = "กลับไปฟาร์มต่อเมื่อบอสจบ", Description = "ระหว่างสู้บอสโลกจะหยุด Auto Farm ชั่วคราว และเปิดกลับเมื่อบอสตายหรือหมดเวลา",
    Default = true, Callback = function(v) wbResumeFarm = v end,
}, "World_ResumeFarm")

-- free respawn only (never the Robux / Diamonds buttons): the death window's "Respawn in town (free)" = RespawnChoice("town")
local wbAutoRespawn = true
sW1:Toggle({
    Name = "Auto Respawn ในเมือง (ฟรี)", Description = "เมื่อตาย จะกด Respawn in town (free) ให้เอง ไม่ใช้ Robux / Diamonds",
    Default = true, Callback = function(v) wbAutoRespawn = v end,
}, "World_AutoRespawn")
task.spawn(function()
    local gen = _G.GolfHub_Gen
    local diedAt
    while gen == _G.GolfHub_Gen do
        task.wait(0.5)
        -- from being dead, not from a window name: the game shows HuntRespawn or the older LumiRespawn, depending on the client.
        -- Not in a dungeon (it revives at the entrance; "town" would give the run up), not while a respawn is counting down.
        local hum = lp.Character and lp.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        local dead = hum ~= nil and hum.Health <= 0
            and lp:GetAttribute("DungeonDowned") ~= true
            and workspace:GetAttribute("PlaceRole") ~= "Dungeon"
            and game.PlaceId ~= DUNGEON_PLACE_ID
            and not (isPlayerInDungeon and isPlayerInDungeon())
            and (tonumber(lp:GetAttribute("RespawnHereAt")) or 0) <= 0
        if wbAutoRespawn and dead then
            diedAt = diedAt or tick()
            if tick() - diedAt >= 2 then
                diedAt = tick() + 6 -- retry no faster than every 8s
                local rem = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("Remotes")
                if rem and rem:FindFirstChild("RespawnChoice") then pcall(function() rem.RespawnChoice:FireServer("town") end) end
            end
        else
            diedAt = nil
        end
    end
end)

-- World-boss dodge planner. Works from the server's own BossFx "warn" zones (exact Center / Radius / Inner / Dir /
-- Half / Length / Width), so it covers every world boss, e.g. Vesperbloom's 9 skills:
--   circle (Petal Storm, Pounce Crush, Great Hop, Thorn Rain) → step out past the edge
--   donut (Sweep Spin, Inner 16.9) → into the hole if that is closer, else out past the edge
--   cone (Scythe Slash 110°) → behind the boss · line (Twin Reap, Devour) → sideways off the beam
--   room with Safe circles (Night Bloom) → into the nearest safe circle
-- The planned spot is checked against every zone of the same warning before it is used.
local wbDodgeGoal, wbDodgeUntil, wbDodgeSkill = nil, 0, nil
local wbExtraMargin = 0 -- extra edge margin for the skill being planned (one-hit skills get more room)
local function wbFlat(v) return Vector3.new(v.X, 0, v.Z) end
local function wbInZone(z, p)
    local c = typeof(z.Center) == "Vector3" and z.Center or nil
    -- box zones (Skull Star Rush, Execution Cleave, ...) come as CFrame + Length + Width, centred on the CFrame
    if not c and typeof(z.CFrame) == "CFrame" and tonumber(z.Length) and tonumber(z.Width) then
        local d = wbFlat(p - z.CFrame.Position)
        local look = wbFlat(z.CFrame.LookVector)
        if look.Magnitude < 0.01 then return false end
        look = look.Unit
        local along = math.abs(d:Dot(look))
        local side = math.abs(d:Dot(Vector3.new(-look.Z, 0, look.X)))
        return along <= tonumber(z.Length) / 2 + 2 and side <= tonumber(z.Width) / 2 + 2.5
    end
    if not c then return false end
    local d = wbFlat(p - c)
    local dist = d.Magnitude
    local kind = tostring(z.Kind or "")
    if z.Safe then return false end
    if kind == "cone" then
        local dir = (typeof(z.Dir) == "Vector3" and wbFlat(z.Dir)) or (typeof(z.CFrame) == "CFrame" and wbFlat(z.CFrame.LookVector)) or nil
        if not dir or dir.Magnitude < 0.01 or dist > (tonumber(z.Radius) or 0) + 2 then return dist <= (tonumber(z.Radius) or 0) and not dir end
        if dist < 1 then return true end
        local zHalf = tonumber(z.Half)
        -- BossFx sends cone z.Half in radians (e.g. 0.611 rad = 35 deg); calling math.rad on it collapsed cones to < 1 deg
        local half = (zHalf and (zHalf <= math.pi and zHalf or math.rad(zHalf))) or math.rad((tonumber(z.Angle) or 90) / 2)
        return math.acos(math.clamp(d.Unit:Dot(dir.Unit), -1, 1)) <= half + 0.15
    end
    if tonumber(z.Length) and tonumber(z.Width) then
        local dir = typeof(z.Dir) == "Vector3" and wbFlat(z.Dir) or nil
        if not dir or dir.Magnitude < 0.01 then return false end
        dir = dir.Unit
        local along = d:Dot(dir)
        local side = math.abs(d:Dot(Vector3.new(-dir.Z, 0, dir.X)))
        return along >= -2 and along <= tonumber(z.Length) + 2 and side <= tonumber(z.Width) / 2 + 2.5
    end
    local inner = tonumber(z.Inner) or 0
    return dist <= (tonumber(z.Radius) or 0) + 1.2 + wbExtraMargin and dist >= inner - 1.0 - wbExtraMargin
end
local function wbPlanDodge(zones, bossPart, skillName)
    -- tight edges for ordinary skills (stay in reach), wide edges for skills that kill in one hit
    wbExtraMargin = 0
    local dngExt = _G.GolfHub_DngExt
    pcall(function()
        local info = dngExt and dngExt.lookupSkill and dngExt.lookupSkill(skillName)
        if info and info.oneHit then wbExtraMargin = 3 end
    end)
    for _, z in ipairs(type(zones) == "table" and zones or {}) do
        if type(z) == "table" and z.OneHit then wbExtraMargin = 3 end
    end
    -- zones carry a "Reach" (Hex Bolt: Radius 7, Reach 8): the real hit reaches past the drawn circle, so for
    -- one-hit skills a quarter of it is added on top (Hex Bolt escape ~14.5 studs from its centre)
    if wbExtraMargin > 0 then
        local reachMax = 0
        for _, z in ipairs(type(zones) == "table" and zones or {}) do
            if type(z) == "table" and not z.Safe and tonumber(z.Reach) then reachMax = math.max(reachMax, tonumber(z.Reach)) end
        end
        wbExtraMargin = wbExtraMargin + reachMax * 0.25
    end
    local r = getCharRoot()
    if not r or type(zones) ~= "table" then return nil end
    local me = r.Position
    local inDngPlan = (me.X <= -3000) and (dngExt ~= nil)
    local safes, danger = {}, {}
    for _, z in ipairs(zones) do
        if type(z) == "table" then
            if z.Safe then safes[#safes + 1] = z else danger[#danger + 1] = z end
        end
    end
    local safeBounds = _G.GolfHub_DodgeSafeBounds
    local function clear(p)
        if inDngPlan and dngExt.kindNow == "halloween" and dngExt.stepNow == 4 and p.X < -4062 then return false end
        if type(safeBounds) == "table" and #safeBounds > 0 then
            local inSafe = false
            for _, s in ipairs(safeBounds) do
                if s.c and wbFlat(p - s.c).Magnitude <= math.max(1.5, (tonumber(s.r) or 8) - 2.0) then
                    inSafe = true
                    break
                end
            end
            if not inSafe then return false end
        end
        for _, z in ipairs(danger) do if wbInZone(z, p) then return false end end
        return true
    end
    local function at(p)
        if inDngPlan and dngExt.snapDungeonFloor then
            return dngExt.snapDungeonFloor(p, me.Y)
        end
        return Vector3.new(p.X, me.Y, p.Z)
    end
    -- room wipe with safe circles: go to the nearest one
    if #safes > 0 then
        local bpos = bossPart and bossPart.Position
        local best, bd, bestZ = nil, math.huge, nil
        for _, z in ipairs(safes) do
            if typeof(z.Center) == "Vector3" then
                -- with a boss, prefer the safe circle closest to him (we keep attacking from inside it)
                local d = bpos and wbFlat(z.Center - bpos).Magnitude or wbFlat(z.Center - me).Magnitude
                if d < bd then bd, best, bestZ = d, z.Center, z end
            end
        end
        if best then
            -- stand on the boss side of the circle, 2.5 studs inside its edge, so the attack still reaches him
            local R = tonumber(bestZ.Radius) or 0
            if bpos and R > 4 then
                local toward = wbFlat(bpos - best)
                if toward.Magnitude > 0.5 then
                    -- as close to the boss as the circle allows, but no closer than his body edge
                    local brad = tonumber(bossPart.Parent and bossPart.Parent:GetAttribute("Radius")) or 1.5
                    local maxStep = math.max(0, toward.Magnitude - (brad + 1))
                    -- 5 studs inside the edge: the game's auto-bot keeps stepping toward the boss during the 3.8 s
                    -- wait, and a spot 2.5 in from the edge let it walk out of the circle (one-hit death seen live)
