                    -- (with the 1-stud snap-back below, 3.5 in from the edge keeps both the reach and a safety margin)
                    return at(best + toward.Unit * math.min(math.max(0, R - 3.5), maxStep))
                end
            end
            return at(best)
        end
    end
    local hit = false
    for _, z in ipairs(danger) do if wbInZone(z, me) then hit = true break end end
    -- dungeon only: the spot we are already heading to (an earlier warning's escape) counts too — a new warning
    -- that covers it must re-plan even while we are not in its zone yet
    local cp = _G.GolfHub_DodgeCheckPoint
    if not hit and typeof(cp) == "Vector3" then
        for _, z in ipairs(danger) do if wbInZone(z, cp) then hit = true break end end
    end
    if not hit then return nil end
    -- candidate spots: per-zone escapes first, then a ring search around us; take the closest clear one
    local cands = {}
    local bp = bossPart and bossPart.Position
    for _, z in ipairs(danger) do
        local c = typeof(z.Center) == "Vector3" and z.Center or nil
        if not c and typeof(z.CFrame) == "CFrame" and tonumber(z.Width) then
            -- box: step sideways off its long axis (both sides offered; the closest clear one wins)
            local look = wbFlat(z.CFrame.LookVector)
            if look.Magnitude > 0.01 then
                look = look.Unit
                local perp = Vector3.new(-look.Z, 0, look.X)
                local off = wbFlat(me - z.CFrame.Position):Dot(perp)
                local need = tonumber(z.Width) / 2 + 5
                cands[#cands + 1] = at(me + perp * ((off >= 0 and 1 or -1) * need - off))
                cands[#cands + 1] = at(me + perp * ((off >= 0 and -1 or 1) * need - off))
            end
        end
        if c then
            local away = wbFlat(me - c)
            if away.Magnitude < 0.5 then away = bp and wbFlat(me - bp) or Vector3.new(1, 0, 0) end
            if away.Magnitude < 0.5 then away = Vector3.new(1, 0, 0) end
            away = away.Unit
            local R = tonumber(z.Radius) or 0
            if tonumber(z.Inner) and tonumber(z.Inner) > 4 then
                cands[#cands + 1] = at(c + away * math.max(1, tonumber(z.Inner) - 5))
            end
            if tostring(z.Kind) == "cone" then
                local dir = (typeof(z.Dir) == "Vector3" and wbFlat(z.Dir)) or (typeof(z.CFrame) == "CFrame" and wbFlat(z.CFrame.LookVector)) or nil
                if dir and dir.Magnitude > 0.01 then
                    dir = dir.Unit
                    local perp = Vector3.new(-dir.Z, 0, dir.X)
                    cands[#cands + 1] = at(c - dir * 7)
                    cands[#cands + 1] = at(c - dir * 4)
                    cands[#cands + 1] = at(c + perp * 7)
                    cands[#cands + 1] = at(c - perp * 7)
                end
            end
            if tonumber(z.Width) and typeof(z.Dir) == "Vector3" and wbFlat(z.Dir).Magnitude > 0.01 then
                local dir = wbFlat(z.Dir).Unit
                local perp = Vector3.new(-dir.Z, 0, dir.X)
                local s = (wbFlat(me - c):Dot(perp) >= 0) and 1 or -1
                cands[#cands + 1] = at(me + perp * s * (tonumber(z.Width) / 2 + 6))
                cands[#cands + 1] = at(me - perp * s * (tonumber(z.Width) / 2 + 6))
            end
            if R > 0 then cands[#cands + 1] = at(c + away * (R + 2.5 + wbExtraMargin)) end
        end
    end
    if type(safeBounds) == "table" and #safeBounds > 0 then
        for _, s in ipairs(safeBounds) do
            if s.c then
                local sr = math.max(1, (tonumber(s.r) or 8) - 3.5)
                cands[#cands + 1] = at(s.c)
                for a = 0, 315, 45 do
                    local rad = math.rad(a)
                    cands[#cands + 1] = at(s.c + Vector3.new(math.cos(rad), 0, math.sin(rad)) * sr)
                end
            end
        end
    end
    for _, dist in ipairs({ 10, 18, 28, 40 }) do
        for a = 0, 330, 30 do
            local rad = math.rad(a)
            cands[#cands + 1] = at(me + Vector3.new(math.cos(rad), 0, math.sin(rad)) * dist)
        end
    end
    -- closest clear spot; with a boss given, spots that keep us in melee reach (<= 9 studs) are preferred so the
    -- attack and skills keep landing while we dodge
    -- Real melee reach (from the game's CombatClient): flat centre distance minus the target's "Radius" attribute
    -- must be <= the player's AttackRange (7 by default) -> reach from the boss centre = AttackRange + Radius.
    -- 1 stud is kept back as a margin (the server allows RangeTolerance 4 on top, so this never falls short).
    local reach = 9
    if bossPart then
        local mdl = bossPart.Parent
        local rad = mdl and tonumber(mdl:GetAttribute("Radius")) or 1.5
        local atk = tonumber(lp:GetAttribute("AttackRange")) or 7
        reach = math.max(4, atk + rad - 1)
    end
    local bestAll, bdAll, bestAny, bdAny = nil, math.huge, nil, math.huge
    local chkOther = inDngPlan and dngExt.isDangerPos
    local keepRangedBand = inDngPlan and (_G.GolfHub_DngRanged == true) and (dngExt.stepNow == 4)
    for _, p in ipairs(cands) do
        if clear(p) then
            local d = wbFlat(p - me).Magnitude
            if bp then
                local dBoss = wbFlat(p - bp).Magnitude
                d = d + (keepRangedBand and math.abs(dBoss - 34) * 0.6 or math.max(0, dBoss - reach) * 0.6)
            end
            if d < bdAny then bdAny, bestAny = d, p end
            if chkOther and d < bdAll and not chkOther(p, 1.5) then
                bdAll, bestAll = d, p
            end
        end
    end
    return bestAll or bestAny
end
pcall(function()
    local rem = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):WaitForChild("Remotes", 10)
    local fx = rem and rem:WaitForChild("BossFx", 10)
    if not fx then return end
    _G.GolfHub_Conn(fx.OnClientEvent, function(action, bossName, skillName, zones, duration)
        local inDng = isPlayerInDungeon and isPlayerInDungeon()
        -- the skill has landed: stop dodging at once and go back to attacking (only if the hit belongs to the active dodge cast)
        if action == "hit" and inDng then
            local hitSkill = tostring(skillName or "?")
            local function hitMatchesRecord(centers, warnAt, warnDur)
                if type(zones) == "table" and #zones > 0 and type(centers) == "table" and #centers > 0 then
                    for _, z in ipairs(zones) do
                        if type(z) == "table" then
                            local cf = z.CFrame or z.cf
                            local c = z.Center or (cf and cf.Position)
                            if typeof(c) == "Vector3" then
                                for _, sc in ipairs(centers) do
                                    local dx, dz = c.X - sc.X, c.Z - sc.Z
                                    if (dx * dx + dz * dz) <= 12.25 then return true end
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                    return false
                elseif warnAt then
                    return (tick() - warnAt) >= ((warnDur or 1) * 0.70)
                end
                return true
            end
            for _, a in ipairs(_G.GolfHub_DngActiveZones or {}) do
                if a.skill == hitSkill and hitMatchesRecord(a.srcCenters, a.warnAt, a.warnDur) then
                    a.untilT = math.min(a.untilT, tick() + 0.15)
                end
            end
            local dd = _G.GolfHub_DngDodge
            -- spins keep hitting for their whole Duration after the "hit" signal: keep away until it ends
            if dd and not dd.spin then
                if dd.oneHitSkill == hitSkill and hitMatchesRecord(dd.oneHitCenters or dd.srcCenters, dd.oneHitWarnAt or dd.warnAt, dd.oneHitWarnDur or dd.warnDur) then
                    dd.oneHitUntil = 0
                    if dd.skillHit or dd.skill == hitSkill then
                        dd.untilT = math.min(dd.untilT, tick() + 0.1)
                    end
                end
                if dd.skill == hitSkill and hitMatchesRecord(dd.srcCenters, dd.warnAt, dd.warnDur) then
                    dd.skillHit = true
                    dd.untilT = math.max(dd.oneHitUntil or 0, math.min(dd.untilT, tick() + 0.1))
                end
            end
            return
        end
        if action ~= "warn" then return end
        -- Dungeon: every telegraphed skill (boss AND normal mobs: Spike Line, Pounce Slam, Scarecrow Charge, ...)
        -- gets a planned safe spot; dungeonCombatStep moves there before doing anything else.
        if inDng then
            -- Inferno Judgment's zones are the 4 braziers to LIGHT, not places to avoid: leave it to the brazier
            -- routine (dodging it walked us away from the braziers and the boss for its whole 8 s window)
            local sl = tostring(skillName or ""):lower()
            if sl:find("inferno judgment") or sl:find("brazier") then return end
            local bossPart
            pcall(function()
                local mf = workspace:FindFirstChild("Monsters")
                for _, m in ipairs(mf and mf:GetChildren() or {}) do
                    if m.Name == bossName or m:GetAttribute("DisplayName") == bossName then
                        bossPart = m:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or m.PrimaryPart
                        break
                    end
                end
            end)
            -- Fire Sword Spin is tracked via activeBossSpinUntil in getRealtimeDungeonHazards (R=15) so it does not
            -- freeze dd for 5.5s and block RangedKite / Low-HP retreat / other boss dodges.
            -- Several warnings in flight at once (the Witch's summons: Spike Line, Thorn Line, Pounce Slam together):
            -- there is one dodge plan, and each new warning used to plan against its own zones only, so its escape
            -- could land inside another line that was about to hit. In Pahtzx's last 11 runs 8 of the 11 downs were
            -- summon skills (Spike Line 4, Pounce Slam 2, Thorn Line 2). Plan against every zone still in flight.
            -- OFF by default since 2026-11-25 (see the swarm-kiting note: runs got slower and downs rose with these
            -- changes together). _G.GolfHub_MultiDodgeOn = true brings it back.
            local nowZ = tick()
            local warnSkillName = tostring(skillName or "?")
            local keepZ, mergedZ, hasLiveOneHit = {}, {}, false
            local newOneHit, newHasSafe = false, false
            local newSrcCenters = {}
            for _, z in ipairs(type(zones) == "table" and zones or {}) do
                mergedZ[#mergedZ + 1] = z
                if type(z) == "table" then
                    if z.OneHit then newOneHit = true end
                    if z.Safe then newHasSafe = true end
                    local cf = z.CFrame or z.cf
                    local sc = z.Center or (cf and cf.Position)
                    if typeof(sc) == "Vector3" then newSrcCenters[#newSrcCenters + 1] = sc end
                end
            end
            pcall(function()
                local info = _G.GolfHub_DngExt and _G.GolfHub_DngExt.lookupSkill and _G.GolfHub_DngExt.lookupSkill(skillName)
                if info and info.oneHit then newOneHit = true end
            end)
            local dngExtNow = _G.GolfHub_DngExt
            local inStep3Multi = (dngExtNow and dngExtNow.stepNow == 3)
            for _, a in ipairs(_G.GolfHub_DngActiveZones or {}) do
                if a.untilT > nowZ then
                    keepZ[#keepZ + 1] = a
                    -- Even when MultiDodgeOn is off, keep active non-safe OneHit zones AND Step 3 summon zones (Spike/Thorn/Charge/Pounce); keep Step 4 fast
                    if _G.GolfHub_MultiDodgeOn == true or (a.oneHit == true and not a.hasSafe) or (inStep3Multi and not a.hasSafe) then
                        if a.oneHit then hasLiveOneHit = true end
                        for _, z in ipairs(a.zones) do
                            if type(z) == "table" and not z.Safe then mergedZ[#mergedZ + 1] = z end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
            keepZ[#keepZ + 1] = {
                zones = type(zones) == "table" and zones or {},
                untilT = nowZ + (tonumber(duration) or 1) + 0.6,
                skill = warnSkillName,
                warnAt = nowZ,
                warnDur = tonumber(duration) or 1,
                srcCenters = (#newSrcCenters > 0) and newSrcCenters or nil,
                oneHit = newOneHit,
                hasSafe = newHasSafe,
            }
            _G.GolfHub_DngActiveZones = keepZ
            local prevDd = _G.GolfHub_DngDodge
            local prevActive = prevDd and prevDd.goal and nowZ < (prevDd.untilT or 0) and not prevDd.spin
            local prevLive = (_G.GolfHub_MultiDodgeOn == true) and prevActive
            local prevOneHitActive = prevActive and (prevDd.oneHit == true) and ((prevDd.oneHitUntil or prevDd.untilT or 0) > nowZ)
            _G.GolfHub_DodgeCheckPoint = prevActive and prevDd.goal or nil
            _G.GolfHub_DodgeSafeBounds = (prevActive and prevDd.safes and not newHasSafe) and prevDd.safes or nil
            local okGoal, goal = pcall(wbPlanDodge, mergedZ, bossPart, skillName)
            _G.GolfHub_DodgeCheckPoint = nil
            _G.GolfHub_DodgeSafeBounds = nil
            if okGoal and goal then
                -- safe circles (Doom Nova): keep them so the combat step can re-pick, every tick, the spot inside a
                -- circle that is closest to where the boss stands NOW (he walks during the 3.8 s warning)
                local safes = {}
                for _, z in ipairs(type(zones) == "table" and zones or {}) do
                    if type(z) == "table" and z.Safe and typeof(z.Center) == "Vector3" then
                        safes[#safes + 1] = { c = z.Center, r = tonumber(z.Radius) or 8 }
                    end
                end
                if #safes == 0 and prevActive and prevDd.safes then
                    safes = prevDd.safes
                end
                local newUntilT = tick() + (tonumber(duration) or 1) + 0.35
                local keepOneHitUntil = newOneHit and newUntilT or (prevOneHitActive and (prevDd.oneHitUntil or prevDd.untilT) or 0)
                local keepOneHitSkill = newOneHit and warnSkillName or (prevOneHitActive and (prevDd.oneHitSkill or prevDd.skill) or nil)
                local keepOneHitCenters = newOneHit and ((#newSrcCenters > 0) and newSrcCenters or nil) or (prevOneHitActive and (prevDd.oneHitCenters or prevDd.srcCenters) or nil)
                local keepOneHitWarnAt = newOneHit and nowZ or (prevOneHitActive and (prevDd.oneHitWarnAt or prevDd.warnAt) or nil)
                local keepOneHitWarnDur = newOneHit and (tonumber(duration) or 1) or (prevOneHitActive and (prevDd.oneHitWarnDur or prevDd.warnDur) or nil)
                _G.GolfHub_DngDodge = {
                    goal = goal,
                    untilT = math.max(newUntilT, (prevLive or prevOneHitActive or (inStep3Multi and prevActive)) and prevDd.untilT or 0),
                    skill = warnSkillName,
                    oneHit = (wbExtraMargin > 0) or (hasLiveOneHit == true) or prevOneHitActive,
                    oneHitUntil = (keepOneHitUntil and keepOneHitUntil > nowZ) and keepOneHitUntil or nil,
                    oneHitSkill = keepOneHitSkill,
                    oneHitCenters = keepOneHitCenters,
                    oneHitWarnAt = keepOneHitWarnAt,
                    oneHitWarnDur = keepOneHitWarnDur,
                    safes = (#safes > 0) and safes or nil,
                    bossPart = bossPart,
                    srcCenters = (#newSrcCenters > 0) and newSrcCenters or nil,
                    warnAt = nowZ,
                    warnDur = tonumber(duration) or 1,
                }
                -- very short warnings (Star Rush follow-up dashes, 0.3 s) cannot wait for the next combat step
                if (tonumber(duration) or 1) < 0.6 then
                    local r, h = getCharRoot()
                    if r and h and teleportDungeonTo then pcall(teleportDungeonTo, r, h, Vector3.new(goal.X, r.Position.Y, goal.Z)) end
                end
            end
            return
        end
        if not wbOn or lp:GetAttribute("InWorldBoss") ~= true then return end
        local boss = wbFindBoss()
        if not boss then return end
        local bPart = boss:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or boss.PrimaryPart or boss:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart")
        local goal = wbPlanDodge(zones, bPart, skillName)
        if goal then
            wbDodgeGoal = goal
            wbDodgeUntil = tick() + (tonumber(duration) or 1) + 0.45
            wbDodgeSkill = tostring(skillName or "?")
            local _, h = getCharRoot()
            if h then pcall(function() h:MoveTo(goal) end) end
        end
    end)
end)

task.spawn(function()
    local gen = _G.GolfHub_Gen
    local lastApproach, lastLock = 0, 0
    while gen == _G.GolfHub_Gen do
        -- fast only while World Boss is on (dodging needs it); off, the loop just refreshes the timer label
        task.wait(wbOn and 0.12 or 1)
        local tt = wbTimerText()
        if valWbTime then pcall(function() valWbTime:UpdateName(tt or "-") end) end
        if not wbOn then
            if wbPausedFarm then wbReleaseAll() end -- World Boss switched off mid-fight: give the modes back
            wbBusy = false
        else
            local boss = wbFindBoss()
            if lp:GetAttribute("InWorldBoss") ~= true then
                wbCounted = false
                -- outside the boss room: walk to the town gate and step into its Trigger (walking only; the game moves us into the room)
                local gate = workspace:FindFirstChild("WorldBossGate")
                local trig = gate and gate:FindFirstChild("Trigger")
                local timerOk = tt and tt ~= "0:00"
                if wbBusy and wbPausedFarm then wbReleaseAll() end
                wbBusy = false
                -- Not going in (no gate / tries used up): hand the character back to Auto Farm. The walk-to-gate path
                -- pauses the farm without setting wbBusy, so the check above never resumed it and the bot stood idle.
                local function wbResumeIfPaused()
                    if wbPausedFarm then wbReleaseAll() end
                end
                if not (trig and timerOk) then
                    wbEntries = 0
                    wbResumeIfPaused()
                    wbSetStatus("รอ World Boss (ยังไม่มีประตูบอสโลกในเมือง)")
                    task.wait(1.5)
                elseif isPlayerInDungeon and isPlayerInDungeon() then
                    -- Only while actually inside a run: between runs World Boss may still pause Dungeon Farm
                    -- (wbPauseAll lists toggleDungeonFarm on purpose) and hand it back after the fight.
                    -- Dungeons run in this same place, so the town gate is visible from inside a dungeon run. Going for it
                    -- here switched Dungeon Farm off and then looped on castHomeWarp, which refuses to work in a dungeon:
                    -- the run was lost and the character stood still. Let the run finish; the gate is taken after it.
                    wbResumeIfPaused()
                    wbSetStatus("มีบอสโลก — อยู่ในดันเจี้ยน รอจบรอบก่อน")
                    task.wait(2)
                elseif wbEntries >= wbMaxTries then
                    wbResumeIfPaused()
                    wbSetStatus(string.format("⛔ เข้าสู้ครบ %d ครั้งแล้ว (ตายทุกครั้ง?) — หยุดรอ World Boss รอบถัดไป", wbMaxTries))
                    task.wait(2)
                elseif not wbAutoEnter then
                    wbSetStatus("มีบอสโลก — เข้าประตูเอง (ปิด Auto Enter อยู่)")
                    task.wait(1.5)
                else
                    local r0, h0 = getCharRoot()
                    if r0 and h0 and h0.Health > 0 then
                        if r0.Position.Z < -25 then
                            -- the game's own Home Warp (stand still ~5 s) — not a script teleport
                            wbSetStatus("🌀 มีบอสโลก — ใช้ Home Warp ของเกมกลับเมืองก่อน")
                            wbPauseAll()
                            local okHome = false
                            pcall(function() okHome = castHomeWarp() end)
                            if not okHome then
                                wbSetStatus("Home Warp ยังไม่พร้อม (คูลดาวน์) — ลองใหม่อีกครู่")
                                task.wait(4)
                            end
                        else
                            wbSetStatus("🚶 มีบอสโลก — เดินเข้าประตูในเมือง")
                            wbPauseAll()
                            pcall(function() setBotRunning(false) end)
                            local goal = Vector3.new(trig.Position.X, 4.3, trig.Position.Z)
                            -- follow the town road (south alley) so the walk never cuts through the festival booth's red circle
                            pcall(function()
                                walkTownWaypoints({
                                    TOWN_COORDS.SouthAlleyEast, TOWN_COORDS.SouthAlleyCenter, TOWN_COORDS.SouthAlleyWest,
                                    Vector3.new(goal.X, 4.3, goal.Z - 8),
                                }, 5)
                            end)
                            local t0 = tick()
                            while wbOn and tick() - t0 < 25 and lp:GetAttribute("InWorldBoss") ~= true do
                                local rr, hh = getCharRoot()
                                if not rr or not hh or hh.Health <= 0 then break end
                                if Vector2.new(rr.Position.X - goal.X, rr.Position.Z - goal.Z).Magnitude < 2.5 then
                                    hh:MoveTo(goal + Vector3.new(0, 0, -1.5)) -- one more step into the trigger box
                                else
                                    hh:MoveTo(goal)
                                end
                                task.wait(0.2)
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            elseif not boss then
                if wbBusy and wbPausedFarm then wbReleaseAll() end
                wbBusy = false
                wbSetStatus("รอ World Boss (ยังไม่พบบอสในห้องนี้)")
                task.wait(1.5)
            else
                if not wbCounted then wbCounted = true wbEntries = wbEntries + 1 end
                local r, h = getCharRoot()
                local hp = tonumber(boss:GetAttribute("HP")) or 0
                local maxhp = tonumber(boss:GetAttribute("MaxHP")) or 1
                if valWbBoss then pcall(function() valWbBoss:UpdateName(string.format("%s · HP %.1f%%", tostring(boss:GetAttribute("DisplayName") or boss.Name), hp / maxhp * 100)) end) end
                if r and h and h.Health > 0 and hp > 0 then
                    if not wbBusy then
                        wbBusy = true
                        wbPauseAll()
                    end
                    local bPart = boss:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or boss.PrimaryPart or boss:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart")
                    local arena = workspace:FindFirstChild("WorldBossArenaLive")
                    local bossName = tostring(boss:GetAttribute("DisplayName") or boss.Name)
                    local bDef = { arenaPos = arena and arena:GetPivot().Position or (bPart and bPart.Position) or r.Position, arenaRadius = 105, name = bossName }
                    local evading, emsg = false, nil
                    if wbDodgeGoal and tick() < wbDodgeUntil then
                        -- exact dodge planned from the server warning (see wbPlanDodge)
                        evading, emsg = true, string.format("🛡️ หลบ %s", tostring(wbDodgeSkill))
                        lastEvadeGoal = wbDodgeGoal
                    else
                        pcall(function() evading, emsg = handleBossSkillEvade(r, h, bDef, boss) end)
                    end
                    if bPart then
                        local bp = bPart.Position
                        local d = Vector2.new(bp.X - r.Position.X, bp.Z - r.Position.Z).Magnitude
                        if evading then
                            pcall(function()
                                if (lp:GetAttribute("MoveLockUntil") or 0) > 0 then lp:SetAttribute("MoveLockUntil", 0) end
                                if (lp:GetAttribute("HardLockUntil") or 0) > 0 then lp:SetAttribute("HardLockUntil", 0) end
                                if lastEvadeGoal then h:MoveTo(lastEvadeGoal) end
                            end)
                            wbSetStatus(emsg or "🛡️ เดินหลบสกิลบอส")
                        else
                            -- lock the boss as target and let the game's AUTO bot attack with skills
                            if tick() - lastLock > 1 then
                                lastLock = tick()
                                pcall(function()
                                    local ct = lp:FindFirstChild("CurrentTarget")
                                    if ct and ct.Value ~= boss then ct.Value = boss end
                                    local rem = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("Remotes")
                                    if rem and rem:FindFirstChild("SetTarget") then rem.SetTarget:FireServer(boss, false, true) end
                                    if lp:GetAttribute("AutoOn") ~= true then
                                        setBotRunning(true, { range = 70, loot = false, openBoxes = false, attackLeader = true, preset = farmPreset, hpPct = farmHpPct, isBossCombat = true })
                                    end
                                end)
                            end
                            -- stand behind the boss, close in: out of its frontal cone / beam and inside ring attacks' hole
                            if tick() - lastApproach > 0.25 then
                                lastApproach = tick()
                                local look = wbFlat(bPart.CFrame.LookVector)
                                if look.Magnitude < 0.1 then look = Vector3.new(0, 0, 1) end
                                -- inside melee reach: AttackRange + boss Radius (game rule), 2 studs in
                                local wbReach = (tonumber(lp:GetAttribute("AttackRange")) or 7) + (tonumber(boss:GetAttribute("Radius")) or 1.5)
                                local spot = bp - look.Unit * math.max(3, wbReach - 2)
                                spot = Vector3.new(spot.X, r.Position.Y, spot.Z)
                                if wbFlat(spot - r.Position).Magnitude > 3 then h:MoveTo(spot) end
                            end
                            wbSetStatus(string.format("⚔️ สู้ %s (ระยะ %.0f)", bossName, d))
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
end)
end)()

-- ====================================================================
-- TAB: AUTO QUEST (AUTO CLAIM & AUTO RE-ACCEPT)
-- ====================================================================
task.wait() -- yield point: spread the start over frames (see the note before TAB: AUTO FARM)
;(function()
local questTab = mainGroup:Tab({
    Name        = "Quest",
    Title       = "Auto Quest & Rewards",
    Description = "ระบบส่งเควสต์ซ้ำและรับเควสต์ใหม่อัตโนมัติ (Auto Claim & Re-accept) รับ EXP/Gems สูงสุด 24 ชม.",
})

-- ---------- SECTION 0: Status & Progress ----------
local secQuestStatus = questTab:Section()
secQuestStatus:Header({
    Name        = "Active Quest Progress",
    Description = "ความคืบหน้าของเควสต์ที่กำลังทำอยู่ และจำนวนรอบที่ส่งสำเร็จ (Real-time)",
    Collapsible = false,
})
Quest.valQuestName         = secQuestStatus:Value({ Name = "Current Active Quest", Text = "กำลังตรวจสอบ..." })
Quest.valQuestProgress     = secQuestStatus:Value({ Name = "Quest Progress", Text = "0 / 40" })
Quest.valQuestReward       = secQuestStatus:Value({ Name = "Quest Rewards", Text = "EXP: 0 | Silver: 0" })
Quest.valQuestClaimedCount = secQuestStatus:Value({ Name = "Total Quests Claimed", Text = "ส่งแล้ว: 0 รอบ (+0 EXP)" })
Quest.valQuestZoneTarget   = secQuestStatus:Value({ Name = "Zone Target Quest", Text = "กำลังตรวจสอบ..." })

-- ---------- SECTION 1: Auto Quest Settings ----------
local secQuestConfig = questTab:Section()
secQuestConfig:Header({
    Name        = "Auto Quest Automation Settings",
    Description = "เปิด/ปิดระบบรับส่งเควสต์และรับของขวัญอัตโนมัติ",
    Collapsible = false,
})

secQuestConfig:Toggle({
    Name        = "Auto Quest (Claim & Re-accept)",
    Description = "ส่งเควสต์ทันทีเมื่อมอนสเตอร์ครบ 40/40 และกดรับเควสต์ซ้ำรอบใหม่ทันที ไม่ให้เควสต์ค้าง",
    Default     = true,
    Callback    = function(val)
        Quest.autoQuestEnabled = val
        if val and not (GOLFGUI.IsLoadingConfig or isBootstrappingConfig) then
            task.spawn(checkAndProcessQuests)
        end
    end,
}, "Quest_AutoClaimRepeat")

secQuestConfig:Toggle({
    Name        = "Auto Accept Zone Repeat Quest",
    Description = "หากย้ายโซนฟาร์มแล้วยังไม่มีเควสต์ของโซนนั้น บอทจะกดรับเควสต์ซ้ำประจำโซนให้อัตโนมัติ",
    Default     = true,
    Callback    = function(val)
        Quest.autoAcceptZoneRepeatQuest = val
        if val and not (GOLFGUI.IsLoadingConfig or isBootstrappingConfig) then
            task.spawn(checkAndProcessQuests)
        end
    end,
}, "Quest_AutoAcceptZone")

secQuestConfig:Toggle({
    Name        = "Auto Claim Playtime & Daily Rewards",
    Description = "รับของขวัญออนไลน์สะสมตามชั่วโมง (Online Rewards) และรางวัลล็อกอินรายวันอัตโนมัติ",
    Default     = true,
    Callback    = function(val)
        Quest.autoClaimDailyPlaytime = val
        if val and not (GOLFGUI.IsLoadingConfig or isBootstrappingConfig) then
            task.spawn(checkAndClaimOnlineRewards)
        end
    end,
}, "Quest_AutoClaimRewards")

-- ---------- SECTION 2: Adventure Book (Achievements) ----------
local secAchieve = questTab:Section()
secAchieve:Header({
    Name        = "Adventure Book — Achievements",
    Description = "รับรางวัลความสำเร็จ (Stats / Titles) จากสมุด Adventure Book อัตโนมัติ",
    Collapsible = false,
})
local valAchieveStatus = secAchieve:Value({ Name = "สถานะ Achievement", Text = "กำลังตรวจสอบ..." })
Quest.autoClaimAchieve = true

local function scanAchieveStatus()
    local ready, gotN, total = {}, 0, 0
    pcall(function()
        local ad = require(game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):WaitForChild("Shared", 5):WaitForChild("AchievementData", 5))
        local list = ad and ad.List
        if type(list) ~= "table" then return end
        total = #list
        local raw = lp:GetAttribute("AchieveJson")
        local aj = (type(raw) == "string" and #raw > 0) and HttpService:JSONDecode(raw) or {}
        local got = type(aj.Got) == "table" and aj.Got or {}
        local cnt = type(aj.C) == "table" and aj.C or {}
        local lv = tonumber(lp:GetAttribute("Level")) or 1
        for _, def in ipairs(list) do
            if type(def) == "table" and type(def.Id) == "string" then
                if got[def.Id] == true then
                    gotN = gotN + 1
                else
                    local cur = (def.Type == "level") and lv or (tonumber(cnt[def.Type]) or 0)
                    local need = tonumber(def.Count) or 1
                    if cur >= need then
                        ready[#ready + 1] = def.Id
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end)
    if valAchieveStatus then
        pcall(function()
            valAchieveStatus:UpdateName(string.format("รับได้ตอนนี้: %d | รับแล้ว: %d / %d", #ready, gotN, total))
        end)
    end
    return ready, gotN, total
end

local function claimAllReadyAchievements()
    if Quest.isClaimingAchieve then return end
    Quest.isClaimingAchieve = true
    local ok, err = pcall(function()
        local ready = scanAchieveStatus()
        if #ready == 0 then return end
        local rem = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("Remotes")
        local achRem = rem and rem:FindFirstChild("AchieveAction")
        if not achRem then return end
        for _, id in ipairs(ready) do
            pcall(function() achRem:FireServer("claim", id, false) end)
            task.wait(0.08)
        end
        task.wait(0.2)
        scanAchieveStatus()
    end)
    Quest.isClaimingAchieve = false
    if not ok then warn("[GOLF HUB] claimAllReadyAchievements:", err) end
end
Quest.claimAllReadyAchievements = claimAllReadyAchievements

secAchieve:Button({
    Name = "🏆 กดรับ Achievement ที่พร้อมทั้งหมด",
    Callback = function() task.spawn(claimAllReadyAchievements) end,
})

secAchieve:Toggle({
    Name        = "⚡ Auto รับ Achievement อัตโนมัติ",
    Description = "กดรับรางวัลจาก Adventure Book -> Achievements ให้อัตโนมัติทันทีเมื่อมีตัวที่ทำครบ",
    Default     = true,
    Callback    = function(val)
        Quest.autoClaimAchieve = val
        if val and not (GOLFGUI.IsLoadingConfig or isBootstrappingConfig) then
            task.spawn(claimAllReadyAchievements)
        end
    end,
}, "Quest_AutoClaimAchieve")

task.defer(function()
    local ready = scanAchieveStatus()
    if Quest.autoClaimAchieve and #ready > 0 then
        task.spawn(claimAllReadyAchievements)
    end
end)
if lp then
    _G.GolfHub_Conn(lp:GetAttributeChangedSignal("AchieveJson"), function()
        local ready = scanAchieveStatus()
        if Quest.autoClaimAchieve and #ready > 0 then
            task.spawn(claimAllReadyAchievements)
        end
    end)
    _G.GolfHub_Conn(lp:GetAttributeChangedSignal("AchieveReady"), function()
        if Quest.autoClaimAchieve and (tonumber(lp:GetAttribute("AchieveReady")) or 0) > 0 then
            task.spawn(claimAllReadyAchievements)
        end
    end)
end
end)()

-- Hook onto QuestsJson attribute change for zero-latency instant claim
if lp then
    _G.GolfHub_Conn(lp:GetAttributeChangedSignal("QuestsJson"), function()
        if Quest.autoQuestEnabled then
            task.spawn(checkAndProcessQuests)
        else
            updateQuestUiLabels()
        end
    end)
end

-- Background runner (fail-safe checks every 3.5s, deferred 8.0s on join)
task.spawn(function()
    local myGen = _G.GolfHub_Gen
    task.wait(8.0)
    while _G.GolfHub_Gen == myGen do
        task.wait(3.5)
        if Quest.autoQuestEnabled then
            pcall(checkAndProcessQuests)
        end
        if Quest.autoClaimDailyPlaytime and tick() - Quest.lastRewardClaimTime > 60 then
            Quest.lastRewardClaimTime = tick()
            pcall(checkAndClaimOnlineRewards)
        end
    end
end)

-- ====================================================================
-- TAB: EQUIPMENT (AUTO EQUIP BEST GEAR PER CLASS)
-- ====================================================================
task.wait() -- yield point: spread the start over frames (see the note before TAB: AUTO FARM)
;(function()
local equipmentTab = mainGroup:Tab({
    Name        = "Equipment",
    Title       = "Equipment & Auto Gear",
    Description = "คัดเลือกและสวมใส่อุปกรณ์ที่ดีที่สุดตรงตาม Class ปัจจุบันแบบ Real-time",
})

-- Real-time Class & Stats Section
local secEquipInfo = equipmentTab:Section()
secEquipInfo:Header({
    Name        = "Real-time Class & Stats",
    Description = "ข้อมูลคลาสและสเตตัสเป้าหมายปัจจุบัน (อัปเดตสดอัตโนมัติ)",
    Collapsible = false,
})

valCurrentClass = secEquipInfo:Value({ Name = "Current Class",   Text = lp:GetAttribute("Job") or "Unknown" })
valCombatPower  = secEquipInfo:Value({ Name = "Combat Power",    Text = tostring(lp:GetAttribute("CombatPower") or 0) .. " CP" })
valBuildFocus   = secEquipInfo:Value({ Name = "Build Specialty", Text = getClassPriorityText(lp:GetAttribute("Job") or "Archer") })
valSetSynergy   = secEquipInfo:Value({ Name = "Target Set Aim",  Text = (CLASS_ARCHETYPES[lp:GetAttribute("Job") or "Archer"] or CLASS_ARCHETYPES.Archer).setBonusText or "SetWarrior 5/5 (ATK +10%)" })
valEquipWeapon  = secEquipInfo:Value({ Name = "Equipped Weapon", Text = getEquippedWeaponText() })

-- Extra Passive advisor: ranks the passives that can go in an EXTRA slot for the current class (rules come from the game's own ExtraNotes + passive list)
do
    local secAdv = equipmentTab:Section()
    secAdv:Header({
        Name        = "Extra Passive Advisor",
        Description = "แนะนำ Passive ที่ควรใส่ช่อง EXTRA ตามอาชีพปัจจุบัน (อ่านจากข้อมูลเกม — ใส่ได้เฉพาะตัวที่คุณเรียนแล้ว)",
        Collapsible = false,
    })
    local valAdvHead = secAdv:Value({ Name = "อาชีพ / ประเภท", Text = "-" })
    local advRows = {}
    for i = 1, 5 do advRows[i] = secAdv:Value({ Name = "อันดับ " .. i, Text = "-" }) end
    local advRanked = {}

    local MAGIC = { Mage = true, Cleric = true, Alchemist = true, Necromancer = true, SwordGod = true }
    -- score per damage type: melee / bow / gun / magic (0 = does nothing for that type); stats from SkillData.Skills
    local RULES = {
        mtheart       = { melee = 98, bow = 98, gun = 98, magic = 75, why = "ATK +15% · Evasion +20% · Cooldown -20% (ลดคูลดาวน์ใช้ได้ทุกสาย)" },
        fatalsense    = { melee = 97, bow = 97, gun = 97, magic = 20, why = "Crit DMG +20% · ATK +20% · ASPD +10% (แรงมากสำหรับสายกายภาพ)" },
        demonbane     = { melee = 95, bow = 95, gun = 95, magic = 95, why = "ATK & MATK +20% · DEF/MDEF +20 (ใช้ได้เต็มทุกอาชีพ)" },
        weighttraining= { melee = 94, bow = 88, gun = 88, magic = 12, why = "STR +10 · ATK +20% · Bag +20 (ช่วยดาเมจกายภาพแรงมาก)" },
        swordmastery  = { melee = 93, bow = 0,  gun = 0,  magic = 15, why = "อาวุธประชิด: ATK +20% · ASPD +10% (ใช้ได้เฉพาะประชิด)" },
        gsbull        = { melee = 92, bow = 93, gun = 96, magic = 0,  why = "ATK +15% · DEF Pen +10% (เจาะเกราะแรงมากสำหรับสายกายภาพ)" },
        owleye        = { melee = 86, bow = 96, gun = 94, magic = 25, why = "DEX +10 · ATK +20% (สายกายภาพ +20% ATK / เวทย์ได้ร่ายเร็วจาก DEX)" },
        zanshin       = { melee = 91, bow = 88, gun = 88, magic = 5,  why = "Crit DMG +10% · ATK +15% AGI" },
        catreflex     = { melee = 90, bow = 90, gun = 90, magic = 98, why = "ASPD +20% ใช้ได้ทุกอาชีพ (ส่วน MATK +20% AGI ช่วยสายเวทย์)" },
        shukuchi      = { melee = 84, bow = 82, gun = 82, magic = 50, why = "Evasion +15 · ASPD +15%" },
        doubleattack  = { melee = 80, bow = 0,  gun = 0,  magic = 0,  why = "Double Hit 50% เฉพาะตีธรรมดาอาวุธประชิด (+Accuracy 10)" },
        vultureeye    = { melee = 0,  bow = 90, gun = 0,  magic = 0,  why = "ใช้ได้เฉพาะธนู: ระยะไกลขึ้น + ดาเมจ +10%" },
        shadowservant = { melee = 0,  bow = 0,  gun = 0,  magic = 99, why = "MATK +20% · MDEF Pen +10% (ดีที่สุดสำหรับสายเวทย์)" },
        mentality     = { melee = 10, bow = 10, gun = 10, magic = 97, why = "INT +10 · MATK +20% · Max SP +10%" },
        sgheart       = { melee = 0,  bow = 0,  gun = 0,  magic = 94, why = "MATK +15% · MDEF Pen +10% (สายเวทย์)" },
        alchemymastery= { melee = 0,  bow = 0,  gun = 0,  magic = 90, why = "MATK +15% ช่วยเฉพาะสายเวทย์" },
        undeadmastery = { melee = 8,  bow = 8,  gun = 8,  magic = 82, why = "Cast Speed +10% (ช่วยสายเวทย์ร่ายเร็วขึ้น)" },
        sp_recovery   = { melee = 35, bow = 35, gun = 35, magic = 70, why = "SP regen +5 · ใช้ SP ลดลง 20%" },
        ironbody      = { melee = 55, bow = 50, gun = 50, magic = 55, why = "VIT +10 · Max HP +10% (เพิ่มความถึก)" },
        improvedodge  = { melee = 48, bow = 48, gun = 48, magic = 45, why = "Evasion +20 · Move Speed +15%" },
        hprecovery    = { melee = 45, bow = 45, gun = 45, magic = 45, why = "HP regen +100% · ยาฮีลแรงขึ้น +50% · SP regen +5" },
        -- shop / carry utility (Merchant): no combat value, keep them behind every combat passive
        discount      = { melee = 3,  bow = 3,  gun = 3,  magic = 3,  why = "ลดราคาร้านค้า 20% — ไม่ช่วยการต่อสู้" },
        overcharge    = { melee = 3,  bow = 3,  gun = 3,  magic = 3,  why = "ขายของได้ราคาดีขึ้น 20% — ไม่ช่วยการต่อสู้" },
    }
    local NAMES = {}
    local okSD, SD = pcall(function() return require(game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):WaitForChild("Shared", 5):WaitForChild("SkillData", 5)) end)

    local function refreshAdvisor()
        if not okSD or type(SD) ~= "table" or type(SD.Skills) ~= "table" then
            valAdvHead:UpdateName("อ่านข้อมูลเกมไม่ได้")
            return
        end
        local job = tostring(lp:GetAttribute("Job") or "?")
        local lv = tonumber(lp:GetAttribute("Level")) or 1
        local kind = MAGIC[job] and "magic" or (job == "Archer" and "bow" or (job == "GunSlinger" and "gun" or "melee"))
        local kindTh = ({ magic = "สายเวทย์ (MATK)", bow = "สายธนู (ATK)", gun = "สายปืน (ATK)", melee = "สายประชิด (ATK)" })[kind]
        valAdvHead:UpdateName(job .. " · " .. kindTh .. " · Lv." .. lv)
        -- only passives the character has learned (SkillsJson); the server rejects anything else in a slot
        local learned = {}
        pcall(function() learned = HttpService:JSONDecode(lp:GetAttribute("SkillsJson") or "{}") end)
        local list = {}
        for id, sk in pairs(SD.Skills) do
            -- own-class passives are always active and the server refuses them in an EXTRA slot, so skip them
            if type(sk) == "table" and sk.Type == "passive" and not sk.NoExtra and learned[id] and sk.Job ~= job then
                local r = RULES[id]
                local score = r and r[kind] or 25
                local why = r and r.why or "ยังไม่มีตัวเลขยืนยัน — เกมไม่ได้แสดงผลของ Passive นี้"
                if score > 0 then list[#list + 1] = { id = id, name = tostring(sk.Name or id), job = tostring(sk.Job or "?"), score = score, why = why, known = r ~= nil } end
            end
        end
        table.sort(list, function(a, b) if a.score ~= b.score then return a.score > b.score end return a.name < b.name end)
        advRanked = list
        for i = 1, 5 do
            local e = list[i]
            if e then
                advRows[i]:UpdateName(string.format("%s (%s) — %s", e.name, e.job, e.why))
            else
                advRows[i]:UpdateName("-")
            end
        end
    end
    -- Put the top-ranked learned passives into the open passive slots (1-based, PassiveSlotCount of them) via the
    -- game's own SetPassiveSlot remote. Slots already holding a wanted passive are left alone.
    local autoExtraOn = false
    local function applyAdvice()
        pcall(refreshAdvisor)
        local rem = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("Remotes")
        local remote = rem and rem:FindFirstChild("SetPassiveSlot")
        if not remote or #advRanked == 0 then return end
        local count = tonumber(lp:GetAttribute("PassiveSlotCount")) or 0
        local cur = {}
        pcall(function() cur = HttpService:JSONDecode(lp:GetAttribute("PassiveSlotsJson") or "[]") end)
        local want = {}
        for i = 1, math.min(count, #advRanked) do want[advRanked[i].id] = true end
        local placed = {}
        for i = 1, count do
            if cur[i] and want[cur[i]] then placed[cur[i]] = true end
        end
        local nextIdx = 1
        for i = 1, count do
            if not (cur[i] and want[cur[i]]) then
                while advRanked[nextIdx] and (placed[advRanked[nextIdx].id] or not want[advRanked[nextIdx].id]) do nextIdx = nextIdx + 1 end
                local e = advRanked[nextIdx]
                if not e then break end
                pcall(function() remote:FireServer(i, e.id) end)
                placed[e.id] = true
                task.wait(0.4)
            end
        end
    end
    secAdv:Button({ Name = "🔄 อัปเดตคำแนะนำ", Callback = function() pcall(refreshAdvisor) end })
    secAdv:Button({ Name = "✅ ใส่ Passive ตามคำแนะนำตอนนี้", Callback = function() task.spawn(applyAdvice) end })
    secAdv:Toggle({
        Name        = "⚡ Auto ใส่ Passive ตามคำแนะนำ",
        Description = "ใส่ Passive อันดับสูงสุดลงช่องว่างทุกช่องให้อัตโนมัติ (เมื่อเปิด, เปลี่ยนอาชีพ, เรียนสกิลใหม่, ปลดช่องใหม่ และทุก 60 วินาที)",
        Default     = true,
        Callback    = function(val)
            autoExtraOn = val
            if val then task.spawn(applyAdvice) end
        end,
    }, "Equip_AutoExtraPassive")
    local function onChange() pcall(refreshAdvisor); if autoExtraOn then task.spawn(applyAdvice) end end
    task.defer(function() pcall(refreshAdvisor) end)
    _G.GolfHub_Conn(lp:GetAttributeChangedSignal("Job"), onChange)
    _G.GolfHub_Conn(lp:GetAttributeChangedSignal("SkillsJson"), onChange)
    _G.GolfHub_Conn(lp:GetAttributeChangedSignal("PassiveSlotCount"), onChange)
    local myGen = _G.GolfHub_Gen
    task.spawn(function()
        while myGen == _G.GolfHub_Gen do
            task.wait(60)
            if autoExtraOn then pcall(applyAdvice) end
        end
    end)
end

-- Auto Equip Controls Section
local secEquipActions = equipmentTab:Section()
secEquipActions:Header({
    Name        = "Auto Equip Controls",
    Description = "สั่งสวมใส่อุปกรณ์ที่ดีที่สุดตรงสายทันที หรือเปิดทำงานอัตโนมัติ",
    Collapsible = false,
})



secEquipActions:Toggle({
    Name        = "⚡ Auto Equip อัตโนมัติ (Always Auto Equip)",
    Description = "สวมใส่อาวุธและอุปกรณ์ที่ดีที่สุดให้อัตโนมัติทันที (ตอนเริ่มสคริปต์, เริ่มฟาร์ม, เลเวลอัพ หรือเมื่อได้อุปกรณ์ใหม่)",
    Default     = true,
    Callback    = function(val)
        autoEquipAlways = val
        if val and equipBestGearForCurrentJob and not (GOLFGUI.IsLoadingConfig or isBootstrappingConfig) then
            task.spawn(function() equipBestGearForCurrentJob(false) end)
        end
    end,
}, "Equip_AutoEquipAlways")

secEquipActions:Toggle({
    Name        = "🃏 Auto Equip Cards (ใส่การ์ดที่ดีที่สุดอัตโนมัติ)",
    Description = "สวมใส่การ์ดที่ดีที่สุดลงทุก Slot ให้อัตโนมัติพร้อมกับอุปกรณ์",
    Default     = true,
    Callback    = function(val)
        autoEquipCards = val
        if val and equipBestGearForCurrentJob and not (GOLFGUI.IsLoadingConfig or isBootstrappingConfig) then
            task.spawn(function() equipBestGearForCurrentJob(true) end)
        end
    end,
}, "Equip_AutoEquipCards")

secEquipActions:Toggle({
    Name        = "🔄 Auto Equip เมื่อเปลี่ยน Class",
    Description = "เมื่อเปลี่ยนอาชีพในเกม จะสลับใส่อุปกรณ์ที่ดีที่สุดของอาชีพใหม่ให้อัตโนมัติทันที",
    Default     = true,
    Callback    = function(val)
        autoEquipOnJobChange = val
    end,
}, "Equip_AutoEquipOnJobChange")

-- ---------- SECTION: Auto Equip Best Star Set ----------
do
    local secStarEquip = equipmentTab:Section()
    secStarEquip:Header({
        Name        = "⭐ ใส่ชุดดาวอัตโนมัติ",
        Description = "สวมใส่ชุดดาว (เซ็ตไอเทม) ที่ดีที่สุดของสีที่เลือกให้อัตโนมัติ (Tier สูงสุด → ดาเมจ/พลังป้องกันเริ่มต้น → ผลรวมออฟชั่น) · ช่องที่ไม่มีของสีที่เลือกจะคงของเดิมไว้",
        Collapsible = false,
    })

    local valStarStatus = secStarEquip:Value({
        Name = "ผลการทำงาน",
        Text = _G.GolfHub_StarEquip.status or "ยังไม่ได้ทำงาน",
    })

    local function runStarEquipOnce(why)
        local se = _G.GolfHub_StarEquip
        if not (se and se.on and type(se.sets) == "table" and next(se.sets)) then
            local msg = "ยังไม่ได้เลือกสีดาว"
            se.status = msg
            pcall(function() if valStarStatus and valStarStatus.UpdateName then valStarStatus:UpdateName(msg) end end)
            return false, "not_configured"
        end
        if isSellingNow then
            return false, "selling"
        end

        local Remotes = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("Remotes")
        if not Remotes or not Remotes:FindFirstChild("EquipItem") then
            return false, "no_remote"
        end

        local rep = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
        local ItemData
        local okData = pcall(function() ItemData = require(rep.Shared.ItemData) end)
        if not okData or not ItemData then return false, "no_itemdata" end

        local function readJson(name)
            local raw = lp:GetAttribute(name)
            if type(raw) ~= "string" or #raw < 2 then return nil end
            local ok, t = pcall(function() return HttpService:JSONDecode(raw) end)
            return (ok and type(t) == "table") and t or nil
        end

        local gear = readJson("GearJson")
        local equip = readJson("EquipJson") or {}
        if not gear then return false, "no_gear" end

        local currentWeapon = lp:GetAttribute("WeaponId")
        local currentJob = lp:GetAttribute("Job") or "Unknown"
        local playerLevel = tonumber(lp:GetAttribute("Level")) or 99
        local better = _G.GolfHub_StarBetter

        local armorSlots = { "HeadTop", "HeadMid", "Body", "Garment", "Shoes" }
        local bestArmor = {}
        local weaponCandidates = {}
        local accCandidates = {}
        local totalStarMatches = 0

        for ref, g in pairs(gear) do
            if type(g) == "table" and g.S and se.sets[g.S] and type(g.Id) == "string" then
                local def = ItemData[g.Id]
                if def and (def.ReqLevel or 0) <= playerLevel then
                    totalStarMatches = totalStarMatches + 1
                    if def.Kind == "weapon" then
                        if def.Jobs and table.find(def.Jobs, currentJob) then
                            table.insert(weaponCandidates, { ref = ref, g = g })
                        end
                    elseif def.Slot == "Acc" then
                        table.insert(accCandidates, { ref = ref, g = g })
                    elseif def.Slot and table.find(armorSlots, def.Slot) then
                        local cur = bestArmor[def.Slot]
                        if not cur or better(g, cur.g) then
                            bestArmor[def.Slot] = { ref = ref, g = g }
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        if totalStarMatches == 0 then
            local stamp = os.date("%H:%M:%S")
            local msg = string.format("ไม่พบไอเทมในสีดาวที่เลือก (%s)", stamp)
            se.status = msg
            pcall(function() if valStarStatus and valStarStatus.UpdateName then valStarStatus:UpdateName(msg) end end)
            return true, 0
        end

        -- Best weapon candidate
        local bestWeapon
        for _, wc in ipairs(weaponCandidates) do
            if not bestWeapon or better(wc.g, bestWeapon.g) then
                bestWeapon = wc
            end
        end

        -- Best accessories (sort descending by better, pick top 2 different)
        table.sort(accCandidates, function(a, b) return better(a.g, b.g) end)
        local bestAcc1 = accCandidates[1]
        local bestAcc2 = accCandidates[2]

        local changedCount = 0

        -- 1. Armor slots: HeadTop, HeadMid, Body, Garment, Shoes
        for _, slot in ipairs(armorSlots) do
            local pick = bestArmor[slot]
            if pick and equip[slot] ~= pick.ref then
                local ok = pcall(function()
                    Remotes.EquipItem:FireServer(pick.ref)
                end)
                if ok then
                    changedCount = changedCount + 1
                    equip[slot] = pick.ref
                    task.wait(0.4)
                end
            end
        end

        -- 2. Weapon slot
        if bestWeapon and currentWeapon ~= bestWeapon.ref then
            local ok = pcall(function()
                Remotes.EquipItem:FireServer(bestWeapon.ref)
            end)
            if ok then
                changedCount = changedCount + 1
                currentWeapon = bestWeapon.ref
                task.wait(0.4)
            end
        end

        -- 3. Accessories: Acc1 and Acc2
        -- A piece already worn in either ring slot stays where it is. A missing pick goes only into a slot that does
        -- not hold the other pick: sending pick 2 to "Acc2" while pick 1 sits in Acc2 would take pick 1 off again.
        -- (Equipping a ref that is worn in the other slot moves it and empties that slot — seen live on 770X880.)
        local wantAcc = {}
        if bestAcc1 then wantAcc[bestAcc1.ref] = true end
        if bestAcc2 then wantAcc[bestAcc2.ref] = true end
        local freeAcc = {}
        for _, s in ipairs({ "Acc1", "Acc2" }) do
            if not wantAcc[equip[s] or ""] then freeAcc[#freeAcc + 1] = s end
        end
        for _, pick in ipairs({ bestAcc1, bestAcc2 }) do
            if pick and equip["Acc1"] ~= pick.ref and equip["Acc2"] ~= pick.ref then
                local slot = table.remove(freeAcc, 1)
                if slot then
                    local ok = pcall(function()
                        Remotes.EquipItem:FireServer(pick.ref, slot)
                    end)
                    if ok then
                        changedCount = changedCount + 1
                        equip[slot] = pick.ref
                        task.wait(0.4)
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        local stamp = os.date("%H:%M:%S")
        local statusMsg
        if changedCount > 0 then
            statusMsg = string.format("ใส่ล่าสุด: %d ชิ้น (%s)", changedCount, stamp)
        else
            statusMsg = string.format("ใส่ครบแล้ว / ไม่มีชิ้นใหม่ (%s)", stamp)
        end
        se.status = statusMsg
        se.lastRun = tick()
        pcall(function()
            if valStarStatus and valStarStatus.UpdateName then
                valStarStatus:UpdateName(statusMsg)
            elseif valStarStatus and valStarStatus.Set then
                valStarStatus:Set(statusMsg)
            end
        end)

        return true, changedCount
    end

    -- One run at a time: the backstop loop, the 2 s debounce and the toggle can all fire while a run is still waiting
    -- between equips (0.4 s each), and two overlapping runs would send every equip twice.
    local starBusy = false
    local function runStarEquip(why)
        if starBusy then return false, "busy" end
        starBusy = true
        local ok, a, b = pcall(runStarEquipOnce, why)
        starBusy = false
        if not ok then return false, a end
        return a, b
    end

    _G.GolfHub_StarEquip.run = runStarEquip

    local pending = false
    local function requestStarEquip(why)
        local se = _G.GolfHub_StarEquip
        if not (se and se.on) then return end
        if pending then return end
        pending = true
        local gen = _G.GolfHub_Gen
        task.delay(2.0, function()
            pending = false
            if gen ~= _G.GolfHub_Gen then return end
            if not se.on or isSellingNow then return end
            pcall(runStarEquip, why or "debounced")
        end)
    end
    _G.GolfHub_StarEquip.request = requestStarEquip

    secStarEquip:Toggle({
        Name        = "เปิดใส่ชุดดาวอัตโนมัติ",
        Description = "ค้นหาและสวมใส่ไอเทมดาวที่ดีที่สุดของสีที่เลือก (จะไม่สวมทับด้วย @best)",
        Default     = false,
        Callback    = function(val)
            _G.GolfHub_StarEquip.on = (val == true)
            if val and not (GOLFGUI.IsLoadingConfig or isBootstrappingConfig) then
                task.spawn(function() pcall(runStarEquip, "toggle_on") end)
            end
        end,
    }, "Equip_StarOn")

    local starEquipNames = {}
    for i, s in ipairs(_G.GolfHub_STAR_SETS) do starEquipNames[i] = s[1] end

    secStarEquip:Dropdown({
        Name        = "สีดาวที่จะใส่",
        Description = "เลือกสีดาวที่ต้องการสวมใส่ (เลือกได้หลายสี)",
        Multi       = true,
        Options     = starEquipNames,
        Default     = {},
        Callback    = function(sel)
            local sets = {}
            for _, s in ipairs(_G.GolfHub_STAR_SETS) do
                if type(sel) == "table" and (sel[s[1]] == true or table.find(sel, s[1])) then sets[s[2]] = true end
            end
            _G.GolfHub_StarEquip.sets = sets
            if _G.GolfHub_StarEquip.on and not (GOLFGUI.IsLoadingConfig or isBootstrappingConfig) then
                task.spawn(function() pcall(runStarEquip, "sets_changed") end)
            end
        end,
    }, "Equip_StarSets")

    -- Signals: GearJson and Level (debounced ~2s)
    _G.GolfHub_Conn(lp:GetAttributeChangedSignal("GearJson"), function()
        if _G.GolfHub_StarEquip and _G.GolfHub_StarEquip.on then
            requestStarEquip("gear")
        end
    end)
    _G.GolfHub_Conn(lp:GetAttributeChangedSignal("Level"), function()
        if _G.GolfHub_StarEquip and _G.GolfHub_StarEquip.on then
            requestStarEquip("level")
        end
    end)

    -- Backstop loop: runs every ~20s
    local myGen = _G.GolfHub_Gen
    task.spawn(function()
        task.wait(5)
        while myGen == _G.GolfHub_Gen do
            local se = _G.GolfHub_StarEquip
            if se and se.on and not isSellingNow then
                pcall(runStarEquip, "backstop")
            end
            task.wait(20)
        end
    end)
end
end)()

-- ====================================================================
-- TAB: AUTO SELL
-- ====================================================================
task.wait() -- yield point: spread the start over frames (see the note before TAB: AUTO FARM)
;(function()
local sellTab = mainGroup:Tab({
    Name        = "Auto Sell",
    Title       = "GOLF HUB — Auto Sell",
    Description = "ระบบขายของอัตโนมัติ — เดินเข้าเสาวาร์ปกลับเมืองไปขายของที่ Barnaby เมื่อกระเป๋าเต็ม",
})

-- ---------- SECTION 1: Bag Capacity & Status ----------
local secSellStatus = sellTab:Section()
secSellStatus:Header({
    Name        = "Bag Capacity & Status",
    Description = "สถานะความจุกระเป๋าและขั้นตอนการขายของ (อัปเดตแบบเรียลไทม์)",
    Collapsible = false,
})

valBagCount   = secSellStatus:Value({ Name = "Bag Capacity", Text = "0 / 240 slots (0%)" })
valSellStatus = secSellStatus:Value({ Name = "Sell Status",  Text = "พร้อมฟาร์ม (Idle)" })
valMerchant   = secSellStatus:Value({ Name = "Merchant NPC", Text = "Barnaby (Merchant · Shop)" })

-- ---------- SECTION 2: Auto Sell Settings ----------
local secSellSettings = sellTab:Section()
secSellSettings:Header({
    Name        = "Auto Sell Settings",
    Description = "ตั้งค่าเงื่อนไขการเดินเข้าเสาเพื่อวาร์ปกลับเมืองไปขายของ",
    Collapsible = false,
})

secSellSettings:Toggle({
    Name        = "เปิดใช้งาน Auto Sell",
    Description = "เมื่อกระเป๋าเก็บไอเทมถึงจำนวนที่กำหนด จะหยุดฟาร์มแล้วเดินเข้าเสาวาร์ปไปขายของทันที",
    Default     = true,
    Callback    = function(val)
        autoSellEnabled = val
    end,
}, "Sell_AutoSellEnabled")

local maxBagSlots = (lp and lp:GetAttribute("BagSlotsMax")) or 240

local sliderThreshold = secSellSettings:Slider({
    Name        = "Bag Full Threshold (จำนวนชิ้น)",
    Description = "เลือกจำนวนไอเทมในกระเป๋าที่จะเริ่มวาร์ปกลับเมืองไปขายของ (10 ถึง " .. tostring(maxBagSlots) .. " ชิ้น)",
    Min         = 10,
    Max         = maxBagSlots,
    Minimum     = 10,
    Maximum     = maxBagSlots,
    Default     = 225,
    Decimals    = 0,
    Precision   = 0,
    Callback    = function(val)
        sellThreshold = math.floor(tonumber(val) or 220)
        pcall(updateSellUiLabels)
    end,
}, "Sell_Threshold")

secSellSettings:Input({
    Name        = "พิมพ์จำนวนชิ้นตรงๆ (Custom Number)",
    Description = "พิมพ์จำนวนไอเทมที่ต้องการให้เริ่มไปขายของได้ทันที (เช่น 65, 100, 200, 220, 240)",
    Placeholder = "เช่น 100, 220",
    Default     = tostring(sellThreshold or 225),
    AcceptedCharacters = "Numeric",
    Width       = 110,
    Callback    = function(text)
        local num = tonumber(text)
        if num and num >= 1 and num <= maxBagSlots then
            sellThreshold = math.floor(num)
            if sliderThreshold and sliderThreshold.UpdateValue then
                sliderThreshold:UpdateValue(sellThreshold)
            end
            pcall(updateSellUiLabels)
        end
    end,
}, "Sell_Threshold_Input")

secSellSettings:Dropdown({
    Name        = "เลือกเกณฑ์การขายด่วน (Quick Presets)",
    Description = "เลือกจำนวนชิ้นแบบสำเร็จรูปตามเปอร์เซ็นต์กระเป๋า",
    Options     = {
        "กำหนดเองตามแถบเลื่อน / กล่องพิมพ์",
        "เต็มกระเป๋า 100% (240 ชิ้น)",
        "เกือบเต็ม 95% (228 ชิ้น)",
        "90% (216 ชิ้น)",
        "80% (192 ชิ้น)",
        "75% (180 ชิ้น)",
        "50% (120 ชิ้น)",
        "100 ชิ้น",
        "65 ชิ้น",
    },
    Default     = "กำหนดเองตามแถบเลื่อน / กล่องพิมพ์",
    Callback    = function(sel)
        local curMax = (lp and lp:GetAttribute("BagSlotsMax")) or 240
        local targetVal = nil
        if sel:find("100%%") then
            targetVal = curMax
        elseif sel:find("95%%") then
            targetVal = math.floor(curMax * 0.95)
        elseif sel:find("90%%") then
            targetVal = math.floor(curMax * 0.90)
        elseif sel:find("80%%") then
            targetVal = math.floor(curMax * 0.80)
        elseif sel:find("75%%") then
            targetVal = math.floor(curMax * 0.75)
        elseif sel:find("50%%") then
            targetVal = math.floor(curMax * 0.50)
        elseif sel:find("100 ชิ้น") then
            targetVal = 100
        elseif sel:find("65 ชิ้น") then
            targetVal = 65
        end
        if targetVal then
            sellThreshold = targetVal
            if sliderThreshold and sliderThreshold.UpdateValue then
                sliderThreshold:UpdateValue(targetVal)
            end
            pcall(updateSellUiLabels)
        end
    end,
}, "Sell_PresetMode")

secSellSettings:Toggle({
    Name        = "ขาย Tier V ด้วย (ของมีค่า)",
    Description = "ปิดไว้ = ขายถึงแค่ Tier IV · ไม่ขายของที่ล็อก สวมอยู่ อาวุธที่ถือ ของอาชีพอื่น ของใน Loadout และของตีบวก ไม่ว่าจะเปิดหรือปิด",
    Default     = false,
    Callback    = function(val)
        sellTier5 = val
    end,
}, "Sell_IncludeTier5") -- new flag on purpose: the old Sell_AutoConfirm was saved as "on" for everyone

-- ---------- SECTION 3: Star lock (keep gear of chosen sets) ----------
-- The 8 coloured stars on gear are the game's gear sets (GearUtil.SetStyle, gear field S). State lives in
-- _G.GolfHub_StarLock (no new top-level locals); _G.GolfHub_SafeGearList reads it for both selling and dismantling.
do
    _G.GolfHub_StarLock = _G.GolfHub_StarLock or { on = false, sets = {} }
    local STAR_SETS = _G.GolfHub_STAR_SETS
    local starNames = {}
    for i, s in ipairs(STAR_SETS) do starNames[i] = s[1] end

    local secStarLock = sellTab:Section()
    secStarLock:Header({
        Name        = "🔒 ล็อกไอเทมดาว",
        Description = "เลือกสีดาว (เซ็ตไอเทม) · เปิดอยู่ = เก็บชิ้นที่ดีที่สุดของแต่ละชนิดในเซ็ตนั้นไว้ (Tier → ดาเมจเริ่มต้น → ออฟ) เจอชิ้นที่ดีกว่าจะเก็บชิ้นใหม่แทน ชิ้นเก่าขาย/ย่อยได้ · ของที่ล็อกเองในเกมยังเก็บเสมอ",
        Collapsible = false,
    })
    secStarLock:Toggle({
        Name        = "เปิดล็อกไอเทมดาว",
        Description = "ห้ามขาย ห้ามย่อย ชิ้นที่ดีที่สุดของดาวที่เลือกด้านล่าง (ทั้ง Auto Sell และ Dismantle)",
        Default     = false,
        Callback    = function(val)
            _G.GolfHub_StarLock.on = (val == true)
        end,
    }, "Sell_StarLockOn")
    secStarLock:Dropdown({
        Name        = "ดาวที่ล็อก",
        Description = "กดเพื่อเปิดรายการ แล้วติ๊กเลือกได้หลายสี",
        Multi       = true,
        Options     = starNames,
        Default     = {},
        Callback    = function(sel)
            local sets = {}
            for _, s in ipairs(STAR_SETS) do
                if type(sel) == "table" and (sel[s[1]] == true or table.find(sel, s[1])) then sets[s[2]] = true end
            end
            _G.GolfHub_StarLock.sets = sets
        end,
    }, "Sell_StarLockSets")
    local TIER_NAMES = { "Tier I", "Tier II", "Tier III", "Tier IV", "Tier V", "Tier VI" }
    secStarLock:Dropdown({
        Name        = "Tier ที่ล็อก",
        Description = "ล็อกเฉพาะ Tier ที่ติ๊ก (ไม่ติ๊กเลย = ทุก Tier) · ชิ้นที่ดีที่สุดจะเลือกจาก Tier ที่ติ๊กเท่านั้น",
        Multi       = true,
        Options     = TIER_NAMES,
        Default     = {},
        Callback    = function(sel)
            local tiers = {}
            for i, n in ipairs(TIER_NAMES) do
                if type(sel) == "table" and (sel[n] == true or table.find(sel, n)) then tiers[i] = true end
            end
            _G.GolfHub_StarLock.tiers = tiers
        end,
    }, "Sell_StarLockTiers")

    -- Puts the game's own Lock (the lock icon in the bag, remote LockItem(ref, true/false), same as the bag's lock
    -- button) on the best piece of each kind. When a better piece of the same set and kind turns up, the new one is
    -- locked first and then the old one is unlocked, but only if the hub locked it: the refs the hub locked are kept in
    -- starlock_hub_locks.json, so locks you set yourself are never removed. Turning the lock off or unticking a set /
    -- tier leaves the existing locks as they are.
    task.spawn(function()
        local gen = _G.GolfHub_Gen
        local file = (GOLFGUI.Folder or "GolfHub_HuntingTogether") .. "/starlock_hub_locks.json"
        local _starLockCache = {}
        local function readJson(name)
            local raw = lp:GetAttribute(name)
            if not raw or #raw < 2 then return nil end
            local cached = _starLockCache[name]
            if cached and cached.raw == raw then return cached.tbl end
            local ok, t = pcall(function() return HttpService:JSONDecode(raw) end)
            if ok and type(t) == "table" then
                _starLockCache[name] = { raw = raw, tbl = t }
                return t
            end
            return nil
        end
        local mine = {}
        pcall(function()
            if isfile(file) then
                local t = HttpService:JSONDecode(readfile(file))
                if type(t) == "table" then mine = t end
            end
        end)
        local function save() pcall(writefile, file, HttpService:JSONEncode(mine)) end
        task.wait(14.0) -- defer initial star-lock evaluation across join
        while gen == _G.GolfHub_Gen do
            task.wait(6)
            local sl = _G.GolfHub_StarLock
            if sl and sl.on and not isSellingNow then
                pcall(function()
                    local R = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("Remotes")
                    local rem = R and R:FindFirstChild("LockItem")
                    local gear, locks = readJson("GearJson"), readJson("LocksJson")
                    if not (rem and gear and locks) then return end
                    local best = _G.GolfHub_StarBest(gear)
                    local changed, bestKeys = false, {}
                    for ref, key in pairs(best) do
                        bestKeys[key] = true
                        if locks[ref] ~= true then
                            rem:FireServer(ref, true)
                            mine[ref] = key
                            changed = true
                            task.wait(0.3)
                        end
                    end
                    for ref, key in pairs(mine) do
                        if gear[ref] == nil or (not best[ref] and locks[ref] ~= true) then
                            mine[ref] = nil -- gone, or you unlocked it yourself: forget it
                            changed = true
                        elseif not best[ref] and bestKeys[key] then
                            -- a better piece of the same set and kind is the best now (and was locked above)
                            if locks[ref] == true then rem:FireServer(ref, false) task.wait(0.3) end
                            mine[ref] = nil
                            changed = true
                        end
                    end
                    if changed then save() end
                end)
            end
        end
    end)
end

end)()

-- ====================================================================
-- TAB: DISMANTLE (uses the game's own BagSalvage remote: list of gear ids "gNNN" -> Equipment Dust)
-- ====================================================================
task.wait() -- yield point: spread the start over frames (see the note before TAB: AUTO FARM)
;(function()
local dismTab = mainGroup:Tab({
    Name        = "Dismantle",
    Title       = "GOLF HUB — Dismantle",
    Description = "สลายอุปกรณ์ที่ไม่ได้ใช้งานเป็น Equipment Dust ด้วยระบบของเกม (ไม่แตะของที่สวมอยู่ / อาวุธที่ถือ / ล็อก / ตีบวก / Loadout / Tier VI)",
})
local dismMaxTier, dismKeepOther, dismAuto, dismMinCount = 3, true, false, 10
local dismBusy = false

local function dismCollect()
    return _G.GolfHub_SafeGearList(dismMaxTier, dismKeepOther) or {}
end

local valDism
local function dismRun(manual)
    if dismBusy then return 0 end
    local R = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("Remotes")
    local rem = R and R:FindFirstChild("BagSalvage")
    if not rem then return 0 end
    dismBusy = true
    local count = 0
    pcall(function()
        local ids = dismCollect()
        if #ids == 0 or (not manual and #ids < dismMinCount) then return end
        for i = 1, #ids, 40 do
            -- read again right before each batch: something may have been equipped or locked meanwhile
            local allowed, chunk = {}, {}
            for _, ref in ipairs(dismCollect()) do allowed[ref] = true end
            for j = i, math.min(i + 39, #ids) do
                if allowed[ids[j]] then chunk[#chunk + 1] = ids[j] end
            end
            if #chunk > 0 then rem:FireServer(chunk) end
            task.wait(0.6)
        end
        count = #ids
        if valDism then pcall(function() valDism:UpdateName("สลายล่าสุด: " .. #ids .. " ชิ้น (" .. os.date("%H:%M:%S") .. ")") end) end
    end)
    dismBusy = false
    return count
end

local secD1 = dismTab:Section()
secD1:Header({ Name = "Dismantle Settings", Description = "เลือก Tier สูงสุดที่จะสลาย (ของที่สวมอยู่/ล็อกจะไม่ถูกแตะเสมอ)", Collapsible = false })
valDism = secD1:Value({ Name = "Status", Text = "พร้อมใช้งาน" })
secD1:Slider({
    Name = "Tier สูงสุดที่สลาย (1-5)", Description = "สลายอุปกรณ์ Tier I จนถึง Tier ที่เลือก (Tier VI เกมไม่ให้สลายในหน้านี้)",
    Minimum = 1, Maximum = 5, Default = 3, Decimals = 0, Precision = 0,
    Callback = function(v) dismMaxTier = math.floor(tonumber(v) or 3) end,
}, "Dism_MaxTier")
secD1:Toggle({
    Name = "เก็บอุปกรณ์ของอาชีพอื่น (Keep other-class gear)", Description = "ไม่สลายของที่ตัวละครใส่ไว้ในสายอาชีพอื่น (เกมเลือกให้ด้วย Select All แต่ฮับข้ามให้ปลอดภัยกว่า)",
    Default = true, Callback = function(v) dismKeepOther = v end,
}, "Dism_KeepOther")
secD1:Toggle({
    Name = "Auto Dismantle", Description = "เช็คทุก 20 วินาที เมื่อมีของที่สลายได้ถึงจำนวนที่กำหนดจะสลายให้เอง",
    Default = false, Callback = function(v) dismAuto = v end,
}, "Dism_Auto")
secD1:Slider({
    Name = "จำนวนขั้นต่ำก่อนสลายอัตโนมัติ", Description = "สลายอัตโนมัติเมื่อมีอุปกรณ์ที่เข้าเงื่อนไขอย่างน้อยกี่ชิ้น",
    Minimum = 1, Maximum = 100, Default = 10, Decimals = 0, Precision = 0,
    Callback = function(v) dismMinCount = math.floor(tonumber(v) or 10) end,
}, "Dism_MinCount")
-- auto-press OK on the game's "Dismantle Complete" popup so it never covers the screen
task.spawn(function()
    local gen = _G.GolfHub_Gen
    while gen == _G.GolfHub_Gen do
        task.wait(0.5)
        pcall(function()
            local g = lp.PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("LumiSalvageResult")
            local pop = g and g.Enabled and g:FindFirstChild("PopupLayer", true)
            if pop and pop.Visible then
                local kb = pop:FindFirstChild("KitButton", true)
                if kb then
                    for _, c in ipairs(getconnections(kb.MouseButton1Click)) do c:Fire() end
                    for _, c in ipairs(getconnections(kb.Activated)) do c:Fire() end
                end
            end
        end)
    end
end)

task.spawn(function()
    local gen = _G.GolfHub_Gen
    task.wait(25.0) -- defer initial dismantle check across join
    while gen == _G.GolfHub_Gen do
        task.wait(20)
        if dismAuto and not isSellingNow then pcall(dismRun, false) end
    end
end)
end)()

-- ====================================================================
-- TAB: PARTY (drives the game's own Find Party -> Quick Match button; the game handles the join / server teleport)
-- ====================================================================
task.wait() -- yield point: spread the start over frames (see the note before TAB: AUTO FARM)
;(function()
local partyTab = mainGroup:Tab({
    Name        = "Party",
    Title       = "GOLF HUB — Party",
    Description = "หา Party อัตโนมัติด้วยปุ่ม Quick Match ของเกม (เลือกแมพตามเลเวล) — ถ้ามีปาร์ตี้อยู่แล้วจะไม่ทำซ้ำ",
})
local pMode, pInterval, pAuto = "level", 30, false
local pLeaveSmall, pMinMembers, pGrace, pPartyKey, pPartySince = true, 3, 45, "", 0
local pMapPick = 1
local LV_STARTS = {1, 9, 17, 26, 34, 42, 51, 59, 67, 76, 84, 92}
local valParty
local pLeaveNoMate, pNoMateId, pNoMateZone, pNoMateFor = true, nil, nil, 0
-- just moved to a new farm zone: give the party 60 s to follow, otherwise find a party there (full party EXP).
-- Farming the same zone: 180 s, so a mate's trip to the shop does not break the party. 120 s between two leaves.
local NO_MATE_WAIT, NO_MATE_WAIT_NEW_ZONE, NO_MATE_COOLDOWN = 180, 60, 120
local pNoMateNewZone = true -- a fresh load (often right after a Quick Match hop) counts as just arrived

local function inParty()
    local j = lp:GetAttribute("PartyJson")
    return type(j) == "string" and #j > 4
end

-- The game shares party EXP by zone and publishes each player's zone as the PartyZone attribute (set for far-away players
-- too), so this compares the game's own value instead of guessing from Z.
-- Returns how many other members are in that zone, or nil when that cannot be known (then we never leave).
_G.GolfHub_PartyMatesInZone = function(members, myId, zone, getPlayer)
    if type(members) ~= "table" or not zone then return nil end
    local n, unknown = 0, false
    for _, m in ipairs(members) do
        local id = tonumber(m.UserId)
        if id ~= myId then
            local plr = id and getPlayer(id) -- not in this server = cannot share
            if plr then
                local z = plr:GetAttribute("PartyZone")
                if z == zone then n = n + 1 elseif z == nil then unknown = true end
            end
        end
    end
    if n == 0 and unknown then return nil end
    return n
end

-- The cooldown is kept in a file because Quick Match can move us to another server, which restarts the hub.
local function noMateCooldownFile() return (GOLFGUI.Folder or "GolfHub_HuntingTogether") .. "/last_party_leave.txt" end

-- every 2 s: leave once nobody from the party has been in the farm zone for NO_MATE_WAIT seconds of farming there
local _cachedPartyJson, _lastPartyJsonRaw
local function noMateTick(dt)
    if not (pAuto and pLeaveNoMate and inParty()) then pNoMateFor = 0 return false end
    local env = getgenv()
    local st = env.GolfHub_GetState and env.GolfHub_GetState()
    -- selling, travelling, boss room, dungeon, dead: the count pauses
    if not st or not st.farmActive or st.bossFarmActive or st.dungeonFarmActive or st.inDungeon or isSellingNow then return false end
    local zone = tostring(st.targetZoneKey or "")
    if not zone:match("^Field%d+$") or lp:GetAttribute("PartyZone") ~= zone then return false end
    local hum = lp.Character and lp.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not hum or hum.Health <= 0 then return false end
    if _G.GolfHub_isWarpingTravel and tick() - (_G.GolfHub_warpStartedAt or 0) < 45 then return false end

    local rawParty = lp:GetAttribute("PartyJson")
    if type(rawParty) ~= "string" or #rawParty < 2 then return false end
    local party = _cachedPartyJson
    if rawParty ~= _lastPartyJsonRaw then
        _lastPartyJsonRaw = rawParty
        local okP, resP = pcall(function() return HttpService:JSONDecode(rawParty) end)
        if okP and type(resP) == "table" then
            _cachedPartyJson = resP
            party = resP
        else
            _cachedPartyJson = nil
            party = nil
        end
    end
    if not party or not party.Id then return false end
    local id = tostring(party.Id)
    if zone ~= pNoMateZone and pNoMateZone ~= nil then pNoMateNewZone = true end -- the farm moved on (level up)
    if id ~= pNoMateId or zone ~= pNoMateZone then pNoMateId, pNoMateZone, pNoMateFor = id, zone, 0 end
    local players = game:GetService("Players")
    local mates = _G.GolfHub_PartyMatesInZone(party.Members, lp.UserId, zone, function(uid) return players:GetPlayerByUserId(uid) end)
    if mates ~= 0 then pNoMateFor, pNoMateNewZone = 0, false return false end

    local wait = pNoMateNewZone and NO_MATE_WAIT_NEW_ZONE or NO_MATE_WAIT
    pNoMateFor = pNoMateFor + dt
    if valParty then
        valParty:UpdateName(string.format(pNoMateNewZone and "ย้ายโซนแล้ว รอเพื่อนตามมา %d/%d วิ" or "ไม่มีเพื่อนในโซน %d/%d วิ",
            math.min(pNoMateFor, wait), wait))
    end
    if pNoMateFor < wait then return false end

    local file = noMateCooldownFile()
    local okR, stamp = pcall(function() return isfile(file) and tonumber(readfile(file)) end)
    if okR and stamp and os.time() - stamp < NO_MATE_COOLDOWN then return false end
    local R = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("Remotes")
    local pa = R and R:FindFirstChild("PartyAction")
    if not pa then return false end
    pcall(writefile, file, tostring(os.time()))
    pa:FireServer("leave")
    if valParty then valParty:UpdateName("ไม่มีเพื่อนในโซน — ออกแล้วหาปาร์ตี้ใหม่") end
    local t0 = tick()
    while inParty() and tick() - t0 < 6 do task.wait(0.3) end
    pNoMateFor, pNoMateNewZone = 0, false
    return not inParty()
end

local function pressGui(b)
    if not b then return end
    pcall(firesignal, b.MouseButton1Click)
    pcall(firesignal, b.Activated)
end

local function partyMapIndex()
    if pMode == "manual" then return pMapPick end
    -- while Auto Farm runs, join a party on the map the farm is working on so a join never teleports us to another zone
    local okS, st = pcall(function() return getgenv().GolfHub_GetState and getgenv().GolfHub_GetState() end)
    local fz = okS and type(st) == "table" and st.farmActive and tostring(st.targetZoneKey or "")
    local fn = fz and tonumber(fz:match("^Field(%d+)$"))
    if fn and fn >= 1 and fn <= 15 then return fn end
    local lv = tonumber(lp:GetAttribute("Level")) or 1
    if lv >= 99 then return 15 end
    local idx = 1
    for i, s in ipairs(LV_STARTS) do if lv >= s then idx = i end end
    return idx
end

-- close the Party windows (the X at the top right) so they never cover the screen
local function partyCloseUi()
    local pg = lp:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
    if not pg then return end
    for _ = 1, 3 do
        local anyOpen = false
        for _, name in ipairs({ "HuntFindParty", "HuntParty" }) do
            local g = pg:FindFirstChild(name)
            if g and g.Enabled then
                anyOpen = true
                local win = g:FindFirstChild("Holder") and g.Holder:FindFirstChild("Window")
                local cb = win and win:FindFirstChild("Close")
                pressGui(cb)
            end
        end
        if not anyOpen then return end
        task.wait(0.6)
    end
end

local function partyQuickMatch()
    local pg = lp:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
    if not pg or inParty() then return false, "อยู่ในปาร์ตี้แล้ว" end
    local menu = pg:FindFirstChild("LumiMenu")
    local g = pg:FindFirstChild("HuntFindParty")
    if not (g and g.Enabled) then
        local hit = menu and menu:FindFirstChild("Hit", true)
        local pb = menu and menu:FindFirstChild("PortalColumn") and menu.PortalColumn:FindFirstChild("party")
