        if pb and pb:FindFirstChild("Hit") and lp:GetAttribute("HuntPartyOpen") ~= true then pressGui(pb.Hit) task.wait(0.8) end
        local hp = pg:FindFirstChild("HuntParty")
        local tab = hp and hp:FindFirstChild("TabFindParty", true)
        if tab then pressGui(tab) task.wait(1) end
        g = pg:FindFirstChild("HuntFindParty")
    end
    if not g then partyCloseUi() return false, "ไม่พบหน้า Find Party" end
    local idx = partyMapIndex()
    local tile = g:FindFirstChild(string.format("Map_Field%02d", idx), true)
    if tile then pressGui(tile) task.wait(0.4) end
    local qm = g:FindFirstChild("QuickMatch", true)
    if not qm then
        -- the window can be stuck on the "YOUR PARTY / on board" view (a posted party that no longer exists): press TAKE OFF BOARD
        local pb = g:FindFirstChild("PBClose", true)
        if pb then
            for _, sig in ipairs({ "MouseButton1Down", "MouseButton1Up", "MouseButton1Click" }) do
                for _, c in ipairs(getconnections(pb[sig])) do pcall(function() c:Fire() end) end
            end
            task.wait(2)
            qm = g:FindFirstChild("QuickMatch", true)
            if qm then
                local tile = g:FindFirstChild(string.format("Map_Field%02d", idx), true)
                if tile then pressGui(tile) task.wait(0.4) end
            end
        end
    end
    if not qm then partyCloseUi() return false, "ไม่พบปุ่ม Quick Match" end
    pressGui(qm)
    task.wait(1.5)
    -- close the window again so it does not cover the screen
    partyCloseUi()
    return true, string.format("Quick Match แมพ Field%02d", idx)
end

local sP1 = partyTab:Section()
sP1:Header({ Name = "Auto Quick Match", Description = "กด Quick Match ให้เองเมื่อยังไม่มีปาร์ตี้ (เกมอาจย้ายเซิร์ฟเวอร์ตอนเข้าปาร์ตี้)", Collapsible = false })
valParty = sP1:Value({ Name = "Status", Text = "ปิดอยู่" })
sP1:Toggle({
    Name = "เปิด Auto Quick Match", Description = "เช็คตามเวลาที่ตั้ง ถ้ายังไม่มีปาร์ตี้ จะกด Quick Match อัตโนมัติ",
    Default = false, Callback = function(v) pAuto = v if valParty then pcall(function() valParty:UpdateName(v and "เปิด — รอรอบเช็ค" or "ปิดอยู่") end) end end,
}, "Party_Auto")
sP1:Dropdown({
    Name = "เลือกแมพ", Description = "ตามเลเวล = เลือกแมพที่ตรงกับเลเวลปัจจุบัน (Lv.99 = Hellhound Ash Fields)",
    Options = { "ตามเลเวล (Auto)", "Rabbit Field", "Boar Forest", "Bear Forest", "Hyena Den", "Rhino Thicket", "Tiger Thicket", "Scorpion Dunes", "Ram Cliffs", "Stone Golem Valley", "Polar Bear Plain", "Snow Wolf Forest", "Ice Golem Peak", "Lava Turtle Hill", "Fire Bat Cliffs", "Hellhound Ash Fields" },
    Default = "ตามเลเวล (Auto)",
    Callback = function(v)
        local val = type(v) == "table" and v[1] or v
        local names = { "Rabbit Field", "Boar Forest", "Bear Forest", "Hyena Den", "Rhino Thicket", "Tiger Thicket", "Scorpion Dunes", "Ram Cliffs", "Stone Golem Valley", "Polar Bear Plain", "Snow Wolf Forest", "Ice Golem Peak", "Lava Turtle Hill", "Fire Bat Cliffs", "Hellhound Ash Fields" }
        pMode = "level"
        for i, n in ipairs(names) do if n == val then pMode, pMapPick = "manual", i end end
    end,
}, "Party_Map")
sP1:Toggle({
    Name = "ออกแล้วหาใหม่เมื่อคนน้อย", Description = "ถ้าสมาชิกในปาร์ตี้น้อยกว่าจำนวนที่ตั้ง (หลังอยู่ครบ 45 วินาที) จะออกจากปาร์ตี้แล้ว Quick Match ใหม่",
    Default = true, Callback = function(v) pLeaveSmall = v end,
}, "Party_LeaveSmall")
sP1:Slider({
    Name = "จำนวนสมาชิกขั้นต่ำ", Description = "ปาร์ตี้ที่มีคนน้อยกว่านี้จะถูกออก (ค่าเริ่มต้น 3)",
    Minimum = 2, Maximum = 6, Default = 3, Decimals = 0, Precision = 0,
    Callback = function(v) pMinMembers = math.floor(tonumber(v) or 3) end,
}, "Party_MinMembers")
sP1:Toggle({
    Name = "ออกแล้วหาใหม่เมื่อไม่มีเพื่อนในโซน", Description = "เกมแบ่ง EXP เฉพาะคนโซนเดียวกัน: ย้ายโซนฟาร์มแล้วไม่มีเพื่อนตามมาใน 1 นาที (หรือฟาร์มโซนเดิมแล้วไม่มีเพื่อน 3 นาที) จะออกแล้ว Quick Match แมพที่ฟาร์มอยู่ (เว้นอย่างน้อย 2 นาทีต่อรอบ)",
    Default = true, Callback = function(v) pLeaveNoMate, pNoMateFor = v, 0 end,
}, "Party_LeaveNoMate")
sP1:Slider({
    Name = "เช็คทุกกี่วินาที", Description = "ช่วงเวลาที่ฮับจะตรวจว่ามีปาร์ตี้หรือยัง",
    Minimum = 10, Maximum = 120, Default = 30, Decimals = 0, Precision = 0,
    Callback = function(v) pInterval = math.floor(tonumber(v) or 30) end,
}, "Party_Interval")

task.spawn(function()
    local gen = _G.GolfHub_Gen
    local last = 0
    task.wait(10.0) -- defer initial party quick match checks across join
    while gen == _G.GolfHub_Gen do
        local waited = task.wait(2)
        if gen ~= _G.GolfHub_Gen then break end
        local okNm, left = pcall(noMateTick, waited)
        if okNm and left then last = 0 end -- search again right away
        -- Boss Hunt hops servers alone (it leaves the party before each hop), and Dungeon Farm runs its own
        -- instance loop (Quick Match can drag us to another server mid-run): never search for a party during either.
        if pAuto and not bossFarmActive and not dungeonFarmActive and tick() - last >= pInterval then
            last = tick()
            -- small party: leave and search again (after a grace period so a fresh party can fill up)
            if pAuto and pLeaveSmall and inParty() and not isSellingNow then
                local rawParty = lp:GetAttribute("PartyJson")
                local pj = _cachedPartyJson
                if rawParty ~= _lastPartyJsonRaw then
                    _lastPartyJsonRaw = rawParty
                    local okj, resj = pcall(function() return HttpService:JSONDecode(rawParty) end)
                    if okj and type(resj) == "table" then _cachedPartyJson = resj pj = resj else pj = nil end
                end
                local count = pj and type(pj.Members) == "table" and #pj.Members or 99
                local key = (pj and pj.Id and pj.Leader) and tostring(pj.Id) .. ":" .. tostring(pj.Leader) or ""
                if key ~= pPartyKey then pPartyKey, pPartySince = key, tick() end
                if count < pMinMembers and tick() - pPartySince >= pGrace then
                    local R = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("Remotes")
                    local pa = R and R:FindFirstChild("PartyAction")
                    if pa then
                        pa:FireServer("leave")
                        if valParty then pcall(function() valParty:UpdateName("ปาร์ตี้เหลือ " .. count .. " คน — ออกแล้วหาใหม่") end) end
                        local t0 = tick()
                        while inParty() and tick() - t0 < 6 do task.wait(0.3) end
                        pPartyKey = ""
                        last = 0
                    end
                end
            end
            local travelling = _G.GolfHub_isWarpingTravel and (tick() - (_G.GolfHub_warpStartedAt or 0)) < 45
            -- inside a dungeon run or the World Boss room a Quick Match join can move us to the party's server and
            -- throw the run / fight away: wait until it is over
            local busyRoom = dungeonFarmActive or (isPlayerInDungeon and isPlayerInDungeon()) or lp:GetAttribute("InWorldBoss") == true
            if travelling or busyRoom then
                last = tick() - pInterval + 5 -- never start a party search while the character is walking to a pillar / warping
            elseif not isSellingNow and not inParty() then
                local ok, res, msg = pcall(partyQuickMatch)
                task.wait(2.5)
                pcall(partyCloseUi) -- the window can re-open a moment after Quick Match
                if valParty then pcall(function() valParty:UpdateName(ok and tostring(msg or "-") or "error") end) end
            elseif valParty and pNoMateFor == 0 then
                pcall(function() valParty:UpdateName("อยู่ในปาร์ตี้แล้ว") end)
            end
        end
    end
end)
end)()

local function refreshConfigDropdown()
    if not configDropdown then return end
    local configs = GOLFGUI:RefreshConfigList()
    if #configs == 0 then
        configs = { userProfileName }
    end
    configDropdown:ClearOptions()
    configDropdown:InsertOptions(configs)
    if table.find(configs, selectedConfig) then
        configDropdown:UpdateSelection(selectedConfig)
    else
        selectedConfig = configs[1]
        configDropdown:UpdateSelection(configs[1])
    end
end

GOLFGUI.OnAutoSaved = function(savedName)
    local stamp = os.date("%H:%M:%S")
    if valAutoSaveStatus then
        pcall(function()
            valAutoSaveStatus:Set("💾 Saved " .. savedName .. ".json @ " .. stamp, "run")
        end)
    end
    if valConfigStatus then
        pcall(function()
            valConfigStatus:UpdateName("💾 Auto-saved '" .. savedName .. ".json' (" .. stamp .. ")")
        end)
    end
    if valAutoloadStatus then
        pcall(function()
            valAutoloadStatus:UpdateName(GOLFGUI:GetAutoload() or savedName)
        end)
    end
end

task.wait() -- yield point: spread the start over frames (see the note before TAB: AUTO FARM)
;(function()
local settingsTab = mainGroup:Tab({
    Name        = "Settings",
    Title       = "GOLF HUB — Settings",
    Description = "การตั้งค่าทั่วไปและ Unload",
})

local secMain = settingsTab:Section()
secMain:Header({ Name = "General", Collapsible = false })
secMain:Value({ Name = "Player",      Text = lp.Name })
secMain:Value({ Name = "Navigation",  Text = "✅ Wall Avoidance + Auto-Jump" })
secMain:Value({ Name = "No Teleport", Text = "✅ Legit Walk Only" })

-- Anti-AFK (globe button, top-left of the hub): beats the 20-minute idle kick WITHOUT moving the character.
-- Roblox fires Player.Idled when no input for ~20 min; we answer it with a virtual right-click tap (VirtualUser) and also disable the game's own Idled listeners.
local antiAfkOn = true
local antiAfkHits = 0
local function onIdled()
    if not antiAfkOn then return end
    antiAfkHits = antiAfkHits + 1
    pcall(function()
        local VU = game:GetService("VirtualUser")
        VU:CaptureController()
        VU:ClickButton2(Vector2.new(0, 0))
    end)
end
local function applyAntiAfk(on)
    antiAfkOn = on
    if not on then return end
    -- the game's own Idled handlers (if any) are what would kick; mute them.
    -- getconnections also returns the hub's own listener below: skip it, or the hub mutes itself (seen live:
    -- the hub's Idled connection was Enabled=false, so only the 4-minute heartbeat was keeping the client in).
    pcall(function()
        for _, c in ipairs(getconnections(lp.Idled)) do
            if c.Function ~= onIdled then pcall(function() c:Disable() end) end
        end
    end)
end
_G.GolfHub_Conn(lp.Idled, onIdled)
pcall(function()
    Window:GlobalSetting({
        Name     = "Anti-AFK (กันหลุด ไม่ขยับ)",
        Default  = true,
        Flag     = "Global_AntiAfk",
        Callback = applyAntiAfk,
    })
end)
applyAntiAfk(true)
-- belt and braces: a tiny input-free heartbeat every 4 minutes so a silent idle never reaches 20 minutes (does not move the character)
task.spawn(function()
    local gen = _G.GolfHub_Gen
    while gen == _G.GolfHub_Gen do
        task.wait(240)
        if antiAfkOn then
            pcall(function()
                local VU = game:GetService("VirtualUser")
                VU:CaptureController()
                VU:ClickButton2(Vector2.new(0, 0))
            end)
        end
    end
end)

task.wait() -- yield point: spread the start over frames (see the note before TAB: AUTO FARM)
-- Anti-stuck watchdog for every automation: the humanoid is told to move (MoveDirection > 0) but does not actually move => jump, side-step, then follow a real path
local antiStuckOn = true
secMain:Toggle({
    Name        = "Anti-Stuck (แก้ตัวละครเดินติด)",
    Description = "ถ้าตัวละครพยายามเดินแต่ขยับไม่ได้เกิน ~1.2 วินาที (ติดกำแพง/ขอบ/สะพาน) จะกระโดด ก้าวหลบข้าง แล้วเดินตามเส้นทางจริงให้เอง",
    Default     = true,
    Callback    = function(v) antiStuckOn = v end,
}, "Settings_AntiStuck")
task.spawn(function()
    local gen = _G.GolfHub_Gen
    local blockedSince, strikes, lastGood = nil, 0, tick()
    local pathMode = nil
    -- learned escape points: where the real-path mode successfully led us out of a stuck spot (keyed by a 30-stud grid cell), saved per player
    -- v3: v2 took the corner from whatever path was computed last, so spots by the town pointed 3000 studs away
    local LEARN_FILE = (GOLFGUI.Folder or "GolfHub_HuntingTogether") .. "/learned_routes_v3.json"
    local learned = {}
    local fallbackUntil = 0 -- the path found nothing: let the walker's own unstuck step work
    local function flat(v) return Vector3.new(v.X, 0, v.Z) end
    -- a corner is only worth remembering next to the spot it frees, and never inside the festival booth
    local function saneCorner(corner, from)
        if flat(corner - from).Magnitude > 45 then return false end
        return flat(corner - Vector3.new(0, corner.Y, 37.15)).Magnitude >= 20.5
    end
    pcall(function()
        if isfile and isfile(LEARN_FILE) then
            local t = HttpService:JSONDecode(readfile(LEARN_FILE))
            if type(t) == "table" then learned = t end
        end
    end)
    local function cellKey(p) return math.floor(p.X / 30) .. ":" .. math.floor(p.Z / 30) end
    local function saveLearned()
        pcall(function() if writefile then writefile(LEARN_FILE, HttpService:JSONEncode(learned)) end end)
    end
    while gen == _G.GolfHub_Gen do
        task.wait(0.15)
        local ok, st = pcall(function() return getgenv().GolfHub_GetState() end)
        local active = ok and type(st) == "table" and (st.farmActive or st.bossFarmActive or st.dungeonFarmActive or _G.GolfHub_isWarpingTravel)
        local r, h = getCharRoot()
        local sleeping = lp:GetAttribute("LumiSleeping") == true
        -- asleep it stays out of dungeons: they have their own watchdog, and a side step would break a dodge
        if antiStuckOn and active and r and h and h.Health > 0 and not h.Sit
            and not (sleeping and st.inDungeon) then
            local v = Vector3.new(r.AssemblyLinearVelocity.X, 0, r.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Z).Magnitude
            local wtp = h.WalkToPoint
            local walkGoal = tick() < (_G.GolfHub_WalkingUntil or 0) and _G.GolfHub_WalkGoal or nil
            local farGoal = wtp ~= nil and flat(wtp - r.Position).Magnitude > 6
            if walkGoal then
                -- a hub walker is walking: judge by where it is going, not by the 6-stud path step in WalkToPoint
                wtp, farGoal = walkGoal, flat(walkGoal - r.Position).Magnitude > 3
            elseif sleeping then
                -- WalkToPoint keeps its last value while standing (126 of 126 idle samples asleep were "far"): not a walk
                farGoal = false
            end
            if tick() >= fallbackUntil and (walkGoal or not sleeping) then _G.GolfHub_WatchdogAt = tick() end
            -- fighting is not being stuck: AUTO is driving while standing in reach, or game is holding character
            local isTryingToWalk = (h.MoveDirection.Magnitude > 0.1) or farGoal
            local held = (tick() - (_G.GolfHub_LandedAt or 0) < 3) or r.Anchored
            local fighting = held
            if not fighting and not isTryingToWalk then
                fighting = (tick() < (_G.GolfHub_AutoDrivingUntil or 0))
                if not fighting then
                    local ct = lp:FindFirstChild("CurrentTarget")
                    local m = ct and ct.Value
                    local mp = m and m.Parent and m:IsA("Model") and m.PrimaryPart
                    if mp and (tonumber(m:GetAttribute("HP")) or 1) > 0 then
                        local reach = (tonumber(lp:GetAttribute("AttackRange")) or 7) + (tonumber(m:GetAttribute("Radius")) or 1.5) + 4
                        fighting = Vector3.new(mp.Position.X - r.Position.X, 0, mp.Position.Z - r.Position.Z).Magnitude <= reach
                    end
                end
            end
            if fighting then
                pathMode = nil
                blockedSince, strikes = nil, 0
                _G.GolfHub_UnstuckUntil = 0
            elseif pathMode then
                -- following a real path around the obstacle (invisible walls such as Barrier_Sky_* have a door gap the straight line misses)
                local moved = flat(r.Position - pathMode.from).Magnitude
                if tick() > pathMode.untilT or moved > 25 then
                    if pathMode.learnedHit then
                        if moved < 5 then -- the remembered corner did not help: forget it
                            learned[pathMode.key] = nil
                            saveLearned()
                        end
                    elseif moved > 25 and moved <= 60 and pathMode.corner and saneCorner(pathMode.corner, pathMode.from)
                        and flat(pathMode.goal - r.Position).Magnitude < flat(pathMode.goal - pathMode.from).Magnitude then
                        -- really escaped (not a warp, and toward the goal): remember the first corner that worked here
                        learned[pathMode.key] = { x = pathMode.corner.X, y = pathMode.corner.Y, z = pathMode.corner.Z }
                        saveLearned()
                    end
                    pathMode = nil
                    blockedSince, strikes = nil, 0
                else
                    pcall(function()
                        -- the first call always answers false while the path is still being computed: wait for it
                        local steering = _G.GolfHub_PathWalk(r, h, pathMode.goal)
                        local ps = _G.GolfHub_PathState
                        -- the corner comes from this escape's own finished path only
                        if not pathMode.corner and ps and not ps.computing and ps.wps and #ps.wps >= 3
                            and ps.at >= pathMode.began and ps.goal and (ps.goal - pathMode.goal).Magnitude < 1 then
                            pathMode.corner = ps.wps[math.min(#ps.wps, 4)].Position
                        end
                        if steering or (ps and ps.computing) then
                            _G.GolfHub_UnstuckUntil = tick() + 0.4
                        else
                            if ps and not ps.wps then fallbackUntil = tick() + 3 end
                            pathMode = nil
                        end
                    end)
                end
            elseif v > 2.5 then
                blockedSince, strikes, lastGood = nil, 0, tick()
            elseif h.MoveDirection.Magnitude > 0.1 or farGoal then
                blockedSince = blockedSince or tick()
                local known = learned[cellKey(r.Position)]
                local corner = known and tonumber(known.x) and tonumber(known.y) and tonumber(known.z)
                    and Vector3.new(known.x, known.y, known.z) or nil
                -- use it only if it is near and on the way to where we are going now
                if corner and not (farGoal and saneCorner(corner, r.Position)
                    and flat(corner - r.Position):Dot(flat(wtp - r.Position)) > 0) then
                    corner = nil
                end
                if tick() - blockedSince > (corner and 0.4 or 1.2) then
                    strikes = strikes + 1
                    blockedSince = tick()
                    pcall(function()
                        if farGoal and _G.GolfHub_PathWalk then
                            -- a fresh state, so this escape never follows another walk's old path
                            _G.GolfHub_PathState = nil
                            if corner then
                                -- we have escaped this exact spot before: walk to the remembered corner first (center of the gap), then continue
                                pathMode = { goal = corner, from = r.Position, untilT = tick() + 6, began = tick(), key = cellKey(r.Position), learnedHit = true }
                            else
                                -- the goal is far away and we are not moving: go straight to the real-path mode
                                pathMode = { goal = wtp, from = r.Position, untilT = tick() + 12, began = tick(), key = cellKey(r.Position) }
                            end
                        elseif strikes % 2 == 1 then
                            h.Jump = true
                        else
                            local dir = h.MoveDirection
                            local side = Vector3.new(-dir.Z, 0, dir.X) * ((strikes % 4 == 0) and 7 or -7)
                            h:MoveTo(r.Position + side + dir * 3)
                            _G.GolfHub_UnstuckUntil = tick() + 0.5
                            h.Jump = true
                        end
                    end)
                end
            else
                blockedSince = nil
            end
        else
            blockedSince = nil
        end
    end
end)

-- UI size for mobile / small screens (the window is 868x700 at 100%)
local function hubFitScale()
    local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
    local vp = cam and cam.ViewportSize or Vector2.new(1280, 720)
    local w, h = 868, 640
    local okSz, sz = pcall(function() return Window:GetSize() end)
    if okSz and typeof(sz) == "UDim2" and sz.X.Offset > 100 then w, h = sz.X.Offset, sz.Y.Offset end
    return math.clamp(math.min((vp.X - 24) / w, (vp.Y - 24) / h), 0.4, 1)
end
task.wait() -- yield point: spread the start over frames (see the note before TAB: AUTO FARM)
local uiScaleSlider
uiScaleSlider = secMain:Slider({
    Name        = "UI Scale (ขนาดหน้าต่าง %)",
    Description = "ย่อ/ขยายหน้าต่าง GOLF HUB สำหรับมือถือหรือจอเล็ก (40–120%)",
    Minimum = 40, Maximum = 120, Default = 70, Decimals = 0, Precision = 0,
    Callback = function(v)
        pcall(function() Window:SetScale(math.clamp((tonumber(v) or 70) / 100, 0.4, 1.2)) end)
        local box = GOLFGUI.Options.Settings_UIScaleInput
        if box and box.SetText then box:SetText(tostring(math.floor(tonumber(v) or 70))) end
    end,
}, "Settings_UIScale")
pcall(function() Window:SetScale(0.7) end) -- the window itself starts at 100%; a saved value replaces this on load
secMain:Input({
    Name        = "พิมพ์ขนาดตรงๆ (Custom %)",
    Description = "พิมพ์เปอร์เซ็นต์ขนาดหน้าต่าง 40–120 เช่น 60, 75, 100 แล้วกด Enter",
    Placeholder = "เช่น 70",
    Default     = "70",
    AcceptedCharacters = "Numeric",
    Width       = 110,
    IgnoreConfig = true, -- the slider above is the saved one; this box only applies a typed value
    Callback    = function(text)
        local num = tonumber(text)
        if num and num >= 40 and num <= 120 then
            num = math.floor(num)
            pcall(function() Window:SetScale(num / 100) end)
            if uiScaleSlider and uiScaleSlider.SetValue then pcall(function() uiScaleSlider:SetValue(num) end) end -- the slider is what gets saved
        end
    end,
}, "Settings_UIScaleInput")
-- first run on a touch device: fit the window automatically (a saved Settings_UIScale overrides this when the config loads)
task.defer(function()
    local UIS = game:GetService("UserInputService")
    if UIS.TouchEnabled and not UIS.KeyboardEnabled then
        local s = math.min(hubFitScale(), 0.7) -- smaller if the screen needs it, never above the default
        pcall(function() Window:SetScale(s) end)
        if uiScaleSlider and uiScaleSlider.SetValue then pcall(function() uiScaleSlider:SetValue(math.floor(s * 100)) end) end
    end
end)

-- Sleep mode: the game's own black "Slide to wake up" screen. Auto Farm keeps running underneath (it is driven by this hub, not by the screen).
local function hubEnterSleep()
    local pg = lp:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
    local btn = pg and pg:FindFirstChild("LumiAuto") and pg.LumiAuto:FindFirstChild("RestModeButton")
    if not btn or lp:GetAttribute("LumiSleeping") == true then return false end
    for _, c in ipairs(getconnections(btn.MouseButton1Click)) do
        local f = c.Function
        if f then
            for _, v in pairs(debug.getupvalues(f)) do
                if type(v) == "function" and debug.info(v, "n") == "enterRest" then
                    return pcall(v)
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return false
end


local sleepState = { on = false, delay = 20, reset = false }
secMain:Toggle({
    Name        = "Auto Sleep เมื่อเริ่มฟาร์ม",
    Description = "เมื่อเปิด Auto Farm / Boss / Dungeon แล้ว จะเข้าโหมด Sleep ให้อัตโนมัติหลังหน่วงเวลา (ปลุกด้วยสไลด์ได้ตลอด)",
    Default     = false,
    Callback    = function(v)
        sleepState.on = (v == true)
        if sleepState.on then sleepState.reset = true end
    end,
}, "Settings_AutoSleep")
secMain:Slider({
    Name = "หน่วงเวลาก่อน Sleep (วินาที)", Description = "รอกี่วินาทีหลังเริ่มฟาร์มก่อนเข้าโหมด Sleep",
    Minimum = 5, Maximum = 120, Default = 20, Decimals = 0, Precision = 0,
    Callback = function(v) sleepState.delay = math.floor(tonumber(v) or 20) end,
}, "Settings_AutoSleepDelay")

task.spawn(function()
    local gen, farmSince = _G.GolfHub_Gen, nil
    while gen == _G.GolfHub_Gen do
        task.wait(2)
        if sleepState.reset then
            sleepState.reset = false
            farmSince = nil
        end
        local ok, st = pcall(function() return getgenv().GolfHub_GetState() end)
        local farming = ok and type(st) == "table" and (st.farmActive == true or st.dungeonFarmActive == true or st.bossFarmActive == true)
        if not farming then
            farmSince = nil
        elseif sleepState.on then
            farmSince = farmSince or tick()
            -- one automatic sleep per farm start; after the user wakes up it does not sleep again until farming restarts or toggle is re-enabled
            if farmSince ~= -1 and tick() - farmSince >= sleepState.delay then
                local okSleep, slept = pcall(hubEnterSleep)
                if okSleep and slept then
                    farmSince = -1
                end
            end
        else
            farmSince = nil
        end
    end
end)

-- ====================================================================
-- SECTION: LOW GRAPHICS MODE (โหมดลดกราฟิกสำหรับเปิดหลายจอ)
-- ====================================================================
-- 4 clients on one PC share CPU/GPU. The stutter is mostly the game's own skill visuals,
-- particle emitters, and post-processing effects in crowded farming areas.
-- This module applies only LOCAL, visual-only reductions and completely restores
-- all modified properties when toggled off or when the hub unloads.
do
    local secLowGfx = settingsTab:Section()
    secLowGfx:Header({ Name = "🖥️ โหมดลดกราฟิก (Low Graphics)", Collapsible = false })

    local masterOn = false
    local qualityOn = true
    local lightingOn = true
    local hideFxOn = true
    local fxScope = "เฉพาะผู้เล่นอื่น"
    local fpsCap = 60
    local disable3DUnfocused = true

    local prevQualityLevel = nil
    local prevLightingState = nil
    local disabledFx = {} -- Strong table: prevents Luau GC from dropping disabled effects before restore
    local disabledFxCount = 0
    local prevFpsCap = nil
    local is3DDisabled = false
    local newFxQueue = {}
    local valLowGfxStatus = nil

    local FX_CLASSES = {
        ParticleEmitter = true,
        Trail           = true,
        Beam            = true,
        Smoke           = true,
        Fire            = true,
        Sparkles        = true,
    }

    local function isLocalPlayerEffect(inst)
        local myChar = lp.Character
        if myChar and inst:IsDescendantOf(myChar) then return true end
        local myName = lp.Name
        local myId = lp.UserId
        local owner = inst:GetAttribute("Owner") or inst:GetAttribute("Creator") or inst:GetAttribute("OwnerUserId")
        if owner == myName or owner == myId then return true end
        local parent = inst.Parent
        if parent then
            local pOwner = parent:GetAttribute("Owner") or parent:GetAttribute("Creator") or parent:GetAttribute("OwnerUserId")
            if pOwner == myName or pOwner == myId then return true end
            if myChar and parent:IsDescendantOf(myChar) then return true end
        end
        return false
    end

    local function disableSingleEffect(inst)
        if not inst or not inst.Parent then return end
        if not FX_CLASSES[inst.ClassName] then return end
        if disabledFx[inst] ~= nil then return end
        if (fxScope == "เฉพาะผู้เล่นอื่น" or fxScope == "ทุกอย่างยกเว้นของเรา") and isLocalPlayerEffect(inst) then return end

        local ok, enabled = pcall(function() return inst.Enabled end)
        if ok and enabled == true then
            disabledFx[inst] = true
            disabledFxCount = disabledFxCount + 1
            pcall(function() inst.Enabled = false end)
        end
    end

    local function restoreAllEffects()
        for inst, wasEnabled in pairs(disabledFx) do
            if wasEnabled then
                pcall(function()
                    if inst and inst.Parent then
                        inst.Enabled = true
                    end
                end)
            end
        end
        table.clear(disabledFx)
        disabledFxCount = 0
        table.clear(newFxQueue)
    end

    local function applyQuality()
        local rend = settings():GetService("RenderSettings")
        if prevQualityLevel == nil then
            pcall(function() prevQualityLevel = rend.QualityLevel end)
        end
        pcall(function() rend.QualityLevel = Enum.QualityLevel.Level01 end)
    end

    local function restoreQuality()
        if prevQualityLevel ~= nil then
            local rend = settings():GetService("RenderSettings")
            pcall(function() rend.QualityLevel = prevQualityLevel end)
            prevQualityLevel = nil
        end
    end

    local function applyLighting()
        local light = game:GetService("Lighting")
        local terr = workspace.Terrain
        if prevLightingState == nil then
            prevLightingState = {
                globalShadows = light.GlobalShadows,
                terrainDecoration = pcall(function() return terr.Decoration end) and terr.Decoration or nil,
                effects = {}
            }
            for _, c in ipairs(light:GetChildren()) do
                if c:IsA("PostProcessEffect") or c:IsA("BloomEffect") or c:IsA("BlurEffect")
                    or c:IsA("SunRaysEffect") or c:IsA("ColorCorrectionEffect") or c:IsA("DepthOfFieldEffect") then
                    prevLightingState.effects[c] = { kind = "enabled", val = c.Enabled }
                elseif c:IsA("Atmosphere") then
                    prevLightingState.effects[c] = { kind = "density", val = c.Density }
                end
            end
        end

        pcall(function() light.GlobalShadows = false end)
        pcall(function() terr.Decoration = false end)
        for inst, info in pairs(prevLightingState.effects) do
            if inst and inst.Parent then
                pcall(function()
                    if info.kind == "enabled" then
                        inst.Enabled = false
                    elseif info.kind == "density" then
                        inst.Density = 0
                    end
                end)
            end
        end
    end

    local function restoreLighting()
        if prevLightingState ~= nil then
            local light = game:GetService("Lighting")
            local terr = workspace.Terrain
            pcall(function() light.GlobalShadows = prevLightingState.globalShadows end)
            pcall(function()
                if prevLightingState.terrainDecoration ~= nil then
                    terr.Decoration = prevLightingState.terrainDecoration
                end
            end)
            for inst, info in pairs(prevLightingState.effects) do
                if inst and inst.Parent then
                    pcall(function()
                        if info.kind == "enabled" then
                            inst.Enabled = info.val
                        elseif info.kind == "density" then
                            inst.Density = info.val
                        end
                    end)
                end
            end
            prevLightingState = nil
        end
    end

    local function recordOriginalFps()
        if type(getfpscap) == "function" and prevFpsCap == nil then
            pcall(function()
                local cur = getfpscap()
                if type(cur) == "number" and cur > 0 then
                    prevFpsCap = cur
                end
            end)
        end
    end

    local function applyFpsCap(targetCap)
        recordOriginalFps()
        if type(setfpscap) == "function" then
            pcall(setfpscap, targetCap or fpsCap)
        end
    end

    local function restoreFpsCap()
        if prevFpsCap ~= nil and type(setfpscap) == "function" then
            pcall(setfpscap, prevFpsCap)
            prevFpsCap = nil
        end
    end

    local function set3DRendering(enabled)
        local rs = cloneref and cloneref(game:GetService("RunService")) or game:GetService("RunService")
        pcall(function() rs:Set3DRenderingEnabled(enabled) end)
        is3DDisabled = not enabled
    end

    local function onFocusChanged(isFocused)
        if not masterOn or not disable3DUnfocused then
            if is3DDisabled then
                set3DRendering(true)
                applyFpsCap(fpsCap)
            end
            return
        end

        if isFocused then
            if is3DDisabled then
                set3DRendering(true)
            end
            applyFpsCap(fpsCap)
        else
            set3DRendering(false)
            recordOriginalFps()
            if type(setfpscap) == "function" then
                pcall(setfpscap, 15)
            end
        end
    end

    local function updateStatus()
        if not valLowGfxStatus then return end
        if not masterOn then
            pcall(function() valLowGfxStatus:UpdateName("ปิดอยู่ (Off)") end)
            return
        end

        local parts = {}
        if qualityOn then table.insert(parts, "Q1") end
        if lightingOn then table.insert(parts, "เงา ปิด") end
        if hideFxOn then
            local scopeLabel = (fxScope == "ทั้งหมด") and "ทั้งหมด" or "ผู้เล่นอื่น"
            table.insert(parts, string.format("เอฟเฟกต์ซ่อน %d (%s)", disabledFxCount, scopeLabel))
        end
        if type(getfpscap) == "function" then
            local curCap = getfpscap()
            table.insert(parts, string.format("FPS %s", tostring(curCap)))
        end
        if disable3DUnfocused then
            table.insert(parts, is3DDisabled and "3D ปิด (นอกจอ)" or "3D เปิด (โฟกัส)")
        end

        local text = #parts > 0 and table.concat(parts, " · ") or "เปิดอยู่"
        pcall(function() valLowGfxStatus:UpdateName(text) end)
    end

    local fxScanGen = 0
    local function applyHideEffectsBatched()
        fxScanGen = fxScanGen + 1
        local myScan = fxScanGen
        local myHubGen = _G.GolfHub_Gen
        task.spawn(function()
            local descs = workspace:GetDescendants()
            for i = 1, #descs do
                if fxScanGen ~= myScan or _G.GolfHub_Gen ~= myHubGen or not (masterOn and hideFxOn) then
                    return
                end
                local inst = descs[i]
                if FX_CLASSES[inst.ClassName] then
                    disableSingleEffect(inst)
                end
                if i % 600 == 0 then
                    task.wait()
                end
            end
            if fxScanGen == myScan and _G.GolfHub_Gen == myHubGen then
                updateStatus()
            end
        end)
    end

    local function syncLowGfx()
        if not masterOn then
            fxScanGen = fxScanGen + 1
            restoreQuality()
            restoreLighting()
            restoreAllEffects()
            set3DRendering(true)
            restoreFpsCap()
            updateStatus()
            return
        end

        if qualityOn then applyQuality() else restoreQuality() end
        if lightingOn then applyLighting() else restoreLighting() end

        if hideFxOn then
            applyHideEffectsBatched()
        else
            fxScanGen = fxScanGen + 1
            restoreAllEffects()
        end

        local focused = true
        if type(isrbxactive) == "function" then
            local ok, active = pcall(isrbxactive)
            if ok then focused = active end
        end
        onFocusChanged(focused)
        updateStatus()
    end

    local function restoreEverything()
        restoreQuality()
        restoreLighting()
        restoreAllEffects()
        set3DRendering(true)
        restoreFpsCap()
    end
    _G.GolfHub_RestoreLowGfx = restoreEverything

    valLowGfxStatus = secLowGfx:Value({
        Name = "สถานะระบบ",
        Text = "ปิดอยู่ (Off)",
    }, "Settings_LowGfx_Status")

    secLowGfx:Toggle({
        Name        = "เปิดโหมดลดกราฟิก",
        Description = "เปิด/ปิด ระบบลดภาระกราฟิกเพื่อเพิ่ม FPS สำหรับเปิดหลายจอ (คืนค่าเดิมทั้งหมดเมื่อปิด)",
        Default     = false,
        Callback    = function(v)
            masterOn = (v == true)
            if masterOn then
                recordOriginalFps()
            end
            syncLowGfx()
            if GOLFGUI.IsLoadingConfig then
                task.spawn(function()
                    local myGen = _G.GolfHub_Gen
                    local t0 = tick()
                    while GOLFGUI.IsLoadingConfig and _G.GolfHub_Gen == myGen and tick() - t0 < 3 do
                        task.wait(0.1)
                    end
                    if _G.GolfHub_Gen == myGen and masterOn then
                        syncLowGfx()
                    end
                end)
            end
        end,
    }, "Settings_LowGfx_Master")

    secLowGfx:Toggle({
        Name        = "ลดคุณภาพเรนเดอร์ (Quality Level 1)",
        Description = "ปรับระดับ Render Quality ใน RAM ชั่วคราวเป็น Level 1 (ไม่บันทึกทับการตั้งค่า Roblox ถาวร)",
        Default     = true,
        Callback    = function(v)
            qualityOn = (v == true)
            if masterOn then
                if qualityOn then applyQuality() else restoreQuality() end
                updateStatus()
            end
        end,
    }, "Settings_LowGfx_QualityLevel")

    secLowGfx:Toggle({
        Name        = "ปิดเงาและเอฟเฟกต์แสง (Lighting & Shadows)",
        Description = "ปิด GlobalShadows, ปิด Bloom, Blur, SunRays, ColorCorrection, DepthOfField, Atmosphere และหญ้า 3D",
        Default     = true,
        Callback    = function(v)
            lightingOn = (v == true)
            if masterOn then
                if lightingOn then applyLighting() else restoreLighting() end
                updateStatus()
            end
        end,
    }, "Settings_LowGfx_Lighting")

    secLowGfx:Toggle({
        Name        = "ซ่อนเอฟเฟกต์ (Hide Effects)",
        Description = "ปิด ParticleEmitter, Trail, Beam, Smoke, Fire, Sparkles (ไม่แตะต้อง BaseParts หรือวงหลบสกิลบอส)",
        Default     = true,
        Callback    = function(v)
            hideFxOn = (v == true)
            if masterOn then
                if hideFxOn then
                    applyHideEffectsBatched()
                else
                    fxScanGen = fxScanGen + 1
                    restoreAllEffects()
                end
                updateStatus()
            end
        end,
    }, "Settings_LowGfx_HideEffects")

    secLowGfx:Dropdown({
        Name        = "ขอบเขตการซ่อนเอฟเฟกต์",
        Description = "ซ่อนเอฟเฟกต์มอนสเตอร์ แมพ และผู้เล่นอื่นทั้งหมด (แสดงเฉพาะเอฟเฟกต์ของตัวเราเอง) หรือซ่อนทั้งหมดในเกม",
        Options     = { "เฉพาะผู้เล่นอื่น", "ทั้งหมด" },
        Default     = "เฉพาะผู้เล่นอื่น",
        Callback    = function(v)
            local val = type(v) == "table" and (v[1] or next(v)) or v
            fxScope = tostring(val or "เฉพาะผู้เล่นอื่น")
            if masterOn and hideFxOn then
                restoreAllEffects()
                applyHideEffectsBatched()
                updateStatus()
            end
        end,
    }, "Settings_LowGfx_EffectsScope")

    secLowGfx:Slider({
        Name        = "จำกัด FPS (FPS Cap)",
        Description = "จำกัดอัตราเฟรมเรตต่อวินาทีเพื่อประหยัด CPU (15-165 FPS)",
        Minimum     = 15,
        Maximum     = 165,
        Default     = 60,
        Decimals    = 0,
        Precision   = 0,
        Callback    = function(v)
            fpsCap = math.floor(tonumber(v) or 60)
            if masterOn and not is3DDisabled then
                applyFpsCap(fpsCap)
                updateStatus()
            end
        end,
    }, "Settings_LowGfx_FpsCap")

    secLowGfx:Toggle({
        Name        = "ปิดภาพ 3D ตอนไม่ได้ดูจอนี้ (Unfocused 3D)",
        Description = "เมื่อสลับไปหน้าต่างอื่น จะหยุดเรนเดอร์ภาพ 3D และจำกัด FPS เหลือ 15 ทันที (คืนค่าเมื่อคลิกกลับมา)",
        Default     = true,
        Callback    = function(v)
            disable3DUnfocused = (v == true)
            if not disable3DUnfocused and is3DDisabled then
                set3DRendering(true)
                applyFpsCap(fpsCap)
            end
            updateStatus()
        end,
    }, "Settings_LowGfx_Disable3DUnfocused")

    -- Event listener for window focus changes
    local uis = cloneref and cloneref(game:GetService("UserInputService")) or game:GetService("UserInputService")
    _G.GolfHub_Conn(uis.WindowFocusReleased, function()
        if masterOn and disable3DUnfocused then
            onFocusChanged(false)
            updateStatus()
        end
    end)

    _G.GolfHub_Conn(uis.WindowFocused, function()
        if masterOn and disable3DUnfocused then
            onFocusChanged(true)
            updateStatus()
        end
    end)

    -- Lightweight DescendantAdded listener: ONLY queues candidates, zero heavy work inside the event signal
    _G.GolfHub_Conn(workspace.DescendantAdded, function(inst)
        if masterOn and hideFxOn then
            if FX_CLASSES[inst.ClassName] then
                table.insert(newFxQueue, inst)
            end
        end
    end)

    -- Background queue processor and focus poll watchdog (0.5s cadence, stops on hub reload)
    task.spawn(function()
        local myGen = _G.GolfHub_Gen
        local lastFocusPoll = tick()
        local lastPrune = tick()
        while myGen == _G.GolfHub_Gen do
            task.wait(0.5)
            if masterOn then
                if hideFxOn and #newFxQueue > 0 then
                    local q = newFxQueue
                    newFxQueue = {}
                    for _, inst in ipairs(q) do
                        if inst and inst.Parent then
                            disableSingleEffect(inst)
                        end
                    end
                end

                -- Periodically prune destroyed instances from disabledFx to prevent memory leaks
                if tick() - lastPrune >= 5.0 then
                    lastPrune = tick()
                    for inst in pairs(disabledFx) do
                        local ok, parent = pcall(function() return inst.Parent end)
                        if not ok or not parent then
                            disabledFx[inst] = nil
                            disabledFxCount = math.max(0, disabledFxCount - 1)
                        end
                    end
                end

                if disable3DUnfocused and type(isrbxactive) == "function" and (tick() - lastFocusPoll >= 1.0) then
                    lastFocusPoll = tick()
                    local ok, active = pcall(isrbxactive)
                    if ok then
                        if active and is3DDisabled then
                            onFocusChanged(true)
                            updateStatus()
                        elseif (not active) and (not is3DDisabled) then
                            onFocusChanged(false)
                            updateStatus()
                        end
                    end
                end

                updateStatus()
            end
        end
    end)
end

secMain:Button({
    Name     = "Unload GUI",
    Primary  = false,
    Callback = function()
        getgenv().GolfHub_Unload(true)
    end,
})
end)()

-- ====================================================================
-- SELECT DEFAULT TAB & INITIAL SYNC (PER-USER AUTOLOAD + AUTO-SAVE INIT)
-- ====================================================================
task.spawn(function()
    isBootstrappingConfig = true
    GOLFGUI.IsLoadingConfig = true
    syncSkillSlotsFromGame()
    updateSkillToggleVisuals()
    task.wait() -- yield point: let skill visuals settle before config autoload
    -- Autoload per-user config if present in workspace/GolfHub_HuntingTogether/<PlayerName>/settings/
    local loadedProfile = nil
    pcall(function()
        loadedProfile = GOLFGUI:LoadAutoLoadConfig()
    end)
    if loadedProfile and loadedProfile ~= "" then
        GOLFGUI.AutoSaveName = loadedProfile
        -- Wait for batched option loaders to really finish loading before enabling real-time auto-save
        local t0 = tick()
        while GOLFGUI.IsLoadingConfig and tick() - t0 < 10 do
            task.wait(0.05)
        end
    else
        GOLFGUI.IsLoadingConfig = false
    end
    isBootstrappingConfig = false
    GOLFGUI.IsLoadingConfig = false
    task.wait() -- yield point: let config settlement breathe before bot sync
    syncSkillSelectionToBotJson()

    -- First-time run for this player: create their dedicated per-user config file & autoload immediately
    if not loadedProfile and GOLFGUI.AutoSaveEnabled and GOLFGUI.AutoSaveName then
        pcall(function()
            GOLFGUI:SetAutoload(GOLFGUI.AutoSaveName)
            local ok, filePath = GOLFGUI:SaveConfig(GOLFGUI.AutoSaveName)
            if ok and type(GOLFGUI.OnAutoSaved) == "function" then
                pcall(GOLFGUI.OnAutoSaved, GOLFGUI.AutoSaveName, filePath)
            end
        end)
    end

    -- ถ้าอยู่ในดันเจี้ยน: เปิด Auto Farm Dungeon + Combat + [F] AUTO หลังโหลด Config เสร็จ 100% (กัน Config เก่าทับเป็น false!)
    if isPlayerInDungeon and isPlayerInDungeon() then
        if dungeonFarmTab then pcall(function() dungeonFarmTab:Select() end) end
        if toggleDungeonCombat then pcall(function() toggleDungeonCombat:Set(true) end) end
        if toggleDungeonFarm then pcall(function() toggleDungeonFarm:Set(true) end) end
        if not dungeonFarmActive then
            dungeonFarmActive = true
            if dungeonThread then task.cancel(dungeonThread) end
            dungeonThread = task.spawn(dungeonMainLoop)
        end
    elseif bossFarmTab then
        pcall(function() bossFarmTab:Select() end)
        if bossFarmActive then
            if not bossFarmThread or coroutine.status(bossFarmThread) == "dead" then
                bossFarmThread = task.spawn(runBossFarmLoopSafe)
            end
        end
    end

    -- on every join / server hop: shrink to the mini bar in the top right corner so it does not cover the screen
    task.wait(1.5)
    pcall(function()
        Window:SetState(false)
    end)
end)

if getgenv then
    -- byUser: red dot / Unload GUI. A reload passes nothing and leaves AUTO running inside a dungeon.
    getgenv().GolfHub_Unload = function(byUser)
        if task.closer then return end
        -- write this copy's dungeon run stats before its threads are parked (see _G.GolfHub_DngStatsSave)
        pcall(function() if _G.GolfHub_DngStatsSave then _G.GolfHub_DngStatsSave("script reloaded") end end)
        _G.GolfHub_DngStatsSave = nil
        task.closer = coroutine.running()
        pcall(function()
            _G.GolfHub_Gen = (tonumber(_G.GolfHub_Gen) or 0) + 1
            isSellingNow = false
            _G.GolfHub_UnstuckUntil, _G.GolfHub_WalkingUntil, _G.GolfHub_AutoDrivingUntil = 0, 0, 0
            if type(_G.GolfHub_Conns) == "table" then
                for _, c in ipairs(_G.GolfHub_Conns) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
                _G.GolfHub_Conns = {}
            end
            _G.GolfHub_isWarpingTravel = false
            if removeBossGuideLine then pcall(removeBossGuideLine) end
            dungeonFarmActive = false
            if dungeonThread then task.cancel(dungeonThread) dungeonThread = nil end
            local r = getCharRoot and getCharRoot()
            local bv = r and r:FindFirstChild("GolfHub_AirHoverBV")
            if bv then bv:Destroy() end
            if _G.DungeonNoclipConnection then
                pcall(function() _G.DungeonNoclipConnection:Disconnect() end)
                _G.DungeonNoclipConnection = nil
            end
            pcall(function()
                local rs = cloneref and cloneref(game:GetService("RunService")) or game:GetService("RunService")
                rs:UnbindFromRenderStep("GolfHub_DngWalkFix")
            end)
            if _G.GolfHub_DngWatchdogConns then
                for _, c in ipairs(_G.GolfHub_DngWatchdogConns) do
                    pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end)
                end
                _G.GolfHub_DngWatchdogConns = nil
            end
            if _G.GolfHub_DemonSkullConns then
                for _, c in ipairs(_G.GolfHub_DemonSkullConns) do
                    pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end)
                end
                _G.GolfHub_DemonSkullConns = nil
            end
            if _G.GolfHub_DngAutoLockConns then
                for _, c in ipairs(_G.GolfHub_DngAutoLockConns) do
                    pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end)
                end
                _G.GolfHub_DngAutoLockConns = nil
            end
            if _G.GolfHub_DngAutoLockThread then
                pcall(task.cancel, _G.GolfHub_DngAutoLockThread)
                _G.GolfHub_DngAutoLockThread = nil
            end
            if _G.GolfHub_FarmLevelConns then
                for _, c in ipairs(_G.GolfHub_FarmLevelConns) do
                    pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end)
                end
                _G.GolfHub_FarmLevelConns = nil
            end
            if getgenv and type(getgenv().GolfHub_NpcConnections) == "table" then
                for _, conn in ipairs(getgenv().GolfHub_NpcConnections) do
                    pcall(function() conn:Disconnect() end)
                end
                getgenv().GolfHub_NpcConnections = nil
            end
            bossFarmActive = false
            if bossFarmThread then task.cancel(bossFarmThread) bossFarmThread = nil end
            farmActive = false
            if farmThread then task.cancel(farmThread) farmThread = nil end
            if byUser or not (isPlayerInDungeon and isPlayerInDungeon()) then
                _G.GolfHub_IsUnloading = byUser and true or nil
                pcall(setBotRunning, false)
                _G.GolfHub_IsUnloading = nil
                local nowRoot, nowHum = getCharRoot()
                if nowRoot and nowHum then nowHum:MoveTo(nowRoot.Position) end -- drop the last walk order
                if byUser then pcall(function() lp:SetAttribute("RespawnKeepBot", false) end) end
            end
            if _G.GolfHub_RestoreLowGfx then
                pcall(_G.GolfHub_RestoreLowGfx)
                _G.GolfHub_RestoreLowGfx = nil
            end
            if Window and Window.Unload then Window:Unload() end
        end)
    end
    -- the red dot only removed the window
    do
        local shutdown = getgenv().GolfHub_Unload
        Window.onUnloaded(function() shutdown(true) end)
    end
    getgenv().GolfHub_ToggleFarm = function(val)
        if toggleAutoFarm then
            toggleAutoFarm:Set(val)
        end
    end
    getgenv().GolfHub_ToggleDungeonFarm = function(val)
        if toggleDungeonFarm then
            toggleDungeonFarm:Set(val)
        end
    end
    getgenv().GolfHub_SelectFarmZone = function(zoneKey)
        selectedFarmZoneKey = zoneKey
    end
    getgenv().GolfHub_Debug = getgenv().GolfHub_Debug or {}
    getgenv().GolfHub_Debug.sell = executeSellRoutine
    getgenv().GolfHub_GetState = function()
        local lv = getLevel and getLevel() or 1
        local targetZone = resolveFarmTargetZone and resolveFarmTargetZone(lv)
        local inDng, curDng = false, nil
        if isPlayerInDungeon then inDng, curDng = isPlayerInDungeon() end
        return {
            farmActive = farmActive,
            bossFarmActive = bossFarmActive,
            dungeonFarmActive = dungeonFarmActive,
            inDungeon = inDng,
            selectedFarmZoneKey = selectedFarmZoneKey,
            targetZone = targetZone and targetZone.name or "nil",
            targetZoneKey = targetZone and targetZone.zone or "nil",
            currentZone = getCurrentActualZone and getCurrentActualZone() or "nil",
            isSellingNow = isSellingNow,
            bagUsed = getBagItemCount and getBagItemCount() or 0,
            autoQuestEnabled = Quest.autoQuestEnabled,
            totalQuestsClaimed = Quest.totalQuestsClaimed,
            totalExpClaimed = Quest.totalExpClaimed,
            activeQuests = getActiveQuestsInfo and getActiveQuestsInfo() or {}
        }
    end
end

Window:Notify({
    Title       = "GOLF HUB — Ready",
    Description = "✅ ย่อไว้ที่มุมขวาบน (คลิกแถบหรือกด RightShift เพื่อเปิดหน้าต่าง)",
    Lifetime    = 5,
})

-- Join trace (debug): when GolfHub_HuntingTogether/trace_on.txt exists, log status/phase/position for 120 s after load
task.spawn(function()
    local ok = pcall(function() return isfile("GolfHub_HuntingTogether/trace_on.txt") end) and isfile("GolfHub_HuntingTogether/trace_on.txt")
    if not ok then return end
    local gen = _G.GolfHub_Gen
    local fn = "GolfHub_HuntingTogether/join_trace.txt"
    local t0 = tick()
    pcall(appendfile, fn, "\n=== join " .. game.JobId .. " " .. os.date("%X") .. "\n")
    local last = ""
    while gen == _G.GolfHub_Gen and tick() - t0 < 120 do
        local r = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character and game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local line = string.format("%s | %s | %s", tostring(_G.GolfHub_BossPhase), tostring(_G.GolfHub_BossStatus), r and string.format("%.0f,%.0f,%.0f", r.Position.X, r.Position.Y, r.Position.Z) or "nochar")
        if line ~= last then pcall(appendfile, fn, string.format("%5.1f %s\n", tick() - t0, line)) last = line end
        task.wait(0.5)
    end
end)

-- Dungeon run stats: one JSON line per run in <player folder>/dungeon_stats.jsonl (time, downs, damage taken per
-- source, dodges, lowest HP, boss time). Lets us see over many runs what still hurts.
task.spawn(function()
    if not (isPlayerInDungeon and isPlayerInDungeon()) and game.PlaceId ~= DUNGEON_PLACE_ID then return end
    local gen = _G.GolfHub_Gen
    local HS = game:GetService("HttpService")
    local file = (GOLFGUI.Folder or "GolfHub_HuntingTogether") .. "/dungeon_stats.jsonl"
    -- The dungeon server keeps the difficulty on its own objects, not as lp.DungeonDifficulty (every record said "?").
    -- Look for any *Diff* attribute on workspace / player / ReplicatedStorage; else the difficulty picked in the hub.
    local function dungeonDiff()
        for _, inst in ipairs({ workspace, lp, game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage") }) do
            local okA, attrs = pcall(function() return inst:GetAttributes() end)
            if okA and type(attrs) == "table" then
                for k, v in pairs(attrs) do
                    if tostring(k):lower():find("diff", 1, true) then return tostring(v) end
                end
            end
        end
        local opt = GOLFGUI.Options and GOLFGUI.Options["Dungeon_SelectedDifficulty"]
        local sel = opt and opt.Value
        if type(sel) == "table" then sel = sel[1] end
        return sel and ("picked " .. (tostring(sel):match("^%S+") or "?")) or "?"
    end
    local S = { job = game.JobId, start = os.time(), downs = 0, minHp = math.huge, dmg = {}, downBy = {}, dodges = 0, warns = {}, bossSeen = nil, bossDead = nil, diff = dungeonDiff() }
    -- seconds spent in each objective step (1 monsters, 2 pillars, 3 Witch, 4 boss, 5 cleared) and while downed:
    -- runs went from ~250 s to ~900 s and the total alone did not show where the time went
    S.stepSecs = {}
    local lastFx, lastFxAt = "melee", 0
    local saved = false
    local function save(reason)
        if saved then return end
        saved = true
        if S.diff == "?" or S.diff:find("^picked") then S.diff = dungeonDiff() end -- set after the hub loaded
        S.reason = reason
        S.secs = os.time() - S.start
        -- which fight mode ran, so runs can be compared from the file alone (Pahtzx tests the modes by itself)
        S.ranged = (_G.GolfHub_DngRanged == true)
        S.swarmKites = tonumber(_G.GolfHub_SwarmKites) or 0
        S.rangedStalls = tonumber(_G.GolfHub_RangedStalls) or 0
        if S.minHp == math.huge then S.minHp = -1 end
        pcall(function() appendfile(file, HS:JSONEncode(S) .. "\n") end)
    end
    -- A reload parks this thread at its next task.wait, so the "script reloaded" save after the loop never ran:
    -- GolfHub_Unload calls this instead. Leaving the dungeon (teleport out) saves too. save() writes once per run.
    _G.GolfHub_DngStatsSave = save
    pcall(function()
        _G.GolfHub_Conn(lp.OnTeleport, function(state)
            if state == Enum.TeleportState.Started or state == Enum.TeleportState.InProgress then
                pcall(save, "teleport out")
            end
        end)
    end)
    local rem = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("Remotes")
    if rem and rem:FindFirstChild("BossFx") then
        _G.GolfHub_Conn(rem.BossFx.OnClientEvent, function(action, who, skill)
            if action == "warn" then
                lastFx, lastFxAt = tostring(who) .. ": " .. tostring(skill), tick()
                S.warns[lastFx] = (S.warns[lastFx] or 0) + 1
            end
        end)
    end
    local function bind(ch)
        local h = ch and ch:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 10)
        if not h then return end
        local last = h.Health
        _G.GolfHub_Conn(h.HealthChanged, function(v) -- dropped on reload with the rest of this copy
            if v < S.minHp and v > 0 then S.minHp = math.floor(v) end
            if v < last - 1 then
                local k = (tick() - lastFxAt < 2.5) and lastFx or "melee / no warning"
                S.dmg[k] = math.floor((S.dmg[k] or 0) + (last - v))
            end
            last = v
        end)
    end
    bind(lp.Character)
    _G.GolfHub_Conn(lp.CharacterAdded, bind)
    _G.GolfHub_Conn(lp:GetAttributeChangedSignal("DungeonDowned"), function()
        if lp:GetAttribute("DungeonDowned") == true then
            S.downs = S.downs + 1
            local k = (tick() - lastFxAt < 2.5) and lastFx or "melee / no warning"
            S.downBy[k] = (S.downBy[k] or 0) + 1
        end
    end)
    local lastDodge
    while gen == _G.GolfHub_Gen do
        task.wait(0.5)
        local dd = _G.GolfHub_DngDodge
        if dd and dd ~= lastDodge then S.dodges = S.dodges + 1 lastDodge = dd end
        pcall(function()
            -- getRealtimeDungeonStep is not reachable from here (the first version only ever recorded "downed");
            -- the combat step publishes the current step in _G.GolfHub_DngStepNow
            local key = (lp:GetAttribute("DungeonDowned") == true) and "downed" or tostring(_G.GolfHub_DngStepNow or "none")
            S.stepSecs[key] = (S.stepSecs[key] or 0) + 0.5
        end)
        local mf = workspace:FindFirstChild("Monsters")
        local bossNow = false
        for _, m in ipairs(mf and mf:GetChildren() or {}) do
            if tostring(m:GetAttribute("MonsterId") or "") == "dgn_crypt_boss" then bossNow = true break end
        end
        if bossNow and not S.bossSeen then S.bossSeen = os.time() - S.start end
        if S.bossSeen and not bossNow and not S.bossDead then S.bossDead = os.time() - S.start end
        -- 2 s, not 20: the hub now leaves the dungeon well within 20 s of the kill, and no run was written to the
        -- file from 2026-11-24 22:42 on although Pahtzx kept clearing runs
        if S.bossDead and os.time() - S.start - S.bossDead >= 2 then save("boss dead") return end
    end
    save("script reloaded")
end)
